Text:

<1>
|| śrīrastu ||
alaṅkāramaṇihāraḥ
śrīhayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namonamaḥ ||
śrīnivāsaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ ||
śrīvāsabrahmatantrottamakalimathanāllabdhacakrāṅkabhāṣyaṃ
śrīvāse deśikendre kalimathanagurāvārpitātmīyabhāram |
raṅgendrabrahmatantrādimapadakalijiddeśikendrāttaturye
śrīkṛṣṇabrahmatantrāgrimapadaparakālārthavaryaṃ śrayāmaḥ ||
śrīveṅkaṭadharaṇīdharaśikharālaṅkāramadbhutākāram | vāṇī mama paricinutānnāṇīyaḥ kimapi maṅgalaṃ jagatām || 1 ||
atrālaṅkāraśabdenāsya prabandhasyālaṅkāraviṣayakatvaṃ sūcitam ||
hṛdayabhuvaṃ mama hayamukhalakṣmīnārāyaṇāvalaṅkurutām | rājayuvarājabhūtau parakālāsthānarājyasīmani yau || 2 ||
sādaramiha vedagiriprādurbhūtaṃ sarojabhūdayitam | ghanasārasarasabhāsaṃ śirasā harimānamāmi paramādyam || 3 ||
vedagirau veṅkaṭādrau ‘vedādrirgaruḍādriśca’ iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt | prādurbhūtaṃ abhivyaktaṃ sarojabhuvaḥ śriyaḥ ‘sā’haṃ vai paṅkaje jātā’ iti mahābhāratokteḥ | dayitaṃ vallabhaṃ ghanasāraḥ jaladharavaraḥ ‘sāro bale sthirāṃśe ca nyāyye klībaṃ vare triṣu’ ityamaraḥ | tamiva sarasabhāsaṃ ādyaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ ataeva paraṃ sarvasmāduttamaṃ hariṃ śrīnivāsaṃ sādaraṃ yathā syāttathā śirasā ānamāmi |
pakṣe sarojabhūrbrahmā tasmin dayā’sya sañjātā dayitaḥ tārakāditvāditac | taṃ tathoktaṃ, ataeva vedagiri nigamavāci sādaraṃ, ataeva prādurbhūtaṃ madhukaiṭabhanirvartitanigamāpahāranirviṇṇe nīrajāsane
etasminnantare rājan devo hayaśirodharaḥ| jagrāha vedānakhilān rasātalagatān hariḥ ||
iti hayaśiraupākhyānoktarītyā vedoddharaṇāya kṛtāvatāramityarthaḥ | ghanasāraḥ karpūraḥ ‘atha karpūramastriyām | ghanasāraḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tamiva sarasabhāsaṃ ramaṇīyaviśadasuṣamaṃ ādyaṃ śirasā hariṃ hayaśirasamityarthaḥ | ‘itthaṃbhūtalakṣaṇe’ iti tṛtīyā | paraṃ atyantaṃ ānamāmi | atra śrīnivāsahayānanayorubhayorapi nantavyatvena prastutayoreva śleṣaḥ || 3 ||
anyonyānuguṇādhikavikasvarollāsilalitamadhuratamam | viśvālaṅkaraṇaṃ tadvibhātu mama hṛdi sadātanaṃ mithunam || 4 ||
anyonyasya parasparasya anuguṇāni adhikaṃ vikasvarāṇi ullāsīni ullāsavanti yāni lalitāni vilāsaviśeṣāḥ taiḥ madhuratamam | yadvā anyonyānuguṇādhikavikasvarollāso’syā stīti tathoktaṃ bhinnaṃ padam | lalitamadhuratamaṃ viśvālaṅkaraṇaṃ sakalalokavibhūṣaṇabhūtaṃ sadātanaṃ mithunaṃ śrīśrīnivāsarūpaṃ mama hṛdi vibhātu |
pakṣe anyonyaṃ anuguṇaḥ adhikaṃ vikasvaraḥ ullāsaścetyete’laṅkārā asmin santīti tathoktam | lalitena lalitanāmnā’laṅkāreṇa madhuratamaṃ viśvaṃ sarvaṃ alaṅkaraṇaṃ alaṅkāraśāstramityarthaḥ | yadvā viśvāni alaṅkaraṇāni yasmin viṣayaviṣayibhāvasambandhena santi tattathoktaṃ tadrūpaṃ mithunamiti yojanā | spaṣṭamanyat | vivakṣitasūcanāyāyaṃ śleṣasaṃrambhaḥ || 4 ||
yuktyullekhavicitraprauḍhoktinidarśanātigambhīrān | saparikarasamādhividhīnvacasā’laṃkṛtimaṇīngurūnkalaye || 5 ||
yuktīnāṃ ullekhena ūhena vicitrā āścaryāvahāḥ yāḥ prauḍhāḥ pragalbhāḥ uktayaḥ tāsāṃ nidarśanena prakāśanena atigambhīrān saparikaraḥ yamaniyamādiparikarasahitaḥ samādhividhiḥ dhyānavidhānaṃ yeṣāṃ tān anārataparabrahmadhyānabaddhādarānityarthaḥ | viśeṣaṇadvayena ‘dve vidye veditavye’ ityuktaparāvarajñānopādātṛtvaṃ sūcitam | kṛtimaṇīn vidvadvariṣṭhān gurūn lakṣmīnāthādyasmadācāryaparyantān alaṃ vacasā kalaye staumītyarthaḥ |
pakṣe yuktiḥ ullekhaḥ vicitraṃ prauḍhoktiḥ nidarśanetyetairalaṅkāraviśeṣeaiḥ atigambhīrān parikaraḥ samādhiḥ vidhirityetairalaṅkāraissaha vartanta iti tathoktān gurūn śreṣṭhān alaṅkṛtayo maṇaya iva alaṅkṛtimaṇayaḥ tān vacasā kalaye grathnāmītyarthaḥ | atra sūcyārthasya śleṣeṇa sūcanam || 5 ||
vakulasarālaṅkāraṃ sakalabhavāmayabharāgadaṅkāram | aśaṭhāntaraṅgasevyaṃ śaṭhārimabhyemyahaṃ dṛḍhābhaktiḥ ||
dṛḍhā bhaktiryasya saḥ dṛḍhabhaktiḥ | atra dṛḍhāśabdasya na puṃvadbhāvaḥ ‘striyāḥ puṃvat’ ityādinā puṃvadbhāvavidhau ‘apūraṇīpriyādiṣu’ iti paryudaste priyādau bhaktiśabdasya pāṭhāt | atrāpyalaṃkāraśabdena vakṣyamāṇasūcanam || 6 ||
upamotprekṣāgocaramuddhṛtaviṣamapratīpadṛṣṭāntam | nāthamuniṃ kalaye’laṃ kriyāvatāṃ brahmavedināṃ mānyam || 7 ||
upamotprekṣāyāḥ sāmyasambhāvanāyāḥ agocaraṃ asadṛśamiti yāvat | uddhṛtāḥ nirmūlitāḥ viṣamaiḥ pratīpaiḥ pratimatakathakaiḥ dṛṣṭāḥ antāḥ niścayāḥ teṣāṃ dṛṣṭāntāśśāstrāṇi vā yena taṃ nirastasakalavimatasiddhāntamityarthaḥ | ‘anto’strī niścaye nāśe svarūpe’gre’ntike’ntare’ iti ratnamālā, ‘dṛṣṭāntāvubhe śāstranidarśane’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe upamotprekṣe alaṅkṛtī gocarau pratipādyau yasya taṃ uddhṛtāḥ sthāpitāḥ viṣamaṃ pratīpaṃ dṛṣṭānta ityete’laṅkārā yena taṃ, alaṃ paryāptaṃ yathā syāttathā kriyāvatāṃ ananusaṃhitaphalakarmānuṣṭhānaśālināṃ ataeva brahmavedināṃ brahmavidāṃ mānyam, anena ‘kriyāvāneṣa brahmavidāṃ variṣṭhaḥ’ iti muṇḍakaśrutiḥ pratyabhijñāpyate | pakṣe alaṅkriyāvatām ālaṅkārikāṇāṃ, anyatsugamam | atrāpi śleṣamahimnā vivakṣitaṃ sūcitam || 7 ||
sādhukalitālpasārāvajñaṃ yāmunamunīndramāmanavai | chekoktiparigranthitasākṣepapratyanīkavakroktim || 8 ||
sādhu yathā syāttathā kalitā kṛtā alpasāreṣu aihikaphalasādhanakarmapratipādakavedapūrvabhāgeṣu avajñā tiraskāro yena taṃ ‘asāramalpasāraṃ ca sāraṃ sārataraṃ tyajet’ ityuktarītyā parityaktālpasāramityarthaḥ | anena asārarūpabāhyakudṛṣṭiśāstrāvajñānaṃ kaimutikanyāyasiddham | idaṃ āmuṣmikaphalapratipādakavedabhāgarūpasārāvajñāyāḥ ātmatatprāptisādhanamātrapratipādakāṃśalakṣaṇasāratarāvajñāyāścopalakṣaṇam | anenaiva ‘bhajetsāratamaṃ śāstre ratnākara ivāmṛtam’ ityuktarītyā paramātmatatprāptisādhanapratipādakavedapradeśarūpasāratamaniṣṭhatā cārthasiddhā | chekāḥ vidagdhāḥ yāḥ uktayaḥ tābhiḥ parigranthitāḥ bādhitāḥ krāntā iti vā, sākṣepāḥ ākṣepasahitāḥ pratyanīkānāṃ pratimatakathakānāṃ vakroktayaḥ anṛjuvyāhārāḥ yena taṃ tathoktaṃ ‘granthitaṃ gumbhite krānte hiṃsite ca triliṅgakam’ iti medinī | pakṣe sādhu yathā syāttathā ka litāḥ racitāḥ alpaṃ sāraḥ avajñā cetyetā alaṅkṛtayo yena taṃ, chekoktiḥ tannāmālaṅkṛtiḥ tayā parigranthite gumbhite miśrite ityarthaḥ | ākṣepeṇa tannāmnā’laṅkāreṇa saha vartete iti sākṣepe, pratyanīkaṃ ca vakroktiścetyete alaṅkṛtī yena taṃ tathoktam | yāmunamunīndraṃ āmanavai dhyāyāni | manu avabodhane, loṭ, uttamaikavacanam | sūcanaṃ pūrvavat | yaścakṣuśśrutināthastrātuṃ vedānmunitvamabhipadya | śruticakṣuragragaṇyo babhūva tamimaṃ bhajeya bhāṣyakṛtam || 9 ||
cakṣuśśrutināthaḥ śeṣaḥ vedān trātuṃ arthataḥ śabdataśca rakṣituṃ munitvaṃ rāmānujamunitvaṃ patañjalimunitvaṃ cābhipadya prāpya śruticakṣuṣāṃ vedavidāṃ agragaṇyo babhūva | ādau cakṣuśśrutināthaḥ munitve śruticakṣuragragaṇya ityetadeva vailakṣaṇyamiti bhāvaḥ | bhāṣyakṛtaṃ śrībhāṣyakāraṃ mahābhāṣyakāraṃ ca bhajeya || 9 ||
smṛtisandehabhrāntimadasaṃgativikalpamīlanollāsī | vedāntadeśikamaṇirmodānvidadhātu jagadalaṅkāraḥ || 10 ||
smṛtiṣu mānavādidharmasaṃhitāsu sandehabhrāntimatāṃ saṃśayabhramavatāṃ kautaskutānāṃ asaṅgatayaḥ saṅgatirahitāḥ asambaddhā ityarthaḥ | ye vikalpāḥ teṣāṃ mīlane nirāse ullāsī | pakṣe smṛtyādyullāsāntāssaptālaṅkārāḥ te santyasminniti tathoktaḥ | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ sarvālaṅkāravattāyāḥ || 10 ||
śrībrahmatantrakaliripugurvāsthānīdhurandharānakhilān | hṛdi bhāvaye’tyudārānvasudhālaṅkāramaṇihārān || 11 ||
vasudhālaṅkāramaṇihārānityanena alaṅkāramaṇihāra iti vivakṣitaprabandhanāmasūcanam || 11 || atimātrā pratipattiśśrīśe yasyādbhutā janairdadṛśe | sumahānparakālamunissa madanvayaśekharo gururjayatāt || 12 ||
yasya guroḥ śrīśe bhagavati viṣaye atimātrā mātrāmatikrāntā ativelā pratipattiḥ gauravaṃ prabodho vā
pratipattiḥ pravṛtau ca prāgalbhye gaurave’pi ca |
saṃprāptau ca prabodhe ca padaprāptau ca yoṣiti ||
iti medinī | pakṣe avidyamānaṃ timātraṃ tivarṇamātraṃ yasyāṃ sā pratipattiḥ prapattiḥ bharanyasanamityarthaḥ | pratipattiśabde tivarṇāpanayane prapattiriti niṣpatteḥ, śabdārthayostādātmyamiha śaraṇam | adbhutā āścaryāvahā satī janaiḥ dadṛśe | bhagavati yasya gauravaṃ jñānaṃ prapadanaṃ cātyāścaryāvahatayā janairdṛṣṭānītyarthaḥ | prapadanasyāścaryatvaṃ ca anenācāryeṇātimātrārtatayā prapadanamanuṣṭhāya svābhīpsita eva samaye mokṣasāmrājyasyāsāditatvāditi tadvaibhavaprakāśikādiṣu suspaṣṭam | dadṛśe ityanena sarvalokasākṣikatvamīdṛśaprapadanasya darśitam | madanvayasya vidyayā janmanā ca madvaṃśasya śekharaḥ | ubhayorapi śrīśailaghanagiryappalāryavaṃśyatvāttathoktiḥ || 12 ||
vādāḥ kudṛśāṃ śamitā jaladeneva pratīpatābhājaḥ | yena sa gururvijayatāṃ śrīvāsabrahmatantraparakālaḥ || 13 ||
jaladena pratīpatābhājaḥ pratilomatāṃ prāptā vādāḥ dāvāḥ ityarthaḥ | ta iva, arthagataṃ bahutvaṃ śabde āropyate | yena ācāryeṇa pratīpatābhājaḥ prātikūlyabhājaḥ kudṛśāṃ vādāḥ śamitāḥ | spaṣṭamanyat || 13 ||
śaraṇagatajanapunarbhavabhayāpanetā jayāya me bhavatāt | śrīvāsadeśikendropapadaśrībrahmatantrayatinetā || 14 ||
ekena yena budhatā gurutā kavitā ca darśitāvidhutā | durgrahabādhāṃ śamayatu sa śrīraṅgendrakaliripuyatīndraḥ || 15 ||
yena ekenaiva vidhutā candratā pakṣe avidhutā aprakampitā anyāparibhāvanīyatetyarthaḥ | budhatā saumyatā prājñatā ca, gurutā vākpatitā ācāryatā ca kavitā bhārgavatā kāvyakartṛtā ca darśitā | durgrahabādhāṃ duṣṭagrahāṇāṃ kujādīnāṃ pīḍāṃ durāgrahavyasanitāṃ ca śamayatu | ekasyaivāsya candrabudhādiśubhagraharūpatvāt durgrahabādhāśamanapaṭīyastvamastīti bhāvaḥ | anyatsugamam ||15||
tātāryasya gurorme khyātātmaguṇasya caraṇanalinayuge | ativelaṃ lagatu manaḥ pratilomaṃ vā’nulomaṃ vā || 16 ||
pratilomaṃ manaḥ nama ityarthaḥ | pratilomānulomaśabdayoḥ ‘acpratyanvavapūrvotsāmalomnaḥ’ ityac || 16 ||
navadurgatātadeśikakṛṣṇāmbāsūnurātanotīmam | śrīśailānvayajanmā kṛṣṇo’laṅkāramaṇihāram || 17 śrīśrīnivāsamanupamamupamādyairakhilajagadala ṅkāram | añcāmo’laṅkārairvāñchāmasmākameṣa saphalayatāt || 18 ||
capalosāvudyuṅkte vipulālaṅkāravarṇanavyājāt | bhagavatsvarūparūpaprabhāvaguṇavibhavaleśamabhidhātum ||
vāṇīṃ hariguṇabhaṇanātkṛtārthayitumeva kṛtiriyaṃ kriyate | lalitatayā bālānāmupakuryāccetprakāmamupakurutām || 20 ||
āracaye kṛtimetāmātmīyairlakṣyalakṣaṇaireva | yakṣādhipo navanidhirbhikṣāka ivānyavasu kimādatte || 21 ||
ānuṣṭubhena lakṣaṇamāryājātena lakṣyajātamapi | vṛttena viracaye’haṃ yattena prīyatāṃ phaṇigirīśaḥ || 22
śrīpatirahigirinilayaḥ prāpayatu nitāntahṛdyatāmenam | svopajñalakṣyalakṣaṇarūpamalaṅkāramaṇihāram || 23 ||
devacchando’nuguṇo dīvyādupamādisarasasaraśatabhāk | lakṣmīvallabhahṛdaye lalito’laṅkāramaṇihāraḥ || 24 || devasya śrīnivāsasya chandasaḥ abhilāṣasya anuguṇaḥ anurūpaḥ ‘chandaḥ padye’bhilāṣe ca’ iti sāntanānārtheṣvamaraḥ | śrīnivāsaprītyucita ityarthaḥ | pakṣe anugato guṇena anuguṇaḥ ‘avādayaḥ kruṣṭādyarthe tṛtīyayā’ iti samāsaḥ | upamādayaḥ alaṅkārā eva sarasāḥ ramaṇīyāḥ sarāḥ yaṣṭayaḥ teṣāṃ śataṃ bhajatīti tathoktaḥ | ‘latikā laḍikā yaṣṭistulyārthāssyussarassariḥ’ ityukteḥ | ata eva devacchandaḥ tannāmā, ‘devacchando’sau śatayaṣṭikaḥ’ ityanuśāsanāt | lalitaḥ alaṅkāramaṇihāraḥ etannāmā prabandhaḥ | pakṣe alaṅkārarūpaścāsau maṇihāraśca lakṣmīvallabhasya hṛdaye manasi vakṣasi ca dīvyāt prakāśatām || 24 ||
sindhumivāsmadgranthaṃ manthāneneva cetasā mathnan | viṣamiva doṣaṃ hitvā maṇimiva guṇamādadātu vibudhendraḥ || 25 ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre alaṅkāraprabandhe upoddhātaḥ
jayatu śrīvṛṣādristhaṃ jagatritayarakṣaṇam |
vadānyaṃ paramaṃ brahma vakṣo’laṅkāralakṣaṇam || 1 ||
vakṣasaḥ alaṅkāraḥ ābharaṇabhūtaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śrīvatsacihnaṃ yasya tat | anenālaṅkāralakṣaṇatvamasyānuṣṭubhaprabandhasya sūcitam | ayamalaṅkāralakṣaṇārambhaślokaḥ ||
<2>
athopamālaṅkārasaraḥ
udbhūtā bhāti sāmyaśrīrubhayoryatra sopamā |
yatra kāvye ubhayorupamānopameyayossahṛdayahṛdayāhlādinī sādṛśyalakṣmīḥ cārusādṛśyamiti yāvat, udbhūtā vyaṅgyabhāvamantareṇa sphuṭatamā satī bhāti niṣedhāpratiyogitayā lasati tatropamālaṅkāra ityarthaḥ | atra yatra dvayossādṛśyaṃ varṇyate tatropamālaṅkāra ityetāvaduktau ‘grathnāmi kāvyaśaśinaṃ vitatārtharaśmim’ ityādau upamālaṅkārāpattiḥ, tatra kāvyaśaśinorartharaśmīnāṃ ca sahṛdayahṛdayāhlādidharmasāmyena sādṛśyapratīteḥ | ata upamānopameyayorityuktam | kavisamayaprasiddhyā upamānopameyatvayogyayorityarthaḥ | na hi kāvyaśaśinorupamānopameyatvayogyatā’stītyadoṣaḥ | ‘gosadṛśo gavayaḥ’ ityādau gogavayayostathātve’pi nopamālaṅkāra iti tatrātivyāptivāraṇāya sahṛdayahṛdayāhlādinīti | idaṃ sahṛdayahṛdayāhlāditvaviśeṣaṇaṃ sarvālaṅkāralakṣaṇānuyāyi | ataeva ‘gaurvāhīkaḥ’ ityādau na rūpakaṃ, ‘sthāṇurvā puruṣo vā’ iti na sandehaḥ’ ‘idaṃ rajatam’ iti na bhrāntimān, ‘nedaṃ rajataṃ kiṃ tu śuktiḥ’ ityādau nāpahnava ityādyāhuḥ | udbhūteti vyaṅgyopamāvyāvṛttyartham
samarāṅgaṇamārūḍhaṃ bhavantaṃ paśyatāṃ nṛṇām |
bhavaṃ kalpāntasaṃkruddhaṃ draṣṭuṃ notkaṇṭhate manaḥ ||
ityādau tathābhūtaṃ tvāṃ dṛṣṭavatastathābhūtabhavadarśanamāsīditi pratīteḥ kiñcitkurvato’śakyavastvantarakṛtirūpaviśeṣālaṅkāraḥ | tena ca tathābhūto bhava iva tadā tvaṃ sthita ityupamā vyajyate | atropamāyā vyaṅgyāyāḥ prādhānyenālaṅkāryatā, na tvalaṅkāratā | ata udbhūtetyasyāvyaṅgyatvaparyavasānādasya na vyaṅgyopamāyāmativyāptiḥ| niṣedhāpratiyogitvavivakṣaṇāt vyatirekavyudāsaḥ | tataśca sahṛdayahṛdayāhlādadāyitve satyavyaṅgyaṃ sādṛśyamupametyetāvadeva lakṣaṇamiha vivakṣitam, na tūpamānopameyatvayogyayorityapi, ‘grathnāmi kāvyaśaśinam’ ityādau cārutvābhāvādevātivyāptyasaṃbhavāt | ataeva ubhayossāmyaśrīrityevoktaṃ, tadapyananvaye’tivyāptinirāsāya |
na caivamapyupameyopamāpratīpayorativyāptiḥ| tayoruktasādṛśyavarṇanasattvenopamālaṅkārāntarbhāvasyeṣṭatvāt, tṛtīyasadṛśavyavacchedasya prasiddhopamānaprātilomyena prasiddhopameyotkarṣasyādhikasya tatra phalasya sadbhāvena tata eva tayorupamāyāḥ pṛthagukteḥ, upameyopamāyāṃ prakṛtāprakṛtagocaropamādvayarūpatayā pratīpe aprakṛtaviṣayatayā ca tasyāḥ pratīteḥ | anyathā aprakṛtaviṣayasmṛtyāderapi prakṛtavaṣayasmaraṇādito’laṅkārāntaratvāpatteśca | ataeva ‘pratīpamupameyopamā cetyubhayamapi saṅgrāhyameveti na tadvyāpanaṃ lakṣaṇasya doṣaḥ’ iti citramīmāṃsāyāmupamālakṣaṇapariṣkaraṇāvasare kaṇṭharaveṇaiva pratipāditam |
kecittu sādṛśyapratītimātroddeśyakakavisaṃrambhaviṣayībhūtacārusādṛśyamupameti vivakṣitam | tena ananvayopameyopamāpratīpeṣu na kvāpyativyāptiḥ, tatra sādṛśyasyānupamatvadyotanatṛtīyasadṛśavyavacchedopameyotkarṣaphalakatvena kavisaṃrambhagocaratayā sādṛśyabodhamātroddeśyakakavisaṃrambhaviṣayatvavirahādityāhuḥ || idaṃ kuvalayānandakṛdanurodhena | rasagaṅgādharakṛdanurodhena tvevaṃ lakṣaṇam
vākyārthopaskārakaṃ yatsādṛśyaṃ cāru sopamā || 2 ||
sādṛśyaṃ sundaraṃ vākyārthopaskārakamupamālaṅkṛtiḥ | cārutvaṃ ca camatkārādhāyakatvaṃ, camatkāraścānandaviśeṣassahṛdayahṛdayasākṣikaḥ| ‘sāgarassāgaropamaḥ’ ityādyananvaye sādṛśyasya dvitīyasatīrthyanivartanamātrārthamupāttatayā svayamapratiṣṭhānādacamatkāritvameva | ataeva tasyānvayābhāvādananvayaṃ tamabhidadhate | ‘nāthatvadvadanaupamyaṃ kathaṃ dhattāṃ sudhākaraḥ’ ityādivyatireke camatkāriṇo niṣedhasya nirūpaṇāya pratiyoginassādṛśyasya nirūpaṇamacamatkārakameva | evamabhedapradhāneṣvapi rūpakāpahnutipariṇāmabhrāntimadullekhādiṣu, bhedapradhāneṣu dṛṣṭāntaprativastūpamādīpakatulyayogitādiṣu camatkāriṣu tattanniṣpādakatvenāvasthitasyāpi sādṛśyasya camatkāritāvaidhuryeṇa nāstyupamālaṅkāratvam | ‘mukhamiva candraḥ’ iti pratīpe, ‘candra iva mukhaṃ mukhamiva candraḥ’ ityupameyopamāyāṃ ca sādṛśyasya camatkāritvānnātiprasaṅgaśśaṅkyaḥ, tayossaṅgrāhyatvāt ||
nanu ‘tvayi kopo mamābhāti sudhāṃśāviva pāvakaḥ’ ityādāvupamānasyātimātramasambhāvitatayā na tāvatsādṛśyameva śakyaṃ pratipattuṃ, camatkāraḥ punardavīyāneveti cet kavinā hi khaṇḍaśaḥ padārthopasthitimatā yathepsitaṃ saṃbhāvitatvenākāreṇa sudhākarādhikaraṇakaṃ pāvakaṃ parikalpya tena saha sāmyasyāpi kalpane bādhakābhāvāt | na ca kalpitamasatsādṛśyaṃ kathaṃkāraṃ camatkāramāracayediti vācyam | sukumārasundaratarasuvarṇapratimātvena | bhāvanayā puratassthitāyā vanitāyāḥ parirambhasya priyambhāvukatādarśanāt | upamānopameyayossattvasya lakṣaṇe praveśābhāvānnātra doṣagandho’pi || ataeva
stanābhoge patanbhāti kapolātkuṭilo’lakaḥ |
śaśāṅkabimbato merau lambamāna ivoragaḥ ||
ityādāvapi nānupapattiḥ ||
pare tu asyāḥ kalpitopamāyā upamānāntarābhāvaphalakatvenālaṅkārāntaratāmāhuḥ | tanna sādṛśyasya camatkāritayopamānta rbhāvasyaivocitatvāt, sannirūpitatvasya lakṣaṇe praveśābhāvāt | upamānāntarābhāvaphalakatvaṃ hyupamāviśeṣatve sādhakaṃ na tūpamābahirbhāve iti ||
pūrṇā luptetyalaṃkāratattvajñaissā dvidhoditā | upamānaṃ copameyaṃ dharmo vācakamityadaḥ |
catuṣṭayamupāttaṃ cetsā hi pūrṇopamā matā || 3 ||
yathā
ānandanilayanāmani vimānadhāmani vibhāsamānaṃ tat | dhāma ciratnaṃ ratnaṃ śyāmalamiva haimasaṃpuṭe jayati || 26 ||
atra śyāmalaṃ ratnaṃ ciratnaṃ dhāma vibhāsamānaṃ ivaśabdaścetyeteṣāmupamānopameyasādhāraṇadharmopamāvācakānāṃ caturṇāmupādānā diyaṃ pūrṇopamā | idaṃ kuvalayānandānusāreṇodāharaṇam ||
rasagaṅgādharānusāreṇa tu yathā
kṛpayā sumahājālākṛṣṭānmīnānivāhiśailamaṇe | mocayasi mohajālāviṣṭāndīnānkadā nu khalvenān || 27 ||
atra pratīyamānā upamā samagravākyārthasya kadeti śāntarasasya sūcitacintopaskaraṇadvārā upaskārakatayā’laṅkāraḥ ||
varṇyopamānayordharmopamāvācakayorapi |
ekadvitryanupādāne bhavelluptopamā’ṣṭadhā || 4 ||
varṇyamupameyaṃ upameyādīnāmekasya dvayostrayāṇāṃ vā’nupādāne luptopamā | sā cāṣṭavidhā bhavet ||
vācakasyātra dharmasya dharmavācakayostathā |
varṇyavācakayorlope tattalluptāḥ khalūpamāḥ || 5 ||
upamānasya lope tu talluptā pañcamī smṛtā |
proktā ṣaṣṭhī vācakopamānalupteti kovidaiḥ || 6 ||
dharmāopamānaluptā’nyā lāepe dharmopamānayoḥ |
aṣṭamī dharmopamānavācakānāṃ vilopane || 7 ||
tāmeva vibhajate atra vācakādīnāṃ lope tattalluptāḥ | tathāhi vācakaluptā | dharmaluptā | dharmavācakaluptā | upameyavācakaluptā | evamupamānaloparahitāścatvāro bhedāḥ | upamānaluptā | vācakopamānaluptā | dharmopamānaluptā | dharmopamānavācakaluptā | evamupamānalopasahitāścatvāro bhedāḥ iti luptopamā aṣṭavidhāḥ ||
tatra vācakaluptā yathā
candiraruciraniṭālaṃ sundarataravārṣikāmbudavinīlam | mandaradharamahigiripatikandaradhāmāstu nāma hṛdi dhāma || 28 ||
atra candiraruciraniṭālaṃ sundarataravārṣikāmbudavinīlamityubhayatrāpi vācakalopaḥ candira iva ruciraṃ vārṣikāmbudamiva vinīlamityarthe “upamānāni sāmānyavacanaiḥ’ iti samāsavidhāyakaśāstranibandhanaḥ ||
dharmaluptā yathā
śaradindusadṛśavadanaṃ sphuradindiramahigirīndrakṛtasadanam | kundasumopamaradanaṃ bṛndaṃ mahasāṃ dhunotu bhayakadanam || 29 ||
bhayakadanaṃ bhavabhayasaṃmardaṃ ‘kadanaṃ mardane pāpe’ iti viśvaḥ | atra śaradindusadṛśavadanaṃ kundasumopamaradanamityubhayatrāpi dharmalopaḥ| sa tvaicchikaḥ na tu śāstravihitaḥ āhlādanādinā śaradindusadṛśavadanamityāderapi suvacatvāt || yathā vā
kusumasame lāvaṇye sarijjharīvegasadṛśi tāruṇye | āyuṣi tṛṇānalanibhe phaṇābhṛdadrīśa naiva viśvasimaḥ || 30 ||
atra kṣipramlānatvamekathā’navasthānaṃ sadyonirvāṇatvaṃ ca samānadharmā anupāttāḥ ||
dharmavācakaluptā yathā
candanagirikandaracarasundarataragandhavāhaśābantī | bhintāmapāṅgarekhā santāpaṃ dandaśūkagirinetuḥ|| 31
atra gandhavāhaśābantītyatra dharmavācakaluptā | gandhavāhaśāba ivācarantītyarthe vihitasya gandhavāhaśābavadānandanātmakācārārthakasya kvipaḥ ivaśabdena samaṃ lopāt | atra dharmalopa aicchikaḥ ānandadāyitayā gandhavāhaśābantīti tadupādānasyāpi sambhavāt | bhintāṃ bhinattu | bhideḥ raudhādikasya svaritettvāttaṅi loṭ || 31 ||
upameyavācakaluptā yathā 
munimanasāmapi dūre svakṛtavihāre’lamupaniṣatsāre| paramapadīyati vibhavaiḥ parame na rameta phaṇigirau ko vā || 32 ||
upaniṣatsāratvenādhyavasite śrīnivāse svakṛtavihāre sati
māyāvī paramānandaṃ tyaktvā vaikuṇṭhamuttamam |
svāmipuṣkariṇītīre ramayā saha modate ||
ityuktaprakāreṇa svasminviharamāṇe satītyarthaḥ | paramapadīyatītyatra vācakopameyalopaḥ ātmānamityupameyasyopamāvācakena sahānupādānāt| naca ‘upamānādācāre’ ityupamānādācārārthe kyaco vidhānātparamapadamivācaratītyarthāvagame’pyātmānamityasya kathaṃ nirṇayaḥ anyasyāpi tatsambhavāditi vācyam |paramapadānurūpācaraṇasya vibhavakaraṇakatvarūpaviśeṣaṇasāmarthyena tadavagamāt, tathāvidhācaraṇe vibhavānāṃ svārasikatayā svakīyatvāvagateḥ | ayaṃ ca lopa aicchikaḥ, svātmānaṃ paramapadīyatītyupameyopādānasyāpi sambhavāt || 32 ||
upamānaluptā vācakopamānaluptā ca yathā
dviradasamālasagamanaṃ suragirigurudhairyamakhilabhayaśamanam | harimīḍe ripudamanaṃ dvirasanagirisadanamindirākamanam || 33 ||
atrādye pāde dviradasamālasagamanamityatra dviradagamanasyopamānasyānupādānādupamānaluptā | dvitīyapāde suragirigurudhairyamityatra vācakasya ivādessuragiridhairyasyopamānasya cānupādānādvācakopamānaluptā ||
dharmopamānaluptā dharmopamānavācakaluptā ca yathā
jalanidhinibhagāmbhīryaṃ kalaye hṛdi kalpakāgamaudāryam | nilayitaśeṣāhāryaṃ jaladhisutācittahāri saundaryam || 34 ||
saundaryamitīdaṃ bhinnaṃ padaṃ, tattvenādhyavasito bhagavānśrī nivāsa ityarthaḥ | atra jalanidhinibhagāmbhīryamityatra niravadhikatvādessādhāraṇadharmasya jalanidhigāmbhīryasyopamānasya cānupādānāddharmopamānaluptā | kalpakāgamasyaudāryamivaudāryaṃ yasya taditivigrahe niravaśeṣābhīṣṭārthaparipūraṇāderdharmasya kalpakāgamaudāryasyopamānasya ivādervācakasya cānupādānāddharmopamānavācakaluptā | anyadagre vakṣyate ||
evaṃ kuvalayānandaprakāreṇopamoditā |
prācāṃ mate vibhāgo’syā upamāyāḥ prapañcyate ||
pūrṇā lupteti bhedena dvividhā sā tayoḥ punaḥ ||
śrautyārthīti dvidhā pūrṇā vākyagā ca samāsagā |
taddhitastheti ca tredhā te dve syātāṃ pṛthakpṛthak ||8
prācīnānāmālaṃkārikāṇāṃ mate upamā pūrṇā lupteti dvividhā | tayormadhye pūrṇā śrautī ārthīti dvividhā te śrautyārthyau dve api pṛthakpṛthak vākyagā samāsagā taddhitageti tredhā syātāmiti pūrṇopamā ṣaḍvidhā bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
yathevavādiśabdānāṃ sākṣātsādṛśyavācinām |
upādāne bhavecchrautītyāhuśśāstravicakṣaṇāḥ || 9 ||
ye dharmivyavadhānena sādṛśyapratipādakāḥ |
sadṛṅnibhādiśabdānāṃ teṣāmārthī prayogataḥ || 10 ||
śrautīmārthīṃ ca lakṣayati | yathetyādinā sākṣātsādṛśyapratipādakayathevādiśabdānāṃ prayoge śrautī śrutyā sādṛśyapratipādanāt | vādītyādiśabdena ‘tatra tasyeva’ iti vihitavatergrahaṇam | tasya ivārthe vihitatvāt | sādṛśyaviśiṣṭadharmiparyantatulyasadṛśasadṛṅnibhasaṅkāśādiśabdānāṃ prayoge ārthī, arthasiddhatvāt | nibhādītyādiśabdena ‘tena tulyaṃ’ iti vihitavatergrahaṇam | tasya tulyārthe vāihitatvāt | yadyapi yathevavādipadāni yasmāccandrādipadātparāṇi prayujyante, tāni tadarthasya candrāderupamānatvapratīterupamānaviśeṣaṇāni santi svasamabhivyāhṛtapadārthasyopamānatvapratītiprayojakāni | na tu mukhāderupameyatvapratītiprayojakāni | tathā’pi yathā rājñaḥ puruṣa ityatra ṣaṣṭhyāssvasamabhivyāhṛtaprātipadikārthe rājñaḥ svārthabhūtasambandhapratiyogitvapratītiprayojakatve’pi puruṣapadasamabhivyāhāre sati vyutpattimahimnā tadanuyogitvapratītirapi tathā mukhādisamabhivyāhāre mukhādyanuyogitvapratītirapi sambhavati | etena padmamiva mukhamityatra padmasādṛśyaṃ mukhamiti pratītissyāt, na tu padmasadṛśaṃ mukhamiti parāstam | yathā rājñaḥ puruṣa ityatra prakṛtipratyayapuruṣapadaiḥ rājā saṃbandhaḥ puruṣa iti pratītāvapi samabhivyāhāravaśādrājasambandhī puruṣa iti pratītiḥ, tathaiva vyutpatteḥ| evamatrāpi viśiṣṭapratītyupapattirityāhuḥ prāñcaḥ |
vastutastvivārthassādṛśyameva | tacca nopamānasya candrāderviśeṣaṇam | kiṃ tu upamānabhūtaścandrādereva sādṛśye pratiyogitāsambandhena viśeṣaṇam | candrādiviśeṣitaṃ ca sādṛśyaṃ mukhādāvanuyogitāsambandhenānveti | candrapratiyogikasādṛśyānuyogimukhamityākārakaścandra iva mukhamityādau bodha iti bodhyam ||
tatra śrautī trividhā yathā
gaganaṃ yathā phaṇīśvaranagapatihṛdayaṃ vinīlamujjvalati | tārāvaliriva hārāvalirārdrāvacca kaustubhe śobhā (kaustubhasya śrīḥ) || 35 ||
atra gaganaṃ yathetyādivinīlamityante śrautī vākye pūrṇā yathetyasamastasya vācakasya gaganamityupamānasya phaṇīśvaranagapatihṛdayamityupameyasya vinīlamiti sādhāraṇadharmasya copādānāt | tārāvaliriva hārāvalirityatra samāse śrautī pūrṇā | ‘ivena saha samāso vibhaktyalopaśca’ iti ivaśabdayoge samāsasya vibhaktyalopasya ca smaraṇāt | vācakādīnāṃ caturṇāmupādānācca | ārdrāvatkaustubhe śobhetyatra taddhite śrautī pūrṇā | saptamīsamarthādvateḥ ‘tatra tasyeva’ iti sūtreṇa ivārthavihitavatervācakasya upamādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ copādānāt ārdrāyāmiva ārdrāvaditi vigrahaḥ | kaustubhasya śrīriti pāṭhe ārdrāyā iveti vigrahaḥ ṣaṣṭhīsamarthādapyuktasūtreṇaiva vatervidhānāt || 35 ||
ārthī trividhā, pūrṇā yathā
śaśadharabimbena samo viśadatayā kundabṛndasaṅkāśaḥ | dhavalasaroruhavadayaṃ pravilasati karodare hareśśaṅkhaḥ || 36 || śaśadharabimbena samo viśadatayetyatrārthī pūrṇā vākye | viśadatayetyetatsarvatrānveti | kundabṛndasaṅkāśa ityatrārthī samāse pūrṇā | dhavalasaroruhavadityatrārthī taddhite pūrṇā ‘tena tulyaṃ kriyā cedvatiḥ’ iti tulyārthavihitasya taddhitasya vatipratyayasyopādānāt | evaṃ ṣaḍvidhā’pi pūrṇā saṃkṣepata udāhṛtā ||
atha luptāssāmānyato vibhajate
trividhā syādekalaptā dviluptā tu caturvidhā |
ekarūpā trilupteti luptā sāmānyato’ṣṭadhā || 11 ||
dharmasyāthopamānasya vācakasya vilopane |
trividhā syādekaluptā dviluptā tu caturvidhā || 12 ||
dharmavācakayorlope tathā dharmopamānayoḥ |
varṇyavācakayostadvadvācakāvarṇyayorapi || 13 ||
triluptā tvekadhā dharmavācakāvarṇyalopataḥ ||
dharmaluptā, upamānaluptā, vācakaluptā ceti trividhā ekaluptā | dviluptā tu dharmavācakaluptā, dharmopamānaluptā, upameyavācakaluptā, vācakopamānaluptā ceti caturvidhā | triluptā tu dharmavācakopamānaluptetyekavidheti sāmānyato luptā’ṣṭavidhā ||
yathā
tvaṃ nidhiriva tava sadṛśo naiva vibhurjaladanīlajalajākṣa | tava sauśīlyamatulyaṃ mitrīyasi natajane’bdhigāmbhīryaḥ || 37 || tvaṃ nidhirivetyatra bhogyatvādidharmānupādānāddharmaluptā ||1|| tava sadṛśo vibhurnaivetyupamānasya sāmānyato niṣedhādupamānaluptā||2|| jaladanīletyatra jalada iveti vācakānupādānādvācakaluptā ||3|| jalajākṣetyatra jalaje ivākṣiṇī yasyeti prasannatāderdharmasya vācakasya cānupādānāddharmavācakaluptā ||4|| tava sauśīlyamatulyamityatra lokottaratvāderdharmasya atulyamiti niṣedhenopamānasya cānupādānāddharmopamānaluptā ||5|| mitrīyasi natajane ityatra mitramivātmānamācarasītyupameyabhūtasyātmano vācakasya cānupādānādupameyavācakaluptā ||6|| vācakopamānaluptā tvagre udāhariṣyate | abdhigāmbhīrya ityatra abdhergāmbhīryamiva gāmbhīryamityasminnarthe upamānasya vācakasya dharmasyāpyanukterdharmavācakopamānaluptā ||7|| iti sāmānyatassapta luptodāharaṇāni saṃgṛhītāni ||
krameṇa dharmaluptādervibhāgo’tha pradarśyate ||14||
pūrṇāvaddharmaluptā’pi śrautyārthīti dvidhā bhavet ||
tatra śrautī taddhite tu na syādanvayahānataḥ || 15 ||
śuddhadharmaluptā pūrṇāvadeva śrautī ārthī ceti dvidhā bhavet | vākya samāse taddhite ca bhavet | tatra śrautī dharmaluptā tu taddhite na sambhavet ṣaṣṭhīsamarthātsaptamīsamarthādvā vihitasya vaterdharmopādānaṃ vinā’nvayasaukaryābhāvāt na hi bhavati madhurāvatpāṭaliputre pāṭaliputrasya vetyatra prākāra iti dharmopādānaṃ vinā’nvayasaukaryam| taddhitādhikāravihitakalpabdeśyādiprayoge’pi kṛṣṇakalpaḥ kṛṣṇadeśya ityādau dharmisāmānādhikaraṇyapratīterārthyeveti śrautyā dharmaluptāyā asaṃbhavātsā pañcavidhaiva || nanu ‘ive pratikṛtau’ ityadhikāre ivārthavihitacchādipratyayāntānāṃ ‘kuśāgrīyā buddhiḥ, śaileyaṃ dadhi’ ityādīnāṃ padānāṃ prayoge dharmopādānaṃ vinā nairākāṅkṣyasadbhāvātsaṃbhavati śrautī dharmaluptā’pīti cenna | ‘ive pratikṛtau’ ityadhikāravihitānāṃ pratyayānāṃ tulyārtha eva vidhānābhyupagamāvaśyaṃbhāvāt | anyathā teṣāṃ sādṛśyaviśiṣṭadharmiparyantatvābhāvena kuśāgrīyā buddhirityādyupameyasāmānādhikaraṇyāsambhavātsādṛśyavidhānamapi sadṛśe paryavasyatīti sūtre ivagrahaṇaṃ na virudhyata iti vadanti ||
śrautī dharmaluptā vākye yathā
jalado yathā tathā tvaṃ nākina iha kekino yathaiva tathā | jaladhiryathā tathā tvaṃ narā nanu sarijjharā yathaiva tathā || 38 ||
atra he bhagavanniti sambuddhissāmarthyāllabhyate | jalado yathā kekino yathetyādau yathetyasamastatayā nirdeśādvākye śrautī dharmaluptā, yathākramamānandāvahatvāderullāsitvādeśca | evaṃ jaladhiryathetyādau prāpyatvādeḥ narā ivetyādau tatpravaṇatvādeśca sādhāraṇadharmasyānupādānāt ||
śrautī dharmaluptā samāse yathā
cintāmaṇidharaṇībhṛti rantāraṃ bhuvi jaganniyantāram | cintāmaṇimiva manujāstvāṃ tāvatsaṃśritā hare sarve || 39 || atrābhīpsitadātṛtvādidharmānupādānāt cintāmaṇimiveti samāse śrautī dharmaluptā | taddhite tviyaṃ na sambhavatītyavocāma | cintāmaṇidharaṇībhṛditi veṅkaṭādrereva nāmāntaram | tathā cocyate vāmanapurāṇe
cintisatya tu siddhyemaṃ cintāmaṇigiriṃ viduḥ || iti || brahmāṇḍapurāṇāntargate veṅkaṭādrimāhātmye ca
śrīveṅkaṭagirirnāma kṣetraṃ puṇyaṃ mahītale | ityupakramya
añjanādrirvṛṣādriśca vedādrirgaruḍācalaḥ |
tīrthādriśśrīnivāsādriścintāmaṇigiristathā |
vṛṣabhādrirvarāhādrirjñānādriḥ kanakācalaḥ |
ānandādriśca nīlādriḥ krīḍādriḥ puṣkarācalaḥ |
siṃhācalaśca śrīśailastathā nārāyaṇācalaḥ ||
vaikuṇṭhādriśśeṣaśaila iti nāmāni viṃśatiḥ || iti ||
ārthī dharmaluptā vākyasamāsataddhitagā yathā
vṛṣaśikhariśikharadharaṇī sadṛśī ramaṇīmaṇermurārātiḥ | mṛgamadatilakanikāśaḥ puṣkariṇī darpaṇīkalpā || 40 ||
atra sadṛśī ramaṇīmaṇeriti vākyagā, mṛgamadatilakanikāśa iti samāsagā, darpaṇīkalpeti taddhitagā cārthī dharmaluptā; rāmaṇīyakaśyāmalimanairmalyādidharmāṇāmanupādānāt ||
kvacitpaṭupaṭurityādau dvirbhāve’pi dharmaluptā dṛśyata iti citramīmāṃsākārāḥ | tadedamudāharaṇam ābhāti madhuramadhuraṃ svargādiphalaṃ na jātu tanmadhuram | phaṇadharadharādharadhuraṃdharaṃ bhajata tajjanāściraṃ madhuram || 41 ||
atra ‘prakāre guṇavacanasya’ iti sādṛśye dvirbhāvavidhānādyatsvabhāvato’madhuramevāpātato madhuravadbhāti tadvacano’yaṃ madhuraśabdo madhurasadṛśe vartata ityārthī dharmaluptā| na caiṣā vākye samāse vā, madhuramadhuramityekapadatvāt ||
rasagaṅgādharakārastu paṭupaṭurityādau vācakasyāpyanupādānādvācakadharmaluptāyāmetadādhikyamudbhāvayitumucitaṃ, na tu dharmaluptāyāṃ, dharmamātraluptāyā eva dharmaluptāśabdena tairvivakṣaṇāt | anyathā ekaluptātvena dviluptānāṃ triluptāyāśca grahaṇātpṛthagupādānamasaṃbaddhameva syāt | na cātra vācakasya dvirbhāvasyaiva sattvānnāsti lopaḥ api tu dharmamātrasyaiveti śakyaṃ vaktuṃ, dvirbhāvasya bhāṣyakaiyaṭādau sādṛśyadyotakatāyā evāṅgīkāreṇa tadvācakatāyā asambhavādityavādīt ||
luptopamānā dvividhā vākyagā ca samāsagā ||
luptopamānā vākyagā yathā
vicite nikhile’pi jagatyucitaṃ śrīveṅkaṭādriṇā sadṛśam | nūnaṃ nopalabheta sthānaṃ jāto’tha jāyamāno vā || 42 ||
atra sadṛśaniṣedhādupamānaluptā vākye | atra ‘veṅkaṭādrisamaṃ sthānaṃ brahmāṇḍe nāsti kiñcana’ iti vacanaṃ smartavyam | na cātropamānābhāvena sāddaśyābhāvasya paryavasānātsādṛśyaparyavasānasya copamājīvitatvādalaṅkārāntarameva nopamānalupteti vācyam | veṅkaṭādriṇā sadṛśaṃ sthānaṃ jāto vā jāyamāno vā nopalabhetetyuktyā janiṣyamāṇena yena kenacit kimapi sthānametattulyamupalapsyeteti sādṛśyaparyavasānamastītyupamānaluptaiveyamupamā, nālaṅkārāntaramiti vaktuṃ yuktatvāt ||
upamānaluptā samāsagā yathā
nāstīha vastu dhanyaṃ kastūrīsadṛśamiti vayaṃ vidmaḥ | bhujagagirīśasya mukhaṃ trijagattilakasya tilakitaṃ hyanayā || 43 ||
atra sadṛśaniṣedhādupamānaluptā samāse ||
vādiluptā samāse syātkarmādhārakyacoḥ kyaṅi |
karmakartrorṇamuli ca ṇinau kvipi ca taddhite ||
evaṃ navaprakāraiṣā jñeyā vācakalopinī || 17 ||
vācakaluptāṃ vibhajate vādiluptā upamānavācakavāyathevādiluptā samāse ‘upamānādācāre’ iti vihitakarmakyaci, ‘adhikaraṇācca’ iti vihite adhikaraṇakyaci, ‘kartuḥ kyaṅsalopaśca’ iti kartṛkyaṅi, ‘upamāne karmaṇi ca’ iti karmaṇamuli, cātkartṛṇamuli, ‘kartaryupamāne’ iti vihite ṇinau, ‘sarvaprātipadikebhyaḥ kvibvā vaktavyaḥ’ ityācārārthavihite kvipi ca taddhite ca bhavantī navavidhā bhavati || atra vācakaluptā samāsagā yathā
vidalitavalaripumaṇivaraśakalavinīlaṃ śrayantamahiśailam | vāraṃ vāraṃ hṛdaye smeraṃ kalayeya kamapi śṛṅgāram || 44 ||
atra śakalavinīlamityatra samāse vācakalopaḥ | ‘candiraruciraniṭālam’ iti pūrvamudāhṛtamapyasyodāharaṇam ||
karmakyaci vācakaluptā yathā
haryaṅke viharantī paryaṅkīyati tametamuttuṅgam | upadhānīyati kamalā tasya bhujaṃ bhujagabhogasukumāram || 45 ||
atra paryaṅkīyati upadhānīyatītyubhayatrāpi paryaṅkamivācarati upadhānamivācaratītyarthe karmakyaci haryaṅkaparyaṅkayoḥ bhujopadhānayośca uttuṅgaṃ bhujagabhogasukumāramiti sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | paryaṅkīyatītyādau ‘upamānādācāre’ iti karmaṇassubantādācārārthe kyaci ‘kyaci ca’ ityākārasya ītvam | haryaṅkaṃ paryaṅkamivācaratītyatra vākye prakrāntaṃ karmadvayaṃ, tatropamānakarmaṇo’ntarbhūtatvāttadapekṣayā’sāvakarmako dhātuḥ | taduktaṃ
dhātorarthāntare vṛtterdhātvarthenopasaṅgrahāt |
prasiddheravivakṣātaḥ karmaṇo’karmakā kriyā ||
iti | anyakarmāpekṣayā tu sakarmaka eva | yadāha bhagavān bhāṣyakāraḥ ‘dve hyatra karmāṇī upamānakarmopameyakarma ceti | upamānakarmāntarbhūtaṃ upameyakarmaṇā sakarmako bhavati’ iti | evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam |
yathā vā
indrīyanti dṛśaste spṛśanti yāṃstānviśo bhṛśaṃ vibhavaiḥ | candrīyanti diśaste yaśaḥprakāśā viśālaviśadimnā || 46 ||
idamapi karmakyaci vācakaluptāyā evodāharaṇam | indrīyanti candrīyantītyubhayatrāpi indramivācaranti candramivācarantītyarthe karmakyaci indrasya viśāṃ ca vibhavairiti sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ candrasya diśāṃ ca viśālaviśadimneti sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ| viśaḥ manujānityarthaḥ | ‘dvau viśau vaiśyamanujau’ ityamaraḥ | dvitīyapāde bhṛśaṃ vibhavairityatra ‘bhṛśamudārāḥ’ iti, caturthacaraṇasya ‘yaśaḥ prakāśāstu kāśanīkāśāḥ’ iti ‘śeṣaśailendoḥ’ iti vā nirmāṇe ubhayatrāpi dharmasyāpyanupādānāddharmavācakaluptā’pīyaṃ bhaviṣyatīti dhyeyam || evaṃcātra dharmopādānānupādāne aicchike iti dhyeyam | asya pūrvodāharaṇādayameva viśeṣaḥ ||
adhikaraṇakyaci vācakaluptā yathā
jaladhau jananagṛhīyati jalaje kelīgṛhīyati manojñe | saudhīyati veṅkaṭapativiśaṅkaṭorasthale’male kamalā || 47 || manojñe ityetajjaladhāvityatrāpyanuṣañjanīyam | atra jaladhau jananagṛhīyatītyādyadhikaraṇakyaci jaladhijananagṛhayoḥ jalajakelīgṛhayośca manojñe iti, bhagavadvakṣasthalasaudhayoḥ amale iti ca sādhāraṇadharmaḥ| manojñe ityasya jaladhāvityatrānanvaye dharmavācakaluptāyāstadudāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati itaradvācakaluptāyāḥ | evaṃ cātrāpi dharmopādānānupādāne vaivakṣike eveti dhyeyam ||
kartṛkyaṅi vācakaluptā yathā
bhagavanbhavaccaritraṃ bhavataptānāṃ vidhūyate nṝṇām |
adasīyarasajñānāṃ tadasīma madhūyate rasajñānām ||
adasīyarasajñānāṃ etadbhagavaccaritrasambandhirasavidāṃ janānāṃ sambandhasāmānye ṣaṣṭhī rasajñānāṃ rasanānāṃ asīma anavadhikaṃ taccaritraṃ madhūyate ityanvayaḥ | atra vidhurivācarati madhvivācaratītyarthe vidhūyate madhūyata iti kyaṅi bhagavaccaritrasya vidhumadhunośca ācārassādhāraṇo dharmaḥ, sa copāttaḥ | ācāramātrārthakatayā kyackyaṅoḥ prakṛtyaiva lakṣaṇayā svasvārthasādṛśyapratipattiritinaye sādṛśyavācakābhāvādvācakaluptā | paryaṅkīyatītyādisamudāyasyaiva paryaṅkādisādṛśyaprayojakācaraṇakartṛśaktatvamitinaye’pi sādṛśyasādṛśyaviśiṣṭānyataramātravācakābhāvādvācakaluptā ||
yathāvā
rādhāyante bhāmāḥ kāmādhikarūpa tava vilokanataḥ |
vedhāyante puruṣāḥ puruṣottama vṛṣagirīśa tata eva ||
tata eva vilokanata evetyarthaḥ | ādau karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | tataḥ kartarīti viśeṣaḥ | atrāpi rādhevācaranti vedhā ivācarantītyarthe rādhāyante vedhāyante iti kyaṅi bhāmāpuruṣayorācārassādhāraṇo dharmaḥ| kāmādhikarūpeti sambuddhirbhāmānāṃ rādhāvadācaraṇaupayikī, puruṣottametyetattu puruṣāṇāṃ vedhovadācaraṇaupayikī | vedhāyanta ityatra ‘kartuḥ kyaṅ salopaśca’ iti kyaṅassalopo viśeṣaḥ | idaṃ tu ācārasya sādhāraṇadharmatāmabhyupayatāṃ prācāṃ mate udā haraṇadvayam | ye tu kyajādyartha ācāro na sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ dharmamātrarūpasyācārasya upamāprayojakatvābhāvāt ‘nārīyate samarasīmni kṛpāṇapāṇerālokya tasya lalitāni sapatnasenā’ ityādau vṛttyantaraniveditaiḥ kātaratvādibhirabhinnatayā’dhyavasitasyācārasyopamāniṣpādakatvāt | yadi ca kyajādyarthaṃ ācāramātramupamāniṣpādakassyāt tadā ‘triviṣṭapaṃ tatkhalu bhāratāyate’ ityādau sāmānyenācāropasthitāvapyupamālaṅkṛteraniṣpattiḥ tasyaiva ‘suparvabhiśśobhitamantarāśritaiḥ’ iti caraṇāntaranirmāṇe tasyā niṣpattiśca na syāt tasmāt kyajādyarthassādhāraṇo’pi nopamāṃ prayojayati upamāprayojakatāvacchedakarūpeṇa sādhāraṇadharmavācakaśūnyatvasyaiva dharmalopaśabdenābhidhānāt, anyathā ‘mukharūpamidaṃ vastu praphullamiva paṅkajam’ ityādau pūrṇopamāpatterityācāramātrasya sādhāraṇadharmatāṃ nābhyupagacchanti, teṣāṃ mate tu nedaṃ vācakamātraluptāyā udāharaṇaṃ bhavitumarhati dharmasyāpi luptatvāt | kintvanyadeva ||
yathā
tribhuvanalobhanavibhavaṃ vibhumahiśaile bhavantamābhāntam | animeṣamīkṣamāṇāssanimeṣā api na rāssurāyante || 50 || atra suranarayoranimeṣekṣaṇaṃ sādhāraṇo dharma ukta iti kartṛkyaṅi vācakamātrasya lopa iti bodhyam ||
karmakartṛṇamulorvācakaluptā yathā
garbhanidhāyaṃnidadhadgarbhe bhuvanānyananyaśaraṇāni |
jananīrakṣaṃrakṣasi ghananīradanīla nīrajākṣa tvam ||
atra garbhāniva nidadhadityarthe garbhanidhāyamiti karmaṇamuli garbhāṇāṃ bhuvanānāṃ ca nidhānamananyaśaraṇatvaṃ ca sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ ‘garbhe bhuvanāni yugavigamasamaye’ iti dvitīyapāde paṭhite’pi nidhānaṃ sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | jananīva rakṣasītyarthe jananīrakṣamiti kartṛṇamuli rakṣasīti bhagavajjananyossādhāraṇo dharmaḥ ‘upamāne karmaṇi ca’ iti ṇamuli ‘kaṣādiṣu yathāvidhyanuprayogaḥ’ iti ṇamulprakṛterdhātoranuprayogaḥ ||
ṇinau vācakaluptā yathā
madamadhuradvipagāmī kāmī kasyāṃcidambudhivibhūtau|
madakalahaṃsālāpī kopīhāpīnadośśriyaṃ dattām ||
kasyāṃcidambudhivibhūtau ambudhivibhūtitvenādhyavasitāyāṃ lakṣmyāmityarthaḥ | kāmī kāmuko bhagavān madakalahaṃsālāpī ‘mattahaṃsasvaro yuvā’ iti śrīrāmāyaṇokteḥ | āpīnāḥ atimātrapīvarāḥ doṣaḥ bhujāḥ yasya tathoktaḥ | atra dvipagāmī haṃsālāpītyubhayatrāpi dvipa iva gacchati haṃsa ivālapatītyarthe ‘kartaryupamāne’ iti vihitaṇinau vācakaluptā | gamanaṃ ālapanaṃ ca samānadharmaḥ | kecittu kokilālāpinītyādisthale ‘upapadamatiṅ’ iti samāsasya sadbhāvena samāsagaṇanayaivaitasyāpi kroḍīkārādvācakaluptāvibhāge ṇineḥ pṛthaggrahaṇaṃ nādriyante ||52||
kvipi vācakaluptā yathā
alibālati harinīlati ghanajālati santataṃ tamālati ca | nīlā tava rucimālā nīlācalamaulivihṛtilolātman || 53 ||
nīlācala iti veṅkaṭādrereva nāmāntaram
nalasya vānarendrasya yasmānnityamavasthitiḥ |
tasmānnīlācalaṃ nāmnā vadantyenaṃ maharṣayaḥ ||
iti vārāhīyaveṅkaṭācalamāhātmyokteḥ | atra alibālatītyādau alibāla ivācaratītyarthe kvipi nīlatvaṃ bhagavadrucimālāyāsteṣāṃ ca sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | ātmanniti pṛthakpadaṃ paramātmanniti bhagavatassambodhanam ||
taddhite vācakaluptā yathā
veṅkaṭapatipadapaṅkajakiṅkaratāyāṃ na jātu yasya ruciḥ | durviṣayeṣveva sadā varvarti naro’śuciḥ kharakuṭī saḥ || 54 ||
kharakuṭī kharanivāsakṣudratamasthānaviśeṣaḥ | atra kharakuṭhīva puruṣa ityasminnarthe ‘ive pratikṛtau’ iti vihitasya kanaḥ ‘lummanuṣye’ iti manuṣyārthe subvidhānāt kharakuṭītyatra vācakalopastaddhite | ‘lupi yuktavadvyaktivacane’ iti yuktavadbhāvena prakṛtiliṅgatā | aśucitvaṃ sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | ‘varvarti sa eṣa kharakuṭīmanujaḥ iti caturthacaraṇanirmāṇe tadanupādānena dharmavācakalopaścātra saṃbhavati | ‘lummanuṣye’ iti sūtre cañcetivat kharakuṭītyapyudāhṛtaṃ kāśikādau ||
dharmavācakaluptā tu varṇitā kvipsamāsayoḥ || 18 ||
yathā
hṛdaye tava mā’lāsīttadapāṅgālistu paramamālāsīt | tvaṃ pitarasi mātarasi bhrātarasi ca devapuṅgava natānām || 55 ||
tava hṛdaye vakṣasi mā lakṣmīḥ alāsīt arājīt tasyāḥ apāṅgāliḥ kaṭākṣarekhā tu paraṃ atiśayitaṃ yathātathā amālāsīt mālevācārīt mālātulyā’bhūdityarthaḥ | mālāśabdādācārārthe kvipi luṅi sici ‘yamaramanamātāṃ sakca’ iti iṭsakau || pitevācarasi pitarasi | evamanyadapi | atra ‘mātā pitā bhrātā’ ityādiśrutiranusaṃhitā | atra mālevācārīt pitevācarasītyādyarthe amālāsīt pitarasītyādau mālādiśabdāḥ kvau lupte dhātavaḥ | tatra mālādiśabdāḥ lakṣaṇayā mālādisādṛśyaṃ bodhayanti | luptopi smṛtaḥ kvibācāraṃ bodhayatīti pakṣe vācakadharmalopasspaṣṭa eva | mālādiśabdā eva lakṣaṇayā sādṛśyābhinnamācāraṃ bodhayantīti pakṣe’pi sādṛśyasyeva dharmasyāpi tanmātrabodhakābhāvāllopa eveti vadanti | devaḥ puṅgava iveti samāse vācakalopasādhāraṇadharmāprayogaśceti dharmavācakaluptā | iyaṃ ca karmādhikaraṇakyacoḥ kharakuṭītyatra taddhite’pi dharmāprayoge sambhavatītyavocāma ||
dharmopamānaluptā tu vākye vṛttau ca taddhite ||
vṛttau samāse ityarthaḥ ||
vākye dharmopamānaluptā yathā
yena samānaḥ kaścana mānavalāke kvā’pi na luloke | tena mayā’pi dayābdhe śrīnilaya prārthyase’pavargamaho || 56 ||
atra yena mayā samāno mānavaloke na luloke iti viśiṣyopamānānupādānāddharmānupādānācca vākye dharmopamānaluptā ||
samāse dharmopamānaluptā yathā
kvāpi sudarśanasadṛśo nādarśi na dṛśyate na ca draṣṭā | yena purā vanamālī māliśirastatsumālivadalāvīt || 57 ||
atra kvāpi sudarśanasadṛśo nādarśītyādinā upamānasya samāsagatasya niṣedhātsamāsagā dharmopamānaluptā | draṣṭeti karmaṇi luṭ | tat duśchedatayā prasiddhaṃ mālinaḥ tannāmno’surasya śiraḥ| sumālivat sumānāṃ kusumānāṃ ālivat paṅktivat sumālināmāsuraśirovat ityapi pratīyate | alāvīt acchinat | anayośca nopamānasvarūpameva pratiṣidhyate | kintūpamānadarśanamiti nānanvayālaṅkṛtibhramaḥ kāryaḥ | nāpi vakṣyamāṇacaturthapratīpaśaṅkāyuktā, tasya varṇyenānyasyopamāyā aniṣpattau camatkāritayā upamānadarśananiṣedhena camatkāriṇo’smādvailakṣaṇyāditi dik || taddhite dharmopamānaluptā yathā
mucukundasya guhāyāṃ yadiha mukundena melanaṃ yadapi | varalābho’syāmuṣmānmanye’ndhakavartakī yametaditi || 58 ||
andhakavartakīyamityetat kākatālīyaśabdatulyatayā ‘samāsācca tadviṣayāt’ iti sūtre kāśikāyāmudāhṛtam | vartako nāma kaścitpakṣiviśeṣaḥ ‘teṣāṃ viśeṣā hārīto madguḥ kāraṇḍavaḥ plavaḥ’ ityupakramya ‘vartako vartikādayaḥ’ iti khagaviśeṣavāciṣu nāmaliṅgānuśāsanāt | atra taddhite dharmopamānaluptā | tathā hi andhakavartakīyamityatra samāsāviṣaye andhakavartakaśabdau tatsamavetakriyāvartinau | tataśca andhakāvasthānamiva vartakāgamanamivetīvārthe ‘samāsācca tadviṣayāt’ iti jñāpakātsamāsaḥ |’supsupā’ iti vā | ubhayatropameyaṃ krameṇa mucukundasya guhāyāṃ kvacidavasthānaṃ tatraiva mukundasya gamanaṃ ca | tena mucukundasya mukundasya ca samāgamaḥ andhakavartakasamāgamasadṛśa iti paryavasyati | tataḥ punarandhakavartakamiva andhakavartakīyamiti dvitīyasminnivārthe ‘samāsācca tadviṣayāt’ iti sūtreṇa ‘ive pratikṛtau’ ityadhikārasthena chapratyayaḥ | tataścākasmādāgato vartako yathā nirudyamenāndhakenāsāditaḥ tathā yadṛcchayāgato mukundo niśceṣṭatayā nidrāṇena mucukundenāsādita iti niṣpadyate | tathācātra pratyayārthopamāyāṃ andhakakṛtavartakopādānarūpasyopamānasya sādhāraṇadharmasya cānupādānāddharmopamānalopaḥ | dharmalopastvaicchikaḥ |
atra
yanmelanaṃ guhāyāṃ mukundamucukundayoryadasyāsmāt |
varalābho yādṛcchikamāsāndhakavartakīyamevedam ||
iti padyasya prakārantareṇa nirmāṇe tu yādṛcchikatvarūpadharmopādānātpratyayārthopamāyāṃ kevalopamānalopasyāpi taddhite idamevodāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati | andhakāvasthānamiva vartakāgamanamiveti samāsārthopamāyāṃ tu mucukundāvasthānamukundāgamanamelanopamānabhūtasyāndhakāvasthānavartakāgamanasamāgamasyānupādānādupamānalopaḥ | tathā samāsavaśādvācakalopaḥ dharmānupādānāddharmalopaśceti dharmavācakopamānalopasyāpīdamevodāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyatītyavahitacittairantevāsibhiranusandheyam ||
kvacilluptā vācakopameyābhyāmapi saṃmatā ||19
yathā
śrīvāsīyati sarvopyāvāsaṃ prāpya tasya vṛṣaśaile | atasīsumasamatadrucikṛtasīmātītakālimā kāmam || 59 ||
sarvo’pi janaḥ vṛṣaśaile tasya śrīnivāsasya āvāsaṃ divyālayaṃ prāpya praviśya kāmaṃ yathepsitaṃ atasīsumasamayā tasya śrīnivāsasya rucyā prabhayā kṛtassaṃpāditaḥ sīmātītaḥ anavadhikaḥ kālimā śyāmatā yasya tathābhūtassan śrīvāsīyati ātmānaṃ śrīnivāsamivācarati nīlavarṇatayā tadvadācaratītyarthaḥ | atra ātmānaṃ śrīnivāsamivācaratīti sāmarthyādgamyamānatayā svātmana upameyasya vācakasya ca anupādānādvācakopameyalopaḥ | ihātmānamityapi suvacatvādupameyalopa aicchikaḥ | na cātra sarva ityupameyopādānamastīti kathamupameyalopa iti vācyam | karmavibhaktyabhāvena sarva ityasya śrīvāsīyatītyatropameyasamarpakatvābhāvāt | na caitadanusāreṇa śrīvāsīyatītyatra śrīvāsa ivācaratīti samānavibhaktikatvakalpanaṃ ghaṭate | kartṛkyaco’nanuśāsanāditi dik ||
kvacitsamāsa eva syādvācakāvarṇyalopinī ||
avarṇyamupamānam | vācakopamānaluptā tu samāsa eva syādityarthaḥ ||
yathā
rahasi vrajasundaryāssamāgamo yaśca yastadupabhogaḥ| lolambāmburuhīyaṃ tadidaṃ śaureryadṛcchayopanatam || 60 ||
lolambāmburuhīyamityatra kākatālīyamityatreva vigrahaḥ | tathāhi lolambāmburuhaśabdau vṛttiviṣaye tadgatakriyārtinau | tena lolambāgamanamiva amburuhavikasanamiva lolambāmburuhamiti pūrvoktadiśā samāsaḥ | ubhayatropameyaṃ krameṇa śaureryatrakvacinniśśalākapradeśe gamanaṃ tatraiva vrajasundaryā avasthānaṃ ca | tena śaurivrajasundarīsamāgamo lolambāmburuhasamāgamasadṛśa iti samāsārthopamāyāḥ paryavasānāttatra lolambāgamanasyāmburuhavikasanasya ca upamānasyānupādānātsamāse vācakalopācca vācakopamānalopaḥ | yadṛcchayopanatamiti dharmopādānānna tallopo’pi || yā dharmavācakāvarṇyaluptā sā syātsamāsagā ||20
dharmavācakopamānaluptā tu samāsagā bhavati | avarṇyamupamānam ||
yathā
ghanavidyullāvaṇyaṃ vinatasurāsuranarāvaliśaraṇyam | vilasatu kimapi vareṇyaṃ vṛṣagiripatihṛdayadhāma tāruṇyam || 61 ||
tāruṇyamiti pṛthakpadam | tāruṇyatvenādhyavasitā śrīrityarthaḥ| atra vidyuto lāvaṇyamiva dedīpyamānaṃ lāvaṇyaṃ yasyetyasminnarthe’nuśiṣṭo vidyullāvaṇyamityatra dharmopamānavācakānāṃ lopaḥ | sarvopyayaṃ lopaḥ ‘saptamyupamāna’ iti śāstrakṛtaḥ | ayaṃ ca trilopaḥ yanmelanaṃ guhāyāmityatra samāsārthopamāyāmapi bhavatītyavocāma prāk | tataśca trilope dharmalopaḥ kvacidaicchiko’pi bhavatīti draṣṭavyam | nanvatra vidyuto lāvaṇyamiva dedīpyamānaṃ lāvaṇyaṃ yasyeti vigrahe sati samāse upameyabhūtalāvaṇyapadasyaiva lopaḥ kiṃ na syāt tathā ca vācakopameyaluptaiveyaṃ sampadyatāṃ na tu dharmavācakopamānalupteti cet upameyalope’pi na trilopavattvahānirityeke ||
vastutastu ‘anekamanyapadārthe’ iti sūtrastha ‘saptamyupamānapūrvapadasyottarapadalopaśca’ iti bhāṣyavārtikānurodhenopamānapadasyaivātra lopo yuktaḥ | tathāhi vārtikasyāyamarthaḥ saptamyupamānapūrvapadasyetyatra saptamī kaṇṭhe stha ityādyekadeśabhūtā kaṇṭhe ityādikā saptamī upamānaṃ uṣṭramukhādiśabdaghaṭakaṃ uṣṭretyādikaṃ upamānaṃ vā pūrvapadaṃ yasya tat saptamyupamānapūrvapadamiti bahuvrīhiḥ | tasya padāntareṇa bahutrīhiḥ, uttarapadasya pūrvasamāsacaramāvayavabhūtasya stha ityasya mukhamityasya ca lopaśca vācya iti | tathāca tatra bhāṣyam | ‘saptamīpūrvasyopamānapūrvasya ca bahuvrīhirvaktavyaḥ | uttarapadasya ca lopo vaktavyaḥ| kaṇṭhesthaḥ kālo yasya kaṇṭhekālaḥ uṣṭramukhamiva mukhamasya uṣṭramukhaḥ’ iti | atra vārtike upamānaśabdena mukhyopamānabhūtasya uṣṭramukhasyāśraya uṣṭro lakṣyate | anyathā uṣṭramukhamityasyopamānapūrvatvaṃ na syāt | ataeva ‘uṣṭramukhamiva mukhamasya uṣṭramukhaḥ’ iti prakṛtavārtikodāharaṇabhāṣyapratīkamādāya ‘avayavadharmeṇa samudāyasya vyapadeśāduṣṭrasyopamānatetyupamānapūrva uṣṭramukhaśabdaḥ’ iti kaiyaṭe vyavriyata | evaṃcātra yathā kaṇṭhesthaḥ kālo yasya sa iti bahuvrīhau kaṇṭhaśabdottaravartisthaśabdasya lopaḥ yathā vā uṣṭramukhamiva mukhamityatra ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣacaramāvayavabhūtamukhaśabdalopaśca, tathā ghanavidyullāvaṇyamiva lāvaṇyaṃ yasyeti bahuvrīhau vidyucchabdottaravartimukhyopamānavācakalāvaṇyapadasyaiva lopaḥ na tūpameyabhūtalāvaṇyapadasyeti dharmavācakopamānaluptāyā evedamudāharaṇaṃ bodhyam | idaṃ sarvamapi haṃsasandeśarasāsvādinyāmādimapadye kalpākārāmityasya vyākhyānāvasare nirūpitamasmābhiḥ ||
prāñcastu kecit ayaśśūlasadṛśena krūreṇācāreṇa svārthamanvicchati khale prayujyamāne āyaśśūlikaḥ khala ityatra krūrācārarūpasyopameyasya taikṣṇyāderdharmasya ivādervācakasya cāprayogāddharmopameyavācakaluptā’pi trilope sambhavatītyāhuḥ |tanna caturaśram ayaśśūlapadena krūrācāraṃ nigīryādhyavasānenātiśayoktyā upameyasyopādānāttatra nopameyalopa iti vadanti | āyaśśūlika ityatra ‘pārśvenānvicchati’ ityadhikāre ‘ayaśśūladaṇḍājinābhyāṃ ṭhakṭhañau’ ityanvīcchatītyarthe ṭhak | tīkṣṇa upayo’yaśśūlaṃ te nānvicchatītyarthaḥ | evamayaṃ pūrṇāluptāvibhāgo vākyasamāsapratyayaviśeṣagocaratayā śabdaśāstravyutpattivaidagdhyapradarśanamātraprayojano nātimātramalaṅkāraśāstre’rhati vyutpādanīyatāmityāhuḥ ||
evamaṣṭavidhā luptāḥ prācāṃ rītyā nirūpitāḥ||20 ||
evaṃ mālopamāraśanopamāsāvayavaniravayavopamādayo’neke bhedāssambhavanto’pyuktabheda evāntargatatvānna lakṣaṇīyāḥ pṛthak | athāpi kāṃścillakṣayāmaḥ
ekasyaivopameyasya yadyanekopamānatā |
tadā mālopamāmetāmāhuḥ kecidvicakṣaṇāḥ || 21 ||
mālāyāṃ yathā ekena puṣpeṇāparasya puṣpasya grathanaṃ tathā upamānāṃ parasparasambandhānmālopametyeṣā vyavahriyate | tathā ca daṇḍī
pūṣṇyātapa ivāhnīva pūṣā vyomnīva vāsaraḥ |
vikramastvayyadhālakṣmīmiti mālopamā matā || iti ||
evaṃvidhavaicitrya evaiṣā | kevalamupamānabāhulye tu vakṣyamāṇā bahūpamaiva ||
yathā
vyālīśati kālīśati kāśati rākāśaśiprakāśati ca | kanakanageśanakhābhā karpūrati gāṅgapūrati ca || 62 ||
vyālīśaśśeṣaḥ | kālīśaśśambhuḥ | kanakanageśaḥ veṅkaṭādrīśaḥ| atraikasyā evopameyabhūtāyāśśrīnivāsanakhaprabhāyāḥ mālārūpeṇa vyālīśādyanekopamānopādānānmālopameyam | atra pūrvaṃ vācakaluptodāharaṇatayoktaḥ ‘alibālati harinīlati’ ityādiśloko’pyudāharaṇaṃ, tatra nīletidharmopādānaṃ viśeṣaḥ | sādṛśyanimittadharmopādāne ca dharmasyaikye bhede ceyaṃ mālopamā bhavati ||
tatra dharmaikye yathā
nikaṣa iva kanakarekhā vidrumalatikeva vilasati samudre| añjanagiripatihṛdaye kiñjalkaśikheva kuvalaye kamalā || 63 ||
rājyaśriyeva rājā prājyatamapratibhayeva kavirājaḥ | kusumasamṛddhyeva madhurmādhavabhaktyā virājate manujaḥ || 64 ||
prātariva padmalakṣmīśśītajyotiḥkaleva sitapakṣe | sāyamiva kuvalayaśrīḥ prāyaśśrīśāśrite vibhāti śrīḥ || 65 ||
madhumāsīva himānī vidhubhāsīvāstameti tāparddhiḥ| śaradīva ca jaladābhā varada tavālokane duritavitatiḥ || 66 ||
dhānyeṣu pulākā iva patageṣu pataṅgikā iva śrīman | maśakā iva kīṭeṣu ca vaśikā nṛṣu te tvayīśa ye vimukhāḥ || 67||
atrodāharaṇapañcake’pi lakṣmīprabhṛtīnāmekaikopameyānāṃ kanakarekhādyanekopamānopādānānmālopamā | nikaṣa ivetyādinā dharmasya bimbapratibimbabhāvena vilasatītyādinā anugāmitayā ca nirdeśaḥ pūrvodāharaṇāpekṣayā viśeṣaḥ | vaśikā ityasya tucchā ityarthaḥ | ‘vaśikaṃ tucchariktake’ ityamaraḥ | atrāpyādyodāharaṇatritaye ślāghyānyupameyāni | antimodāharaṇadvitaye garhye upameye iti vailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyam | iyameva stabakopamā mālārūpā | yathoktaṃ daṇḍinā
anekasyārthayugmasya sādṛśyaṃ stabakopamā |
śritosmi caraṇe viṣṇorbhṛṅgastāmarase yathā ||
iti | naca vidhubhāsīva pataṅgikā ivetyādau liṅgabheda upamādoṣa iti śaṅkyam | sahṛdayahṛdayānudvegadāyitayā tasyādoṣatvāt | yathā’’ha daṇḍī
na liṅgavacane bhinne na hīnādhikate api |
upamādūṣaṇāyālaṃ yatrodvego na dhīmatām ||
strīva gacchati ṣaṇḍo’yaṃ vaktyeṣā strī pumāniva |
prāṇā iva priyo’yaṃ me vidyā dhanamivārjitā||
bhavāniva mahārāja devarājo virājate |
alamaṃśumataḥ kakṣyāmāroḍhuṃ tejasā nṛpaḥ ||
ityevamādi saubhāgyaṃ na jahātyeva jātucit |
asti ca kvacidudvegaḥ prayoge vāgvidāṃ yathā ||
haṃsīva dhavalaścandrassarāṃsīvāmalaṃ nabhaḥ |
bhartṛbhakto naraśśveva khadyoto bhāti bhānuvat ||
īdṛśaṃ varjyate sadbhiḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra cintyatām | iti |
dharmabhede yathā
jaladhivadatigambhīrā jalaruhadalavannitāntasukumārāḥ | kalpāgamavadudārāḥ kalpantāṃ naśśubhāya haridārāḥ || 68 ||
pūrvodāharaṇeṣu sarvatra sādhāraṇadharma eka eva | iha tu bhinnabhinnaḥ | pūrvatra samāsagā vākyagā ca pūrṇopamā | iha tu taddhitagā seti bhedaḥ | mālārūpatvaṃ tu tulyameva ||
rudraṭastu dharmabhede satyeva mālopamāṃ manyate | tathā ca kāvyālaṃkāre
mālopameti seyaṃ yatraikaṃ vastvanekasāmānyam |
upamīyetānekairupamānairekasāmānyaiḥ ||
iti | udājahāra ca
śyāmā lateva tanvī candrakalevātinirmalā sā me |
haṃsīva kalālāpā caitanyaṃ harati nidreva || iti |
mate tvasmin ‘jaladhivat’ ityādikameva mālopamodāharaṇaṃ
bodhyam ||
bharatastu
ekasyaikena sā kāryā’nekenāpyathavā punaḥ |
anekasya tathaikena bahūnāṃ bahubhissaha ||
ityupamāyāścāturvidhyaṃ prādarśayat | sā upametyarthaḥ | tatra ekasyaikenopamārūpaḥ prathamaprakāraḥ ‘gaganaṃ yathā phaṇīśvaranagapatihṛdayam’ ityādiprāgudāhṛtapadyeṣu draṣṭavyaḥ ||
ekasya bahubhirupamārūpo dvitīyaprakārastu tenetthaṃ pradarśitaḥ
tathāhi
śyenabarhiṇabhāsānāṃ tulyākṣa iti yā bhavet |
ekasya bahubhissāmyādupamā sā prakīrtitā ||
iti | daṇḍī ca imameva prakāraṃ bahūpameti vyavāhārṣīt | tathāca kāvyādarśe
candanodakacandrāṃśucandrakāntādiśītalaḥ |
sparśastavetyatiśayaṃ bodhayantī bahūpamā ||
iti | tadanurodhenedamudāharaṇam | yathā
kuvalayajaladharayamunātamālasabrahmacāriruciraruciḥ| sarasijakisalayavidrumasahacaracaraṇo mamācyutaśśaraṇam || 69 ||
atra sabrahmacārisahacaraśabdau upamānārthakau| tathāca daṇḍī
ivavadvāyathāśabdāssamānanibhasannibhāḥ |
tulyasaṃkāśanīkāśaprakāśapratirūpakāḥ ||
pratipakṣapratidvandvipratyanīkavirodhinaḥ |
sadṛksadṛśasaṃvādisajātīyānuvādinaḥ ||
pratibimbapraticchandasarūpasamasammitāḥ |
salakṣaṇasadṛkṣābhasapakṣopamitopamāḥ ||
kalpadeśīyadeśyādi prakhyapratinidhī api |
savarṇatulitau śabdau ye vā’(cā)nyūnārthavācakāḥ ||
samāsaśca bahuvrīhiśśaśāṅkavadanādiṣu |
spardhate jayati dveṣṭi druhyati pratigarjati ||
ākrośatyavajānāti kadarthayati nindati |
viḍambayati saṃrundhe hasatīrṣyatyasūyati ||
tasya pu(mu)ṣṇāti saubhāgyaṃ tasya kāntiṃ vilumpati ||
tena sārdhaṃ vigṛhṇāti tulāṃ tenādhirohati ||
tatpadavyāṃ padaṃ dhatte tasya kakṣyāṃ vigāhate |
bandhuścorassuhṛdvādisatīrthyasahacāriṇaḥ ||
tamanvetyanubadhnāti tacchīlaṃ tanniṣedhati |
tasya vā’nukarotīti śabdāssādharmyabodhakāḥ ||
upamāyāmime proktāḥ kavīnāṃ buddhisaukhyadāḥ || iti ||
atra tasya vā’nukarotītyatra itiśabdaḥ prakāravācī | evaṃprakārāśśabdāssādharmyabodhakā ityarthaḥ | tena sagotrasabrahmacāridāyādaprabhṛtīnāmapyupamānabodhakatvaṃ sidhyati | atra ivādayo nipātāḥ | vaditi ivārthe tulyārthe ca vihito vatipratyayaḥ | kalpadeśyadeśīyāstaddhitapratyayāḥ | yadyapyete īṣadasamāptyarthe vihitāḥ tathā’pi tulyārthā eva | īṣadasamāpterapi sādṛśye paryavasānāt | yathoktaṃ kaiyaṭena ‘guḍa iveṣadasamāptā drākṣā guḍakalpā | yadyapyatropamānaṃ nopāttaṃ, tathā’pi sāmarthyādupamānopameyabhāvaḥ’ iti | tatra pratyayānāmupamānottarameva prayogaḥ tasyaiva tatprakṛtitvāt | nibhādīnāṃ padānāṃ tu ‘vācyaliṅgāssamastulyaḥ’ ityārabhya ‘syuruttarapade tvamī | nibhasaṅkāśanīkāśapratīkāśopamādayaḥ’ iti nityasamāsaviṣayatānuśāsanāt candreṇa samaṃ candranibhamityasvapadena vigrahaḥ pradarśanīyaḥ | taduktaṃ kṣīrasvāminā ‘uttarapade ityukteścandreṇa nibhamiti na bhavati’ iti | vairisuhṛdvācakāssādṛśyārthāḥ |śaśāṅkavadanetyādau upamānapūrvapado bahuvrīhiḥ | pratibimbaḥ spardhata ityādayassādṛśyākṣepakā ityalam | prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ | anekasyaikopamārūpastṛtīyaprakāraśca bharatenaivaṃ pradarśitaḥ
candravatsamprakāśante jyotirbhājo dvijottamāḥ |
ekena sā tvanekeṣāmupamā saṃprakīrtitā || iti ||
tatredamudāharaṇam
amṛtamayalokabāndhavadṛṣṭiprathitā jagatsthitikṛtāsthāḥ | hṛdi sacchriyaṃ dadhānā bhagavattulyā vibhānti bhāgavatāḥ || 70 ||
amṛtamayalokaḥ aprākṛtalokaḥ paramaṃ padamityarthaḥ | tasya bāndhavī prāpayitrīti yāvat | dṛṣṭiḥ jñānaṃ darśanaṃ vā yeṣāṃ te tathāvidhā iti prathitāḥ niśśreyasapradānanidānajñānapauṣkalyavattayā prasiddhāḥ tathāvidhadarśanā iti prasiddhā iti vā’rthaḥ | pakṣe amṛtamayaścandramāḥ lokabāndhavo bhānuḥ tāveva dṛṣṭī akṣiṇī yasya tathokta iti prathita iti vacanavipariṇāmena yojyam | jagataḥ sthitiḥ varṇāśramādidharmamaryādā ‘sthitiḥ striyāmavasthāne maryādāyāṃ ca sīmani’ iti medinī | tasyāṃ kṛtāsthāḥ ‘aviplavāya dharmāṇāṃ maryādāsthāpanāya ca’ ityārabhya ‘manīṣī vaidikācāraṃ manasā’pi na laṅghayet’ ityuktarītyā svānuṣṭhānādinā jaganmaryādāpālayitāra ityarthaḥ | pakṣe jagatāṃ sthitau pālane kṛtāsthaḥ ||
nahi pālanasāmarthyamṛte sarveśvaraṃ harim |
sthitau sthitaṃ mahāprājña bhavatyanyasya kasyacit ||
sṛjatyeṣa jagatsraṣṭā sthitau pāti sanātanaḥ ||
ityukteḥ | hṛdi manasi satāṃ śrīḥ sacchrīḥ tāṃ trayīmityarthaḥ | ‘ṛcassāmāni yajūm̐ṣi | sā hi śrīramṛtā satām’ itiśruteḥ | dadhānā hṛdgatasakalavedatadarthā ityarthaḥ | pakṣe satīṃ śriyaṃ lakṣmīṃ dadhāna iti | bhāgavatāḥ bhagavattulyāḥ vibhānti | atrānekeṣāṃ bhāgavatānāṃ bhagavāneka evopamānaṃ samānadharmaśśleṣamūlakaḥ | bhagavattulyā ityatropamānavācipadagatavacanasya vṛttiluptatayā’nudvejakatvānna bhinnavacanatālakṣaṇadoṣa iti dhyeyam | iyaṃ sāmānyadharmasyaikya eva bhavati upamānasyaikatvāditi dhyeyam ||
bahūnāṃ bahubhirupamārūpasturīyaḥ prakārastu pūrvamudāhṛte ‘dhānyeṣu pulākā iva’ ityādau draṣṭavyaḥ ||
pūrvapūrvopameyasya bhavedyadyuttarottaram |
upamānatvameṣoktā kavibhī raśanopamā || 22 ||
prathamopamāyāṃ yadupameyaṃ tacceduttaropamāyāmupamānaṃ bhavati sā raśanopametyarthaḥ || yathā
hṛdayamiva hāri vadanaṃ vadanamivānandadāyi tava vacanam | vacanamiva lalitavarṇaṃ nayanaṃ nayanaśrutīndraśikharimaṇe || 71 ||
atra pūrvapūrvaṃ pratyupameyatvenopāttasya vadanāderuttarottaraṃ pratyupamānatvenopādānamuttarottarasya śṛṅkhalāyāmiva pūrvapūrvasambandhitayeti raśanopamā | hṛdayavadanayoḥ hārīti hāravattvamanoharatvarūpārthadvayakroḍīkāreṇa samānadharmaḥ | evaṃ vacananayanayorapi lalitavarṇamiti madhurākṣaratvaramaṇīyaviśadatādivarṇavattvarūpārthadvayābhedā dhyavasāyena | vadanavacanayostu ānandadāyītyanugāmitayeti vailakṣaṇyamanusandheyam || iyaṃ dharmabhede raśanopamā ||
dharmaikye yathā
aiśvaryamiva sthairyaṃ sthairyamivaudāryamahigirisuradroḥ | audāryamivāvāryaṃ śauryaṃ śauryamiva hanta gāmbhīryam || 72 ||
atrāvāryamityeka eva dharmaḥ | evaṃ dharmalope’pyudāhāryam ||
iyamupamā’pi rūpakavatkevalaniravayavā mālārūpaniravayavā samastavastuviṣayasāvayavā ekadeśavivartisāvayavā kevalaśliṣṭaparamparitā mālārūpaśliṣṭaparamparitā kevalaśuddhaparamparitā mālārūpaśuddhaparamparitā cetyaṣṭadhā | tatropamāyāṃ kevalatvaṃ mālānantargatatvaṃ niravayavatvaṃ copamāntaranirapekṣatvam | iyaṃ ca bahudhā pūrvamevodāhṛtā | mālārūpaniravayavā’pi ‘alibālati harinīlati, vyālīśati kālīśati’ ityādāvudāhṛtaiva ||
samastavastuviṣayā sāvayavā yathā
racitodayā kalindādrucirodārā gireriva mukundāt | saridiva suṣamālaharirharate paritāpamiva ghanaṃ pāpam || 73 ||
yathā vā
yamunāhradamiva hṛdayaṃ kamalamivāvaimi kaustubhamamuṣmin | ḍiṇḍīrā iva hārāḥ kuṇḍaliśikharīndramaṇḍana tavaite || 74 ||
samastāni vastūni avayavī avayavāśca viṣayā yasyāssā samastavastuviṣayetyarthaḥ | atra ślokadvaye’pi avayavino’vayavānāṃ copamānānāṃ śabdaireva sāmastyenopādānātsamastavastuviṣayatā’syāḥ, avayavopamānaniṣpādyamānatvācca sāvayavatā ca | atrādye padye dharmasyopādānaṃ dvitīye tu tanneti viśeṣaḥ | iyamanekevaśabdā vākyārthopameti daṇḍī | yathoktaṃ kāvyādarśe tena
vākyārthenaiva vākyārthaḥ ko’pi yadyupamīyate |
ekānekevaśabdatvātsā vākyārthopamā dvidhā ||
tvadānanamadhīrākṣamāvirdaśanadīdhiti |
bhramadbhṛṅgamivālakṣyakesaraṃ bhāti paṅkajam ||
nalinyā iva tanvaṅgyāstasyāḥ padmamivānanam |
mayā madhuvrateneva pāyaṃpāyamaramyata ||
iti | ekevaśabdavākyārthopamā tvagre pradarśayiṣyate ||
ekadeśavivartinī sāvayavā yathā
nanu bhavadāhitagarbhā prakṛtiḥ prāsūta nātha haṃsīva | kośamiva brahmāṇḍaṃ śrīśa tato’jani vidhātṛkādambaḥ || 75 ||
atra
mama yonirmahadbrahma tasmin garbhaṃ dadhāmyaham |
sambhavassarvabhūtānāṃ tato bhavati bhārata ||
sarvayoniṣu kaunteya mūrtayassambhavanti yāḥ|
tāsāṃ brahma mahadyonirahaṃ bījapradaḥ pitā |
iti gītāślokārtho’nusaṃhitaḥ | ‘kṛtsnasya jagato yonibhūtaṃ mama mahadbrahma yat yā prakṛtiḥ tasmin garbhaṃ dadhāmi ‘itastvanyāṃ prakṛtiṃ viddhi me parām | jīvabhūtām’ iti nirdiṣṭācetanapuñjarūpā yā parā prakṛtiḥ seha sakalaprāṇibījatayā garbhaśabdenocyate | tasminnacetane yonibhūte mahati brahmaṇi cetanapuñjarūpaṃ garbhaṃ dadhāmi acetanaprakṛtyā bhogakṣetrabhūtayā bhoktṛvargapuñjabhūtāṃ cetanaprakṛtiṃ saṃyojayāmītyarthaḥ | tataḥ tasmātprakṛtidvayasaṃyogānmatsaṅkalpakṛtātsarvabhūtānāṃ brahmādistambaparyantānāṃ saṃbhavo bhavati | sarvāsu yoniṣu devagandharvādiyoniṣu tattanmūrtayo yāssambhavanti tāsāṃ brahma mahadyoniḥ kāraṇaṃ mayā saṃyojitacetanavargā mahadādiviśeṣāntāvasthā prakṛtiḥ kāraṇamityarthaḥ | ahaṃ bījapradaḥ pitā tatratatra tattatkarmānuguṇyena cetanavargasya saṃyojakaścāhamityarthaḥ” iti bhāṣyam | kośamiva aṇḍamiva ‘peśī kośo dvihīne’ṇḍam’ ityamaraḥ | atrottarārdhe vidhātṛkādamba ityatra upamitasamāsa eva, viśeṣaṇasamāsavedyasya tādātmyasya prakṛte’nupayogāt | upamānaprāyapāṭhācca | ‘śrīśa tato’jani marāla iva dhātā’ iti turīyapādapāṭhe tu spaṣṭaivopamā | atra bhagavataḥ kalahaṃsopamā śabdenānabhihitā’pyaṅgopamābhirākṣiptā pratīyata ityekadeśavivartinīyamupamā | avayavāvayavinorekadeśe viśeṣeṇa vācyatayā vartata ityekadeśavivartinīti dhyeyam | atra vidhātṛkādamba iti jīvasamaṣṭibhūtacaturmukhajananavarṇanādanyeṣāmapi jananamarthasiddhamityudāhṛtavacane ‘sambhavassarvabhūtānāṃ’ iti sarvabhūtotpattikathanenedaṃ na virudhyate | evaṃ sati
apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamapāsṛjat |
tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham ||
tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ |
iti manuvacanamapyanuguṇaṃ bhaviṣyatīti draṣṭavyam ||
kevalaśliṣṭaparaṃparitā yathā
ādityābhyudayasya pratyūṣa iva prakāśabhūmānam | vidadhāno ghanadhāmā śrīmānābhāti śeṣagiridhāmā || 76 ||
ādityānāṃ devānāṃ ādityasya bhānośca abhyudayaḥ aiśvaryaṃ udayaśca | atra śleṣopasthāpitena devābhyudayena suryodayasyopamā bhagavataḥ pratyūṣopamopāya iti kevalaśliṣṭaparaṃparitā | anyonyopāyatārūpasyaiva paraṃparitatvasyeha paribhāṣaṇāt mālā rūpatāvirahācca ||
kevalā mālārūpā śliṣṭaparaṃparitā yathā
sattvasya himālaya iva rajasastvaṃ prāvṛṣeṇyajalada iva | tamaso mahasāṃ nidhiriva kamalekṣaṇa bhāsi kanakaśikharīndo || 77 ||
sattvasya sattvaguṇasya mṛgādijantujātasya ca | jātyabhiprāyakamekavacanam | himālaya iva yathā himādrissarvasattvaṣoṣaṇadhurīṇaḥ tathā’yaṃ bhagavān svāśritānāṃ sattvapoṣaṇadhurīṇa iti bhāvaḥ | ‘mahān prabhurvai puruṣassattvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ’ iti hi śrūyate | rajasaḥ rajoguṇasya pāṃsośca prāvṛṣeṇyajalada iva nirvāpaka iti bhāvaḥ | tamasaḥ tamoguṇasya timirasya ca mahasāṃ nidhiriva niśśeṣanivartaka iti bhāvaḥ | atra śleṣotthāpitaiḥ jantupāṃsutimiraissattvarajastamoguṇānāmupamāḥ himācalaprāvṛṣeṇyajaladabhānubhirbhagavata upamānāmupāyāḥ | nanvatra jantupāṃsutimirāṇāmiva sattvarajastamasāmityetādṛśī upamā kathamiva suśakā pratyetum| upamānopameyaśabdānāṃ pṛthagadarśanāditi cet śleṣasthale hyekapadopāttatvena rūpeṇābhedādhyavasānasyeva tenaiva sādharmyeṇa sādṛśyādhyavasānasyāpi suvacatvāt | tasyaiva prakṛte prayojyāyā upamāyā upāyatvāditi vadanti ||
yathā vā
śākhikulāmodānāṃ mādhava iva mādhavāhidharadhāman| sukavirivāstokānāṃ ślokānāṃ ghana ivāsi bhuvanānām || 78 ||
ahidharadhāman he mādhava śrīnivāsa mādhava iti śrīnivāsasya nāmāntaraṃ ‘mādhavaṃ veṅkaṭagirau’ iti bhagavacchāstrokteḥ | śākhā eṣāṃ santīti śākhinaḥ taravaḥ vedabhedādhyetāro dvijāśca teṣāṃ kulasya āmodāḥ parimalāḥ ānandāśca teṣāṃ ‘śākhā pakṣāntare bāhau vedabhāgadrubhāgayoḥ’ iti medinī | mādhava iva vasanta iva pravartaka iti bhāvaḥ | astokānāṃ ślokānāṃ padyānāṃ yaśasāṃ ca sukaviriva nidānabhūta iti bhāvaḥ | ‘tasya nāma mahadyaśaḥ’ iti śruteḥ | ‘padye yaśasi ca ślokaḥ ityamaraḥ | bhuvanānāṃ jalānāṃ jagatāṃ ca ghana iva ādhārabhūta iti bhāvaḥ | atra śleṣonmīlitaiḥ tarukulaparimalapadyajalaiḥ vaidikapariṣadānandayaśolokānāmupamāḥ vasantasukavighanāghanairbhagavadupamānāmupāyāḥ ||
yathā vā
ullāsayasi nikāmaṃ śaradāgama iva śucidvijān śrīman | kalyāṇānāṃ mahatāmādhārastvaṃ sumeruriva bhūman || 79 ||
he bhūman bhagavan ‘yatra nānyatpaśyati nānyacchṛṇoti nānyadvijānāti sa bhūmā’ iti śruteḥ | śucidvijān haṃsān | pakṣe 
yo’rthe śucirhi sa śucirna mṛdvāriśuciśśuciḥ |
kṣetrajñasyeśvarajñānādviśuddhiḥ paramā matā ||
ityuktaprakārasarvavidhaśuddhimato viprān kalyāṇānāṃ suvarṇānāṃ
maṅgalānāṃ ca ‘kalyāṇaṃ hemni maṅgale’ iti hemacandraḥ | atra śleṣopasthāpitābhyāṃ haṃsasuvarṇābhyāṃ śuddhavipramaṅgalayorupame śaradāgamasumerubhyāṃ bhagavata upamayorupāyaḥ | pūrvodāharaṇayordharmonuktaḥ iha tūkta iti bhidā | yadyapi ‘sattvasya’ iti prāthamikodāharaṇe bhāsītyanena sādhāraṇadharma upātta iva bhāti | tathā’pi tatrāvaśyaṃ vivakṣitānāṃ poṣaṇanirvāpaṇaniśśeṣanivartanānāmeva samānadharmatāyāssvārasikatayā kevalabhānasyātathātvenācamatkāritayā ca sa na vivakṣita iti dhyeyam | kiṃ ca pūrvodāharaṇayorbahūnāṃ mālārūpatā antimodāharaṇe dvayoriti bhedaḥ| ādyayorapi parasparaṃ bhedaḥ anupadameva sphuṭībhaviṣyati ||
kevalaṃ śuddhaparaṃparitā yathā
ahipatigiripatihṛdaye prahasitalasitāsitāmbujāta iva alisaṃhatiriva vilasati tulasīdalasambhṛtā mālā || 80 ||
prahasiteti bhāve ktaḥ | prahasitena vikāsena lasite asitāmbuje indīvara ityarthaḥ | tulasīdalamālāyā alisaṃhatyā upamā bhagavadvakṣasthalasyendīvaropamāyāssādhiketyupamādvayamapi saṃjātaparaṃparam ||
kevalā mālārūpā śuddhaparaṃparitā yathā
puṃgavasi gavantīnāṃ purā kareṇavasi kariṇayantīnām | nandavrajasudatīnāṃ candanasi mukunda vallayantīnām || 81 || purā puṃgavasi puṃgava ivācarasi ‘puri luṅcāsme’ iti purāśabdayogādbhūte laṭ | gāva ivācarantyaḥ gavantyaḥ tāsām | kareṇurivācarasi kareṇavasi kariṇya ivācarantyaḥ kariṇayantyaḥ tāsām | vallya ivācarantīti vallayantyaḥ tāsām | sarvatrācārārthe kvip | atra gopikānāṃ gavādibhirupamā bhagavataḥ puṃgavādyupamāyā upāya ityupamā etāssaṃjātaparamparā mālārūpāḥ kevalaṃ śuddhāḥ ||
yathā vā
nayagahanasya pathīnati paravidyājalanidhermathīnati ca | kṛtamatidiviṣadvitateḥ kṛṣṇa ṛbhukṣīṇati tvayi praṇataḥ || 82 ||
he kṛṣṇa tvayi praṇato janaḥ nayaḥ śāstraṃ tat gahanamiva duravagāhaṃ vanamiva nayagahanamityupamitasamāsaḥ, pathīnatītyupamānālliṅgāt | na tu mayūravyaṃsakādiḥ | tasyehānupayogāt | tasya pathīnati panthā ivācarati pathīnati ‘upamānādācāre’ iti kvip | ‘anunāsikasya kvijhaloḥ’ ityupadhādīrghaḥ | evamagre’pi | anena ‘tatrāparā ṛgvedaḥ’ ityādyuktāparavidyāpravaktṛtvaṃ sūcitam | paravidyā ‘atha parā yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate’ ityuktā brahmavidyā | jaladhiriveti pūrvavatsamāsaḥ | tasya mathīnati manthā ivācarati | anena brahmavidyāsarvasvaparāmarśitvaṃ sūcitam | ata eva kṛtamatayaḥ vaijñānikāḥ diviṣada iva teṣāṃ vitateḥ ṛbhukṣā ivācaratīti ṛbhukṣīṇati | atra nayaparavidyākṛtamatīnāṃ gahanajalanidhidiviṣadupamāḥ bhagavatpraṇatasya pathimathilaṛbhukṣyupamānāmupāyā ityupamā etāḥ pūrvavadeva paraṃparitāḥ mālārūpāśśuddhāśca | atra ‘pathimathyṛbhukṣām’ iti sūtranibaddhapathyādiśabdārthānāmupamānavidhayā krameṇa nibandhanādvakṣyamāṇaratnāvalyalaṅkāreṇa saṅkīrṇatvaṃ pūrvodāharaṇādviśeṣaḥ ||
evamupamānayoranyonyamupameyayoścānukūlye upameyayoreṣopāyatā nirūpitā ||
prātikūlye upāyatā yathā
jaladāyate sadā khalu nidāghadāvāgnidāhate mahate | dvipadāṃ kulāya vipadāṃ pṛdākuvaragirimadāvalendro’yam || 83 ||
nidāghadāvāgnidāhavadācarate mahate dvipadāṃ manujānāṃ sambandhine vipadāṃ kulāya jaladāyate jalada ivācarati | pṛdākuvaragiriśśeṣādriḥ tasya madāvalendraḥ tattvenādhyavasitaśśrīnivāsa ityarthaḥ | atropamānayordavāgnidāhajaladayoranyonyamupameyayośca manujavipatphaṇiśikharināthayoḥ prātikūlye’pi upamayoḥ parasparamānukūlyādupāyataiva ||
iyaṃ mālārūpatāyāmapi bhavati | yathā
sutrāmāṇasi mahatāṃ sapakṣaśekharīṇatāṃ suradviṣatām | pūṣāṇasi himatāmiha rājānasi tamasatāṃ ca śaure tvam || 84 ||
sutrāmevācarasi sutrāmāṇasi sutrāmanśabdāt ‘sarvaprātipadikebhyaḥ kvibvā vaktavyaḥ’ iti kvipi ‘anunāsikasya kvijhaloḥ’ ityupadhādīrghaḥ | evaṃ śikharīṇatāmityādāvapi | sapakṣaśikhariṇa ivācarantīti sapakṣaśikharīṇantaḥ teṣām | klibantācchatā | pūṣevācarasi pūṣāṇasi himānīvācarantīti himantaḥ teṣām | rājeva candra ivācarasi rājānasi tamāṃsīvācarantīti tamasantaḥ teṣāṃ | sarvāṇi ṣaṣṭhyantapadāni suradviṣāmityasya viśeṣaṇāni | evaṃ kevalaśliṣṭaparaṃparitamālārūpopamāyā udāharaṇe ‘sattvasya himālaya iva’ iti prāthamikapadye ‘rajasastvaṃ prāvṛṣeṇyajalada iva’ ityādau prātikūlye upāyatā draṣṭavyā | evaṃ ca sā ānukūlyaprātikūlyamiśreti siddham | itaścānye’pi bhedāḥ kuśāgrīyamatibhiḥ kṛtibhissvayameva vibhāvanīyā iti vistarabhayāduparamyate ||
atropamāyāṃ pūrṇāyāṃ dharmaḥ kvacidanugāmyeva nirdiśyate | kvacitkevalaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpannaḥ | kvacidvastuprativastubhāvena karambhitaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpannaḥ | kkacicchuddhavastuprativastubhāvamāpannaḥ| kvacidasannapyupacaritaḥ | kvacicchabdātmakaḥ, ayameva śleṣātmakaḥ | kvacitsamāsāntarāśrayaṇamūlakaḥ | kvacideṣāṃ yathāsambhavaṃ miśraṇena ||
tatrānugāmī dharmo yathā
jyotsnācandramasāviva vṛṣagirikalitaukasau mahāmahasau | sutarau jagatāṃ pitarau buddhau nidadhīya nityasambaddhau || 85 ||
atra nityasaṃbaddhāviti sakṛnnirdeśāt jyotsnācandramasoḥ śrīśrīnivāsayośca sādhāraṇo dharmo’nugāmī|| yathā vā
kādambinīva nīlā kākodaragiridarīvihṛtilolā | āpyāyayati sumahatī kā’pyeṣā devatā’dbhutā jagatīm || 86 ||
atra nīleti āpyāyatīti ca sakṛnnirdeśātparadevatākādambinyossādhāraṇadharmo’nugāmī ||
yathā vā
nidhimanavadhimiva phaṇigirinihitapadaṃ hitapadaṃ | vidhutavipadam | nirviśya nīrajākṣaṃ nitarāṃ ko nāma bhavati dīnātmā || 87 ||
hitapadaṃ hitādhāyakaṃ atra hitapadatvādirdharmo’nugāmī ||
yathā vā
tāvakapadapadmayugīsevakatāṃ yena jātvapīpsanti | nāśāyaiva hi jātāśśrīśābdherbudbudā iva ta ete ||
yathā vā
sukṛtekṣaṇajanuṣāndhāssakṛdapi na namanti ye ramākāntam | akṛtahitā vyarthamamī śakṛduditāḥ krimaya iva vinaśyanti || 89 ||
sukṛtekṣaṇe puṇyadarśane viṣaye januṣāndhāḥ jātyandhāḥ ‘puṃsānujo januṣāndha iti ca’ ityaluk | sukṛtavyutpattiśūnyā ityarthaḥ | atra bhagavadapraṇatānāṃ nindāyāmeva tātparyādupamānasya nihīnatvaṃ na doṣaḥ | pratyuta guṇa eva | ubhayatra nāśo’nugāmīdharmaḥ ||
kevalaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpanno dharmo yathā
kamalābhujayugalāñcitaparitaḥparirambhamadbhutaṃ kimapi | kanakaśalākāvalayitamarakatanibhamastu manasi mama vastu || 90 ||
kamalābhujayugalena añcitaḥ prāptaḥ paritaḥ parirambhaḥ sarvatassaṃśleṣo yasya tat kimapi adbhutaṃ vastu śrīnivāsaḥ atra kamalābhujayugalakanakaśalākāderbimbapratibimbabhāvena sādhāraṇadharmatā ||
nanu kamalābhujayugalāderasādhāraṇatvātkathamupameti cet atrāhuḥ upameyagatānāmupamānagatānāṃ ca asādhāraṇānāmapi dharmāṇāṃ sādṛśyamūlenābhedādhyavasāyena sādhāraṇatvakalpanādupamāsiddhiḥ | na ca bhramātmakenāhāryābhedabodhena kathaṃ nāma kamalābhujayugalakanakaśalākādervastuto bhinnasya sādhāraṇatvasiddhaye’tyantamasannabhedaśśaknuyātseddhum, bhrameṇārthasiddherayogāditi vācyam | ‘tava kopo samābhāti sudhāṃśāviva pāvakaḥ’ ityādāvapyupamānopameyayoratimātramasattve’pi yathā kalpanāmātreṇa niṣpattiḥ tathaiva prakṛte sādhāraṇadharmasyāpīti dhyeyam | ayameva hi ‘vastuto bhinnayordharmayoḥ parasparasādṛśyādabhinnatayā’dhyavasitayordvirupādānaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ’ iti prācīnairabhidhīyate ||
vastuprativastubhāvena karambhitaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpannastu dharmastrividhaḥ viśeṣaṇamātrayoḥ viśeṣyamātrayoḥ tadyugalayorvā vastuprativastubhāvena karambhitaḥ || tatrādyo yathā
uparicaranmadhukarakulakamalanibhaṃ vṛṣabhaśailaśikharaśaśin | upariṣṭāccaladalakaṃ vadanaṃ tava viśvasampadāṃ sadanam || 91 ||
atra uparicaratvopariṣṭāccalatvayorvastuta ekavidhayorapi pṛthakchabdābhyāṃ nirdeśādvastuprativastubhāvaḥ tadviśeṣaṇakayormadhukarakulālakayorbimbapratibimbabhāva iti tatkarambhitatvaṃ dharmasya ||
dvitīyo yathā
smitarucimasṛṇitamadharaṃ phaṇadharadharaṇīdharādhineturnaḥ | dugdhodadhimugdhormisnigdhaṃ vidrumamivāvaleḍhi manaḥ || 92 ||
atra masṛṇatvasnigdhatvayorvastuta ekarūpayorapi śabdadvayenopādānādvastuprativastubhāvaḥ tadviśeṣyakayoḥ smitarucidugdhodadhimugdhormyorbimbapratibimbabhāva iti tatkarambhito’yaṃ sādhāraṇadharmaḥ ||
yathā vā
jāmbūnadādriśṛṅge jambūtarumiva cakāsataṃ satatam | kanakābhidhagiriśikhare kanantamīḍīya kamapi śubharāśim || 93 ||
atra cakāsattvakanattvayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ tadviśeṣyakayorjāmbūnadādriśṛṅgakanakābhidhagiriśikharayorbimbapratibimbabhāva ityayamapi tatkarambhita eva | kanakābhidhagirirvaikaṭādriḥ| śubharāśitvenātra bhagavato’dhyavasānam || yathā vā
hīrakuruvindahāraprasṛmararuciśabalitā tanulatā śrīḥ | deva tava gāṅgasārasvatapūravicitriteva mitrasutā || 94 ||
atrāpi śabalitatvavicitritatvayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ | tadviśeṣyakayoḥ hīrakuruvindahārarucigāṅgasārasvatapūrayośca bimbapratibimbabhāva ityayamapi tatkambhita eva ||
tṛtīyo yathā
śiśiratarasalilarucirā śaśiśekharamakuṭavāhinīvaiṣā | śītalatarakaruṇārasamadhurā’hidhuraṃdharādrināthavadhūḥ || 95 ||
atra viśeṣaṇayośśiśirataratvaśītalataratvayoḥ viśeṣyayoḥ ruciratvamadhuratvayośca vastuprativastubhāvenobhayatassaṃpuṭitassalilakaruṇārasayorbimbapratibimbabhāva ityayamapi tatkarambhita eva ||
yathā vā
sundarakuruvindamaṇīsamudgakāntasthahīrahāralatām | mañjularadanacchadayugamadhyagatā jayati nātha daśanālī || 96 ||
atrāpi viśeṣaṇayossundaratvamañjulatvayoḥ viśeṣyayoścāntasthatvamadhyagatatvayoḥ vastuprativastubhāvenobhayatassaṃpuṭitaḥ kuru vindamaṇīsamudgakadaśanacchadayugayorbimbapratibimbabhāva ityayamapi tatkambhitaḥ || yathā vā
asitaiḥ kusumairmasṛṇāśśālābibhrattamālasāla iva | nīlairmaṇibhirnibiḍā bāhā dadhadahigireḥpatirjayati || 97 ||
atra kusumamaṇiviśeṣaṇayorasitatvanīlatvayostadviśeṣyayośca masṛṇatvanibiḍatvayoḥ evameva śālābāhāviśeṣaṇatvamāpannayoḥ masṛṇatvasnigdhatvayoḥ tadviśeṣyayorbibhrattvadadhattvayośca vastuprativastubhāvenobhayatassaṃpuṭitaḥ kusumānāṃ maṇīnāṃ śālānāṃ bāhānāṃ ca bimbapratibimbabhāva ityayamapi vastuprativastubhāvakarambhita eva | pūrvatra saṃpuṭita eko bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ iha tu dvāviti viśeṣaḥ | śālāḥ skandhaśākhāḥ ‘skandhaśākhāśāle’ ityamaraḥ | skandhānnirgatā śākhā skandhaśākhā skandho nāma mūlācchākhāvadhiḥ prakāṇḍaḥ ‘astrī prakāṇḍasskandhassyānmūlācchākhāvadhestaroḥ’ ityamaraḥ | skandhaśākhā śālā cetyetau śabdau uktaśākhāvācinau | ayaṃ tṛtīyaḥ prakāro rasagaṅgādharakāreṇoktaḥ| citramīmāṃsāyāṃ tu prāguktau dvāveva prakārau darśitau | yathoktaṃ tatra ‘ekasyaiva dharmasya sambandhibhedena dvirupādanaṃ vastuprativastubhāvaḥ | sa tu śuddho na sambhavati kiṃ tu viśeṣaṇatayā viśeṣyatayā vā sarvatra bimbapratibimbabhāvakarambhitaḥ’ iti | evaṃ ca śuddhavastuprativastubhāvāpanno dharmo na saṃbhavatītyeva te manyante ||
rasagaṅgādharakārastu ‘vimalaṃ vadanaṃ tasyā niṣkalaṅkamṛgāṅkati’ ityatra vaimalyaniṣkalaṅkatvayorvastuta ekarūpayorbimbapratibimbabhāvanirmuktaṃ vastuprativastubhāvamāpannayorupamāniṣpādakatvaṃ yadyasti tadā śuddhavastuprativastubhāvāpannopyeṣa ṣaṣṭho dharma ityavādīt | etadabhiprāyānurodhenaivāsmābhiśśuddhavastuprativastubhāvāpanno’pi bhedo’dhastātparigaṇitaḥ ||
sa yathā
vimale vilocane tava viśadaśaradvikasitāmbujāyete | mṛgarājāhvayavilasannagarājādhīśvarasya jāyete || 98 ||
mṛgarājāhvayavilasannagarājādhīśvarasya śrīveṃkaṭādrinetuḥ jāye sahadharmiṇi te vimale vilocane viśade svacche śaradvikasitāmbuje ivācarataḥ viśadaśaradvikasitāmbujāyete, kyaṅantāllaṭprathamapuruṣadvivacanam | atra vimalatvaviśadatvayorvastuta ekarūpayorbimbapratibimbabhāvanirmuktaṃ vastuprativastubhāvamāpannayorupamāniṣpādakatvamiti śuddhavastuprativastubhāvāpannatā dharmasyati dhyeyam ||
na ca ‘kamalabhujayugalāñcitaparitaḥparirambham’ ityādau marakatabhagavatorupamāyāṃ dharmāntarasyānavagamātkamalābhujayugala kanakaśalākayoḥ parirabdhatvavalayitatvayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvo yathā’vaśyamabhyupeyaḥ prakṛte tu na tathā vastuprativastubhāvaḥ, vilocanāmbujayormanoharatvarūpasādhāraṇadharmasya pratīyamānatvena dharmāntarānapekṣaṇāditi vācyam | evaṃ tarhi ‘yāntyā muhurvalitakandharamānanaṃ tadāvṛttavṛntaśatapatranibhaṃ vahantyā’ iti bhavabhūtipadye’pi pratīyamānena saundaryeṇaiva sāmānyena nirvāhe kandharāvṛntayorbimbapratibimbabhāvasya valitatvāvṛttatvayorvastuprativastubhāvasya ca sarvairālaṃkārikaissvīkāro virudhyeta | ato yathā’vasthitameva sādhu ||
asannapyupacarito yathā druhiṇasmaraharamukhasuramahimāno’pyahaha tāvakamahimni | jalajavilocana jaladhau kulācalā iva kilābhimajjanti || 99 ||
atra majjanaṃ mūrtadravyadharmo mahimasūpacaritaḥ ||
yathā vā
prasabhaṃ praveśitamapi prāyaścarite taveśa śaṭhahṛdayam | naiva dravati samudre’pyayo ghanataraṃ payojanayana yathā || 100 ||
prasabhaṃ praveśitamityasya uttarārdhe samudre ityatrāpyanvayaḥ | ghanataraṃ ayo yathā kālāyasamivetyarthaḥ | atra praveśanadravayormūrtadravyadharmatayā hṛdaye upacāraḥ ||
śleṣātmako yathā
mañjulatamālasarcchrīrañjanadharaṇīdhareśahṛdayagatā| vanamāleva vicitrā vanamālā prasṛmarāmodā || 101||
mañjulaiḥ tamālaiḥ tāpiñchaiḥ satī praśastā śrīśśobhā yasyāssā tathoktā | pakṣe mañjulatamā lasarcchrīriti chedaḥ, mañjulatamā atimanojñetyarthaḥ | lasantī śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ yasyāṃ sā | añjanadharaṇīdhareśasya añjanākhyagirivarasya | pakṣe veṅkaṭādrināthasya bhagavataḥ hṛdayaṃ madhyabhāgaṃ vakṣaśca gatā prāptā | prasṛmaraḥ visṛtvaraḥ āmodaḥ parimalaviśeṣo yasyāssā | anyatra prakṛṣṭaḥ sṛmarāṇāṃ mahācamarīmṛgāṇāṃ āmodo harṣaḥ yasyāṃ sā | ‘sṛmaro gavayaśśaśaḥ’ iti mṛgaviśeṣanāmasvamaraḥ| vanamāleva vipinapaṅktiriva vanamālā vaijayantī vibhiḥ pakṣibhiḥ citrā vismayāvahā | pakṣe ‘pañcavarṇā tu yā mālā vaijayantī gadābhṛtaḥ’ ityuktarītyā pañcavarṇatayā śabaletyarthaḥ | atra vanapaṅktivaijayantyośleṣakṛtassādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | atra upamānopameyagatasyārthasyaikasyābhāvācchabda eva sādhāraṇadharma ityanye ||
yathāvā
nijavibhavamahimagāyakamayūravibhrāḍudāttaghanatejāḥ | varṣāsamaya ivāyaṃ tarṣāñśamayati ramāsakho jagatām || 102 ||
nijavibhavamahimagāyakāḥ mayavaḥ kinnarāḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘syātkinnaraḥ kiṃpuruṣasturaṅgavadano mayuḥ’ ityamaraḥ | ravibhrāṭ sūrya iva bhrājamānaḥ | udāttaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ ghanaṃ sāndraṃ tejo yasya sa tathoktaḥ iti bhagavatpakṣe | varṣāsamayapakṣe tu nijavibhavamahimnaḥ gāyakāḥ kekāravaiḥ prakāśakāḥ ye mayūrāḥ taiḥ vibhrāṭ bhrājiṣṇuḥ | udāttaṃ ghanānāṃ tejo yasmin tathoktaḥ | tarṣān āśāḥ udanyāśca śamayati | atrāpi śleṣakṛtassamānadharmaḥ || samāsabhedāśrayaṇamūlako dharmo yathā
māṃsalaśaṅkhasudarśanahaṃsāvartā sanābhinālīkā | śamitāmitabhavadamunā yamunālaharīva tava tanuśśaure || 103 ||
māṃsalo pṛthulau śaṅkhasudarśanau haṃsāvartāviva śaṅkhasudarśanahaṃsāvartau yasyāṃ sā tathoktā | anyatra śaṅkhasudarśanāviva haṃsāvartāviti samāsaḥ | nābhernālīkaṃ tena saha vartata iti tathoktā | anyatra nābhyā madhyasthakarṇikayā saha vartanta iti tathoktāni nālīkāni padmāni yasyāṃ seti vā nābhitulyaṃ nālīkaṃ tena saha vartata iti vā samāsaḥ | bhavaḥ damunāḥ agniriveti | pakṣe bhavasadṛśaḥ damunāḥ iti samasaḥ | evaṃ samāsabhedāśrayaṇena samānadharmatā | kecidimaṃ sādhāraṇadharmaṃ pṛthaṅnādriyante, samāsabhedanibandhanayorarthayośleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyenaikadharmatāsampādanāt | śleṣātmake samānadharma evāsyāpyantarbhāva iti teṣāmāśayaḥ ||
miśraṇeṣvanugāmitvabimbapratibimbabhāvāpannadharmayormiśraṇaṃ yathā
phalavāniva dharaṇiruho jalavāniva jāhnavīhrado jagati | 
balavāniva dharaṇipatirjalajekṣaṇabhaktimān janaśślāghyaḥ || 104 ||
atra bhagavadbhaktimattva phalavattvādi ca bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpannau dharmau ślāghyatve anugāmini dharme abhedamāsādya sthitau || yathāvā
api sumanaḥkulajātāḥ kheṭāstvanmahimasampadanabhijñāḥ | api sumanaḥkulajātāḥ kīṭā iva dūratastyājyāḥ || 105 ||
sumanaḥkule vidvadvaṃśe dvitīyārdhe kusumasamudaye jātāḥ kheṭāḥ garhyā janāḥ ‘kupūyakutsitāvadyakheṭagarhyāṇakassamāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | atra bhagavanmahimasampadanabhijñānāṃ garhyajanānāṃ kīṭānāṃ ca dūratastyājyatvarūpo’nugāmī dharmaḥ vidvadvaṃśajātatvakusumasamūhabhavatvayorbimbapratibimbabhāvamapekṣya labdhātmakaḥ ||
yathāvā
paratāsauśīlyābhyāṃ duradhigamo nātha bhavasi sugamaśca | uccaistvaphalitvābhyāmuttamanaga iva nṛṇāṃ khagagirīndo || 106 ||
atra duradhigamatvaṃ sugamatvaṃ ca paratvasauśīlyayoruccaistvaphalitvayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvamapekṣya labdhātmakamanugāmī sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | atrādyaṃ mālārūpaṃ, dvitīyaṃ yamakena tṛtīyaṃ yathāsaṅkhyena saṅkīrṇamiti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
samrāja ivānamrā bhūpālātityamapyanativelāḥ | śirasā dadhate śāsanamaraviṃdāsanamukhāśca tava bhūman || 107 ||
samrāja iti ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ padam | atrānamratvādikaṃ sāmānyadharmassamrāḍbhagavatorbimbapratibimbabhāvamupajīvyādhigatasattākamiti dhyeyam |
yathāvā
phaṇiśailanātha masṛṇitamaṇivaramauktikasaro hare bhavataḥ | pratibhāti hṛdayadeśaḥ pratibimbitacandratāra iva sindhuḥ || 108 ||
maṇivaraḥ kaustubhaḥ | evaṃ pūrvamudāhṛte ‘ānandanilaya ‘ iti padye’pi miśraṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | idaṃ śuddhabimbapratibimbabhāvānugāminormiśraṇam ||
anugāmitvavastuprativastubhāvakarambhitabimbapratibimbabhāvayordharmayormiśraṇaṃ yathā
upasṛpyate janairabhyuditastvajjñānabhajanavairāgyaiḥ| pādapa iva paramātmanprasūnaphalapallavaissamullasitaḥ ||
abhyuditaḥ abhyudayaṃ prāptaḥ | atrābhyuditatvasamullasitatvayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ | tadviśeṣyakāṇāṃ bhagavajjñānabhajanavairāgyāṇāṃ prasūnaphalapallavānāṃ ca bimbapratibimbabhāva iti tatkarambhito’ya dharma upasarpaṇe anugāmini dharme abhedamāpannaḥ ||
yathāvā
vṛjinavrajavinimagno gaja iva sīdāmi makaramukhalagnaḥ | tvāmantareṇa ko me kāmaṃ kṣemaṃkaro hare bhavitā || 110 || hare iti karivaradatvābhiprāyakaṃ, haryavatāra eva gajendramokṣaṇāt | atra vinimagnatvalagnatvayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ, tadviśeṣyakayoḥ vṛjinavrajamakaramukhayorbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | tadavalambanenasīdāmītyanugāmī prāptasattāka ityanayormiśraṇam | na ca vinimagnatvalagnatvayorvibhinnatvānna vastuprativastubhāvassambhavatīti vācyam | vṛjinavraje nimajjanasyāsambhavāttatrāpi sambandhamātrasyaiva vivakṣaṇena tayorekarūpatvāt ||
yathāvā
aviraladalakusumamayastabakastimito dvirepharāja iva | ghanaghṛṇiharimaṇimayaphaṇiśikharivilīno harirmano harati || 111 ||
atra aviralatvaghanatvayorvastuta ekarūpayorapi śabdadvayenopādānādvastuprativastubhāvaḥ | tadviśeṣaṇakānāṃ dalānāṃ ghṛṇīnāṃ ca | evaṃ stimitatvavilīnatvayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ | tadviśeṣyakayoḥ kusumamayastabakaharimaṇimayaphaṇiśikhariṇośca bimbapratibimbabhāva iti tatkarambhito’yaṃ dharmo manoharaṇe anugāmini dharme abhedamāsādya sthita ityanayormiśraṇam | pūrvodāharaṇadvaye viśeṣyayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ | iha tu viśeṣyasya viśeṣaṇasya ceti dvividho vastuprativastubhāva iti bhidā ||
kvaciddhetuhetumadbhāvena yathā
saulabhyajanisthānaṃ sevyassarvairnaraissarojākṣaḥ | saurabhyajanmabhūmissarasīruhanivaha iva madhukaraughaiḥ || atra janisthānatvajanmabhūmitvayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ | tadviśeṣyake saulabhyasaurabhye bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanne sarojākṣasarasīruhanivahayossevyatve’nugāmini hetuḥ | idaṃ miśraṇaṃ śuddhabimbapratibimbabhāve’pi bhavatīti dhyeyam ||
anugāmitvaśleṣayormiśraṇaṃ yathā
