narasiṃhagirivaro’yaṃ śirasā sukalābhipūrṇavidhubimbam | udayādririva dadhānaḥ pradadātyānandamakhilalokānām || 113 ||
sukalābhipūrṇaṃ ‘mantramantreśvaranyāsātsā’pi ṣāḍguṇyavigrahā’ ityuktarītyā sakalakalāparipūrṇamiti bhagavadbimbapakṣe’rthaḥ | anyatra tu sphuṭa eva vidhubimbaṃ śrīnivāsārcāṃ
sarvātiśāyi ṣāḍguṇyaṃ saṃsthitaṃ mantrabimbayoḥ |
tenārcyo bhagavān sākṣānnopacāradhiyā kvacit ||
iti bhagavacchāstravacanānyatrānusandheyāni | anyatra candrabimbamityarthaḥ | atra sukalābhipūrṇavidhubimbaśabdaśleṣopajīvanena prāptātmasthitikamakhilalokānandapradatvamanugāmī sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ ||
śleṣavastuprativastubhāvabimbapratibimbabhāvamiśraṇaṃ yathā
vaiśadyaṃ naiti naro vidyāṃ saṃśikṣito’pyasuraśīlaḥ| śāṇollikhito’pyacyuta malinaprakṛtiryatheṅgālaḥ || 114 || asuraśīlaḥ
dvividho bhūtasargo’yaṃ daiva āsura eva ca |
viṣṇubhaktiparo daivo viparītastathā’’suraḥ ||
ityuktaprakāreṇāsuraprakṛtirityarthaḥ | vidyāṃ ‘sā vidyā yā vimuktaye’ ityuktāṃ brahmavidyāṃ saṃśikṣito’pi samyaganuśiṣṭo’pi vaiśadyaṃ paratattvabodhanaṃ naiti |
janmāntarasahasreṣu yā buddhirbhāvitā purā |
tāmeva bhajate janturupadeśo nirarthakaḥ ||
naivāsuraprakṛtayaḥ prabhavanti boddhum |
ityādyukteriti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe vaiśadyaṃ dhāvalyaṃ anyatsugamam | atrāsuraśīlatvamalinaprakṛtitvayorvastuprativastubhāvakarambhitaḥ bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ śīlaprakṛtyorvastuta ekarūpayorvibhinnaśabdābhyāṃ nirdeśāt, tadviśeṣyakayoḥ asuramalinayorvastuto’bhinnayorapi sārūpyeṇaikatvādhyavasānācca | vidyāsaṃśikṣitatvaśāṇollikhitatvayoḥ kevalaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | vaiśadyaṃ naitīti śliṣṭassamānadharmastābhyāmuttejita iti draṣṭavyam ||
śleṣopacārabimbapratibimbabhāvamiśraṇaṃ yathā
prekṣāvata iva sākṣātparamasya brahmaṇo vṛṣagirīndoḥ | vidyeva śrīrhṛdyā cetohṛtpadyamujjvalayatīyam ||
prekṣāvataḥ prajñāśālino janasyeva sākṣātparamasya brahmaṇaḥ śrīnivāsasya vidyeva kāvyacchandovyākaraṇādividyeva śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ hṛdayasthā cittadhṛtā ayātayāmā satītyarthaḥ | bhavārthe hṛdayaśabdāddigāditvādyat | anyatra hṛdayapriyā satī ‘hṛdayasya priyaḥ’ iti yat | ubhayatra ‘hṛdayasya hṛllekhayadaṇlāseṣu’ iti hṛdādeśaḥ | cetohṛt manoharaṃ padyaṃ ślokaṃ kāvyamityarthaḥ | ujjvalayati pravartayatīti yāvat ||
śrīpakṣe ca itaḥ hṛt iti tripadatayā chedaḥ | caśabdo bhinnakramaḥ | ita iti sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ| asya śrīnivāsasyetyarthaḥ | hṛt vakṣaḥ padyaṃ padaṃ caraṇamasmin dṛśyamiti padyaṃ, yāvakaviliptanijacaraṇatalāṅkitaṃ sadityarthaḥ | padyaṃ yathā syāttatheti vā | ‘padamasmin dṛśyam’ iti padaśabdāduktārthe yat | ujjvalayati ca kaustubhādyapekṣayā utkṛṣṭatvena prakāśayati cetyarthaḥ | jvalaterṇijantāllaṭi ghaṭāditvena mitvāddhrasvaḥ | atra ‘pūrṇaṃ tejassphurati bhavatīpādalākṣārasāṅkam’ ityādikamanusandheyam | atra prekṣāvatparabrahmaṇorbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | hṛdyā cetohṛtpadyamiti śleṣaḥ, ujjvalayatītyupacāra ityeteṣāṃ miśraṇam ||
kevalabimbapratibimbabhāvavastuprativastubhāvakarambhitabimbapratibimbabhāvaśleṣānugāmitānāṃ yathā
bhavana ivākhilabhuvane sudīptinidhayassukīrtiśevadhayaḥ| dīpā ivābhirūpāśśrīparibṛḍha saṃcaranti bhavadīyāḥ || 116 ||
sudīptinidhayaḥ dīpā iva sukīrtiśevadhayaḥ bhavadīyāḥ abhirūpāssantaḥ bhavaneṣviva bhuvaneṣu saṃcarantīti yojanā | abhirūpāḥ manoharāḥ vidvāṃsaśca | ‘prāptarūpasurūpābhirūpā budhamanojñayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | atra bhavanabhuvanayoḥ kevalaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ, nidhiśevadhyoḥ vastuprativastubhāvaḥ, tadviśeṣyakayoḥ dīptikīrtyorbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ tatkarambhitaḥ abhirūpā iti śleṣaḥ, saṃcarantītyanugāmitvamityeteṣāṃ miśraṇam ||
anugāmitvaśleṣabimbapratibimbabhāvopacārāṇāṃ miśraṇaṃ yathā
jagati samantātprathitaṃ pravitatasārasvatoditaṃ niyatam | amṛtamiva tvaccaritaṃ surā iva narāḥ pibanti muravairin || 117 ||
sārasvataṃ sarasvata āgataṃ ‘tata āgataḥ’ ityarthe prāgdīvyatīyo’ṇ | uditaṃ udayaḥ āvirbhāvo yasya tat, pravitataṃ ca tat sārasvatoditaṃ ceti vigrahaḥ, vistṛtaṃ dugdhodadhijanitaṃ cetyarthaḥ | pakṣe pravitatāni yāni sārasvatāni sarasvatīvibhūtibhūtāni śāstrāṇi ‘yatsārasvatadugdhasāgara’ ityādiprayogāt | taiḥ uditaṃ udīritam | atra samantātprathitatvamanugāmī, pravitatasārasvatoditamiti śliṣṭaḥ, amṛtacaritayorbimbapratibimbabhāvāpannaḥ pibantītyupacaritassādhāraṇadharma ityeteṣāṃ miśraṇam | evamanyairapi vyāmiśraṇaṃ sudhībhirunneyamatibhūmitvādeṣāṃ diṅmātramudāhārṣma | tathācoktaṃ kāvyāloke
arpayanto vikalpanāṃ rūpakopamayoryataḥ |
diṅmātraṃ darśitaṃ dhīrairanuktamanumīyatām || iti ||
‘pūrṇāyāmeveme dharmabhedāḥ | luptāyāṃ tu naivaṃ bhedāḥ tasyāṃ sādhāraṇadharmasyānugāmitvaniyamāt’ iti citramīmāṃsākṛtaḥ | rasagaṅgādharakārastu nemaṃ niyamamabhyupaiti | tathāca tadgranthaḥ “yaccāppadīkṣitaiḥ luptāyāṃ tu naivaṃ bhedāḥ tasyāṃ sādhāraṇadharmasyānugāmitāniyamāditi | tanna ‘malaya iva jagati pāṇḍurvalmīka ivādhidharaṇi dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ’ ityatrānugāmidharmasyāpratyayāccandanānāṃ pāṇḍavānāṃ sarpāṇāṃ duryodhanādīnāṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvasyaiva pratipatteḥ | na ca śabdenopāttatvaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāve tantramityāgraho viduṣāmucitaḥ, śrautatvārthatvābhyāṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvasya dvaividhyaucityāt | ataevāprastutapraśaṃsādau prakṛtāprakṛtavākyārthayoraupamyamavayavabimbapratibimbabhāvamūlaṃ saṃgacchate” ityabhāṇīt | ayaṃ cātra viśeṣaḥ ‘ubhayamadhye pratibimbasyaivopādāne upamāne dharmahānirdoṣaḥ tatra tatsthānīyadharmānukteḥ | bimbamātropādāne tūpamāne dharmādhikyaṃ doṣaḥ upameye tatsthānīyadharmānukteḥ | tasmādbimbapratibimbabhāvāpannayordvayoreva śābdatvaṃ dvayoreva vā’rthatvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ, na tvekasya śābdatvamanyasyārthatvamiti tattvam’ iti vadanti | evaṃca kvacidarthagamyo’pi bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanno dharmassambhavatīti siddham ||
yathā
saṃkrandananandanavanabhūmirivānandabhūmidharabhūmiḥ | vyāleśācalaramaṇaḥ prāleyācalavareṇya iva || 118
ānandabhūmidharaśśeṣādriḥ ānandādririti tannāmasu pāṭhāt | vacanaṃ tvekonatriṃśe ‘cintāmaṇidharaṇībhṛti’ iti śloke udāhāṣmardhastāt | atra nandanavanaśeṣācalabhūmyoḥ himācalaśrīnivāsayoścānugāmyādidharmāntarāpratyayena kalpatarośśrīnivāsasya ca gaṅgāyā dayāyāśca bimbapratibimbabhāvasyaiva gamyamānatvādupamāniṣpattiḥ || yathāvā
pavanāśanācalāgraṃ dhanādhināthasya kośabhavanamiva | khaniriva jalanidhitanayāpraṇayavatī devatā parā saiṣā || 119 ||
jalanidhitanayāyāṃ praṇayavatī parā devatā śrīnivāsa ityarthaḥ| atra śeṣācalāgradhanadakośabhavanayoḥ khaniparadevatayoścānu gāmyādidharmāntarāpratītyā bhagavato nidheśca guṇānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca bimbapratibimbabhāvasyaiva pratīyamānatvānniṣpattirupamāyā iti dhyeyam ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre upamāsaraḥ prathamaḥ
<3>
athopameyopamā
upameyopamā sā syādupamānopameyayoḥ |
pūrvayorvaiparītyaṃ cetparataḥ punarucyate || 23 ||
pūrvopamāyāmupameyasya dvitīyopamāyāmupamānatvaṃ tatropamānasyopameyatvaṃ ca yatra varṇyate sā upameyopametyarthaḥ ||
yathā
śrīvāsahṛdayalolā vananidhibāleva kanati vanamālā | vanamāleva virājati vananidhibālā vibhāsiguṇajālā || 120 || atra vananidhibālāvanamālayorvākyabhedena upamānatvamupameyatvaṃ ca varṇitam | tatra prathame vākye śriya upamānatvaṃ vanamālāyā upameyatvaṃ dvitīye tu vyatyayeneti lakṣaṇasamanvayaḥ | lakṣmīvanamālayoḥ kasyāścitkayācitsādṛśye’bhihite tasyā apyanyayā sādṛśyamarthasiddhamapi kaṇṭharaveṇābhidhīyamānaṃ tṛtīyasadṛśavyavacchedaṃ phalati | atrodāharaṇe prakṛtayorevopamānopameyatvakalpanaṃ, prakṛtāprakṛtayorapyeṣā sambhavatīti kuvalayānande sthitam ||
tatredamudāharaṇam
ghanapatiriva bhuvanapatirbhuvanapatirivaiṣa ghanapatirlasati| 
capalevaiṣā kamalā kamaleva cakāsti capalā’pi || 121 ||
bhuvanapatiḥ bhagavān, capalā vidyut | atra bhuvanapatiḥ kamalā ca prakṛtau, eṣaḥ eṣeti nirdeśāt | iyaṃ ca tāvaddvividhā ‘uktadharmā vyaṅgyadharmā ceti | uktadharmā tāvadanugāmyādibhiḥ prāguktairdharmairanekadhā | tatrānugāmī dharmastvavyavahitodāharaṇe lasati cakāstītyukta eva ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanno dharmo yathā
śaradambudaparivītaḥ kalaśāmbudhirājavīcimadhyagataḥ | bhujjagadharo mandara iva sa mandaro bhujagadhara iva vibhāyāt || 122 ||
vibhāyāditi sambhāvanāyāṃ liṅ | atra parivītatvamadhyagatatvābhyāṃ viśeṣyābhyāṃ parasparaṃ vastuprativastubhāvamāpannābhyāṃ karambhitaḥ śaradambudakalaśāmbudhivīcirūpaḥ parasparaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpanno dharmaḥ ||
kevalaṃ vastuprativastubhāvāpanno yathā
kamalālayākapolau vimalau susnigdhavajramukurasakhau | susnigdhavajramukurau suvimalakamalālayākapolanibhau || 123 ||
atra vimalatvasusnigdhatvayoḥ kevalaṃ vastuprativastubhāvaḥ ||
upacarito yathā
madhumathanabhaktacaraṇā vidhukiraṇā iva jagadviśadayanti | vidhukiraṇā iva śiśirimamasṛṇā madhumathanabhaktacaraṇāśca || 124 ||
atra śiśirimamasṛṇā ityetatpūrvārdhe’pyanvetavyam | śiśirimamasṛṇā viśadayantīti copacaritaḥ | bhaktacaraṇā ityatra caraṇaśabdo gurucaraṇā ityādāviva pūjyavācī ||
śleṣātmako yathā
vikasitapuṣkaravisṛmaratararasaśīkarataraṅgitātmaruciḥ| kariṇī vā puṣkariṇī puṣkariṇīvāhiśailagā kariṇī || 125 ||
ahiśailagā puṣkariṇī svāmipuṣkariṇī kariṇī vā, vāśabda upamāvācakaḥ | vikasitāni yāni puṣkarāṇi kamalāni tebhyaḥ, anyatra vikasitaṃ yatpuṣkaraṃ śuṇḍāgraṃ tasmāt visṛmarataraiḥ rasaśīkaraiḥ makarandakaraiḥ salilakaṇaiśca taraṅgitā atiśayitā ātmano ruciḥ prabhā svādutā ca yasyāssā tathoktā | atra vikasitetyādiśleṣarūpaḥ puṣkariṇīkariṇyossamānadharmaḥ ||
vyaṅgyadharmā yathā
kalpalatevāmbhonidhisutā’bdhiduhiteva hanta kalpalatā | nandanavanamiva śaurerhṛdayaṃ hṛdayamiva nandanavanaṃ ca || 126 ||
iyameva paramparitarūpā’pi | evaṃ ghanapatiriveti prāgudāhṛte padye’pi | eṣā sarvā’pi sphuṭe vākyabhede prapañcitā ||
ārthe tu vākyabhede yathā
yadveṅkaṭagiribhāgyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ daugdhasaindhavaṃ yadapi tanmithunaṃ bhavane’sminnunmiṣati parasparopamaiśvaryam ||
atra parasparopamaiśvaryamiti saṃkṣiptādvākyādidamīyamaiśvaryametadīyenaiśvaryeṇa etadīyamidamīyena ca sadṛśaṃ bhavatīti vākyadvayaṃ vivaraṇarūpamunmiṣati | saubhāgyaṃ daugdhasaindhavamityatra hṛdbhagasindhvante’ ityubhayapadavṛddhiḥ | evaṃ pūrṇāluptādayo’pyasyā upamāyā iva prāyaśassarve’pi bhedāssambhavanti ||
tatra diṅmātramudāhriyate
nītiryathā vinītiryathā vinītistathaiva nītirapi | khyātiriva bhūtiracyuta bhūtiriva khyātirapi tava janānām || 128 ||
āyatyā tulyā’’dṛtirādṛtyā’pyāyatistathā tulyā | sannatirunnatisadṛśī sannatisadṛśī tathonnatiścāpi || 129 ||
dhṛtivanmatirvijayate mativaddhṛtirahipatikṣitidharendo| etāvatī samṛddhissphītā bhavitā kimabhavadīyānām || 130 ||
idaṃ padyatrayamekānvayatvādviśeṣakam | ‘dvābhyāṃ yugmamiti proktaṃ tribhiśślokairviśeṣakam’ iti lakṣaṇāt | antimapadye vidyamānaṃ vijayata ityetat pūrveṣu sarveṣvapi vākyeṣu samānadharmatayā’nveti | atra śrautīṃ taddhitagāmantareṇa yathākramaṃ prathame’rdhe śrautī vākyagā, dvitīye’rdhe śrautī samāsagā ivena saha samāsavidhānāt, tṛtīye’rdhe ārthī vākyagā, turīye’rdhe ārthī samāsagā, pañcame ārthī taddhitagā ceti śrautyārthī ca pañcavidhā pūrṇopameyopamā draṣṭavyā | atraiva tṛtīyaślokaprathamārdhe ‘dhṛtideśīyā matirapi matideśīyā dhṛtiḥ phaṇigirīndrā’ iti paṭhite dharmasyānupādānātpañcavidhā’pi vācakaluptā bhaviṣyati | śrautī dharmaluptā tu taddhitagā na saṃbhavatītyavocāma ||
meravati veṅkaṭādrirmeruśśrīveṅkaṭādrayati kāmam | gaṅgāyate’bhragaṅgā surapathagaṅgāyate gaṅgā || 131 || abbhragaṅgāsurapathagaṅgetyābhyāṃ veṅkaṭādrigatā ākāśagaṅgocyate ‘svāmipuṣkariṇī caiva viyadgaṅgā tataḥparam’ iti brāhmokteḥ | atra kvipi kyaṅi ca dharmavācakaluptopameyopamā | anayaiva diśā pratibhāśālibhiranye’pi tatprabhedā yathāsambhavamunneyāḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre upameyopamāsaro dvitīyaḥ
<4>
athānanvayasaraḥ
ananvayo yadekasyaivopamānopameyatā ||
yatraikasyaivopamānopameyabhāvo nibadhyate tatrānanvayaḥ | varṇyamānamapi svasya svena sādhamaryaṃ nānvetīti vyutpatteḥ | yathā rudrarodanavapocchedanādyarthavāde stutinindābhūtasyāsadarthasya nibandhanaṃ stutinindayorupayujyate tathaivānanvayino’pi svasya svena sādṛśyavarṇanaṃ dvitīyasabrahmacārivyavacchedenānupamatvadyotanāyopayujyate | evaṃ ca dvitīyasadṛśavyavacchedaphalakavarṇanaviṣayībhūtamekopamānopameyakaṃ sādṛśyamananvaya iti paryavasyati | sa ca kasyāpyupaskārakatve’laṃkāraḥ| anyathā tu svavaicitryamātraviśrāntaḥ | evamalaṃkārāntareṣvapi ||
savidhe vicarati bhagavati samuditamecakimavṛṣaśikhariśikharam | prāvṛṣi vijṛmbhamāṇairambhodairiva karambhitaṃ bhāti || 132 ||
atra śrīnivāsadivyavigraharucimecakitaṃ bhujagaśikhariśikharaṃ svenaiva prāvṛṣi nīlāmbudaiḥ karambhitenopamīyata iti tatsādṛśyavāraṇāya bhūtāntaṃ viśeṣaṇam | na hyatra sādṛśyavarṇanasya phalaṃ dvitīyasabrahmacārivyavacchedaḥ tasyāpratipatteḥ ||
vadane pṛdākugirivarasadanasya haressmitaprabhā lasati | śāradaravikaravikasitanīraja iva candracandrikā sāndrā || 133 ||
iti kalpitopamānikāyāmupamāyāmatiprasaṅgavāraṇāyaikopamānopameyakamiti | atrāsata upamānasya kalpanayā sadupamānamaṃ astīti dvitīyasadṛśavyavacchedasyāsti pratītiḥ |
udāharaṇaṃ
vidhutatrividhāvadhikaṃ vedastvāṃ vadatu nissamaṃ bhagavan | nipuṇaṃ vicintane punarūpamā bhavato’sti nanu bhavāneva || 134 ||
vidhutaḥ trividho’vadhiḥ deśakālavastuparicchedo yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ ‘satyaṃ jñānamanantam’ iti śruteḥ | tvāṃ vedaḥ ‘na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate’ iti śrutiḥ nissamaṃ dvitīyasadṛśavasturahitaṃ vadatu | upamāsadṛśa ityarthaḥ ‘pratikṛtirupamopamānaṃ syāt’ ityamaraḥ | ‘sahakāra na prapede madhupena tavopamā jagati’ iti prayogaśca ||
yathā vā 
akhilaṃ bhuvanaṃ vicitaṃ teṣāṃ svarūpamapi viditam | arthijanepsitadāne tīrthegirīśa tvameva tava sadṛśaḥ || 135 ||
tīrthagiririti śeṣādrereva nāmāntaram | “sarvatīrthamayatvācca tīrthādriṃ prāhuruttamāḥ’ iti vārāhe tannāmanirvacanāt | pūrvasmin padye sarvotkṛṣṭatvarūpo dharmo’nugāmī vyaṅgyaḥ, iha tu arthijanepsitadāne iti vācya iti vailakṣaṇyam | ubhayatrāpi bhagavadviṣayakaratyupaskārakatvādalaṃkāro’yam | bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanno dharmastvatra nāsti, tasmiṃśca sati kiṃciddharmāvacchinnena svena sādṛśyasya dharmāntarāvacchinne svasminnanvaye bādhakābhāvātsadṛśāntaravyavacchedāpratipatteścānanvaya eva na syāt | ayaṃ cānanvayaḥ pūrṇo luptaśceti tāvaddvividhaḥ | pūrṇastūpamāvat ṣaḍvidho’pi saṃbhavati ||
yathā
śrīśśrīryathā ghanakṛpā kamalā kamaleva pūrṇasaubhāgyā | nātho’cyutena sadṛśo’cyuta eva harirhito harisadṛkṣaḥ || 136 ||
śrīveṅkaṭādripativaddhyeśśrīveṃkaṭādripatireva | śrīśrīnivāsapadavadbhaktirnaśśrīnivāsapada eva ||
atra yugme ādyapāde śrautaḥ pūrṇo vākyago’nanvayaḥ | dvitīyapāde samāsagaśśrautaḥ pūrṇaḥ | ivena saha samāsavidhānāt | nātha ityādyacyuta evetyante ārthassamāsagaḥ pūrṇaḥ| śiṣṭe turīyapāde harirityādau samāsaga ārthaḥ pūrṇaḥ | dvitīyaślokādyārdhe ‘tena tulyam’ iti vatessattvādārthastaddhitagaḥ pūrṇaḥ | taddvitīyārdhe ‘tatra tasyeva’ iti vatessattvācchrautastaddhitagaḥ pūrṇa iti dhyeyam | lupteṣvapi dharmaluptaḥ pañcavidho’pi saṃbhavati, sa ca prāguktapadyayoreva dharmavācakapadamapanīya kamalā yathaiva kamaletyādirītyā padāntaranyāse bhaviṣyati ||
vācakalupto’pi tathā saṃbhavati, yathā
veṅkaṭabhūmidharo’yaṃ veṅkaṭabhūmīdharāyate sumahān | tatra vasan bhagavānapi tatravasadbhagavadujjvalo jayati ||
atra kyaṅsamāsayorvācakaluptaḥ
vṛṣabhādrināthadarśaṃ vṛṣabhācalanāthamagrataḥ paśyan | vitatapramodabāṣpassatataṃ nandāmi mannandam || 139
vṛṣabhādrināthamiva vṛṣabhādrināthadarśaṃ paśyan ahamiva sannandaṃ nandāmi | atra karmakartṛṇamulorvācakaluptaḥ ||
kaustubhati kaustubho’yaṃ lakṣmāṇati lakṣma hārati ca hāraḥ| vakṣasi phaṇigiribandhossindhorduhitā ca sindhuduhitarati || 140 ||
atra dharmavācakayorlopaḥ| ayameva mālārūpo’pyananvayaḥ ||
salilanidhiṃ gāmbhīrye tulayantassanti kati kati na devāḥ | eṣa tu śeṣagirīśaśśeṣagirīśānugāmbhīryaḥ || 141 || atra vācakadharmopamānānāṃ lopaḥ | atra copamānaluptādayo’nye bhedāstvasaṃbhavādacārutvācca nodāhṛtāḥ ||
idaṃ tāvadatrāvadheyam prāyeṇopamādiralaṅkāro yadā samagreṇa vākyena pradhānatayā dhvanyate tadā parāstālaṅkārabhāvo dhvanivyapadeśabhāgbhavati | asmiṃścālaṃkāravyapadeśaḥ kadācidapyalaṅkārabhāvamanāpanneṣu samudgakādyantarnihiteṣu hārakeyūrakaṭakakuṇḍalādiṣvivālaṃkurvāṇahārādigatadharmamātrasaṃsparśanibandhanaḥ | tatrādāvupamādhvanirudāhāryā | eṣā copamā kvacicchabdaśaktimūlānudhvananaviṣayaḥ, kvacidarthaśaktimūlānudhvananaviṣayaḥ ||
tatrādyā yathā
kaṭakojjvalapunnāgo gandharvakulairudīrṇasaubhāgyaḥ |dharaṇībhṛccūḍāmaṇiranaṇīyaśśrīnivāsatāṃ dhatte ||
kaṭakeṣu sānuṣu rājadhānīṣu ca ujjvalāḥ punnāgāḥ taruviśeṣāḥ puṃgajāśca yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘kaṭakaṃ valaye sānau rājadhānīnitambayoḥ’ iti viśvaḥ | gandharvāṇāṃ devayoniviśeṣāṇāṃ turagāṇāṃ ca kulaiḥ | dharaṇībhṛtāṃ girīṇāṃ nṛpāṇāṃ ca cūḍāmaṇiḥ anaṇīyān śrīnivāso yasmin tasya bhāvaṃ anaṇīyasyāḥ śriyaḥ saṃpadaḥ nivāsatāṃ dhatte ||
yathā vā
tāpāturā nikāmaṃ tamasā pratyastadṛṣṭayaḥ sthitvā | daivādvidhūdaye sati nandantitarāṃ dvijāḥ katicideva || 143 || nikāmaṃ tāpaiḥ ādhyātmikādibhiḥ ātapajanitaiśca āturāḥ, tamasā tamoguṇena timireṇa ca pratyastā nirastā dṛṣṭiḥ jñānaṃ cakṣuśca yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ sthitvā kaṃcitkālamiti bhāvaḥ | daivāt bhāgyaviśeṣāt vidhoḥ bhagavataḥ udaye anavaratabhāvanāmūlakapratyakṣatāpattau | pakṣe vidhoḥ candramasaḥ āvirbhāve sati katicideva dvijāḥ bhāgyaśālino viprādaya eva nānye bhāgyadavīyāṃsa iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe cakorādayaḥ khagaviśeṣā eva nandanti atrodāharaṇadvaye’nekārthānāmapi padānāṃ prakaraṇena kṛte’pi śaktisaṃkoce tanmūlakena dhvananena pratīyamānasya mahārājarūpasya khagaviśeṣarūpasya cārthāntarasthāprastutasyābhidhānaṃ mā prasāṅkṣīditi prakṛtāprakṛtayorupamānopameyabhāvaḥ pradhānavākyārthatayā kalpyate | atra śliṣṭānāṃ kaṭakādiśabdānāṃ nitambādiśabdapāravṛttyasahiṣṇutayā śabdaśaktimūlānudhvananaviṣayo’sāvupamā ||
arthaśaktimūlānudhvananaviṣayo yathā
asamānarocirahamityabhimānastava ghanāghana mahīyān | nanu nāmāḍhaukiṣṭhā na manāgapi vā phaṇābhṛdacalaśikhām || 144 ||
yathā vā
ratnākara iti garvaṃ yatnādvijahīhi mama girodanvan | na calasi padātpadaṃ vā nā(kvā)drākṣīśśrīnivāsakośagṛham || 145 ||
atra mūḍhādipadāprayogādasūyāderapratyayātprādhānyenārthaśaktyā upamaiva dhvanyate || evam
gaṅgāṃ kuto jigamiṣasyaṅgātra visṛtvarīmimāṃ svairam | saṅgāhasva vṛṣācalaśṛṅgāradayājharīmimāṃ gāḍham || 146 ||
ityatrāpyarthaśaktimūlopamādhvanirdraṣṭavyā ||
alaṅkāreṇopamādhvaniryathā
phaṇiśikhariṇi vilasantaṃ vīkṣya hariṃ taṃ ramāsamāśliṣṭam | sacapalajaladanirīkṣaṇanirāsthatāṃ dadhrire mayūragaṇāḥ || 147 ||
atra mayūrāṇāṃ savidyujjaladanirīkṣaṇāsthāsambandhe’pi tadasaṃbandhoktirūpātiśayoktyā bhagavataśśrīnivāsasya tadupamā dhvanyate evamupamādhvanidigudāhṛtā ||
athopameyopamādhvanidigudāhriyate
śaradijavikartanasya hi muravairisudarśano dvitīyo’yam | muraripusudarśanasya tu śaraduditavikartano’pi tathā || 148 ||
atra dvitīyaśabdasya sādṛśyaviśiṣṭe śaktyabhāvādvyaktireva ||
yadi tu lakṣaṇā tadedamudāharaṇam
haripadayuganakharekhā śaraduditasudhāmayūkharekhā ca | garvāyete’nyonyaṃ lāvaṇyādhyāpanāya bhṛśamantaḥ || atra haripadayuganakharekhādikartṛkasyānyonyādhyāpanasya bādhāllāvaṇyasaṃkrāntiviśeṣasya lakṣaṇayā pratīyamānasya prayojanaṃ svaprayojyānyonyopamānopameyabhāva iti dhyeyam ||
athānanvayadhvanirudāhriyate
darśaṃdarśaṃ samyaṅmarśaṃmarśaṃ mayā’dya viśrāntam | bhinnatayā pannagapatisannaganetustulā yato naikṣi ||
atra bhinnatayā tulā naikṣītyanena abhinnatayā aikṣīti dhvanyate ||
yathā vā
kati kati na santi girayaḥ katamo dadhre’cyutena caramāṅge | prādurakarocca kamalāṃ yenānye mandarasya sadṛśāssyuḥ || 151 ||
atra ko vā mandaretaro mahīdharo bhagavatā caramāṅge dhṛtaḥ ko vā śriyamabdheḥ prādurabhāvayat yenānye girayo mandarasadṛśāssyurityarthena tasya punarbhagavatā caramāṅgadhṛtatve śrīprādurbhāvayitṛtve ca sa eva tattulya ityartho’nanvayātmā mandaranirupamatvaparyavasāyī anyapadamahimnā vyajyate ||
evaṃ
paramaṃ padamapi hitvā ciramavasatkasya śikhariṇaśiśakhare | paramassaramassa pumān phaṇidhara yasmiṃstavopamāṃ vidmaḥ || 152 ||
ityatrāpyananvayadhvanirdraṣṭavyaḥ | atra ‘māyāvī paramānandaṃ tyaktvā vaikuṇṭhamuttamam’ ityādyartho’nusaṃhitaḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre ananvayasarastṛtīyaḥ
<5>
athāsamālaṃkārasaraḥ
upamāyāssarvathaiva niṣedho’sama ucyate || 24 ||
sarvathaivopamāniṣedho’samākhyo’laṃkāraḥ | ayaṃ cānanvaye vyaṅgyopi taccamatkārānuguṇatayā rūpakadīpakādāvupameva na bhajate pṛthagalaṃkāravyapadeśyatām | vācyatāyāṃ tu svātantryeṇa camatkāritayā pṛthagvyapadeśabhāgbhavati ||
yathā
sabrahmacāri kiṃcinna brahmāpīkṣituṃ tava paṭīyān | yasya tava nissamatvaṃ nissaṃdigdhaṃ śrutirbravīti hare || 153 ||
yathā vā
aiśvaryavīryadhairyasthairyaudāryādimairguṇairbhagavan | kastvattulanākalanāyogyassaṃbhāvyatāṃ kadā kva nu vā || nāsīnnāsti ca bhavato nātha samaṃ vastu vedhasassarge | agre’pi yathāpūrvaṃ sraṣṭā’yaṃ kiṃ tadā’pi tallabhyam || 155 ||
tadvastu naiva sasṛje na sṛjyate srakṣyate’pi viśvasṛjā | yatra bhavataḥ prarohedupamālavaleśagandhopi || 156 ||
yathā vā
asti samānaṃ śaurervastviti mūrkho bravīti cedbrūtām | yatra kva vā samānaṃ labheta kimasāvaṭannapi jaganti || 157 ||
so’sau mūrkhaḥ jaganti sarvāṇyaṭannapi yatra kva vā mānaṃ bhagavatsamānavastusattāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ labheta kiṃ na labhetaivetyarthaḥ | yadvā mānaṃ pūjāṃ labheta kiṃ, bhagavata itarasādharmyaṃ vaktuḥ kvāpi pūjyatā na syādeveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe asau mūrkhaḥ samānaṃ bhagavatassadṛśaṃ vastu yatra kva vā na labhetaivetyarthaḥ | atra śleṣacamatkāraḥ pūrvodāharaṇebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
tvaṃ sadvitīya eva hi sṛjasi jagatpāsi saṃhariṣyasi ca | tadapi śrutirabjekṣaṇa vadati tvāmadvitīyameva bata || 158 || sadvitīyaḥ dvitīyavastunā sahitaḥ cidacidviśiṣṭa evetyarthaḥ | dvitīyayā sahadharmiṇyā śriyā sahita evetyapyarthaḥ | śrīviśiṣṭasyaiva jagajjanmādikāraṇatvātsadvitīya eva sṛjasītyādyuktiḥ | tadapi tathā’pi śrutiḥ ‘ekamevādvitīyam’ iti śrutiḥ advitīyaṃ dvitīyavasturahitaṃ asadṛśamiti tu vāstavārthaḥ ||
yatha vā
asadṛśa iti nigadaṃstvāṃ jano’bhinandyeta sadṛśamāheti | sadṛgasti taveti vadanna sadṛgiti janairvinindyate citram || 159 ||
he bhagavan! tvāṃ asadṛśa iti vadan janaḥ sadṛśamāha anurūpaṃ yuktamāheti janairabhinandyate | tavāsadṛśatvasya vastusato yathāvaduktatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tava sadṛk sadṛśaḥ astīti vadan janaḥ na sadṛk sadṛśo na yogyo na ananurūpārthakathanāditi bhāvaḥ | dṛśā saha vartata iti sadṛk na andha eva jñānahīna evetyarthaḥ | yadyasya dṛṣṭirevābhaviṣyattarhi tava sadṛgastīti nāvadiṣyadeveti bhāvaḥ | iti janairvinindyate, citraṃ asadṛśaṃ vaktussadṛśavaktṛtvena sadṛśaṃ vakturasadṛktvena ca kathanamāścaryāvahamiti bhāvaḥ | anayorudāharaṇayośśleṣamūlakavirodhasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ pūrvebhyo vailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyam ||
evam
na kimapi kaustubhatejassamamabhavadbhavati vā’tha bhavitā vā | yatra kila mitraśaśadharakṛśānutārāssphuliṅgakākārāḥ || 160 || nāsīdasti bhaviṣyati vidhātṛsarge kva vā’pi mādṛkṣaḥ | tvayi sati śubhaguṇavipaṇau yo’nyaṃ pipaṇāyiṣāmi kṛpaṇamanāḥ || 161 ||
ityādāvapi kāvyaliṅgādisaṃkīrṇatā draṣṭavyā | sarveṣveteṣūdāharaṇeṣu sarvathaivopamānaniṣedhādasamālaṃkāraḥ, kvācitkassadṛśapratiṣedha upamānaluptāyā viṣayaḥ | asya tvātyantikasadṛśapratipedha iti nopamānaluptopamayā cāritārthyayam | sarvātmanaivopamānapratiṣedhena sādṛśyasyāpratiṣṭhānānnātropamāgandhopi |
nanvasamālaṃkāradhvananenaiva camatkāropapatterananvayasya pṛthagalaṃkāratā kathamiti cet satyaṃ, dīpakāderapyupamābhivyaktyaiva camatkāropapattau kathaṃnāma pṛthagalaṃkāratvamiti tulyam | na ca dīpakādāvupamāvyaṅgyatve’pi guṇībhāvāt prakṛte tu svasādṛśyasya svasminnatitamāṃ tiraskāreṇāsamālaṃkārasyaiva mukhyatayā dhvananādvaiṣamyamiti vācyam | yathā hi dīpakasamāsoktyādau guṇībhūtavyaṅgyasattve’pyalaṃkāratvaṃ na hīyate evamananvaye pradhānavyaṅgyasattve’pi na kiṃcidviruddham | ananvayaśarīrasya sādṛśyamātrasya vācyatvena vācyālaṅkāravyapadeśo’pi mukhya eva | dīpakādyalaṅkārakāvye guṇībhūtasya vyaṅgyasya sattvādastunāma guṇībhūtavyaṅgyatvam | dhvanitvaṃ punarna kvāpyalaṅkṛtikāvye dṛṣṭamiti cet paryāyoktasādṛśyamūlāprastutapraśaṃsādikāvye dhvanitvasya sphuṭatvāt, iti vadanti ||
prāñco nedamalaṅkārāntaramityeva manvate ||
ayamasamālaṃkāro vyajyamāno’pi sambhavati | yathā sarvajñopi girīśastava sadṛśaṃ vastu kimapi nijigadiṣuḥ| śrīśa tadapratipatteradyāpi sthāṇutāṃ na vijahāti || 162 ||
atra yastvāṃ tuṣṭūṣussarvajño girīśo’pyetāvantaṃ samayaṃ tvattulyavastvapratibhānāt sthāṇurevāsīt so’gre’pyevameva syāt | anyathābhāve mānābhāvāditi sarvathaiva tvatsadṛśaṃ vastu naivāstīti gamyate | evaṃca vyajyamāno’pyatrāsamālaṃkāraḥ pradhānībhūtabhagavatstutyutkarṣopaskārakatayā’laṃkāra eva ||
mukhyatayā dhvanyamāno yathā
darvīkarādriśekhara sarvaṃ padamarthavattvayā vyaraci | tulyābhidhāyi tu padaṃ kālatritaye’pi nārthavadaho te ||
tvayā sarvaṃ padaṃ suptiṅantātmakaṃ indrādisthānaṃ ca arthavat abhidheyavat vibhavavacca vyaraci | taṃ tava tulyābhidhāyi sadṛśārthābhidhāyi padaṃ tu kālatritaye’pi nārthavat na dhanavat nābhidheyavacca kasmādityāścaryam ||
yathā vā
gaganalikhiteṣu pannaganagavaradhaureya citrapurūṣeṣu | sa gaṇayitavyaḥ puruṣo’nuguṇo yastvadupamālavasyāpi || 164 ||
anayorudāharaṇayorasamatvaṃ prādhānyena vyajyate | ayaṃ kvacidupamānasya niṣedhāt, kvacicca sākṣādupamāyā eva | tatrādya upadarśitaḥ || dvitīyo yathā
antarbahirapi kamalākānta samantāttvayaiva paripūrṇam | jagadidamakhilaṃ bhavato jagadīśa tulā padaṃ kva nidadhīta || 165 ||
he jagadīśa! bhavatastulā upamā kartrī kva padaṃ nidadhīta | sarvasya jagatastvayaivāntarbahiśca paripūrṇatvāttvadupamāyāḥ padanyāsasyāpi nāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ ||
ityasamālaṃkārasaraścaturthaḥ
<6>
athodāharaṇālaṃkārasaraḥ
sāmānyoktasubodhāya tadekāṃśanirūpaṇāt |
ukte tayoravayavāvayavitva udāhṛtiḥ || 25 ||
sāmānyena nirūpitasyārthasya sukhapratipattaye tadekadeśaṃ nirūpya tayossāmānyaikadeśayoravayavāvayavibhāve ukte udāhṛtiḥ udāharaṇaṃ nāmālaṃkāraḥ | atrārthāntaranyāsavāraṇāyokta iti | vacanaṃ ca ivayathānidarśanadṛṣṭāntapramāṇodāharaṇasākṣyādiśabdaiḥ kāvyeṣu vyaktam | arthāntaranyāse hyavayavāvayavibhāvo vyaṅgyaḥ | na ca ivayathāśabdayossādṛśyavacanayoravayavāvayavibhāve viśeṣasāmānyātmake nāsti vṛttiriti vācyam | lakṣaṇāyāssāmrājyāt | anyathā hyutprekṣābodhakatā’pi durghaṭā syāt | atrālaṃkāre sāmānyaviśeṣayorviśeṣaṇāni pṛthagvā sakṛdvā nirdiśyanta iti dhyeyam || yathā
malinamapi guṇamahimnā mahati pade vastu mānyatāmeti | mṛganābhissurabhitayā bhagavata iva niṭilaphalakasīmāyām || 166 ||
na cātra malinavastumṛganābhyorupamā śakyā vaktum | tayossāmānyaviśeṣabhāvena sādṛśyasyānunmeṣāt | tathā sati tu ivādiśabda iva sadṛśādiśabdā apyalaṃkāre’smin prayujyeran | atra mṛganābhisurabhitābhagavadvadanāni malinavastuguṇamahimamahāpadānāṃ viśeṣāḥ | atra padye ivaśabdenāvayavāvayavibhāvoktiḥ ||
yathā vā
saṃsargānmahatāmiha sarvajñenāpi lālyate kaścit | garuḍadhvajapadasaṅgādgaṅgāpūro yathā purāmariṇā ||
atra garuḍadhvajapadasaṅgādayo mahatsaṃsargādīnāṃ viśeṣāḥ | atra yathāśabdenāvayavāvayavibhāvoktiḥ ||
yathā vā
lokottaravastu vahanneko loke mahonnatiṃ dhatte | kākodaraśikharīndro nidarśanaṃ bibhradatra lokeśam ||
eka ityasya padārtha iti viśeṣyamupaskāryam | evaṃ pūrvapadye kaścidityasyāpi | atra nidarśanaśabdena ||
yathā vā
yatra kva vā’stu jātastejasvī bhavati bhūṣaṇaṃ mahatām | udadhau jātaśśrīpatyurassthitaḥ kaustubho’tra dṛṣṭāntaḥ || 169 ||
atra udadhijātakaustubhau yatra kva vā jātatejasvinorviśeṣau | evamagre’pi draṣṭavyam | atra dṛṣṭāntaśabdena ||
yathā vā
uttamacetanasaṃśrayadattātiśayo girīśatāṃ dhatte | kaścidacetanabhāve’pyāścaryamiha pramāṇamahiśailaḥ ||
uttamacetanaḥ śreṣṭho jīvaḥ, anyatra bhagavānityarthaḥ | girīśatāṃ vāgviṣaye īśvaratvaṃ, anyatra śailendratām | avidyamānā cetanā jñānaṃ yasya tasya bhāve ajñatve ityarthaḥ | anyatra cetanabhinnatve gireracetanatvādityarthaḥ | atra pramāṇaśabdena ||
yathā vā
tvāmantarnidadhānaśśrīman kheṭopi bhavati tejasvī | ambujakadambabandhorbimba iha syādudāharaṇam || 171 ||
kheṭaḥ kutsitaḥ ‘kupūyakutsitāvadyakheṭagarhyāṇakāssamāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe khe aṭatīti kheṭaḥ gaganacārītyarthaḥ | ‘tatpuruṣe kṛtiḥ’ ityaluk | atrodāharaṇaśabdenāvayavāvayavibhāvakathanam ||
yathā vā
api bahuṣu vastuṣu mahānādatte svānurūpavastve va | udadhibhaveṣviha kamalāmupādadāno’tra mādhavassākṣī || 172 ||
ādau tāmasahitatāṃ dadhadapi vastu tvadāśritaṃ syāccet | ante rasavadbhavitā haridayite tatra sākṣi tāmarasam || 173 ||
ādau prathamaṃ tāmasebhyo hitatāṃ dadhadapi | pakṣe tāmetyābhyāṃ varṇābhyāṃ sahitatāṃ dadhadityarthaḥ | ante rasavat brahmānubhavavat ‘raso vai saḥ’ ityādiśruteḥ | rasaśabdo brahmaparaḥ | pakṣe ante caramabhāge rasābhyāṃ rephasavarṇābhyāṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | atra padyadvaye’pi sākṣiśabdenāvayavāvayavibhāvavacanam ||
ivayathādiśabdaprayoge sāmānyārthaprādhānyaṃ vākyaikyam | nidarśanadṛṣṭāntādiśabdaprayoge tu viśeṣaprādhānyaṃ vākyabhedaśca viśaṣaḥ | prāñcastu ”nāyamalaṃkāro’tiriktaḥ, upamayaiva gatārthatvāt| na ca sāmānyaviśeṣayorasādṛśyānullāsātkathamupameti vācyam | ‘nirviśeṣa na sāmānyam’ iti sāmānyasya yatkiñcidviśeṣaṃ vinā prakṛtatvāyogāt | tādṛśaviśeṣamādāya viśeṣāntarasya sādṛśyollāse bādhakābhāvādivādibhirāmukhe pratīyamānasyāpi sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvasya pariṇāme sādṛśya eva viśrānteḥ” ityāhuḥ ||
nanvevamudāharaṇālaṃkārābhyupagame sāmānyasya viśeṣeṇa samarthanaṃ nārthāntaranyāsabhedaśśaknuyādbhavitum, anenaiva caritārthatvāditi vācyam | tatra ivavāyathādiśabdaprayogābhāvasyaiveto vailakṣaṇyāt evamapi sa vācakābhāvādārtha udāharaṇālaṃkāra evāstu na tvarthāntaranyāsabheda iti cet, idamastyanayorvailakṣaṇyaṃ sāmānyārthasamarthakasya viśeṣavākyārthasya dvayī hi gatiḥ | anuvā dyāṃśamātre viśeṣatvam | vidheyāṃśastu sāmānyagata evetyekā | anuvādyavidheyobhayāṃśe’pi viśeṣatvamityaparā | tatrādyā udāharaṇālaṅkārasya viṣayaḥ | dvitīyā tvarthāntaranyāsabhedasya | evaṃ ca ‘mṛganābhissurabhitayā bhagavata iva niṭilaphalakasīmāyām’ ityudāharaṇālaṅkāragate viśeṣe mānyatāmetīti prācīnasāmānyārthagataiva yathoktarūpeṇa kriyā vidheyā | ‘bhagavadvadane bibharti tilakatvam’ iti turīyapādapāṭhenārthāntaranyāsagate tu pṛthagupāttaviśeṣarūpeṇa | evaṃ ‘yatra kvavā’stujātastejasvī bhavati bhūṣaṇaṃ mahatām’ ityādyudāharaṇālaṅkāraślokeṣvapi ‘yadayaṃ janimānudadhau hṛdaye pracakāsti kaustubho’cyuta te’ ityādyuttarārdhapāṭhe udāharaṇārthāntaranyāsaviśeṣayorvailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyamiti | yadi cāyamalpo viśeṣo nārthāntaranyāsaviśeṣasyodāharaṇalaṅkārātpṛthagalaṅkāratāmīṣṭe sādhayituṃ, api tu tadviśeṣatāmityucyeta, tadā udaharaṇālaṅkāro’rthāntaranyāsasya, prativastūpamā dṛṣṭāntasya, atiśayoktī rūpakasya ca viśeṣaḥ, upamaiva cārthī smaraṇabhrāntimatsandehā ityapi suvacaṃ syāt, tatrāpi viśeṣasyālpatvāt | upamayaivemaṃ gatārthaṃ manvānāḥ prāñcastu viśeṣeṇa sāmānyasya samarthanamarthāntaranyāsaṃ vinā’nyatra neṣṭe praveṣṭumityabhiprayantīti dik ||
ityalakāramaṇihāre udāharaṇālaṅkārasaraḥ pañcamaḥ
<7>
atha pratīpālaṅkārasaraḥ
pratīpamupamānasya varṇyā cedupameyatā || 26 ||
prasiddhopamānavaiparītyena varṇyamānamaupamyaṃ pratīpam | tadvaiparītyaṃ ca tadupamānopameyayorupameyopamānatvakalpanayā | atra prasiddhaupamye yadupameyaṃ tasyaivopamānatvādādhikyasya yaccopamānaṃ tasyopameyatvānnyūnatvasya ca pratyayaḥ phalam | idameva caupamyasyāviśeṣe’pyupamālaṅkārādasya vailakṣaṇye bījam | nanvevamaupamyasyopamānopameyasādhāraṇye’pi yadekasyādhikyamanyasya nyūnatvaṃ ca gamyata ityetatkuta iti cedavadhehi upamāne hi sādhāraṇadharmasambandho’nūdya upameye vidhīyata iti tāvatsarvasaṃpratipannam | vidhyanuvādau ca sādhyatvasiddhatvābhyām | te ca krameṇopameyopamānayornyūnatvādhikye prayojayete | loke’pi niścitarājabhāvo yathā mānyate tathā nāniścitarājabhāva iti vyaktameva | te ca sādhyatvasiddhatve vaktṛvivakṣādhīne iti nātra doṣaḥ | atra pañcavidhe pratīpe vakṣyamāṇe ādyeṣu triṣūpamitikriyāniṣpattirasti | anyatra tu sā neti bhedaḥ ||
yathā
jalpatu jaradgavī hiraṇyavarṇāṃ śaraṇyadayitatatame | śucitaptaṃ yadi tatsyātsuciraṃ tvadvarṇatā bhajetāpi || 174 ||
jaradgavī śrutiḥ jīrṇā gaurityapi gamyate | śucitaptaṃ grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyakṛtatapaḥ | yadvā śucīti bhinnaṃ padam | śuci śuddhaṃ taptaṃ tapasvi cetyarthaḥ | agnau puṭapākena śodhitamiti tu hṛdayam | atra pūrvārdhopamāgamyamupamānatāprayuktaṃ suvarṇavarṇādhikyaṃ tiraskṛtya dvitīyārdhe pratīpaṃ śrīlakṣmīvarṇasyopamānatvaprayuktamādhikyaṃ gamayati | śucitāpaṃ vinā pratīpamapi durlabham, upamā tu svapne’pi dūrato’pāsteti jaradgavī jalpatvityābhyāṃ gamyate | śaraṇyadayitatame iti śriyassaṃbuddhiḥ || yathā vā
ādityavarṇatāṃ te śrutissvatantrā bravītu jagadamba | udayenmahātapaśśrīssa yadi tvatpādavarṇa eva syāt || 175 ||
śrutiḥ,’ādityavarṇe tapaso’dhijātaḥ iti śrutiḥ | saḥ ādityaḥ mahatī tapaśśrīḥ ātapaśrīśca yasya sa tathoktaḥ udayet | abhyudayamāvirbhāvaṃ ca prāpnuyādyadi tadā’pi tvatpādavarṇa eva tava caturthāṃśavarṇa iva varṇo yasya sa tathokta eva syāt, na tu paripūrṇatvadvarṇa iti bhāvaḥ | tvaccaraṇāruṇavarṇa iti tu hṛdayam | atrāpi pūrvārdhopamāgamyamupamānatāprayuktādityavarṇādhikyaṃ tiraskṛtya dvitīyārdhe pratīpaṃ jagajjananīvarṇasya upamānatāprayuktamādhikyaṃ gamayati | mahātapaśrīsāhityenoditatvamantarā pratīpamapi durghaṭaṃ, kuto vā upamāvārteti śrutissvatantrā bravītvityanena gamyate | tvatpādavarṇa eveti śleṣo’pyasyaivārthasyopaskurute ||
avarṇyasyopameyasya lābhādvarṇyatiraskṛtiḥ | lābhādvarṇyopameyasya syādanyānādaraśca tat || 27 ||
advitīyaguṇaśālitayā varṇyamānasyānyatra svasādṛśyamakṣamamāṇasya upameyaṃ kaṃcitpradarśya tāvatā tasya tiraskāro dvitīyaṃ pratīpaṃ pūrvasmādapi vicchittiviśeṣaśāli | atyutkṛṣṭatayā kvacidapyupamānabhāvamasahamānasyāvarṇyasya varṇyamupameyaṃ parikalpya tāvatā tasya tiraskāraḥ pūrvapratīpavaiparītyena tṛtīyaṃ pratīpam ||
yathā
ahameva pālayitrī jagatāmiti dṛpya mā jaladhiputri | phaṇigiripatihṛdi dhātrīsadṛśī karuṇā tavāsti śubhadātrī || 176 ||
tvamanantahṛdyabhūṣaṇamahamiti garvaṃ jahīhi dhāmanidhe | saṃkalayasi sadṛśaṃ tava veṅkaṭanāthasya maṇimurasi na kimu || 177 ||
atrādyodāharaṇe tava sadṛśītyupamitikriyāniṣpattāvapi karuṇāyā upameyīkaraṇena varṇyāyā lakṣmyā anādaraḥ | dvitīyodāharaṇe tu anantahṛdyabhūṣaṇatayā kvacidapyupamānabhāvamakṣamamāṇasyāvarṇyasya dhāmanidhestādṛśaṃ śrīnivāsorassthakaustubhamupameyaṃ parikalpya tasyānādara uktaḥ | anantasya gaganasya śrīnivāsasya ca hṛdyaṃ manoharaṃ hṛdayasthaṃ ca bhūṣaṇamiti śleṣopaskṛtamidam | anayorbhedayoḥ phalaṃ sphuṭameveti na vivecanīyamasti ||
kvacidanādarāpratītāvapyupameyasyopamānatākalpanamātreṇa pratīpaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
karacaraṇanayanasadṛśaṃ sarasiruhaṃ paramapuruṣa tava kamalā | karabhūṣaṇayati gṛhayati sarayati saṃśrayati cāpi tannāma || 178 ||
atrāruṇyasaugandhyamārdavādimahimnā prasiddhopamānabhāvasya sarasiruhasyopameyatvamātramuktam | na tu tasyānādaraḥ | upamāyāmivāsminnapi pūrṇaluptādayo bhedāssambhavanto buddhimadbhirunneyāḥ || upamāyā aniṣpattirvarṇyenānyasya cāpi tat ||
varṇyeneti tṛtīyāyā nirūpitatvamarthaḥ | varṇyanirūpitā anyasyāvarṇyasya yā upamā tasyā aniṣpattivacanaṃ caturthaṃ pratīpam | ādyayoravarṇyasyopameyatvakalpanayā tṛtīye varṇyasyopameyatvakalpanayā copamitikrayāniṣpattirasti | atra tu sā nāstīti pūrvebhya utkarṣaśālīdam ||
yathā
api puṇḍarīkamakṣṇorupamā syāduragaśailanātha tava | sāhasikī manye śrutirāha yathā puṇḍarīkamevamiti || 179 ||
he uragaśailanātha tava akṣṇoḥ puṇḍarīkamapi upamā syāditi kākuḥ | apītyanena puṇḍarīkasya kādācitkamlānyādinā arāmaṇīyakaṃ vyañjitam | tarhyantarādityavidyāyāṃ ‘tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī’ iti śrutiḥ kathamāha puṇḍarīkasādharmyaṃ bhagavallocanayorityata āha sāhasikīti | yā śrutiḥ ‘tasya yathā kapyāsam’ ityādiḥ puṇḍarīkasādṛśyamāha, manye sā sāhasikīti | sahasā vartate sāhasikī ‘ojassaho’mbhasā vartate’ iti ṭhak | ṭiḍḍheti ṅīp | netre nimīlya vadatīti bhāvaḥ | atra api puṇḍarīramakṣṇorupamā syādityanena upamā na sambhavatīti gamyate | sāhasikītyuttarārdhamamumevārthamupodbalayati ||
upamānasya kaimarthyaṃ pratīpaṃ pañcamaṃ viduḥ || 28 || kaḥ arthaḥ prayojanaṃ yasya sa kimarthaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ kaimarthyaṃ nirarthakatvam | upameyasyaivopamānakāryadhūrvahatayā upamānakaimarthyamupamānaprātilomyātpañcamaṃ pratīpam ||
yathā
guṇavaśitaparabrahmaṇi phaṇiśikhariṇi viracitādbhutānande | nivasantaḥ khalu santaḥ paramavyomnā’pi kimiti manyante || 180 || |
yathā vā
padmāvatyāḥ pāṇiṃ parigṛhṇānaḥ phaṇābhṛdacaleśaḥ | līlābjamapi nirāstho nirāsthadetatkimarthamiti || 181 ||
atra krameṇa upameyayoḥ phaṇiśikharipadmāvatīpāṇyoreva niratiśayānandadānādikāryadhūrvahatayā upamānabhūtayoḥ paramavyomalīlākamalayoḥ kaimarthyamuktam ||
tadevaṃ pañcavidhaṃ pratīpaṃ prācāmanurodhānnirūpitam | vastutastu ādyāstrayo’pyupamāyāmevāntargatā bhedāḥ | turīyastvanuktavaidharmye vyatireke | pañcamastu keṣāṃcidākṣepaḥ | tathāhi niṣpadyamānaṃ sundaraṃ vā sādṛśyamupamā | na hyādye pratīpe mukhamivakamalamityādau sādṛśyasyāniṣyattirasaundaryaṃ vā’sti | yenopamāto bahiṣkṛtissyāt | saundaryaviśeṣasya tvayā’pyabhyupagamāt | viśeṣasya sāmānyānivārakatvāt | na ca prasiddhakamalādipratiyogikameva sādṛśyamupametyasti vedānuśāsanam | na copamāviruddhavācinaḥ pratīpaśabdasya mahimnaiva tathāvidhaṃ sādṛśyamupameti śakyaṃ vaditum | upamāviśeṣaviruddhavācakatvenāpi tadupapatteḥ | evaṃcādyaṃ pratīpaṃ prasiddhopamāvadupamāviśeṣa eva | ata eva dvitīyatṛtīyāvapi bhedāvupamāviśeṣāveva, upamānopameyayostiraskārastūpamāntarādvailakṣaṇyaṃ prayojayenna tūpamāsāmānyāt | tadanusyūtatvenaiva tatpratīteḥ | na hi śarkarā mādhuryātiśayena pārthivāntarādvilakṣaṇetyapārthivī bhavet | api ca yadyupamānopameyayostiraskāro’laṅkāratāprayojakassyāttarhi tatpuraskāro’pi tathā syāt ||
yathā
nanu pātakino vayamityanutāpaṃ mā sma durjanāḥ kuruta | kati kati na bhavādṛkṣāḥ kalāvihāsmṛtasarīsṛpagirīśāḥ || 182 ||
atraupamyapradarśanasya nopamānatiraskāraḥ phalaṃ, tasya dṛptatvādyavivakṣaṇāt | kintu tadanutāpaśāntiḥ, evaṃ ca phalavailakṣaṇyamātreṇālaṅkārāntaratvaṃ bruvāṇenāsyāpyalaṅkāratvamabhyupeyaṃ syāt pratīpaṣaṣṭhaprabhedatvaṃ vā | kiṃca tvaduktapratīpabhedānāmapi parasparavailakṣaṇyena pṛthagalaṅkāratā syānna tu pratīpabhedatvaṃ, pratīpasya sakalaprabhedasādhāraṇasāmānyalakṣaṇābhāvāt | anyatamatvaṃ tu dūṣaṇasahasrakabalitatvādalakṣaṇameva | upamālakṣaṇaṃ tu sarvasādhāra ṇam | pañcamaprabhedastu yeṣāṃ matenākṣepasteṣāmastunāmālaṅkāraḥ | turīyasya tūktaiva gatiḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre pratīpālaṅkārasaraḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ
<8>
atha rūpakam

yatsyādviṣayirūpeṇa viṣayasyoparañjanam |
rūpakaṃ tadvidhā’bhedatādrūpyāvasitaṃ viduḥ || 29 ||
viṣayi upamānabhūtaṃ candrādi, viṣayastadupameyabhūtaṃ varṇanīyaṃ mukhādi | viṣayiṇo rūpeṇa yadviṣayasyoparañjanaṃ uparañjanamivoparañjanaṃ svoparaktabuddhiviṣayīkaraṇamiti yāvat | tadrūpakaṃ, anyarūpeṇa rūpavattvaniṣpādanāt | tacca kvacitprasiddhaviṣayyabhede paryavasitaṃ, kvacidbhede pratīyamāna eva tadīyadharmāropamātre paryavasitam | tataśca rūpakaṃ tāvaddvividham abhedarūpakaṃ tādrūpyarūpakaṃ ceti | tadetadāha

ārope satyabhedasyābhedarūpakamucyate |
tādrūpyarūpakaṃ tatsyāttādrūpyasya sa cedbhavet || 30 ||
tādrūpyasya saḥ āropo bhavedyadi tādrūpyarūpakamityarthaḥ | abhedāropaśca ‘mukhaṃ candraḥ’ ityādāvāhāryacandrābhedaniścayaḥ| tādrūpyāropastu ‘mukhamaparaścandraḥ’ ityatrāpara iti bhedasya sphuṭatayā mukhe candrābhedāropāsaṃbhavāccandrakāryakāritvarūpatādrūpyasyāhāryaniścayaḥ | na ca tasya mukhe sattvātkathamāropa iti vācyam | candrakāryasajātīyakāryakāritvasyaiva mukhe sattvāt | yadyapi nāmārthayorabhedānvayānurodhādihāpi candrapadalakṣitasya tatkāryakāriṇo’bhedasaṃsargeṇaiva mukhe’nvayādabhedarūpakameva, tathā’pyupamānatāvacchedakarūpeṇābhedabhāne evābhedarūpakatvaṃ, tatkāryakāritvādirūpeṇābhedabhāne tu tādrūpyarūpakatvamityāhuḥ ||
taddvayaṃ ca tridhā’’dhikyanyūnatvānubhayoktibhiḥ ||
dvividhamapi rūpakaṃ pratyekaṃ trividham | prasiddhaviṣayyādhikyavarṇanena tannyūnatāvarṇanena ādhikyanyūnatārāhityoktyā cetyevaṃ rūpakaṃ ṣaḍvidham | tatrābhedarūpakeṣvadhikābhedarūpakaṃ lakṣayati

pūrvāvasthāta āropyamāṇasyādhikyavarṇanam |
varṇyāyāṃ cedavasthāyāmadhikābhedarūpakam || 32
yathā
asurāsuravikṣubhitādahirājadhareśajalanidheruditam | vitaratyajarāmaratāmamitāṃ smitamamṛtametadanusaratām || 184 ||
atrāhirājadhareśajalanidheriti jaladhinā saha śrīnivāsasyābhedaṃ pratipādya asurāsuravikṣubhitādityanena viśeṣaṇena jaladheḥ, evaṃ smitamamṛtamityamṛtena saha varṇanīyasya smitasya tādātmyaṃ pratipādya amitāmajarāmaratāṃ vitaratītyanavacchinnājarāmaratvadānokttyā amṛtasya ca pūrvāvasthāta utkarṣavibhāvanādadhikābhedarūpakadvayaṃ paraṃparitam | atra prāthamikaṃ samastaṃ rūpakaṃ, dvaitīyīkaṃ tu vyastamiti viśeṣaḥ |
nyūnatā prāgavasthātaścennyūnābhedarūpakam ||
yathā
anupadamanupamaphaṇipatigirimaṇiguṇagarimamasṛṇitā kavitā | trayyeva pauruṣeyī dhunoti duritaṃ tanoti sukhamamitam || 185 ||
atra varṇanīyāyāḥ bhagavadguṇaviṣayakārvāktanakavitāyāḥ trayyeveti prasiddhatrayītādātmyaṃ prakāśya puruṣeyītyapauruṣeyatvavaikalyapratipādanānnyūnābhedarūpakam | puruṣeṇa kṛtā pauruṣeyī ‘sarvapuruṣābhyāṃ ṇaḍhañau’ ityatra ‘puruṣādvadhavikārasamūhatenakṛteṣviti vaktavyam’ iti ḍhañi ‘ṭiḍḍha’ iti ṅīp | bhāṣyakāraprayogāttenetyasya dvandvamadhye niveśaḥ | nyūnatāpratipādanamapyabhedadārḍhyāpādakatvāccamatkāri ||
ādhikyanyūnatābhāve’nubhayābhedarūpakam || 34 ||
yathā
ṣaṭpadamantrodgītaṃ sakamalarekhaṃ rasodayaniketam | kalayāmi śrīśaraṇaṃ nalinaṃ sākṣādramāpateścaraṇam ||
ṣaṭpadamantreṇa ‘ṣaṭpado’yaṃ dvikhaṇḍaḥ’ ityuktamantraratnena udgītaṃ, tatra bhagavaccaraṇayorupāyatāpratipādanāt | anyatra ṣaṭpadānāṃ bhramarāṇāṃ mantraiḥ guñjitairiti yāvat, udgītam | sakamalarekhaṃ kamalākārarekhābhisahitaṃ padmapaṅktisahitaṃ ca | rasāyāḥ bhūmeḥ udayaniketaṃ janigṛhaṃ ‘padbhyāṃ bhūmiḥ’ iti śruteḥ | anyatra rasasya marandasya udayaniketaṃ, śriyaḥ saukumāryādilakṣmyāḥ śrīdevyāśca śaraṇaṃ āśrayaṃ janmaniketanaṃ ca | atra sākṣāditi viśeṣaṇena nalinatādātmyaṃ bhagavaccaraṇasya pratipādya ṣaṭpadamantrodgītamityādiviśeṣaṇaistasya pūrvāvasthāta utkarṣāpakarṣayoravarṇanādanubhayābhedarūpakam ||
tādrūpyarūpake’pyevaṃ traividhyaṃ sadbhirūhyatām || 35
tatrādhikatādrūpyarūpakaṃ yathā
paramandarakṣobhaṃ na bhajati jātvapi bhujaṅgagirinetuḥ | svāntasaritkānto’smin jayati samudro budhānna rañjayati || 187 ||
jātvapi paramaṃ adhikaṃ dareṇa bhayena saṃkṣobham | pakṣe paraṃ atiśayitaṃ mandareṇa giriṇā saṃkṣobhaṃ mathanaṃ bhujaṅgagirinetuḥ svāntaṃ saritkāntaḥ na bhajati, asmin svāntasaritkānte jayati sati samudraḥ prasiddhassindhuḥ budhān devān viduṣaśca na rañjayati atathāvidhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra svāntasaritkānta ityatra saritkāntapadaṃ saritkāntakāryakāritvamātraparaṃ, samudro budhānna rañjayatīti prasiddhasamudrādbhedasya vivakṣitatvāt | tathāca paramandarasaṃkṣobhaṃ na bhajatītyanena svāntasaritkāntasyotkarṣapratipādanātprasiddhasamudre tadvailakṣaṇyasya sphuṭatayā ādhikyaṃ varṇitamityadhikatādrūpyarūpakam ||
yathā vā
garjanviṣaṃ pradatte parjanyaśśītako malinavaktraḥ | phaṇigiripatiparjanyaḥ pradadātyamṛtamapi sukhasukhenaiva || 188 ||
parjanyaḥ śītakaḥ alasaḥ śītaṃ karotīti śītakaḥ ‘śītoṣṇābhyāṃ kāriṇi’ iti kan | ‘ālasyaśśītako’lasaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | anyatra śītaṃ kaṃ udakaṃ yasya sa iti vigrahaḥ | malinaṃ krodhadūṣitatayā malīmasaṃ, pakṣe nisargato nīlaṃ mukhaṃ vadanaṃ agrabhāgaśca yasya sa tathoktaḥ | garjan tarjayan | pakṣe stanan san | viṣaṃ garalaṃ jalaṃ ca pradatte | evamalasaḥ krodhamalinamukho garjannapyantato viṣameva pradatte na tūpādeyaṃ vastu | phaṇigiripatiparjanyastu amṛtaṃ sudhāmapi niśśreyasamapi ca sukhasukhenaiva analaso’malinamukho’tarjayannakleśenaiveti bhāvaḥ | pradadāti | “akṛchre priyasukhayoranyatarasyām’ iti dvirvacane karmadhārayavadbhāvātsubluk | atra prasiddhaparjanyāpekṣayā bhagavatparjanyasyādhikyamuktam ||
nyūnatādrūpyarūpakaṃ yathā
praṇamanti ye bhajanti ca phaṇigirikāntaṃ stuvanti cāñcanti | te’mī bhagavadbhaktā bhūmījanuṣo’pare muktāḥ || 189 ||
atra mukteṣvapara iti viśeṣaṇātprasiddhamuktebhyo bhagavadbhaktānāṃ bhedāviṣkaraṇādbhūmījanitatvarūpanyūnatāpratipādanācca nyūnatādrūpyarūpakam ||
anubhayatādrūpyarūpakaṃ yathā
vibudhebhyo’mṛtavitaraṇanipuṇamidaṃ hariṇakiraṇajaniśaraṇam | phaṇidharaṇidharaśiromaṇihṛdayaṃ jānīmahe’paraṃ jaladhim || 190 ||
vibudhebhyaḥ devebhyo vidvadbhyaśva amṛtasya sudhāyāḥ niśśreyasasya ca vitaraṇe nipuṇam | hariṇakiraṇasya candramasaḥ janiśaraṇaṃ janmagṛham | abdheścandrodayasya prasiddheḥ, pakṣe ‘candramā manaso jātaḥ’ iti śruteḥ | atrāparaṃ jaladhimiti prasiddhajaladherbhedamāviṣkṛtya tasya ca prasiddhajaladhikāryakāritvamātravarṇanenotkarṣāpakarṣayoravarṇanādanubhayatādrūpyarūpakam | tadevaṃ kuvalayānandakṛtāṃ matamanurudhyobhayavidhatvaṃ rūpakasya tatrāpyekaikasya traividhyaṃ ca varṇitam ||
maṅakhukamammaṭabhāmahaprabhṛtayaḥ prācīnāstu abhedarūpakamevāṅgyakārṣuḥ na tu tādrūpyarūpakam | teṣāmayamāśaya unnīyate abhedarūpakaṃ tādrūpyarūpakamiti na yujyate vibhāgaḥ, ‘mukhamaparaścandra’ ityādāvapyabhedarūpakasyaiva saṃbhavāt | na copamānatāvacchedakāvacchinnābhedabhāne hyabhedarūpakaṃ, uktasthale cāparapadena candrabhedaḥ syaiva pratītyā candrābhedabhānāsaṃbhavātkathamabhedarūpakaṃ syāt | ataścandrapadasya tatra candrakāryakāritvāvacchinnaparatāmāśritya tādrūpyāropāt tādrūpyarūpakamevābhyupeyamiti vācyam | aparādipadasyedṛśasthale svārthānvayitāvacchedakāśrayayatkiścidvyaktipratiyogikabhedamātrabodhakatvavyutpatteḥ prasiddhacandravyaktibhedabhāne’pi candrasāmānyabhedābhānāccandradvitvabhramamūlakasya mukhādau candratvāvacchinnābhedabhānasya saṃbhavāt | anyathā candrapadasyātra candrakāryakāriparatve’pi candrakāryakāritvena prasiddhacandrāsādhāraṇena rūpeṇa prasiddhacandravyaktyabhedabhānasyaivābhyupeyatayā’nupapattitādavasthyāt | candrakāryakāritāyāḥ prasiddhacandretarasādhāraṇye ca mukhe tadāropasyaivānāvaśyakatayā rūpakasyaivāprarohāt | evaṃ ‘mukhaṃ candrakāryakāri’ ityādāvapi tādrūpyarūpakaprasaṅgaḥ | tasmādabhedāropa eva rūpakaṃ na tu tādrūpyāropa iti | yadi tu abhedarūpakaṃ tādrūpyarūpakamiti vibhāgasaṃgamanamāvaśyakamityāgṛhyate tadā upamānatāvacchedakāvacchinnārope abhedarūpakaṃ upamānatāvacchedakadharmārope tādrūpyarūpakamityevāśrayaṇīyam | ādyasyodāharaṇaṃ ‘mukhaṃ candraḥ, mukhamaparaścandraḥ’ ityādikaṃ viṣayiviṣayavācakapadayossāmānādhikaraṇyasthalaṃ sarvameva | dvitīyasya tu ‘āsye pūrṇaśaśāṅkatā’ ityādīti dhyeyam | tatrāpyabhedarūpakaṃ dvividham | abhedasya saṃsargatve ekaṃ padārthatve’param ||
yathā
cintāmaṇirasi bhajatāṃ santāno vā tarustvamabjākṣa | harinīlādvaitaṃ tava tanuretyathavā tamālatādātmyam || 191 ||
cintāmaṇerharinīlādvaitaṃ santānatarostamālatādātmyamapyāścaryamiti bhāvaḥ | atra tvaṃ cintāmaṇiḥ saṃtānatarurvetyetadādyasyodāharaṇam | atra hyabhedassaṃsargarūpaḥ | dvitīyasya tu tava tanurharinīlādvaitaṃ tamālatādātmyaṃ vā etīti | atra hyabhedaḥ padārtharūpaḥ | evaṃ ‘mukhaṃ candraḥ’ ityādau ‘candrādvaitameti’ ityādāvapyabhedasya saṃsargarūpatā padārtharūpatā ca draṣṭavyā | anayoścābhedatādrūpyarūpakayorupapādyopapādakabhāvo’pi saṃbhavati ||
yathā
hārāstavāmbudhārāḥ kanakapaṭīyaṃ taṭitprabhārabhaṭī | amburuhekṣaṇa tattvayyambudatāṃ niyatameva jānīmaḥ || 192 ||
mandasmite sudhātvaṃ mandāratvaṃ tavācyuta kaṭākṣe | phāle bālendutvaṃ bhāti tadambhonidherabhinnastvam || atrādye padye abhedarūpakamupapādakaṃ tādrūpyarūpakamupapādyaṃ, dvitīye tu tadviparītam | tatrāpyādye udāharaṇe upapādake rūpake abhedassaṃsargarūpaḥ, dvitīye tūpapādyarūpake sa padārtharūpa iti bhidā | tadevaṃ kuvalayānandakṛdbhiḥ pradarśitasya rūpakavibhāgasya saṃgamane’pi tadupadarśitāni ‘vaktrendau tava satyayaṃ yadaparaśśītāṃśurujjṛmbhate | sādhvīyamaparā lakṣmīḥ’ ityādipadyāni tādrūpyarūpakasyānudāharaṇānyevetyalaṃ dūradhāvanena ||
prācāṃ mate vibhāgopi rūpakasya nirūpyate |
tridhā sāṅgaṃ niraṅgaṃ ca paraṃparitamityadaḥ || 36 ||
samastavastuviṣayamekadeśavivarti ca |
iti sāvayavaṃ cāpi dvividhaṃ tatra rūpakam || 37
adaḥ rūpakaṃ sāṅgaṃ sāvayavaṃ niraṅgaṃ niravayavaṃ paraṃparitamiti tridhā triprakāram | tatra uktarūpakatrayamadhye sāvayavaṃ rūpakaṃ samastavastuviṣayaṃ ekadeśavivarti ceti dvividham ||
sāvayavaṃ lakṣayati
yatparasparasāpekṣaniṣpattīnāṃ nibadhyate |
rūpakāṇāṃ tu saṃghāto bhavetsāvayavaṃ hi tat ||
anyonyasāpekṣaniṣpattīnāṃ rūpakāṇāṃ saṃghātassāvayavarūpakamityarthaḥ ||
tatrāpi samastavastuvṛtti sāvayavaṃ lakṣayati
nirūpaṇe’vayavino’vayavānāṃ ca śabdataḥ |
samastavastuviṣayaṃ bhavetsāvayavaṃ hi tat || 39 ||
yatra samastānyavayavāvayavirūpavastūnyāropyamāṇāni śabdopāttāni tatsamastavastuviṣayaṃ sāvayavarūpakamityarthaḥ ||
yathā
harihṛdayapañjare navamuktāmaṇihārajālake naddham | śrīvatsakīrahetordāḍimaphalameva kaustubhamaṇīndraḥ || 194 ||
atra saṃghātātmakasya sāvayavarūpakasyāvayavānāṃ sarveṣāmapi vastugatyā samarthyasamarthakabhāvasyānyonyaṃ samānatve’pi kaverdāḍimaphalarūpakasyaiva samarthyatayā vivakṣitatvātsamarthakatayopādānamanyeṣāmiti pratīyate | evaṃ sati samarthakarūpakāṇāṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇoḥ pṛthagvibhakteraśravaṇādanuvādyatve’pi samarthyakaustubhadāḍimarūpakasya viṣayaviṣayiṇoḥ pṛthagvibhaktiśravaṇādvidheyatayā tadādāya saṃghātātmakasya sāvayavarūpakasyāpi vidheyatvamatra vyapadiśyate | yathā yodhasaṃghātāntargatasya pradhānasya kasyacidyodhasya vijayaparājayābhyāṃ yodhasaṃghāto vijitaḥ parājitaścetyucyate | na caivamādau pratīyamānotprekṣā śakyā vaktum | abhedasya niścīyamānatvāt | satyāṃ cotprekṣāyāṃ saṃbhāvyamānatā syāt | anyathā ‘mukhaṃ candraḥ’ ityādāvapi pratīyamānotprekṣāpattyā rūpakapralayāpatteḥ ||
yathā vā
siṃhadharādharaśekharasiṃhalasaṃbhūtamasitavidyotam | śrīrucikanakopacitaṃ śaurimahānīlamādriye hṛdaye|| 195 || śrīrucikanake upacitaṃ pratyuptaṃ ‘hemnīvopacito maṇiḥ’ ityādāvupacitaśabdasya tathāvidhārthakatvadarśanāt | siṃhadharādharaśekharaḥ śeṣādriśreṣṭhaḥ sa eva siṃkṣalaḥ dvīpaviśeṣaḥ tasmin saṃbhūtaṃ ‘siṃhalasyākarodbhūtā mahānīlāstu te smṛtāḥ’ ityagastyokteḥ | hṛdaye manasi vakṣasi ca | asya sāvayavarūpakasya tvanuvādyatvameva, viṣayaviṣayiṇoḥ pṛthagvibhakteraśravaṇāt | udāharaṇadvaye’pyavayavino’vayavānāṃ ca samastānāṃ vastūnāmāropyamāṇānāṃ śābdatayā samastavastuviṣayaṃ sāvayavarūpakam ||
ekadeśavivarti sāvayavaṃ lakṣayati
āropyamāṇaṃ śābdaṃ cetkvacidārthaṃ ca kutracit |
ekadeśavivartyākhyamidaṃ sāvayavaṃ viduḥ || 40 ||
yatra kvacidavayave śabdopāttamāropyamāṇaṃ kvaciccārthasāmarthyākṣiptaṃ tat ekadeśe śabdānupāttaviṣayike avayavarūpake vivartanāt | svasvarūpagopanenānyathābhāvena vartanādekadeśavivartītyarthaḥ | yadvā ekadeśe upāttaviṣayike avayave viśeṣeṇa vācyatayā vartanādekadeśavivartīti |
yathā
bhavajalanidhimativipulaṃ vividhamahāvāsanānadīśabalam | loko’yaṃ taratitarāṃ kākodaragiripaterupāsya kṛpām || 196 ||
atra bhavādiṣu jaladhyādirūpakaiḥ kṛpāyāṃ nautvamākṣipyata ityekadeśavivarti rūpakam || yathā vā
vitataiśśubhaguṇakiraṇairvitaransatkokalokasammodam | phaṇiśikharimaṇidyumaṇirdharaṇisutāmukhavikāsakṛjjayati || 197 ||
dharaṇisutā yajñārthaṃ bhuvaṃ śodhayatā ākāśarājena labdhā bhūtalādudgatā dharaṇyākhyasvamahiṣīhaste dattā tayā saṃvardhitā bhagavatā śrīnivāsena pariṇītā padmāvatīnāmnī sākṣāllakṣmīti vārāhe veṃkaṭācalamāhātmye prasidhyati | pūrvasminnudāharaṇe kavessamarthyatvenābhimatasya kṛpāyāṃ nautvāroparūpakasyākṣepaḥ | iha tu samarthakatvenābhimatasya dharaṇisutāmukhe padmarūpakasyākṣepa iti viśeṣaḥ | evaṃca samarthyarūpakasyaivākṣepo na samarthakarūpakasyeti nāgrahaḥ kāryaḥ| evameva rasagaṅgādharādāvudāhṛtatvāt | ata evaikadeśavivartitvasaṃjñā’pyasya yujyate | ekadeśe samarthyarūpe avayavini samarthakarūpe avayave vā vivartata iti tadarthāt ||
idaṃ sāvayavaṃ dvividhaṃ śliṣṭamapi dṛśyata iti vimarśinīkāraḥ ||
tatra samastavastuvṛtti sāvayavaṃ śliṣṭaṃ yathā
maṇimayaśirassahasreṇottuṅgassādhuguptabahupādaḥ | maṇikiraṇarasanavisaraḥ phaṇabhṛddharaṇīdharo bhāti ||
maṇimayaśirassahasrameva maṇimayaśirassahasraṃ tena uttuṅgaḥ| sādhu yathā syāttathā guptāḥ rakṣitāḥ gūḍhāśca bahavaḥ pādāḥ pratyantaparvatāḥ yena tathoktaḥ bahupādā eva bahavaścaraṇāḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘kuṇḍalī gūḍhapāt’ ityamaraḥ | phaṇabhṛddharaṇīdharaśeṣādrireva phaṇabhṛddharaṇīdharaśśeṣaḥ | atra śiraādiśabdāḥ śliṣṭāḥ ‘śiraḥ pradhāne senāgre drumādyagreṣu mastake’ iti ratnamālā ||
yathā vā
durviṣayagahanavipathe karṣantaṃ māṃ svarūpaharaṇāya | nigalaya nikhilaniyantarhṛdayaikāgārikaṃ tava pade’bje || 199 ||
durviṣayagahanavipathe durindriyārthaduḥkhaduradhve eva durdeśakāntārakadadhvani ‘viṣayo gocare deśe, gahanaṃ vanaduḥkhayoḥ’ iti ca ratnamālā | svarūpasya bhagavaccheṣataikarūpasya haraṇāyānyathākaraṇāyaiva svarūpaharaṇāya praśastavittāpahārāya praśastaṃ svaṃ svarūpam | ‘praśaṃsāyāṃ rūpap’ iti rūpappratyayaḥ | māṃ karṣantaṃ hṛdayameva ekāgārikaṃ coraṃ ‘aikāgārikaṭcore’ iti nipātanātsādhuḥ | ekamasahāyamagāraṃ prayojanamasya mumuṣiṣossa aikāgārika iti vigrahaḥ | tava abje padmasadṛśe pade caraṇa eva abje jalamadhyasthite jaladhimadhyagata iti yāvat | pade sthāne kārāgāra iti yāvat | nigalaya | atra niścalīkaraṇameva nigalavattāsaṃpādanam | ‘nikhilaniyantaḥ’ itisaṃbuddhirīdṛśanigrahānugrahasvatantratābhiprāyikī | atrāpi durviṣayādiśabdāśśliṣṭāḥ ||
niravayavarūpakaṃ lakṣayati
niraṅgaṃ tadviduryatsyādaṅgimātrasya rūpaṇam| taddvidhā kevalaṃ mālārūpaṃ ceti satāṃ matam ||
niraṅgaṃ niravayavam | aṅgimātrasya avayavimātrasya ||
tatra kevalaṃ niravayavaṃ yathā
vātaṃdhayagirijātaṃ sphītaṃ dhanamakhilaśaraṇamavadātam | khyātaṃ dhanapatigītaṃ dhyātaṃ dhavalayati hṛtsarojātam || 200 ||
sphītadhanatvena bhagavānadhyavasyate | hṛtsarojātaṃ hṛdayakamalaṃ dhavalayati prasādayatītyarthaḥ | atra hṛtsarojātamiti rūpakaṃ sāpekṣarūpakasaṃghātātmakatāvirahānniravayavam | mālātvavirahātkevalam ||
niravayavaṃ malārūpakaṃ yathā
saundaryabṛndakandaṃ mucukundānandacandanaṃ vande| vandārukabṛndārakamandāraṃ dandaśūkaśikharīndum ||
ekaviṣayakanānāpadārthāroparūpatvānmālārūpamidam | anyonyasāpekṣatāvirahānniravayavam ||
vimarśinīkārastv anekaviṣayakānekapadarthārope’pīdaṃ niravayavamālārūpakaṃ śliṣṭāśliṣṭabhedena dṛśyata ityāha |
tatrāśliṣṭānekaviṣayakānekāropo yathā
nayane naline vadanaṃ sadanaṃ lakṣmyāstava smitaṃ tvamṛtam | gaṇḍau darpaṇakhaṇḍau kuṇḍalivetaṇḍaśikharimārtaṇḍa || 202 || atra nayanādīnāmanekeṣāmaśleṣanibandhanānekāropaḥ ||
śleṣanibandhanānekāropo yathā
tava puṣkaraṃ hi vadanaṃ hṛdayaṃ ratnākaro dyutiḥ kṛṣṇā | hṛttāpanāśinī dṛktattīrthagirīśa bhavasi tīrthamayaḥ || 203 ||
tīrthagiririti veṅkaṭādrereva nāmāntaramityavocāma | atra puṣkararatnākarakṛṣṇahṛttāpanāśinīśabdāḥ śliṣṭā ityanekeṣāṃ vadanādīnāmaneke śliṣṭā āropāḥ | tadiyaṃ śliṣṭārtharūpakamālā | puṣkaraṃ kamalaṃ prasiddhatīrthaviśeṣaśca | ratnānāṃ kaustubhādīnāmākaraḥ sthānaṃ samudraśca | kṛṣṇā nīlā nadīviśeṣaśca | hṛttāpanāśinī manastāpanivartinī vīkṣāraṇyakṣetragatatīrthaviśeṣaścetyarthaḥ | tīrthamayaḥ ‘sarvatīrthātmakassarvagraharūpī’ iti hi tannāmasu paṭhyate ||
yathā vā
akṣi tava puṇḍarīkaṃ kumudaṃ smitamañjanaṃ prabhāvisaram | caritaṃ tu sārvabhaumaṃ jānaṃstvāṃ vedmi diggajātmānam || 204 ||
atrāpi puṇḍarīkakumudāñjanasārvabhaumaśabdāssitāmbhojādyarthakā diggajārthakāśceti śliṣṭā evāneke anekeṣvakṣyādiṣu diggajāropā iti śliṣṭārtharūpakamālā | ‘airāvataḥ puṇḍarīko vāmanaḥ kumudo’ñjanaḥ | puṣpadantassārvabhaumassupratīkaśca diggajāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | sārvabhaumaṃ sarvasyāṃ bhūmau viditaṃ ‘tatra viditaḥ’ ityadhikāre ‘sarvabhūmipṛthivībhyāmaṇañau’ ityaṇ | anuśatikāditvāhubhayapadavṛddhiḥ | pakṣe diggajaviśeṣamityarthaḥ |
ekaṃ viṣayi yadyanyaviṣayyanvitamucyate |
kāryakāraṇabhāvena syātparaṃparitaṃ tadā || 42 ||
viṣayi āropyamupamānaṃ hetuhetumadbhāvāpannaṃ ekāropyānvitamanyadāropyaṃ viṣaye tādātmyena nibadhyate cettatparaṃparitarūpakam ||
yathā
vinatākhilajanatājalanidhatārānātha devavara bhavatā | nanu tādṛśabhavamayatapajanitātanutāpasaṃpadupaśamitā || 205 ||
tārānāthaścandramāḥ | tapo grīṣmaḥ | atra vinatākhilajanatājalanidhitārānāthetyatra hetuhetumadbhāvāpannajalanidhitārānātharūpakayormadhye jalanidhirūpake āropyabhūte jalanidhinā’nvitameva tārānātharūpamāropyaṃ viṣayabhūte bhagavati tādātmyena nibadhyate | jalanidhisambandhitārānāthatvenaiva rūpaṇasya vivakṣitatvāditi bhavatyatra paraṃparitarūpakam | viṣayiṇi viṣayyantarānvitatvaviśeṣaṇena sāvayavarūpake ‘siṃhadharādharaśekharasiṃhalasaṃbhūtamasitavidyotam | śrīrucikanakopacitaṃ śaurimahānīlamādriye hṛdaye’ | ityādau nātivyāptiḥ | tatra hi śaurau mahānīlarūpakasya siṃhadharādharādau siṃhalādirūpakasya ca hetuhetumadbhāvasadbhāve’pi āropyamāṇamahānile rūpakāntarapraviṣṭasiṃhalādirūpāropyasyānvayavirahānnātiprasaṅgaḥ | tatra śaurermahānīlatvenaiva rūpaṇaṃ na tu siṃhalādisaṃbandhimahānīlatveneti ||
etena ‘niyatāropaṇopāyassyādāropaḥ parasya yat | tatparaṃparitam’ ||
iti prācīnalakṣaṇasya sāvayavarūpake ‘jyotsnābhasmacchuraṇadhavalā’ ityādau ativyāptirdurvārā | tatrāpi kāpālikītvarūpaṇe bhasmādirūpaṇasya niyamena hetutvāśrayaṇasaṃbhavāt | yathā hi ‘vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādau rājñi haṃsatvarūpaṇaṃ vidvanmānase mānasasarastvarūpaṇamantarā nopapadyate, tathā rātryāṃ kapālikītvarūpaṇamapi jyotsnādau bhasmādirūpaṇamantarā nopapadyata iti sāmyāt |’vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādāvevaikarūpakasya niyamena rūpakāntarasāpekṣatvaṃ nānyatretyasya tu śapathamātrasādhyatvāditi parāstam | tādṛśalakṣaṇasyāsmadupadarśitalakṣaṇa eva paryavasānasyābhimatatvāt āropye āropyāntarānvayasthale hi ekāropa āropāntare niyamena heturbhavatīti tallakṣaṇasya tathā paryavasānaṃ saṃbhavati |
etena ‘āropadvayamātraparyavasitaṃ paramparitaṃ sāvayavaṃ tu na tathā’ iti keṣāṃciduktiranādaraṇīyā | ‘vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādau prakāśādyudāhṛte paraṃparitarūpake āropabahutvasyaiva vartamānatayā avyāptiprasaṅgāt | na cātra padye vidvanmānasahaṃsādibhāge pratyekaṃ rūpakadvayamātratvamastyeveti nāvyāptiḥ | ata evaitatpadyaṃ paraṃparitarūpakabāhulyānmālārūpaparaṃparitodāharaṇatvenopāttamiti vācyam | evaṃ sati ‘jyotsnābhasma’ iti padye’pi ‘bibhratī tārakāsthīni, rātrikāpālikīyam’ ityaṃśe rūpakadvayamātratvasaṃbhavenātiprasaṅgāpātaḥ | na ca rūpakāntaranirapekṣahetuhetumadbhāvāpannarūpakadvayamātratvameva paraṃparitalakṣaṇam | tathāca ‘jyotsnābhasma’ ityādau kāpālikīrūpake yathā ‘tārakāsthīni’ iti rūpakasāpekṣatvaṃ tathā ‘jyotsnābhasma’ ityādirūpakasāpekṣatvasyāpi sattvānnātiprasaṅgaḥ | ‘vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādau ca haṃsādirūpake pratyekaṃ mānasarūpakādyekaikarūpakasāpekṣatvameveti lakṣaṇasaṃgatiśceti vācyam | evamapi ‘amuṣminpotakṣmāramaṇasutasūrakṣitipateryaśaśśeṣe so’yaṃ svapiti gaganaśrīsahacaraḥ’ ityādau śeṣarūpakasāpekṣaśrīsahacararūpake sāvayavarūpakatvena kāvyadarpaṇodāhṛte atiprasaṅgāpātāt | na ceṣṭāpattiryujyate, ‘niyatāropaṇopāyassyādaropaḥ parasya yat’ iti paraṃparitaṃ lakṣayatāṃ kāvyaprakāśakārādīnāmanabhimatatvāt | yaśasi śeṣatvamarūpayitvā’pi ‘yaśovyāje śeṣe svapiti gaganaśrīsahacaraḥ’ iti padyapāṭhena śrīsahacararūpakopapādanasaṃbhavāttatratyarūpakayorniyatahetuhetumadbhāvāsaṃbhavāt | na caivaṃ sati niyatahetuhetumadbhāvāpannatvameva rūpakadvaye niveśya pariṣkriyatāṃ lakṣaṇamiti vācyam | evamapi ‘caturdaśalokavallikandaḥ’ ityādau prakāśādyabhimate paraṃparite avyāptiprasaṅgāt | tatrāpi ‘caturdaśalokavallihetuḥ’ iti vā ‘caturdaśalokamūlakandaḥ’ iti vā nibandhasaṃbhavena niyatahetuhetumadbhāvasya durupapādatvāt | tasmādekarūpakīyaviṣayyanvitaviṣayikarūpakāntaratvameva paraṃparitarūpakalakṣaṇamāstheyam | kāvyaprakāśasarvasvādiprāktanamānanīyanibandhopātteṣu sarveṣveva paraṃparitarūpakodāharaṇeṣūttararūpake viṣayavācakapadaṃ viṣayivācakapadenāsamastatayaiva dṛśyata iti bhavati tatra pūrvarūpakāropyānvitasyaivottararūpakīyāropyasya tadīyaviṣaye’nvayaḥ ||
evam
nigamamakuṭīvadhūṭīvataṃsacaraṇaṃ śrayāmahe śaraṇam | bhavajaladhitaraṇikaruṇaṃ tarūṇāmbudanīlamahidharābharaṇam || 206 || ityādau paraṃparitarūpakodāharaṇe uttararūpakadvaye caraṇakaruṇārūpaviṣayavācakapadayoḥ vataṃsataraṇirūpaviṣayivācakapadābhyāṃ samastatayā darśane’pi pūrvarūpakīyavadhūṭījaladhyāropyānvitatayaivottararūpakīyāvataṃsataraṇirūpāropyayostadīyacaraṇakaruṇārūpaviṣayābhyāmanvayaḥ | tathā tadupātteṣu sāvayavodāharaṇeṣu ca na kvacidapyekarūpakāropye rūpakāntarāropyānvayaupayikassamabhivyāhārostīti nātiprasaṅgaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ | na caivaṃ ‘mugdhāriprāṇadugdhāśanamasṛṇarucistvatkṛpāṇo bhujaṅgaḥ’ iti rasagaṅgādharīyaparaṃparitodāharaṇe pūrvarūpakāropyasya dugdhasyāśanādipadavyavadhānāduttararūpakāropye bhujaṅge anvayāsaṃbhavādavyāptiriti vācyam | āropye āropyānvitatvasya sākṣātparaṃparāsādhāraṇasyaiva niveśāt ||
nanvevaṃ sati
suvipulaśubhaguṇajalanidhiphaṇadharagirirājapatikṛpāgaṅgām | avagāhya narāśśiśirāṃ na vigāhante kadā’pi tāpakathām || 207 ||
ityatra pūrvarūpakāropyasya jalanidheruttararūpakāropyāyāṃ gaṅgāyāṃ paraṃparayā’tvayasaṃbhavādativyāptiriti cenna | lakṣaṇe rūpakadvaye hetuhetumadbhāvāpannatvaviśeṣaṇopādānādeva tadvāraṇāt | atra hi pradarśitarūpakadvayasya na hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ | yadyapi ‘paryaṅko rājalakṣmyāḥ’ iti padyaṃ mālāparaṃparitodāharaṇatayopanyasya atra ‘kṣmāsauvadallaḥ’ iti paraṃparitamapyekadeśavivartīti sarvasvakāroktestādṛśaikadeśavivartiparaṃparitarūpake’vyāptiḥ | tatra kṣmāyāṃ rājapatnyāropasyārthatayā sauvidallarūpe uttararūpakāropye pūrvarūpakāropyabhūtāyā rājamahiṣyā anvayābhāvāt | tathā’pi paraṃparitasyaikadeśavivartitvaṃ na sarvasvakṛto’bhimatam | svakīyaprāktanavibhāgavyāghātāt | tatra hi ‘idaṃ tu niravayavam’ ityādinā sāvayavāvāntarabhedatayaiva ekadeśavivartirūpakaṃ gaṇyate, na tu paraṃparitāvāntarabhedatayā’pīti | upadarśitasarvasvavākyaṃ tu atra padye ‘kṣmāsauvidallaḥ’ ityetadekadeśavivartirūpakaṃ ‘paryaṅkaḥ’ ityādiparaṃparitamālāsamānādhikaraṇamapītyetadarthakatayā kathaṃcitsaṃgamanīyam | anyathā ākṣiptarūpakasaṃpannarūpakadvayamātratayā tatra paraṃparitarūpakasvīkāre ‘nirīkṣya vidyunnayanaiḥ’ iti padye ekadeśavivartirūpakapratipādanaparasamāsoktiprakaraṇasthatadvākyavirodhaprasaṅgāt | ata eva prakāśādiṣu prāktaneṣu rasagaṅgādharādiṣvarvāktaneṣu cālaṃkāranibandheṣu paraṃparitamadhye ekadeśavivartividhāyā aparigaṇanamupapadyata iti ||
vastutastu ‘kṣmāsauvidallaḥ’ ityatrākṣepalabhyarājamahiṣīrūpakeṇa paraṃparitarūpakābhyupagame’pi nāsmadupadarśitalakṣaṇasya tatrāvyāptiprasaṅgaḥ | ‘kṣmāsauvidallaḥ’ ityasmādapi kṣmāmahiṣīsauvidallaḥ ityevānvayabodhasyākṣepasahakṛtaśabdajanyasya svīkāreṇa prathamarūpakāropyānvitatvasya dvitīyarūpakāropye nirbādhāt | ākṣepalabhyasyāpyarthasya upāttaśabdārthena saha śābdabodhe bhānaṃ jātiśaktyādyabhyupagantṛtāntrikasamayasuprasiddhameveti ||
evaṃca sarvasvakārīyavibhāgavākyasyopalakṣaṇatvamavaśyāśrayaṇīyam | anyathā tena paraṃparitamātre pradarśitasya śliṣṭāśliṣṭavidhādvayasya tadvyākhyātṛvimarśinīkāreṇa sāvayave’pi pradarśanaṃ ‘kṣmāsauvidalla iti paramparitamapyekadeśavivarti | evamādayo’nye’pi bhedā leśatassūcitā eva’ iti sarvasvakṛta evoktiḥ, ‘evamādayaḥ’ iti pratīkamādāya ‘paraṃparitamapyekadeśavivartītyevaṃprakārāḥ’ iti vimarśinīkṛduktiśca svarasato na saṃgaccheta | na saṃgacche’ta ca śliṣṭaparaṃparitanirūpaṇāvasare ‘vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ iti padyamudāhṛtya ‘idaṃ hi ekadeśavivartyapītyucyate’ iti kāvyapradīpagranthaḥ, idaṃ hīti pratīkamādāya ‘mūle idamapītyanvayaḥ | ekapadopasthāpitayorantaḥkaraṇasaroviśeṣayorabhedādhyavasāye’pi naikatrāparāropaḥ tasya bhedena viṣayanirdeśāpekṣatvāt | anyathā viṣayanigaraṇātmikāyāmatiśayoktāvapi tatprasaṅgāt | tasmādārtha evāyamāropa ityekadeśavirtitvamatrāpi yuktam’ iti prabhākhyatadvyākhyānaṃ ca | tasmāt atra ‘kṣmāsauvidallaḥ’ ityādisarvasvavākyaṃ yathāśrutagatyā paraṃparitasyāpyekadeśavivartitvaṃ pratyāyayatīti svīkaraṇameva samucitamityuktāvapi na kṣatiḥ ||
nanvevaṃ paraṃparitasyāpyekadeśavivartitvāṅgīkāre
saundaryasya taraṅgiṇī taruṇimotkarṣasya harṣodgamaḥ
kānteḥ kārmaṇakarma narmavacasāmullāsanāvāsabhūḥ |
vidyā vakragirāṃ vidheranavadhiprāvīṇyasākṣātkriyā
prāṇāḥ pañcaśilīmukhasya lalanācūḍāmaṇissā priyā ||
ityasya padyasya citramīmāṃsāyāṃ mālāniravayavarūpakodāharaṇatvamuktamasaṅgataṃ syāt | tatra ‘saundaryasya taraṅgiṇī’ ityādau saundaryādau salilatvādyāropaṇamantarā taraṅgiṇītvāropasyāsaundaryādākṣepalabdhasalilarūpāropyasya taraṅgiṇīrūpāropyeṇānvitatayā paraṃparitatvasyāvyāhatatvāditi cetsatyam | etadanyeṣāṃ ‘kānteḥ kārmaṇakarma’ ityādīnāṃ mālāniravayavarūpakodāharaṇatetyadoṣaḥ | tatra hi priyāyāṃ kārmaṇakarmādimātramāropyate na tu kāntyādau kimapi | tathā ca paraṃparitasaṃkīrṇamevedaṃ mālāniravayavarūpakamiti dhyeyam | dṛśyate hyekālaṃkāramukhyodāharaṇeṣvapyalaṃkārāntarasaṃkīrṇatā | yathā ‘jyotsnābhasma’ iti sāvayavarūpakodāharaṇādau ‘lāñchanasya chalena’ ityādikaitavāpahnutyādisaṃkīrṇatā ||
idaṃ tu śuddhamālāniravayavasyodāharaṇam
namadamaramastamakuṭaṃ kamalābhūmīkarāgralīlābjam | taccaritaśrotṛjanaśrotravataṃsaṃ śraye hareścaraṇam || 208 ||
nīlāmbujasaṃvananaṃ kālāmbudakārmaṇaṃ tamālaśrīḥ | vyālācalalīlārasalolā mūrtirharerharatyārtim || 209 ||
saṃvananaṃ vaśakriyā | kārmaṇaṃ mūlakarma ‘vaśakriyā saṃvananaṃ mūlakarma tu kārmaṇam’ ityamaraḥ | śrīḥ saṃpat | atrādye udāharaṇe haricaraṇe viṣaye makuṭalīlābjavataṃsamātrāropaḥ | dvitīye tu harimūrtau nīlāmbujasaṃvananakālāmbudakārmaṇatamālasaṃpanmātrāropa iti śuddhamālāniravayavam | evameva vaiyadhikaraṇyenārope ‘dvirbhāvaḥ puṣpaketorvibudhaviṭapināṃ paunaruktyam’ ityādāvapi śuddhamālāniravayavatvaṃ bodhyamityalaṃ pallavitena ||
tatparaṃparitaṃ ca vibhajate
śliṣṭāśliṣṭavibhedena tatparaṃparitaṃ dvidhā ||
kevalaṃ mālikā ceti taddvayaṃ dvividhaṃ bhavet |
evamaṣṭavidhaṃ prācāṃ mate rūpakamiṣyate || 43 ||
tatparaṃparitaṃ dvidhā | tatrāpi samarthakatvena vivakṣitasyāropasya śleṣamūlakatve śliṣṭaparaṃparitam | tadubhayaṃ ca kevalaṃ mālārūpaṃ dvividham | atra paraṃparitarūpake uttararūpakasya pūrvarūpakaṃ prati hetutvamiti maṅkhukādayo manyante | teṣāmayamāśayaḥ viddhanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādau rājādeḥ varṇyatvena purassphūrtikatayā tatra haṃsādyāropapūrvakamevāvarṇye vidvanmānasādau paścāt śleṣeṇa saroviśeṣādyāropaḥ | evamaśliṣṭaparamparite’pīti || mammaṭādayastu pūrvarūpakasyottararūpakahetutvaṃ manvate | yadāhuḥ ‘evamāropanimitto haṃsatvādyāropaḥ’ iti | teṣāmayaṃ bhāvaḥ ‘vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādau mānasetyādiśleṣasya prāthamikatayā tanmūlakārthadvayābhedādhyavasāyopajīvirūpakasya prathamapratītyā tadbuddheruttararūpakabuddhiṃ prati hetutvaṃ yuktamiti | hetuhetumadbhāvāpannaikaviṣayyanvitānyaviṣayikatvaṃ paramparitatvamityasmadīyalakṣaṇane matadvayasyāpi saṃgraho bhaviṣyati ||
tatra kevalaṃ śliṣṭaparaṃparitaṃ yathā
bhaja hṛdaya racitayatnaṃ bhujagādhipabhūmibhṛcchiroratnam | puruṣaṃ kamapi pratnaṃ prasādhanaṃ tava tato’pi kimu nūtnam || 210 ||
atra dvayorapyāropayossamarthyasamarthakabhāvasya vastutastulyatve’pi bhūmibhṛt śaila eva bhūmibhṛt rājeti śleṣamūlakenāropeṇa bhagavati śiroratnatādātmyāropasya samarthanīyatāyāṃ kaverāśayaḥ | ata eva caturthacaraṇaniveditaḥ prasādhanāntaratiraskāro’pi saṃgacchate ||
yathāvā
vipulāhirājavipulādharavipulāvāsarasika kamalākṣa | capalāvalāhakastvaṃ bahuśastoyaśśriyaṃ samedhayase || 211 ||
vipulaḥ viśālaḥ ahirājavipulādharaḥ śeṣādriḥ tasya vipulayāṃ bhūmyāṃ vāse rasika he kamalākṣa capalāvalāhakaḥ lakṣmīvidyudvārivāhaḥ ‘capalā kamalāvidyutpuṃścalīpippalīṣu ca’ iti medinī | tvaṃ bahuśaḥ toyaśriyaṃ salilasaṃpadaṃ samedhayase | pakṣe bahuśastaḥ ayaḥ śubhāvaho vidhiḥ tasya śriyaṃ saṃpadam | yadvā bahuśastaḥ yaḥ śriyamiti chedaḥ bahuśastaḥ tvaṃ capalāvalāhakaḥ yaḥ tvaṃ śriyaṃ saṃpadaṃ samedhayase iti vākyabhedena yojanā | atra yacchabdasyottaravākyasthatayā na pūrvavākye tacchabdopādānāvaśyakatvam | atrobhayorapyāropayoḥ parasparaṃ samarthyasamarthakabhāvataulye’pi capalā lakṣmīreva capalā vidyuditi śleṣabhittikenāropeṇa bhagavati balāhakatādātmyāropassamarthanīya iti kavayiturabhiprāyaḥ | ata eva caturthacaraṇagatasabhaṅgaśleṣāveditaṃ bahuśastoyaśrīsamedhanamapi yujyate | udāharaṇadvaye’pi rūpakadvayaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvena saṃjātaparaṃparatvātparaṃparitaṃ śliṣṭaśabdanibandhanaṃ mālārūpatvavirahātkevalaṃ ca ||
tadeva mālārūpaṃ yathā
sakalasumanovasantaṃ sādhukadambasya ghanataṭitvantam | muktālisarasvantaṃ svāntaṃ śrayatānmamāhigirikāntam || 212 ||
sakalasumanasāṃ samastaviduṣāmeva kusumānām | sādhukadambasya sajjananivahasyaiva ramaṇayinīpataroḥ | muktānāṃ prakṛtibandhaviyuktānāṃ āliḥ paṅktireva mauktikāvaliḥ tasyāḥ | svāntaṃ hṛdayaṃ kartṛ ||
yathāvā
animiṣakulasalilanidhiṃ ghanavibhavavilāsabharatapartvavadhim | paramahimatāhimānīdharamaravindekṣaṇaṃ bhuvi namāni || 213 ||
animiṣakulaṃ daivatayūdhameva mīnakulaṃ tasya salilanidhim | ghanassāndro vibhavavilāsabhara eva jaladavaibhavalīlātiśayaḥ tasya tapartvavadhiṃ grīṣmāvasānaṃ varṣāsamayamityarthaḥ | paramahimatā śreṣṭhamāhātmyavatteva atiśayitahimavattā tasyāḥ himānīdharaṃ himavantaṃ aravindekṣaṇaṃ bhuvi namāni nameyam |
praṇamya kṛṣṇaṃ sahasā pāṃsukliṣṭe mahītale |
niṣkalmaṣo bhavetsadyo lalāṭe pāṃsumaṇḍanāt ||
ityādipramāṇānusaṃdhānena bhuvi namānītyuktiḥ | namaterloḍuttamaikavacanam | atra sumanasāmeva sumanasāṃ vasantamityādau śleṣamūlakenāropeṇa śrīnivāse vasantatvādyāropasya samarthanīyatayā’bhimatasya bahudhā nibandhanāt śliṣṭamālāparaṃparitamidam || atra kecit ‘vidvanmānasahaṃsa’ ityādau varṇye haṃsatvādyāropāccittādiṣu sarastvādyāropasyārthātpratīterekadeśavivarti rūpakamevedamityāhuḥ | vastutastu śliṣṭaśabdanibandhanābhedāroparūpavaicitryanaiyatyāttato bhinnamevedamiti dik ||
evameva
bhadraśrīmalayādriṃ śrīvatsavilāsadānasurasurabhim | haṃsollāsanaśaradaṃ varadaṃ vande vṛṣācaladviradam ||
ityatrāpi śliṣṭaparaṃparitaṃ draṣṭavyam | bhadraśrīḥ maṃgalasaṃpadeva bhadraśrīḥ candanam | śrīvatsa eva śrīyukto vatsaḥ | haṃsāḥ jñānina eva marālāḥ ||
śuddhaparaṃparitaṃ kevalaṃ yathā
pavanodaraṃbharigireravanau lavanāya ripukulavanānām | avanāya ca bhuvanānāṃ bhavanamakārṣiśśritābjabhuvana hare || 215 ||
bhavanaṃ āvirbhāvam | śritābjabhuvanetyatra bhagavati salilatvāropaḥ śriteṣvabjatvāropasamarthyatvenābhimata iti paraṃparitam | mālāśleṣavirahātkevalaṃ śuddhaṃ ca ||
idameva mālārūpaṃ yathā
bhūkekinīpayodaṃ rākenduṃ taṃ ramākuvalayinyāḥ | nākeśajalajabhānuṃ lokeśaṃ vandiṣīya śeṣādrau ||
atra lokeśe śrīnivāse payodādyanekāropasya bhūmyādiṣu kekinyādyāropapratīkṣatvānmālāparaṃparitaṃ śuddham || idamekadeśavivartyapi bhavatītyavocāma, tadyathā
kānternartanaraṅgaṃ śānterantaḥpuraṃ mahottuṅgam | kṣānterharitturaṅgaṃ svānte kalayema dhāma divyāṅgam ||
atra bhagavati nartanaraṅgādirūpaktaiḥ kāntiśāntikṣāntiṣu nartakīrājñīpadminīrūpakāṇi pratīyanta ityekadeśavivarti mālāparaṃparitaṃ śuddham ||
bhuvanānāmādhāraṃ bhānāmavalambanaṃ phaṇigiristham | śākhikulollāsakaraṃ kiṃcana saubhāgyamañca mama hṛdaya || 218 ||
atra bhuvanānāṃ lokānāmeva salilānāṃ, bhānāṃ tviṣāmeva tārāṇāṃ, śākhikulasya vedaśākhādhyetṛnivahasyaiva tarunikarasyeti śliṣṭarūpakairbhagavati viṣaye samudrasudhākaravasantarūpakāṇāṃ gamyamānatayā idamekadeśavivarti śliṣṭaparaṃparitamiti dik ||
jñānaṃ dhanaṃ matimatāṃ bhānaṃ dhanamiha mahasvināṃ mahatām | sūnaṃ dhanaṃ madhukṛtāṃ nūnaṃ dhanamahigirīśitā namatām || 219 ||
atra viṣayamālākṛto na kaściccamatkāraviśeṣa iti na pṛthagbhedagaṇanāyāṃ gaṇyate | āropyamāṇamālā tu camatkāraviśeṣaśālitvādgaṇyata eva | atha kathaṃ śliṣṭaparaṃparite ‘bhujagādhipabhūmibhṛcchiroratnam, sakalasumanovasantam’ ityādāvekasyāropasyāropāntarahetutvaṃ, yasmāt śleṣeṇa śailasya rājñaśca viduṣāṃ kusumānā cābhedamātramatra pratīyate naikatrānyāropaḥ tasya svatantraviṣayanirdeśāpekṣatvāt | na ca śuddhābhedapratyaya evāropaḥ, viṣayanigaraṇātmikāyāmatiśayoktāvatiprasaṅgāditi cet satyam, śleṣeṇa śuddhābhedapratītau satyāṃ prakṛtāropasamarthanāya antarā mānasasya bhagavatsaṃbandhini girau śiroratnasaṃbandhirājābhedāropasya evaṃ bhagavatsaṃbandhiṣu deveṣu vasantasaṃbandhikusumābhedāropasya ca kalpanānnānupapattiḥ ||
atha kathaṃ
saundaryaratnasindhuṃ saubhāgyaśaratsarojinībandhum | svānte yateya bandhuṃ kaunteyasakhaṃ phaṇīndragiribandhum || 220 ||
ityādau saundaryaratnasiṃdhumityādiparaṃparitarūpake rūpakatvaṃ, abhedāropasya sattve’pi tasya sādṛśyamūlakatvābhāvāt | na hi bhagavatsindhyossādṛśyamatrocyate iti cet, na samarthakena saundarye ratnāropeṇa dharmaikyasaṃpādane sādṛśyasya niṣpratyūhatvāditi dik | bandhumiti bandhatestumun ||
evaṃ ‘bhūkekinīpayodam’ ityādāvāropyamāṇayoḥ parasparamāropaviṣayayoścānukūlye rūpakayoranugrāhyānugrāhakabhāvo darśitaḥ ||
prātikūlye yathā
kṛtipatiśatanutaśīlaṃ ditisutasaṃghātabhūtivātūlam | nilayitaphaṇipatiśailaṃ kalaya paraṃ hṛdaya daivamativelam || 221 || ditisutasaṃghātasya bhūtiraiśvaryameva bhūtirbhasma tasyāḥ vātūlam | idaṃ śliṣṭakevalaparaṃparitam ||
idameva mālārūpaṃ yathā
bhuvanajaḍatāvasantaṃ saṃtamasakadambakaikabhāsvantam | svāntāhiśailakāntaṃ cintaya durvyālakulagarutmantam || 222 ||
he svānteti saṃbuddhiḥ | bhuvanajaḍatā lokajāḍyameva jalaśaityaṃ tasya vasantam | duṣṭāḥ ye vyālāḥ śaṭhāḥ daityādayaḥ | ta eva vyālāḥ bhujagāḥ ‘bhedyaliṅgaśśaṭhe vyālaḥ puṃsi śvāpadasarpayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | teṣāṃ garutmantam | nāśyanāśakabhāvarūpaprātikūlyamupamānayostathaivopameyayośca| anugrāhyānugrāhakabhāvaḥ punarāropayoraviśiṣṭa eva | evamagre’pi ||
yathā vā doṣollāsavibhātaṃ daityāvaśyāyacaṇḍakarametam | bhaja bhujagadharābharaṇaṃ śaraṇāgatapaṅkajātasitakiraṇam || 223 ||
doṣollāsa eva rātrivikāsaḥ tasya vibhātaṃ prabhātam | daityānāmavaśyāyo garva eva himaṃ ‘avaśyāyo himagarvayoḥ’ iti ratnamālā | śaraṇāgatānāṃ paṅkajātaṃ duritabṛndameva kamalam | atra bhajetyuktiḥ svahṛdayaṃ pratīti bodhyam ||
yathā vā dustṛṣṇākāsāraṃ sāsāraghanaṃ bhavābhitāpānām | veṃkaṭanāthaṃ kalaye jagadātaṅkāgadaṅkāram || 224 || dustṛṣṇā durviṣayalipsaiva niravadhikapipāsā | ‘tṛṣṇā syāttarṣalipsayoḥ’ iti medinī | tasyāḥ kāsāraṃ saraḥ | bhavābhitāpā eva tāpāḥ teṣāṃ sāsāraṃ dhārāsahitaṃ ghanam | jagatāṃ ātaṅkāḥ bhayānyeva ātaṅkāḥ rogāḥ teṣāṃ agadaṃkāraṃ vaidyam | ātaṅkassaṃjvare roge sādhvase murajadhvanau’ itiratnamālā ||
prātikūlye kevalaparaṃparitaṃ yathā
aghayūdhatimiradhūnanavibhātamahirājabhūdharaniketam | saṃprāpya bhavaniśāyāṃ śayitaḥ ko nāma na prabodhamiyāt || 225 ||
prātikūlye mālāparaṃparitaṃ yathā
sauhārdacandradarśaissaumukhyalatāvānalasparśaiḥ | saukhyajalātapamarśairalamanyaistvayi satīśa durdarśaiḥ
he īśa bhagavan ||
yathā vā
dititanujamṛgavitaṃsaṃ duritadviradālikesarivataṃsam | phaṇiśikharipreyāṃsaṃ bhaje vipatpākaghūkakulahaṃsam || 227 ||
dititanujamṛgāṇāṃ vitaṃsaṃ bandhanopakaraṇaṃ ‘vitaṃsastūpakaraṇaṃ bandhane mṛgapakṣiṇām’ ityamaraḥ | vipadāṃ vipākaḥ paripākaḥ sa eva ghūkakulaṃ divāndhayūdhaṃ tasya haṃsaṃ bhānum | pūrvamudāharaṇaṃ bhagavaditarāśrayaṇanindanaparaṃ, idaṃ tu bhagavatpraśaṃsāparamiti viśeṣaḥ || yathā vā
haribhaktibījarājeririṇaiśśubhaśīlabālatṛṇahariṇaiḥ | śamadamavanadavadahanairalaṃ khalairativiśṛṅkhalairdṛṣṭaiḥ || 228 ||
haribhaktireva bījarājiḥ tasyāḥ iriṇaiḥ ūṣaraiḥ ‘iriṇaṃ śūnyamūṣaram’ ityamaraḥ | śubhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ śīlaṃ sadvṛttaṃ tadeva bālatṛṇaṃ tasya hariṇaiḥ kuraṅgaiḥ | śamaḥ kāmakrodhādyabhāvaḥ damaḥ tapaḥ kleśasahanaṃ indriyanigraho vā | ‘śamathastu śamaśśāntirdāntistu damatho damaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tayossamāhāraḥ śamadamaṃ tadeva vanaṃ tasya davadahanaiḥ | ativiśṛṅkhalaiḥ khalaiḥ dṛṣṭairalaṃ taddarśanamapyasmākamanabhimatamiti bhāvaḥ |
yathā vā
sajjanagovrajarakṣaṇatarakṣubhiḥ kṣubhitamabhita idamasuraiḥ | duritadavaśamanapavanairbhavanaṃ ko’cyuta vinā’vitā bhavitā || 229 ||
atra rakṣaṇaśamanapade virodhilakṣaṇayā viparītārthabodhake | ayamarthaḥ dvayoreva mālārūpatvaṃ ca pūrvebhyo’tra viśeṣaḥ | evaṃ padārtharūpakaṃ diṅmātraṃ darśitameva | vākyārthe viṣaye vākyārthāntarasyārope vākyārtharūpakam | yathā hi viśiṣṭopamāyāṃ viśeṣaṇānāmupamānopameyabhāva ārthaḥ tathā’trāpi vākyārthaghaṭakānāṃ padārthānāṃ rūpakamarthāvaseyamiti rasagaṅgādharakṛdādayaḥ | anye tu vākyārthe vākyārthāntarāropaṃ nidarśanāṃ manvānāḥ rūpake na kvacidapi bimbapratibambabhāvāpannadharmaviśiṣṭatayā viṣayaviṣayiṇorupādānam | sāvayavarūpake’pi viśeṣyarūpaṇātpṛthagevāvayaveṣu rūpaṇaṃ na tvavayavaviśiṣṭarūpeṇa viśeṣye
aṅghridaṇḍo harerūrdhvamutkṣipto balinigrahe |
vidhiviṣṭarapadmasya nāladaṇḍo mude’stu vaḥ ||
ityatrāpi aṅghridaṇḍanāladaṇḍayorna bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannavastuviśiṣṭarūpatā | tayoḥ pratīyamānena śyāmalatvavidhātṛviṣṭarakamalaśliṣṭatvādyanugāmidharmeṇaivopamānopameyabhāvāt | kiṃtu vidhātṛviṣṭarakamalaśliṣṭatvarūpasādhāraṇadharmavattāsaṃpādanāya viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭatāmātramupameyasyeti vākyārthe vākyārthāropaṃ na rūpakamityāhuḥ
tatra prāthamikamatena vākyārtharūpakaṃ yathā
śrutiśatanutayaśasaste pratiyatno mādṛśāṃ vacobhiryat | ghanasārasyānyaistatsurabhīkaraṇaṃ nisargatassurabheḥ || 230 ||
pratiyatno guṇādhānam | atra bhagavati vacassu cāropaviṣayaviśeṣaṇatayā bimbabhūteṣu ghanasārasya sugandhivastvantarāṇāṃ ca viṣayiviśeṣaṇatvena pratibimbānāṃ rūpakaṃ gamyamānaṃ pradhānībhūtaviśiṣṭarūpakāṅgam | rūpake bimbapratibimbabhāvāsaṃbhavānnedaṃ rūpakamiti vadatāṃ vacanaṃ tvanādartavyameva | yayorivādiśabdaprayoge upamā tayorekatrānyārope rūpakamiti niyamāt | yadāhuḥ ‘upamaiva tirobhūtabhedā rūpakam’ iti | yadyatra nābhyupagacchasi rūpakaṃ māsmaivābhyupagacchaḥ tarhi tatra ivayathādiśabdaprayoge mābhyupagā upamāmapi | evaṃ
tvayi kopo mahīpāla sudhāṃśāviva pāvakaḥ|
ityādau | tvayi nigrahasaṃkalpaḥ payasijanilaye dayāsudhājaladhau | śuklapratipadudañcitacandrakalāyāṃ kalaṅka iva bhāyāt || 231 ||
ityādau ca svakalpitena viśiṣṭena dharmiṇā sādṛśyasya pratyayādyadyupamāṃ bravīṣi brūhi tāvattarhi tatraiva ivasyāpasāraṇe | ‘tvayi kopo mahīpāla sudhāṃśau havyavāhanaḥ’ ‘śuklapratipadudañcitacandrakalāyāṃ kalaṅko’yam’ ityādau rūpakamapi | nanvevaṃ rūpake bimbapratibimbabhāvasaṃbhave
tvatpādanakharatnānāṃ yadalaktakamārjanam |
idaṃ śrīkhaṇḍalepena pāṇḍurīkaraṇaṃ vidhoḥ ||
ityatrāpi rūpakameva syāt | bimbapratibimbabhāvena dharmasya sadbhāvāditi cet iṣṭaiveyamāpattiḥ | tarhyasya prācīnairnidarśanodāharaṇatoktirvirudhyeteti cet kāmaṃ virudhyatāṃ nidarśanodāharaṇatvāyogādasya | yadyatra ‘mukhaṃ candraḥ’ ityādirūpakāntara iva satyapi śrautārope nedaṃ rūpakaṃ, kiṃtu nidarśanaivetyāgṛhyate tadā mukhacandra ityapi nidarśanetyucyatāṃ nirasyatāṃ ca rūpakamukhāvalokanadākṣiṇyam | kiṃca tvatpādetyādau kiṃ padārthanidarśanā? āhosvidvākyārthanidarśanā? nādyaḥ bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannapadārthaghaṭitaviśiṣṭārthayorevātrābhedapratīteḥ | kuvalayānandoktarītyā dharmyantare padārthe tadavṛttidharmasya padārthasyābhedenāropasyābhāvācca | na dvitīyaḥ, vākyārtharūpakocchedāpatteḥ | iṣṭāpattau vaiparītyasyāpi suvacatvācca | asmābhirnidarśanāprakaraṇe vakṣyamāṇayā saraṇyā abhedasya śrautatvārthatvābhyāṃ uddeśyavidheyabhāvāliṅganānāliṅganābhyāṃ ca rūpakanidarśanayorvailakṣaṇyena sakalavyavasthopapatteḥ | tasmādatra vākyārtharūpakameva | na vākyārthanidarśanā | tasyāścaivamudāharaṇaṃ nirmātavyaṃ
tvatpādanakharatnāni yo rañjayati yāvakaiḥ |
induṃ candanalepena pāṇḍurīkurute hi saḥ ||
iti | atra kartrorabhedasya śābdatve’pi kriyayorabhedasyāśābdatvāttasyaiva samagrabhārasahiṣṇutvānnidarśanaiva | yadapi bimbapratibimbabhāvo rūpake nāstītyuktaṃ, tadayuktaṃ, vimarśinīkārādibhiḥ ‘kandarpadvipakarṇakambumalinaiḥ’ ityādinā bimbapratibimbabhāvena rūpakasyodāhṛtatvāt | vistarastu rasagaṅgādhare draṣṭavyaḥ ||
phaṇipatigiricūḍāmaṇiharisevanajṛmbhito mamānandaḥ| udayagiriśikharagatavidhuvilokalulito mahābdhirudvelaḥ ||231 ||
ityādāvapi viśiṣṭarūpakaṃ bodhyam | ‘tvayi nigrahasaṃkalpaḥ’ ityatra viṣayiṇassvabuddhiparikalpitatvātkalpitaṃ viśiṣṭarūpakam | iha tu na tatheti vailakṣaṇyam ||
idaṃ rūpakaṃ vaiyadhikaraṇyenāpi dṛśyate, yathā vā
vasanarucā pallavitaṃ hāragamuktāgaṇena korakitam | kaustubhamaṇinā phalitaṃ śrīvihṛtivanaṃ hareruro lalitam ||232 ||
vasanarucā pītāmbaranatviṣā | hareruraḥ śrīvihṛtivanamiti yojanā | atra viṣayāṇāṃ vasanarucyādīnāṃ viṣayiṇāṃ pallavādīnāṃ ca vibhaktibhedaḥ | vasanaruceti tṛtīyāyā abhedo’rthaḥ | vasanarugabhinnapallavavaditi bodhaḥ | vasanarugabhede tu pallaveṣu gṛhyamāṇe harerurasi vihāravanatvarūpakaṃ paripoṣyate ||
sādhāraṇadharmaścātrāpyupamāyāmiva kvacidanugāmī kvacidbimbapratibimbabhāvāpannaḥ kvacidupacaritaḥ kvacicca śleṣātmā kevalaśabdarūpaḥ so’pi kvacicchabdenopāttaḥ kvacitpratīyamānatayā nopāttaḥ ||
tatropātto’nugāmī yathā
durviṣahaviṣamaviṣanibhajanmajarādivyadhāturānlokān | pālayituṃ vikirasi phaṇiśailapate nanu kaṭākṣamamṛtaṃ tvam || 233 ||
atra pālayitumiti tumunnantena śabdenopāttaṃ lokapālanaṃ mṛtabhagavatkaṭākṣayoranugāmī dharmaḥ ||
ayamevānupātto yathā
paripāṭī tuhinānāṃ paripākaśśītakiraṇakiraṇānām | parivartanamamṛtānāṃ paraṃparā śrīpaterapāṅgānām || 234 ||
atra tuhinaparipāṭīprabhṛtīnāṃ śrīnivāsāpāṅgānāṃ cānandadānādiranupāttaḥ pratīyamāno dharmaḥ ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannastu viśiṣṭarūpakaprasaṅge nirūpitaḥ ||
upacarito yathā
śiśiratayā tava karuṇā śiśirāṃśumarīcivīcikā nūnam | hṛdayaṃ śirīṣakusumaṃ padayugamahiśailadīpa padmayugam || 235 ||
atra śiśirāṃśumarīcivīcikārūpake viṣaye bhagavatkaruṇāyāmupacaritaśśiśiratārūpo dharmaśśabdenopāttaḥ | śirīṣakusumādirūpake ca mārdavasaukumāryādirupacarito dharmo nopāttaḥ ||
kevalaśleṣātmako yathā
acalaśikhāvalamānā suśobhinī rādhikā’mbaragrāhī | (rādhikādharāmṛtadā) | śampāvanī dadhānā śriyamapi harimūrtirambudharamālā || 236 ||
acalaśikhāyāṃ śeṣādriśikhare valamānā saṃcarantī, anyatra acalaḥ sthiraḥ śikhāvalānāṃ kekināṃ mānaḥ cittasamunnatiryasyāsseti pañcamībahuvīhiḥ | suṣṭhu śobhata iti suśobhinī | rādhikāmbaragrāhī ambaraṃ gṛhṇātītyambaragrāhī ‘karmaṇyaṇ’ ityaṇi aṇṇantalakṣaṇo ṅīp | rādhikāyāḥ ambaragrāhī sicayagrāhiṇī | bhagavatkartṛkasyāmbaragrahaṇasya tadvigrahe upacāraḥ | pakṣe suśobhi yat nīraṃ jalaṃ tena adhikā | bhinnaṃ padam | ambaraṃ antarikṣaṃ gṛhṇāti svasaṃcārārthamādatta ityambaragrāhī gaganacāriṇīti yāvat | dvitīyārdhasya ‘suśobhinī rādhikādharāmṛtadā’ iti pāṭhāntaram | tadā suśobhinī ukto’rthaḥ | rādhikāyāḥ adharāmṛtaṃ dadātīti tathoktā rādhāyai svādharasudhāṃ prayacchantītyarthaḥ | anyatra suśobhinīreṇa adhikā ata eva dharāyāḥ bhuvaḥ amṛtadā salilavarṣiṇītyarthaḥ | śaṃ pāvanīti bhinne pade pāvanī pavitrā śaṃ sukhaṃ śriyaṃ padmāmapi dadhānā anyatra śaṃpāyāḥ taṭita avanī pālanī ‘śampā śatahradā hrādinyairāvatyaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | śriyaṃ śyāmāṃ śobhāmiti yāvat | dadhānā apiśabdaḥ pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇasamuccāyakaḥ | harimūrtiḥ śrīnivāsatanuḥ ambudharamālā kādambinyeva | atra acalaśikhāvalamānādiśabda upātta eva pratīyate na tu luptaḥ ||
yathā vā
