bhujagendrabhūbhṛti sadā saurabhasātkṛtatathāvidhābhyudayaḥ | haricandanatarurindhe samunnataśśrīlatā manojñavapuḥ || 237 ||
bhujagendrabhūbhṛti śeṣādrau | sadā asau iti chedaḥ| rabhasāt varṣāt śaraṇāgataparitrāṇatvarāviśeṣādvā ‘rabhaso vegaharṣayoḥ’ iti viśvaḥ | kṛtaḥ tathāvidhaḥ āgopālaṃ sākṣātkārārhaḥ abhyudayaḥ āvirbhāvo yena sa tathoktaḥ | mudā saha vartanta iti samudaḥ natāḥ śaraṇāgatāḥ yasya saḥ samunnataḥ | śrīrasminnastīti śrīlaṃ śrīśabdasya sidhmādiṣu pāṭhāt ‘sidhmādibhyaśca’ iti matvarthīyo lac | ‘lakṣmīvān lakṣmaṇaśśrīlaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | śrīlasya bhāvaḥ śrīlatā tayā manojñaṃ vapuryasya satathoktaḥ lakṣmīviśiṣṭatayā ramaṇīyadivyamaṅgalavigraha ityarthaḥ | harireva haricandanataruḥ sadā indhe iti bhagavatpakṣe ||
candanatarupakṣetu sadāsaurabhasātkṛtatathāvidhābhyudaya iti samastaṃ padam | sadāsaurabhasātkṛtaḥ parimalavyāptaḥ kṛtaḥ tathāvidhaḥ abhyudayaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ utkarṣo vā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘abhividhau saṃpadā ca’ iti kṛbhvastiyoge vaikalpikatayā vihitassātipratyayaḥ | abhividhirvyāptiḥ | samunnatā samadhikā śrīḥ kisalayakusumādiśobhā yāsāṃ tāḥ latāḥ śākhāḥ ‘same śākhālate’ ityamaraḥ | tābhiḥ manojñaṃ abhirāmaṃ vapuryasya sa tatoktaḥ haricandanataruḥ bhujagendrabhūbhṛti malayagirāviti gamyate | indhe iti yojanā ||
ayameva sādhāraṇo yatra yuktirūpeṇopanyasyate taddheturūpakam || yathā
amburuhalocano’yaṃ jambūtarureva śātakumbhādrau harati kathamitarathā pṛthumadhuraphalaiśchāyayā ca janatāpān || 238 ||
śātakumbhādrau śeṣādrau | pṛthubhiḥ vipulaiḥ madhuraiḥ āsvādanīyaiḥ priyaiśca phalaiḥ prasiddhaiḥ, muktyaiśvaryāntaiḥ prayojanaiśca chāyayā anātapena divyamaṅgalavigrahalāvaṇyena ca | janānāṃ tāpān ātapabhavān ādhyātmikādīṃśca | atra śleṣeṇa jambūtarorbhagavati tādrūpyapratyayāddhi rūpakam | hetustu śliṣṭa eva | evamanye’pi prakārā vijñeyāḥ ||
saridadhipayaśobṛndaṃ sarasīruhanābhalocanānandam | karayugadhṛtāravindaṃ śaraṇaṃ tatkimapi daivatamavindam || 239 ||
atra upameye upamānasya nāropaḥ apitu kāraṇe kāryasyeti na rūpakaṃ bhavatīti prācīnāḥ | etanmatānusāreṇaivāsmābhirapi lakṣitam | ucchṛṅkhalāḥ punarāropamātraṃ rūpakamabhidadhānā īdṛśasthale’pi rūpakamevābhidadhate | vastutastviha vakṣyamāṇo hetuhetumadaikyarūpo hetvalaṅkāra eva ||
nanu
haribhaktibījarājeririṇaiśśubhaśīlabālatṛṇahariṇaiḥ|
śamadamavanadavadahanairalaṃ khalairativiśṛkhalairdṛṣṭaiḥ ||
ityatra iriṇahariṇadavadahanaiḥ khalānāṃ kiṃ sādharmyaṃ yena teṣāmapi rūpakamudīryata iti cet haribhaktibījarājyoḥ śubhaśīlabālatṛṇayoḥ śamadamavanayośca tādrūpye śabdādupasthāpite’nantaramupasthitaṃ haribhaktirūpabījarājyādyabhāvavattvameva taditi gṛhāṇa | evamapi iriṇādīnāṃ khalānāṃ ca tādrūpyasiddhau iriṇādirūpakhalāvṛttitvena haribhaktibījarājyādīnāṃ tādrūpyaṃ sidhyet haribhaktibījarājyādīnāṃ tādrūpyasiddhau ca haribhaktirūpabījaparājyādiśūnyatvena iriṇādīnāṃ khalānāṃ ca tādrūpyamityanyonyāśrayo na śaṅkanīyaḥ | sarvasiddheḥ kalpanāmayatvena kalpanāyāśca svapratibhākalpalatādhīnatvāt | śilpavedibhiranyonyāvaṣṭambhamātrāyattasthitikābhirupaleṣṭakābhirvicitrabhavanaviśeṣanirmiteśceti ||
atredamavadheyam camatkāraviśeṣādhāyakatayā rūpakasaṃdohātmakamapi sāvayavarūpakaṃ rūpakālaṃkārabhedagaṇanāyāṃ gaṇyate yathā vajraratnālaṃkārabhedagaṇanāyāmekaṃ vajracūḍāratnamiva saṃdohātmakavajrakaṭakāṅgadādayo’pi gaṇyante | anyathā mālārūpasyopamādestadbhedagaṇane’gaṇanāprasaṅgāt | etena gavāṃ saṃghāto gobhedānāṃ kapilādīnāṃ gaṇanāyāṃ yathā na gaṇyate tathā rūpakabhedagaṇanāprastutau na saṃghātātmakaṃ sāvayavaṃ gaṇanīyamiti parāstam | evamasmātsaṃghātātmakātsāvayavānmālārūpakasya saṃghātātmakatvenāviśeṣe’pyekaviṣayakatvaparasparanirapekṣatvābhyāmasti mahadvailakṣaṇyamiti ||
vimarśinīkārastu rūpakanirūpaṇāvasare ‘kvaciccābhedameva draḍhayituṃ viṣayiṇo niṣedhapūrvakamāropyamāṇatvena tadīyasya vā’bhedahetordharmasya hānikalpanenādhikyena vā dṛḍhāropatvenāpīdaṃ dṛśyate’ ityabhāṇīt | tatrādyasyodāharaṇaṃ paryastāpahnutinirūpaṇāvasare darśayiṣyati | dvitīyatṛtīyayorudāharaṇe tu kuvalayānandakṛnmatena rūpakanirūpaṇāvasare darśite nyūnādhikābhedatādrūpyarūpakodāhāharaṇe eveti dhyeyam ||
athāsya dhvaniḥ
tatra śabdaśaktimūlo yathā
vimateṣu tvaṃ jiṣṇurdaṇḍadharaścāsi puṇḍarīkākṣa | vinateṣu tu pracetā dhanado’pi ca bhavasi kanakaśikharimaṇe || 240 ||
kanakaśikharī śeṣādrirityavocāma | prasannaṃ ceto yasya saḥ pracetāḥ | ‘prādibhyo dhātujasya’ ityuttarapadalopaḥ | atra prakaraṇena śaktiniyantraṇe’pīndrayamavaruṇakuberādyabhedā bhagavati vyajyante ||
yathā vā
aiśānīmāśāṃ tvaṃ dhatse dhṛtapadmako mahotsedhaḥ || karapuṣkaralulitābjo deva tvaṃ supratīka evāsi || he deva tvaṃ aiśānīṃ śambhusaṃbandhinīṃ āśāṃ manorathaṃ diśaṃ ca dhatse puṣṇāsi bibharṣi ca | dhṛtā padmā śrīḥ yena sa dhṛtapadmakaḥ ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | pakṣe dhṛtaṃ padmakaṃ bindujālakaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ | mahān utsedhaḥ utkarṣa aunnatyaṃ ca yasya saḥ | karapuṣkareṇa pāṇipadmena śuṇḍāgrabhāgena ca lulitaṃ abjaṃ śaṃkho yena saḥ lulitāni abjāni kamalāni yena saḥ śobhanaṃ pratīkaṃ vigraho yasya saḥ pakṣe diggajaviśeṣaśca | atrāpi prakaraṇavaśādbhagavati śaktiniyantraṇe’pi supratīkadiggajābhedo vyajyate ||
arthaśaktimūlo yathā
bhagavanbhavatpratāpo haritaḥ paritaḥ prakāśayati satatam | vidalayati kamalavalayaṃ śokaṃ lumpati ca kokalokānām || 242 ||
atra haritprakāśakatvādinā bhagavatpratāpastapanābhinna iti rūpakaṃ dhvanyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre rūpakasarassaptamaḥ
<9>
he deva tvaṃ aiśānīṃ śambhusaṃbandhinīṃ āśāṃ manorathaṃ diśaṃ ca dhatse puṣṇāsi bibharṣi ca | dhṛtā padmā śrīḥ yena sa dhṛtapadmakaḥ ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | pakṣe dhṛtaṃ padmakaṃ bindujālakaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ | mahān utsedhaḥ utkarṣa aunnatyaṃ ca yasya saḥ | karapuṣkareṇa pāṇipadmena śuṇḍāgrabhāgena ca lulitaṃ abjaṃ śaṃkho yena saḥ lulitāni abjāni kamalāni yena saḥ śobhanaṃ pratīkaṃ vigraho yasya saḥ pakṣe diggajaviśeṣaśca | atrāpi prakaraṇavaśādbhagavati śaktiniyantraṇe’pi supratīkadiggajābhedo vyajyate ||
arthaśaktimūlo yathā
bhagavanbhavatpratāpo haritaḥ paritaḥ prakāśayati satatam | vidalayati kamalavalayaṃ śokaṃ lumpati ca kokalokānām || 242 ||
atra haritprakāśakatvādinā bhagavatpratāpastapanābhinna iti rūpakaṃ dhvanyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre rūpakasarassaptamaḥ
atha pariṇāmasaraḥ
viṣayī svātmanā yatra prakṛtānupayogataḥ |
viṣayātmatvamabhyeti pariṇāmassa īritaḥ ||
yatrāropyamāṇo viṣayī kiṃcitkāryopayogitayā nibadhyamānassvātantryeṇa tasya tadupayogitvāsaṃbhavātprakṛtātmatāpariṇatimapekṣate sa pariṇāmaḥ | ata evānvarthasaṃjño’yam | atra ca viṣayābhedo viṣayiṇyupayujyate | rūpake tu naivamiti bhavati rūpakādasya bhidā | ayaṃ ca pariṇāmo dvividhassāmānādhikaraṇyavaiyadhikaraṇyābhyām | sāmānādhikaraṇye’pi samāsago vākyagaśceti dvividhaḥ | tatrādyo yathā ||
dviradādhipavaradānasphuradādaraṇasya phaṇigiriniyantuḥ | suradānavanutakīrtervaradāyi karābjamavatu sukumāram || 243 ||
atra hyabjasya varadānādikaṃ nibadhyate na tu karasya | mayūravyaṃsakādisamāsenottarapadārthaprādhānyāt | na ca karaḥ abjamivetyupamitasamāsāvalambanena pūrvapadārthapradhānataiva kuto na syāditi vācyaṃ, sāmānyāprayoga eva tadanuśāsanāt | iha ca sukumāramiti sāmānyadharmaprayogāt | abjasya svato varadānādikāryānupayogitayā prakṛtabhagavatkarātmatāpariṇatyapekṣaṇātpariṇāmaḥ ||
yathā vā
tāvakaśubhaguṇasaurabhavisarairadhivāsitaṃ satāṃ svāntam | durdāntadaivatāntaradurgandhaṃ dūrato jahāti hare || 244 ||
atra saurabhasya svātantryeṇa satpuruṣasvāntādhivāsane’nupayogādbhagavacchubhaguṇatayā pariṇāmaḥ || yathā vā
budhvā’’cāryānugrahasiddhāñjanato’ñjanācalendo tvām | vaśayanviśadasvānto bhṛśamabhinandeyamupaśamitatṛṣṇaḥ || 245 ||
atra siddhāñjanasyācāryānugrahatayaiva bhagavajjñānatadvaśīkaraṇakṣamatvam ||
yathā vā
śrīveṅkaṭādriśekharakaṭākṣamayakaṅkaṭāvṛto yogī | caṇḍakaramaṇḍalamapi praveṣṭumīṣṭe sahasrahetimayam || 246 ||
hetayaḥ śastrāṇi arcīṃṣi cetyarthaḥ| ‘raverarciśca śastraṃ ca vanhijvālā ca hetayaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | kaṅkaṭaḥ kavacaḥ | atra kaṅkaṭasya bhagavatkaṭākṣatayā pariṇāmādeva caṇḍakaramaṇḍalapraveśakṣamateti dhyeyam ||
vākyago yathā
kiṃnu tayā saṃpattyā vaidhātryā vā’pi karmapariṇatyā | tvāṃ prepsorvitara hare bhaktiṃ dhanameva me viraktighanam || 247 ||
‘karmaṇāṃ paripākatvādā virañcādamaṅgalam’ ityuktasvarūpayā brāhmyā’pi saṃpadā tvāmeva prepsorme kiṃ sādhyam | bhaktiṃ tvadbhaktirūpameva dhanaṃ dehītyarthaḥ | atrāropyamāṇaṃ dhanaṃ bhaktirūpāpannameva vitaraṇarūpakāryopayogi, na svātmanā | kaverbhagavatprāptimātramapekṣamāṇasya vairiñcivibhūterapi tṛṇīkaraṇena kevaladhanārthitāvirahāt ||
yathā vā
phaṇadharadharaṇidharendā smaraṇaṃ tava caraṇayoḥ katakareṇuḥ | saṃśayadhūsaratāṃ nassaṃśamayati haṃta sarvatatvārthe || 248 ||
atrāropyamāṇaḥ katareṇurbhagavatsmaraṇatāpattyaiva saṃśayadhūsaratāsaṃśamanalakṣaṇakārye upayujyate na svātantryeṇeti bhavati pariṇāmaḥ | udāharaṇabāhulyaṃ buddhivaiśadyāya ||
vyadhikaraṇo yathā
adhararucā rāgavatī nalinavatī nayanapāṇicaraṇena | lakṣmīḥ prabhātalakṣmīrādhatte naḥ prabodhamanavadhikam || 249 ||
atrānavadhikaprabodhādhānaṃ parimitaprabodhādhāyinyāḥ prabhātalakṣmyāssvātmanā | bādhitaṃ lakṣmīrūpeṇa tu saṃgacchata iti bhavati pariṇāmaḥ | sa ca parasparāsāpekṣabahusaṃghātātmakatayā sāvayavaḥ| tatra pūrvārdhagatau dvāvavayavau vyadhikaraṇau uttarārdhagataścaikassamānādhikaraṇaḥ| anavadhikaṃ prabodhaṃ asaṃkucitaṃ jñānaṃ muktyaiśvaryamiti yāvat ||
atredamavadhātavyam yatra viṣayasya viṣayitādrūpyāpattyā prakṛtakāryopayogitvaṃ tatra rūpakam, yatra tu viṣayiṇo viṣayatādrapyāpattyā kāryopayogitvaṃ tatra pariṇāma iti | kāvyaprakāśakārādibhistu ubhayatrāpyuttarapadārthapradhānamayūravyaṃsakādisamāsāviśeṣādīdṛśaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ nātīva camatkārayiti pariṇāmaḥ pṛthaganupāttaḥ ||
kecittu ”kvacitkevalo viṣayassvātmanā na prakṛtopayogītyayamāropyamāṇābhinnatayā’vatiṣṭhate tatrāropyamāṇapariṇāmaḥ, yathā ‘vadanenendunā tanvī śiśirīkurute dṛśau’ atra vadanamindvabhinnatayā’vatiṣṭhate, kevalasya vadanasya dṛkchiśirīkārakatvāyogāt | kvaciccāropyamāṇassvātmanā na prakṛtakāryopayogītyayaṃ viṣayābhinnatayā’vatiṣṭhate | tatra viṣayapariṇāmaḥ, yathā ‘vadanenendunā tanvī smaratāpaṃ vilumpati’ atrendurvadanābhinnatayā’vatiṣṭhate, kevalasyendoḥ smaratāpāpanodakatvāyogāt | evaṃ ca pariṇāmadvayātmakamidaṃ rūpakameva bhavitumarhati viṣayatāvacchedakaviṣayitāvacchedakānyatarapuraskāreṇa niścīyamānaviṣayiviṣayānyataratvasya tallakṣaṇatvāt | ata evoktaṃ ‘tadrūpakamabhedo ya upamānopameyayoḥ’ iti | tasmānna rūpakātpariṇāmo’tiricyate” iti vadanti | vastutastūktarītyā vicchittiviśeṣasya sadbhāve alaṃkārāntaratvamavaśyābhyupetyam | anyathā tulyayogitādīpakādīnāmapi yatkiṃcidvailakṣaṇyasadbhāvena pṛthagalaṃkāratā na syāditi dik ||
atha pariṇāmadhvaniḥ
tatrārthaśaktimūlapariṇāmadhvaniryathā
kalpamahīruha kalpaya niratiśayāṃ saṃpadaṃ nitāntaṃ naḥ | yāṃ santo’nubhavanto janmajarādivyathāṃ na dhāvanti || 250 ||
atra vaktuḥ paricchinnaiśvaryaviraktatayā vyajyamānabhagavadabhinnatayā kalpatarurvivakṣitaḥ, tena rūpeṇaiva tasyottarārdhavivakṣitamuktyaiśvaryarūpaniratiśayasaṃpatsaṃpādakatvāt | na cātra viṣayanigaraṇātmakātiśayoktiśśakyā vaktum | tasyāṃ hyāropyamāṇābhinnatvena viṣayasya pratyayaḥ | yathā ‘kamalaṃ kanakalatāyām’ ityādau kanakalatābhinnāyāṃ vanitāyāṃ kamalābhinnaṃ mukhamiti | atra tu bhagavataḥ kalpamahīruhābhinnatvena pratyaye punarniratiśayasaṃpatsaṃpādanalakṣaṇaprakṛtakāryasiddhiriti kalpakasyāropyamāṇasya bhagavadrūpaviṣayābhinnatvaṃ mṛgyam | tacca vyaṅgyatāyāmeva bhavatīti pariṇāmadhvanirevāyam, nātiśayoktiḥ ||
śabdaśaktimūlo yathā
kaṃjāyatākṣi mugdhe saṃjvaramanuviṃdase sakhi kimartham | āśāsva ghanaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ yena tvaṃ śāntimeṣyasi kṣipram || 251 ||
idaṃ kāñcidgopikāṃ prati kasyāścitsakhyā vacanam | atra jhagiti saṃjvaraśamanahetutvenopasthite nīlāmbude paścānmugdhārūpaboddhavyāviśeṣyakasmarasaṃjvaravattāvaiśiṣṭyabuddhau satyāṃ sahṛdayāyāstasyāḥ tādṛśasaṃjvaraśāntikāriśrīkṛṣṇarūpaviṣayatādrūpyabuddhirjāte | ayaṃ śabdaśaktimūlaḥ ||
yathāvā
ayi dūyase kimarthaṃ sādho dūraṃ vidhehi jāḍyabhayam | pratyāsannaṃ mādhavamīkṣasva ya etadupaśamaṃ netā || 252 ||
idaṃ śiśirartau veṅkaṭācalaprānte nivasantaṃ kaṃcana sādhuṃ prati kasyacidvacanam | jāḍyaṃ śaityaṃ māndyaṃ ca | mādhavaṃ vasantaṃ bhagavantaṃ ca | atrāñjasā jāḍyaśāntinimittatayopasthite vasante paścādbodhanīyasādhuviśeṣyakajāḍyavattāvaiśiṣṭyabuddhau satyāṃ sahṛdayasya tādṛśajāḍyaśamayitṛśrīnivāsarūpaviṣayatādrūpyabuddhirbhavatītyayamapi śabdaśaktimūlaḥ pariṇāmadhvaniḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre pariṇāmasaro’ṣṭamaḥ
<10>
athollekhasaraḥ
grahītṛbhiranekairyadekasyānekadhā grahaḥ |
rūcyādikāraṇavaśāttamullekhaṃ pracakṣate || 43 ||
ekasya vastuno nimittavaśādyadanekairgrahītṛbhiranekaprakārakaṃ grahaṇaṃ sa ullekhaḥ | rucyadītyādiśabdenārthitvabhayādīnāṃ saṃgrahaḥ | ruciḥ abhiratiḥ | arthitvaṃ lipsā | bhayaṃ prasiddhameva | anekadhollekhe rucyādīnāṃ yathārhaṃ prayojakatvam ||
kanakācaleśvarakare kanantamabjaṃ sudhāṃśuriti cakram | dinamaṇiriti ca viditvā’harṣi cakoraiśca cakravākaiśca || 253 ||
atra cakoracakravākaiḥ pāñcajanyasudarśanayossudhāṃśutvena dinamaṇitvena ca grahaṇe bhrāntirūpe’tiprasaṅgavāraṇāyaikasya vastuna iti ||
vārinidhervātsalyaṃ vārijavipinasya vibhavapauṣkalyam | śaurermahimaujjvalyaṃ nārīratnaṃ smareyamanukalyam || 254 ||
ityādimālārūpake’tiprasaṅgavāraṇāyānekairgrahītṛbhirityaviva kṣitabahutvaṃ grahaṇaviśeṣaṇam ||
harimahiśiloccayāgre vilokya matvā balāhako’yamiti | mādyanti dantinivahā nṛtyanti kalāpināmapi kulāni || 255 ||
atra harirūpasyaikasya vastuno’nekairdantikalāpikulairgrahītṛbhirekenaiva balāhakatvarūpeṇa prakāraṇena grahaṇamiti tatrātiprasaṃgavāraṇāyānekaprakārakamiti | grahaṇamiti grahaṇasamudāyo vivakṣitaḥ | ekatvaṃ jātau, anekagrahītṛkasyaikasya grahaṇasyāprasiddheḥ | anekairgrahītṛbhiranekaprakārakaṃ grahaṇamityanena dvābhyāṃ dvi’dhāgrahaṇe’pyayamalaṃkāro’kṣata eva | ayaṃ ca dvividhaḥ | śuddholaṃkārāntarasaṃkīrṇaśceti | tatra śuddho yathā
ārto lakṣmīpatirityarthārthī tvāṃ mahāvadānya iti | jijñāsurjñānamiti jñānī tu śaraṇya iti bhajati bhagavan || 256 ||
atra
caturvidhā bhajante māṃ janāssukṛtino’rjuna |
ārtojijñāsurarthārthī jñānī ca bharatarṣabha ||
itiślokārtho’nusaṃhitaḥ | sukṛtinaḥ puṇyakarmāṇaḥ māṃ śaraṇamupagamya māmeva bhajante | te ca sukṛtatāratamyena caturvidhāḥ | sukṛtagarīyastvena pratipattivaiśeṣyāduttarottarādhikatamā bhavanti | ārtaḥ pratiṣṭhāhīno bhraṣṭaiśvaryaḥ punastatprāptikāmaḥ lakṣmīpatiriti bhajati aiśvaryadānasāmarthyābhiprāyakaṃ lakṣmīpatipadam | evamagre’pyūhyam | arthārthī aprāptaiśvaryatayā apūrvaiśvaryakāmaḥ | tayormukhabhedamātram | aiśvaryaviṣayatathaikyādeka evādhikāraḥ | mahāvadānya iti bhajati | jijñāsuḥ prakṛtiviyuktabrahmātmakātmasvarūpāvāptīcchuḥ jñānamevāsya svarūpamiti jijñāsurityuktam | jñānamiti bhajati | jñānasvarūpajīvaśarīraka iti bhajati | jñānī tu bhagavaccheṣataikarasātmasvarūpavit prakṛtiviyuktakevalātmanyaparyavasyan bhagavantaṃ prepsuḥ bhagavantameva paramaprāpyaṃ manvānaḥ śaraṇya iti paramaprāpya iti bhajatītyarthaḥ | atra ca lipsārucibhyāṃ nimittābhyāmārtādyanekagrahītṛkalakṣmīpatitvādyanekaprakārakagrahaṇasamudāyo bhagavadviṣayaratibhāvopaskārakaḥ śuddha evātrāyamullekhālaṃkāraḥ, rūpakādibhiramiśritatvāt ||
saṃkīrṇo yathā
tāvakapadanakharekhāṃ matvendukalāṃ namanmṛḍo makuṭam| spṛśati nijamahigirīśvara vajraṃ matvekṣate svakaramindraḥ || 257 ||
atra mṛḍādyekaikagrahaṇarūpayā bhrāntyā samudāyātmaka ullekhassaṃkīrṇaḥ ||
vṛṣagiripatiṃ tamālaṃ katicitkaticidvadantu harinīlam | brūmo vayamativelaṃ puñjitalakṣmīkaṭākṣarucijālam || 258 ||
atra bhagavatastamālatvādeḥ paramatatvena niṣedhyatayopanyāsādapahnutyā saṃkīrṇa ullekhaḥ ||
sandehasaṅkīrṇo yathā
tapano vā jvalano vetyarilalanā vetti tava kare cakram | svīyaṃ lakṣmīstu hare keyūraṃ vā’tha nūpuraṃ veti || 259 ||
atra dvayorgrahaṇayoḥ pratyekaṃ saṃdehatvaṃ samudāyasya tūllekhatā ||
avarodhasadma padmā manute bhūmirnivāsabhūmiṃ tvām | nīlā līlāśālāṃ nīlācalamaulilolanīlamaṇe || 260 || nīlācala iti śeṣādrereva nāmāntaram | atra yadyavarodhasadmatvādīnāmuparañjakatāmātreṇānvayastadā rūpakasaṃkīrṇa ullekhaḥ | yadi tādrūpyānubhavagocaratayā tadā bhrāntimatsaṃkīrṇaḥ ||
ayaṃ ca svarūpahetuphalollekhanarūpatvāttrividhaḥ | tatra svarūpollekhassamanantaramevodāhṛtaḥ ||
hetūllekho yathā
dānātkaticana kecidgānānnāmnāṃ viduḥ prasadanaṃ te | dhyānātpare vayaṃ tu śrīnāthākiṃcanatvavibhavena ||
atraikasyaiva prasadanasya hetūnāmanekadhātvenānekairullekhanam ||
phalollekho yathā
vipadapahṛtaye dīnāśśrīdānāyārthalipsavo manujāḥ | vṛddhāssvabandhamuktyai taddhāma vṛṣādribhuvi vadantyuditam || 262 ||
atraikasyaiva bhagavadudayasya phalānāmanekadhātvenollekhanam | taddhāma tattejaḥ paraṃ brahmetyarthaḥ ||
atha dvitīya ullekhaḥ
ekasya viṣayādīnāmanekatvanibandhanam |
naikadhātvaṃ grahītraikye’pyullekhassopi saṃmataḥ || 44
viṣayādīnāmityatrādiśabdenāśrayādigrahaṇam | yatrāsatyapi grahītṛbhede viṣayāśrayasaṃbandhināmanyatamānekatvaprayuktasya vastuno’nekaprakāratvamayamanya ullekhaḥ | tatra viṣayasaptamīnirdiṣṭo viṣaya ityucyate | adhikaraṇasaptamīnirdiṣṭastvāśrayaḥ | ṣaṣṭhyā nirdiṣṭassaṃbandhīti vivekaḥ | ayamapi śuddhasaṅkīrṇabhedena dvividhaḥ ||
tatra śuddho yathā
snigdhāḥ kaustubharatne digdhāḥ kṛpayā’tra mayi harau mugdhāḥ | dugdhāmburāśiduhitussragdhārā iva jayanti dṛgdhārāḥ || 263 ||
kaustubharatne snigdhāḥ snehayuktāḥ sahodaratvāt | atra asmin mayi kṛpayā digdhāḥ dīnatamatvāt | harau bhagavati mugdhāḥ vyāmohaśālinyaḥ | vallabhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | iha dṛgdhārāṇāṃ viṣayānekatvaprayukta ullekhaḥ ||
hasite sitamasitaṃ rucyetaṃ puruṣaṃ tathāmbare pītam | śoṇamadhare purāṇaṃ karacaraṇatale’ruṇaṃ śraye śaraṇam || 264 ||
rucīti saptamyantam | atraikasyaiva bhagavataḥ hasitādiṣu viṣayeṣvanekeṣu sitādivarṇānekatvamekenaiva grahītrollikhitamityāśrayānekatvaprayukto’yamullekhaḥ | etaṃ purāṇaṃ puruṣamiti yojanā | etaṃ uktarītyā nānāvarṇatvācchabalamityapi gamyate | ‘citrakimmīrakalmāṣaśabalaitāśca karbure’ ityamaraḥ ||
saṃbandhyanekatvaprayuktastvanantaramevodāhariṣyate | saṃkīrṇo yathā
mṛgamadatilakati niṭile mṛgapatigirinetururasi lāñchanati| bhramarati nābhīkamale kamalāyāḥ prasṛmarā kaṭākṣaruciḥ || 265 ||
atropamayā saṅkīrṇaḥ | yadvā tattadadhikaraṇaucityātkastūrītilakāditādātmyasambhāvanāyāmupamārthe vihito’pi kvibutprekṣāyāṃ paryavasyatītyupamopakramotprekṣā | etadvimarśastūtprekṣānirūpaṇāvasare bhaviṣyati | asmin pakṣe utprekṣayā saṃkīrṇo’yamāśrayānekatvaprayukta ullekhaḥ ||
yathā vā
mauktikasi dantapaṅktau nīlasi vāleṣu vidrumasyadhare | hīrasi nakheṣu kamale satyaṃ ratnākarātmajātvamiti || 266 ||
atropamāsaṅkīrṇaḥ kāvyaliṅgaśiraskaḥ ||
yathā vā
ūruyuge rambhā’si śrīrnāsāyāṃ tilottamābhikhyā | vaibhavato viśvācī tvamapsarojyāyasīti yuktamidam || 267 ||
he śrīḥ uruyuge rambhā kadalī tannāmakāpsarāśca| nāsāyāṃ tilottamasya tilakusumaśreṣṭhasya abhikhyā śobheva śobhā yasyāssā, tilottametyabhikhyā nāma yasyāssā, tathoktā tannāmāpsarā iti ca | ‘abhikhyā nāmaśobhayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | vaibhavataḥ vaibhave sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | viśvamañcatīti viśvācī sarvajagadvyāpinī, tannāmāpsarāśca | tasmāt tvaṃ apsarasāṃ jyāyasi śreṣṭhā tato’pyatisundarītyarthaḥ | pakṣe apsaraso jyāyasyo jyeṣṭhāḥ yasyāsseti bahuvrīhiḥ | ‘nadyṛtaśca’ iti prāptasya kapaḥ ‘īyasaśca’ iti niṣedhaḥ | ‘īyaso bahuvrīherneti vācyam’ iti niṣedhāt na ‘gostriyorupasarjanasya’ iti hrasvaḥ | viṣṇupurāṇādāvapsarasāṃ śrīlakṣmyapekṣayā prāgevāvirbhāvo’bhāṇi | purāṇāntareṣu yadi śriyo’nantarameva tāsāmutpattirupalabhyeta tadā ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣo’pyastu | itīdaṃ yuktamityanvayaḥ | atra śleṣarūpakasaṃkīrṇaḥ | kāvyaliṅgasaṃkīrṇasamālaṃkāraśiraskaḥ ||
saṃbandhyanekatvaprayukto yathā
bhaktānāṃ mandārā bhavataptānāmamūssudhādhārāḥ | nārāyaṇagirineturdārāṇāṃ santu mayi dṛgāsārāḥ ||
atra kavinā mandāratvādinā rūpeṇa kaṭākṣāṇāṃ rūpavatāṃ karaṇādrūpakeṇa bhaktādīnāmeteṣu kaṭākṣeṣvabhīpsitārthadānādinā mandāratvādibhrāntirapi saṃbhavatīti bhrāntimatā, bhaktādibhiranekairgrahītṛbhirmandāratvādyanekadharmeṇollekhanātprāguktollekhaprakāreṇa ca saha saṃkīrṇo’yaṃ saṃbandhiṣaṣṭhyantapadabhedaprayuktavarṇyānekatvaprakāra ullekhaḥ ||
kecittu sahacarānekatvaprayuktamanekavidhatvamapyullekhamāhuḥ | tadedamudāharaṇaṃ gopeṣu goparūpo bhūpeṣu vibhāsi dhanvimūrdhanyaḥ | tāpasayūdhe tāpasa eko’nekākṛtistvamabjākṣa ||
atra gopādisahacarabhedaprayuktaṃ bhagavato’nekavidhatvam | evamanyeṣāṃ sambandhināṃ bhede’pyūhyam ||
athollekhadhvaniḥ
yathā
nanu nātha mohabhugnā vyasanavivignā virodhibhirbhagnāḥ| upatāpairapi rugṇāstvāṃ vīkṣyānandasaṃplave magnāḥ || 270 ||
atra caraṇatrayoditānāṃ caturṇāṃ vīkṣaṇakartṝṇāmānandasaṃplavamagnatvoktyā krameṇa mohavyasanavirodhyupatāpanirāsakatvaprakārakāṇi grahaṇānyākṣipyante | ayaṃ ca śuddhasyollekhasya dhvaniḥ ||
saṃkīrṇasya yathā
ahigiriparisaradharaṇau viharati harinīlaruciraruci bhavati | anagacchanti kariṇyo nanu nṛtyanti ca rameśa barhiṇyaḥ || 271 ||
atra dhvanyamānayā ekaikagrahaṇarūpayā dviradajaladabhrāntyā tadubhayasamudāyarūpa ullekhassaṃkīrṇaḥ| na cātra bhrāntireva camatkāriṇītyullekhaśśakyo’pahnotum | anekakartṛkānekadhāgrahaṇasyālaṃkārāntaraviviktaviṣayasya vicchittiviśeṣasyehāpi jāgarūkatvāt ||
dvitīyasyollekhasya dhvaniryathā
gagane visṛtvarī te sthagayati kakubhāṃ mukhāni tanusuṣamā | dharaṇau tu vibudhataṭinīṃ pariripsurbhagavati phaṇigirisuradro || 172 ||
atrādhikaraṇabhedaprayuktamekasyāmeva śrīnivāsavigrahasuṣamāyāṃ kādambinītvayamunātvarūpānekavidhatvaṃ rūpakasaṃkīrṇaṃ dhvanyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre ullekhasaro navamaḥ
<11>
atha smṛtyalaṃkārasaraḥ
yā sādṛśyaparijñānodbuddhasaṃskāratassmṛtiḥ|
prayojyā sā smṛtirnāmālaṃkṛtiḥ kathyate budhaiḥ ||
sādṛśyajñānodbuddhasaṃskāraprayojyā smṛtiḥ smṛtirnāmālaṃkāraḥ | ayameva smaraṇaṃ smṛtimānityapi vyavahriyate |
yathā
suruciramauktikamaṇisaravisṛmaramasṛṇataraśucirucinimagne | kamalekṣaṇahṛdi kaustubhakamale dugdhābdhinivasatiṃ smarataḥ || 273 || kaustubhaśca kamalā ca kaustubhakamale mauktikasaraśucirucinimagne satyau dugdhābdhinivasatiṃ smarata iti yojanā ||
yathā vā
abhayaṃ dātuṃ sarabhasamibhavaraśirasi svahastamādhātuḥ | cetaḥ phaṇigirineturyātaṃ kamalākucadvayaṃ sphītam || 274 ||
atrādye smṛtirvācyā, dvitīye tu lakṣyeti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
lakṣmīkaṭākṣalaharīṃ vīkṣya śaratphullamallikā rātrīḥ | tāssmarati phaṇigirīndū rāsavilāsaṃ ca tāsu gopīnām || 275 ||
śaratphullamallikāḥ tāḥ rātrīrityetaiḥ padaiḥ ‘bhagavānapi tā rātrīśśaradotphullamallikāḥ’ iti śrībhāgavatīyapadyaṃ pratyabhijñāpyate | atra śaradrātrirāsavilāsayossmaraṇaṃ yadyapi na tatsādṛśyadarśanodbuddhasaṃskāraprayojyaṃ, tathā’pi lakṣmīkaṭākṣagatalaharīsādṛśyodbuddhayamunāviṣayakasaṃskārajanyatatsmaraṇādhīnatvādbhavatyeva yatkiṃcitsādṛśyadarśanodbuddhasaṃskāraprayojyam | na hi sādṛśye smaryamāṇasaṃbandhitvaṃ vivakṣitam | evaṃ vācyayośśaradrātrirāsavilāsasmaraṇayoḥ etatkāraṇatayā’’kṣiptasya yamunāsmaraṇasya cāviśeṣeṇa saṃgrahāya lakṣaṇe janyatvamapahāya prayojyatvamupāttam | kecittu sadṛśajñānodbuddhasaṃskārajanyaṃ sadṛśaviṣayakameva smaraṇamalaṃkāra ityāhuḥ, teṣāṃ śaradrātrirāsādismṛtiranalaṃkāra eva ||
adhibhujagarājaśailaṃ yaṃ ghananīlaṃ vilolavanamālam | niravarṇayamativelaṃ tameva devaṃ smarāmi śubhalīlam || 276 ||
atra smṛtiḥ cintodbuddhasaṃskāraprayojyatvānnālaṃkārapadavīmavagāhate | vyaṅgyatvavirahācca na bhāvapadavīm ||
evaṃ
kaliripuguruvarakaruṇāsurataṭinīdhūtamohamālinyaḥ | śeṣādripaternityaṃ śeṣitvaṃ śeṣatāṃ ca me’smārṣam || 277 ||
kaliripuguruvarāḥ śrīmatparakālayatīndramahādeśikāḥ | śeṣitvaṃ svāmitvaṃ śeṣatāṃ paragatātiśayādhānecchayā upādeyatvasvarūpatāṃ ca asmārṣam ||
svojjīvanecchā yadi te svasattāyāṃ yadi spṛhā |
ātmadāsyaṃ haressvāmyaṃ svabhāvaṃ ca sadā smara ||
ityuktarītyā smṛtavānityarthaḥ | ihāpi smṛtirnālaṃkāro nāpi bhāvaḥ | vyaṅgyasyaiva vyabhicāriṇo bhāvatvāt ||
yathā
gambhīrimā śiśirimā garimā paramādbhuto madhurimā ca | tattādṛgahivaragireruttaṃsādanyataḥ kva vā dṛṣṭaḥ || 278 ||
ayaṃ hyālaṃkārikāṇāṃ saṃpradāyaḥ yatsādṛśyamūlakatve smṛtirnidarśanādivadalaṃkāraḥ, tadvirahe vyaṅgyatāyāṃ bhāvaḥ | tayorabhāve tu vastumātramiti | yastu ‘sadṛśānubhavādvastvantarasmṛtiḥ smaraṇam’ iti smaraṇālaṃkāralakṣaṇaṃ tanna caturaśram, sadṛśasmaraṇādudbuddhena saṃskāreṇa janite smaraṇe avyāpteḥ ||
yathā
bhramara ciraṃ jīva tvaṃ smārayatā tvadavalambamamburuham | yena tvayā tadupame vilocane smārite vṛṣagirīndoḥ || 279 ||
atra bhramaradarśanādekasaṃbandhijñānājanitenāparasaṃbandhino’mburuhasya bhagavannayanasadṛśasya smaraṇena janitaṃ bhagavannayanasmaraṇaṃ bhagavadviṣayakaratibhāvāṅgatayā’laṃkāraḥ | yadi tu sadṛśānubhavādityetadapahāya sadṛśajñānāditi lakṣaṇe niveśyeta tadā bhavatyasyāpi saṃgraha iti dik ||
atredamavadheyaṃ sādṛśyaprayojakasya sādhāraṇadharmasya sākṣādupādānānupādānayorupamāyāmivātrāpi vyavasthā | tathāhi upamāyāṃ tāvatkvaciddharmo niyamena pratīyamānassākṣānnopādeya eva | yathā ‘śaṃkhavatpāṇḍuracchaviḥ’ ityatra pāṇḍuratvam | ‘śaṅkhavatpāṇḍuro’yam’ ityādau tu nānāvidheṣu dharmeṣvekenaiva dharmeṇa sādṛśyamityasya durvijñānatvāt sarvatropamānopameyasādhāraṇasya śliṣṭaśabdātmakasyānyasya vā svavivakṣitasya sādhāraṇadharmasyopamāprayojakatvasaṃbhavāttadvāraṇāya pāṇḍuratvādirdharmo vācyatāṃ nīyate | yathā vā ‘aravindamiva sundaraṃ mukhaṃ’ ityādau sundaratvādiḥ | na nīyate ca dharmaḥ kvacidvācyatāṃ vakturanyadharmānupasthiteḥ prasiddhaḥ prābalyāt | yathā ‘aravindamiva mukham’ ityādau sundaratvādireva | aprasiddhastu dharmo’vaśyaṃ sākṣādupādeya eva | anyathā tasyāpratipattau kavestadupamānirmāṇaprayāsavaiyarthyāpatteḥ | yathā ‘ghanā iva mahasārā janā yasyāṃ pratiṣṭhitāḥ’ ityādau śliṣṭaśśabdātmakaḥ | tathāca kaścitsādhāraṇo dharmassākṣādanupādeya eva, kaścidupādeyo’nupādeyaśca, kāścidupādeya eveti sahṛdayasammatassamayaḥ | evamevopamājīvātuke’smin smṛtyalaṃkāre’pīti | tatrānugāmini dharme ‘vilocane smārite vṛṣagirīndoḥ’ ityanupadodāhṛte padye niveditameva smaraṇam | tatrāmburuhabhagavadvilocanayorvipulatvavimalatvasundaratvādiranupāttassamāno’nugāmī dharmaḥ ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanne tvidamudāharaṇam
śāradanīradanibiḍitavṛṣagiriśikharāvalokanena hariḥ | śucikañcukamasmārṣītpadmāvatyāḥ payodharadvaṃndvam || 280 ||
atra bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanne samānadharme smṛtiḥ ||
upacarite yathā
madhurataraṃ tava caritaṃ madhumathanāhaṃ yadā niśamayāmi | adhyemi tadā cetomadhye madhuvidhusudhāmadhurasānām || 281 ||
madhurasā drākṣā ‘mṛdvīkā gostanī drākṣā svādvī madhuraseti ca’ ityamaraḥ | adhyemi smarāmi ‘ik smaraṇe’ ityasmāddhātoradhipūrvāllaṭ ‘adhīgartha’ iti ṣaṣṭhī | cetomadhye hṛdayāntare || yathā vā
nibirīsasaukumāryaṃ śirīṣakusumaṃ nirīkṣamāṇasya | smṛtisṛtimadhirohati mama sarīsṛpagirīśasahacarīhṛdayam || 282 ||
atra madhurataratvādirdharma upacaritaḥ | iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ yadekatrānubhūyamāne bhagavaccarite smaryamāṇasya madhuprabhṛtessādṛśyasya siddhiḥ | aparatra smaryamāṇe bhagavaddayitāhṛdaye’nubhūyamānasya śirīṣakusumasya sādṛśyasiddhiriti, ubhayāśrayatvātsādṛśyasya ||
śleṣātmake yathā
viniśāmyate yadā tava vibhāsinīhārabhāramāśliṣṭā | hṛdayasthalī tadā’cyutahṛdayaṃ me viśati haimanasamṛddhiḥ || 283 ||
vibhāsinī hārabhāsā ramayā ca samāśliṣṭā | pakṣe vibhāsī cāsau nīhārabhāraḥ himātiśayaḥ taṃ āśliṣṭā | kartari ktaḥ | hemantasyeyaṃ haimanī sā cāsau samṛddhiḥ hemantasaṃbaṃndhilakṣmīrityarthaḥ | hemantaśabdāt ‘sarvatrāṇ ca talopaśca’ ityaṇi talopaḥ | ‘ṭiḍhḍha’ iti ṅīp, puṃvadbhāvaśca ||
yathā vā
mṛdvīkāmitaphaladā yadā’kṣipadavīṃ gatā hareḥ pramadā | udyānavanī hṛdyā hṛdyārūḍhā tadā na kasya bhavet || 284 || atrādye vibhāsinītyādiśabdaḥ śrīnivāsahṛdayasthalīhaimanasamṛddhyoḥ sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | dvitīye mṛdvīkāmitaphaladetiśabdo haripriyodyānavanyoḥ | mṛdvī dayāmṛdulā kāmitaphaladā | pakṣe mṛdvīkānāṃ drākṣāṇāṃ amitāni phalāni dadāti tathoktā | evamanye’pi bhedāssudhībhirunneyāḥ ||
athāsya dhvaniḥ
yathā
ayameva me savidyunniyamena ghano manovinodī syāt | svayameva yadi vṛṣādrau na rameta ramāsakhaṃ paraṃ brahma || 285 ||
atra savidyutā ghanena salakṣmīkasya bhagavataḥ smaraṇamalaṃkāryāntarābhāvādanupasarjanam | lakṣmīvidyullakṣaṇasya bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannasya sādhāraṇadharmasya vācyatve’pi tatprayojitasādṛśyamūlakasya svasya śabdavācyatāvirahādvyaṅgyaṃ ca ||
yathā vā
maṇisāramayi masāraṃ vyapasāraya sārasākṣi rabhasāttvam | mama gokulamaulimaṇirmānasamativelameva lolayati || 286 ||
ayi sārasākṣi! maṇisāraṃ ratnaśreṣṭhaṃ masāraṃ indranīlaṃ ‘masāra indranīle syāt’ iti śabdārṇave ‘masāragalvarkamukhaḥ’ iti śrīrāmāyaṇe prayogaśca | rabhasāt vyapasārayeti yojanā | asminbhagavatā nandanandanena viśliṣṭāyāḥ kasyāścinnāyikāyāssakhīṃ pratyuktirūpe padye’pi nīlamaṇijñānādhīnatatsadṛśanandanandanasmṛtiḥ prādhānyena dhvanyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre smṛtisaro daśamaḥ
<12>
atha bhrāntimadalaṃkārasaraḥ
camatkṛtimatī bhrāntiryasmiṃtsādṛśyahetukā | anūditā syātsandarbhe tamāhurbhrāntimāniti || 46
sadṛśe dharmiṇi dharmyantaraprakārako’nāhāryaniścayassāddaśyaprayojyaścamatkārī prakṛte bhrāntiḥ | sā ca suranaratiryagādigatā yasmin vacanasandarbhe’nūdyate sa bhrāntimān | atra ca bhrāntimātramalaṃkāraḥ | bhrāntimāniti vyavahṛtistvaupacārikī | yadāhuḥ
pramātrantaradhīrbhrāntirūpā yasminnanūdyate |
sa bhrāntimāniti khyāto’laṃkāre tyaupacārikaḥ ||
iti | lakṣaṇe mīlitasāmānyatadguṇavāraṇāya dharmigrahaṇadvayam | rūpakavittivāraṇāyānāhārya iti | kavibhinnagata iti vā | saṃśayavāraṇāya niścaya iti, idaṃ rajatamiti raṅgaviśeṣyakabodhavāraṇāya camatkārīti | kavipratibhānirvartita ityarthaḥ | raṅge rajatamiti buddherlaukikatayā na kavipratibhānirvartitatvam || yathā
jalavihṛtau prativīci pratibimbānyabdhijādṛśorvīkṣya| pṛthuśapharabhramasakalāśśakulā dūraṃ dravanti bhayavikalāḥ || 287 ||
pṛthuśapharāḥ mahāmīnāḥ iti bhrameṇa sakalāḥ saṃpūrṇāḥ ‘samagrasakalākhaṇḍapūrṇādīnyapyanūnake’ ityamaraḥ | śakulāḥ matsyāḥ arbhakā iti bhāvaḥ | atra śakulabhayāvegopaskārakatayā tadgatā bhrāntiralaṃkāraḥ ||
yathā vā
kamalākapolaphalake kalitaḥ kanakācaleśvarakaṭākṣaḥ | dṛgdoṣadhūtidattāñjanabindubhrāntidaśśriyaṃ dattām || 288 ||
atrāviśeṣoktessuranarādisarvagatā bhrāntiralaṃkāraḥ | sādṛśyanimittaiva bhrāntiralaṃkāraviṣayo na nimittāntaropasthāpitā ||
yathā
nāneha nāsti kiṃcana jāne sarvāntarātmabhūtaṃ tvām | bhrāmyati yo’sminnarthe bhrāmyati sa bhavecirādahigirīndo || 289 ||
he ahigirīndo! iha tvayītyarthaḥ | nānā bhedaḥ nasti, parabrahmabhūtastvameka evetyarthaḥ | tatra hetuḥ jāne ityādiḥ | tvāṃ sarvāntarātmabhūtaṃ sarvadeśakālavartināṃ padārthānāṃ antarātmabhūtaṃ jāne | asminnarthe yo bhrāmyati bhrānto bhavati saḥ bhave saṃsāre cirāt bhrāmyati bhramaṇaṃ prāpnoti | anena
yadeveha tadamutra yadamutra tadanviha |
mṛtyossa mṛtyumāpnoti ya iha nāneva paśyati ||
manasaivedamāptavyaṃ neha nānā’sti kiṃcana |
mṛtyossa mṛtyuṃ gacchati ya iha nāneva paśyati ||
iti śrutyartho’nusaṃhitaḥ | atra bhrānterasādṛśyanibandhanatvānnālaṃkāratvam | sādṛśyahetukā’pi bhrāntirvicchittyarthaṃ kavipratibhonmeṣitaiva gṛhyate | yathā anupadameva ‘jalavihṛtau’ ityudāhṛte padye | na svarasonmeṣitā śuktirajatavat | evaṃ sādṛśyanimittakatvādanugāmyādayo’sya sādhāraṇadharmā yathāsambhavaṃ bhavanti |
tatrānugāmī dharmo yathā
upagūhitumupasarpati capalatayā śrīssarīsṛpagirīndau | dyumaṇiriti kaustubhamaṇiṃ ghṛṇimantaṃ saṃvṛṇoti pāṇibhyām || 290 ||
atra ghṛṇimantamityanugāmitayā nirdiṣṭo dharmaḥ ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanno yathā
romāliśikharalagne kaustubharatne sanālanalinadhiyā| jhagiti laganti bhramarāḥ kamalākarṇāvataṃsakahlārāt || 291 ||
jhagiti jhaḍitītyarthaḥ | evamanyadapyūhyaṃ buddhimadbhiḥ || kvacidbhrānteruttarottaraṃ pallavena camatkāraḥ, yathā
marakataśikhāmaṇiṃ śukamatha makuṭaṃ śoṇamaṇimayaṃ śaureḥ | tanniṣkulākṛtaṃ śrīrdāḍimamavayanti tava vilāsaśukāḥ || 292 ||
he śrīḥ tava vilāsaśukāḥ śaureḥ marakatarūpaṃ śikhāmaṇiṃ kirīṭakoṭigataṃ śukaṃ avayanti jānanti, atha anantarameva śoṇamaṇimayaṃ padmarāgapracuraṃ taiḥ khacitamityarthaḥ | makuṭaṃ tena śukena niṣkulākṛtaṃ troṭīkoṭīkuṭṭanena bahirāviṣkṛtabījarājikaṃ dāḍimaphalamavayantītyanuṣajyate | niṣkulākṛtamityatra ‘niṣkulānniṣkoṣaṇe’ iti sūtreṇa nirgataṃ kulamantaravayavānāṃ samūho yasmāditi bahuvrīherḍāc ||
kvacitparasparaviṣayabhrāntinibandhanena vicchittiviśeṣaḥ, yathā
bibhyati vīkṣya mayūrān kādambā nūtanāmbudabhrāntyā | te’pi nirīkṣya marālān śāradanīradadhiyā vṛṣādrivane || 293 ||
athāsya dhvaniḥ
yathā
praṇatamṛḍamakuṭasariti prativīci tvatpadapraticchandān | praṇaman vilokya gajamukhaśiśussvaśuṇḍāṃ prasārayati śaure || 294 ||
atra bhagavaccaraṇapratibimbeṣu padmabhrāntirdhvanyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre bhrāntisara ekādaśaḥ
<13>
atha sandehasaraḥ
sambhāvanāniścayānyatarabhinnā manoramā |
sādṛśyahetukā buddhissandehālaṃkṛtirmatā || 47 ||
sādṛśyahetukā saṃbhāvanāniścayānyatarabhinnā ramaṇīyā dhīssandehālaṃkāraḥ | utkaṭaikakoṭikassaṃśayassaṃbhāvanā | niścayatvaṃ tu virodhikoṭyantarāprakārakajñānatvamātram | na tu viruddhakoṭidvayaprakārakajñānabhinnatvam | tathāniveśe prayojanābhāvāt | saṃbhāvanetyādiviśeṣaṇamutprekṣārūpakādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāya | sthāṇurvā puruṣo veti laukikasaṃśayavāraṇāya ramaṇīyeti, camatkāriṇītyarthaḥ | etacca viśeṣaṇamalaṃkārasāmānyalakṣaṇaprāptameva | evamupaskārakatvamapīti dhyeyam | ayaṃ ca saṃdehālaṃkārastrividhaḥ, śuddho niścayagarbho niścayāntaśca | tatra śuddho yasya saṃśaya eva paryavasānam ||
yathā
kiṃnu tuṣārāsāraḥ kiṃ ghanasāraḥ kimamṛtasāro vā | śamayati yadidaṃ klamamiti samaśāyi janairharerdṛśo viṣayaiḥ || 295 || dṛśaḥ kaṭākṣasya | idamiti sāmānyena napuṃsakena harikaṭākṣanirdeśaḥ | samaśāyi sandidihe ||
yathā vā
tārāsārāṃśo vā taṭillataughasphuṭollasacchrīrvā | iti muraripuhṛdayagatāṃ vīkṣyābdhisutāṃ janena saṃdidihe || 296 ||
ayaṃ ca saṃdehaḥ kvacidviṣayiṇāmeva saṃdihyamānatve kvacicca viṣayaviṣayiṇorapyalaṃkāro bhavati | ubhayatrāpi sāmānyalakṣaṇāvagamāt | aniyatobhayāṃśāvalambī hi vimarśassandehaḥ| udāharaṇadvaye viṣayimātrasaṃdeha uktaḥ | atra hi prakṛtayorbhagavatkaṭākṣalakṣmyossaṃdehakoṭitvavirahādviṣayiṇāṃ tuṣārāsāratārāsārāṃ śādīnāmeva saṃdehaḥ ||
viṣayaviṣayiṇoryathā
kiṃ nakharucivalayaṃ jvaladathavā dostejasāṃ śikhāvalayam | aruṇaruci harikarābje nirupamametaccakāsti cakraṃ vā || 297 ||
atra viṣayiṇo nakharucivalayāderiva viṣayasya cakrasyāpi saṃdehakoṭitvam ||
yathā vā
harito’dhyaiṣṭa giriḥ kiṃ girito vā hariridaṃ mahaunnatyam | sarvonnatāvubhāvapi darvīkaraśailatadadhipau bhavataḥ || 298 ||
niścayagarbhaḥ yassaṃśayopakramo niścayamadhyassaṃśayāntaśca | sa yathā
kimiyaṃ gaṅgā na harermukhe bhavetsā’thavā kimu jyotsnā | sā’pi tathaiveti janāśśrīpatihasitaṃ vilokya sandihate || 299 ||
sā’pi jyotsnā’pi tathaiva harermukhe na bhavedevetyarthaḥ | atropakramopasaṃhārayossaṃśaya eva ||
niścayāntaḥ yatra saṃśaye upakramaḥ niścaye paryavasānam |
yathā
jaladabhrameṇa lagnā taṭinnu gaganabhramānnu candrakalā | iti saṃśayya haristāṃ smayamānāṃ hṛdi viniścinoti ramām || 300 ||
lagnetyetadgaganabhramādityatrāpyanuṣajyate ||
kvacidāropyamāṇānāṃ bhinnāśrayatvena saṃdeho dṛśyate | yathā
sarve nagāstamālīkṛtāḥ kimu khagāśśikhīkṛtāḥ kiṃ nu | jaladīkṛtā nu kakubho visṛtvareṇācyutadyutibhareṇa || 301 || atrāropaviṣaye acyutadyutibhare tamālatvādirnagādibhinnāśrayatvenāropitaḥ | nagāḥ taravaḥ | eṣūdāharaṇeṣu saṃdehasyaiva prādhānyenānyānupaskārakatvātsaṃpuṭādigatakaṅkaṇādiṣviva tadyogyatāmātreṇa gauṇo’laṃkāravyapadeśaḥ | evaṃca
bhagavannahinagavasate jagadīśatve tavāpi ye sandihate | sattve teṣāmeṣāṃ sattvaikadhanā janāḥ paraṃ sandihate || 302 ||
ityatrāpi satyapi camatkāre sādṛśyamūlakatvavirahānna sandehasyālaṃkāratā | teṣāṃ sandihānānāṃ janānāṃ sattve brāhmaṇyādiprayuktaprāśastye viṣaye sattvaikadhanāḥ sātvikā janāḥ paraṃ atiśayitaṃ yathā syāttathā sandihate ||
viṣṇubhaktivihīno yassarvaśāstrārthavedyapi |
brāhmaṇyaṃ tasya na bhavettasyotpattirvicāryatām ||
ityuktarītyā saṃśerate ityarthaḥ | evamāropamūlo’yaṃ saṃdehālaṃkāraḥ adhyavasānāśrayatve’pi bhavatīti sarvasvakāraḥ | ayaṃ ca svarūpahetuphalānāṃ sandihyamānatvena tridhā bhavatīti vimarśinīkāraḥ ||
tatra svarūpasaṃdeho yathā
kiṃ marakatarucipūrairapūri kimalepi harimaṇimahobhiḥ | phaṇiśikharimandirāntaramiti saṃśayadaṃ harerjayati tejaḥ || 303 || ayaṃ ca saṃdeho haritejoviṣayakakavigataratiparipoṣakatvena lalanākaradhṛtavalayādiriva prādhānyenālaṃkāravyapadeśabhāgbhavati | atra ca vivakṣite vivecyamāne haritejasi marakatarucipūrādikoṭikaḥ paryavasyati sandehaḥ | sa ca na sāropaḥ, viṣayaviṣayiṇostadanurūpavibhaktyadarśanāt | ataḥ marakatarucipūratvādinā saṃdehadharmiharitejo’dhyavasīyata iti ||
hetusandeho yathā
smayase yadidaṃ svāmin kiṃ tanmaccaritacintanādathavā | īdṛkṣarakṣyalābhātkiṃ vā mama durlabhepsitodyogāt || 304 ||
atra smayanahetau niratiśayānandatve kavinā svacaritacintanādihetutrayamadhyavasitam ||
phalasandeho yathā
sukhadamidameva padamiti sūcayituṃ kiṃ nvidaṃ hi paramapadam | iti kathayituṃ nu pannagapatigiripatinā karo’vanamito’yam || 305 ||
hariṇā śrīnivāsena varadānārthamavanamitaḥ ayaṃ karaḥ dakṣiṇahasta iti bhāvaḥ | idaṃ padaṃ svacaraṇameva sukhadaṃ janānāmiti sūcayituṃ kimavanamitaḥ | idameva kṣetraṃ paramapadamiti kathayituṃ vyañjayitumiti yāvat | avanamito nu iti yojanā | atra hastāvanamanaphale varadāne sūcanādiphalamadhyavasitam || kecittu īdṛśasthale ivādiśabdavat nu kiṃ ityādiśabdānāṃ saṃbhāvanādyotakatvādutprekṣāmācakṣate | rasagaṅgādharakārastu nedṛśasthale’dhyavasāyassambhavatīti manyate | tanmatenātredaṃ vicāryate ‘kiṃ marakatarucipūraiḥ’ iti padye tāvat marakatarucipūrādikaraṇakaparipūritatvādikoṭikaḥ phaṇiśikharigatabhagavanmandirāntaradharmikassandehaśśabdātpratīyate | tasmiṃśca sandehe kimidaṃ marakatarucipūrassyāt āhosvit harimaṇimahassyāditi haritejodharmikaṃ sandehāntaramānuguṇyamādhatte, yathā purovartini vṛṣabhe sthāṇurvā puruṣo veti saṃdeho bhūtalamidaṃ sthāṇumatpuruṣavadveti saṃdehe | evaṃca haritejodharmikassaṃdeho guṇībhūto vyañjanāgamyatvādviṣayaviṣayiṇorāropānukūlavibhaktikatāṃ nāpekṣate | apekṣate ca sākṣācchabdavedyatāyāmiti kvādhyavasāyamūlatā saṃdehasyeti | etenādhyavasānamūlatāṃ saṃdehasya nirūpayatāmuktiḥ parāstā ||
evaṃ
nayanaṃ kimañjanāktaṃ mṛgamadapaṅkaiḥ kimaṅgamāliptam | iti saṃśayaṃ janānāṃ vidadhānā’cyutarucirdhunātvaśubham || 306 ||
ityādivibhinnāśrayādhyavasāyamūlasaṃdeheṣvapi draṣṭavyam | asmiṃśca saṃdehe nānākoṭiṣu kvacideka eva samānadharmaḥ, kvacitpṛthak | sopi kvacidanugāmī, kvacitkevalabimbapratibimbabhāvamāpannaḥ, kvacidvastuprativastubhāvakarambhitaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvamāpannaḥ, kvacidanirdiṣṭaḥ, kvacinnirdiṣṭaḥ | tatra ‘kimiyaṃ gaṅgā na hareḥ’ iti prāgudāhṛte padye dhāvalyaṃ dharmiṇo bhagavanmandahasitasya koṭyorgaṅgājyotsnayoścaika evānugāmī dharmaḥ, pratīyamānatvādanirdiṣṭaḥ | evaṃ ‘jaladabhrameṇa lagnā’ ityādipadye’pi | nirdiṣṭastu ‘harito’dhyaiṣṭa giriḥ kim’ iti prāgudāhṛta eva śloke tatra mahattvaṃ sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ | dharmiṇo bhagavadaunnatyasya koṭyantarasya giryaunnatyasya ca sākṣānnirdiṣṭaḥ | dharmasya pṛthaṅnirdeśopi ‘kiṃ nakharucivalayaṃ jvalat’ iti prāgudāhṛtapadya eva | tatra hi nakharucivalayasya jvalattvaṃ, dostejaśśikhāvalayasyāruṇarucitvaṃ ca saha cakrasya pṛthaganugāmī samāno dharmaḥ | atraiva jvalat aruṇarucīti viśeṣaṇadvayānupādāne sa evānirdiṣṭaḥ ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanno yathā
pratibimbitaravibimbaṃ harimaṇiśikharaṃ sakaustubhaṃ ca harim | munayo’bhivīkṣya phaṇadharagiridharaṇau nantumeva sāṃśayikāḥ || 307 ||
atra pratibimbitaravibimbatvasakaustubhatvarūpo dharmaḥ bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannaḥ ||
yathāvā
sahavidyunnu taṭitvān sadyumaṇirvā harinmaṇīśikharaḥ| sakamala iti sandidihe samaṇikirīṭassarīsṛpagirīnduḥ || 308 ||
atra vidyuddyumaṇī bimbabhūtau lakṣmīkirīṭayoḥ pratibimbakoṭyoḥ pṛthaṅnirdiṣṭau | śikharaśabdaḥ pulliṅgopyasti ‘śikharaṃ vā nā’ ityamarānuśāsanāt | ‘alagitavato vindhyaśikharān’ iti murāriprayogaśca || ākāśarājanṛpaterālayagarbhaṃ tadā nirīkṣya naraiḥ | jaladherjaṭharaṃ kimidaṃ kanakācalasānu veti sandidihe || 309 ||
atra tadeti prakaraṇasāhyavaśādākāśarājagṛheṇa dharmiṇā’skṣiptāyāstatkālāvatīrṇāyāḥ bhagavatyāḥ padmāvatyā jaladhijaṭharādisaṃśayakoṭidvayākṣiptassādhāraṇo maṇiḥ pratibimbabhūtaḥ | imau ca bimbapratibimbabhāvānirdiṣṭāvapi pratīyamānau sādṛśyaṃ prayojayataḥ | etenānugāmyeva dharmo luptassambhavati na tu bimbita iti vadantaḥ parastāḥ ||
ayaṃ ca kvacidanāhāryaḥ kvacidāhāryaḥ, yatra hi kavinā paraniṣṭhassaṃdeho nibadhyate tatra prāyeṇānāhāryaḥ | yathā ‘kiṃ nu tuṣārāsāraḥ’ ityādiṣu prāgudāhṛteṣu padyeṣu ||
yathā
kimu ghanalagnā saurī marīcirathavendranīlamaṇikhacitā | kuruvindalateti ramā harihṛdayagatā na kena vā’śaṅki || 310 ||
atrāpyanāhārya eva saṃdehaḥ | tatra janasya saṃdihānasya grāhyānirṇayāt | atra ca lagnā khacitetyetayoḥ gatetyasya ca vastuprativastubhāvaḥ tatkarambhito ghanendranīlamaṇyoḥ harihṛdayasya ca bimbapratibimbabhāva iti viśeṣaḥ ||
yatra ca svaniṣṭha eva saṃdeho nibadhyate tatrāhāryaḥ | yathā ‘harito’dhyaiṣṭa giri kim’ ityādiprāgudāhṛtapadyeṣu || yathā vā
mihirakaramasṛṇanijarucimarakataśikharaṃ nu phaṇabhṛdacale’smin | kamalāruciruciraṃ kiṃ brahma paraṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalaṃ yadidam || 311 ||
yadidaṃ purastāt dṛśyamānaṃ vastu kṛṣṇaṃ ca tatpiṅgalaṃ śyāmalapiśaṅgavarṇaṃ ‘varṇo varṇena’ iti samāsaḥ | ‘puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam’ iti śrutiratra smāritā | atra vaktuḥ kavestattvajñatayā saṃdeha āhārya eva | atra mihirakarakamalārucyorbimbapratibambabhāvaḥ masṛṇanijarucirucirayorvastuprativastubhāvaḥ, kṛṣṇapiṅgalamityasyānugāmitā ca draṣṭavyā ||
śleṣātmako’pi dharmassaṃbhavati yathā
siṃhalasadgotrākarabhavano’yaṃ kiṃ mahāmahānīlaḥ | uta bhagavāniti kavayo’pyatisāṃśayikā vilokya śaure tvām || 312 ||
siṃhalasya siṃhaladvīpasya satī praśastā gotrā bhūḥ tasyāṃ yaḥ ākaraḥ khaniḥ tatra bhavanaṃ utpattiḥ yasya sa iti mahānīlapakṣe | ‘siṃhalasyākarodbhūtā mahānīlāstu te smṛtāḥ’ ityagastyokteḥ | bhagavatpakṣe siṃha iti lasan prakāśamānaḥ gotraśśailaḥ siṃhādriḥ śeṣagirirityarthaḥ | sa evākara ādhāro yasya tattathoktaṃ bhavanaṃ divyālayaḥ sattā vā yasya sa tathoktaḥ ||
viṣayiṇāṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇorvā sādṛśyamūlakasaṃdeha evāyamalaṃkāraḥ, na tu kevalasaṃdehe | tena vidhubhadraśrīvyatikarasusmeramidaṃ spṛśeyamiti śaurau | vadati samaśeta rādhā spṛśenmukhaṃ vā stanadvayaṃ veti || 313 ||
ityatra nātiprasaṅgaḥ | atra hi mukhastanadvayakarmakaṃ sparśanaṃ viṣayaviṣayibhāvānāpannaṃ saṃdihyate | vidhoḥ candramasaḥ bhadrā kalyāṇī yā śrīḥ tasyāḥ vyatikarassaṃbandhaḥ tena susmeraṃ smayamānaṃ candranibhamiti yāvat | anyatra vidhoḥ karpūrasya bhadraśriyaḥ candanasya ca vyatikareṇa vilepanena susmeraṃ ‘vidhuśśaśāṅke karpūre’ iti ratnamālā | ‘bhadraśrīścandanostriyām’ ityamaraḥ | spaṣṭamanyat ||
paramparitopyayaṃ saṃbhavati, yathā
śrutivanamadāvalo vā lakṣmīvarṣāśikhāvalo va’yam | darvīkaragirimaṇimiti nirvarṇayatā janena saṃdidihe || 314 ||
atra saṃdehasya paraṃparitatvaṃ ca paraṃparitarūpakapūrvakatvam | na tu saṃdehahetukasaṃdehatvam | śrutyādīnāṃ vanatvādisaṃdehāviṣatvāditi dhyeyam ||
kvacitparaniṣṭhopi kavinā nibadhyamāna āhāryo bhavati, yathā ‘jaladabhrameṇa lagnā taṭit’ iti prāgudāhṛte padye | tatra bhagavatassarvajñasya saṃśaya āhārya eva | tatra ca jaladabhrameṇa lagnā taṭinnu gaganabhramāllagnā candrakalā nu iti koṭyoḥ hṛdayalagnaramārūpadharmisādṛśyadārḍhyāyāvidyamānamapi bhramāllagnatvamāropyate | evamādayo’nye’pi prakārā unneyāḥ | ukteṣveteṣūdāharaṇeṣu so’yaṃ saṃdehālaṃkārassvaśabdavedyatvādvācyaḥ ||
lakṣyo yathā
līlājalaruhamacalābālāyāḥ karamapīkṣamāṇasya | dolāyate sma hṛdayaṃ vyālādripateḥ karagrahāvasare || 315 ||
acalābālāyāḥ dharaṇīgarbhasaṃbhūtāyāḥ padmāvatyāḥ karagrahāvasare pāṇipīḍanasamaye | atra paryāyeṇa koṭidvayālambitayā dolāsādṛśyātsaṃdeho’tra dolāpadena lakṣyate ||
vyaṅgyo yathā
utsavagatassa bhagavānudayantaṃ bhānumantamabhivīkṣya | vakṣassahasā’sprākṣīdabjākṣassvīyamahidharādhyakṣaḥ || 316 ||
atra bhagavatassahasā vakṣassthalasparśena tatra sthita eva kaustubhamaṇistataścyutaḥ kiṃ purastāt dṛśyata iti saṃdeho vyaṅgyaḥ ||
yathā vā
tilakitanijamukhavīkṣaṇakutukavataśśrīsakhasya nayanayugam| yātāyātacchrāntaṃ mukure kamalākapolaphalake ca || 317 ||
atra mukuradharmiko’bhedena saṃsargeṇa purovartivyaktidvayaprakārakaḥ mukuro’yaṃ vā’yaṃ veti bhagavannayanayugagatassaṃdeho vyaṅgyaḥ | atra bhagavadgatasya saṃdehasya tannayanayuge upacāraḥ | na ca mukurābhedadhiyo nayanayugapravṛttyupāyatvenāpekṣaṇādidaṃpadārthābhedadhīrniṣprayojaneti vācyam | ekapadārthadharmikāparapadārthadhiyaḥ aparapadārthadharmikaikapadārthābhedabodhaprayojakatayā mukurābhedabodhasāmrājyāt mukuratvametadvṛtti tadvṛtti veti saṃśayākāraḥ | so’yaṃ saṃdehadhvaniḥ ||
drutakanakapratimāvā’’vartitavidyullatāpratikṛtirvā | kuruvindalekhikā vā mukundahṛdaye’ravindanilayā vā || 318 ||
yadyapīhāpi vācakaśabdānupādānātsaṃdeho vyaṅgya evārhati bhavitum, tathā’pi viṣayanirūpaṇena spaṣṭatayoktatvānna dhvanivyapadeśyatāyā hetuḥ, api tu guṇībhūtavyaṅgyaprabhedavyapadeśyatāyāḥ ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre sandehasaro dvādaśaḥ
<14>
athāpahnutisaraḥ
āhāryābhedabuddheścenniṣedho’ṅgyaṅgameva vā |
etāṃ sāmānyataḥ prāhurapahnutimalaṃkṛtim ||
āhāryābhedapratipattinirūpitāṅgāṅgibhāvānyataravanniṣedhatvamapahnutyalaṃkārasāmānyalakṣaṇam | niṣedhaśca nañādyupādāne vācyaḥ, kvacittu tadabhāvātkaitavacchalādipadaiḥ paramatattvopanyāsādibhiśca vyaṅgyaḥ, tathā kvacidabhedapratipattisamānādhikaraṇaḥ kvacittu tadvyadhikaraṇa iti vivekaḥ | evamabhedaḥ kvacidāropyamāṇaḥ, kvacittu svābhāvikaḥ | evaṃ tatpratipattirapi kvacidvyañjanayā prāyaśastu vācyavṛttyaiveti dhyeyam | niṣedho’pahnutirityetāvaduktau ‘na dyūtametatkitava krīḍanaṃ niśitaiśśaraiḥ’ iti prasiddhaniṣedhānuvādarūpe pratiṣedhālaṃkāre’tivyāptiḥ | ato’bhedapratipattinirūpitetyādiviśeṣaṇam | tatra hi samarapravṛttaṃ kitavaṃ prati samare dyūtatvābhāvo nirjñāto’pi kaṇṭharaveṇocyamāno dyūta eva tava vaidagdhyaṃ na samare ityupahāsārtho na tu yuddhābhedapratipattyaṅgam, tasyāstadupanyāsaṃ vinā’pi siddhatvāt | nāpyaṅgī, nirjñātatve naiva tadupāyānapekṣaṇāt | abhedapratipattinirūpitāṅgitvamātroktau śuddhāpahnutihetvapahnutiparyastāphnutikaitavāpahnutiṣvavyāptiḥ, tatra sarvatra nāyaṃ sudhāṃśurityāderniṣedhasya vyomagaṅgāsaroruhādyabhedapratipattyarthatvena tadaṅgatayā’ṅgitvābhāvāt | abhedapratipattinirūpitāṅgatvamātroktau bhrāntāpahnuticchekāpahnutyoravyāptiḥ, tayorbhrāntiśaṅkāvāraṇarūpaniṣedhasya prādhānyenābhedapratipattyaṅgatvābhāvāt | atastadanyataratvaniveśaḥ | yadāhuḥ
sāmyāyāpahnavo yatra sā vijñeyā tvapahnutiḥ |
apahnavāya sādṛśyaṃ yasminneṣā’pyapahnutiḥ ||
iti | sarvaṃ caitattadudāharaṇavivaraṇāvasare sphuṭībhaviṣyati | ‘na pañcaviśikhaḥ kāmaḥ kiṃtvalaṅkhyaśilīmukhaḥ’ ityādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāya pratipatterabhedaviṣayakatvaviśeṣaṇam | atra hi nāsaṅkhyaśilīmukhaśabdābhilapanīyasya kasyacidabhedasamāropaḥ kriyate tathāvidhasya kasyacidvastunaḥ kvāpyaprasiddheḥ | nāpyanyatrāprasiddhasya ‘aṅkaṃ ke’pi’ ityādāviva ihaiva kalpitasya vastunastathāvidhasyābhedena samāropa iti śakyaṃ pratipattuṃ, tathākalpanāyāmiha kaverasaṃrambhāt | tasmādasaṃkhyaśilīmukhaśabdenāpi kāma eva vivakṣita iti viṣayabhūte kāme pañcaviśikhatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ pratiṣidhyāsaṃkhyaśilīmukhatvaṃ dharma eva bhedena samāropyata ityabhyupetyam | tathāceha vivakṣitāyāḥ bhedasaṃsargakapratīterevāṅgaṃ pratiṣedho nābhedapratīterityatiprasaṅgavāraṇam | ‘nāyaṃ sudhāṃśuḥ’ ityādau tu sarvatrāpahnavodāharaṇe vyomagaṅgāsaroruhāderdharmiṇa eva tattaddharmaviśiṣṭatayā’nyatra prasiddhasya kavikalpitasya vā viṣaye abhedena pratītirvivakṣiteti lakṣaṇasaṃgatiḥ | ‘apahnutirapahnutya kiṃcidanyārtharopaṇam’ iti pratipatterabhedaviṣayakatvamanādṛtya kevalāpahnutipūrvakānyārthāropasyaivāpahnutitvaṃ lakṣayato daṇḍino mate tūdāhṛte ‘na pañcaviśikhaḥ’ ityatrāpi naivātiprasaṃgaḥ ||
saṃgrāmāṅgaṇasaṃmukhāgatakiyadviśvaṃbharādhīśvaravyādīrṇīkṛtamadhyabhāgavivaronmīlannabhonīlimā |
aṅgāraprakharaiḥ karaiḥ kabalayan sadyo jaganmaṇḍalaṃ mārtaṇḍo’yamudeti kena paśunā loke śaśāṅkīkṛtaḥ ||
ityādau virahijanavākye nāyaṃ śaśāṅkaḥ apitu sacchidramārtaṇḍa iti pratīyamāne’rthe śaśāṅkaniṣedho mārtaṇḍābhedapratipatterevāṅgamiti tatrātivyāptivāraṇāyābhedapratipatterāhāryeti viśeṣaṇaṃ, atra ca mārtaṇḍāropasya doṣaviśeṣajanyatayā’nāhāryatvāt | ata eva ‘atra chāyāmātramapahnuteḥ na tvapahnutyalaṃkāraḥ tajjñānasya doṣaviśeṣajanyatvenānāhāryatvāt, kiṃtu ‘bhrāntyalaṃkāraeva’ iti rasagaṅgādharakṛduktissaṃgacchate | asya ca virahijanavacanatāviraheṇa kevalakavimativikāsajanyatve tu bhavatyevedamapi śuddhāpahnuterudāharaṇamiti rahasyam | ata eva ‘nendustīvro na niśyarkassindhoraurvo’yamutthitaḥ’ ityasya hetvapahnuterudāharaṇatvaṃ ca saṃgacchate | anyathā’syāpi virahijanavacanatābhisaṃdhau alakṣyatāpātātprāktanaiḥ kṛtasya tadudāharaṇasyāsaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva ||
na caivamapi sāmānyalakṣaṇe’sminnāhāryaviśeṣaṇopādānaṃ na yujyate, bhrāntāpahnutāvavyāptiprasaṃgāt | tatra hi ‘tāpaṃ karoti sotkampaṃ jvaraḥ kiṃ na sakhi smaraḥ’ ityādau tātvikasmarābhedapratīterāhāryatvavirahāditi vācyaṃ, āhāryatvasyānāhāryatvabhramatvobhayābhāvaparyavasitasyaiva niveśanīyatayā sakaladoṣanivāraṇāt | bhrāntāpahnutau hyabhedapratipatteranāhāryatve’pi bhramatvābhāvenaiva ekasattve dvayaṃ nāstīti nyāyena ubhayābhāvaviśeṣaṇamahimnā lakṣaṇasaṃgatiḥ | itareṣvapahnutiprabhedeṣu tu abhedapratipatterbhramatve’pyanāhāryatvaviraheṇaivobhayābhāvassūpapāda iti tatrāpi syādeva lakṣaṇasaṃgatiḥ | upadarśite virahijanavākye tu mārtaṇḍābhedapratīteranāhāryatayā doṣaviśeṣajanyabhramarūpatayā ca tadubhayasyaiva sadbhāvena tadubhayābhāvo durghaṭa iti noktātiprasaṅgaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ | tathāca anāhāryatvabhramatvobhayābhāvavatī yā abhedapratītiḥ tadviśiṣṭapratītiviṣayatve sati pratiṣedhatvamapahnavālaṃkārasāmānyalakṣaṇam | pratītau tādṛśapratītivaiśiṣṭyaṃ ca svaniṣṭhāṅgatānirūpitāṅgitāvattvasvaniṣṭhāṅgitānirūpitāṅgatāvattvānyatarasaṃbandheneti dhyeyam | cārutvaviśeṣaṇaṃ tvadhikāralabdhameveti na viśiṣyātropādātavyamityalaṃ bahunā ||
itthamapahnutessāmānyalakṣaṇamabhihitam | atha tadbhedān lilakṣayiṣustatrādau śuddhāpahnutiṃ lakṣayati
anyāropaphalo yassyātprakṛte dharmanihnavaḥ |
alaṃkṛtiriyaṃ śuddhāpahnutirnāma gadyate || 49 ||
prakṛte varṇye vastuni aprakṛtadharmāropaphalakastadīyadharmanihnavaśśuddhāpahnutiralaṃkāraḥ | tathāca upameye upamānāropaphalaka upameyadharmatvenābhimatasya niṣedhaśśuddhāpahnutiriti paryavasyati lakṣaṇam | atra cānuktanimittatvena kaitavacchalādipadāvyaṅgyatvena ca niṣedho viśeṣaṇīyaḥ | tena hetvapahnutikaitavāpahnutyornātiprasaṃgaḥ | ‘kāntaḥ kiṃ na hi nūpuraḥ’ iti chekāpahnutau upameyadharmasya kāntatvasya niṣedhasadbhāvādatiprasaṅgassyāditi tadvāraṇāya prāthamikaviśeṣaṇam | tatra hi na kāntatvaniṣedho nūpurāropaphalakaḥ api tu nūpurāropa eva śaṅkitakāntatvaniṣedhaphalaka iti tadvyāvṛttiḥ | paryastāpahnutivāraṇāyopameyadharmetyuktam | tatropamānadharmasyaiva sudhāṃśutvāderniṣedha iti nātivyāptiriti | atra ca nihnavāropayorna paurvāparyaniyamaḥ, lakṣaṇe dharmanihnavasyānyāropaphalakatvamātrokteḥ | na hyatra ‘nihnutya viṣaye dharmamanyadāropyate yadi’ ityasmābhirnibaddhaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ, yena ktvāpratyayovalambanaṃ nihnavasya paurvakālikatvameveti niyamyeta, tena vakṣyamāṇe āropapūrvakāpahnave nāvyāptiḥ ||
daṇḍī tu ‘apahnutirapahnutya kiṃcidanyārtharopaṇam’ ityāropasya sādṛśyamūlakatvaṃ prakṛte vastuni dharmanihnavaṃ cānādṛtya yatkiṃcinnihnavapūrvakayatkiṃciddharmāropamātramapahnavaṃ lakṣayati sma | tanmate vakṣyamāṇāni padyānyapyapahnuterudāharaṇānyeva | atrāpahnutyeti ktvāpratyayārtho niṣedhasya pūrvakālatā na vivakṣitā | tena vakṣyamāṇodāharaṇāni saṃgaṃsyante || yathā
etya ruciṃ jetuṃ tava ghanassacāpastvayā vilūnajyaḥ | mūtastayaiva śaure jyāmūto’yaṃ tato na jīmūtaḥ || 320 ||
ghanaḥ tava ruciṃ jetuṃ sacāpa etya tvayā vilūnajyaḥ chinnamaurvīkaḥ tayaiva jyayaiva mūtaḥ baddhaḥ ‘mūṅ bandhane’ karmaṇi ktaḥ ‘baddhe saṃdānitaṃ mūtam’ ityamaraḥ | tataḥ tasmāt ayaṃ ghanaḥ jyāmūtaḥ jyāmūta iti vyavahartumarhaḥ na tu jīmūta itītyarthaḥ ||
yathā vā
nātha tava caritasāraṃ nānāptassādaro bhavannādau | yaḥ pradade lokānāṃ na nāradassārado’yamiti yuktam || 321 ||
yaḥ muniḥ anāpto na bhavatīti nānāptaḥ ‘saṃbhāvyaniṣedhanivartane dvau pratiṣedhau’ iti vāmanaḥ | atyāptatamaḥ anāptatāsaṃbhāvanā’pyasya na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | tadukteḥ prāmāṇikatādyotanārthamidaṃ viśeṣaṇam | sādaro bhavan bhavapathagatāgatapariśrāntā ete lokā ujjīvyāsurisyādaravān san ādau pūrvaṃ prācetasādīnāmapi bhagavaccaritasārādhigamasya tanmūlakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | he nātha! tava caritasāraṃ lokānāṃ pradadau, ayaṃ na nāradaḥ
nāraṃ pānīyamityuktaṃ tatpitṛbhyassadā bhavān |
dadāti tena te nāma nāradeti bhaviṣyati ||
iti,
nāraṃ narāṇāṃ saṃghātaṃ kalahenaiva yo dyati |
tasmānnāradanāmā’sau loke vikhyāyate muniḥ ||
iti vā niruktaśabdavācyo na, kiṃtu sārada iti yuktam | upapāditarītyā bhagavaccaritasāradatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra nāradaśabdaḥ ādau prathamaṃ nānāptaḥ nā ityākārakavarṇenānanuṣaktaḥ tyaktanākāra ityarthaḥ, atha sādaraḥ tatraiva sā ityākārakavarṇe ādaro yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ādau sākāraghaṭita iti yāvat | evaṃcennāradaśabdassārada iti niṣpadyata iti vaicitryaṃ ca garbhitam ||
yathā vā
kṛṣṇāyāḥ sphītāmbaradānāt sphītāmbareti vaktavye | amahāprāṇoccāraṇapaṭavaḥ pītāmbareti jagadustvām || 322 ||
amahāprāṇoccāraṇapaṭavaḥ mahāprāṇavarṇoccāraṇāśaktāḥ kecit pītāmbareti tvāṃ jagaduḥ | sphītāmbara pītāmbareti saṃbuddhyante pade | sphītāmbaretyatra sakāraphakārau hi mahāprāṇau anye alpaprāṇāḥ | ‘vargāṇāṃ prathamatṛtīyapañcamāḥ prathamatṛtīyayamau yaralavāścālpaprāṇaḥ anye mahāprāṇāḥ’ ityukteḥ | eṣu padyeṣu jyāmūtatvasāradatvasphītāmbaratvāropaphalakaḥ jīmūtatvanāradatvapītāmbaratvanihnavo na sādṛśyamūlakaḥ ||
evaṃ
raśmibhirābadhya raviṃ kaustubhavasuvibhavabhāracora iti | cārayati prakaṭayituṃ śaurirna tu kālamiha paricchettum || 323 || ityatrāpyasādṛśyamūlakatvamanusandheyam | pūrvodāharaṇeṣu dharmanihnavo’nyadharmāropārthaḥ, iha tu kālaparicchedaphalāpahnavaścoratāprakaṭanarūpaphalāropārtha iti viśeṣaḥ ||
niyataṃ na kalaṅko’yaṃ śaśāṅkabimbe yadetadānīlam | tvanmukharucivijitatayā hṛnmadhye dhṛtamanena bhayaśalyam || 324 ||
atrāprakṛta eva kalaṅke bhagavanmukharucivijitatvanibandhanahṛdayadhṛtabhayaśalyatvāropaphalakaḥ kalaṅkatvanihnavo nibaddhaḥ ||
evam
indorekaḥ kiraṇaḥ paratvamicchustava smitādamba | madhye’bhihato’paratāṃ gataḥ kiṇo’bhūttamāhuraṅka iti ||
ityatrāpyaprakṛta eva kalaṅke kiraṇatvāropaphalakaḥ kalaṅkatvanihnavaḥ | he amba! indoḥ ekaḥ kiraṇaḥ sārabhūto madhyasthita iti bhāvaḥ | tava smitādapi paratvamutkarṣaṃ icchuḥ ‘na loka’ iti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhyā niṣedhaḥ | ata eva madhye icchāphalāvāptyorantarāla eva abhihataḥ tava smiteneti bhāvaḥ | ata eva aparatāṃ anutkarṣaṃ gatassan kiṇaḥ abhūt | taṃ kiṇameva aṅka ityāhuḥ | ayaṃ kiṇa eva nāṅka iti bhāvaḥ | atra kiraṇakiraṇaśabdaḥ madhye abhihataḥ ata eva aparatāṃ apagatarephatāṃ gatassan kiṇaḥ abhūt | kiraṇaśabde rephāpanayane kiṇa iti niṣpadyata ityartho’pi camatkārī ||
kāvyaprakāśādyanuyāyyasmadupadarśitaśuddhāpahnutilakṣaṇasya tu nedaṃ lakṣyam, api tu vakṣyamāṇameveta dhyeyam | asyāṃ ca nañādibhissākṣātparamatatvādyupanyāsaiśca kiṃcidvyavadhānena prakṛtasya niṣedhe bodhyamāne prāyeṇa vākyasya bhedaḥ| viṣayāsatyatvapratipādakamiṣachalachadmakapaṭavyājādipadairviṣayāsatyatvaparyavasāyirūpavapurnāmātmabhaṅgyādiśabdaiśca niṣedhe bodhyamāne tu ekavākyatā | kvacidapahnavapūrvakatvaṃ kvaciccāropapūrvakatvaṃ kvacidaprakṛtatādrūpyaprakṛtaniṣedhayorekasya śābdatvamekasyārthatvaṃ kvacidubhayośśābdatvamathobhayorārthatvaṃ vidheyatvamanuvādyatvaṃ cetyevamaneke prakārāssaṃbhavanto’pi camatkṛtiviśeṣānādhāyakatayā na gaṇanīyāḥ | evamapi diṅmātramudāhriyate
na mukhamidaṃ lāvaṇyāmbudhireṣa na cādharaḥ pravālaste | neme daśanā muktā na nayanayugametadīśa mīnayugam || 326 ||
he īśeti saṃbuddhiḥ | te tava idaṃ sarvavākyānvayi | iyaṃ cānugrāhyānugrāhakabhāvāpannāvayavakasaṃghātātmakatayā sāvayavā | atrāpahnavapūrvakatvamubhayośśābdatvaṃ vidheyatvaṃ vākyabhedaśca ||
yathā vā
yadiha tvadvadane’sitamuditamidaṃ bhrūyugaṃ vadantvajñāḥ| nātha vayaṃ nigadāmo nāsāvaṃśāṅkurādyadalayugmam || 327 ||
iyaṃ niravayavā | atra vaktṛgatājñatvakathanena tadgatabhrāntipratipattivyavahitā niṣedhapratipattiriti niṣedha ārthaḥ | tādrūpyaṃ śābdam | vidheyatvavākyabhedāpahnavapūrvakatvāni pūrvavadeva || yathā vā
harikaranihitaṃ śaṅkhaṃ katicana haṃsaṃ pare sitāmbhojam | abhidadhatu vrajamuṣitaṃ navanītaṃ brūmahe’dhunā’pi dhṛtam || 328 ||
iyamapi niravayavā | atrāpyapahnavapūrvaka āropaḥ| apahnavastu parapakṣopanyāsādārthikatvena vyavahitaḥ ||
yathā vā
maṇimakuṭataraṇitakṣaṇajanitā yamunaiva bhāti tava vadane | mṛgapatigirivaraśekharamṛgamadatilakaṃ vadantyamūṃ mugdhāḥ || 329 ||
yathā vā
haricaraṇavimukhamanasāṃ nilayaṃ santo vidurnirayameva | ralayorabhidāśrayaṇādvadantyanudvejanāya nilaya iti || 330 ||
atrodāharaṇadvaye’pyapahnutirniravayavaiva | apahnavastvāropapūrvaka iti viśeṣaḥ | tādrūpyasya śābdatvaṃ niṣedhasyārthatvaṃ vidheyatvavākyabhedau pūrvavadeva | haricaraṇavimukhamanasāṃ nilayaṃ santo nirayameva viduḥ | tarhi niraya ityeva kuto na vyavaharantītyata āha ralayoriti | ralayorabhedamāśritya nirayameva nilayaṃ vadantītyarthaḥ | tadapi kuta ityata āha anudvejanāyeti | niraya ityucyamāne udvijerannime lokā iti nilaya iti vadanti | ‘yayā’syodvijate vācā nālokyāṃ tāmudīrayet’ iti niṣedhāditi bhāvaḥ ||
evam
svātyevaiṣā janani tvanmukhacandreṇa gāḍhamupagūḍhā | adhararūcirohiṇīyaṃ nāsāmukteti mugdhajanavādaḥ || 331 ||
ityatrāpyāropapūrvakatvādikaṃ draṣṭavyam | svātīnakṣatrasya padmarāgavarṇatayā tadāropaḥ | rohiṇī rohitā | ‘varṇādanudāttātopadhātto naḥ’ iti ṅīp, tatsanniyogaśiṣṭaṃ natvaṃ, tato ṇatvam ||
āropagarbhatvācceyaṃ sādṛśyādvā bhavati saṃbandhāntarādveti vimarśinīkāraḥ | sādṛśye’pyasyāssādhāraṇadharmā upamāyāmiva draṣṭavyāḥ | tatrānugāmī dharmaḥ ‘niyataṃ na kalaṅko’yaṃ śaśāṅkabimbe yadetadānīlam’ ityādau prāgudāhṛtapadye nirdiṣṭaḥ ||
śuddhasāmānyarūpaṃ yathā
adhivṛṣagirināthāṅgaṃ yadiha piśaṅgaṃ na hemavasanaṃ tat | munijanamānasasarasijanivasanalagnaṃ supiṅgalaṃ hi rajaḥ || 332 ||
atra piśaṅgapiṅgalatvayośśuddhasāmānyarūpatvam | ayameva vastuprativastubhāvaḥ ||
vastuprativastubhāvakarambhitabimbapratibimbabhāvenānugāmitayācca yathā
tapanīyavimānāntarna paramapuruṣo’yamahigireśśikhare | kanakasaroruhajaṭhare kanati vilagno madhuvratavareṇyaḥ || 333 ||
atra tapanīyakanakayoḥ antarjaṭharayośca vastuprativastubhāvaḥ | tatkarambhito vimānasya saroruhasya ca bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ kanatītyasyānugāmitvaṃ ceti draṣṭavyam ||
saṃbandhāntarādyathā
na bhagavadāvirbhāvaḥ phaṇiśikhariṇi yadvibhāvyate munibhiḥ | api tu jagatāṃ vinamatāmaśubhapiśācītirobhāvaḥ || 334 ||
atra bhagavadāvirbhāvasya kāraṇasya niṣedhena aśubhapiśācītirobhāvasya kāryasya vidhiḥ | evamāropagarbhā saprapañcaṃ pradarśitā | adhyavasānasagarbhā punaḥ pradarśyate, yathā
na nigamamayabimbatvānna bhāsvaratvānna lokabandhutvāt | sandhyāsu vandyate’rkaḥ kiṃtu vṛṣādrīśa hṛdayamaṇidāsyāt || 335 ||
vṛṣādrīśahṛdayamaṇiḥ kaustubhaḥ, tasmin dāsyāt tadanuvartitejastvāditi yāvat | atrārkasya vandyatve ‘udyantamastaṃyantamādityamabhidhyāyan’ ityādipramāṇāvabodhitaprabhāvahetukatvaṃ nigīrya tatra hetvantaramadhyavasitam ||
yathā vā
nāyaṃ suśītalatvānna komalatvānna vā sacapalatvāt | abhinandyo lokānāmabhinavajalado | hare tavaupamyāt || 336 ||
atrābhinandyatvasya jīvanadānādihetukatvaṃ nigīrya tatra hetvantaramadhyavasitam ||
yathā vā
hṛdaye kaustubharatnaṃ na bhūṣaṇārthaṃ svabhūrayaṃ dhatte | bahumānayituṃ kamalāsahajanmetyahidhuraṃdharādrīśaḥ || 337 ||
atra kaustubharatnahṛdayadhāraṇasya
ātmānamasya jagato nirlepamaguṇāmalam |
bibharti kaustubhamaṇisvarūpaṃ bhagavān hariḥ ||
ityuktaṃ jīvatattvābhimānitvaprayuktasatkaraṇatvaṃ phalaṃ nigīrya phalāntarādhyavasāyaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
sāvayavāropā’pīyaṃ dṛśyata iti rasagaṅgādharakṛt ||
yathā
hṛdayahradān budhānāṃ prasādayantī ramāsyarākendum | susmeraṃ vidadhānā śaradeṣā na tu harerapāṅgaruciḥ || 338 ||
āropamātropāyatve paraṃparitā’pyeṣā saṃbhavatīti sa evāha ||
yathā
tadidaṃ tava navapallavamadavidalanakṛtpadaṃ vadantvadhiyaḥ | tigmabhavātapavāraṇanavātapatraṃ bravāṇyahaṃ tu hare || 339 ||
evaṃ prāgudāhṛte ‘maṇimakuṭataraṇi’ iti padye’pi paraṃparitā’nusaṃdheyā ||
asyāśca dhvaniryathā
eṣā na vaijayantī puro lasantī mahendracāpalatā | naiṣā’pi jaladhiduhitā paraṃ tu vidyullatā samunmiṣitā || 340 ||
atra naiṣā vaijayantī api tu indradhanurvallī, nāpyeṣā lakṣmīḥ api tu vidyullateti pūrvottarābhyāṃ dve apahnutī tāvat spaṣṭameva nirdiṣṭe | tābhyāṃ ca nāyaṃ bhagavān kiṃ tu prāvṛṣeṇyaghanāghana iti tṛtīyāpahnutirvyañjanayā prādhānyenāvedyate | tadīyavastupratiṣedhāropayoḥ tatpratiṣedhāropāvedakatāyā nyāyyatvāt ||
atha hetvapahnutiḥ
sa evāpahnavo yuktipūrvaśceddhetvapahnutiḥ || 50 ||
yathā
neyaṃ śrīrurasi harerna hi nivasennityamatra capalā sā | api tu harihṛdayasaudhasthiratarakuruvindadīparekhaiva || 341 || atra capalatvahetukanityanivāsāsaṃbhavasaṃbhāvanārūpayuktyā lakṣmyāṃ lakṣmītvanihnavaḥ sthiratarakuruvindadīparekhātvāropārthaḥ ||
yathā vā
nāṅko’yamurasi śaurernisargato’muṣya niṣkalaṅkatvāt | ratikhedasuptakamalānayanāñjanabinduriha na saṃdehaḥ || 342 ||
atra bhagavato niṣkalaṅkatvarūpayuktyā aṅke aṅkatvanihnavaḥ añjanabindutvāropārthaḥ ||
atha paryastāpahnutiḥ
paryastāpahnutiryatra kvacittaddharmanihnavaḥ |
varṇanīye tadāropaphalako varṇyate yadi || 51 ||
yatra kvacidvastuni tadīyadharmāpahnavo varṇanīye vastuni tasya dharmasyāropārtho varṇyate cetsā paryastāpahnutiḥ ||
yathā
yadiha parastādrajasassthānaṃ padamāmananti paramamiti | paramaṃ na padaṃ tatsyādapi tu phaṇādharapatikṣitibhṛdeva || 343 || yathā vā
siṃhalasaṃbhūtamaṇirna mahānīlo harirmahānīlaḥ| nīlayati sa hi svalpaṃ kṣīraṃ kṣīrābdhimeva tatstho’yam || 344 ||
saḥ siṃhalasaṃbhūtamaṇiḥ | ayaṃ śrīhariḥ tatsthaḥ kṣīrasthaḥ kṣīrābdhimadhyasthaśca | atra
siṃhalasyākarodbhūtā mahānīlāstu te smṛtāḥ |
kṣīramadhye kṣipennīlaṃ kṣīraṃ cennīlatāṃ vrajet ||
mahānīla iti khyātaṃ ratnaṃ tadatidurlabham |
iti ratnaśāstrārtho’nusaṃhitaḥ ||
yathā vā
indurna satāṃ netā sannetā pannagādripatireva | saccakrakhedamodapradayorvada ko bhavetsatāṃ netā || 345 ||
saccakrasya satpuruṣanivahasya sataścakravākasya ca | khedaprada induḥ modaprado bhagavān ||
yathā vā
aruṇakiraṇo na taraṇistaraṇiḥ phaṇidharaṇidharaśaraṇa eva | kamatītaratsa saṃsṛtijalanidhimeṣa tu jaganti tārayati || 346 ||
taraṇiḥ dyumaṇiḥ nauśca | ‘taraṇirdyumaṇau puṃsi kumārīnaukayoḥ striyām’ iti medinī | atītarat tārayati sma | taraterṇyantāllaṅi caṅ | saḥ aruṇakiraṇaḥ | eṣaḥ veṅkaṭagirinilayaḥ ||
yathā vā
nāpāṃnidhirnadīno nadīna eṣa śrito hare yastvām | kṣubhitassa sarvato mukhabhaṅgādetyeṣa naikamapi bhaṅgam || 347 ||
nadīna iti naśabdena samāsa | adīnaḥ netyanvayaḥ, kiṃtu dīna evetyarthaḥ | nadīnāṃ ina iti vastusthitiḥ | bhagavadāśrita eva nadīnaḥ adīnaḥ | saḥ apāṃ nidhiḥ sarvatomukhabhaṅgāt viśvatomukhātparājayāt, pakṣe jalataraṅgāt, bhaṅgaṃ parājayaṃ ‘bhaṅgo jayaviparyaye | bhedarogataraṅgeṣu’ iti medinī | atra ‘yadiha parastāt’ iti prāthamikodāharaṇe rajasaḥ parasmin sthāne paramapadatvanihnavo varṇanīye śeṣādrau tadāropārthaḥ | anyeṣu triṣu siṃhaletyādiṣu mahānīlamaṇitvādyapahnavaḥ prastute bhagavati tadāropārthaḥ prathamodāharaṇe nihnave heturnoktaḥ dvitīyādiṣu heturuktaḥ | tatrāpi siṃhaletidvitīyapadye śuddhaḥ, tṛtīyacaturthapadyayośśleṣasaṃkīrṇaḥ | pañcame ‘nāpāṃnidhiḥ’ itipadye samudre nadīnatvanihnavo bhagavadāśriteṣu prakṛteṣu tatsamāropārthaḥ | hetūktyādikaṃ tu pūrvavadeva | tataśca śuddhāpahṛutivadatrāpi dvaividhyamanusandheyam | ayamapahnutibhedaḥ kuvalayānandānurodhenoktaḥ | rasagaṅgādhare tu “anyatra tasyāropārthaḥ paryastāpahnutiśca saḥ’ ityetadupādāya ‘nāyamapahnuterbhedo vaktuṃ yuktaḥ apahnutisāmānyalakṣaṇānākrāntatvāt | tathā hi ‘prakṛtaṃ yanniṣidhyānyatsādhyate sā tvapahnutiḥ’ upa meyamasatyaṃ kṛtvā upamāne satyatayā yat sthāpyate sā’pahnutiḥ’ iti kāvyaprakāśoktalakṣaṇabahirbhāvastāvat sphuṭa eva | evaṃ ‘viṣayāpahnave vastvantarapratītāvapahnutiḥ’ ityalaṃkārasarvasvoktalakṣaṇamapi nātra pravartate ||
prakṛtasya niṣedhena yadanyatvaprakalpanam |
sāmyādapahnutirvākyabhedābhedavatī dvidhā ||
iti citramīmāṃsāgataṃ lakṣaṇamapi tathaiva | tasmāt ‘nāyaṃ sudhāṃśuḥ kiṃ tarhi sudhāṃśuḥ preyasīmukham’ ityatra dṛḍhāropaṃ rūpakameva bhavitumarhati nāpahnutiḥ | upameyatopamānatāvacchedakayossāmānādhikaraṇyasya niṣpratyūhaṃ bhānāt | taduktaṃ vimarśinyām ‘na viṣaṃ viṣamityāhurbrahmasvaṃ viṣamucyate’ atra viṣasya niṣedhapūrvakaṃ brahmasve āropyamāṇatvāt dṛḍhāropaṃ rūpakameva nāpahnutiriti” iti ||
tarhi dīkṣitānāṃ kathamidaṃ parasparavipratiṣiddhavāditvamiticecchṛṇu citramīmāṃsālakṣaṇaṃ prācāmanurodhena, idaṃ tu ratnākarādyanurodheneti na virodhaḥ ||
atha bhrāntyapahnutiḥ
bhrānternivāraṇe’nyasya śaṅkāyāṃ bhrāntyapahnutiḥ ||
anyasya bhrānternivāraṇe tattvākhyāne sati bhrāntyapahnutiḥ | imāṃ bhrāntāpahnutirityapyāhuḥ | tadā bhrāntamiti bhāve ktaḥ | tasyāpahnutiḥ | evaṃ ca tattvakathanahetukabhrāntiviṣayaniṣedho bhrāntyapahnutiriti lakṣaṇaṃ bodhyam || yathā
śiśirayate mama hṛdayaṃ kiṃ ghanasāro’tha gandhasāro vā | na hi na hi sapadi smṛtipathamahigirinetā sakhe samānītaḥ || 348 ||
atra śiśirayate mama hṛdayamiti bhagavadvṛttānte gadite tasya hṛdayaśabdapratipādyacittavakṣorūpārthadvayaśleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasāyena ghanasārādisādhāraṇyātkevalabāhyaviṣayapravaṇena kenacitsakhyā kiṃ ghanasāro’tha gandhasāro vetyanuyukte nahinahītyādinā bhrāntivāraṇaṃ kṛtam | idaṃ saṃbhavadbhrāntipūrvikāyāṃ bhrāntāpahnutāvudāharaṇam ||
kalpitabhrāntipūrvā yathā
na ghanatatiḥ kabarīyaṃ na vidyudeṣā cakāsti sīmantaḥ| śailadhṛtau saṃnahyasi kiṃ kṛṣṇetyāha phaṇigiripatiṃ śrīḥ || 349 ||
sīmantaḥ keśavīdhī ‘sīmantamastriyāṃ strīṇāṃ keśamadhyastha paddhatiḥ’ iti vaijayantī | śailadhṛtau govardhanadhāraṇe, ata eva kṛṣṇeti saṃbodhanam | śailatvenādhyavasitastanagrahaṇe ca | atra kalpitā bhrāntiḥ na ghanatatirityādiniṣedhamātronneyā pūrvavatpraśnavaidhuryāt | daṇḍī tu īdṛśasthale tattvākhyānopametyupamābhedamamanyata | yadāha
na patraṃ mukhamevedaṃ na bhṛṅgau cakṣuṣī ime |
iti vispaṣṭasādṛśyāttatvākhyānopamā matā ||
iti | vastutastvatropamābodhakasya ivāderabhāvādupamāprabhedo na yuktaḥ | tadabhāve’pyupamābhyupagame rūpakasyāpyupamātvāpatteḥ |
yathā vā
na taṭidiyaṃ jaladhisutā na taṭitvāneṣa śeṣagirinetā | haṃsānandavidhāyī kiṃ sīdasi cittahaṃsa śaṃsa mama || 350 ||
atrāpi kalpitā bhrāntiḥ niṣedhamātronneyā praśnavirahāt | na taṭittvāniti niṣedhe haṃsānandavidhāyīti hetūktiḥ cittahaṃseti rūpakasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ ca pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
chekāpahnutiḥ
anyasya śaṅkayā chekāpahnutistathyanihnutau || 53 ||
kasyacitkaṃcitprati rahasyakathane itareṇa śrute svavacanasyāśayāntaraparikalpanena satyasyāpalāpe chekāpahnutirityarthaḥ | chekāḥ vidagdhāḥ ‘cheko vidagdhe viśvaste’ iti hemacandraḥ | tatkṛtā apahnutiḥ chekāpahnutiriti lakṣyanirdeśaḥ | vākyānyathāyojanahetukaḥ śaṅkitatātvikavastuniṣedha iti lakṣaṇam | anyasya śaṅkayetyanyaśaṅkāyā nivartanīyatvena hetutayā vyapadeśaḥ kṣudhā’nnamattītivat | atra ca
kasya vā na bhavedroṣaḥ priyāyāssavraṇe’dhare |
sabhṛṅgaṃ padmamāghrāsīrvāritā’pi mayā’dhunā ||
iti vyājoktāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāyādyaṃ viśeṣaṇam | yadvakṣyate ‘chekāpahnuterasyāścāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ yattatra vacanasyānyathānayanenāpahnavaḥ asyāmākārasya hetvantaravarṇanena gopanam’ iti ||
udāharaṇam
nīlarucirmama citte khelati satataṃ kimañjanādrīśaḥ | na hi na hi suravaramaṇiriti caturā padmāvatī sakhīmāha || 351 ||
atra padmāvatyā narmasakhīṃ prati nīlarucirmama hṛdaye khelatīti svapriyatamaśrīnivāsavṛttānte nivedyamāne tanniśamayya kimañjanādrīśa iti śaṅkitavatīmanyāṃ prati na hi na hi suravaramaṇiriti satyasyaivāpahnavaḥ ||
yathā vā
abhimukhametyābhimṛśannamṛtatuṣāreṇa māṃ sakhikareṇa | adhiniśamajīhṛṣadalaṃ śrīvatsāṅkaḥ kimāli na mṛgāṅkaḥ || 352 ||
amṛtena amṛtamiva ca tuṣāraśśītalaḥ tena kareṇa kiraṇena hastena ca adhiniśaṃ rātrau ajīhṛṣat harṣayati sma | hṛṣerṇyantātkartari luṅi caṅ | atrāpi kayācidvrajalalanayā krīḍāsakhīṃ prati abhimukhametyetyādinā nandanandanodante nivedyamāne tadākarṇya śrīvatsāṅkaḥ kimiti śaṅkitavatīmanyāṃ prati na mṛgāṅka iti tathyasyaivāpahnavaḥ | idamarthayojanayā satyanihnutāvudāharaṇadvayam | vastutastu dvitīyamudāharaṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇarītyā śabdārthobhayayojanayā’pahnuterevodāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
arthayojanayā nihnutirnāma vivakṣitāvivakṣitasādhāraṇasyārthasyāvivakṣitārthasaṃbandhitvavarṇanayā tasyāpalāpaḥ | śabdayojanayā sā tu śabdaśleṣamātreṇa tatsaṃbandhitvavarṇanayā’palāpaḥ ||
yathā
kamale vipule nayane dhyāyāmi tavāhamiti haṭhādgadite | kupitāyāṃ nīlāyāṃ tatparatāṃ tasya varṇayati śauriḥ || 353 ||
atra kamale vipule ityanayorarthabhede’pi śabdaśleṣamātreṇānyathā yojanayā tatthyanihnavaḥ | he kamale ! lakṣmi ! vipule viśāle tava nayane dhyāyāmīti vā, he vipule ! dharaṇi ! tava nayane kamalasadṛśī dhyāyāmīti vā pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | nīlāparatve tu he nīle ! iti saṃbuddhirarthāllabhyate | tadā tava nayane kamale vipule ca dhyāyāmīti yojanā | atra ramābhūmisaṃbodhyakastannayanadhyānarūpo na vākyārthaḥ kiṃtu tvatsaṃbodhyakaḥ kamalarūpavipulatvannayanadhyānarūpa ityapahnavastatparatāṃ tasya kalpayati śaurirityanena prakāśyate | tasya uktavacanasyetyarthaḥ | na cātra lakṣmībhūmīnīlāsādhāraṇyamarthasya, api tu kamale vipule iti liṅgavacanaśliṣṭaśabdayojanaiveti ||
yathā vā
priyahṛdaye hāriṇi niśi suptā na viveda kimapi subhage’ham | kiṃ śaurihṛdi na hīmāṃ bahudhā saṃbāodhya śayanasukhamavadam || 354 ||
hāriṇi muktāhāravati subhage sundare priyasya bhagavato nandanandanasya hṛdaye vakṣasi | pakṣe he hāriṇi manohare! he priyahṛdaye! priyaṃ hṛdayaṃ cittaṃ yasyāssā matpriyakāriṇīti yāvat | he subhage! saubhāgyaśālinīti sakhyāssaṃbuddhayaḥ | kiṃ śauririti taṭasthāyāḥ praśnaḥ | na hītyādikaṃ tathyanihnavottaraṃ nāyikāyāḥ | atrāpi priyahṛdaye ityādīnāmarthabhede’pi śabdaśleṣamātreṇānyathāyojanayā tathyanihnavaḥ | na vivedeti sukhapāravaśyakṛtaḥ parokṣanirdeśaḥ | ‘bahu jagada’ ityādivat ||
evam
niśi tamasi nimīlantīṃ svakareṇa hṛdi spṛśā samunnidrām| bhṛśamuditassamatānītkiṃ śauristvāṃ na kumudinīminduḥ || 355 ||
ityatrāpi śabdayojanayā tathyanihnavo jñeyaḥ | niśi rātrau tamasi timire nimīlantīṃ suptāṃ nimīlitakumudāṃ ca | hṛdispṛśā vakṣasi spṛśatā, pakṣe manohareṇa vikāsakeneti vā | ‘hṛdyubhyāṃ ca’ iti saptamyā aluk | svena nijena kareṇa hastena kiraṇena ca | jātāvekavacanam | bhṛśamuditaḥ ativelaprahṛṣṭassan, pakṣe bhṛśaṃ udita iti chedaḥ udayaṃ prāptassan | bhṛśamityetat unnidrāmityanenānveti | unnidrāṃ nidrāyā ḍadgatāṃ tyaktanidrāmityarthaḥ | pakṣe vikasitāṃ ‘tathā puṣpitamunnidram’ ityamaraḥ | samatānīditi narmasakhīṃ prati nāyikāyā niśi svapriyaviracitavilāsavṛttāntavarṇanam | kiṃ śauristvāmiti taṭasthāyāḥ praśnaḥ | kumudinīminduriti nāyikāyāstathyanihnavottaram ||
śabdārthobhayayojanayā yathā
vraṇayatyadharaṃ vapuṣo vepathukunmama viśeṣato’dhiniśam | ārādasau himānī śauriḥ kiṃ na sakhi himatatirmahatī || 356 ||
asau himānītyatra śabdayojanayā nihnavaḥ anyatrārthayojanayeti dhyeyam | tathyārthe hi mānī iti padacchedaḥ, nihnavapakṣe himānītyekaṃ padam | asāvityetatpuṃstrīliṅgasādhāraṇam | atrāsau hi mānīti śauriparatayā puṃliṅgena nirdiṣṭasya śabdasyaiva asau himānīti strīliṅgatayā himānīparatāvarṇanācchabdayojanayā’pahnava iti | mahaddhimaṃ himānī ‘himāraṇyayormahattve’ iti ‘indravaruṇa’ iti sūtraprāpta ānugāgamasanniyogaśiṣṭo ṅīṣ niyamyate ||
yathā vā
ehi sakhi satyabhāme rukmiṇi dayitā tvamiti haṭhādukte | ruṣṭāṃ rādhāṃ tatparamāracayaṃstatprasādayati śauriḥ || 357 ||
rādhāparatve he sati ! sādhvi ! he abhāme! akopane! he rukmiṇi! rukmamasyā astīti tathokte! suvarṇābharaṇavatītyarthaḥ | he sakhi! tvameva me dayiteti yojanā | tat svoktaṃ vacanaṃ tatparaṃ rādhāparaṃ āracayan | satyabhāme rukmiṇītyatra śabdayojanayā sakhi dayitetyatrārthayojanayā’pahnava iti draṣṭavyam || sarvamidaṃ viṣayāntarayojanāyāmudāharaṇam | viṣayaikye’pyavasthābhedena yojanāyāṃ yathā
kṛṣṇo mama kamana iti bruvatī bhartre sakhībhramādatha tam | jñātvā sakhi kiṃ jagada svapnādahamityapūri dhūrtaikā || 358 ||
atra vivakṣitaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaviṣayakānurāgavṛttāntaṃ nidrājāgaryāvyatikarajātātsahaśayāne bhartari sakhībhramādudīritavatyā kayā’pi gopikayā tāvataiva jāgarūkatayā bhartṛjñānajanitabhayasaṃbhrāntayā tadvṛttāntatatparasya vākyasya svapnāvasthāgatatadvṛttāntaparatvaṃ sakhi kiṃ jagada svapnādahamiti śeṣapūraṇena tatrāpi jagadeti pārokṣyābhidhāyiliḍuttamaprayogeṇa ca sphuṭīkṛtam | atra nāyikāvivakṣitaśrīkṛṣṇānurāgavṛttāntasya yathārthabhūtajāgarāvasthāgatatvanihnavo’rthasiddha iti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ kāryā | jagadetyatra ‘ṇaluttamo vā’ iti ṇittvasya vaikalpikatvānnopadhāvṛddhiḥ | apūrītyatra kartari luṅi ‘dīpajanabudhapūri’ ityādinā vaikalpikaściṇ | apūrayadityarthaḥ ||
yathā vā
svairaṃ vyaharāvāhaṃ śauriśceti pramādato gaditam | kā’pi vadhūrgurunikaṭe bālyamayantraṇamiti sma pūrayati || 359 ||
kā’pi vadhūḥ gopasnuṣā ahaṃ śauriśca svairaṃ vyaharāva vihṛtavantau iti vipūrvakāddharaterlaṅuttamadvivacanam | gurunikaṭe śvaśrūprabhṛtigurujanasaṃnidhau pramādataḥ anavadhānāt gaditaṃ ātmanoktaṃ vacanaṃ bālyaṃ bālakarma bālaiḥ kriyamāṇo’nabhisaṃdhikavyāpāra ityarthaḥ | ayantraṇaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ bālavyāpāre idamevaṃkartavyamidamevaṃ neti nirbandho na śakyaḥ kartumiti bhāvaḥ | iti pūrayati sma | anaticirānubhūtanandanandanasvacchandopabhogarasāyanāviratāsvādaikatānāntaraṅgayā kayācidvrajalalanayā tenaiva saṃskāreṇa pramādavaśādgurujanasaṃnidhau svairaṃ vyaharāveti gaditasya vṛttāntasya bālyāvasthāgataśrīkṛṣṇasahakṛtadhūlīkeliprabhṛtivṛttantaparatvaṃ bālyamayantraṇamiti śeṣapūraṇena sphuṭīkṛtam | pūrvodāharaṇe svapnāvasthābhedena yojanā | atra tu bālyāvasthābhedeneti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
sarajaskāṃ snātāmatha śaurirmāmāhvayatsamaṃ bhoktum | ityavaśāduktvaikā bālye maitrī hyakṛtrimetyagadīt || 360 ||
atra ṛtusnātasvakarmakopabhogaphalakaśaurikṛtāhvānarūpaṃ vākyārthaṃ tathyabhūtaṃ gurujananikaṭe pramādādudīrya tāvataiva prabuddhayā bālye maitrī hyakṛtrimeti vadantyā kayācana vidagdhayā nāyikayā bālyanirvartitadhūlīkelīdhūsaritatvanimittakasnānakartṛsvakarmakasahabhojanakriyāphalakatādātvikaśaurikṛtāhvānarūpatayā sa evānyathā yojitaḥ | atrāpyavasthābhedena yojanā pūrvavadeva | śleṣopaskṛtatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ | tāttvikārthapakṣe śauriḥ rajasā ārtavena saha vartata iti sarajaskā tāṃ, anyatra sadhūlikām | atha ārtavānantaraṃ dhūlīlepānantaraṃ ca | snātāṃ māṃ samaṃ parasparānurūpaṃ bhoktuṃ upabhoktuṃ anyatra sahabhojanāya, māṇavakadaśāyāṃ pumāṃsaḥ kanyābhissaha svairaṃ bhuñjata iti prasiddhamevedam | āhvayat āhūtavān iti avaśāt pramādāt uktvā bālye śaiśave maitrī sakhyaṃ akṛtrimā nirvyājetyagadīt avādīdityarthaḥ | bālye maitrītyādyuktissvoktasyārthasya bālyanirvartitatvadyotanāya ||
kaitavāpahnutiḥ
chalacchadmanibhādyaiśca vapurātmādikaiḥ padaiḥ | nihnutiścedabhivyaṅgyā kaitavāpahnutirhi sā || 54 ||
atra chalādiśabdā viṣayāsatyatvapratipādakāḥ | vapurādiśabdāstu viṣayāsatyatvaparyavasāyina iti vivekaḥ |chalādītyādipadena kapaṭakaitavavyājamiṣāpadeśopadhidambhādiśabdā gṛhyante | vapurātmādītyādipadena rūpanāmarītibhaṅgyādiśabdā iti dhyeyam ||
yathā
dharaṇīnibharamaṇīmaṇiphaṇiśikharicchalakucāgravinyastaḥ | harikaitavamṛgamada iha haratāṃ svaguṇena jaḍatāṃ naḥ || 361 ||
svaguṇena jñānaśaktivātsalyādinā anyatra auṣṇyena jaḍatāṃ ajñatāṃ śaityaṃ ca haratāṃ apanayatāt | prasiddhaṃ hyajñānahāritvaṃ hareḥ śaityahāritvaṃ mṛganābheśca | atra viṣayāsatyatvapratipādakanibhacchalakaitavaśabdairneyaṃ dharaṇī kiṃtu ramaṇītyādiniṣedhabodhanādekavākyatvaṃ niṣedhatādrūpyayorārthatvaṃ vidheyatvaṃ ca, jaḍatāharaṇasya vidheyatvāt | sāvayavā ceyaṃ anugrāhyānugrāhakabhāvāpannāvayavakasaṃghātarūpatvāt || yathā vā
śitabhānurapi prathayati sitabhānuriva dvijanmarājattvam | maṇinūpurayugavapuṣā phaṇināyakaśailanāthapadayugme || 362 ||
atra nedaṃ maṇinūpurayugaṃ apitu dvijanmarājattvaṃ prakāśayaṃstigmabhānurevetyasatyaparyavasāyivapuśśabdabalānniṣedhaḥ pratīyate | ekavākyatvādikaṃ pūrvavat | niravayavā ceyam | dvijanmarājatvaṃ dvijarājatvamityarthaḥ | ‘soma eva no rājetyāhurbrāhmaṇīḥ prajāḥ’ iti śruteścandramaso dvijarājatvam | rājattvamityatrārthāntarānurūpyāya ‘anaci ca’ iti takārasya dvitvam | pakṣe rājato bhāvaḥ rājattvaṃ dvividhe janmanī dvijanmanī tābhyāṃ rājattvaṃ prakāśamānatvamityanayorarthayoḥ śleṣeṇābhedādhyavasāyaḥ ||
vimarśinīkārastu chalādītyādiśabdena tṛtīyayā’pi kvacidasatyatvaṃ pratipādyata ityabhāṇīt | tatredamudāharaṇaṃ
phaṇigirimiṣodayādrau madhumathanacchalasudhākarābhyudaye | tadupatyakāvanacchalajaladhiḥ phenāyate sma kusumaughaiḥ || 363 ||
phenamudvamati phenāyate “phenācceti vaktavyam’ iti vārtikena udvamanārthe kyaṅ | atra kusumaughairiti tṛtīyayā nāyaṃ kusumaughaḥ kiṃtu phena evetyapahnavanibandhanam | yadyapīdṛśasthale ‘nadyā śekhariṇe’ ityādāviva vaiyadhikaraṇyenāroparūpaṃ rūpakameveti yuktam, athāpyapahnavopakramānurodhenānte’pyapahnavasya tṛtīyayā pratipāditatvaṃ vimarśinīkārānurodhenoktam | vastutastu kaitavāpahnutyanuprāṇitaṃ vyadhikaraṇarūpakamevedaṃ bhaviṣyati | ata eva vimarśinīkārānurodhinā’pi rasagaṅgādharakāreṇāyaṃ pakṣo’nādṛta iti dhyeyam ||
iyaṃ mālārūpā’pi bhavati | tatrāpi bahūnniṣidhya ekasyārope ekaṃ niṣidhya bahūnāmārope ceti dvidheti kaustubhakārādayaḥ ||
tatrādyā yathā
maṇisānumiṣāddharaṇau phaṇiśaya phaṇimaṇimiṣeṇa pātāle | surasaraṇau dyumaṇimiṣātpratāparāśiḥ kṛtāvatāraste || 364 ||
maṇisānuḥ sumeruḥ | phaṇiśayeti bhagavatsaṃbuddhiḥ | anyatsugamam ||
vidrumamiṣeṇa jaladhau kaladhautāmbujamiṣeṇa surasariti | karuvindabṛndamiṣato dviṣatāṃ mauliṣvabhātprabhāvaste || 365 ||
kaladhautaṃ hema ‘kaladhautaṃ rūpyahemnoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | mandākinyāṃ hemāravindasthitiḥ prasiddhā | prabhāvaḥ pratāpaḥ | ‘syātpratāpaḥ prabhāvaśca’ ityamaraḥ | atra pītavarṇatayā prathitasya kanakācalādeḥ lohitatayā prasiddhasya vidrumādeśca lohitatayaiva kavisamayasiddhaṃ pratāpaṃ pratyupamānatāvarṇanaṃ pītalohitayorvarṇayorabhedasyāpi kavisamayasiddhatvādityavadheyam | tathācoktamalaṃkāraśekhare 
kamalāsaṃpadoḥ kṛṣṇaharitornāgasarpayoḥ |
pītalohitayossvarṇaparāgāgniśikhādiṣu ||
candre śaśaiṇayoḥ kāmadhvaje makaramatsyayoḥ|
dānavāsuradaityānāmaikyamevābhisaṃhitam || iti ||
śītāṃśurucimiṣāddivi śītādryanimiṣadhunī miṣādavanau | pātāle śeṣamiṣātkhyātāni yaśāṃsi tava vibhānti vibho || 366 ||
śītādriḥ himagiriḥ | animiṣadhunī gaṅgā | tayormiṣāt vyājāt | he vibho bhagavan !||
ūrdhvaṃ nabhastalanibhādadhaśca vasudhātalacchalādbhagavan | jaladhicchalātsamantādvibhā vibhaktā vibhāti tava bhūman || 367 ||
vasudhātalasya nailyaṃ ‘yatkṛṣṇaṃ tadannasya’ iti śrutyanurodhinā ‘kṣmāmaṇḍalaṃ kuvalayākarakomalābham’ itivarṇanena siddhamiti dhyeyam ||
kvacidiha marakatarūpaṃ kvacidabbhranibhaṃ kvacittamālanibham | kvacidindīvaradambhaṃ mahojjvalaṃ tava maho jagati bhāti || 368 ||
nibhaśabdo’tra vyājavācī ‘nibhastu kathito vyāje’ iti medinī | atra śyāmalatvena prasiddhasya bhagavanmahasaḥ haridvarṇamarakatopamānakathanaṃ anupadodāhṛtapramāṇena nīlaharidvarṇayorabhedapratipādanāt || hīramiṣāttāṭaṅke hāramiṣādamba kaṃdharāyāṃ te | mālyamiṣāddhammille smitaruciśakalā harervibhāntyamalāḥ || 369 ||
eṣūdāharaṇeṣu sumeruprabhṛtīnbahūnniṣidhya tatra pratāpāderekasyāropaḥ ||
dvitīyā yathā
vedāṃstatpratipādyānpuruṣārthāṃstatkṛte nṛbhirūpāsyān | caturo bhagavadvyūhānbāhāvyājādbibharṣi janani tvam || 370 ||
catura ityetat vedapuruṣārthabhagavadvyūhānāṃ trayāṇāmapi viśeṣaṇam| vedāḥ ṛgyajussāmātharvākhyāḥ puruṣārthāḥ dharmārthakāmamokṣākhyāḥ | bhagavadvyūhāḥ vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhākhyāḥ | atra lakṣmībāhāniṣedhapūrvakaṃ tatra vedādīnāṃ bahūnāmaikyāropaḥ ||
bhuvanatrayīṃ trayīṃ vā triguṇāṃ vā prakṛtimahigiridyumaṇe | kukṣau nidhāyabhagavanrakṣasyaviralavalitrayīvyājāt || 371 ||
trayīṃ ṛgādivedatrayaṃ ‘striyāmṛksāmayajuṣī iti vedāstrayastrayī’ ityamaraḥ | triguṇāṃ sattvarajastamoguṇātmikām | atrāpi valitrayīniṣedhapūrvakaṃ tasyāṃ bhuvanatrayyādīnāmanekeṣāmaikyāropaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre apahnavasarastrayodaśaḥ
<15>
athotprekṣāsaraḥ
yadanyadharmasaṃbandhādanyasyānyatvatarkaṇam |
sotprekṣoktā tridhaiṣā syādvastuhetuphalātmanā ||
uktānuktāspadatvena vastūtprekṣā dvidhā matā |
hetūtprekṣāphalotprekṣe siddhāsiddhāspadatvataḥ ||
anyasya viṣayiṇo yo dharmaḥ tatsambandharūpānnimittāt anyasya anyaviṣayakaṃ anyatvatarkaṇaṃ anyatvena saṃbhāvanaṃ viṣāyiṇastādātmyena saṃbhāvanamityarthaḥ | anyasyeti ṣaṣṭhyartho viṣayatādharmitārūpā, viṣayiṇa iti ṣaṣṭhyarthastu viśeṣaṇatārūpā viṣayatā | anyatvenotkīrtanaṃ ca saṃbhāvanāyā āhāryatāsūcanāya | tathāca viṣayiniṣṭhadharmasaṃbandhaprayuktaṃ viṣayadharmikaṃ tādātmyasaṃsargeṇa viṣayividheyakamāhāryasaṃbhāvanamutprekṣeti paryavasitam | tanniṣṭhadharmasaṃbandhaprayuktamāhāryatatsaṃbhāvanamiti tu niṣkarṣaḥ | itarāṃśastu avyāvartakatvena svarūpakathanamātraparaḥ | ‘mukhaṃ candraṃ manye’ ityutprekṣāyāṃ candraniṣṭhāhlādakatvādidharmasaṃbandhaprayuktaṃ mukhe candrasaṃbhāvanamāhāryamastīti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ | bādhādyabhāvadaśāyāṃ tu jāyamānā mukhādau candrādisaṃbhāvanā notprekṣeti tadvāraṇāyāhāryeti ||
etena
dundubhikāhalahalahalabandhuramakhilaṃ digantarālamabhūt | yattanmanye phaṇigiryuttaṃso bhajati sapadi jayayātrām || 372|| ityatra dundubhiprabhṛtikolāhaladigantarālavyāptirūpajayayātrāpravṛttadharmasaṃbandhaprayuktāyāṃ bhagavati tatsaṃbhāvanāyāmapi nātivyāptiḥ | tasyā anāhāryatvāt | ‘saṃbhāvanaṃ yadītthaṃ syādityūho’nyasya siddhaye’ iti saṃbhāvanālaṃkāravaṣaye
yadi jaladhirvijalassyādyadi visuvarṇassuvarṇabhūbhṛtsyāt | jahyādrakṣādīkṣāmahyadriśikhāmaṇistadā manye || 373 ||
ityādāvativyāptivāraṇāya prayuktāntam | atra yadi jaladhirvijalassyādityādisaṃbhāvanasya viṣayiniṣṭhadharmasambandhaprayuktatvābhāvāt ||
kākodaragirimenaṃ lokottararāmaṇīyakaṃ manye | padamapi paramaṃ vijahadviharati yadiha śriyā saha mukundaḥ || 374 ||
