ityādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāya tanniṣṭheti saṃbhāvyamānavṛttitvaṃ dharmaviśeṣaṇam | tatra paramapadatyāgapūrvakaśrīsahitaviharaṇarūpo dharmo mukundavṛttiḥ | na tu saṃbhāvyamānavṛttiriti nātiprasaṅgaḥ | nanu saṃbandhāntareṇotprekṣāyā asaṃgraha iti cenna | sarvatrābhedainaivotprekṣaṇamiti prācīnamatānurodhenaivamabhidhānāt | etatsūcanāyaiva lakṣaṇe tādātmyopādānamiti dhyeyam ||
sā cotprekṣā vastuhetuphalātmatāgocaratayā trividhā, vastuhetuphalātmatā ca vastuhetuphalatādātmyam | atra vastunaḥ kasyacidvastvantaratādātmyasaṃbhāvanā prathamā vastūtprekṣetyucyate | iyameva svarūpotprekṣā | ahetorhetubhāvena aphalasya phalatvenotprekṣā hetūtprekṣā phalotprekṣetyucyate | tatrādyā svarūpotprekṣā uktānuktāspadatvena dvividhā | āspadaṃ viṣayaḥ | uktaviṣayā anuktaviṣayā ceti dvividhetyarthaḥ | hetūtprekṣāphalotprekṣe siddhaviṣayā asiddhaviṣayā ceti pratyekaṃ dvividhe ||
krameṇodāharaṇāni
hariśekharitādahipatidharaṇidharādadhikatāṃ parāṃ lipsuḥ | hariśirasi bhāsamānassuragiriśiśuriva mahākirīṭo’vyāt || 375 ||
atra kanakamaṇimayatvādinā dharmasaṃbandhena nimittena mahākirīṭasya sumeruśiśutādātmyasaṃbhāvanā svarūpotprekṣā mahākirīṭasya viṣayasyopādānāduktaviṣayā ||
yathā vā
visṛmaranijarucibharaparivalayitatanurahigirīndrapatidayitā | prakaṭayatīva hiraṇyaprākārātvaṃ svamāgamaproktam || 376 ||
atra piśaṅgatvādinā gamyamānena nimittena lakṣmītanurucivalayasya hiraṇyaprākāratādātmyasaṃbhāvanā | vastutastu prakaṭanakriyotprekṣāyā evātra prādhānyena nirdiṣṭatvādvakṣyamāṇarītyā kriyāsvarūpotprekṣāyā evedamudāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyatīti dhyeyam | anyattu agre nirūpayiṣyate | atra ‘hiraṇyaprākārāmārdrām’ iti śrutyartho’nusaṃhitaḥ || anuktaviṣayā vastūtprekṣā yathā
abhiṣiñcatīva māmayamahirājadharādharādhipatiramṛtaiḥ | dayanīyasya mamālaṃ nayane divyāñjanairanaktīva || 377 ||
atrāpyāyakatvādinā nimittena gamyamānena bhagavatkartṛkānandanasya tatkartṛkāmṛtābhiṣekatādātmyotprekṣā divyāñjanāñjanatādātmyoprekṣā cānuktaviṣayā svarūpotprekṣā, ākhyātopasthite kartari bhagavatastādātmyenānvaye karturdhātvarthe abhiṣecane añjane cānvayāt tasya ivaśabdārthe saṃbhāvane tasya ca viṣayatayā ānandane anvayādbhagavatkartṛkābhiṣecanāñjanasaṃbhāvanāviṣaya ānandanamiti vākyārthabodhaparyavasānāt ubhayatrāpi viṣayabhūtasyānandanasya viṣayivācakābhyāmabhiṣiñcatyanaktibhyāmanyenānupādānācca ||
nanvatra abhiṣiñcatyanaktīti dhātvarthayorabhiṣecanāñjanayorākhyātopātte kartari viśeṣaṇatvenānvayamabhyupetya karturivaśabdārthe saṃbhāvane’nvayena abhiṣecanāñjanakartṛtādātmyamevotprekṣyatām | tataśca bhagavato viṣayasyopādānena uktaviṣayatālābhāt bhagavataḥ prathamāntapadopasthāpitasya mukhyaviśeṣyatayā prathamāntaviśeṣyakabodho’pi tāntrikasaṃpratipanna upapādito bhavatītyutprekṣādvayamuktaviṣayamevāstu | sakṛduccaritābhyāmabhiṣiñcatyanaktibhyāṃ śaktyā abhiṣekāñjanayoḥ sādhyavasānalakṣaṇayā sādhāraṇadharmapuraskāreṇānandanasya copasthāpanāyā yugapadvṛttidvayavirodhenāsaṃbhavāditi cenmaivaṃ, ‘laḥ rkamaṇi ca’ iti sūtrānusāreṇākhyātasya kartrādiśaktatayā prakṛte abhiṣiñcatyanaktītyābhyāmākhyātābhyāṃ karturupasthitāvapi ‘bhāvapradhānamākhyātam’ iti yāskasmaraṇāt bhāvo dhātvarthaḥ pradhānaṃ viśeṣyaṃ yasya tadākhyātamityarthakāddhātvarthabhūtābhiṣecanāñjanakriyayoreva viśeṣyatayā tadupasarjanatvenānvitasya karturutprekṣāviṣayatayā ivādiśabdārthabhūtasaṃbhāvanāyāmanvayāsaṃbhavāt | evamākhyātasya kriyoparsajanatvādeva
limpatīva tamo’ṅgāni varṣatīvāñjanaṃ nabhaḥ |
itīdamapi bhūyiṣṭhamutprekṣālakṣaṇānvitam ||
keṣāṃcidupamābhrāntirivaśrutyeha jāyate |
nopamānaṃ tiṅantenetyatikramyāptabhāṣitam ||
ityādinā utprekṣāsthale ‘limpatīva’ ityatropamābhrāntiṃ keṣāṃcitsaṃbhavantīṃ pratikṣipatā daṇḍinā
kartā yadyupamānaṃ syānnyagbhūto’sau kriyāpade |
svakriyāsādhanavyagro nālamanyadvyapekṣitum ||
iti karturupamāyāmupamānatayā’nvayo’pi nirākṛtaḥ | tathāhi kārikārthaḥ ‘limpatīva’ ityatra lepanakarturivaśabdārthe sādṛśye upamānatvenānvayasaṃbhavādupamaivātra kiṃ na syādityāśaṅkāyā uttaratvenāyaṃ śloko’vatāritaḥ | kartā yadyupamānaṃ syāttadeyaṃ śaṅkā yujyeta, na cedaṃ saṃbhavati, tatra hetuḥ nyagbhūta ityādiḥ | asau kartā kriyāpade tiṅante nyagbhūtaḥ kriyopasarjanībhūta ityarthaḥ |
phalavyāpārayordhāturāśraye tu tiṅassmṛtāḥ |
phale pradhānaṃ vyāpārastiṅarthastu viśeṣaṇam ||
ityukteḥ | nyagbhūtatāmevottarārdhenopapādayati sveti | svakriyāyāḥ lepanādirūpāyāḥ sādhane vyagraḥ vyāpṛtaḥ asau kartā anyat svasādhyakriyāpekṣayā itarat vyapekṣituṃ ākāṅkṣituṃ sādhayitumiti yāvat nālaṃ na samarthaḥ | yathā kasyacidadhīno nānyasya śaknuyātsādhayituṃ kāryaṃ tathā ekasyāḥ kriyāyā viśeṣaṇatvenādhīnaḥ kartā’nyasyopamānarūpasya viśeṣaṇatāṃ nāptumīṣṭe, itaraviśeṣaṇatvenopasthitasyānyatra viśeṣaṇatvenānvayāyogāt | ato nātra karturupamānatvaṃ saṃgacchata iti | tathāca ivaśabdārthassaṃbhāvanaṃ sādṛśyaṃ vā’stu, ubhayathā’pi na tatra karturanvaya iti nopamā yukteti | tasmāttamasi lepanakartṛtvamutprekṣyamiti matamanupādeyameva | tasyāpi karturviśeṣaṇatvāt | vivakṣitavivekena lepanasyaivotprekṣaṇīyatvācca | evaṃcodāhṛtapadye pradhānabhūtābhiṣecanāñjanakriyāgocarā saṃbhāvanaiva nigīrṇānandanaviṣayeti siddham| na ca yugapadvṛttidvayavirodhadoṣatā, tadanabhyupagamāt | ye tu’limpatīva tamo’ṅgāni varṣatīvāñjanaṃ nabhaḥ’ ityatra tamonabhasorviṣayayostatkartṛkalepanavarṣaṇasvarūpadharmotprekṣetyāhuḥ, tanmate svarūpotprekṣāyāṃ dharmyutprekṣā dharmotprekṣā cetyevaṃ dvaividhyam | tatra tamonabhasoḥ kartṛtvena viṣayatvena ca dvivāramanvayakleśaḥ | tathā dharmyutprekṣā, sādharmyaprayuktā dharmotprekṣā tu tatsahacaritadharmasaṃbandhaprayukteti lakṣaṇānanugamaḥ | na ca tanniṣṭhatatsamānādhikaraṇānyataratvena dharmaniveśānnānanugama iti vācyam | sarvatra sādṛśyanimittāyā evotprekṣāyāssaṃbhavenānyataratvādiniveśaprayuktagauravasyānupādeyatvāditi vadanti ||
yathā vā
śrutyañcalasaṃsaktā muktāśśrutyantacapalamakṣiyugam | avalokya bhuvanamātastava devi bhṛśaṃ hasantīva || 378 || atrāpi karṇābharaṇamauktikasaṃbandhiprakāśasya tatkartṛkahāsatādātmyotprekṣā anuktaviṣayā | prakāśasya viṣayasya nigīrṇatvāditi bodhyam |
siddhaviṣayā hetūtprekṣā yathā
khañjanarucikhañjanabalabhañjanamaṇimañjurucinicayanicule | añjanaśikhariṇi satatāsañjanato nūnamasita eṣa hariḥ || 379 ||
khañjayati gativikalaṃ karotīti khañjanaḥ | khañjanaruceḥ khañjarīṭākhyakhagatviṣaḥ khañjanaḥ tatopyatiśayitanīlimetyarthaḥ | ‘khañjarīṭastu khañjanaḥ, khoḍe khañjaḥ’ iti cāmaraḥ | balabhañjanamaṇayaḥ indranīlaratnāni teṣāṃ mañjurucinicayaḥ niculaḥ nicolaḥ yasya tasmin ‘niculastu nicolake’ iti viśvaḥ | nicolaḥ pracchadapaṭaḥ ‘vinayaniculitaiḥ’ iti murāriprayogaśca | atra bhagavato naisargike śyāmalimni vastuto’ñjanagirisatatāsañjanaṃ na heturityahetostasya hetutvasaṃbhāvanā hetūtprekṣā añjanagirisatatāsañjanasya siddhatvātsiddhaviṣayā añjanaśabdaśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyamūlā | prasiddhaṃ hyañjanasya nailyam ||
asiddhaviṣayā hetūtprekṣā yathā
śrīvatsa iti hare tvaṃ vātsalyādiva ramāśraye hṛdaye | kiṃcana lāñchanamañcasi kañcukivarabhūbhṛdañcalābharaṇa || 380 ||
śrīvatsaḥ lakṣmīputraḥ ‘vatsaḥ putrādivarṣayoḥ’ iti medinī | ‘vatsassutādau tarṇake triṣu’ iti ratnamālā ca | pakṣe śrīvatsa iti lāñchananāma | añcasi pūjayasi ādriyase | pūjanaṃ cātra ‘sa ca pūjyo yathā hyaham | pūjyā lālayitavyāśca’ ityādāvivādaraṇaparam | pakṣe prāpnoṣītyarthaḥ | atra śrīvatsanāmalāñchanasya ramāśrayabhūtahṛdayasthalādhikaraṇakāñcane lakṣmīvatsatāprayuktaṃ vātsalyaṃ na heturityahetostasyāsiddhasya hetutāsaṃbhāvanā asiddhaviṣayā hetūtprekṣā śleṣojjīvitā ||
yathā vā
bhajati bhujageśanagapatiyuvatimaṇīrucikacākacijiteva | vidyunmukhe vikāraṃ tāntatvaṃ ca dyumadhyanīlātvam || 381 ||
bhujageśanagapatiyuvatimaṇī lakṣmīḥ tasyāḥ rucyā prabhayā kacākacijiteva keśeṣu keśeṣu gṛhītvā pravṛttena yuddhena parāsteva vidyut mukhe vadane vikāraṃ parābhūtatvacihnaṃ vaivarṇyaṃ tāntatvaṃ glāniṃ dyumadhye yatrakvāpi gaharābhyantarāntarikṣamadhye līnatvaṃ adṛśyatayā’vasthānaṃ ca bhajati | dyumadhyetyatra vakārāntasya dyuśabdasya ‘diva ut’ ityutvam | yadvā dyu ityavyayaṃ gaganavāci | ‘yu klībamahni gagane ca’ iti medinyanuśāsanāt ||
vāstavārthastu mukhe ādau vikāraṃ vi ityākārakamakṣaraṃ tāntatvaṃ takārāntatvaṃ dyuḥ dyu iti varṇasamudayaḥ madhye vi t ityanayorvarṇayorantarāle līnaḥ sthitaḥ yasyāssā tasyāḥ bhāvaḥ dyumadhyalīnātvam | vidyutpade mukhavikāravattvatāntatvadyumadhyalīnātvāni draṣṭavyāni | dyumadhyalīnāśabdasya samastatvenāguṇavacanatvāt ‘tvatalorguṇavaca sya’ iti na puṃvadbhāvaḥ | atra saṃjñājātikṛdantataddhitāntasamastasarvanāmasaṃkhyāśabdātiriktaśśabdo guṇavacanaśabdenocyate | ākaḍārasūtrabhāṣyatastathaiva lābhāt | ata eva ‘ekataddhite ca’ iti sūtre bhāṣye ‘ekatvamiti puṃvattvātsiddham’ ityāśaṅkya ‘na sidhyati uktametat | tvatalorguṇavacanasya’ ityuktam | ekaśabde sarvanāmasaṃjñayā guṇavacanasaṃjñāyā bādha iti tadāśayaḥ | idaṃ ca ‘tvataloḥ’ iti sūtraśekhare spaṣṭam | atra vidyuto mukhavikāravattvādike naisargike lakṣmīruciparāstatvaṃ na heturiti taddhetutvasaṃbhāvanā hetūtprekṣā | vastutastatparāstatāyā abhāvādasiddhaviṣayā śleṣottambhitā | pūrvodāharaṇe vātsalyāditi hetutvaṃ tāvatpañcamyaiva nirdiṣṭam, atra tu jiteti dharmiviśeṣaṇatayā jayasya hetutvamārtham | yathā ‘pūjitastuṣyati’ ityādau pūjanādeḥ |
nanu kathaṃ vidyucchabdagataṃ viprabhṛtivarṇavattvaṃ tadvācye vastuni saṃbhavet, yena upapāditaśleṣamūlakābhedakalpanena ukto’rtho niruhyeteti cet | śabdārthatādātmyasya sāmrājyādityavehi | tathā hyāhuśśābdikāśśabdaśaktinirūpaṇāvasare ”saṃketastu padapadārthayoritaretarādhyāsarūpaḥ smṛtyātmako yo’yaṃ śabdasso’rtho yo’rthassa śabdaḥ” iti pātañjalabhāṣyamudāharantaḥ padapadārthayossaṃbandhāntarameva śaktirvācyavācakabhāvāparaparyāyā | tadgrāhakaṃ cetaretarādhyāsamūlaṃ tādātmyaṃ tadeva sambandhaḥ | ubhayanirūpitatādātmyavānubhaya ityarthapadayorvyavahārāt | tasya ca tādātmyasya nirūpakatvena vivakṣito’rthaśśakyaḥ, āśrayatvena vivakṣitaśabdaśśaktaḥ| śabdārthayostādātmyādeva ślokamaśṛṇot arthaṃ vadatīti vyavahāraḥ ‘āmityekākṣaraṃ brahma, rāmeti dvyakṣaraṃ nāma mānabhaṅgaḥ pinakinaḥ, vṛddhirādaic’ iti śaktigrāhakaśrutismṛtiviṣaye samānādhikaraṇyena prayogaśca | tādātmyaṃ ca tadbhinnatve sati tadabhedena pratīyamānatvamiti bhedābhedasamaniyatam | abhedasyādhyastatvācca na tayorvirodhaḥ’ iti | vistarastu mañjūṣādau | tasmācchabdārthatādātmyāvalambanena vidyucchabdagatamapi viprabhṛtivarṇavattvaṃ tadarthe’pi nirargalamevetyupapādito’rtho niruhyata iti na saṃkaṭaṃ kiṃcit | evamevānyatrāpi boddhavyaṃ buddhimadbhiḥ ||
yathā vā
arthijanapīḍanānāmasāsahimahirnūnamahimahīdhramagāḥ | kva vasantamarthinivahā vadānyamabjākṣa jīvayantyete || 382 ||
arthijanapīḍanānāṃ asāsahirnūnaṃ asahiṣṇuriva ‘sāsahi vāvahi’ ityādinā yaṅantātsahateḥ kipratyayāntatayā nipātanātsādhuḥ | pīḍanānāmiti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | ahimahīdhraṃ śeṣādriṃ āśīviṣamayaṃ girimityapi gamyate | atibhayaṃkaratvenānyadurgamatvamanena dyotyate | agāḥ prāptavānabhūḥ | he abjākṣa ! ete parasaṃkaṭaṃ manāgapyanālocayantassvārthaikakṛpaṇā iti bhāvaḥ | arthinivahāḥ vadānyaṃ priyavacanapurassaradānaśīlaṃ kva vasantaṃ jīvayantīti lokoktiḥ | na khalu sudurgamamapi pradeśamāsthitavantaṃ na pīḍayeranniti bhāvaḥ | atra bhagavato’himahīdhraprāptyahetorapyarthijanapīḍanāsahiṣṇutvasya taddhetutvasaṃbhāvanā asiddhaviṣayā, tādṛśāsahanasyāsiddhatvāt | atrāpi pūvodāharaṇa iva heturārthaḥ | pūrvodāharaṇadvayaṃ śleṣasaṃkīrṇam | idaṃ tu śuddhamiti vailakṣaṇyam || siddhaviṣayā phalotprekṣā yathā
vinatānāṃ janatānāṃ tamasāmavasādanāya kila śaure | śastaghṛṇiṃ kaustubhamaṇimaruṇāṃśusahasranibhamurasi vahasi || 383 ||
atra kaustubhamaṇidhāraṇaṃ pratyaphalasyāpi tamo’vasādanasya tatphalatvena saṃbhāvanā siddhaviṣayā phalotprekṣā |
teṣāmevānukampārthamahamajñānajaṃ tamaḥ |
nāśayāmyātmabhāvastho jñānadīpena bhāsvatā ||
ityuktaprakāreṇa bhagavatastamo’vasādanasya svatassiddhatvāt ||
asiddhaviṣayā phalotprekṣā yathā
sodarasudhānirantarasādarasahavāsakutukaparipūrtyai | kākodaragirināthaḥ kamalāyai smitasudhāṃ vyanakti kila || 384 ||
kiletyutprekṣāvyañjakam | atra bhagavatā lakṣmīṃ prati smitasudhāyā vyañjanasya sodarasudhānirantarasādarasahavāsakutukaparipūrtirna phalamiti tasyāḥ gaganāravindāyamānāyāstatphalatvena saṃbhāvanā asiddhaviṣayā phalotprekṣā ||
yathā vā
ahamapi vijito’smyanayā ramayeti vyañjanāya kila bhagavan | tannilaye nijahṛdaye bhāntīṃ badhnāsi vaijayantīṃ tvam || 385 || vaijayantīṃ vijayapatākāṃ vanamālāṃ ca | atra bhagavata lakṣmīnilayabhūte nijahṛdaye vanamālā’bhinnatvenādhyavasitāyā vaijayantīśabdavācyāyā jayapatākāyā bandhanaṃ prati svasyāpi śriyā vijitatvābhivyañjanaṃ na phalamiti tasya phalatvena saṃbhāvanā uktaphalasyāsiddhatvādasiddhaviṣayā phalotprekṣā | iyaṃ rūpakaśleṣābhyāmuttambhitā | pūrvā tu rūpakasaṃkīrṇeti bhidā ||
yathā vā
lakṣmījayaketanatāmātmīyorassthalasya viśadayitum | badhnāsi vaijayantīṃ bahuvarṇāṃ tatra nūnamabjākṣa || 386 ||
he abjākṣa! ātmīyorassthalasya lakṣmyāḥ jayaketanatāṃ vijayadhvajayaṣṭitāṃ ketanaśabdo’tra dhvajayaṣṭivācī | pakṣe vijayagṛhatvam | ‘ketanaṃ tu dhvaje kārye nimantraṇanivāsayoḥ’ ityajayaḥ | viśadayituṃ nūnaṃ tatra vijayaketanabhūte ātmīyorassthale bahuvarṇāṃ vicitrāṃ, pakṣe ‘pañcavarṇā tu yā mālā vaijayantī gadābhṛtaḥ’ ityuktavarṇāṃ vaijayantīṃ patākāṃ vaijayantīśabdo’tra dhvajapaṭīvācakaḥ | badhnāsi | yadyapi ‘patākā vaijayantī syātketanaṃ dhvajamastriyām’ iti patākādayaścatvāro’pi śabdā dhvajaparyāyatayā sudhādau vyākhyātāḥ | tathā’pi patākāvaijayantīśabdayordhvajapaṭīvācitvaṃ ketanadhvajaśabdayostadyaṣṭivācitvaṃ cāvaśyābhyupeyaṃ, tathaiva prasiddheḥ | anyathā ‘patākādhvajamālinam, cañcvagradaṣṭacaṭulāhipatākayā’nye svāvāsabhāgamuragāśanaketuyaṣṭyā, prabhāvanīketanavaijayantīḥ’ iti śrīrāmāyaṇamāghakāvyādiṣu bhūyassu pradeśeṣu patākādhvajādiśabdānāṃ sahaprayogo virudhyeta | na hi ghaṭakalaśādīnāṃ paryāyaśabdānāṃ sahaprayogo dṛṣṭa iṣṭaśca | kiṃca udāhṛte māghakāvyapadye caṭulāhipatākayetyatrāheḥ dhvajapaṭītvena rūpaṇasyaiva uragāśanaketuyaṣṭyetyetadanurūpatā | ata eva ‘vyākṛttaśīrṣakarako vimalāsthidanto raktahradāṃśukadharo rathaketudaṇḍaḥ’ iti campūbhāratapadye ketuyaṣṭeḥ yatidhāryadaṇḍatvena rūpaṇaṃ, ‘asau te pūrveṣāṃ sucaritapatākā yadamarasravantī’ iti murārimiśrapadye mandākinyāssucaritapatākātvena rūpaṇaṃ ca yoyujyate | ‘dhvajārdhasammitāṃ caiva patākāṃ caiva citrayet’ iti dhvajārohaṇaprakaraṇagatapādmasaṃhitāvacanaṃ cātraivānukūlam | atra viśadayitumiti tumuno’rthaḥ phalam | evaṃ vakṣyamāṇodāharaṇe’pi | pūrvodāharaṇeṣu caturthyarthaḥ phalam | atra bhagavatā nijorassthalasya lakṣmījayaketanatābhivyañjanaṃ tatra vaijayantībandhanaṃ pratyaphalaṃ tatphalatvena saṃbhāvyata iti phalotprekṣā tādṛśavyañjanasyāsiddhatvādasiddhaviṣayā ketanavaijayantīśabdapratipādyagṛhadhvajavanamālāpatākārūpārthadvayaśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyamūlātiśayoktyanuprāṇiteti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
añjanabhūdharavibhunā vyañjayituṃ mādhavatvamiva naijam | hṛdyā’timṛdulatāśrīrāmodaṃ prāpitopavanamālam || 387 ||
añjanabhūdharapatinā naijaṃ mādhavatvaṃ madhumāsatvaṃ śrīpatitvaṃ ca | ‘mādhavo’je madhau rādhe’ iti medinī | vyañjayitumiva upavanamālaṃ vanarājīsamīpe vaijayantīsamīpe ca | hṛdyā hṛdayasya priyā vakṣasi sthitā ca | atimṛdvyaśca tāḥ latāśca tāsāṃ śrīssamṛddhiḥ, pakṣe atiśayitā mṛdulatā sukumāratā yasyāssā śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ āmodaṃ saurabhaṃ harṣaṃ ca prāpitā | atra bhagavatā svata evātimṛdulatāśrīrāmodaṃ prāpyate na tu svaṃ mādhavatvamabhivyañjayitumityaphalasya tadvyañjanasya phalatvena saṃbhāvanāphalotprekṣā tādṛśavyañjanasyāsiddhatvādasiddhaviṣayā | iyaṃ sabhaṅgaśleṣeṇottambhitā | pūrvā tvabhaṅgaśleṣeṇeti bhidā ||
yathāvā
sarasahitatāmavāptuṃ vihāya tāmarasatāṃ svatassiddhām | uragavaragiripate bhavadurasi sarasijāni mālyatāṃ prāpuḥ || 388 ||
sarasebhyo rasikebhyaḥ hitatāṃ sarasatāṃ hitatāṃ vā avāptuṃ tāṃ tathāvidhāṃ arasatāṃ arasikatvaṃ, pakṣe tāmarasatāmiti samastaṃ padam | tāmarasatvamityarthaḥ | atra sarasahitatāvāpteśśaśaśṛṅgāyamānāyāssarasijakartṛkamālyatāvāptiṃ pratyaphalasyāpi phalatayā saṃbhāvanā asiddhaviṣayā phalotprekṣā iyamapi pūrvavatsabhaṅgaśleṣadattahastaiveti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
nyasituṃ padamahiśikhariṇyakṛtakanagarāttvarānvito’vataran | kṣubhitaṃ valagnamacyuta saṃvāhayituṃ kilātra karamadadhāḥ || 389 ||
atra paramavyomāvadhikasatvarāvataraṇaprayojyakṣobhāśrayasvāvalagnakarmakasaṃvāhanasya bhagavatkartṛkasya tatkartṛkasvābhāvikanijāvalagnādhikaraṇakasavyapāṇinyasanaṃ pratyaphalasyāpi phalatvena saṃbhāvanā phalotprekṣā saṃvāhanasya viṣayasyāsiddhatvādasiddhaviṣayā | iyaṃ śuddhā pūrvāstu saṃkīrṇā iti vailakṣaṇyam ||
atraivaṃ bodhaprakāraḥ vastūtprekṣodāharaṇe ‘suragiriśiśuriva mahākirīṭaḥ’ ityatra suragiriśiśutvāvacchinnasya ivārthe saṃbhāvanāyāṃ prakāratayā’nvayaḥ | saṃbhāvanāyāśca viṣayatāsaṃbandhena mahākirīṭe’nvayaḥ | tathā ca suragiriśiśuprakārakasaṃbhāvanāviṣayo mahākirīṭa iti bodhaḥ | mahākirīṭaviṣayā suragiriśiśuprakārikā ca saṃbhāvanā tādātmyasaṃsargeṇaiva, tathā’nubhavāt, camatkṛtiprayojakasya saṃsargāntarasyābhāvācca | na tvevaṃ suragiriśiśutvaprakārikā, tasya suragiriśiśūpasarjanatvāt | evaṃ dhruvaṃ nūnamityādiśabdasamabhivyāhāre’pi bodhyam | ‘mukhaṃ candraṃ manye’ ityādau tu candrābhinnamukhaviṣayā saṃbhāvaneti bodhaḥ, nāmārthayorabhedānvayavyutpatteḥ | evaṃ ‘mukhaṃ candraṃ saṃbhāvaye tarkaye’ ityādāvapīti dhyeyam | abhiṣiñcatīvetyanuktaviṣayotprekṣodāharaṇe tu abhiṣiñcatinā sādhyavasānalakṣaṇayā abhiṣecanānandobhayasādhāraṇenāpyāyakatvādinā rūpeṇopasthāpite ānandane abhiṣecanasaṃbhāvanānvayādāpyāyakaṃ bhagavatkartṛkasvakarmakābhiṣecanaprakārakasaṃbhāvanāviṣaya iti bodhaḥ | evamanaktīvetyādāvapi jñeyam | “limpatīva tamoṅgāni’ ityatra vyañjanayopasthāpite vyāpane tādṛśasaṃbhāvanānvayaḥ” iti kāvyapradīpakāraḥ ||
hetūtprekṣodāharaṇe ‘añjanaśikhariṇi satatāsañjanato nūnamasita eṣa hariḥ’ ityatra pañcamīsthānikataserheturarthaḥ | tatra cābhedena prakṛtyarthānvayaḥ hetośca svaprayojyatāsaṃbandhenāsitatvaviśiṣṭe’nvayaḥ | viśeṣaṇasya prayojyatve viśiṣṭasyāpi tatprayojyatvāvyāhateḥ| yadvā svaprayojyāśrayatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ| tasya prakāratāsaṃbandhena saṃbhāvanāyāṃ tasyāśca viṣayatayā harāvanvayaḥ | tathācāñjanagiryadhikaraṇakasatatāsañjanābhinnahetukāsitaprakārakasaṃbhāvanāviṣayo haririti bodhaḥ | na ca tādātmyasaṃbandhena hetu vidheyakatvābhāvānneyaṃ hetūtprekṣā syāt | kiṃtu tādātmyena tathāvidhāsitasvarūpotprekṣaiveti vācyam | vivakṣitavivecanayā’ñjanagirisatatāsañjane hetvabhedasyotprekṣaṇīyatayā satatāsañjanaṃ prati viśeṣyabhūtasyāpi hetorvidheyatvābhyupagamāt | mukhacandra iti rūpaka ivānuyogitvamukhasyābhedasya satatāsañjanasaṃsargatvābhyupagamena ca satatāsañjane hetvabhedabhānasaṃbhavāt | hetuviśiṣṭasvarūpotprekṣāyā mukhataḥ pratīyamānatve’pi vivakṣāvaśena hetūtprekṣātvenaiva vyapadeśaḥ | yathā ‘dadhnā juhoti’ ityatra mukhato dadhiviśiṣṭahomapratītāvapi vivakṣitavivecanayā home dadhividhitvavyapadeśa iti jñeyam | na ca svaprayojyatvasaṃbandhena svaprayojyāśrayatvasaṃbandhena vā heturūpadharmotprekṣaiva asitapadārthe svīkriyatāṃ kimetādṛśakalpanāyāseneti vācyaṃ, dharmotprekṣābhyupagame dūṣaṇasya pūrvamevodīritatvāt | ata eva harṣāllagnā manye lalitatanu te pādayoḥ padmalakṣmīḥ’ iti hetūtprekṣāmudāhṛtavataḥ kāvyaprakāśakṛto’pi tatra harṣahetukalaganatādātmyasaṃbhāvanameva svābhāvike lagane’bhimatam | asmaduktarītyā tu lakṣmīrūpe viṣaye yathoktaharṣahetukalagnatādātmyasaṃbhāvanamucite laganasya dharmyupasarjanatvena saṃbhāvanāyāmanvayāyogāditi dik | evamudāhṛtāsiddhaviṣayahetūtprekṣāyāmapi draṣṭavyaṃ, viṣayasya siddhatvāsiddhatvābhyāṃ bodhe viśeṣābhāvādityalam ||
vinatānāṃ janatānāmiti phalotprekṣodāharaṇe tamasāmavasādanāyeti caturthyarthaḥ phalam | tatra cābhedena prakṛtyarthānvayo hetūtprekṣāvadeva | phalasya ca prayojakatāsaṃsargeṇa kaustubhadhāraṇe svaprayojakāśrayatāsaṃbandhena tadāśraye vā’nvayaḥ | tattādātmyasaṃsargakasaṃbhāvanāyāśśaurau viṣayatayā | evaṃca tamo’vasādanābhinnaphalakakaustubhavahanāśrayasaṃbhāvanāviṣayaśśauririti bodhaḥ| evaṃ tumunnantasthale phalaṃ tumuno’rthaḥ bodhastu tulya eva | phalotprekṣātvaṃ coktarītyopapādanīyamiti dik | evaṃ kuvalayānandānusāreṇa utprekṣāyāṣṣaḍvidhatvaṃ darśitam ||
prācāṃ mate tu utprekṣā tāvaddvividhā, vācyā pratīyamānā ca | iva nūnaṃ śaṅke saṃbhāvayāmi manye jāne tarkaye ūhe utprekṣe avaimi pratyemi vedmi dhruvaṃ prāyaḥ kiletyādibhiḥ pratipādakaiśśabdaissahitā yatra saṃbhāvanāsāmagrī tatrotprekṣā vācyā | yatra ca pratipādakaśabdarahitaṃ sāmagrīmātraṃ tatra pratīyamānā | yatra tatsāmagrīvidhuraṃ pratipādakaśabdamātraṃ tatra saṃbhāvanāmātrameva notprekṣā | sāpi pratyekaṃ tridhā svarūpotprekṣā hetūtprekṣā phalotprekṣā ceti| tatra jātiguṇakriyādravyarūpāṇāṃ tadabhāvarūpāṇāṃ ca padārthānāṃ tādātmyenetareṇa vā saṃbandhena jātiguṇakriyādravyātmakairvyestaissamuccitaiḥ upāttairanupāttaiḥ niṣpannaiḥ niṣpādyairvā nimittabhūtairyathāsaṃbhavaṃ jātiguṇakriyādravyātmakeṣu viṣayeṣūtprekṣaṇaṃ svarūpotprekṣā | tatrābhedena saṃsargeṇa dharmisvarūpotprekṣā, saṃsargāntareṇa dharmasvarūpotprekṣā cocyate | uktavidheṣu padārtheṣu prāguktaprakārāṇāṃ padārthānāṃ tathāvidhaireva nimittairyathāsaṃbhavaṃ hetutvena phalatvena ca saṃbhāvanaṃ hetūtprekṣā phalotprekṣā cocyate | etāśca kvacinniṣpannaśarīrāḥ kvacinniṣpādanīyaśarīrāścetyevamādyanalpavikalpāssaṃpadyante | tatra diṅmātraṃ praparśyate || tatra upāttajātinimittakajātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā vṛṣaśaileśakaṭākṣaśriyo’navarataṃ nivāsasarasiruham | amba mukhaṃ tava manye bhramarakanikareṇa bhṛśamupari calatā || 390 ||
kaṭākṣasya śrīśśobhaiva śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ tasyāḥ | bhramarakāḥ dvirephāḥ lalāṭagatacūrṇakuntalāśca, teṣāṃ nikareṇa | atra mukhatvajātyavacchinne sarasiruhatvajātyavacchinnaṃ tādātmyena saṃbhāvyate | tatra śleṣasampāditābhedoparicaladbhramarakanikararūpajātirnimittamupāttam ||
upāttaguṇanimittajātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
niśvasanaprotkṣiptānadharoṣṭhanirodhapaṅktibhāvagatān | galaśaṅkhajānvijāne viśadānmauktikamaṇīṃstavāmba radān || 391 ||
niśvasanena niśvāsamārutena prautkṣiptān ūrdhvaṃ praṇunnān adharoṣṭhayoḥ nirodhena paṅktibhāvaṃ śreṇībhūyāvasthānaṃ gatān prāptān ‘dvitīyā śrita’ ityādinā samāsaḥ | yathā mārutotkṛtāni dhānyāni yatkiñcidvyavadhāyakavastupratighātena śreṇībhūyāvatiṣṭhante tatheti bhāvaḥ | atra dantatvajātyavacchinneṣu mauktikatvajātyavacchinnāstādātmyenotprekṣyante, tatra viśadatvaguṇo nimittamupāttam |
yathāvā
lāvaṇyajalanidhau tava vadane’higirīśa bhāti gaṅgeva | mṛganābhitilakayamunāmilitā mauktikalalāṭikā dhavalā || 392 || lalāṭe bhavā lalāṭikā ‘karṇalalāṭātkanalaṅkāre’ iti kan | lalāṭe lambatayā nibaddhaḥ patrapāśyākhyābharaṇaviśeṣaḥ ‘patrapāśyā lalāṭikā’ ityamaraḥ | atra mauktikalalāṭikātvajātyavacchinne gaṅgātvajātyavacchinnaṃ tādātmyenotprekṣyate | tatra dhavaleti guṇo nimittamupāttam ||
yathāvā
tapanātmabhavā’bhūvaṃ tuhināṃśorapi sutā bhaveyamiti | asitā vadanendau tava janitā yamuneva tilakarekhaiṣā || 393 ||
atra yamuneveti jātyutprekṣāyāṃ asiteti guṇo nimittamupāttam | gaṅgāyamunayordravyatvena vivakṣāyāṃ dravyotprekṣāyā evedamudāharaṇadvayaṃ bhaviṣyatīti dhyeyam ||
upāttakriyānimittajātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
vṛṣagiripatihṛdi saudhe nāyakamaṇinā dṛḍhopagūḍhāyāḥ | muktāhāralatāyāḥ pulakā iva bhāntyuditvarāḥ kiraṇāḥ || 394 ||
atra kiraṇarūpajātau pulakatvarūpajātyavacchinnānāṃ tādātmyena saṃbhāvanam | tatropagūhanakriyāyā nimittatā ||
upāttadravyanimittakajātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
phaṇiśikhariśikharabhāsvanvikasvarāmbhoruhaṃ bhavadvadanam| saṃbhāvayāmahe nanu lakṣmyā’nāratamihaivaviharantyā || 395 || ihaiva vadana eva viharantyā lakṣmyā suṣamayā śriyā ca bhavadvadanaṃ vikasvarāmbhoruhaṃ saṃbhāvayāmahe nirantaralakṣmīvihārāśrayatvātpraphullakamalatvenotprekṣāmahe ityarthaḥ | atra mukhatvajātyavacchinne ambhoruhatvajātyavacchinnatādātmyotprekṣaṇam | tatra śleṣasaṃpāditasuṣamābhedaramārūpadravyaṃ nimittamupāttam | atra lakṣmīśabdasyaikavyaktivācitvāt dravyatvam | etenotprekṣāyāṃ jātidravyayornimittatvamaparigaṇayanta upekṣaṇīyāḥ | uktaṃ ca citramīmāṃsāyām
utprekṣāyāṃ nimittatvaṃ yajjātidravyayorapi |
tenāpi vikalāṃ vidmo nimattagaṇanāmimām ||
iti | imāṃ nimittagaṇanāṃ prācīnoktamityarthaḥ | udāhṛtaṃ ca tatra jātidravyanimittakotprekṣādvayam ||
anupāttanimittajātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
bhrūyugaladhanurlastakasaṃhitanāsākarīrasaṃlagnam | madhuripumṛgamadatilakaṃ nanu bhuvanajigīṣumadanaśaraphalakam || 396 ||
atra mṛgamadatilakatvajātyavacchinne madanaśaraphalakatvajātyavacchinnatādatmyasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ asitatvasūkṣmāgratvādinimittamanupāttam | vastutastu prathamārdhapratipādito dharmaḥ vakṣyamāṇarītyā rūpakeṇa viṣayaviṣayisādhāraṇīkṛta ityupātta eva | asitatvādirevānupāttaḥ tasmādidaṃ padyamupāttānupāttanimittakajātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣāyā eva vakṣyamāṇarītyā udāharaṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | na tu kevalānupāttanimittāyāḥ || śuddhānupāttanimittā yathā
haraphālanayanavitanuṃ smaramamunaivopajīvayitumanasā | hariṇā svaphālakalitaṃ nayanamivordhvāñcalaṃ jayati tilakam || 397 ||
harasya phālanayanena vitanuṃ vigataśarīraṃ smaraṃ amunaiva phālanayanenaiva upajīvayitumanasā hariṇā śrīnivāsena svasya phāle lalāṭe kalitaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ ‘kavīnāṃ kaliḥ kāmadhenuḥ’ityāhuḥ | ūrdhvaṃ añcalaṃ agraṃ yasya tattathoktaṃ nayanamiva tilakaṃ kastūrīcitrakaṃ jayati loke nayanayorvartulamūlatvakuñcitasūkṣmāgratvadarśanena tilakasya tatsaṃbhāvanālābhāya ūrdhvāñcalaṃ nayanamityabhihitam | na hi nayanasyordhvāñcalatvamantareṇa vartulamūlākuñcitasūkṣmāgrakastūrītilakatādātmyasaṃbhāvanaṃ saṃbhavati | atra harahariśabdābhyāṃ haraṇarūpapravṛttinimittakābhyāmubhayostaulyanimittakaspardhā dyotyate | sā copapāditāyā utprekṣāyā upaskuruta ityavadheyam | atra nayanatvajātyavacchinnatādatmyasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ nailyādinimittamanupāttam ||
yathāvā
śikharagaśikhipiñchāñcalacandrakamālā ramānivāsagireḥ | phaṇibhūbhṛttvaṃ prathayitumunmiṣitā lasati nayanamāleva || 398 ||
phaṇibhūbhṛttvaṃ bhujagarājatvaṃ śeṣādritvaṃ ca | atra candrakamālāyāṃ jātau nayanamālājātyavacchinnaṃ saṃbhāvyate | śyāmāruṇatvādinimittamanupāttam || evaṃ śuddhā upāttanimittakā anupāttanimittakā api jātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣāḥ pradarśitāḥ ||
atha miśrāstā udāhriyante
hṛdayāspadayā ramayā prasāritā nābhikamalagaṃ svasutam | udvoḍhuṃ bhujayugalī kila śaurerbhāti yaṣṭirālambā || 399 ||
yaṣṭirnāma
ekāvalī nāma yatheṣṭasaṃkhyā hastapramāṇā maṇibhiḥ prayuktā |
saṃyojitā yā maṇinā tu madhye yaṣṭīti sā bhūṣaṇavidbhiruktā ||
iti varāhamihiroktalakṣaṇa ekāvalyaparanāmadheyo muktāhāraviśeṣaḥ | ālambata ityālambā lambamānetyarthaḥ | atra bhagavato hārayaṣṭyāṃ jātau tadvakṣassthalāvasthitalakṣmīsaṃbandhibhujatvajātyavacchinnastādātmyenotprekṣyate | tatra ca hārayaṣṭigatānāṃ lāvaṇyaprasāritatvalambatvānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ nimittatāniṣpattaye viṣayilakṣmībhujayugalīgatatvamavaśyaṃ saṃpādanīyam | teṣāṃ ca madhye anupāttasya lāvaṇyasya jakṣmīdivyavigrahasaṃbandhitvādeva bhujayugalīgatatvaṃ saṃpannam | itarayorapi saṃpādanāya bhagavannābhīkamalagatasvasutacaturānanasamudvahanaṃ phalamutprekṣitam | tatsādhanatājñānasya prasāritatvalambatvānukūlayatnajanakatvāt | evaṃ ca viṣayagatatāddaśodvahanaphalakaprasāritatvalambatvābhyāṃ viṣayagatayossvābhāvikaprasāritatvalambatvayorabhedādhyavasānātiśayoktyā sādhāraṇyasaṃpattau satyāṃ nimittatā bhavati | na cātra phalasyāpyutprekṣaṇātphalotprekṣeti yuktaṃ vaktum| utprekṣyamāṇaphalaniṣpāditanimittottambhitāyāṃ svarūpotprekṣāyāmeva vidheyatvena camatkṛterviśramāt utprekṣāpratipādakasya kilaśabdasya phalenānanvayācca tayaivātra vyapadeśo yuktaḥ | anigīrṇaviṣayā ceyamupāttānupāttakriyāguṇātmakanimittā niṣpādyaviśiṣṭaśarīrā tādātmyotprekṣā | lakṣmībhujayugalyāssvatassiddhatve’pi prasāritatvaviśiṣṭāyāstasyāḥ kavinaiva niṣpāditatvāt ||
yathā vā 
sodarakamalālālanamodagalatkaustubhāśrubharabindūn | dhārākārān śaurerhārānānābhilambino manye || 400 ||
atra śaurihāre jātau tadvakṣassthalāvasthitakaustubhānandabāṣpabindutvajātyavacchinnasya tādātmyenotprekṣaṇam | tatra ca dhārākāratvānābhilambitvathālathalyānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ nimittatvaniṣpattyai viṣayikaustubhānandabāṣpabindugatatvasaṃpādanamāvaśyakam | teṣāṃ ca madhye anupāttasya thālathalyasyānandabāṣpasaṃbandhitayaiva tadbindugatatvaṃ saṃpannam | anyayordhārākāratvalambitvayorapi saṃpādanāya sodarakamalālālanamodagalattvaṃ hetutvena saṃbhāvitam | evaṃ ca viṣayigatatādṛśagalanahetukadhārākāratvalambatvābhyāṃ viṣayagatayossvābhāvikadhārākāratvalambatvayorabhedādhyavasānamūlātiśayoktyā satyāṃ sādhāraṇyasaṃpattyāṃ syānnimittatā | uktaviṣayā ceyamupāttānupāttakriyāguṇanimittā niṣpādyaviśiṣṭaśarīrā jātyutprekṣā, kaustubhāśrubindūnāṃ kavinaiva niṣpāditatvāt |
yathā vā
nātha tava nābhisarasīmagnanijasvasṛramāhṛduddhṛtaye | hṛdayagakaustubhalambīkṛtā bhujayugīva bhāti hāralatā || 401 ||
atrāpi hāralatāyāṃ jātau bhujayugītvajātyavacchinnaṃ tādātmyena saṃbhāvyate | tatra ca lambatvaprakāśavattvayordharmayornimittatānirvṛttaye viṣayibhūtakaustubhabhujayugīgatatvamavaśyasaṃpādyam | tayormadhye anupāttasya prakāśavattvasya kaustubhānubandhitvenaiva bhujayugīgatatvaṃ saṃpannam | lambatvasyāpi tadgatatvasaṃpādanāya bhagavannābhīsarasīnimagnanijasvasṛramāmānasoddharaṇaṃ phalatvenotprekṣitam | evaṃca viṣayigatatādṛśoddharaṇaphalakalambatvena viṣayagatasya svābhāvikalambatvasyābhedādhyavasāyamūlakātiśayoktyā sādhāraṇye saṃpanne nimittatvaṃ niṣpadyate | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvatarodāharaṇavadeva | iyamapyupāttānupāttakriyāguṇātmakanimittā niṣpādyaviśiṣṭaśarīrā jātyutprekṣaiva, kaustubhabhujayugasya kavinaiva niṣpāditatvāditi ||
yathā vā
niravadhiparimalalobhānnāsācampakanibhālanabhayācca | mṛgamadatilako mādhavavadanābje niścalaḥ kila bhramaraḥ || 402 ||
nibhālanaṃ darśanam | atrāpi naiścalyaviśiṣṭe śrīnivāsakastūrītilake jātau viṣaye naiścalyaviśiṣṭabhramarajātyavacchinnasya tādātmyenotprekṣaṇam | tatra tilakarātasya naiścalyasya bhramaragatatvamantareṇa tilakabhramarayorabhedasya durupapādatayā tatsiddhyarthaṃ tasya viṣayagatatvaṃ sādhyam | tatra nailyasyānupāttasya utprekṣya māṇe viṣayiṇi siddhatvānniścalatvasya niṣpādanāya niravadhiparimalalobhaḥ nāsācampakanibhālanabhayaṃ cetyetadubhayaṃ hetutvenotprekṣitam | ihāpi pūrvavatsvābhāvikakalpitayorabhedādhyavasānātsādhāraṇyam | pūrvaṃ hi yathā phalasyotprekṣaṇe’pi na phalotprekṣā tathehāpi hetorutprekṣaṇe na hetūtprekṣeti dhyeyam ||
yathā vā
cibuke visṛtvarāstava hāralatāghṛṇiśikhā janani bhānti | hrīnamitamukhonnamanaprasāritā iva tadīyakaraśākhāḥ || 403 ||
atra hāralatākiraṇaśikhārūpajātau aṅgulirūpajātyavacchinnatādātmyasaṃbhāvanā | tatra ca anupāttasyaujjvalyasya dharmasya upāttayoḥ visṛtvaratvaprasāritatvayordharmayorvastuprativastubhāvāpannayorabhedācca viṣayaviṣayisādhāraṇyam | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavadeva | evamanyatrāpyūhyam ||
evaṃ jātibhāvasvarūpotprekṣā digudāhṛtā ||
tadātmyena guṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
nīlaguṇenākrānte suvarṇavarṇasya tava vapuṣi śaure | bahirudgataṃ vijāne pītaṃ guṇamujjvalaṃ kanakavasanam || 404 ||
he śaure! tava kanakavasanaṃ suvarṇavarṇasya ‘āpraṇakhātsarva eva suvarṇaḥ | rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyam’ ityādiśrutismṛtibhissuvarṇavarṇatvenoktasya tava vapuṣi nīlaguṇena ākrānte sati bahirudgataṃ tatrānavakāśādbahirutsṛtvaramityarthaḥ | pītaṃ guṇaṃ vijāne utprekṣe | atra kanakavasane jātau pītaguṇastādātmyenotprekṣyate | tatra ujjvalatvaṃ guṇa upātta ityupāttaguṇanimittakaguṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣeyam ||
yathā vā
madhuratamāṃ sarveṣāṃ mahāgasāṃ majjanāya suniṣevyām | jāne tava karuṇāṃ śrījāne svāmiṃstvadīyapuṣkariṇīm || 405 ||
madhuratamāṃ atipriyāṃ atisvādvīṃ ca ‘svādupriyau tu madhurau’ ityamaraḥ | mahat āgaḥ pāpaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktānāṃ ‘pāpāparādhayorāgaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | sarveṣāṃ sarvairjanairityarthaḥ | ‘kṛtyānāṃ kartari vā’ iti kartari ṣaṣṭhī | majjanāya snānārthaṃ suniṣevyām | anyatra mahatāṃ āgasāṃ aparādhānāṃ majjanāya adarśanāyeti yāvat | sarveṣāṃ akhilairaparādhijanairityarthaḥ | suniṣevyāṃ sukhena sevanīyām ! atra svāmipuṣkariṇīrūpadravye karuṇārūpaguṇatādātmyasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ śleṣottambhito mādhuryaguṇaḥ sevyatvarūpakriyā ca nimitte upātte iti viśeṣaḥ ||
evaṃ jātyādinimittakaguṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā apyudāhāryāḥ
anupāttanimittakaguṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
ūrdhvaprasaranirodhakasarojabāndhavavilokanakrodhāt | āruṇyamivodbhinnaṃ phaṇiśikhariśikhāsu maṇikulaṃ kanati || 406 || maṇayo’tra padmarāgā vivakṣitāḥ | atra padmarāgakule jātau āruṇyaguṇastādātmyena saṃbhāvitaḥ | aujjvalyādikaṃ nimittamanupāttam ||
yathā vā
rasanāruṇyākramaṇāddaśanālyāṃ sthātumantaramalabdhvā | tata udgataṃ pratīmassitaguṇamakhilāmba tava mitaṃ hasitam || 407 ||
atra smite jātau sitaguṇasya tādātmyena saṃbhāvanaṃ, tatra nimittaṃ kimapyanupāttam ||
yathā vā
antaramānāduccairudvāntaṃ hastayorivāruṇyam | tatrollasadabjayugaṃ citte karavāṇi bhuvanajanani tava || 408 ||
atra padme jātau āruṇyaguṇotprekṣā anupāttanimittā ||
yathā vā
manye kaustubharatnaṃ pratiyatnaṃ śrīśavakṣaso’nūtnam | śrītanusarjanaśeṣaṃ dhātrā lāvaṇyarāśimupanihitam ||
atra kaustubharatnasya lāvaṇyarāśirūpaguṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣāyāṃ darśanīyatvādirguṇo’nupāttaḥ ||
evam
mūrtimadiva sauśīlyaṃ sphūrtimadiva bhāvayāmi vātsalyam | dhāma kimapyatinailyaṃ śrīmatphaṇiśikhariśikharagamatulyam || 410 ||
ityādāvapi guṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā anupāttanimittā bodhyā | mahato mandaissaha nīrandhrasaṃśleṣaṇasvabhāvatvaṃ sauśīlyatvam | doṣatiraskāriprītitvaṃ vātsalyatvam | atinailyamiti bahuvrīhiḥ ||
evaṃ guṇabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā dikpradarśitā | atha kriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā |
tatra upāttajātinimittakakriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
śṛṅgāgrapatinijajharaśīkaranikarairmarutprasaranunnaiḥ | arpayati viyadgaṅgā puṣpāñjalimiva phaṇīśagiripataye || 411 ||
atra puṣpāñjalisamarpaṇarūpakriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣāyāṃ marutprasaranunnaśīkaranikararūpajātirnimittamupāttam | puṣpāñjalimarpayatītyatrāñjalāvarpaṇasya bādhāttadviśeṣaṇeṣu puṣpeṣvanvayaḥ | svargī dhvasta ityādivat | añjalisthapuṣpāṇi vikiratītyarthaḥ | ‘saviśeṣaṇe hi vidhiniṣedhau viśeṣye bādhe viśeṣaṇamupasaṃkrāmataḥ’ iti hi nyāyavidaḥ | ata eva ‘sītāṃ saṃtarjayantīstā rākṣasīraśṛṇotkapiḥ’ ityādiprayogāssaṃgacchante | vaktavyāntaraṃ tu lakṣmīsahasraprādurbhāvastabakaratnaprakāśikāyāṃ nirūpitamasmābhiḥ ||
upāttaguṇanimittakakriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
paṭutaraśaśadharaviśadasphaṭikaśilāpracuraśikharadhavalimnā | sphuṭamapahasati mahīdhrān sphaṭādharagirirhariṃ vahannanyān || 412 ||
atrāpahasanakriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣāyāṃ sphaṭikaśilāśikharadhāvalyarūpaguṇo nimittamupāttam ||
upāttakriyānimittakabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
tuṅgebhyaśśṛṅgebhyassamudgirannirjharānudāratarān | svasminmukundavāsānandādbāṣpāyate kila vṛṣādriḥ ||
bāṣpamudvamati bāṣpāyate ‘bāṣpoṣmabhyāmudvamane’ iti kyaṅ | atra bāṣpodvamanarūpakriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣāyāṃ nirjharodgiraṇakriyānimittamupāttam ||
evamupāttadravyanimittā’pyudāhāryā |
yathā vā
karapadmena dveṣānmadhye bhitvā prakoṣṭhake protam | krandati sitakarabimbaṃ śiñjanmaṇivalayamamba tava manye || 414 ||
atra prathamāntaviśeṣyakaśābdabodhavādināmabhedasaṃsargeṇa śiñjadviśiṣṭe mauktikavalaye karapadmakartṛkadveṣahetukamadhyacchidrīkaraṇapūrvakaprakoṣṭhādhikaraṇakapravayanakarmābhinnotprekṣitasitakarabimbatādatmyotprekṣaṇapūrvakaṃ krandanakartṛtvaṃ dharma utprekṣyate | tatra tādātmyotprekṣaṇe dharmyutprekṣāyāṃ sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ, saṃbandhāntareṇotprekṣaṇe dharmotprekṣāyāṃ tatsamānādhikaraṇo dharmaśca viṣayagato nimittamiti sthite prastute krandanakartṛtvarūpadharmotprekṣāyāṃ tatsamānādhikaraṇapravayanakarmatvarūpadharmasya viṣayagatatvasiddhaye anuvādyatayā mauktikavalayatādātmyamanupāttadhāvalyanimittakamutprekṣyate | tatra yathā viśiṣṭopamāyāmupamānopameyaviśeṣaṇānāmārthamaupamyaṃ evamatrāpi viṣayabhūtamauktikavalayaviśeṣaṇasya pravayanasya mūlotprekṣāviṣayibhūtasitakarabimbaviśeṣaṇena pravayanenābheda ārthaḥ | tataśca sitakarabimbakarmakapravayane siddhe mukhyotprekṣā nirvyūḍhā bhavati | krandanaśiñjanayorapi bimbapratibimbabhāvenābhedaḥ | tena prakoṣṭhakaprotaśiñjadabhinno mauktikavalayaḥ prakoṣṭhakaprotasitakarabimbābhinnaḥ krandanakriyānukūlavyāpāravāniti bodhaprakāraḥ | ākhyāte bhāvanāprādhānye tu abhedena krośanakriyotprekṣā | tatra śābdavṛttau valayaviśeṣaṇatayā pratīyamānamapi śiñjanaṃ saṃbhāvanāviṣayatayā’vatiṣṭhate adhyavasānavaśāt | krandanakriyāyāṃ ca tādṛśavalayo viśeṣaṇam | tādṛśavalaye cābhedena tādṛśasitakarabimbam | na tu sitakarabimbameva sākṣātkriyāyām | tathā sati valayasyānanvayāpatteḥ | viṣayiviṣayaviśeṣaṇayoḥ prāgvadeva bimbapratibimbabhāvenābhedapratipattiriti ||
evaṃ kriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣāmārgaḥ pradarśitaḥ ||
anupāttanimittakakriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
tattādṛśi phaṇiśikhariṇi klaptāvasathasya kasyaciddhāmnaḥ | smaraṇaṃ tadīyamūrteścitte mama vajralepamiva datte || 415 ||
atra vajralepanadānakriyotprekṣāyāṃ aviśleṣeṇa saṃnidhāpanaṃ nimittaṃ nopāttam || atha dravyabhāvotprekṣā
tatra upāttajātinimittakadravyabhāvotprekṣā yathā
bhagavati madhuripumahile bhavatīmukhamasya saṃmukhīnena | dravatā vidhukāntena svānte saṃtarkayeta ko nendum || 416 ||
asya mukhasya sammukhīnena abhimukhāvasthāyinā dravatā dayārdreṇa rāgadrutahṛdayena vā pakṣe jalasrāviṇā | vidhukāntena vidhurbhagavāneva kāntaḥ vallabhaḥ tena | pakṣe 
amṛtāṃśukarasparśādyatkṣaratyamṛtaṃ kṣaṇāt |
candrakāntaṃ tadākhyātaṃ durlabhaṃ syātkalau yuge ||
iti skāndoktalakṣaṇaścandrakāntamaṇiḥ tena | atra mukharūpajātau indurūpadravyatādātmyotprekṣaṇe śleṣakalpitābhedaścandrakāntamaṇirūpa jātirnimittamupāttam ||
upāttaguṇanimittakadravyabhāvotprekṣā yathā
karacaraṇādyavayavavat sphuradākāśaṃ vinīlamahiśaile | uditaṃ brahma vijāne yadidamanantaṃ vadanti nigamāntāḥ || 417 ||
atra śrīnivāse parabrahmaṇi dravye nīlatvānantatvaguṇanimittakamākāśarūpadravyatādātmyotprekṣaṇam | ākāśasya ekatvādidaṃ dravyam | nigamāntāḥ ‘satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma’ ityādyā upanipadaḥ | anantaṃ trividhaparicchedarahitaṃ brahma | pakṣe antarikṣaṃ ‘anantaṃ gagane klībe pumānkeśavaśeṣayoḥ’ iti ratnamālā | atra nīlatvādirguṇa upāttaḥ ||
upāttakriyānimittakadravyabhāvotprekṣā yathā
svātyujjvalatvahetoḥ pramodavarṣaṃ dadātyalaṃ yadayam | tvannāsāmaṇimenaṃ śukraṃ manye tato’mbunidhikanye ||
yat yasmāt ayaṃ tvannāsāmaṇiḥ nāsikābharaṇamauktikaṃ ‘ratnaṃ maṇirdvayoraśmajātau muktādike’pi ca’ ityamaraḥ | svātyāṃ svātīnakṣatre ujjvalatvaṃ anastagatvena prakāśamānatvaṃ tasmāddhetoḥ | pakṣe svasya ātmanaḥ atyujjvalatvahetoḥ aujjvalyasadbhāvāddhetoḥ alaṃ paryāptaṃ pradatto modo yena tat pramodaṃ ‘prādibhyo dhātu jasya vācyo vā cottarapadalopaḥ’ iti dattaśabdasya lopaḥ | varṣaṃ vṛṣṭiṃ dadāti |
varuṇasya diśi svātyāṃ carankaviranastagaḥ|
saṃpūrṇāṃ tanute vṛṣṭiṃ jagadānandadāyinīm ||
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe pramodavarṣaṃ avicchinnamānandamityarthaḥ | dadāti | tataḥ kāraṇāt tvannāsāmaṇiṃ śukraṃ bhārgavaṃ manye | atra nāsāmaṇau jātau śukro dravyaṃ tādātmyena saṃbhāvyate | tatra ca upapāditaśleṣakalpitābhedasvātyujjvalatvahetukapramodavarṣadānalakṣaṇakriyārūpaṃ nimittamupāttam |
upāttadravyanimittakadravyabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
śārṅgaṃ yadetadahigiriśṛṅgavihārinvibhāti tava bhūman | saṃbhāvayāmyanantaṃ tadetadadhirūḍhayā jyayā śirasi || 419 || tadetat śārṅgaṃ śirasi mūrdhni agre ca ‘śiraḥ pradhāne senāgre drumādyagreṣu mūrdhni ca’ iti ratnamālā | adhirūḍhayā jyayā bhūmyā maurvyā ca | ‘jyā maurvībhūmimātṛṣu’ iti ratnamālā | anantaṃ śeṣaṃ saṃbhāvayāmi utprekṣe | atra śārṅge dravye śeṣo dravyaṃ tādātmyena saṃbhāvyate | tatra ca jyāśabdapratipādyamaurvībhūmirūpārthadvayābhedādhyavasāyena śirodhirūḍhabhūmirūpadravyaṃ nimittamupāttam
anupāttanimittadravyabhāvotprekṣā yathā
bhitvā dveṣādbhānorbimbaṃ madhye tato vinirgacchan rāhuriva bāhuracyuta tava maṇikeyūraveṣṭito bhāti ||
atra bhagavataḥ keyūraveṣṭite bāhau madhyabhedanapūrvakabhānumaṇḍalavinirgato rāhurdravyaṃ tādātmyena saṃbhāvyate | tatra na kiṃcidapi nimittamupāttam ||
evaṃ jātyādibhāvasvarūpotprekṣādikpradāśatā | athābhāvābhimānavatyassvarūpotprekṣāḥ diṅmātramudāhriyante ||
tatra jātyabhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
nīlairnijarucijālairdinakaraśūnyāmivācaranvanyām | phaṇipatiśikhariṇi tasyāṃ ramayati rātriṃdivaṃ ramāṃ śauriḥ || 421 ||
tasyāṃ vanyāyāmityarthaḥ | atra dinakaraśūnyāmiti jātyabhāva utprekṣyate | tatra nīlarucirūpaguṇo nimittam ||
yathā vā
kañcukigirimahimabharaiḥ kañcukitaṃ vasumatītalaṃ nikhilam | dṛśye’pi girisahasre niśśailamivātivelamābhāti || 422 ||
atra niśśailamiti jātyabhāvotprekṣāyāṃ śeṣādrimahimavyāptatvalakṣaṇakriyārūpaṃ nimittamupāttam | evaṃ upāttajātidravyanimittake jātyabhāvasvarūpotprekṣe apyudāhārye ||
yathā vā
mahimā vijṛmbhate’sāvahimūrdhanyācalāvataṃsasya | sakalamapi daivatāntaravikalaṃ kila bhuvanamācaranko’pi || 423 ||
atra daivatāntaravikalamiti jātyabhāvotprekṣāyāṃ nimittamanupāttam ||
guṇābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
vimalaviśṛṅkhalavisṛmarakamalārucinicayaparicayapiśaṅgam | phaṇigirimastakaratnaṃ pratyastamitātmanīlimevāsīt || 424 ||
atra nīlaguṇābhāvotprekṣāyāṃ kamalārucinicayaparicayapiśaṅgatvaguṇo nimittamupātam ||
evamupāttajātikriyādravyanimittakāḥ anupāttanimittakā ca guṇābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā udāhāryāḥ ||
kriyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
kandarpakoṭikoṭiprayutāyuta divyarūpalāvaṇyam | nirvarṇya vṛṣādrīnduṃ nispanda ivābhavannikhilalokaḥ || atra nispanda iti kriyā’bhāvotprekṣāyā nirvarṇanarūpakriyātmakaṃ nimittamupāttam | nispanda ityatra sasya na ṣatvam | aṣopadeśatvena tadaprāpteḥ | tathāca vāmanaḥ ‘niṣpanda iti ṣatvaṃ cintyaṃ lakṣaṇādarśanāt’ iti | lakṣaṇaṃ śāstramiti | haṃsasandeśarasāsvādinyāṃ ‘dattanispandadṛṣṭiḥ’ ityasya vyākhyānāvasare nirūpitamasmābhiḥ ||
evamupāttajātiguṇadravyarūpanimittāḥ anupāttanimittāśca kriyā’bhāvasvarūpotprekṣā apyudāhāryāḥ ||
dravyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
viśadayatā’khilabhuvanaṃ yaśasā bhujageśaśikhariśekhara te | aśaśāṅkamivākāśaṃ na kalaṅkī bhāti bhātyapakalaṅke || 426 ||
apakalaṅke niṣkalaṅke bhāti sati kalaṅkī na bhātīti yojanā | atrāśaśāṅkamiti dravyābhāvotprekṣāyāṃ yaśaseti jātirnimittamupāttam | yaśaso guṇatvavivakṣāyāṃ iyamupāttaguṇanimitteti dhyeyam ||
yathā vā
satyajñānānande nityaṃ vṛṣaśailaśikharato’spande | pariramati ramāvāse paramapadaṃ bhavati nūnamabrahma ||
atra paramapade abrahmatvamutprekṣyata iti dravyābhāvasvarūpotprekṣeyaṃ vṛṣaśailādhikaraṇakapariramaṇakriyārūpanimittopādānādupāttanimittā | brahma dravyaṃ ekatvāt | vṛṣaśailaśikharataḥ aspande akṛtakiñciccalane pariramati parito viharati sati | ‘vyāṅparibhyo ramaḥ’ iti parasmaipadam ||
yathā vā
tāvakamahasevanamiladanavadhijanacaraṇahatijareṇunibhāt | dharayordhvaṃ visṛmarayā vigaganamiva khagagirīśa jagadāsīt || 428 ||
mahaḥ utsavaḥ ‘maha uddhava utsavaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | adanto’yam | reṇunibhāt parāgavyājāt | khagagiririti śeṣādrereva nāmāntaram | atra vigaganamiti dravyābhāvotprekṣāyāṃ reṇuvyājenotsṛtvarī bhūmirdravyaṃ nimittamupāttam ||
eṣā mālārūpā’pi saṃbhavati, yathā
marakatamaṇiriva vicaran karacaraṇamanoharo’mburāśiriva | sphuradaṅga ivānaṅgo vrajabhuvi vijahāra yadukulakumāraḥ || 429 ||
atra bhagavadgatānāṃ saṃcāritvādīnāṃ marakatāditādātmyavirodhināṃ virodhopaśamāya viṣayiṣu marakatādiṣvāropeṇa sādhāraṇīkaraṇam | na cehopamā śakyā nirūpayitum, vicarattvādīnāmukternaiṣphalyāpatteḥ | na copamāniṣpādakaṃ teṣāṃ sādhāraṇyaṃ tadabhāve’pi nailyādibhirdharmaiḥ pratīyamānaistasyā niṣpatteracārutvādupamāniṣpādakatvena kaveravivakṣitatvācca | na hyatra vicarattvādibhirdharmairmarakatādisādṛśyaṃ bhagavataḥ kavisaṃrambhaviṣayaḥ | evaṃ vicarattvādīnāṃ marakatādiṣvāropo’pi satyāmupamāyāṃ niṣprayojana eva bhavet | abhedapratipattau tu avicaratā akaracaraṇenānaṅgena ca tena tena kathamasyābhedassaṃbhavediti pratikūlamatimapanetuṃ viṣayigatānāṃ vicarattvādyāropāṇāmastyevopayogaḥ | atraiva ivaśabdasyābhāve dṛḍhāropaṃ rūpakam | viṣayigataviśeṣaṇānāmabhāve upamā | ivaśabdaviśeṣaṇayorekatarasyāpyabhāve śuddharūpakamiti vivekaḥ ||
evaṃ jātyādibhāvābhāvasvarūpotprekṣāḥ diṅmātreṇodāhṛtāḥ | atha hetūtprekṣā | tatra jātibhāve hetūtprekṣā yathā
vṛṣagirineturmaṇimayasaudhenevorasā’bdhirājasutā | vismāritā nivāsaṃ makarandaparāgapaṅkile kamale || 430 ||
atra saudheneti jātiḥ kamalanivāsavismaraṇaṃ prati hetutvenotprekṣyate tadevotprekṣyamāṇahetornimittamityupāttaguṇanimittā jātibhāvahetūtprekṣā | hetuphalayorutprekṣāviṣayatve nimittasyopādānameva, na tvanupādānam | tathāhi sādhye anupātte sādhanatvena nirdeśo nānvayaṃ puṣṇāti tathā sādhane anupātte sādhyatvena nirdeśo’pi | tayoritaretarasāpekṣatvāt hetūtprekṣāyāṃ hi nimittaṃ phalam | tasyānupādāne kaṃ prati hetutā saṃgacchatām | phalotprekṣāyāṃ hi sādhanaṃ nimittam, tasminnanupātte kaṃ prati phalatvaṃ saṃghaṭatām | evaṃ ca nimittanaimitikayoranyataropādāne’nvayaparipoṣavirahādubhayopādānaniyame nimittopādānaṃ niyatamevetyavadheyam | tathāca udāhṛte’sminpadye utprekṣāyā nimittasya kamalanivāsavismāraṇasyānupādāne saudheneti hetūtprekṣā na śobhate | evaṃ vakṣyamāṇaphalotprekṣāyāmapyūhyam | tasmāt svarūpotprekṣāyāmeva nimittasyopādānānupādane | na tu hetuphalotprekṣayoriti draṣṭavyam ||
guṇahetūtprekṣā yathā
acchāccharucirayaṃ mama sacchātra iti pramodato nūnam | svābhimukhe deve’nupraviśati kaustubhamaṇiṃ sahasrāṃśuḥ || 431 ||
acchāccharuciḥ atisvacchadyutitvāt akuṭilaprītitvāccetyarthaḥ | hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇam | ayaṃ kaustubhamaṇiḥ mama sacchātraḥ iti pramodato nūnaṃ deve śrīnivāse svābhimukhe sati sahasrāṃśuḥ kaustubhamaṇiṃ anupraviśati yogakṣemādau tadaikarasyaṃ bhajatīti bhāvaḥ | tatra pratibimbito bhavatīti vāstavārthaḥ | bhagavata ābhimukhye satyeva tadvakṣassthite kaustubhe sahasrāṃśupratiphalanasaṃbhavātsvābhimukhe ityuktiḥ | atra sacchātratāhetukapramodo guṇaḥ kaustubhānupraveśaṃ prati hetutvenotprekṣyate | sa evānupraveśo nimittamupāttam ||
kriyāhetūtprekṣā yathā
harimukharākācandrālokāniśasevanādiva ramāyāḥ | nayanendīvarayugalaṃ nityotphullaṃ nimīlanājñamabhūt || 432 ||
atra secanāditi kriyā nityotphullatāṃ prati hetutayotprekṣyate saivotphullatā nimittam || yathāvā
svāmiṃstvanmukhahimarucidhāmakṛtātparibhavādiva nitāntam | jīvanabhaṅgavilolā kamalālī vahati kiṃ nu sā’rasatām || 433 ||
he svāmin ! tvanmukhameva himaruciḥ ātmapratyanīkaścandramāḥ kamalālīdhvaṃsanopakaraṇavattvadyotanāya himaruciśabdaḥ | tasya dhāmnā tejasā prabhāveṇa ca ‘gṛhadehatviṭprabhāvā dhāmāni’ iyamaraḥ | kṛtāt paribhavādiva sā’rasatāmityatra sā arasatāmiti chedaḥ, sā atiramaṇīyatayā prasiddhā kamalālī padmapaṅktiḥ | jīvanasya jīvitasya bhaṅge vilolā viśeṣeṇa saktā ‘satāṃ mlāne māne’ ityādyuktarītyā jīvitaparityajanakṛtasaṃkalpetyarthaḥ | pakṣe jīvanasya salilasya bhaṅgaistaraṅgaiḥ vilolā cañcalā `lolaścalasatṛṣṇayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | arasatāṃ sarvaviṣayavairasyaṃ vahati | paraparibhavadūnānāṃ jīvitatyajanadattadṛṣṭīnāṃ sarvatrānāsthaiva bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | anyatra sārasatāmiti samastaṃ padam | sarojatāmiti vāstavārthaḥ | atra paribhavarūpā kriyā arasatāvahanaṃ prati hetutvena saṃbhāvyate | tadeva tatra nimittam | iyaṃ śleṣasaṃkīrṇā ||
dravyahetūtprekṣā yathā
balavānapi bahupādo’pyalamahibhūbhṛnmahāgatāḍhyopi | neṣṭe padamapi calituṃ svasminviśvaṃbhareṇa kila vasatā || 434 || balavān sāmarthyavānapi | pakṣe sthaulyavānapi ‘sthaulyasāmarthyasainyeṣu balam’ ityamaraḥ | balasattve’pi karaṇavirahe kathaṃ gamanasaṃbhava ityata āha bahviti | bahupādo’pi bahucaraṇopi | pakṣe bahupratyantaparvatopi | ata eva mahat ślāghyaṃ gataṃ gamanaṃ, bhāve ktaḥ | tena āḍhyopi | pakṣe mahāntaḥ agāḥ taravaḥ yasmiṃstathoktaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ mahāgatā tayā āḍhyopi | ahibhūbhṛt śeṣādriḥ śeṣa ityapi gamyate | svasmin vasatā viśvaṃbhareṇeva padamapi calituṃ neṣṭe na śaknoti | atra svasminnavasthito viśvaṃbharo dravyaṃ ahibhūbhṛto niścalatāṃ prati hetutvenotprekṣyate | sā niścalataiva nimittamupāttam ||
yathāvā
nirmaladṛṣṭiprasaraṇanirodhanirmāṇanipuṇanijamahimā | candrādaparasmādiva sāndrā śaurestamastatirgalati || 435 ||
nirmalā bhramavipralambhādisaṃbhāvanādavīyasī | pakṣe viśadāyā dṛṣṭiḥ jñānaṃ, pakṣe dṛk ‘dṛṣṭirjñāne’kṣṇi darśane’ ityamaraḥ | tasyāḥ prasaraṇasya nirodhanirmāṇe nipuṇaḥ nijaḥ mahimā mahattvaṃ yasyāssā | tamastatiḥ ajñānasaṃtatiḥ | dhvāntasaṃhatiśca | atra tamastatigalanaṃ prati candro dravyaṃ hetutayotprekṣyate | tamastatigalanameva nimittam | śleṣottambhiteyam | etena dravyasya hetutvenotprekṣaṇaṃ na saṃbhavatīti vadantaḥ pratyuktāḥ ||
yathāvā
kaladhautagiriṃ bhavatā suśrīkailāsamṛddhamadhivasatā | apareṇeva murāre śrīdenālaṃkṛtottarāśā sā || he murāre! suśrīkāḥ elāḥ elālatāḥ tābhiḥ samṛddhaṃ, anyatra ṛddhaṃ samṛddhaṃ suśrīścāsau kailāsaśca taṃ tannāmānaṃ kaladhautagiriṃ kanakācalaṃ śeṣādrereva nāmāntaramidamityavocāmāsakṛt | pakṣe rajatagirim | ‘kaladhautaṃ rūpyahemnoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | adhivasatā śrīdena bhavatā apareṇa śrīdena kubereṇeva sā uttarā śreṣṭhā āśā puruṣārthaviṣayakatṛṣṇā, āśāsānānāmiti śeṣaḥ | alaṃ paryāptā kṛtā paripūritetyarthaḥ | pakṣe uttarāśā udīcī dik alaṃkṛtā bhūṣitā | ‘bhūṣaṇe’lam’ iti gatisaṃjñāyāṃ ‘kugati’ iti samāsaḥ | tadadhīśatvāttasyeti bhāvaḥ | atra śrīdeneti dravyasya uttarāśālaṅkaraṇahetutvena saṃbhāvanaṃ śleṣottambhitam | atrāparaśabdāprayoge prācāṃ mate śabdamātrasādharmyāt śleṣa eva syāt | navīnapakṣe arthasādharmye tu śrīdasya siddhatayā upamaiva | tatprayoge tu prakṛto bhagavānevāparaśrīdatvenādhyavasīyate, tasya ivaśabdena sādhyatvapratīterutprekṣaiva | ivaśabdāprayoge tu siddhatvādadhyavasāyasyātiśayoktiḥ | ivāparaśabdayorubhayorapyaprayoge rūpakamityādayo viśeṣā yathāsaṃbhavamūhyāḥ ||
idamudāharaṇadvayaṃ vidyānāthānurodhena | tena hi
pratāparudradevena kṣmābhṛtpakṣavijṛmbhitam |
samunmūlitamāmūlādapareṇeva vajriṇā ||
iti dravyahetūtprekṣā udāhriyata | yadyapīdaṃ padyaṃ dravyasvarūpotprekṣāyā evodāharaṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | atra hi ivaśabdāpanayane pratāparudre viṣaye aparavajryabhedarūpakameveti tāvadavivādam | tathāca sati tatra ivaśabdayojane pratāparudre aparavajrirūpadravyasvarūpotprekṣaiva ivaśabdena pratyāyyeta | yatra asatīvaśabde yatpadārthe yatpadārtharūpakaṃ tatraiva satīvaśabde tatpadārthe tatpadārthasvarūpotprekṣeti niyamadarśanāt | tathā’pi yadīha pratārudradevena samunmūlitaṃ aparavajriṇevetyanvayavivakṣayā pratāparudrahetuke kṣmābhṛtpakṣavijṛmbhitasamunmūlane aparavajrihetukatvasaṃbhāvanaviṣayatvaṃ pratipipādayiṣitaṃ tadā bhavatīdaṃ dravyahetūtprekṣāyā api kathaṃcidudāharaṇamiti tadāśaya unnīyate | citramīmāṃsārasagaṅgādharakārau tu īdṛśadravyasvarūpotprekṣāgandhadaviṣṭhameva padyaṃ dravyahetūtprekṣāyāmudāharetām | tadanurodhena nissaṃdigdhamudāharaṇaṃ tu ‘balavānapi bahupādopi’ iti prāgevopadarśitamasmābhirityalaṃ bahunā ||
athābhāvābhimānavatyaḥ tatra jātyabhāvahetūtprekṣā yathā
gaganatale svaprabhayā sthagite bhānorabhāvato nūnam | ghanarucirurasā kaustubhaminakoṭirucaṃ vahatyahigirīśaḥ || 437 ||
atra bhānorjāterabhāvaḥ kaustubhadhāraṇahetutvenotprekṣyata ityupāttakriyānimittajātyabhāvahetūtprekṣeyam ||
yathāvā
śaurerjayayātrotthairbhūreṇubharairmuhuḥ kabalitāyām | dyusariti salilābhāvānmarutāṃ svapade dhruvaṃ maruttvamabhūt || 438 ||
svapade svasthāne svavācakapade ca maruttvaṃ marubhūmitvaṃ marucchabdatvaṃ ca | ādyapakṣe takārasya ‘anaci ca’ iti dvitvam | ‘marutau pavanāmarau, samānau marudhanvānau’ iti cāmaraḥ | atra maruttvaprāptiṃ prati salilarūpajātyabhāvo hetutayotprekṣyate | śleṣojjīviteyam ||
guṇābhāvahetūtprekṣā yathā
viharati mayi sānandaṃ saha ramayā sa harireva kiṃ me’nyat | āśāsyamityanākulabhāvātprāyeṇa niścalo’higiriḥ || 439 ||
prāyeṇetyetadutprekṣāvyañjakam | atra anākulabhāvāditi guṇabhāvo naiścalyaṃ prati hetutvenotprekṣyate | iyaṃ calanarūpakriyābhāvanimittā guṇābhāvahetūtprekṣā | evaṃ jātyādinimittā apyūhyāḥ ||
kriyābhāvahetūtprekṣā yathā
madhyamatāṃ tyaktuṃ tava madhyamamarvākchiraḥ pratapyāpi | tadalābhātkraśimānaṃ nūnaṃ prāpāhiśailapatidayite || 440 ||
tava madhyamaṃ madhyamatāṃ utkarṣāpakarṣaśūnyatāṃ, pakṣe avalagnatāṃ tyaktuṃ arvākchiraḥ pratapyāpi adhaśśirastapastaptvā’pi vilomatayā’vasthānamevādhaśśirastapasyātvenādhyavasīyate | tadalābhāt madhyamatātyāgāprāpteḥ madhyamamiti varṇāvalervilomatve’pi madhyamamityevāvasthānācchabdārthayostādātmyamupajīvya tadalābhādityuktam | kraśimānaṃ prāpa | atra lābhakriyābhāvaḥ kraśimaprāptiṃ prati hetutvenotprekṣyata iti kriyānimittakriyābhāvahetūtprekṣeyaṃ śleṣātiśayoktyuttambhitā | evaṃ jātyādinimittakriyābhāvotprekṣā apyūhyāḥ || dravyābhāvahetūtprekṣā yathā
ahigirigā harirucibhissvadakṣiṇārdhāṅgake’pi mecakite | kālī gaurī samajani śarvābhāvādivātyartham || 441 ||
kālī nisargato nīlavarṇā harārdhaśarīrasthā pārvatī ‘jānapada’ ityādinā ṅīṣ | ahigirigā bhagavatsevārthaṃ śeṣādrigatā satīti bhāvaḥ | harirucibhiḥ śyāmalābhiriti bhāvaḥ | svadakṣiṇārdhāṅgake’pi bhartṛrūpe svadakṣiṇārdhāvayave’pi ardhanārīśvarasya pārvatīdakṣiṇārdhaśarīrasthatvāt | mecakite śyāmalite sati | apiśabdassvaśarīraśyāmalimānaṃ samuccinoti | śarvābhāvādiva svavallabhābhāvādiva śarīradakṣiṇavāmārdhayorubhayorapyaviśeṣeṇa nipadyamāne kālimni patyardhaśarīre’pi svaśarīrārdhāntarasaṃbhāvanayā patyurabhāvamāśaṅkya nirvedamaśnuvāneti bhāvaḥ | atyarthaṃ gaurī babhūva pāṇḍurā’’sīt | anyathā svataḥ kālavarṇāyā asyāḥ kathaṃ gauravarṇatā syāditi bhāvaḥ | ekasyā eva pārvatyāḥ kālavarṇatvagauravarṇatvavirodho janmabhedena parihāryaḥ | atra śarvarūpadravyābhāvaḥ kālyāḥ nirvedapūrvakagauratāṃ prati hetutvena saṃbhāvita iti guṇanimitteyaṃ dravyābhāvahetūtprekṣā | ‘anyārdhābhyāmivotpannamardhanārīśvarāntaram’ iti dravyotprekṣodāharaṇe ‘vastuto’rdhanārīśvarasyaikatvāt dravyotprekṣā’ ityuktaṃ citramīmāṃsāyām | ato’rdhanārīśvarasya dravyatvamavyāhatam ||
yathāvā
cintāmaṇerabhāvāddharaṇau cintāmaṇikṣitidharendram | sarvamanorathavitaraṇadhūrvahamasṛjajjagatsraṣṭā || atra dharaṇyadhikaraṇakacintāmaṇyabhāvaścintāmaṇigirisarjanaṃ prati hetutayā saṃbhāvitaḥ | kriyānimitteyaṃ dravyābhāvahetūtprekṣā | cintāmaṇigiririti śeṣādrereva nāmāntaramityavocāma, tanna vismartavyam | iti hetūtprekṣādik ||
atha phalotprekṣā tatra jātiphalotprekṣā yathā
ramayā saha vṛṣaśikhariṇi samaye vijihīrṣato murārāteḥ | paṭamaṇḍapāya nūnaṃ jṛmbhante’mbhodharāssasaṃrambhāḥ || 443 ||
atra paṭamaṇḍapatvarūpajātyavacchinnaḥ ambhodharajṛmbhaṇasya phalatayotprekṣyata iti kriyānimittajātiphalotprekṣā | na cātra saṃpādanakriyāmantarā śuddhāyā jāteraphalatvātkriyāyā eva phalatvamiti vācyam | saṃpādanasya saṃsargatayā taddvāreṇaiva jātyādeḥ phalatvopapatteḥ | anyathā phalatvabodhakacaturthyā anupapatteḥ | ata eva ‘yaśase vijigīṣūṇāṃ prajāyai gṛhamedhinām , brāhmaṇyāya tapastepe viśvāmitrassudāruṇam’ ityādayaḥ prayogā upapadyante | evaṃ jātyādinimittā api jātiphalotprekṣā udāhāryāḥ ||
guṇaphalotprekṣā yathā
asitaghanarucira iti matsadṛśākhyo hariraśaṃsi nigamāntaiḥ | iti tādṛśanīlimne vidhuḥ kalaṅkaṃ dadhāti kiṃ svāṅge || 444 ||
atra harividhuśabdau bhagavaccandrobhayavācinau | asitaghanaruciraḥ nīlāmbudamanohara iti | nigamāntaiḥ ‘nīlatoyadamadhyasthā’ śyāmācchabalaṃ prapadye śabalācchayāmam’ ityādibhiḥ aśaṃsi prāśasyata | atra nīlimā guṇaḥ phalatvenotprekṣyate kalaṅkadhāraṇaṃ nimittamiti kriyānimittaguṇaphalotprekṣā | evaṃ jātyādinimittā api guṇaphalotprekṣā ūhanīyāḥ ||
kriyāphaloprakṣā yathā
traivikramamiva vikramamabhyasyantaḥ paraṃ taṭitvantaḥ | uddāmānaḥ prāvṛṣyujjṛmbhante’higirimuparyupari ||
abhyasyanta iva abhyasitumivetyarthaḥ | ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ’ iti śatā | arjayanvasatītyādāviva heturiha phalam | ahigirimuparyupari ahigiressamīpaparipradeśa ityarthaḥ | ‘uparyadhyadhasassāmīpye’ iti dvirbhāvaḥ ‘dhiguparyādiṣu triṣu’ iti tadyoge dvitīyā | atrābhyasyanta iti kriyā phalatvenotprekṣyate | ujjṛmbhaṇakriyā nimittamiti kriyānimittā kriyāphalotprekṣā | jātyādinimittā apyamuyaiva diśā pratipattavyāḥ ||
dravyaphalotprekṣā yathā
surabhilavanamālāmiladalikulato nābhikamalaparilagnān | dadhadalino madhudalano dhātre’nantāya kiṃ nu saṃnaddhaḥ || 446 ||
vanamālāmiladalikulāt nābhikamale saṃlagnān saṃbaddhānityarthaḥ | anena alināṃ tadāruṇyasaṃkrāntirdyotyate | madhudalanaḥ madhumathana ityarthaḥ | anantāya dhātre anekāṃścaturmukhānnirmātumiti yāvat | atra dhātā dravyaṃ phalatayotprekṣyate | alidhāraṇakriyā nimittamiti kriyānimittadravyaphalotprekṣā | evaṃ jātyādinimittā api dravyaphalotprekṣā udāhāryāḥ ||
athābhāvābhimānavatyaḥ phalotprekṣāḥ tatra jātyabhāvaphalotprekṣā yathā
yamalārjunayorharipadakamalāmarśādvimuktimālocya | manye vasantyahigirāvanye taravopyabhūruhatvāya || 447 ||
atrābhūruhatvāyeti jātyabhāvaḥ phalatvena saṃbhāvyate | vimuktyālocanakriyā nimittamiti kriyānimittajātyabhāvaphalotprekṣā | evaṃ jātyādinimittā apyūhyāḥ | abhūruhatvāya atarutvāya bhuvijananābhāvāya ca, niśśreyasāyeti yāvat ||
guṇabhāvaphalātprekṣā yathā
taikṣṇyamiha lokabandhoranucitamiti kaustubhobhavanbhāsvān | nūnamataikṣṇyāya harerhṛdayaṃ śrayate kṛpājharīśiśiram || 448 ||
atrātaikṣṇyāyeti guṇabhāva phalatvenotprekṣyate | kṛpājharīśiśiraharihṛdayāśrayaṇakriyā nimittam | evaṃ jātyādinimittā api jñeyāḥ ||
kriyābhāvaphalotprekṣā yathā
sarveṣāṃ svadayitatamahṛdayasthityanavalokanāyeva | vidyutprabhānibhābhissvabhābhirācchādayati dṛśo lakṣmīḥ || 449 || sarveṣāmityetat dṛśa ityatrānveti | atrāvalokanakriyāyā abhāvaḥ phalatvenotprekṣyate | ācchādanakriyā nimittam | evaṃ jātiguṇadravyanimittā api vibhāvanīyāḥ |
dravyābhāvaphalotpekṣā yathā
himaśikharitridaśasaritkalaśodadhigiriśaśaśivilopāya | śaṅke’hiśailaśekharayaśovilāso diśo viśo (bha)dhayate || 450 ||
atra himaśikharimukhadravyābhāvaḥ phalatvena saṃbhāvyate viśo (bha) dhanakriyā nimittam | evamevānyadapyūhyam |
iha jātyādayo hi bhedāḥ prācāmanurodhādudāhṛtāḥ | vastutastu naiteṣāmasti camatkṛtau vailakṣaṇyamityanudāharaṇīyā evaite | camatkṛtivailakṣaṇyaṃ svarūpahetuphalātmakānāṃ trayāṇāṃ prakārāṇāmeveti dhyeyam ||
evaṃ manye śaṅke dhruvaṃ prāyo nūnamityevamādibhiḥ |
utprekṣā vyajyate śabdairivaśabdopi tādṛśaḥ ||
ityuktaprakāravācakaśabdaghaṭitāḥ vācyotprekṣāḥ padarśitāḥ | tadaprayoge gamyotprekṣā bhavati ||
tatra vastūtprekṣā yathā
īpsitasarā nikāmaṃ tava sarahitatāṃ madamba gacchantī | prāpyātāntonmukhatāṃ sarojayugyalamabhūdurojayugī || 451 || sarojayugī nikāmaṃ īpsitāḥ sarāḥ muktāhārāḥ yayā sā īpsitasarā | pakṣe īpsitaṃ saraḥ kāsāro yasyāssā īpsitasarāḥ | sānto’yaṃ śabdaḥ | tava sarahitatāṃ hārahitatvaṃ anyatra savarṇarahitatvaṃ gacchantī ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ’ iti śatā | heturiha phalam | gantumityarthaḥ | anyatra hetvarthe | ata eva alaṃ atāntā amlānā satī unmukhatāṃ autsukyaṃ, pakṣe atāntonmukhatāmiti samastaṃ padam | atāntaḥ tavarṇarahitacaramabhāgaḥ ut uvarṇa ityarthaḥ | udityatra takārotsādane u ityevāvaśeṣāt | saḥ mukhe prārambhe yasyāssā tasyāḥ bhāvaḥ atāntonmukhatā tāṃ ‘śaradaḥ kṛtārthatā’ ityādāviva sāmānye napuṃsakaṃ tatastal | prāpya tava urojayugī stanadvayī abhavat tattvena jātetyarthaḥ | pakṣe uktarītyā sarojayugīśabdaḥ urojayugīśabdo niṣpanna ityarthaḥ | atra urojayugyāssarojayugītādātmyotprekṣāyāṃ vācakaśśabdo nopātta iti pratīyamāneyam ||
tatraiva hetūtprekṣā yathā
nīlānayanendīvaravisṛmararucinicayasatataparicayataḥ | cakṣuśśrutigiriśikhare sākṣādbrahmāttamecakima bhāti || 452 ||
atra paricayata iti hetūtprekṣāyāṃ vācakaśśabdo’nupātta itīyamapi pratīyamānā arthasāmarthyāvaseyatvāt ||
tatraiva phalotprekṣā yathā
nanvamba karṇatāṃ svāmanvarthayituṃ tava śravaṇayugmam | sahajojjvalamaṇikuṇḍalasaṃsparśanataḥ pratītatāmeti || 453 ||
nanvamba tava śravaṇayugmaṃ kartṛ svāṃ karṇatāṃ karṇaśabdavācyatāṃ ‘śrotrarādheyayoḥ karṇaḥ’ iti ratnamālā | anvarthayituṃ sahajaṃ svajanmasamaye sahaiva jātaṃ ujjvalaṃ ca, pakṣe nisargata eva dedīpyamānaṃ yanmaṇikuṇḍalaṃ sahajenojjvalamiti samāsaḥ | tasya saṃsparśanataḥ vitaraṇāt, pakṣe avamarśāt | ‘sahajastu nisarge nā sahotthe punaranyavat, sparśano mārute puṃsi dāne sparśe napuṃsakam’ iti ca medinī | pratītatāṃ hṛṣṭatāṃ eti śarīreṇa saha jātamapi maṇikuṇḍalamutkṛtya yācate dvijaveṣabhāje surarāje datvā’pi nānvatapyata apitu hṛṣṭa evāsīditi tadaudāryātiśayapraśaṃsanam ||
yadvā sparśanataḥ dāne viṣaye, saptamyā ādyāditvāttasiḥ | pratītatāṃ sādaratāmityarthaḥ
yācato modamādatte vāñchitādadhikapradaḥ |
anuvrajya priyaṃ brūte tasmātkarṇastavādhikaḥ ||
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe pratītatāṃ uktavidhamaṇikuṇḍalasparśanena bhūṣitatāmityarthaḥ | ‘pratītassādare jñāte hṛṣṭaprakhyātayostriṣu, pratītaḥ prathite hṛṣṭe parijñāte vibhūṣite’ iti medinīratnamāle | eti prāpnoti | ata eva karṇaśabdānvarthanamiti bhāvaḥ | mahābhārate dānāya sahajakavacakuṇḍalayoḥ kartanādeva hi rādheyasya kṛntatīti karṇa iti nāma vyutpāditam | atra karṇatāmanvarthayitumiti phalotprekṣāyāṃ vācakamanupāttam | evaṃ prāgudāhṛteṣveva padyeṣu vācakaparityāge tāssarvā api pratīyamānā bhavantīti nātra tadviśeṣāḥ pratāyante ||
dharmasvarūpotprekṣā yathā
manye bhavatkṛpāyā mohamapāraṃ phaṇīndraśikharividho | pāpīyasopi jantoḥ paramaṃ śreyo dadāti yā’kasmāt || 454 ||
pāpīyasopi jantoḥ gṛdhrāderiti bhāvaḥ | atra pūrvārdhotprekṣitamoharūpadharmasiddhaye dvitīyārdhe ākasmikaniratiśayaśreyodānamupāttam ||
idamatrāvadheyam utprekṣyamāṇeṣvapi yasya viṣayiṇa utprekṣā vidheyatayā’vabhāsate tadīyotprekṣayaiva vyapadeśaḥ prādhānyāt | tena ‘acchāccharuciḥ’ iti śloke sahasrāṃśugatatayā pramodasyotprekṣaṇe’pi na tadutprekṣayā vyapadeśo nyāyyaḥ | tasyā aṅgatvenānuvādyatvāt kiṃtu pañcamyarthotprekṣayā, tasyā eva nūnaśabdavedyatvena vidheyatvāt | tathā ‘traivikramamiva vikramam’ iti padye traivikramavikramābhyasanotprekṣayā’pi vyapadeśo na yuktaḥ kiṃtu śatrarthaphalotprekṣayā | evaṃ ‘hṛdayāspadayā’ iti ‘nātha tava nābhisarasi’ ityādipadyeṣu na phalotprekṣayā vyapadeśo yujyate | nāpi ‘karapadmena’ iti padye sitakarabimbatādātmyotprekṣayā tadunmīlitayā karapadmakartṛkadveṣahetukamadhyacchidrīkaraṇapūrvakaprakoṣṭhavayanakarmatādātmyotprekṣayā vā, pūrvoktādeva hetoḥ | evameva sarvatrāpi ‘pradhānena vyapadeśā bhavanti’ iti nyāyātpradhānabhūtotprekṣayaiva vyapadeśa iti | dharmopi tāvadatra dvividhaḥ svata eva sādhāraṇaḥ, sādhāraṇīkaraṇopāyenāsādhāraṇopi sādhāraṇīkṛtaśca | sa copāyaḥ kvacidrūpakaṃ kvacchileṣaḥ kvacidapahnutiḥ kvacidbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ kvacittatkarambhitavastuprativastubhāvaḥ kvacidupacāraḥ kvacidabhedādhyavasāyarūpo’tiśayaḥ | tatra svata eva sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ prāgudāhṛte ‘nīlaguṇenākrānte’ iti padye viṣayaviṣayiṇoḥ kanakavasanapītaguṇayoraujjvalyam | sādhāraṇīkṛtastu prāgudāhṛta eva ‘bhrūyugaladhanurlastakasaṃhitanāsākarīrasaṃlagnam’ iti padye prathamārdhagato dharmaḥ rūpakeṇa viṣayaviṣayisādhāraṇīkṛtaḥ ||
śleṣeṇa sādhāraṇīkṛto yathā
surabhisvairasparśanalīlāpyāyitasamastalokastvam | yanmādhavosi tattvāṃ vasantamahiśailanātha jānīmaḥ || 455 ||
surabheḥ kāmadhenoḥ svairasparśanaṃ svacchandavitaraṇaṃ tasya līlayā kāmagavīvādānyakatulyavādānyakenetyarthaḥ | anyatra surabhiḥ ghrāṇatarpaṇassugandhirityarthaḥ |
surabhiścampake svarṇe jātīphalavasantayoḥ |
sugandhau ca manojñe ca vācyavatsurabhissmṛtaḥ ||
iti viśvaḥ | svairaḥ ‘mandasvacchandayossvairaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | yaḥ sparśano malayamāruta ityarthaḥ | ‘sparśano mārute puṃsi dāne sparśe napuṃsakam’ iti medinī | tasya līlayā vilāsena prasaraṇeneti yāvat | āpyāyitasamastalokaḥ ubhayatra tulyo’rthaḥ | mādhavaḥ śrīnivāsa mādhavākhya ṛtuśca yat yasmāt asi, tat tvāṃ vasantaṃ jānīmaḥ | ayamupātta eva bhavati nānupāttaḥ aprasiddhatvāt | śleṣetarastu anupāttaścāpi bhavati yathā ‘marakatamaṇiriva vicaran’ ityādau nailyādiḥ| na cātra vicaratvādirūpa upātta eva sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ sādhāraṇyārthameva hi viṣayiṇyāropa iti vācyam | tasyāropeṇa kṛte’pi sādhāraṇye asundaratvenotprekṣonmeṣakatvavaidhuryāt | sādhāraṇīkaraṇaṃ tu pratibandhanirāsārthamityavocāmaiva ||
yathā
satpadmaśaṅkhakacchapamakaraṃ ghananīla tava mukundapadam | varakundanakhaṃ manye sumahāpadmaṃ tato nidhimayaṃ tvām || 456 ||
he ghananīla! meghaśyāmala! pakṣe ghanaḥ nīlo nidhiviśeṣo yasya sa tathoktaḥ tasya saṃbuddhiḥ | he mukunda! muktidāyin! pṛṣodarāditvātsādhuḥ | pakṣe he mukundanidhe! tava padaṃ caraṇaṃ santaḥ padmaśaṅkhakacchapamakarāḥ yasmiṃstat padmādyākārarekhāsaṃpannamityarthaḥ | pakṣe padmādināmanidhiviśeṣavadityarthaḥ | varāṇi kundāni kundakusumānīva nakhāni, pakṣe varakundau nidhiviśeṣau nakheṣu yasya tattathoktam | tataḥ tasmāt mahatī padma śrīryasya taṃ, pakṣe mahāpadmanidhiṃ ca | tvāṃ nidhimayaṃ ‘mahāpadmaśca padmaśca’ ityuktanidhimayaṃ manye | atrotprekṣyamāṇasya nidhimayasya dharmeṣu padmādinidhyāśritacaraṇatvādikeṣu viśeṣaṇībhūtaistattannidhibhirviṣayasya bhagavato dharmeṣu kamalaśaṅkhādirekhāśritapadatvādiṣu viśeṣaṇānāṃ padmādīnāṃ śleṣeṇa tādātmyasaṃpādanadvārā tathāvidhadharmāṇāṃ sādhāraṇyasaṃpattiḥ ||
yathā vā
iha bhārgavīsamṛddhissarasvatī bhātyupatyakā durgā | acyutadhātrīśayitastrimūrtimaya iva vṛṣādrirayamindhe || 457 ||
iha adrau bhārgavī prapātasaṃbandhinī ‘prapātastu taṭo bhṛguḥ’ ityamaraḥ | samṛddhissaṃpat bhāti | pakṣe saṃtatā ṛddhiryasyāssā samṛddhiḥ mahāvibhūtirityarthaḥ | bhārgavī lakṣmīḥ bhāti | upatyakā āsannabhūmiḥ | sarāṃsyasyāṃ santīti sarasvatī | bhūmni matup | ‘tasau matvarthe’ iti bhasaṃjñāvidhānānna padakāryarutvotve | pakṣe bhāratītyarthaḥ| gantumaśakyā durgā ca | pakṣe gaurī ca bhāti | acyutaṃ yathā syāttathā dhātryāṃ bhūmyāṃ śayitaḥ sthita iti yāvat | śīṅaḥ kartari ktaḥ | pakṣe acyuto bhagavān dhātā brahmā īṭ īśānaśca acyutadhātrīśaḥ | idaṃ dvitīyābahuvacanam | itaḥ prāptaḥ | iṇaḥ kartari ktaḥ | yadvā itaḥ asmingirau saptamyāssārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | acyutadhātrīśa iti prathamābahuvacanaṃ arthastūkta eva | bhāntīti vacanavipariṇāmenānvayaḥ | dhātrīśayita ityatra dhātrīśas ita iti sthite rutvayatvayoḥ kṛtayoḥ ‘lopaśśākalyasya’ iti yalopasya vaikalpikatvānneha yakāralopaḥ | ata eva ayaṃ vṛṣādriḥ trimūrtimaya iva hariviriñciharamaya iva indhe dīpyate | atra utprekṣyamāṇasya trimūrtimayasya dharmeṣu samṛddhibhārgavyādyadhikaraṇatvādiṣu viśeṣaṇībhūtābhirbhārgavyādibhirviṣayasya vṛṣādrerdharmeṣu bhṛgusaṃbandhisamṛddhimattvādiṣu viśeṣaṇībhūtānāṃ bhṛgusaṃbandhisamṛddhyādīnāṃ śleṣeṇa tādātmyasaṃpādanadvārā tādṛśadharmāṇāṃ sādhāraṇatāsaṃpattiḥ ||
yathā vā
śubhapuṣyarāgarucijuṣi sumahānīle vidūrabhavakalite | acyuta ratnamayatvaṃ tvayi niyatamavaimi padmarāgapade || 458 ||
śubhaṃ puṣṇātīti śubhapuṭ tasmiṃstathokte arāgeṣu virateṣu rucijuṣi prītibhāji | pakṣe śubhāḥ puṣyarāgāḥ ratnaviśeṣāḥ tai rucijuṣi parabhāgabhājīti yāvat | sumahānīle atimātraśyāmale | pakṣe maṇiviśeṣe | vidūraḥ davīyān bhavassaṃsāro yeṣāṃ taiḥ aspṛṣṭasaṃsāragandhairnityamuktādibhiḥ kalite sevite | pakṣe vidūrabhavaiḥ vaidūryaiḥ kalite padmasya rāga iva rāgo yayoste pade caraṇe yasya tasmin | pakṣe padmarāgāṇāṃ maṇīnāṃ pade āśraye ata eva he acyuta ! tvayi uktarūpe tvayi ratnamayatvaṃ avaimi saṃbhāvaye | atrotprekṣyamāṇasya ratnamayasya dharmeṣu śubhapuṣyarāgarucibhāktvādikeṣu viśeṣaṇībhūtaistattadratnaiḥ viṣayasya bhagavato dharmeṣu śubhapoṣakatvādiṣu viśeṣaṇībhūtānāṃ śubhapoṣaṇādīnāṃ śleṣeṇa tādātmyasaṃpādanam ||
pūrvodāharaṇayorādye ‘satpadma’ iti padye abhaṅgaśleṣeṇa dvitīye ‘iha bhārgavī’ iti padye sabhaṅgābhaṅgaśleṣābhyāṃ sādhāraṇīkaraṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇaratnavalyā rūpakeṇa ca saṃkīrṇatā ca | asmin śubhapuṣyarāga’ iti padye tūbhayavidhaśleṣeṇa sādhāraṇīkṛtimātramiti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
svāyattahemakūṭaḥ prāyassaṃpūjyate mahendreṇa | prasravaṇasevitosāvete dāsāḥ kimasya śeṣagireḥ ||
atra hemakūṭamahendraprasravaṇaśabdā giriviśeṣavācinaḥ | arthāntaraṃ tu spaṣṭameva | ‘utsaḥ prasravaṇam ‘ ityamaraḥ | ete giraya ityarthaḥ | atra hemakūṭādiśaileṣu viṣaye śeṣādridāsatādātmyotprekṣāyāṃ tadāyattatvādilakṣaṇo viṣayidharmaḥ śleṣeṇa viṣayāṇāṃ tadāśritānāṃ ca kanakaśikharavṛtrārinirjjharāṇāmabhedasaṃpādanadvārā viṣayasādhāraṇīkṛtaḥ ||
apahnutyā sādhāraṇīkaraṇaṃ yathā
hṛdayāspadā tava dayā vadanāt smitabhānibhādudañcantī | bhuvi phaṇigirimaṇiramaṇi svaviṣayamakhilaṃ samīkṣate manye || 460 ||
yathā avarodhagatā mahārājñī vilāsavaśāduccaissaudhāntarādudetya nikhilaṃ nijadeśaṃ paśyati tathā tava hṛdayagā dayā uccairvadanamadhiruhya svagocaramakhilaṃ janaṃ samīkṣata iveti bhāvaḥ| atra svaviṣayavilokanasya dayāyāmutprekṣaṇe tadarthamāntarapradeśādbahirudgamanamapekṣitaṃ, tacca bahiḥpradeśasambandharūpaṃ smitaprabhāmātravṛti dayāyāṃ na saṃbhavatīti smitaprabhāpahnavena dayāgataṃ kriyate ||
yathāvā
makuṭādimaṇivyājātparito nānātvametya bhavato nayane | paśyata iveśa viṣvakprakāśanāyaiṣa viśvataścakṣuriti || 461||
he īśa! sarveśvara! bhavato nayane kartṛṇī eṣaḥ sarveśvaro bhagavān viśvataścakṣuḥ sarvatomukhalocanavāniti prakāśanāya paśyata iveti yojanā | atra ‘viśvataścakṣuruta viśvatomukhaḥ’ iti śrutyartho’nusaṃdheyaḥ | iha yugapat viṣvagdarśanasya nayanayorutprekṣāyāṃ tannimittaṃ tayorviṣvagvṛttitvamapekṣitaṃ, tacca makuṭādigatamaṇivṛtti na tu nayanavṛtti bhavatīti maṇyapahnutyā nayanagataṃ vitanyate ||
yathāvā
tāvakacaritrarūpaṃ deva śravaṇāntarātpraviśyāntaḥ| siddhāñjanaṃ kimapi naśśuddhāmantardṛśaṃ vyanaktīva ||
he deva! tāvakacaritrarūpaṃ kimapi siddhāñjanaṃ śravaṇayorantarādvivarāt antaḥ praviśya naḥ antaḥ dṛśaṃ dṛṣṭiṃ jñānaṃ ca śuddhāṃ nirdoṣāṃ satīṃ sarvavastusākṣātkaraṇakṣamāmiti yāvat | vyanakti prakāśayati | atrāntardṛṣṭiśodhanasya siddhāñjane utprekṣāyāṃ tannimittaṃ bahiḥpradeśādantaḥpradeśo’pekṣitaḥ| sa cāntaḥpradeśasambandharūpo bhagavaccaritramātravṛttissiddhāñjane na saṃbhavatīti bhagavaccaritrāpahnavena siddhāñjananiṣṭhaḥ kriyate | bimbapratibimbabhāvena sādhāraṇīkaraṇaṃ tu ‘karapadmena dveṣāt ityatra nirūpitam |
yathāvā
upariharinīlamaṇirucikavacitahāramaṇi bhagavato hṛdayam | bījākṛtamiva vilasati bahuphaladānāya tena kedāram || 463 ||
upari harinīlamaṇirucyā kavacitāḥ ācchāditāḥ hārasya maṇayo mauktikāni yasya tattathoktam | nīlamaṇiprabhāntaḥprakāśa mānamauktikamiti bhāvaḥ| bhagavato hṛdayaṃ tena bhagavatā janānāmiti śeṣaḥ| vahūni phalāni dhānyarūpāṇi, pakṣe aihikāmuṣmikāpavargikākhilaprayojanāni | teṣāṃ dānāya bījākṛtaṃ yāvanālādibījena sahakṛṣṭaṃ uptakṛṣṭaṃ ādau bījānyuptvā upari halaviśeṣakarṣaṇena samīkṛtakṛṣṇamṛditi bhāvaḥ | ‘kṛño dvitīyatṛtīyaśambabījātkṛṣau’ iti bījaśabdātkṛño yoge kṛṣirūpārthe ḍāc | ‘bījākṛtaṃ tūptakṛṣṭam’ ityamaraḥ | kedāraṃ kṣetramiva bhāti | atra harivakṣasaḥ kedāratādātmyasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ upariharinīlamaṇirucikavacitahāramaṇibījākṛtayorbimbapratibimbabhāvāpannayorabhedādhyavasāyena sādhāraṇyasaṃpādanam ||
evaṃ ‘bhitvā bhānoḥ’ ityādāvapi ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvakarambhitavastuprativastubhāvena yathā
kamalāruciparivītaḥ kaustubhamaṇireṣa śobhate sutarām | svasṛpativiḍambanāya svayamapi pītāmbareṇa kiṃ channaḥ || 464 ||
svasṛpateḥ nijabhaginījānerbhagavataḥ viḍambanāya parihāsārthānukaraṇāya | atra kamalārucipītāmbarayorbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | tatkarambhitaśca parivītacchannayorvastuprativastubhāva iti tena sādhāraṇyam ||
upacāreṇa yathā
madhurāmatigambhīrāṃ viśadāṃ śiśirāṃ vigāhamānānām | garuḍagiriśikharamaṇḍana gaṅgāṃ manyāmahe kathāṃ bhavataḥ || 465 || atra bhagavatkathāyāṃ mādhuryagāmbhīryavaiśadyaśaiśiryavigāhanānāṃ mukhyānāmasaṃbhavādāsvādāvikṣobhasupraveśatvatāpaharaṇaśravaṇānāmamukhyānāmupacāreṇa mukhyaissādhāraṇīkaraṇam | lakṣaṇayā śakyābhedena lakṣyabodhanāt |
abhedādhyavasāyamātreṇa yathā
saridadhipasutāsadmanyurasi harernaḥ kuto nivāsa iti | harṣādiva kṛtahāsā hārā nārāyaṇādriśekhara te || 466 ||
atra prakāśaprakaṭanahāsakaraṇayorabhedādhyavasāya eva harṣahetūtprekṣānimittatayopāttasya hāsakaraṇasya prakaṭitaprakāśakṛtahāsobhayasādhāraṇye bījam | evaṃ sarvatra hetuphalayorutprekṣaṇe yasya hetuḥ phalaṃ vā utprekṣyate so’nena prakāreṇa sādhāraṇīkṛto nimittamityavocāmāsakṛt ||
evaṃ kvacidupātto dharma viṣayaviṣayisādhāraṇyavirahādacārutvādvā svayamutprekṣāṃ sākṣāduttambhayitumanīśo’pi taduttambhanapaṭudharmāntarottambhanenākūlyasaṃpādanādupayujyate |
yathā
madhubhedanajayayātrādundubhininadaistathā diśo vyāptāḥ| śrotrendriyasṛṣṭikathādūramivāsīdyathā’khilaṃ bhuvanam || 467 ||
atra diśāṃ dundubhininavyāptirūpo dharma upātto bhuvanasya śrotrendriyasṛṣṭikathādūratvasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ vaiyadhikaraṇyenāprayojakopi svaprayojyaśravaṇavivarātimātraparipūraṇaprayuktaśrāvaṇajñānasāmānyaśūnyatvasya tathāvidhasaṃbhāvanānimittasyottambhanenānukūlyasaṃpādanādupayujyate ||
viṣayopyupāttaḥ pūrvodāharaṇeṣu pratipādita eva | kvacidviṣayo’pahnutopi bhavati | yathā
matsuhṛdindusagotraṃ mukhamidamityutpalena nīlena | satkartumarpitaṃ taddalamekaṃ harimukhe nu tilakanibhāt || 468 ||
atra tilakarūpaviṣayāpahnavo nīlotpalaikadalatādātmyasaṃbhāvanādārḍhyāyeti dhyeyam ||
iyameva sāpahnavotprekṣeti pratipādayatā’laṃkārasarvasvakṛtā svarūpotprekṣāmātramudājahre | kuvalayānandacitramīmāṃsayostu hetuphalotprekṣe api sāpahnave darśite | tatra svarūpotprekṣā anupadamevodāhṛtā | yathāvā
kanati kila ratnagarbhāstana iva pavanodaraṃbharigirīndraḥ| cūcukamiva harikapaṭānmecakamamṛtaṃ dadāti nijamukhataḥ || 469 ||
amṛtaṃ payaḥ niśśreyasaṃ ca nijamukhataḥ svāgrabhāgāt | pakṣe svenaivopāyena | upāyasthānaniviṣṭeneti bhāvaḥ | ‘mukhaṃ nissaraṇe vaktre’ prārambhopāyayorapi’ iti medinī | ‘upāyassvaprāpterupaniṣadadhītassa bhagavān’ iti hyanvagṛhyatācāryaiḥ | yatra haritvamapahnutaṃ tatraiva cūcukasaṃbhāvanācchuddhāpahnutigarbhatvam | ivaśabdamahimnā saṃbhāvanaṃ kapaṭaśabdaprayogādapahnavo gamyate || yathāvā
vadanendāvamṛtarasāsvādātkāṭavajihāsayā lagne | nimbadale ete nanvamba bhrūyugamiṣeṇa tava manye ||
atra bhrūyugalanihnavena nimbadalayugalasvarūpotprekṣeyam | yadyapi amṛtarasāsvādanimittakakāṭavajihāsāhetukatvamapi nimbadalayorjagajjananīvadanendulagnatāyāmutprekṣyate | tathā’pīyamuktasvarūpotprekṣāyā aṅgatayā nibaddheti na prādhānyamasyāḥ | yatra hyutprekṣādvayamaṅgāṅgibhāvena nibadhyate tatra pradhānabhūtayaivotprekṣayā vyapadeśo nyāyya ityavocāma, tanna prasmartavyam |
yathāvā
svayamapi muktābhikhyāṃ labdhuṃ radanāvalicchalādvajrāḥ | jāne mukharucijaladhau jātā mātastavāsyaśuktipuṭe || 471 ||
muktā ityabhikhyāṃ abhidhānaṃ śobhāṃ ca | atra radanāvalinihnavena vajratādātmyotprekṣāyāḥ muktābhikhyāṃ labdhumiti phalotprekṣā mukharucijaladhāvityādirūpakaṃ cāṅgatayā nibaddhe ||
yathāvā
brahmādipāvayitryāḥ pādajatā nocitā mameti tava | vadane smitamiṣato’bhūnmanye gaṅgā bhujaṅgaśikharīndo || 472 ||
brahmādeḥ brāhmaṇādivarṇasya caturmukhādikasya ca | pāvayitryāḥ mama pādajatā śūdratā caraṇajanyatā ca nociteti gaṅgā smitamiṣataḥ tava vadane abhūt ajani | bhagavanmukhajanyatve brāhmaṇyasiddhayā pādajatāprayuktasaṃkoco’payātīti bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
anukūlanagabhido’mī kanakādrau sarvato visarpantaḥ | tārakajayiśarajātā na nirjharāḥ kiṃtu nirjarā nūnam || 473 ||
anukūlaṃ kūle | vibhaktyarthe’vyayībhāvaḥ | ye nagāḥ taravaḥ tān bhindanti unmūlayantīti tathoktāḥ | pade anukūlaḥ nagabhit vajrī yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ | kanakādrau śeṣaśaile sumerau ca| sarvataḥ visarpantaḥ pravahantaḥ saṃcarantaśca | tārakāṇyuḍūni jayantīti tathoktāni śarāṇāṃ kāśakusumānāṃ jātāni bṛndāni yeṣu te tathoktāḥ | pakṣe tārakamasuraviśeṣaṃ jayatīti tathoktaḥ śarajātaḥ ṣaṇmukhaḥ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ amī dṛśyamānāḥ vastuviśeṣāḥ nirjharāḥ vāripravāhāḥ na kiṃtu nirjarā eva nūnam | iyaṃ śleṣasaṃpāditasādhāraṇyā śuddhāpahnutigarbhā jātisvarūpotprekṣā |
yathāvā
aśrīnivāsanilayānasarvatīrthāśrayānavedamayān | anyānacalānahigiriravadhīrayatīva harininādamiṣāt || 474 ||
harīṇāṃ siṃhānāṃ ninādamiṣāt | iyaṃ sāpahnavakriyāsvarūpotprekṣā | iyaṃ paryastāpahnutigarbhā’pi saṃbhavatīti dīkṣitāḥ | yathā
sthāne’yameva viṣṇurjāne’khilabhuvanavardhanādinduḥ | indostu sphuṭamasmādadharatvaṃ sa hi pade’sya paricarati || 475 ||
akhilasya bhuvanasya salilasya na tu jaladhisalilamātrasyeti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe jagataḥ vardhanāt utsecanāt samedhanācca ayaṃ viṣṇureva induriti sthāne yuktaṃ jāne indostu asmāt viṣṇoḥ adharatvaṃ nihīnatvaṃ sphuṭam | hi yasmāt saḥ induḥ asya viṣṇoḥ pade caraṇe saptamyekavacanaṃ, tena akāre parataḥ prakṛtibhāvaśaṅkāyā nāvakāśaḥ | paricarati śuśrūṣate | tasmādadharatvaṃ vyaktamityarthaḥ | pakṣe asmāt vakṣyamāṇādetasmāddhetorityarthaḥ| adharatvaṃ dharāsaṃbandhavaidhuryaṃ sphuṭam | kuta ityatrāha sa iti | hi yasmāt saḥ induḥ asya pade viṣṇoḥ pade vyomnītyarthaḥ | paricarati paritassaṃcaratīti | atra prasiddhendau indutvāpahnavena sakalabhuvanābhivardhanānnimittādbhagavati tatsaṃbhāvanetīyaṃ paryastāpahnutigarbhā||
yathāvā
vibudhaikādhāraśśrīvṛṣagiriracyuta na meruriti manye | yadi vibudhaikādhāro merussyātsa hi na karburamayassyāt || 476 ||
vibudhānāṃ devānāmekeṣāmevādhāraḥ śivabhāgavatavatsamāsaḥ | pakṣe vibudhānāṃ mukhyādhāraḥ | meruḥ vibudhaikādhāro yadi syāt tadā saḥ karburamayaḥ rakṣomayaḥ na syāt suvarṇamaya iti tu vastusthitiḥ | ‘karburaṃ salile hemni karburaḥ pāparakṣasoḥ’ iti medinī | atra merau vibudhaikādhāratvanihnavena śrīvṛṣagirau tadutprekṣā paryastāpahnutigarbhā uttarārdhoktatarkarūpayuktipūrvā |
yathāvā
indurna dvijarājo dvijarājo vāha eva tava nūnam | sa hinasti viprayogina eṣa tu kamalākṣa vipramodakaraḥ || 477 ||
he kamalākṣa! induḥ dvijarājaḥ ‘tasmātsomarājāno brāhmaṇāḥ’ iti śrutyuktarītyā brāhmaṇānāṃ rājā na, kiṃtu tava vāha eva garutmāneva dvijarājaḥ ukto’rthaḥ | nūnam | atra hetuḥ sa ityādiḥ | saḥ induḥ viprāśca te yoginaśca tāneva hinasti ataḥ kathaṃ sa dvijarājo bhavediti bhāvaḥ | virahiṇo hinastīti vāstavārthaḥ | eṣaḥ tava vāhastu viprāṇāṃ modakaraḥ, pakṣe vīnāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ pramodakaraḥ tasya tadīśvaratvāt | ato’yameva dvijarāja iti bhāvaḥ | atrendau dvijarājatvāpahnavena garutmati tadutprekṣā sahetukaparyastāpahnutigarbhā ||
yathāvā
na sahasrāṃśurdyumaṇiḥ kaustubhamaṇimeva tarkaye dyumaṇim | sa pariṣkurute’nantaṃ divaiva naktaṃdivamayaṃ tu || 478||
saḥ sahasrāṃśuḥ anantaṃ antarikṣaṃ divaiva pariṣkurute | ayaṃ kaustubhamaṇistu anantaṃ antarikṣaṃ bhagavantamiti tu vāstavārthaḥ | naktaṃdivaṃ rātriṃdivaṃ pariṣkuruta iti hetūktiḥ | atra sahasrāṃśau dyumaṇitvāpahnavena kaustubhamaṇau tadutprekṣā pūrvavadeva sahetukaparyastāpahnutigarbhā | sarveṣvapyudāharaṇeṣu śleṣasaṃkīrṇatvam ||
hetūtprekṣāyāṃ sāpahnavatvaṃ yathā
kamalāstanasuhṛdayamiti nūnaṃ śṛṅgāriśekharo’ higirau | vasati sadā nityayuvā mṛṣaiva jagaduddidhīrṣayeti vacaḥ || 479 ||
atra bhagavataśśeṣādrinityanivāse jagaduddidhīrṣāyā hetutvamapahṛtya tatra kamalāstanasuhṛttvahetukatāyāssaṃbhāvanātparyastāpahnutigarbhā hetūtprekṣā ||
phalotprekṣāyāṃ sāpahnavatvaṃ yathā
śritakāmadhenutāṃ tvaṃ śrīveṃkaṭanātha sūcayitumeva| adhivatsaṃ śrīvatsaṃ dhatse nūnaṃ na tatpariṣkartum ||
śrīvatsaṃ śrīyuktaṃ tarṇakam | pakṣe tannāma lāñchanam | adhivatsaṃ vakṣasi | ‘uro vatsaṃ ca’ ityamaraḥ | atra śrīvatsadhāraṇaṃ prati phale vakṣaḥpariṣkāre phalatvanihnavapūrvakamāśritakāmadhenutvasūcanarūpaphalotprekṣā paryastāpahnutigarbhetyalaṃ dūradhāvanena ||
alaṃkārasarvasvakārādayastu ‘kvacitpadārthānvayavelāyāṃ sādṛśyābhidhānādupakrāntā’pyupamā vākyārthatātparyasāmarthyādutprekṣāyāṃ paryavasyati’ ityāhuḥ | tadanusṛtya prāgullekhālaṃkāraprakaraṇe ‘mṛgamadatilakati’ iti padye upakrāntā’pyupamā aucityādutprekṣāyāṃ paryavasyatītyavocāma || yathāvā
jaṅghe tava jalajekṣaṇa jagatīvijigīṣumanasijaniṣaṅgau | vapurupavanaprasāritataruṇimalāvaṇyakarikarāyete || 481 ||
he jalajekṣaṇa ! tava jaṅghe vapureva upavanaṃ tasmin prasāritau taruṇimalāvaṇye eva kariṇau tayoḥ karau tāvivācarata ityarthaḥ | atra karikarāyete ityatra upamānātkartussubantādācāre vihitena kyaṅā āmukhe upamāpratītāvapi vapurupavanetyādiviśeṣaṇanairarthakyādaucityenotprekṣāyāmeva paryavasānam ||
evamupamāvācakānāṃ nibhādiśabdānāṃ kalpabādipratyayānāṃ ca prayoge’pi sāmagryā aucityādutprekṣāyāmeva paryavasānaṃ bodhyam ||
yathā
naraharibhujāśikharaśikhānakhāṃśavo ditijahṛdayagā vyarucan | tadasumarudaśanasatvaravisṛmaraphaṇiphaṇaśikhāsrarasananibhāḥ || 482 ||
vāṅmādhuryādhyayanopasannapīyūṣavīcideśyamidam | vadanavidhūtsaṅgaluṭhajjyotsnāśiśukalpamamba tava hasitam || 483 ||
nābhīhradakṛtamajjanamadanagajodastahastadeśīyā | kaustubhabhānūtsaṅgacyutayamunābhā’cyutasya romālī || rasagaṅgādharakārastu kyaṅācārakvibādīnāmapyutprekṣāpratipādakatvamevābhāṇīt | tasyāpi paryavasāne utprekṣāpratipādakatva eva tātparyam | yadvā utprekṣāvācakā eva te syuḥ | yathoktaṃ kalpataro
kalpadeśīyadeśyādyāḥ spardhipratibhaṭādayaḥ |
nibhavadvādayaścāpi kvacitsaṃbhāvanāparāḥ ||
iti | vistarastu haṃsasaṃdeśarasāsvādinyāṃ ‘viṣṇorvāsāt’ iti padyavyākhyāvasare kṛtosmābhiḥ | evaṃ rūpakādipratītisthaleṣvapyutprekṣāyāṃ paryavasānaṃ saṃbhavatīti citramīmāṃsāyāṃ pratipāditam | tadudāharaṇāni vistarabhayānneha pradarśyante ityalam ||
nanu gamyotprekṣāyā alaṃkāratākathanamanucitaṃ, tasyā alaṃkāradhvanitvāditi cechrūyatāṃ yatra viṣayaviṣayinimitteṣūpātteṣu kevalamivādivācakānupādānamātreṇotprekṣā’vagamyate sā’timātrasphuṭatayā vācyāyamānatvādalaṃkāratāmevāśnute | yathodāhriyata ‘īpsitasarā nikāmaṃ’ ityādi | yatra cotprekṣaṇīyasya viṣayiṇopyanupādānaṃ nibaddhena vastunā alaṃkāreṇa vā utprekṣā’vagamyate tatraivotprekṣādhvaniḥ ||
yathā
upagūḍhāṃ hariṇīṃ śriyamurasā kṛṣṇena vīkṣya vṛṣaśaile | abhivīkṣante śaśvatkṛṣṇaṃ nijavallabhaṃ vanahariṇyaḥ || 485 ||
kṛṣṇena bhagavatā urasā upagūḍhāṃ hariṇī ‘hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīm’ iti haritavarṇatayā śrutāṃ haritaśabdāt ‘varṇādanudāttāt’ ityādinā ṅīpsānniyogaśiṣṭastakārasya nakārādeśaḥ, ṇatvaṃ ca ‘hariṇī syānmṛgī hemapratimā haritā ca yā’ ityamaraḥ | śriyaṃ lakṣmīṃ vīkṣya vanahariṇyaḥ vipinakuraṅgyaḥ | kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇasāramityarthaḥ | ‘vinā’pi pratyayaṃ pūrvottarapadayorlopaḥ’ iti bhīmasenādiśabdavaduttarapadabhūtasāraśabdasya lopaḥ| ata eva ‘kṛṣṇājinena saṃvṛṇvan’ ityādayaḥ prayogā nirvicikitsāḥ | nijavallabhaṃ īkṣante | śrīriva vayamapi hariṇīśabdavācyāḥ bhagavānivāsmadvallabhopi kṛṣṇaśabdābhilapanīya iti paśyantīti bhāvaḥ | atra hariṇīkartukakṛṣṇasārakarmakavīkṣaṇena vastunā śrīkṛṣṇavakṣassamāliṅgitā hariṇīśabdābhilāpyā śrīriva svayamapi tathā bubhūṣava ivetyutprekṣā dhvanyate ||
yathāvā
praṇinaṃsayā sumanasā mauliṣu namiteṣu sumanasastatsthāḥ | pura eva tatopyapatannuragadharādharaśiromaṇeścaraṇe || 486 ||
sumanasāṃ devānām | tatsthāḥ maulisthāḥ sumanasaḥ kusumāni | atra sumanaḥpatanena vastunā nāmasāmyaprayuktaspardhayā tridaśamaulibhyopyagre svayameva praṇinaṃsava ivetyutprekṣā dhvanyate|
nirvelabhavadavānalakharvetaratāpavilulitā manujāḥ| tvadvadanasudhājalanidhimudvīkṣante muhurmurārāte || 487 ||
atra rūpakeṇa nimaṅktumivetyutprekṣā dhvanyate || yathāvā
