ativele bhavajaladhāvarivijayāyāvitīryamāṇapathe | raghukulavīra taṭasthaṃ vīkṣante vānarā iva narāstvām || 488 ||
ativele bhavajaladhau pūrvaṃ jaladhāviveti bhāvaḥ | arīṇāṃ kāmādīnāṃ dviṣāṃ daśānanādīnāmiveti bhāvaḥ | vijayāya avitīryamāṇaḥ adīyamānaḥ panthāḥ mārgaḥ upāyaśca yena tathokte sati, taṭasthaṃ tīrasthaṃ udāsīnaṃ ca tvāṃ vānarā iva narāḥ paśyanti kuto’yamamuṃ bhavajaladhimapi śaraiśśoṣayituṃ setunā banddhuṃ vā nodyuṅkta iti vīkṣanta iti bhāvaḥ | ata eva raghukulavīreti saṃbodhanam | atra rūpakojjīvitayā upamayā jaladhimiva bhavajaladhimapi śuśoṣa iṣava iveti vā bibandhayiṣava iveti vā utprekṣā dhvanyate ||
yathāvā
udvelataraṃ sutarāmutsikte tava yaśaḥpayorāśau | uragadharādharabhāsvannuḍupaṃ kṣaṇamapi na muñcati jyotsnā || 489 ||
jyotsnā kaumudī uḍupaṃ indumeva plavaṃ na muñcati striyo hi nitarāṃ bhīrava iti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi rūpakeṇa nimajjanabhayenevetyutprekṣāyā dhvaniḥ ||
nanu adhyavasāyamūlatāyā atiśayoktyutprekṣayostulyatvātkathamanayorbheda iti cet śṛṇv adhyavasāyo nāmābhinnatvena pratibhāsanam | sa ca dvividhaḥ siddhassādhyaśceti | yatra lakṣyamāṇaguṇasaṃbandhādviṣayiṇo viṣayagatatvenāropyamāṇatayā bhinnasyāpi viṣayiṇo viṣayakroḍīkāreṇa pravṛttatvādabhinnatayā pratītiḥ tatrādhyavasāyassiddhaḥ | etadāśrayeṇātiśayokteḥ prathamaḥ prakāro’nupadameva pradarśayiṣyate | yatra punarupāttaviṣayatvādviṣayiṇo bhinnatvena pratibhānaṃ tatra vyañjanavyāpāreṇābhinnatvapratītessādhyamānatvātsādhyaḥ | etadavalambanena pravṛttissaṃbhāvanamūho vitarka ityādyaparaparyāyāyā utprekṣāyā ityanayorbhada iti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre utprekṣāsaraścaturdaśaḥ
<16>
athātiśayoktisaraḥ
nigīrya viṣayaṃ yatra viṣayyevādhyavasyate |
rūpakātiśayoktiṃ tāmalaṃkāravido viduḥ ||
āropaviṣayasya mukhāderviśiṣya tadvācakapadopādānena vinā’pi viṣayivācakaiścandrādipadaireva grahaṇaṃ viṣayanigaraṇam | tatpūrvakaṃ viṣayasya viṣayirūpatayā’dhyavasānaṃ yatra tatra rūpakātiśayoktiḥ | viṣayirūpatayetyatra rūpaṃ ca abhedatādrūpyānyatarat | evaṃ ca anupāttaviṣayadharmikāhāryaniścayaviṣayībhūtaṃ viṣayyabhedatādrūpyānyataradrūpakātiśayoktiriti lakṣaṇaṃ bodhyam | rūpakavāraṇāyānupātteti| tatra hi viṣayo mukhādirupāttaḥ | ayameva hi rūpakādasyāṃ viśeṣo’tiśaya ucyate | bhrāntimadvāraṇāyāhāryeti | utprekṣāvāraṇāya niścayeti | atra ca viṣayaviṣayiṇorekapadopāttatvānnoddeśyavidheyabhāvassaṃbhavati | vibhinnapadopāttayoreva tadavatārāt || yathā
timiranirāsapaṭīyānkamalānāmāvahanmahāmodam | kardamamardayatitamāṃ durdamamambhojabāndhavo dyutimān || 489 ||
atra hi ambhojabāndhavatvena kaustubhasya nigaraṇe timiranirāsetyādipādadvayagate dve viśeṣaṇe tadanugrahārthaṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇossādhāraṇadharmatayā sākṣādupātte | tṛtīyamapi tṛtīyacaraṇagatamaśābdikānāṃ naye | śābdikānāṃ tūpāttayā ambhojabāndhavābhinnakartṛkayā kardamamardanakriyayonnītaṃ tādṛśakartṛtvaṃ sādhāraṇadharmatvena tayorviṣayaviṣayiṇossthitam | prathame caraṇe ajñānasya timiratvena, dvitīye jananayanānāṃ kamalatayā, tṛtīye duritasya kardamatvena ca nigaraṇaṃ tasminnevānugrāhakatayā | evaṃca sāvayaveyamatiśayoktiḥ ||
yathā vā
timirādhaḥkṛtaminduṃ kamalayugaṃ tatra satatasaṃphullam | kalayantī bhāti ghane māyā devasya kā’pyavijñeyā || 490 ||
atra timirendukamalaghanamāyārūpairviṣayibhiścikuravadananayanabhagavacchriyo viṣayā nigīrṇāḥ | atra ‘janakasya kule jātā devamāyeva nirmitā’ ityetadanusandheyam | māyā nāma vicitrakāryakarī śaktiḥ |
yatra hyanugrāhakaṃ na nigaraṇāntaraṃ kiṃtu śuddhaṃ sādhāraṇadharmādi sā niravayavā || yathā
madhurān sudhāpravāhānvidhuśiśirānabudhajanaduravagāhān | pramuṣitatāpavyūhan pravigāhya jahāmi sakalasaṃmohān || 491 ||
abudhajanaiḥ ajñajanaiḥ devetaraiśva duravagāhān, atra śrīnivāsadayāvibhavā nigīrṇāḥ | prathamodāharaṇe ambhojabāndhavapadena sādhyavasānalakṣaṇayā śakyalakṣyobhayānugatatimiranirāsapaṭīyastvādyupāttadharmapuraskāreṇopasthite kaustubhe śaktyupasthitasyāmbhojabāndhavatvaviśiṣṭasyābhedasaṃsargeṇānvayaḥ | śaktyupasthitayoḥ kṛtīṣṭasādhanatayoriva śaktilakṣaṇābhyāmupasthitayorapyekapadārthayostātparyavaśādanvayābhyupagame bādhakavirahāt | evaṃ ca ambhojabāndhavābhinnaḥ timiranirāsapaṭīyastvādimāniti bodhādiyamabhedātiśayoktirityucyate | evamanantarodāharaṇayorapi | nanvevaṃ sati kimasya rūpakādvailakṣaṇyamiti cenmaivam | rūpake viṣayibhedavyāpyasya viṣayatāvacchedakasya bhānena vailakṣaṇyasya spaṣṭatvāt | yadā tu nābhedabhāne tātparye kiṃtu bhedabhāne tadā tādrūpyasyaiva bodhāttādrūpyātiśayoktirvakṣyate | atra lakṣaṇe viṣayaṃ nigīrya viṣayyevādhyavasyata iti sāmānyata evokteśśuddhasādhyavasānalakṣaṇayā abhedopacāre’pyatiśayoktireva ||
tatra dharme dharmyabhedādhyavasāyo yathā
sakalajagadagragaṇyaṃ sarasijanayanasya pariṇataṃ puṇyam | asmādṛśāṃ śaraṇyaṃ vismayanīyaṃ vibhātu lāvaṇyam || 492 || atra lāvaṇyāśraye bhagavatyāṃ lakṣmyāṃ lāvaṇyāropaḥ | candro’yamityādau sādṛśyasyādhyavasānanimittatvādgauṇatvam | āśrayatvādisaṃbandhasya nimittatve tu śuddhatvamiti vivekaḥ | ‘bhedāvimau ca sādṛśyātsaṃbandhāntaratastathā | gauṇau śuddhau ca vijñeyau’ iti kāvyaprakāśokteḥ | imau bhedau sāropasādhyavasānarūpabhedāvityarthaḥ | tasmātsādṛśyātiriktasaṃbandhena yatrānyasminnanyatādātmyāropaḥ sā śuddhasādhyavasāneti dhyeyam ||
kārye kāraṇābhedādhyavasānaṃ yathā
vacanarasadhutamarandaṃ vapurasitimnā’vadhīritamilindam | vandārudevavṛndaṃ vinatānandaṃ kamapyahamavindam || 493 ||
atra vinatānandarūpakārye tatkāraṇabhagavadabhedādhyavasāyaḥ | ‘hetuhetumatoraikyaṃ hetuṃ kecitpracakṣate ‘ ityuktalakṣaṇahetvalaṃkāraviśeṣasya tu śuddhasāropalakṣaṇāmūlakatvenopāttaviṣayakaṃ ‘lakṣmīvilāsā viduṣāṃ kaṭākṣā veṅkaṭaprabhoḥ’ ityādikaṃ viṣaya iti nānenaiva kāryakāraṇābhedarūpaśuddhasādhyavasānalakṣaṇāmūlakena gatārthatā ||
sāropā tatra yatroktau viṣayī viṣayastathā |
sāropā dvividhā seyaṃ gauṇī śuddheti bhedataḥ ||
sādṛśyātmakasaṃbandharūpā gauṇītyudīryate |
śuddhā tadanyasaṃbandharūpā sā tu nigadyate ||
mukhendurnayanānandastanvyā vijayate tarām |
nigīrya viṣayaṃ yatra viṣayyeva nibadhyate ||
tatra sādhyavasānā syādgauṇī śuddheti sā dvidhā |
sarojakāhalīrambhāsaikatākāśabhūdharaiḥ ||
śaṅkhendutimiraissṛṣṭā vallī kāmijanotsavaḥ ||
iti kāvyadarpaṇe sodāharaṇe sāropasādhyavasānalakṣaṇe pradarśite | anupadapadyodāhṛtakāvyaprakāśakārikārthasphuṭapratipattaye kāvyadarpaṇalakṣaṇodāharaṇam | etena pratyāsattirasau rasasya jayati trasyatkuraṅgekṣaṇā’ ityalaṃkārakaustubhakṛto viśveśvarasya kāryakāraṇābhedādhyavasānalakṣaṇātiśayokterudāharaṇamacaturaśraṃ veditavyam | tatra trasyatkuraṅgekṣaṇeti viṣayasyopādānenātiśayokterviṣayanigaraṇapradhānāyā aviṣayatvāduktarītyā hetvalaṃkārapārthakyāvaśyaṃbhāvena tadudāharaṇatāyā evāsya yuktatayā udāharaṇāntarapradarśanasyaivocitatvāt | ‘abhedenābhidhāheturhetorhetumatā saha’ iti sāhityadarpaṇoktahetvalaṃkāralakṣaṇaṃ ca ‘hetuhetumatoraikyaṃ hetum’ iti pradarśitacandrāvalokalakṣaṇāvisaṃvādyeva abhedena abhedāropeṇa abhidhā uktiriti tadarthāt | yatpunaḥ
tāruṇyasya vilāsassamadhikalāvaṇyasaṃpado hāsaḥ |
dharaṇitalasyābharaṇaṃ yuvajanamanaso vaśīkaraṇam ||
iti tadīyamudāharaṇaṃ, tattu ‘lakṣmīvilāsā viduṣām’ ityādisaṃpratipannodāharaṇavisaṃvāditayā’nādartavyam | atra vaśīkaraṇaheturnāyikāvaśīkaraṇatvenokteti vivṛtatayā sādhyavasānalakṣaṇāmūlakātiśayoktyudāharaṇārhatvāttasya | kaustubhakārastu “abhedenābhidhāhetuḥ” iti sāhityadarpaṇoktahetvalaṃkārasyātiśayoktāvevāntarbhāvaḥ” ityabhāṇīt ||
sāhityadarpaṇokteti hetvalaṃkāraṃ viśiṣato’syāyaṃ bhāvaḥ ‘yuvajanamanaso vaśīkaraṇam’ iti tadīyodāharaṇe sādhyavasānalakṣaṇāmūlakahetuhetumadabhedadarśanena tallakṣaṇasya tathāvidhahetuhetumadabhedatatparatāvagamāttādṛśahetvalaṃkārasyātiśayoktāvevāntarbhāvaḥ na tu sāropalakṣaṇāmūlakahetuhetumadabhedasyāpīti || ullekhādīnāmapyatraivātiśayoktau ktaustubhakṛtā’ntarbhāvanaṃ tu nātīva ramaṇīyamiti svayameva vidāṃ kurvantu sahṛdayā iti viramyate vistarabhayāt ||
mālārūpā’pīyaṃ dṛśyate yathā
saurājyaṃ jagatīnāṃ saumaṅgalyaṃ suparvasudatīnām | saubhāgyamahigiripatessaulabhyaṃ hanta nayanayorbhajate || 494 ||
atra saurājyādirūpakāryeṣu tatkāraṇabhagavadabhedādhyavasāyaḥ ||
atrātiśayoktau rūpakaviśeṣaṇaṃ rūpake pradarśitāḥ prakārā atrāpi saṃbhavantīti sādṛśyena pradarśanārtham | mukhyarūpakābhedasyātiśayoktāvasaṃbhavādrūpakapadaṃ ‘māsamagnihotraṃ juhoti’ ityatrāgnihotrapadavattaddharmātideśena tatsadṛśaparam | tenātrāpyabhedātiśayoktistādrūpyātiśayoktiriti vidhādvayaṃ tatrāpyādhikyanyūnatānubhayavibhāgaścetyetatsarvaṃ vibhāvanīyam ||
tatrādhikābhedarūpakātiśayoktiryathā
añjanagiriśikharollasadañjanamadbhutamidaṃ nṛṇāṃ jātu| sañjitamiha locanayorvyañjayati sadā svameva nidhim || 495 || atra kadācillocanāsañjitātsvātiriktanidhivyañjakātprasiddhāñjanādañjanābhedādhyavasitasya kadācillocanagocarīkṛtasya bhagavatassatatamapi svābhinnanidhivyañjakatokterādhikyaṃ draṣṭavyam ||
yathā ca
jāmbūnadādriśṛṅge jambūradbhutatameyamudbhūtā | na phalapratyayalopo yasyāḥ paridṛśyate kadācidapi |
jāmbūnadādreḥ śeṣagireḥ śṛṅge adbhutatamā iyaṃ jambūḥ jambūtvenādhyavasito bhagavāniti bhāvaḥ | udbhūtā avatīrṇā | adbhutatamatvamevāha netyādi | yasyāḥ jambvāḥ phalaiḥ pratyayaḥ prathitatvaṃ tasya lopaḥ phalavattāprasiddhayabhāva ityarthaḥ ||
pratyayaḥ prathitatve’pi sanādijñānayorapi|
ācāre śapathe randhre viśvāsādhīnahetuṣu ||
iti medinī | bhagavatpakṣe tu phalapratyayasya tattadabhīpsitaprayojanasiddhiviṣayakaviśvāsasyetyarthaḥ | lopaḥ kadācidapi na paridṛśyate | prasiddhajambvāḥ kadācitphalaprasiddhilopassaṃbhavedapi asyāstu na tathā | etadāśritānāmīpsitaṃ phalaṃ sidhyati vā na veti viśayena viśvāsalopaḥ kadācidapi na bhavediti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe jambūḥ jambūśabdaḥ arthagatasya strītvasya śabde āropaḥ | adbhutatamā | tadevāha neti | yasyāḥ pañcamyekavacanamidam | phalapratyayalopaḥ kadācidapi na paridṛśyate | jambūśabdātphalarūpe’rthe vaikārike aṇi ‘jambvā vā’ iti vaikalpike lupyanuśiṣṭe’pi asyāḥ jambūśabdavyakteḥ kadācidapi phalapratyayalopaḥ phalārthavihitāṇpratyayalopaḥ na dṛśyata ityadbhutatvamiti bhāvaḥ | vāstavārthastūkta eva | atra prasiddhajambvapekṣayā prasiddhajambūśabdāpekṣayā ca jambūtvenādhyavasitasya bhagavata uktarītyā’’dhikyaṃ śleṣamūlakaṃ draṣṭavyam | śabdaparārthāntaraṃ camatkārātiśayāya ||
nyūnābhedarūpakātiśayoktiryathā
sauśīlyamukhyaśubhaguṇarāśīnbhavato hare śrutiśatoktān | avidantaḥ paśavo’mī vinadantyacatuṣpadā vṛthā dikṣu || 497 ||
atra paśvabhedenādhyavasitānāmacatuṣpadatvokternyūnatā || anubhayābhedarūpakātiśayoktistu ‘timiranirāsapaṭīyān, madhurānsudhāpravāhān’ iti prāgudāhṛtapadyadvaye draṣṭavyā ||
adhikatādrapyarūpakātiśayoktiryathā
nīrandhraguṇaprotaṃ sphāraṃ viśadayati nijarucibhiramṛtam | anyadidamāsitaratnaṃ dhanyaṃ māṃ vidadhadahiśikharikhanijam || 498 ||
nīrandhraiḥ sāndraiḥ guṇaiḥ sarvajñatvādibhiḥ protaṃ saṃvalitam | anyatra nīrandhraṃ suṣiraṃvirahitaṃ tathā’pi guṇena tantunā protaṃ syūtaṃ ceti viśeṣaṇobhayapadakarmadhārayaḥ | ahiśikharikhanijaṃ na tu siṃhalākarajaṃ anyat lokavilakṣaṇaṃ idaṃ asitaratnaṃ mahānīlaratnaṃ tattvenādhyavasitaśśrīnivāsa iti yāvat | māṃ dhanyaṃ kṛtārthe dhanaṃ labdhāraṃ ca vidadhatsat nijarucibhiḥ svaviṣayakaprītiviśeṣaiḥ anyatra svaprabhābhiḥ | amṛtaṃ svānubhavarūpaṃ muktyaiśvaryaṃ viśadayati vivṛṇute | pakṣe amṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ viśadayati pāṇḍuraṃ karoti, na tu nīlam | atra yamevaiṣa vṛṇute tena labhyasta yaiṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanūṃ svām’ iti śrutyartho’nusaṃdheyaḥ | tathāhi ‘eṣa paramatmā yaṃ sādhakaṃ prārthayate tena labhyaḥ | prārthanīyena puṃsā labhya ityarthaḥ | tatprārthanīyatvaṃ ca tatpriyatamasyaiva puṃsaḥ | tatpriyatamatvaṃ ca tatprītimata eva | tataśca bhagavadviṣayiṇī upāsakasya prītiḥ bhagavata upāsake prītimutpādya tatprāptiheturbhavati | tasyopāsakasya eṣa ātmā paramātmā svāṃ tanuṃ svarūpaṃ vivṛṇute prakāśayati svānubhavaṃ prayacchati’ ityabhāṣi | vṛṇuta iti pāṭhe’pi sa evārthaḥ | atra anyadityanena nirjñātanīlaratnādvailakṣa ṇyaṃ nīrandhretyādinā nīrandhraguṇaprotatvāhiśikharikhanijatvāmṛtaviśadīkaraṇaissarandhraguṇaprotātsiṃhalākarajāt kṣīranīlatāniṣpādakāttasmādādhikyaṃ ca darśitam |
evam
kānapi bhaje vilāsānkaṇṭhīravabhūdharendrakṛtavāsān | lakṣmīkaṭākṣadāsān lalitaśaraccandracandrikāhāsān || 499 ||
ityādāvapyadhikatādrūpyarūpakātiśayoktirdraṣṭavyā ||
nyūnatādrūpyarūpakātiśayoktiryathā
pannagasannaganetussannayavinnairudīritaṃ nāma | vācā’pi nābhidadhato nīcāḥ ke’mī piśācakā dṛśyāḥ || 500 ||
sannayo vedāntaśāstraṃ vinnaḥ vicārito yaiḥ tathoktaiḥ ‘radābhyām’ iti niṣṭhātasya pūrvasya dasya ca natvaṃ ‘vinnavittau vicārite’ ityamaraḥ | āhitāgnayāderākṛtigaṇatvānniṣṭhāyāḥ paranipātaḥ | nāmāpi vācetyanvayaḥ | atra ke’mī piśacakā ityanirjñātatvaprakāśanānnirjñātapiśācakebhyo vailakṣaṇyaṃ, dṛśyā ityanenādṛśyebhyastebhyo nyūnatā ca darśite ||
anubhayatādrūpyarūpakātiśayoktiryathā
śitikaṇṭhakṛtaniṣevaṇamavikuṇṭhasparśanaprathitavibhavam | kamapi ghanaṃ phaṇiśikhariṇi kalayāmi ghanāgamāntasaṃlakṣyam || 501 ||
śitikaṇṭhena mahādevena mayūraiśca kṛtaniṣevaṇaṃ avikuṇṭhaṃ nirargalaṃ sparśanaṃ vitaraṇaṃ tena, pakṣe apratihatena mārutena ‘sparśano mārute puṃsi dāne sparśe napuṃsakam’ iti medinī | prathitavibhavaṃ, ghanāḥ ye āgamāntāḥ upaniṣadaḥ taiḥ saṃlakṣyaṃ jñeyaṃ teṣu pratipādyaṃ vā | ‘taṃ tvaupaniṣadam’ iti śruteḥ | pakṣe ghanāgamasya prāvṛṣaḥ ante madhye saṃlakṣyaṃ dṛśyam | kamapi ghanaṃ kalayāmi ||
yathāvā
sumanaḥkadambatānteśśamanaṃ tapanodbhavoṣmabhayadamanam | kamapi ghanāghanamenaṃ kalayannujjīvanaṃ labhe nūnam || 502 ||
sumanasāṃ mālatīnāṃ kadambānāṃ nīpānāṃ ca pakṣe devanivahānāṃ ‘sumanāḥ puṣpamālatyoḥ,’ ‘kadambaṃ nikurumbe syānnīpasiddhārthayoḥ pumān’ iti ca medinī | tānte glāneḥ śamanam | tapanāt bhānoḥ udbhavatītyudbhavaḥ pacādyac | ya ūṣmā tasmāt bhayaṃ tasya damanaṃ grīṣmātapoṣmapraśamathitāramityarthaḥ | pakṣe tapano narakaviśeṣaḥ ‘tapanā grīṣmabhallātanarakāntarabhāskarāḥ’ iti ratnamālā | tasmāt udbhavo ya ūṣmā tāpaḥ tadbhayasya śamanam | yadvā tapyate aneneti tapanaḥ tāpahetuḥ uddhataścāsau bhavassaṃsāraḥ tasmin ye ūṣmāṇaḥ ādhyātmikādayastāpāḥ tadbhayaśamanamityarthaḥ | kamapi ghanāghanaṃ śrīnivāsamiti yāvat | kalayan sevamānaḥ ujjīvanaṃ utkṛṣṭaṃ salilaṃ, pakṣe uttaraṇamiti yāvat| labheya nūnamiti saṃbhāvanāyām | atra ‘svojjīvanecchā yadi te’ ityādikaṃ smartavyam | udāharaṇadvaye’pi kamapītyanena lokasiddhaghanādvailakṣaṇyaṃ pradarśitaṃ na tvādhikyaṃ nyūnatā vetyanubhayatādrūpyarūpakātiśayoktiriyaṃ śleṣojjīvitā ||
idaṃ sarvaṃ kuvalayānandādyanurodhena | jagannāthastu ‘viṣayivācakapadasya viṣaye sādhyavasānalakṣaṇāyāśśakyatāvacchedakamātraprakārakalakṣyaviśeṣyakavodhatvaṃ kāryatāvacchedakam | evaṃca nigaraṇe sarvatra viṣayitāvacchedakadharmarūpeṇaiva viṣayasya bhānaṃ na viṣayyabhinnatveneti sthite abhedātiśayoktistādrūpyātiśayoktiriti dvaividhyamayuktam’ ityabhāṇīt | atra vaidyanāthaḥ ‘idaṃ prauḍhivilasitaṃ, śakyatāvacchedakasya lakṣye pūrvamapratītatvena tadviśiṣṭatayā lakṣaṇāyā asaṃbhavāt | yaddharmaviśiṣṭe śakyasaṃbandhagrahastaddharmaprakārakalakṣyopasthitessamānaprakārakaśābdabodhe hetutvāt, lakṣaṇāparihāryāyā anupapattestadavasthatvācca | evamapi tātparyavaśāttādṛśabodhāṅgīkāre tadvaśādeva śakyābhedaprakārakabodhe’pi bādhakābhāvānmātrapadena viṣayatāvacchedakasyaiva vyāvartanāt ‘ ityavādīt || nanu ‘añjanagiriśikharollasat’ ityādyuktodāharaṇeṣu idamiti viṣayasyopādānātkathamatiśayoktiriti cecchrūyatām idaṃ tvasya viṣayiviśeṣaṇatvena vivakṣāyāmatiśayoktiḥ | viṣayāviśeṣaṇatvavivakṣāyāṃ tu rūpakameva | evaṃ gaurayamityādāvapi | ata evātiśayoktāvabhedo’nuvādya eva na vidheya iti prācāmuktissaṃgacchate | ata eva ca prakāśakṛtā ‘nayanānandadāyīndorbimbametatprasīdati’ ityatiśayoktirūpakasaṃkarasyodāharaṇaṃ ca yoyujyata iti dik |
sāpahnavātiśayoktiḥ
uktā sāpahnavā saiva yadyapahnutigarbhitā ||
saiva rūpakātiśayoktireva apahnutigarbhitā cetsāpahnavātiśayoktiruktā bhavati | tathāca sāpahnavatvanirapahnavatvabhedenātiśayoktirdvividheti dhyeyam ||
yathā
adhiphaṇidharaṇidharāgraṃ nidhirakṣayyo’khilopajīvyosti| dhanadāvasathe nidhiriti manute sarvo’pi kiyadidaṃ maugdhyam || 503 ||
atra ādhiphaṇidharaṇidharāgraṃ nidhirityatiśayoktiḥ dhanadāvasathastho na nidhirityapahnutigarbhā | nihavo’tra maugdhyopanyāsādarthasiddhaḥ ||
yathāvā
mṛgapatinagaśikharalagajjagadāndhyaṃ harati khalu jagaccakṣuḥ | andhānkaticana racayannindhāṃ na ravirbhavejjagaccakṣuḥ ||504||
jagatāmāndhyamajñānamiti yāvat | katicana divāndhādīn | indhāṃ dīpyatām| “ñi indhī dīptau” | kartari loṭprathamaikavacanam | atra mṛgapatinagaśikharalagajjagaccakṣurityatiśayektiḥ na ravirjagaccakṣuriti paryastāpahnutigarbhā ||
yathāvā
gārutmatamidameva hi gārutmataśailaśirasi yadratnam | ajñāstadgārutmatamāhuḥ prathate yadaśmagarbha iti || 505 ||
garutmato’yaṃ gārutmataḥ sa cāsau śailaḥ śeṣādrireva ‘vedādrirgaruḍādriśca’ iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt | tasya śirasi śikhare yadratnaṃ bhagavāniti yāvat | gārutmatagiriśikharabhavatvādidameva gārutmataratnaṃ, ajñāstu aśmā garbho janmasthānaṃ yasya saḥ aśmagarbhaḥ kevalaprastarajanya iti yadratnaṃ prathate tadgārutmatamāhuḥ | kevalāśmajanyasya gārutmataratnatāmabhidadhānā mūḍhā evetyarthaḥ | atra gārutmate taddharmanihnavena bhagavati tadadhyavasānādapahnutigarbhā’tiśayoktiḥ | ajñā āhurityanena nihnavasyārthikatvam | atrādye udāharaṇe kevalāpahnutigarbhatvaṃ, itarayostu jagaccakṣuṣṭvagārutmatatvanihnave yuktipradarśanātsahetukaparyastāpahnutigarbhatvamiti dhyeyam | evaṃca sāpahnavātiśayoktāvapi dvaividhyamabhyupagantavyam |
bhedakātiśayoktiryatprastutasyānyatocyate || prastutasyānyatvaṃ abhede’pi bhedaḥ sa yatra lokottaratvapratipattyartho varṇyate sā bhedakātiśayoktirityarthaḥ | abhede bhedavarṇanaṃ cāhāryaṃ jñeyam | evamuttaratrāpi | tena bhrāntau nātiprasaṅgaḥ ||
yathā
anyadidaṃ tava maugdhyaṃ kanye’bdheranyadeva vaidagdhyam | yena jagajjaniheturnūnaṃ krītaḥ phaṇīndragiriketuḥ || 506 ||
maugdhyaṃ saundaryaṃ ‘udayadyauvanā mugdhā lajjāvijitamanmathā’ ityuktalakṣaṇāvasthāviśeṣo vā, vaidagdhyaṃ cāturyam ||
yathāvā
amṛtamayī kācidiyaṃ sṛṣṭiryastvadguṇāngṛṇāti janaḥ | na prākṛtajanagandhaṃ sahate jātvapi yadṛcchayā’pyeṣaḥ || 507 ||
yaḥ janaḥ tvadbhaṇan gṛṇāti stauti, iyaṃ amṛtamayī niśśreyasamayī kācitsṛṣṭiḥ | iyamiti vidheyasṛṣṭiśabdānurodhinī strīliṅgatā | ata eva eṣa tvadguṇastotā janaḥ jātvapi yadṛcchayā’pi prākṛtāḥ prakṛtipariṇāmabhūtāḥ pakṣe pāmarāḥ ye janāḥ ‘vivarṇaḥ pāmaro nīcaḥ prākṛtaśca pṛthagjanaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | teṣāṃ gandhaṃ saṃbandhaṃ na sahate | iyaṃ prākṛtī cetsṛṣṭiḥ kathaṃ prākṛtajanagandhaṃ na saheta ato manye naiṣā prākṛtī sṛṣṭiḥ kiṃtvanyaivāmṛtamayīti bhāvaḥ | atra prasiddhaprākṛtasṛṣṭyantargatasyāpi bhagavadupaślokitṛjanasya tadbhedo varṇitaḥ | yathāvā
anyā manaso vṛttirmānyānāṃ deva tāvakīnānām | āhaṃkārikatāṃ yat kvacidapi nopāśnute kathaṃcidapi || 508 ||
āhaṃkārikatāṃ sātvikāhaṃkārapariṇāmatāṃ, pakṣe ahaṃkāramarhatītyāhaṃkārikaṃ tasya bhāvaṃ ‘tadarhati’ iti ṭhak | ahaṃyutvamityarthaḥ | atrāhaṃkārikasyāpi bhāgavatamanasastadbhedo varṇeitaḥ | ādyā śuddhā | anye śleṣasaṃkīrṇe ||
asaṃbandhe’pi saṃbandho varṇotkṛṣṭyai yadīryate |
saṃbandhātiśayoktiṃ tāmalaṃkāravido jaguḥ ||
saṃbandhaścābhedabhinnatve satyanyatvādyanirūpito grāhyaḥ | tena rūpakātiśayoktyādiprabhedeṣu nātiprasaṅgaḥ |
yathā
rucirataranijatanūrucinicayaprācuryamecakitabhuvanam | caladalakavilasadalikaṃ kalayāmyanilāśanādripatitilakam || 509 ||
atrākhilabhuvanasya śrīnivāsadivyavigraharucimecakitatvāsaṃbandhe’pi tatsaṃbandhoktiḥ ||
yathāvā
vayamapi katipayadivasaiḥ payasijanayanāṅghridhūlidhutajaḍimāḥ | strītvaṃ sphuṭayema svakamityahigiripatiśilāḥ pramodante || 510 || jaḍimā acetanatvamityarthaḥ | dhutajaḍimā ityatra ‘ḍābubhābhyām’ iti ḍāp | strītvaṃ sphuṭayema sāṃprataṃ svavācake śabde śāstramātrasamadhigamyamāste strītvaṃ, itaḥparaṃ bhagavaccaraṇareṇuvyatikaravinirdhūtaprastarabhāvāḥ ‘stanakeśavatī nārī’ ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ laukikastrītvaṃ cārthagataṃ krameṇa prakāśayema gautamaśāpādgrāvabhāvabhāgahalyeveti bhāvaḥ | atrāhigiriśilānāmuktavidhapramodāsaṃbandhe’pi tatsaṃbandhakalpanaṃ varṇyotkarṣārtham ||
yathā vā
ravinibhakaustubhatejasi visarati paritastavāntike namatām | dyusadāṃ kirīṭamaṇayo hare parāḥ kīṭamaṇaya evāsan || 511 ||
he hare! parāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ kirīṭamaṇayaḥ kīṭamaṇayaḥ khadyotā evāsan ravinibhakaustubhatejasi khadyotavattirohitaruco’bhavannityarthaḥ | ‘khadyote syātkīṭamaṇiryotirmīlī tamomaṇiḥ’ ityabhidhānacintāmaṇipariśiṣṭe tiryakkāṇḍe | pakṣe aparā iti chedaḥ apagatarephāḥ kirīṭamaṇayaḥ kīṭamaṇayaḥ iti niṣpannā ityarthaḥ | rephottaramakāroccāraṇaṃ mukhasukhārthaṃ, repharūpavyañjanamātrāpasāraṇe kakāro ttaravartina ikārasya rephottaraśrutasya īkārasya ca savarṇadīrgha tathātvasya darśanāditi bhāvaḥ | atra kirīṭamaṇīnāṃ kīṭamaṇitvāsaṃbandhe’pi tatsaṃbandhakalpanam | arthāntaraṃ tu camatkārātiśayāya ||
yoge’pyayogo’saṃbandhātiśayoktirudīryate||
saṃbandhe’pyasaṃbandhakalpanamasaṃbandhātiśayoktirityarthaḥ | yathā
darvīkaragiriśekharasarveśvarasevanena dhanyatamāḥ | apunarbhavāya kecinnipuṇāḥ spṛhayanti no kadācidapi || 512 ||
apunarbhavāya niśśreyasāya | atra mokṣaspṛhāsaṃbandhe’pi tadasabandhavarṇanam ||
yathā vā
sārohaśramamahigiripāroditamenamarcayantu narāḥ | avarohaśramavarjitamavarodhaissamamihārcayantyamarāḥ || 513 ||
atrāvarohaśramasaṃbandhe’pi tadasaṃbandhavarṇanam ||
hetukāryasahatve syātseyamakramapūrvikā ||
kāryakāraṇayostulyakālatve seyamatiśayoktirakramaśabdapūrvikā akramātiśayoktissyāt ||
yathā
saṃdadhati cāpamārāddviṣatāṃ nayanāni saṃdadhati cāpam | añjanagiripatipāṇau gāṃ jahati ca te’pi gā jahati || 514 ||
añjanagiripatipāṇau cāpaṃ kārmukaṃ saṃdadhati adhijyaṃ kurvati sati dviṣatāṃ nayanāni cāpaṃ dhanussaṃdadhati | pakṣe ca āpamiti chedaḥ| nayanāni ca āpaṃ apāṃ samūhaṃ saṃdadhati | atyāhitena bāṣpaṃ muñcantītyarthaḥ | tasmin bhagavatpāṇau gāṃ bāṇaṃ jahati sati te dviṣanto’pi gāṃ bāṇaṃ, pakṣe bhuvaṃ ca jahati tyajantītyarthaḥ | saṃdadhati jahatītyatra saṃpūrvakāddadhāterjahāteśca laṭprathamapuruṣabahuvacanam | ‘svargeṣupaśuvāgvajradiṅnetraghṛṇibhūjale | lakṣyadṛṣṭayā striyāṃ puṃsi gauḥ’ ityamaraḥ | atra yadā dhanuṣassajjīkaraṇaṃ tadaiva dviṣatāṃ bāṣpamocanaṃ, yadā bāṇatyāgastadā kṣoṇītyāga iti hetukāryayossahatvaṃ varṇitam |
yathā vā
saṃbhramataścalati purassaṃbhramataścalati bhavati ripunivahaḥ | cakraṃ tyajati ca cakraṃ tyajati mṛgendrācalendramaulimaṇe || 515 ||
bhavati tvayi saṃbhramataḥ ripunirāse tvarayā atyādareṇa vā puraḥ agrataḥ calati sati ripunivaho’pi saṃbhramataḥ ukto’rthaḥ, pakṣe āvegādbhayādvā ‘saṃbhramastvarā’ ityabhara, ‘saṃbhramo’tyādare bhītāvāvege’ iti ratnamālā ca | puraḥ agrataḥ svanagarācca calati | tvayi cakraṃ sudarśanaṃ tyajati prayuñjāne sati ripunivahopi cakraṃ sudarśanaṃ rāṣṭraṃ ca tyajati muñcati lokāntaraṃ gacchatīti bhāvaḥ | ‘cakraṃ caye jalāvarte rathāṅge rāṣṭrasainyayoḥ’ iti ratnamālā | atra yadā bhagavato ripunigrahārthaṃ puraścalanaṃ tadaiva ripūṇāṃ nagaratyajanaṃ, yadā sudarśanaprayogastadā rāṣṭratyāga iti kāryakāraṇayoryaugapadyamabhihitam ||
yathāvā
kaṅkaṭakaṃ viśati tvayi veṅkaṭagirinātha vairivijayāya | kaṃ kaṭakaṃ praviśāma svayamiti ripavo’ pi saṃbhramantitarām || 516 ||
he veṅkaṭagirinātha! tvayi kaṅkaṭakaṃ kavacaṃ ‘uraśchadaḥ kaṅkaṭakaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | viśati sati ripavopi kaṅkaṭakaṃ pakṣe kaṃ kaṭakaṃ girinitambaṃ praviśemeti saṃbhramantitarām | ‘kaṭakostrī nitambo’dreḥ’ ityamaraḥ | atrāpi kavacapraveśagirinitambapraveśayorgaugapadyaṃ darśitam ||
hetuprasaktimātreṇa kāryaṃ cedvinibadhyate capalātiśayoktassā nipuṇairvinigadyate ||
hetvabhāve’pi hetuprasaktimātreṇa kāryavarṇanaṃ capalātiśayoktirityarthaḥ | prasaktirjñānam |
yathā
kāścana sapadmamālā līlā viharanti padmavanalolāḥ | āvāseṣu tadaiṣāṃ śrīvāsasiṣeviṣā yadā yeṣām || 517 ||
atra śrīnivāsasevābhāve’pi tadicchāmātreṇa bhaktajanāvāseṣu śrīvihārarūpakāryaṃ varṇitam | atra śrīrlīlātvenādhyavasyate||
yathāvā
tvayi jāgaraṃ vivikṣati jātu raṇenotsuke murārāte | jāgaramāviśati sadā raṇaraṇakena svayaṃ dviṣannivahaḥ || 518 || he murārāte ! tvaṃ jātu kadācit raṇenosuke samare udyukte ‘prasitotsukābhyāṃ tṛtīyā ca’ iti raṇaśabdāttṛtīyā | ata eva jāgaraṃ kavacaṃ ‘jāgaraḥ kavacostriyām’ ityamaraḥ | vivikṣati praveṣṭumicchati na tu praviṣṭe sati dviṣannivahaḥ raṇaraṇakena bhayasaṃbhrameṇa sadā na tu kadācit jāgaraṃ kavacaṃ nidrākṣayaṃ ca viśati prāpnoti | atra kavacasannaddhatvarūpahetvabhāve’pi tadicchāmātreṇa ripūṇāṃ nidrākṣayarūpakāryaṃ varṇitam ||
atrodāharaṇadvaye’pi prasiddhaśrīnivāsasevanayuddhasannaddhatvādikāraṇavirahe’pi siṣeviṣugṛhādhikaraṇakaśrīviharaṇadviṣatkartṛkajāgaraṇaprāptirūpakāryodayavarnādvibhāvanālaṃkāreṇaiva camatkṛterne capalātiśayoktirityalaṃkārāntaramabhyupeyam, ‘apyalākṣārasāsiktaṃ raktaṃ tvaccaraṇadvayam ‘ iti lākṣārasāsecanarūpakāraṇavirahe’pi raktimalakṣaṇakāryodayavarṇanalakṣaṇavibhāvanāto vailakṣaṇyābhāvāt | ata eva naināṃ vyavajahrire prāñcaḥ | dīkṣitāstu vibhāvanāyāṃ kāraṇābhāvo vācyaḥ,atra kāraṇaprasaktikathanena kāraṇābhāvo gamya ityetāvanmātreṇa vicchittiviśeṣasadbhāvamanusaṃdhāyālaṃkārāntaramāhuḥ ||
hetoḥ kāryasya ca syāccetpaurvāparyaviparyayaḥ |
atyantātiśayoktiṃ tāmāhuḥ kāvyavicakṣaṇāḥ ||
yathā
enāṃsi davīyāṃsi śreyāṃsi ca purata eva bhūyāṃsi | paścānnṛṇāṃ rasajñā veṅkaṭanāthābhidhāmṛtarasajñā ||519 || yathāvā
udriktarajāstāpassarvapathīnaḥ praśāmyati puraiva | varṣati kāmaṃ paścādghanassa kuṣṇo vṛṣādriśikharasthaḥ || 520 ||
sarvapathānvyāpnoti sarvapathīnaḥ ‘tatsarvādeḥ pathi’ ityādinā vyāpnotītyarthe khaḥ | sarvatomukha ityarthaḥ | udriktaṃ rajaḥ guṇaviśeṣaḥ pāṃsuśca yasmin saḥ tathābhūtaḥ tāpaḥ ādhyātmikādiḥ graiṣmaśca puraiva śāmyati | paścāt vṛṣādriśikharasthaḥ ghanaḥ sakalakalyāṇaguṇasāndraḥ kṛṣṇo bhagavān pakṣe kṛṣṇaḥ śyāmalaḥ ghanaḥ meghaḥ kāmaṃ varṣati īpsitārthaṃ pradatte | pakṣe paryāptaṃ yathā syāttathā varṣati siñcatītyarthaḥ||
yathāvā
prathamatarameva tamasāṃ valayaṃ vilayaṃ prayāti vinatānām | paścātteṣu prasarati parisphuranpadminīsakhālokaḥ || 521 ||
tamasāṃ ajñānānāṃ timirāṇāṃ ca | padminī śrīḥ kamalinī ca tasyāssakhā bhagavān bhānuśca tasya ālokaḥ kaṭākṣaḥ prakāśaśca | ‘padminī kariṇīstrīśrīsaro’bjanalinīṣu ca’ iti ratnamālā | udāharaṇatraye bhagavannāmoccāraṇavarṣaṇapadminīsakhālokaprasaraṇānāṃ kāraṇānāṃ paścādbhāvaḥ enodūrībhavanatāpaśamanatamovilayanānāṃ kāryāṇāṃ prāthamyaṃ ca varṇitamiti lakṣaṇānugatiḥ | ādyā śuddhā, dvitīyatṛtīye tu śleṣasaṃkīrṇe iti viśeṣaḥ | etāstisro’pyatiśayoktayaḥ kāryaśaighryapratyāyanārthāḥ|| athokteṣu prabhedeṣu anugatapravṛttinimittavirahātkathamatiśayoktipadavācyatvamiti cedatrāhuḥ tāvatprabhedānyatamatvameva sarvānugatamatiśayoktipadapravṛttinimittaṃ tadeva ca sāmānyalakṣaṇamiti prāñcaḥ | anye tu saṃbandhe asaṃbandhaḥ asaṃbandhe saṃbandha iti bhedadvayaṃ nātiśayoktiḥ īdṛśātiśayasya rūpakadīpakopamāpahnutyādiṣu svabhāvoktibhinneṣu prāyeṇa sarveṣvalaṃkāreṣu sadbhāvāt | na hi yathā’vasthitavastukathane’sti kācidvicchittiḥ kāryakāraṇapaurvāparyaviparyayasyāpi tenaiva vyāptatayā bhedāntaratānāpateśca | tasmādviṣayiṇā nigīryādhyavasānaṃ viṣayasya tasyaivānyatvaṃ yadyādiśabdairasaṃbhavino’rthasya kalpanaṃ kāryakāraṇapaurvāparyaviparyayaścetyetadanyatamatvamatiśayoktitvamityāhuḥ | na vyāstu upamānenopameyasya nigīryādhyavasānamevātiśayoktiḥ | prakārāntare tvatiriktālaṃkārāntarakalpanamevocitam | na hyetaccatuṣṭayāsādhāraṇātiśayoktilakṣaṇaṃ saṃbhavi, yenaikadharmāvacchinnatvenaikālaṃkāratocyeta | na caitadanyatamatvameva sarvānugatamastīti vācyam | vicchittivailakṣaṇyasadbhāve’nyatamatvasyāprayojakatvāt | anyathā upamānarūpakādikatipayānyatamatvaṃ sakalānyamatvaṃ vā’tiśayoktilakṣaṇaṃ vidhāya upamādīnāmapyetadbhedāpādanasya suvacatvāt | na cātiriktālaṃkārakalpane gauravamiti vācyaṃ, pradhānotkarṣakatvarūpasyālaṃkāratvasya tvayā’pyaṅgīkārāt | padārthāntarasya ca mayā’pyakalpanāt | alaṃkāravibhājakopādhiparigaṇanasya ca pauruṣeyatvāditi vadanti ||
vastutastu rūpakabhinnatve sati camatkṛtijanakāhāryāropaniścayaviṣayatvamevātiśayoktisāmānyalakṣaṇam | rūpakavāraṇāya satyantaṃ, bhrāntivāraṇāyāhāryeti | utprekṣānirāsāya niścayeti | rūpakātiśayoktāvabhedasya dvitīye prabhede’nyatvasya tṛtīye saṃbandhasya caturthe asaṃbandhasya pañcame sahatvasya ṣaṣṭhe hetuprasaktijanyatvasya saptame pūrvāparatvayośca tathāvidhāropaviṣayatvasya sadbhāvātsarvatra lakṣaṇānugatiḥ | na caivaṃvidhāropasya rūpakasvabhāvoktivyatirikteṣvalaṃkāreṣu prāyaśassattvādatiprasaṅga iti vācyaṃ iṣṭāpatteḥ | camatkṛtau pradhānabhūtasyālaṃkārāntarasyāṅgatayā’tiśayokteravasthāne’pi tathā vyapadeṣṭumanarhatvāt | pradhānabhūtālaṃkārasyaiva vyapadeśārhatvācca | ata eva kāvyaprakāśakṛtā viśeṣālaṃkāraprastāve “sarvatraivaṃvidhe viṣaye’tiśayoktireva prāṇatvenāvatiṣṭhate, tāṃ vinā prāyeṇālaṃkāratvābhāvāt | ata evoktaṃ
saiṣā sarvatra vakroktiranayā’rtho vibhāvyate |
yatnosyāṃ kavibhiḥ kāryaḥ ko’laṃkāro’nayā vinā || iti ||
daṇḍinā’pyuktam
alaṃkārāntarāṇāmapyekamāhuḥ parāyaṇam |
vāgīśamahitāmuktimimāmatiśayāhvayām || iti ||
ityabhihitamityapi vadanti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre’tiśayoktisaraḥ pañcadaśaḥ
<17>
atha tulyayogitāsaraḥ
varṇānāmeva vā’nyeṣāmeva vā dharma ekakaḥ |
anvito varṇyate yatra tatra syāttulyayogitā ||
vāśabdo vyavasthitavikalpārthaḥ | tathāca prakṛtānāmeva vā aprakṛtānāmeva vā yatraikadharmānvayitvaṃ sā tulyayogitā, tulyānāṃ prākaraṇikāprākaraṇikānyataramātranibandhanasādṛśyavatāṃ yogitā ekadharmānvayitvaṃ yatreti vyutpatteḥ | varṇyānāmityādau bahutvaṃ dvayorapyupalakṣaṇam | dharmaścātra camatkārītiṃ jñeyam | evaṃca anekaprastutamātrasaṃbaddhaikacamatkāridharmānekāprastutamātrasaṃbaddhaikadharmānyataratvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ bodhyam | ‘mukhaṃ vikasitasmitaṃ’ ityādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāyāneketi | tatra ca mukhaprekṣitādirūpānekavarṇyasaṃbaddho naiko dharma iti tannirāsaḥ | dīpakavāraṇāya mātreti | prastutāprastutaprabhedasādhāraṇyāyānyataratvaniveśaḥ | dharmaścātra prāyaśo guṇakriyārūpa eva saṃbhavatīti prastutānāmaprastutānāṃ vā guṇakriyārūpakadharmānvayastulyayogitetyuktaṃ kuvalayānande | tadetadupalakṣaṇaṃ dharmamātrasya | etaccodāharaṇāvasare sphuṭībhaviṣyati| aupamyaṃ cātra gamyaṃ, tatprayojakasya samānadharmasyopādānādvācakābhāvācca | ata evālaṃkārikāṇāmapi sādṛśyaṃ padārthāntaraṃ, na tu sādhāraṇadharmarūpamiti vijñāyate | anyathā aupamyasyātra gamyatvokteranupapatteḥ | kecittu sādṛśyabhāva evātiriktaḥ | sādṛśyaṃ tu tattatsādhāraṇadharmātmakameva | sa cevādipadānāṃ śakyatāvacchedakaḥ tattatsādhāraṇadharmavācakaistu tattatsādhāraṇadharmasya svaśakyatāvacchedakatattaddharmarūpeṇa bodhane’pi sādaśyabhāvarūpeṇa bodho vyañjanasādhya evetyāpi vadanti ||
yathā
aśrūdañcati cakṣuṣi vapuṣi ca pulako’tha saṃbhramo manasi | añjanabhūdharadhāmannañjalirapi mastake tava smaratām || 522 ||
atra bhāgavatānāṃ varṇyatvāttadīyatvena prakṛtānāṃ kartṝṇāmaśruprabhṛtīnāmudañcanakriyā samānadharmatvenopāttā | cakṣurādīnāmadhikaraṇānāṃ ca | kārakāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ kriyānvayitvasya tulyatvāt | evamaṣṭānāṃ kriyarūpadharmaikye’pi dvayordūyorevaupamyaṃ pratīyate, na parasparamaṣṭānām | tadadhikaraṇatvatatkartṛtvarūpe viśeṣe sāmānyasya paryavasānāt | tatra smaratāmityatra ‘adhīgartha’ iti ṣaṣṭhī ||
caraṇe’mbujamadaharaṇe soḍhanatainasi madamba tava manasi | vacane ca madhuraracane mṛdutā lokottarā vibhātitarām || 523 ||
atra mṛduteti guṇaḥ tadanvitatvaṃ caraṇādīnāṃ, vibhānakriyāyāssākṣāddharmibhirananvayāt ||
yathāvā
yenāśrito dhavalimā viparīto’kāri dhavalimā yena | tasmin śaṅkhe cakre’pyahiśailapatermaho jvalati kimapi || 524 ||
yena śaṅkhena dhavalimā pāṇḍurimā āśritaḥ, yena cakreṇa dhavalimā viparītaḥ vyatyastaḥ akāri dhavalimā parāsta iti yāvat | ityāpātato virodhaḥ | dhavalimā ityānupūrvyā vaiparītye mālivadha iti niṣpadyate | prakṛtamātrasyāpi mālivadhetyasya subantatā kalpanīyā | ‘na kevalā prakṛtiḥ prayoktavyā’ iti niṣedho’pi kaciccitrakāvye na doṣāya | yathoktaṃ kāvyārthacintāmaṇau
prātilomyādicitreṣu prakṛtereva hi kvacit |
prayogo’pi na doṣāya nadī dīno yathocyate ||
iti | yena cakreṇa mālināmno rākṣasasya vadhaḥ akārīti tu vāstavārthaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyaṃ na vismartavyam | ahiśailapateḥ śaṅkhe cakre’pi maha iti yojanā | atra maha iti guṇarūpaikadharmānvayitvaṃ śaṅkhacakrayoḥ | yadi ca ‘te pāñcajanyacakre dīpayataśśeṣaśailanāthakarau’ ityuttarārdhaṃ paṭhyate tadā kriyārūpo dharmaḥ | yadica ‘te pāñcajanyacakre dīptiṃ dhatto’dbhutāṃ vṛṣādripateḥ’ ityucyate tadā guṇaviśiṣṭā kriyā, kevalaguṇena sākṣātsaṃbandhābhāvāt | kevalakriyāyāścāhṛdyatvāt | atra dvayoreva dharmānvayaḥ ||
vadanaṃ nayanayugaṃ vā hṛdayaṃ vā nāprasannamamba tava | karayugalamadharabimbaṃ caraṇayugaṃ vā’pi bhavati nānaruṇam || 525 ||
atra nāprasannaṃ nānaruṇamityabhāvarūpe dharme prastutānāṃ vadanādīnāṃ karayugalādīnāṃ cānvayaḥ | ata eva lakṣaṇe sāmānyato dharma ityeva niveśitaṃ na tu guṇakriyārūpaviśeṣitatayā | idaṃ ca rasagaṅgādharakṛdanurodhena | sa hi ‘prastutānāmaprastutanāṃ vā guṇakriyārūpaikadharmānvayastulyayogitā’ iti sarvasva kuvalayānandoktiṃ dūṣayan ‘guṇakriyārūpetyuktirāpātataḥ
śāsati tvayi he rājannakhaṇḍāvanimaṇḍalam |
na manāgapi niścinte maṇḍale śatrumitrayoḥ ||
ityatrābhāvarūpasyaiva dharmasyānvayāt ityabhāṇīt | anye tu ‘śāsati’ iti padye niścinte neti niścintabhedaśśatrumitramaṇḍaladharmatayopātto guṇasvarūpa eva, cintābhāvavadbhedasya cintānatiriktatvāt | anyathā cintābhāvābhāvasyāpyatiriktatvāpatteḥ | atha tatrāpīṣṭāpattāvapi abhāvasya kathaṃguṇabahirbhāvaḥ jātikriyādravyātiriktasyaiva ‘catuṣṭayī śabdānāṃ pravṛttiḥ’ iti vadadbhiśśābdikaistadanuyāyibhirālaṃkārikaiśca guṇatvābhyupagamādityāhuḥ | ata eva jātiguṇayorvirodhe prakāśakṛtā ‘girayopyanunnatiyujaḥ’ iti, vidyānāthenāpi ‘amadassārvabhaumopi’ iti codāhṛtaṃ saṃgacchate | anunnatīyāderabhāvasyāguṇatve tadudāharaṇāsaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva | tasmādabhāvasyāpi guṇatvāddharmasya guṇakriyārūpatoktāvapyadoṣa eva | evaṃcāsminnudāharaṇe’pi nāprasannamityādau guṇarūpa eva dharma iti dhyeyam | vastutastu lakṣaṇe tena rūpeṇa dharmo niveśito’smābhiriti na kopi doṣaḥ ||
adhiphaṇigirimaṇinīradamacañcalāsīdramā ca vibhutā ca| kṣamayāvipulāsīttanmānasavṛttiśca tasya karuṇā ca || 526 ||
phaṇigirimaṇirbhagavān nīrada iva tasmin adhiphaṇigirimaṇinīradaṃ, vibhaktyarthe’vyayībhāvaḥ | ramā acañcalāsīt cañcalevācārīt, vidyudivābhādityarthaḥ | cañcalāśabdādācārakvipi liṅ , aḍāgamaḥ | vibhutā aiśvaryaṃ ca acañcalā niścalā āsīt abhavat |’asa bhuvi’ ityasmāllaṅ | ‘ ‘astisico’pṛkte ‘ itīṭ, ‘śnasoḥ’ ityallopasyābhīyatvenāsiddhatvādāṭ | tasya bhagavato mānasavṛttiḥ kṣamayā kṣāntyā avipulāsīt vipulevācārīt bhūtulyā’’sīdityarthaḥ | vipulāśabdādācārakvipi luṅ pūrvavat | pakṣe vipuleti chedaḥ | tasya karuṇā ca vipulā mahatī āsīt | śrīśrīnivāsasyaiśvaryakṣamākṛpāḥ śrīlakṣmīnivāsanibandhanā iti bhāvaḥ | atrobhayasādhāraṇayorguṇākriyayorabhāvācchabdamātraṃ śleṣamūlenābhedādhyavasānena sapiṇḍito’rtho vā dharmaḥ || yathā vā
dantālī grīvā’pi ca muktālyā nāsitā tava murāre | nāpramadaṃ jātucidapi vakṣaścetaśca vārijākṣa tava || 527 ||
he murāre ! tava dantālī muktālyā iti chedaḥ , muktāhārāpekṣayā nāsitā naśabdena samāsaḥ asitā na bhavati kiṃtu sitā dhavalaivetyarthaḥ | grīvā’pi ca muktālyeti tṛtīyaikavacanaṃ, muktāhāreṇa asitā asaṃdānitā na bhavati kiṃtu saṃdānitaiva ‘baddhe saṃdānitaṃ mūtaṃ mudditaṃ saṃditaṃ sitam’ ityamaraḥ | he vārijākṣa ! tava vakṣaḥ cetaśca apramadaṃ avidyamānamāninīkaṃ avidyamānalakṣmīkamityarthaḥ, avidyamānapraharṣaṃ ca na, kiṃtu salakṣmīkameva saharṣameveti bhāvaḥ | atra sitatvasapramadatvaniṣedhasaṃbhāvanā’pi nañdvayena vyudasyate | yathoktaṃ vāmanena ‘saṃbhāvyaniṣedhānevartane dvau pratiṣedhau’ iti | atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇavadeva dharmaḥ, tasyābhāvarūpatvaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ ||
kvacidadhyāhṛtopi dharmavācakaśśabdastulyayogitāṃ prayojayati | yathā
divyatamaṃ rūpaṃ tava bhavyatamā śrīriyaṃ ca vanamālā | stavyatamo maṇiracyuta bhāvyaṃ viśayena kiṃ tava paratve || 528 ||
tava paratve viśayena saṃśayena bhāvyaṃ kiṃ saṃdeho na bhavatyevetyarthaḥ | atra paratvasaṃśayakṣepakaraṇatayā prastutānāṃ divyarūpādīnāmastīti kriyāyāmanvayaḥ | ‘astirbhavantiparoprayujyamānopyasti’ ityanuśāsanāt | bhavantiriti laṭassaṃjñā | ‘bhuvo jhic’ ityauṇādiko bhavaterjhicpratyayaḥ | ‘jho’ntaḥ ‘ iti jhasyāntādeśaḥ | āstekriyāyā’ aprayoge’pi tadākṣepaśśīghropasthitikatvāt | tarhi prativākyamapi kutonādhyāhriyatāmiti cenna | lāghavādekatrādhyāhṛtasyaivānyatrānuṣañjanīyatāyā nyāyyatvāt | viśveśvarīyālaṃkārakaustubhe īdṛśamudāharaṇaṃ pradarśitam ||
aprakṛtānāmeva yathā
mānavavargo nikhilo dānavavargaśca namati nantavyam | ahirājagirikhiśāmaṇimatra kathaṃ namadabhedavārtā syāt || 529 ||
nantavyaṃ ‘nantavyaḥ paramaśśeṣī’ ityuktarītyā sarvaśeṣitayā namikarmīkaraṇārhaṃ ahirājagiriśikhāmaṇiṃ nikhilo mānavavargo dānavavargaśca namati | atra namatāṃ mānavānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca varge abhedavārtā kathaṃ syāt, namane availakṣaṇye’pi mānavānāṃ dāna vānāṃ ca parasparaṃ vailakṣaṇyāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā atra nantavye bhagavati abhedavārtā namadbhissahābhedakathā kathaṃ syāt | nantṛnantavyayo śeṣaśeṣiṇorabhedāyogāditi bhāvaḥ | athavā atra namatormānavadānavavargayoḥ madabhedavārtā mado nāma kulaśīlādyabhimānenānyāvadhīraṇahetuścittavikāraviśeṣaḥ yo hyariṣaḍvarge gaṇitaḥ | tasya bhedavārtā vidāraṇavṛttāntaḥ kathaṃ na syāt bhagavatpraṇantṝṇāṃ kāmādinikhiladoṣadūrībhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe atra mā navadānavavargaśabdayoḥ madabhedavārtā makāradakārayorvailakṣaṇyakathā kathaṃ na syāt | tāvanmātrabhedasya dṛśyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāprakṛtayormānavadānavavargayornamanakriyārūpadharmaikyam || yathā vā
yatsādhumanīṣāyāṃ pararūpaṃ sphurati taddhi mārtaṇḍe’pi | āha sadākṛtigaṇyaṃ paramekaṃ śrutiravaimi śābdoktiśca || 530 ||
yat parasya brahmaṇo rūpaṃ sādhūnāṃ yogināṃ manīṣāyāṃ buddhau skurati,
eṣa sarveṣu bhūteṣu gūḍhotmā na prakāśate |
dṛśyate tvagryayā buddhyā sūkṣmayā sūkṣmadarśibhiḥ ||
iti śrutyuktarītyā antaryāmivigrahāvaśiṣṭaṃ svarūpaṃ sūkṣmadarśibhissūkṣmayā buddhyā aikāgryasaṃpannayā dṛśyata ityabhiprāyaḥ | taddhi tadeva ‘hi hetāvavadhāraṇe’ ityamaraḥ | pararūpaṃ parasya brahmaṇo rūpaṃ ‘ya eṣo’ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣo dṛśyate ‘ ityādi śrutaṃ divyamaṅgalavigrahaviśiṣṭaṃ svarūpaṃ mārtaṇḍe āditye’pi sphurati dīpyate | kathamanayoraikyaṃ tvayā viditamityatrāha āheti | śrutiḥ ‘sa yaścāyaṃ puruṣe | yaścāsāvāditye | sa ekaḥ ‘ iti śrutiḥ sadākṛtigaṇyaṃ satyā ākṛtyā vigraheṇa gaṇyaṃ praśasyamiti yāvat | divyamaṅgalavigrahaviśiṣṭamityarthaḥ | paraṃ paramātmasvarūpaṃ ubhayatra vidyamānamiti bhāvaḥ | ekaṃ abhinnaṃ āha avaimi tasmājjāne, vākyārthaḥ karma | śabdoktiścemamevārthamāha tathāhi, pakṣe yat sādhu samyaganuśiṣṭaṃ pararūpaṃ pūrvavarṇasya paravarṇatāpattirūpa ekādeśa ityarthaḥ | manīṣāyāṃ manīṣāśabde sphurati prakāśate | tatra manasa īṣeti vigrahe nakārānantarabhāvinaḥ as ityasya īkārarūpaparavarṇarūpasaṃpatteḥ sphuṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | taddhi tadeva pararūpaṃ mārtaṇḍe mārtaṇḍaśabde’pi sphurati | mṛte aṇḍe jāto mārtaṇḍa ityatra taddhitaprakṛtibhūtamṛtaṇḍaśabde takārottaravartyakārasya tatparabhūtākārarūpaikādeśadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | paraṃ anayośśabdayormadhye paratra paṭhitaṃ mārtaṇḍaśabdaṃ ekaṃ ekameva | na tu manīṣāśabdamapi, tasya vakṣyamāṇagaṇe pāṭhadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | sarvadā ākṛtigaṇyaṃ ākṛtyā pararūpavattvākāreṇa gaṇyaṃ śakandhvādigaṇaṃ labdhāraṃ ‘dhanagaṇaṃ labdhā’ iti yat | labdheti tṛnnantena lābhatācchīlyaṃ dyotitam | uktagaṇe paṭhitumarhamiti yāvat | śābdoktiḥ śabdaśāstrīyaṃ ‘śakandhvādiṣu pararūpaṃ vācyam’ iti vārtikamityarthaḥ | āha vadatīti avaimi saṃbhāvaye | manīṣāśabdavanmārtaṇḍaśabdasya gaṇe pāṭhābhāvāduktapararūpavattvākāreṇāyaṃ śakandhvādigaṇe paṭhanīya iti sabhāvaye | anyadhā pararūpavidhāyakaśāstrāntarādarśanādayamasādhutāmaśnuvīteti bhāvaḥ | ata eva śakandhvādigaṇapāṭhānte ‘ākṛtigaṇo’yaṃ mārtaṇḍaḥ’ ityuktiśca saṃgacchate | śakandhvādiśabdarūpaniṣpattyanuguṇaṃ teṣu śabdeṣu ṭeḥ pararūpaṃ vācyamiti vārtikārthaḥ | atrāprakṛtayoḥ śrutiśābdoktyorāheti kriyāyāmanvayaḥ| śleṣopaskṛteyam ||
yathā vā
suravaramāṇikyaṃ nanu suravaragāṇikyamapi vṛṣādrimaṇe | sevāte tava lakṣmīṃ māgābhinne ime khalu samāne || 531 ||
nanu vṛṣādrimaṇe ! suravaramāṇikyaṃ indranīlaratnaṃ suravarāṇāṃ devottamānāṃ gāṇikyaṃ gaṇikāsamūhopi ‘gaṇikāyā yañiti vaktavyam’ iti gaṇikāśabdasamūhārthe yañ | ‘gaṇikādestu gāṇikyam’ ityamaraḥ | tava lakṣmīṃ śobhāṃ pakṣe śriyamityarthaḥ | tava lakṣmīmityanena ‘viṣṇośśrīranapāyinī’ ityādiprasiddhaṃ nityavyavasthitapatipārārthyaṃ vyañjitam | “ tavaivocitayā tava śriyā’ ityetadatrānusaṃhitam | sevāte bhajete | indranīlaprabhāyā api tava divyavigrahaśobhā sutarāmatiśete iti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe rambhādyapsaronivahaḥ kiṃkarībhūya tava divyamahiṣīṃ varivasyatīti bhāvaḥ| ime suravaramāṇikyasuravaragāṇikye bhinne parasparavilakṣaṇe mā gāḥ mā jñāsīḥ ‘ye gatyarthāste jñānārthakāḥ ‘| kutaḥ? ime samāne tāvakalakṣmīsevanarūpadharmavattayā anyonyaṃ tulye khalu | pakṣe ime suravaramāṇikyasuravaragāṇikyarūpe pade māgābhinne iti samastaṃ padaṃ, māgāvarṇābhyāmeva bhedavatī parasparavilakṣaṇe ityarthaḥ | samāne anyavarṇānupūrvyā tulye khalu | atrāpyaprakṛtayossu ravaramāṇikyasuravaragāṇikyayorbhagavallakṣmīkarmakasevanakriyāyāma nvayaḥ pūrvavat |
harirucikṛtaparibhavabharacintādanturanijāntaraṅgāṇām | niścetanatā’’virabhūdindīvarajaladharendranīlānām || 532 ||
atra guṇaḥ, āvirbhāvakriyāyāssākṣāddharmibhirananvayāt| ‘cintādanturanijāntaraṅgāṇi | sutarāṃ paryamlāsiṣuḥ’ iti dvitīyatṛtīyapādayoḥ ‘indīvajaladharendranīlāni’ iti turyapādasya ca kalpane kriyā | ‘dadhate niścetanatām’ iti tṛtīyapāde kṛte guṇaviśiṣṭā kriyā | evamuttaratra yathāsaṃbhavamūhyam ||
mahasā tavāsurāṇāṃ malīmase mānase ca vadane ca | yaduvīra nirmalāni tridaśamṛgadṛśāṃ diśāṃ ca vadanani || 533 ||
atra malīmasatvaṃ guṇaḥ pūrvārdhe | uttarārdhe nirmalānīti mālinyābhāvarūpo dharmaḥ keṣāṃcinmate | vastutastu sopi guṇa eva ||
yathā vā
bhūṣayati vṛṣagiriṃ tvayi surāsurāḥ kinnarā narā vā’pi| nābhaktijuṣo bhagavannāprāptamanorathāśca jātu syuḥ || 534 ||
atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇavadguṇa eva ||
yathā vā
ādau viruddhavarṇe parasparamathekṣaṇena te prāyaḥ| bhavato rame savarṇe sa cañcarīkaśca puṇḍarīkaṃ ca ||
he rame ! saḥ nīlavarṇatvena prasiddhaḥ cañcarīkaḥ bhramaraḥ puṇḍarīkaṃ sitāmbhojaṃ ca | ādau tvatkaṭākṣasānnidhyātpūrvaṃ parasparaṃ viruddhe varṇe yayoste tathokte asitasitavarṇatvādanyonyaviruddhavarṇe ityarthaḥ | ‘napuṃsakamanapuṃsakena’ ityādinā napuṃsakaliṅgaśeṣaḥ | atha anantaraṃ kadāciditi yāvat | te tava īkṣaṇena kaṭākṣavīkṣaṇena savarṇe samāno varṇo yayoste tathokte bhavataḥ tulyavarṇe saṃpadyete |kaṭākṣasya kālimnā dhavalimnā vā asitavarṇe sitavarṇe vā bhavata iti bhāvaḥ | ‘kaṭākṣāḥ kālindīkuvalayakacagrāhirucayaḥ, vadhukiraṇadhārāsahacaro kaṭākṣāṇāṃ rekhā’ ityādau kaṭākṣāṇāṃ dvaivarṇyasya kavisamayasiddhatvāt | prāya iti saṃbhāvanāyām | pakṣe cañcarīkapuṇḍarīkapade ādau caturvarṇātmakayossvayoḥ prathamabhāge parasparaṃ viruddhau varṇau akṣare yayoste tathābhūte bhacataḥ| ādibhāgasthayoḥ cañca puṇḍa ityanayoranyonyaviruddhākāratvāditi bhāvaḥ | atha varṇadvayānantaraṃ īkṣaṇe na iti chedaḥ | netyetaduttarānvayi | īkṣaṇe darśane sati samyagvimarśe satītyarthaḥ | rame asavarṇe iti chedaḥ | asminpakṣe te ityetatprathamādvivacanam | te cañcarīkapuṇḍarīke prāyaḥ bhūmnā na tu sarvātmanā, ante visargabindubhyāmasāvarṇyadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | asavarṇe na bhavataḥ, kiṃtu savarṇe eva | yadvā īkṣaṇenetei tṛtīyāntamabhinnapadamevāstu | rame savarṇe ityevāstu, tathāca darśanena vicāreṇa prāyaḥ savarṇe eva tulyavarṇānupūrvīke eva bhavata ityarthaḥ | rīketi varṇānupūrvyāstulyatvādubhayatreti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi guṇa eva, bhavanakriyāyāssākṣāddharmibhyāmananvayāt aprakṛtayoścañcarīkapuṇḍarīkayoruktena guṇenānvayaḥ | iyaṃ ca vakṣyamāṇatadguṇālaṃkārasaṃkīrṇā śabdaparārthavarṇanarūpacamatkāraviśeṣopaskṛteti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
bhuvanaṃ dadhadapyudare bhuvanāntarviśadapīha nityajaḍam | bhagavaṃstavopamāyāmabbhraṃ cendīvaraṃ ca bāhyamabhūt || 536||
atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇa iva guṇa eva | udarādhikaraṇakabhuvanadhāraṇabhuvanāntaḥpraveśābhyāṃ yathākramamabbhrendīvarayorbhagavatsāmyasaṃbhāvane’pi nityajaḍatvātatsāmye tayorbahiṣkṛtiriti bhāvaḥ | iyaṃ bhuvanajaḍaśabdaśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyanirvyūḍhā || avato jaganti bhagavanbhavato mahasā phaṇīndragiribhāsvan | dviṣatāṃ tanmahasāmapi nikurumbaṃ nijagṛhe’pyabhāsi khalu || 537 ||
bhavato mahasā tejasā dviṣatāṃ nikurumbaṃ nijagṛhe nigṛhītaṃ, nipūrvakādgraheḥ karmaṇi liṭ | ata eva abhāsi dviṣannigrahītṛtvādeva tena vyarocīti bhāvaḥ | bhāsaterbhāve luṅi ciṇ | apissamuccaye | tanmahasāṃ dviṣattejasāṃ nikurumbamapi nijagṛhe’pi svabhavane’pi bhāso bhāsanaṃ prakāśaḥ, bhāsaterghaña | so’syāstīti bhāsi tanna bhavatītyabhāsi | svagehe’pyaprakāśaṃ kimutānyatreti bhāvaḥ | khalviti prasiddhau | atrāprakṛtayordviṣattattejasossādhāraṇaguṇakriyābhāvācchabdamātraṃ śleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasānasaṃpiṇḍito’rtho vā dharmaḥ ||
yathāvā
vindanparamaghavattvaṃ tvayekṣito jana upekṣitaścāmba | śāmyati mahāmarāvatyudīrṇatṛṣṇo’bhyudūḍhalekhaśca || 538 ||
he amba ! tvayā īkṣitaḥ janaḥ paraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ ca tanmaghavattvaṃ indrattvaṃ, pakṣe paramiti vyastaṃ padam | atyantamiti tadarthaḥ | aghavattvaṃ duḥkhitvaṃ pāpitvaṃ vyasanitvaṃ vā ‘aṃho duḥkhavyasaneṣvagham’ ityamaraḥ | vindan, abhyudūḍhāḥ sarvāpadbhyassamuddhṛtāḥ lekhāḥ,devāḥ yena sa tathoktaḥ | pakṣe abhyudūḍhāḥ vahanakarmīkṛtāḥ lekhāḥ rājakīyapatrāṇi yena sa tathoktaḥ | lekhavāhaka ityarthaḥ | ‘lekho lekhye sure’ iti medinī | mahatī ca sā amarāvatī ca tasyāṃ udīrṇā tṛṣṇā uttarottarābhivardhanaspṛhā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | pakṣe mahati marau dhanvabhūmau ‘samānau marudhanvānau’ ityamaraḥ | atyudīrṇā atimātrapravṛddhā tṛṣṇā pipāsā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘tṛṣṇe spṛhāpipāse dve’ ityamaraḥ | śrāmyati ekatra devoddharaṇādivyāpāraiḥ anyatra lekhyavahanādibhiriti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva dharmo jñeyaḥ ||
iyaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvenāpi dṛśyate, yathā aśrūdañcati cakṣuṣi vapuṣi ca pulakaḥ’ ityādiprāgudāhṛtapadye cakṣurvapurādīnāṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam |
kecittu raśanārūpā’pīyaṃ dṛśyata iti vadanti |
sā yathā
mīnacakorakuraṅgāssaṃgāhante vanaṃ tvadakṣijitāḥ| vidrumapallavabimbānyapyamba tvadadharāvadhūtāni ||
vanamityatra yathārhamaraṇyamudakaṃ cetyartho grāhyaḥ | vidrumāṇāmabdhijanyatvādvanasthitiḥ | mīna iva cakoraścakora iva kuraṅga iti pratīteḥ raśanārūpateti dhyeyam | vastutastviyaṃ bahūnāmaprastutānāmekadharmānvayarūpaiva tulyayogitā | lakṣaṇe varṇyānāmanyeṣāṃ veti bahuvacananirdeśāt ||
yatra ca prakṛtānāmeva vā aprakṛtānāmeva vā kriyāṇāṃ ekakārakānvayassākārakatulyayogiteti jagannāthādayaḥ ||
sa yathā
bhartuṃ jaganti bhuvamuddhartuṃ hartuṃ prapannajanavipadam | kartuṃ śreyo niravadhi kākodaragiripateḥ ka īṣṭe’nyaḥ||
matāntare tvatra vakṣyamāṇassamuccayālaṃkāra eva ||
iyaṃ vyaṅgyā yathā
naktaṃdivaṃ murāre jvalatastava tejasaḥ kathā yatra | tatra kadāciddīvyanko vā bhānuḥ kṛśānurapi ||
atra ko vetyanena vācyalakṣyavyatiriktasyāgaṇanīyatvasya bhānukṛśānubhyāmaprakṛtābhyāmanvayo vyajyate ||
dvitīyatulyayogitā
hite’hite ca yadvṛttestaulyaṃ sā tvaparā matā |
hitāhitaviṣaye vyavahārataulyamanyā tulyayogitetyarthaḥ||
yathā
śapatāṃ caidyādīnāṃ japatāmapi yogināṃ bhavannāma| adadāḥ padaṃ samānaṃ vada dākṣyaṃ yadukumāra kimidaṃ te ||442 ||
atra yaduvaṃśyatvena bhagavatassaṃbodhanaṃ vivakṣitārthasya tadavatārīyatvajñāpanāya | tatrāpi kumāretyuktirhitāhitavibhāgamugdhatābhiprāyeṇa ||
yathāvā
kalaśābdhisutākamitassuhṛdasuhṛcca tvayā kṛtaśśāmitaḥ| vadato’sti na me dveṣyo na priya iti te na caitadāścaryam || 543 ||
suhṛt arjunādiḥ śaṃ sukhaṃ itaḥ prāptaḥ kṛtaḥ | evamasuhṛdapi | pakṣe śamitaḥ vināśita ityarthaḥ ||
yathā vā
tvaccaraṇasammukhīnaṃ tatra parācīnamapyahigirīndo | tvaṃ vaibhavasaṃmūrchanabhājaṃ vitanoṣi kā nu te prauḍhiḥ || 544 ||
vaibhavasya vibhutvasya saṃmūrchanamabhivyāptiḥ ‘murchā mohasamucchrāyayoḥ’ iti dhātorlyuṭ | tadbhājamiti sammukhīna pakṣe | parācīnaviṣaye tu vai iti prasiddhyarthakamavyayam saṃsāre saṃmūrchanabhājaṃ mohabhājaṃ vitanoṣītyarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
kriyate hi sadāsthāvarajanmā janatā’cyuta tvayā vinatā | vimatā’pi tathā’titarāṃ mama tāvadvismayo mahāneṣaḥ ||545||
he acyuta ! tvayā vinatā vimatā ca janatā sadāsthāvarajanmā kriyate, vinatapakṣe satsu brahmavitsu āsthā śraddhā yasyāssā satī praśastā āsthā yasyāsseti vā | varaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ janma yasyāssā tathoktā ca kriyate | mannantatvānna ṅīp | anyatra sadā santataṃ sthāvarajanmā vṛkṣādirūpajanmabhāk kriyate hiḥ prasiddhau | spaṣṭamanyat | yathā vā
paripuṣṭadhenukānāṃ bālānā savayasāṃ ca tālānām | kṛṣṇa phalābhyavahāraṃ kalpayasi smāvimṛśya batabhedam || 546 ||
he kṛṣṇa ! iyaṃ ca saṃbuddhistadavatārāsādhāraṇacaritamatra śloke vivakṣitamiti jñāpanāya | paripuṣṭāḥ dhenavo gāvo yaiste paripuṣṭadhenukāḥ “śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | pakṣe paripuṣṭaḥ dhenukaḥ tannāmā gardabharūpo’suro yeṣu te teṣāṃ savayasāṃ vayasyānāṃ ‘snigdho vayasyassavayāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe sapakṣiṇāmityarthaḥ | ‘vayaḥ pakṣiṇi bālyādau’ iti viśvaḥ | ādau samānaṃ vayo yeṣāmiti vigrahe ‘jyotirjanapada’ ityādinā samānaśabdaśya sabhāvaḥ | dvitīye vayobhissaha vartata iti sahapūrvapadabahuvrīhau ‘vopasarjanasya’ iti sahaśabdasya sabhāvaḥ | bālānāṃ gopālabālakānāṃ, tālānāṃ tṛṇarājatarūṇāṃ ca bhedaṃ tāratamyaṃ avimṛśya phalānāṃ phalairvā abhyavahāraṃ ahāram , pakṣe phalānāṃ abhyavahāraṃ adhaḥpātanaṃ kalpayasi sma | batetyāścarye | atra śrībhāgavatīyā dhenukāsuravadhakathā’susaṃdheyā | bālatālaśabdayoḥ batabhedaṃ bakāratakārābhyāṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ avimṛśya anādṛtyetyartho’pi camatkārātiśayaṃ puṣṇāti | atra tālānāmahitatvaṃ cāhitadhenukāsurāśrayatayeti dhyeyam ||
yathā vā
saddveṣaruciramārgapravaṇo’riṣṭānvito’tiriktātmā | ahito hitaśca bhavatā kriyate phaṇiśailaśekhara vicitram || 547 || ahitaḥ satāṃ dveṣe ruciryasya tathoktaḥ amārge śāstraviruddhe pathi pravaṇaḥ ariṣṭena aśubhena anvitaḥ atyantaṃ riktaḥ tucchaḥ ātmā buddhiryasya sa tathoktaḥ ‘vaśikaṃ tucchariktake’ ityamaraḥ | buddhihīna ityarthaḥ | kriyate | hitaścaivaṃ kriyate | pakṣe san praśastaḥ veṣaḥ ruciraśāstrīyatayā ramaṇīyaḥ yo mārgaḥ panthāśca tayoḥ pravaṇaḥ | ‘vayasaḥ karmaṇo’rthasya’ ityuktarītyā vayaḥkarmādyanurūpaveṣādika iti bhāvaḥ | ariṣṭena śubhena anvitaḥ ‘ariṣṭe tu śubhāśubhe’ ityamaraḥ | atiriktaḥ samadhikaḥ ātmā svabhāvo yasya sa tathoktaḥ | lokottarasvabhāva ityarthaḥ | ‘atiriktassamadhikaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | kriyate vicitram||
yathāvā
sphuṭamaṇikāntikamacyuta vidadhānaṃ prāgryabhāśritaṃ rāṣṭram | hanta kulālaṃkṛtyāyogyaṃ hitamahitamapi tanoṣi janam || 548 ||
he acyuta ! hitaṃ janaṃ sphuṭā vyaktā maṇīnāṃ kāntiśśobhā yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | rāṣṭraṃ prāgryayā parārthyayā bhayā prabhayā āśritaṃ vidadhānaṃ sarvarāṣṭrasamṛddhidāyinamiti yāvat | kulasya anvayasya alaṃkṛtyāḥ alaṃkārasya yogyaṃ tanoṣi | ahitamapyevameva tanoṣītyāścaryam | vastutastu ahitaṃ janamapi sphuṭāḥ maṇikā mṛdvikārāḥ antike yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ ‘alañjarassyāmaṇikaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | rāṣṭraṃ prāgryeṇa agrimeṇa bhā bh iti varṇena bhā ityetattṛtīyaikavacanam | śritaṃ vidadhānaṃ bhrāṣṭraṃ vinirmimāṇamityarthaḥ | rāṣṭraśabdasyādau vyañjanamātrabhakārasaṃśleṣaṇe bhrāṣṭramiti niṣpatteḥ | ata eva kulālaṃ kumbhakāraṃ ata eva kṛtyānāṃ satkarmaṇāṃ kṛtyeṣu vā ayogyaṃ anarhaṃ
rajakaścarmakāraśca naṭo buruḍa eva ca |
kaivartamedabhillāśca svarṇakārastu saucikaḥ ||
takṣakastilayantrī ca sūnaścakrī tathā dhvajī |
nāpitaḥ kārukaścaiva ṣoḍaśaite janaṅgamāḥ ||
iti parāśaroktestasya grāmacaṇḍāleṣvantarbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
paurahitatāṃ prapannaṃ pulomajāteśvaratvamadhidharaṇi| prāptaṃ kalayatyacyuta mitramamitraṃ ca hantatava śaktiḥ ||
he acyuta ! tava śaktiḥ parākramaḥ mitraṃ sakhāyaṃ yudhiṣṭhirādikaṃ, paurebhyo nāgarebhyaḥ hitatāṃ anukūlatāṃ prapannaṃ prāptaṃ ‘caturthī tadartha’ ityādinā samāsaḥ | adhidharaṇi dharaṇau pulomajāteśvaratvaṃ śacīpatitvaṃ bhūmīndratvaṃ sārvabhaumatvamiti yāvat | prāptaṃ kalayati | amitraṃ caivameva kalayati | hantetyāścarye | vastutastvamitrapakṣe pau puvarṇe rahitatāṃ viśliṣṭatāṃ prapannaṃ pulomajāteśvaratvaṃ lomajāteśvaratvamityarthaḥ | tatra puvarṇotsāraṇe tathāniṣpatteḥ| meṣādilomakrayavikrayikamatyarthanihīnamiti bhāvaḥ |
yathāvā
hanta tvayi prapanno vipratipannaśca yastayorjanani | avikṛtameva vidhatse bhuvaneśvari viśvabhogyamaiśvaryam || 550 || he bhuvaneśvari ! bhuvaneśvarasya bhagavatasstrī | tasyāssaṃbuddhiḥ| yadvā bhuvanānāmīśvarītyeva vigrahaḥ ‘aśnoterāśukarmaṇi varaṭca’ ityaśnotervaraṭi ṭittvalakṣaṇo ṅīp | lokānāmāśuvaradātrītyarthaḥ| ‘āśukarmavaradānādi’ iti hi vyākhyātam | prapañcitaṃ caitallakṣmīsahasraratnaprakāśikāyāṃ prādurbhāvastabake ‘akhileśvarīṃtyām’ ityetadvivaraṇāvasare | tvayi prapanno yaḥ vipratipannaśca yaḥ tayorubhayorapyavikṛtameva viśvabhogyamaiśvaryaṃ vidhatse | hanta | ekatra avikṛtaṃ avikāryeva viśveṣāṃ bhogyaṃ bhogārhaṃ sakalajagadupakārakamityarthaḥ| aiśvaryaṃ vidhatse | aparatrāvidyamānaḥ viḥ vivarṇo yasya tat avi, tathāvidhaṃ kṛtaṃ viśvabhogyaṃ śvabhogyamityarthaḥ | atitucchamiti bhāvaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyam | aiśvaryaṃ vidhatse ||
yathāvā
devānāṃ daityānāmapi tava caraṇadvayaṃ prabhāsanadam | teṣāmubhayeṣāmapi sāmānyaṃ taddhi daivataṃ bhagavan || 551 ||
he bhagavan ! tava caraṇadvayaṃ devānāṃ daityānāmapi prabhāsanadam | kathamidaṃ hitāhitayostulyaphalapradānamucitamityāśaṅkya samādhatte teṣāmiti | teṣāmubhayeṣāmapi tat tava caraṇadvayaṃ sāmānyaṃ daivataṃ hi | ubhayasādhāraṇadaivatatvādubhayeṣāṃ tulyaphalapradānaṃ yuktameveti bhāvaḥ | hītyetat ‘devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca sāmānyamadhidaivatam | sarvadā caraṇadvandvam’ iti pramāṇaprasiddhiṃ dyotayati | prabhāsanaṃ prakāśaṃ dadātīti tathoktaṃ devapakṣe | anyatra prabhāyāḥ asanaṃ nirāsaṃ dadātīti tathoktam | eṣūdāharaṇeṣvādyaṃ śuddham | anyāni śleṣasaṃkīrṇāni yathāyathamalaṃkārāntarasaṃkīrṇāni ca ||
bhagavadvarṇanautsukyapāravaśyena darśitam |
bahūdāharaṇaṃ tatra na kāryā doṣadhīrbudhaiḥ ||
iyaṃ sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharaṇoktā tulyayogitā | atra kecit “neyaṃ tulyayogitā pūrvoktatulyayogitāto bhedamarhati, ‘varṇyānāmitareṣāṃ vā’ iti lakṣaṇākrāntatvāt | ‘pradīyate parā bhūtiḥ’ ityatra ekānupūrvībodhitavastukarmakadānapātratvasya paraṃparayā tādṛśaśabdasya vā dharmasyaikyāt | ‘yaśca nimbam’ ityatrāpi kaṭutvaviśiṣṭanimbasyaiva paraṃparayā chedakasecakapūjakadharmatvasaṃbhavāt “ ityahuḥ | anye tu ‘tadetadapeśalaṃ, tathāhi yatrānekā nvayitvena jñāto dharmasteṣāmaupamyagamakatvena camatkṛtijanakastatra pūrvoktaḥ prakāraḥ | yatra tu hitāhitobhayaviṣayakaśubhāśubharūpaikavyavahārasya vyavahartṛgatastutinindānyataradyotakatayā camatkṛtijanakatvaṃ tatrāpara iti bhedāt | na hyatra parābhūtiśabdasya tadarthakarmadānasya vā paraṃparayā śatrumitragatatvena bhānaṃ, apitu śleṣabalādekatvenādhyavasitasya tādṛśadānasya rājagatatvenaiveti kathaṃ pūrvoktalakṣaṇākrāntatvam | etena ‘yaśca nimbam’ ityatra kaṭutvaviśiṣṭasya nimbasyaiva paraṃparayā chedakasecakapūjakadharmatvamiti nirastam | vastugatyā taddharmasyālaṃkāratāsaṃpādakatvābhāvāt | anyathā ‘saṃkucanti sarojāni’ ityetāvataiva tulyayogitāpatteḥ | kiṃtvanekagatatvena jñāyamānadharmatvasyaiva tulyayogitāprayojakatvamiti tadabhāve tadantargatikathanamasamañjasameva” ityāhuḥ ||
tṛtīyatulyayogitāyadutkṛṣṭaguṇaissāmyavacanaṃ sā’parā matā ||
utkṛṣṭaguṇaissāmyavicakṣayā sādhāraṇadharmakathanamaparā tulyayogitā | dharmasya varṇyāvarṇyagatatvāduktavilakṣaṇeyam | ‘yatprāyaṃ śrūyate yacca tattādṛgavagamyate’ iti vākyavidabhimatanyāyānurodhena prasiddhaguṇotkṛṣṭasāmyasya varṇanīye pratītestulyayogitāśabdānvarthatā’pyastīte bodhyam ||
yathā
maṇirājo vanamālā śrīvatso daugdhasaindhavī bālā | mṛnmayasumamālā’pica tava bhūṣaṇatāmavāpa kila śaure || 552 ||
atra varṇanīyā mṛnmayasumamālā aprakṛtaiḥ kaustubhādibhirbhūṣaṇatvena samīkṛtyoktā | mṛnmayasumamālāyā eva varṇanīyatvena vivakṣaṇāllakṣaṇasaṃgatiḥ| kiletyaitihye | ‘kenacidbhaktimatā’rpitāṃ mṛnmayīṃ sumamālāṃ kadācitphaṇigirau bhagavānmūrdhnyadhārṣīt ‘ iti paurāṇikī kathā’trānusaṃdheyā | yaivocyate ‘dattāṃ kenāpi sūnāvalimadhimakuṭaṃ mṛnmayīmeca dadhre’ iti | atra kaustubhādīnāṃ bhagavadīyatvena prastutatvābhisaṃdhau prāthamikatulyayogitaivaiṣoti nedaṃ śuddhamudāharaṇam | idaṃ tu śuddham
kalpadruḥ kāmagavī cintāratnaṃ tvadāśritaścāpi| audāryājjagato’cyuta khedānvyādhūya kalpayati modān || 553 || yathāvā
kamaṭhapatiḥ kākodarakadambanetā haritkaristomaḥ | urvīṃ veṅkaṭagirirapi sarvāmetāṃ bibhartitarām ||
anayorudāharaṇayoḥ prakṛtayorbhagavadāśritaveṅkaṭaśailayoraprakṛtaguṇotkṛṣṭakalpadrukamaṭhapatiprabhṛtibhissamīkṛtya sādhāraṇadharmavarṇanam ||
evam
tvatpratipāditamahaso bhānussitabhānurapi bṛhadbhānuḥ | tvadanugato manujo’pica bhagavan jagatāṃ tamāṃsi dhūnvanti || 555 ||
ityādāvapi draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
tvadvimukhacittavṛttirdhavaletarapakṣacaramarātriśca | haryālokavihānātparyāptaṃ tāmasī bhavatyamba ||
he amba ! harerbhagavataḥ | pakṣe haryoḥ sūryācandramasoḥ ālokasya kaṭākṣasya dīpteśca vihānāt abhāvāt paryāptaṃ yathā syāttathā tāmasī saṃpadyate | pakṣe tamisravatī bhavati ||
yathāvā
adhyātā tava vidvānatrātā narapatiśca dīnānām | dhanavānnaropyadātā dharaṇerbhārāyate vṛṣādrimaṇe || pūrvatrodāharaṇatraye stutiḥ antimodāharaṇadvaye nindā | evaṃcotkṛṣṭaguṇairiti lakṣaṇe utkṛṣṭapadamapakṛṣṭasyāpyupalakṣakaṃ, tulyatyāvagatihetoḥ prāyapāṭhasyātrāpyaviśeṣāt | iyaṃ kāvyakṣadarśe darśitā | imāṃ tulyayogitāṃ kecitsiddhiriti vyavāhārṣuḥ | tathāca jayadevaḥ kāvyalakṣaṇapratipādake tṛtīye mayūkhe
siddhiḥ khyāteṣu cennāma kīrtyate tulyatoktaye |
yuvāmeva hi vikhyātau tvaṃ balairjaladhirjalaiḥ ||
iti | sarvasvakārādimatāntareṣvatra samuditayorvarṇyāvarṇyayorbhagavadvimukhacittavṛttidarśarātryostāmasī bhavatītyekadharmānvayādvakṣyamāṇaṃ dīpakameva ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre tulyayogitāsaraṣṣoḍaśaḥ
<18>
atha dīpakālaṃkārarasaraḥ
taddīpakaṃ syādyadvarṇyāvarṇyayorekadharmatā ||
prakṛtāprakṛtayorekasādhāraṇadharmānvayo dīpakam | atra dvivacanamavivakṣitam ‘maṇiśśāṇollīḍhaḥ’ ityādau bahūnāmapyekasādhāraṇadharmānvaye dīpakadarśanāt | tulyayogitāyāmivātrāpyaupamyaṃ gamyam | prastutārthamupātto dharmaḥ prasaṅgādaprakṛtamapi dīpayati prakāśayati sundarīkarotīti dīpakam | yadvā dīpa iva dīpakaṃ ‘ive pratikṛtau’ ityadhikārasthena ‘saṃjñāyāṃ ca’ ityanena kan | dīpasādṛśyaṃ ca prakṛtāprakṛtaprakāśakatveneti dhyeyam || yathā
ācāryakṛpāplutatā vācā madhumadhurakavanagambhīrā| sā cācyutāṅghribhaktiḥ prācāṃ paripāka eva sukṛtānām || 558 ||
vāceti ṭābantaśśabdaḥ | atra prastutāyā acyutāṅghribhakteḥ aprastutāyā ācāryakṛpāplutatādeśca prācīnasukṛtaparipākalakṣaṇaguṇarūpaikadharmānvayaḥ | atra kasyacideva prastutatve dīpakaṃ, anyathā tulyayogitaiva ||
yathā
raktivihīnā vanitā śaktivihīnā na śobhate nṛpatā | yuktivihīnā kavitā bhaktivihīnā matistvayi ca śaure || 559 ||
atrābhāvassādhāraṇo dharmaḥ, matāntare tvabhāvo’pi guṇa eveti tulyayogitānirūpaṇāvasare’vocāma | atrāpi kasyacideva prakṛtatāyāṃ dīpakaṃ sarveṣāṃ prakṛtatve aprakṛtatve vā tulyayogitetyanusaṃdheyam | yatra tu kriyā sādhāraṇo dharmaḥ tatra yāvatāṃ kartrādikārakāṇāṃ sannidhānaṃ teṣāṃ svasajātīyenānyena saha tulyayogitā dīpakaṃ vā pṛthakpṛthagbhavati, aupamyasyāpi pṛthageva bhāsamānatvāt ||
yathā
lāvaṇyaṃ lalanāmaṇimāruṇyaṃ vidrumaṃ vṛṣādrimaṇe | naipaṇyaṃ naranāthaṃ kāruṇyaṃ tvāṃ ca saṃprasādhayati || 560 ||
atra kartṛkarmaṇoḥ ||
yathāvā
tapanīyakuṇḍalaṃ yadruciraṃ tapanīyamaṇḍalaṃ yadapi | kuṅkumavarṇaviśeṣaṃ taddvayamabhirañjayatyanantamapi ||
ruciraṃ sundaraṃ yat tapanīyakuṇḍalaṃ svarṇakarṇaveṣṭanaṃ yadapi tapanasya bhānoridaṃ tapanīyaṃ ‘vā nāmadheyasya’ iti vṛddhasaṃjñāyāṃ ‘tasyedam’ ityarthe ‘vṛddhācchaḥ’ iti chapratyaya | maṇḍalaṃ bimbaṃ kuṅkamasya kāśmīrajanmanaḥ varṇaviśeṣa iva varṇaviśeṣo yasya tattathoktaṃ aruṇavarṇamityarthaḥ | tadvayamapi anantaṃ bhagavantamapi | pakṣe gaganamapi tamālaśyāmalamapīti bhāvaḥ | abhirañjayati aruṇayatītyarthaḥ| kiṃca tapanīyakuṇḍalaṃ yat yadapi tapanīyamaṇḍalaṃ ku ma ityākāraphavarṇāveva viśeṣo vailakṣaṇyaṃ yasya tattathoktaṃ tadvayamapi kuṃ bhuvaṃ anantamantarikṣamapi abhirañjayati | svavarṇavyāpanena lohitīkarotītyarthaḥ | kumiti pṛthakpadapakṣe ‘vā padāntasya ‘ iti parasavarṇa iti dhyeyam | atrāpi kartukarmaṇoḥ| śleṣasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
audāryaṃ kalpatarorgāmbhīryaṃ śrīnivāsa salilanidheḥ | śaiśiryaṃ tuhinagireraiśvaryaṃ tava ca bhāsate sahajam || 562 || atra kevalakartuḥ ||
satyavihīnaṃ vacanaṃ vittavihīnaṃ kuṭumbināṃ bhavanam | vṛttavihīnaṃ jananaṃ cittaṃ haribhaktihīnamapiśocyam || 563 ||
atra karmaṇaḥ ||
indirayā tava vakṣaścandirakalayā’ntarikṣamabjākṣa | sundaratāmanavarataṃ vindatyaravindarekhayā ca saraḥ || 564 ||
atra kartṛkaraṇayoḥ ||
kavinā vacasā sahṛdayahṛdayaṃ sudṛśā daśā mano yūnām | bhavatā sauśīlyena krītaṃ bhuvanaṃ samagramapi bhūman || 565 ||
atra kartṛkaraṇakarmaṇāmiti viśeṣaḥ | sauśīlyaṃ mahato mandaissaha nīrandhreṇa saṃśleṣasvabhāvaḥ ||
jaladassa cātakebhyassudhāmayūkhaścakoranikarebhyaḥ| ahigiripatirnatebhyo’pyamṛtaṃ nirapekṣameva saṃdatte || 566 ||
atra kartṛsampradānayoḥ | amṛtaṃ salilaṃ pīyūṣaṃ niśśreyasaṃ ca yathākramamarthaḥ | amṛtaśabdasyāvṛttyā karmaṇo’pīti bodhyam | nirapekṣameva pratyupakārābhisandhividhuramevetyarthaḥ || aravindānmakarandaṃ mākandānmadhuravṛtti phalabṛndam | kundādvindedgandhaṃ muktyānandaṃ jano mukundācca || 567 ||
atrāpādānakarmaṇoḥ | madhurā vṛttiḥ sthitiryasya tat madhuravṛtti svabhāvamadhuramityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
aruṇāttrāsastamasāṃ hariṇādhīśānmadāvalakulānām | hariṇāṅkāttāpānāṃ śaraṇāgatitastavāmba pāpānām || 568 ||
atrāpādānakartroḥ | katoṃ cātra ‘kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti’ iti kṛdyogalakṣaṇaṣaṣṭhībodhyaḥ ||
ghṛṇinivaho’mbujabandhau maṇinivaho bhāsate mahāsindhau | guṇanivaho’khilabandhau phaṇiśikharīndau ca nandayanviśvam || 569 ||
atra kartradhikaraṇayoḥ ||
kārakadīpakamapi dīpaka evāntarbhāvitaṃ daṇḍiprabhṛtibhiḥ | atra kecit dīpakamapi tulyayogitāyāmevāntarbhavati, dharmasya sakṛdvṛttimūlakavicchitterubhayatrāviśeṣāt | prakṛtāprakṛtatvādiviśeṣasya cāvāntarabhedasādhakatve’pyalaṃkārāntaratāyāmasādhakatvāt | anyathā tulyayogitāyāṃ dharmiṇāṃ kevalaprakṛtatvasya kevalāprakṛtatvasya ca viśeṣasya sattvādalaṃkāradvaitāpatteḥ, śleṣe’pyevaṃ daividhyāpatteḥ, sarveṣāmapyalaṃkārāṇāṃ prabhedavailakṣaṇyādvailakṣaṇyāpatteśca | tasmātprakṛtānāmeva aprakṛtānāmeva prakṛtāprakṛtānāṃ caikadharmānvaya iti tulyayogitāyā eva trayaḥ prabhedā vaktumucitā iti dīpakasya tulyayogitātaḥ pṛthagalaṃkāratāmācakṣāṇānāṃ prācāṃ durāgraha eveti vadanti | anye tu
nānādhikaraṇasthānāṃ śabdānāṃ saṃpradīpakaḥ|
ekavākyena saṃyogo yastaddīpakamucyate ||
yathā
sarāṃsi haṃsaiḥ kusumaiśca vṛkṣā mattadvirephaiśca saroruhāṇi |
goṣṭhībhirudyānavanāni caiva yasminnaśūnyāni sadā kriyante||
iti bharatamuninā daṇḍinā’pi dīpakamātrasyābhyupagamāttatraiva tulyayogitāntarbhāvanasya aucityāttulyayogitāyāṃ dīpakāntarbhāvanaṃ manvate’nucitam | amumalaṃkāraṃ dharmasya guṇakriyādyātmakasyādimadhyāvasānagatatvena trividhamāmananti rudraṭādayaḥ | tanmate yathā atrodāhṛteṣveva padyeṣu ‘aruṇāttrāsastamasām’ ityatrādau, raktivihīnā vanitā’ ityatra madhye ‘lāvaṇyaṃ lalanāmaṇiṃ’ ityādāvavasāne ca dharmo nirdiṣṭaḥ | vastutastu tatra dharmasyādimadhyāvasānagatatve’pi vicchittiviśeṣavirahāt traividhyoktirāpātamātrāt | anyathā dharmasyopādyupamadhyopāntagatatvaṃ tatopi kiṃcinnyūnādhikadeśavṛttitve cānantabhedaprasaṅgāt | anupadapradarśiteṣūdāharaṇeṣu kevalānugāmisādhāraṇadharmatāyāṃ darśitaṃ dīpakam | ‘raktivihīnā vanitā’ ityādau bimbapratibimbabhāvenāpyetadadarśi || yathāvā
nayaśālī vibudhendro jayaśālī bhāti sāṃyugīnendraḥ | rayaśālī khagarāḍucchrayaśālī cāpi veṅkaṭagirīndraḥ || 570 ||
evaṃ tulyayogitāyāmapi saṃbhavatītyudāhārṣma | atra bimbapratibimbatāyāṃ na kevalaṃ kriyārūpamanugāmimātraṃ camatkārakāraṇam | kiṃtūdāhṛtarītyā nayādipratibimbabhāvakarambhitam | iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ yatkevalabimbapratibimbabhāvenāpyupamādīnāṃ bhavati niṣpattiḥ | yathā prāgupamāyāmudāhṛte ‘kamalābhujayugalāñcita’ ityādau | prakṛte tu na tathā, anugāminaṃ vinā dharmasvarūpasyaivāniṣpatteḥ| na hi bimbapratibimbamātreṇa dharmasya sakṛdvṛttissaṃbhavati | śleṣeṇāpyetatsaṃbhavati ||
yathā
nanu nātha śabdavidyāvidaḥ parabrahmavedinaścāpi| śeṣagirīśvara niyataṃ bhavati hi gatiracchagatyarthe ||
śabdavidyāvidaḥ vyākaraṇavettuḥ parabrahmavedinaḥ parabrahmavettuśca acchagatyarthe gatirbhavati niyatam | tatrādyapakṣe accha acchetyavyayaṃ gatyarthe gatyarthakadhātuyoge gatiḥ gatisaṃjñako bhavati | ‘gatiśca’ ityanuvartamāne ‘accha gatyarthavadeṣu’ | iti sūtreṇācchetyavyayasya gatyarthakadhātuyoge gatisaṃjñāvidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | dvitīyapakṣe acchā prakṛtisaṃvandhavimocayitrī yā gatiḥ arcirādimārgeṇa gamanaṃ tasyai tadarthaṃ ‘arthe kṛte ca śabdau dvau tādarthye’vyayasaṃjñitau’ | gatiḥ jñānaṃ bhavati niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | atrā prakṛtasya śabdavidyāvidaḥ prakṛtasya parabrahmavidaśca acchagatyarthe gatirbhavatīti śleṣeṇa samānadharmatā ||
atredamavadheyaṃ, yadyapi
durdamadāridryatamonirdalanacaṇāvubhau samindhāte| cintāmaṇiśikharimaṇiścintāmaṇiriti ca yassuparvamaṇiḥ || 572 ||
ityatra prastutāprastutayoryugapaddharmānvayaḥ pratibhāti | ‘raktivihīnā vanitā’ ityādiṣu prāgudāhṛteṣu padyeṣu aprastutasyaiva prathamaṃ dharmānvayaḥ, tathā’pi tasya prāsaṅgikatvaṃ na hīyate | vastugatyā prastutoddeśena pravṛttasyaiva varṇanasya prastute’pyanvayāt | na hi dīpasya rathyāprāsādayoryugapadupakārakatvena jāmātrarthaṃ śrapitasya sūpasyātithibhyaḥ prathamapariveṣaṇena ca prāsaṅgikatvaṃ hīyate | tulyayogitāyāṃ tvekaṃ prastutamanyadaprastutamiti viśeṣāgrahaṇātsarvoddeśenaiva dharmoktiriti viśeṣaḥ | ayaṃ canayoraparo viśeṣaḥ ubhayoranayorupamālaṃkārasya gamyatvāviśeṣe’pi atrāprastutamupamānaṃ prastutamupameyamiti vyavasthita upamānopameyabhāvaḥ, tatra tu viśeṣāgrahaṇādaicchikassa iti | idaṃ sarvaṃ kuvalayānande spaṣṭam | kaiścittu mālādīpakamapyatraivāntarbhāvitam, tadayuktam | ‘smareṇa hṛdaye tasyāstena tvayi kṛtā sthitiḥ’ ityādau sādṛśyasaṃparkābhāvāditi | etacca mālādīpakanirūpaṇāvasare prapañcayiṣyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre dīpakasarassaptadaśaḥ
<19>
athāvṛttidīpakasaraḥ
samastayorvyastayorvā’’vṛttau padatadarthayoḥ |
āvṛttidīpakaṃ prāhustrividhaṃ budhasattamāḥ ||
padatadarthayossamastayorbyastayoścānekopakārakatayā dīpasthānīyayorāvṛttau trividhamāvṛttidīpakamityarthaḥ | padatadarthayorubhayorāvṛttāvekaḥ prakāraḥ, padasyaiva arthasyaiva cāvṛttau dvau prakārāviti trayo’sya prakārāḥ| ekakriyāyā anekakārakānvayavadanekakārakasyāvṛttakriyānvayādāvṛttidīpakamityanvarthābhidhānamidam ||
yathākramamudāharaṇāni
haribhūbhṛdudayabhājo harermahasphūrtiparicaye nṝṇām | vikasati dṛgabjabṛndaṃ vikasati hṛdayābjabṛndaṃ ca || 573 ||
haribhūbhṛt veṃkaṭagiriḥ sa eva udayaḥ taṃ bhajatīti tathoktaḥ | tasya hareḥ bhagavata eva bhānumataḥ mahasphūrtirutsavasphūrtireva tejassphūrtiḥ tasyāḥ paricaye sati | atra vikasanakriyātadvācakapadayordvayorāvṛttiritīdamubhayāvṛttidīpakam ||
yathā vā
karṣati dṛgekato māṃ śrutireṣā’nyatra karṣati murāre | rasanā paratra karṣati sādhvī bhaktiḥ kimatra kurvīta || 571 || māṃ tvāṃ draṣṭuṃ tvatkathāṃ śrotuṃ tvannāma vaktuṃ cābhīpsantamiti bhāvaḥ dṛk cakṣurindriyaṃ ekataḥ svaviṣaye ityarthaḥ | evamuttaratrāpi | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | atra karṣaṇakriyātadvācakapadayorubhayorapyāvṛttiḥ | idaṃ dhūrtabahusapatnīkadarthitasādhvīdayitavṛttāntasamāroparūpasamāsoktyā saṃkīrṇaṃ, pūrvaṃ tu rūpakeṇa ||
yathāvā
prasajedyadi pāpaṃ naḥ prasajeddātuṃ hariḥ phalaṃ tasya | prasajetkṛpā’pi lakṣmyāḥ prasajetsā svadayitaṃ prasādayitum || 575 ||
yathāvā
karivaraninado mūrchati harivaraninado’pi mūrchati samantāt | mūrchati ca tatpratisrudguhāgṛhābhyantareṣu śeṣagireḥ || 576 ||
yathāvā
kūjanti cakravākāḥ kūjanti śukāḥ pikāśca kūjanti | kūjanti sārasā api karṇānandapradāḥ pṛdākugirau || 577 ||
idamudāharaṇatritayaṃ śuddham | atrāpyādyayorudāharaṇayoḥ kāraṇamālā dhvanyate | antimaṃ tūdāharaṇaṃ śuddhatamam ||
padāvṛttidīpakaṃ yathā
vyālottamācalendorālokāddūratastamo dravati | śaraṇagatānāmantaḥkaraṇasudhākiraṇadṛṣadapi dravati ||
atra dravatīti padasyaivāvṛttiḥ na tu tadarthasya, ādyasya dhāvatīti antyasyārdrā bhavatītyarthayorbhadāt ||
yathāvā
svairaṃ yaśo visṛmaraṃ smeraṃ bhavato vidhūyate śaure | sphāraṃ yaśopyasuhṛdāṃ dūraṃdūraṃ vidhūyate dikṣu ||
atrāpi vidhūyate ityanayoḥ vidhurivācaratīti prakampyata iti cārthabhedādidamapi padāvṛttidīpakam ||
yathāvā
bahuśo dhayati svāminnahiśaile dhanya eva tava tīrtham | bahu śodhayati ca tamimaṃ kalaśodadhijādhinātha tattīrtham || 580 ||
he svāmin ! ahiśaile dhanyaḥ puṇyavāneva tava tīrthaṃ tvada bhiṣekatīrthamityarthaḥ, bahuśaḥ bahu dhayati pibati | ‘gheṭ pāne’ tamimaṃ tīrthadhātāraṃ janaṃ tattīrthaṃ kartu bahu yathā syāttathā śodhayati śuddhaṃ karoti apāstasamastāṃhastatiṃ tanotītyarthaḥ | atrāpi padamātrāvṛttiḥ ||
yathāvā
ceto mama bahu dhāvati vātaṃdhayagirijamadbhutaṃ kimapi | bahudhā’vati ca mahāmbhovahadhāma tadadbhutaṃ smarantaṃ mām || 581 || mama cetaḥ kartṛ | vātaṃdhayagirijātaṃ kimapyadbhutaṃ sarvāścaryanidhiṃ śrīnivāsamityarthaḥ | dhāvati smaratīti yāvat | mahāmbhovahadhāma mahāvārivāhanīlamityarthaḥ | tadadbhutaṃ bhagavānityarthaḥ | smarantaṃ māṃ bahudhā avati prīṇayati rakṣati ca | atrāpi padamātrāvṛttiḥ | ādyodāharaṇadvaye abhaṅgaśleṣeṇa anantarodāhṛtyossabhaṅgaśleṣeṇa nirvyūḍhamidam ||
yathāvā
yo’ntardhatte kamalākāntaṃ kākodarādribhāsvatam | antardhatte tasya ca saṃtamasaṃ bhavamayaṃ sa hi nitāntam || 582 ||
yaḥ pumān antaḥ manasi dhatte dhyāyatītyarthaḥ | tasya puṃsaḥ bhavamayaṃ saṃtamasaṃ saḥ bhagavān antardhatte pidadhāti vinivartayatīti yāvat | atrāpi padasyaivāvṛttiḥ ||
arthāvṛttidīpakaṃ yathā
kṣālayati veṅkaṭācalapālayituraghāni sapadi nāmasudhā | mārjayati kujanasannutyārjitamūrjitamapīha daurjanyam || 583 ||
atra kriyāpadabhede’pi tadarthasya śodhanasyābhedādarthāvṛttidīpakamidam ||
yathāvā
yatrendhe vanamālā cakāsti kaustubhamaṇirlasati ca śrīḥ| kiyadiva tadbhāgyaṃ syātpaṅkajamālā’pi tatra dīvyati cet || 584 || atrāpi indhe ityādikriyāvācipadabhede’pi tadarthasya prakāśasyābheda iti dhyeyam | idaṃ ca utkṛṣṭavyaktisamucitotta masthānatattulyakakṣyāsamindhānāpakṛṣṭavyaktibhāgadheyapraśaṃsālakṣaṇavyavahārasamāroparūpasamāsoktisaṃkīrṇamiti vibhāvanīyam ||
yathāvā
prāpnoti jayaṃ labhate śriyaṃ hriyaṃ vindate nayaṃ bhajate | priyamayate’pica niyataṃ śriyaḥpate tvāṃ śrayannayaṃ lokaḥ || 585 ||
hriyaṃ akāryebhyo lajjāṃ vindate | ayaṃ mahānguṇaḥ| yathocyate tatratatra ‘hrīniṣevī’ iti | ādyodāharaṇadvaye kartṝṇāmāvṛttakriyānvayaḥ, iha tu jayamityādikarmaṇāmiti viśeṣaḥ ||
nanvatra dharmanirdeśasya prastutāprastutobhayaviṣayatvābhāvātkathaṃ dīpakaśabdavyapadeśa iti cenna, anekopakārakatvasāmyena tadupapatteḥ | ata eva kuvalayānande ‘āvṛttīnāṃ prastutāprastutobhayaviṣayatvābhāve’pi dīpakacchāyāpattimātreṇa dīpakavyapadeśaḥ’ ityuktam | atra mātrapadaṃ prayuñjānairdīkṣitaiḥ pṛthagevāyamalaṃkāro na tu dīpakaprabheda iti sūcitam | ata evoktaṃ daṇḍinā
arthāvṛtiḥ padavṛttirubhayāvṛttirityapi |
dīpakasthānameveṣṭamalaṃkāratrayaṃ yathā ||
iti | triprakāro’yamalaṃkāro dīpakasadṛśa iti tadarthaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre āvṛttidīpakasaro’ṣṭādaśaḥ
<20>
atha prativastūpamāsaraḥ
padārthārabdho vākyārtha iti padārthagatālaṃkārānantaraṃ vākyārthagatālaṃkāraprastāvaḥ | tatra sāmānyadharmasya ivādyupādāne sakṛnnirdeśe upamā | vastuprativastubhāvena sakṛnnirdeśe’pi saiva | ivādyanupādāne sakṛnnirdeśe dīpakatulyayogite | asakṛnnirdeśe tu śuddhasāmānyarūpatvaṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvo vā | ādyaḥ prakāraḥ prativastūpamā | dvitīyaprakārāśrayeṇa dṛṣṭānto vakṣyate | tadevamaupamyāśrayeṇaiva prativastūpamā | tathāca tallakṣaṇam
sadṛśyāvasite vākyadvaye ceddharma ekakaḥ |
dvirupātto bhavetsā tu prativastūpamocyate ||
anyonyamaikavākyalābhārthamupamānopameyabhāvaparyavasannayorvākyayorguṇakriyārūpa eka eva dhamo vastuprativastubhāvāpannatayā śabdāntareṇa pṛthaṅnirdiśyate cetsā prativastūpamā | prativastu upamānopameyabhāvadiśi upamā tatprayojakasādhāraṇadharmo’syāmiti vyutpatterityarthaḥ | arthāntaranyāsavāraṇāya sādṛśyeti | tatra tu samarthyasamarthakabhāvamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ na tu sādṛśyam | vimbapratibimbabhāvena dṛṣṭānte’pi dharmopādānāttatrātiprasaṅgavāraṇāyaika iti | tatra hi bhinna eva dharma upādīyata iti na doṣaḥ ||
yathā
nārāyaṇagirireva sphārāṃ kīrtiṃ bibharti khalu giriṣu | airāvata eva paraṃ smarāṃ dīptiṃ dadhāti dikkariṣu || 586 || atra bibharti dadhātītyeka eva dharma upameyopamānavākyayoḥ pṛthagbhinnapadābhyāṃ nirdiṣṭaḥ | ayameva hi vastuprativastubhāva ityucyate ||
yathāvā
bhajatāṃ kathaṃ nu mahatā bhavatā’lpā devatā hare samatām | kācamaṇirnīcaghṛṇirhīreṇa sadṛkkimatyudāreṇa || 587 ||
bhajatāmiti loṭ ||
yathāvā
aharaharapi gacchanto mūḍhāstvāṃ brahma nāpnuvanti hare | vindantyakṣetrajñā uparyupari na hi nidhiṃ carantopi || 588 ||
atra ‘tadyathā hiraṇyanidhiṃ nihitamakṣetrajñā uparyupari saṃcarantopi na vindeyurevamevemāssarvāḥ prajā aharahargacchantya etaṃ brahmalokaṃ na vindantyanṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ’ iti daharavidyāgataśrutyartho’’nusaṃhitaḥ | asyārthaḥ yathā adhastānnikṣiptaṃ hiraṇyanidhiṃ nidhimatkṣetrasvabhāvajñānahīnā nidheruparyupari saṃcarantopi na labhante, evamevemāssarvāḥ prajāḥ suṣuptikāle aharahargacchantyaḥ suṣuptau ‘sati saṃpadya na viduḥ’ ityuktarītya avibhāgaṃ gacchantyaḥ etaṃ daharākāśākhyaṃ brahmarūpaṃ lokaṃ na vindanti na labhanta na jānantītyarthaḥ | brahmalokaśabdau niṣādasthapatinyāyena samānādhikaraṇā | tatra hetumāha anṛtena hi pratyūḍhā iti| anṛtena karmaṇā pratāpaṃ nītāḥ svabhāvāntaraṃ prāpitāḥ| ācchāditā iti yāvat | yadvā aharahargacchantya iti na suṣuptikālikaṃ gamanamucyate, apitu antarātmatvena sadā vartamānasya daharākāśasya hiraṇyanidhivatparamapuruṣārthabhūtasya uparyupari aharahargacchantyaḥ sarvasminkāle vartamānāstamajānatyastaṃ na vindanti na labhanta ityarthaḥ| udāharaṇe mūḍhā ityasyānṛtena pratyūḍhā ityarthaḥ | atra vākyadvaye prāptyabhāvarūpassādhāraṇadharmaśśabdabhedenopāttaḥ ||
nanu ‘kṣālayāte veṅkaṭācala’ ityudāhṛtārthāvṛttidīpakāda sthāḥ kiṃ vailakṣaṇyamitei cenna, prastutānekadharmigataikadharmanirdeśaḥ aprastutānekagatatathāvidhadharmanirdeśo vā arthāvṛttiḥ | iyaṃ tu prastutāprastutobhayaniṣṭhatathāvidhadharmanirdeśarūpetyasyāstato vailakṣaṇyasadbhāvāt | kiṃcārthāvṛttidīpakaṃ vākyayorarthasyāvṛttāveva bhavati, sā ca sādharmya eva na vaidharmye | iyaṃ tu vaidharmyeṇāpi bhavatīti tatopyasyā bhedaḥ | tadetadukta kuvalayānande ‘“ arthāvṛttidīpakaṃ prastutānāmaprastutānāṃ vā’ bhavati | prativastūpamā tu prastutāprastutānāmiti viśeṣaḥ | ayaṃ cāparo viśeṣaḥ āvṛttidīpakaṃ vaidharmyeṇa na sambhavati prativastūpamā tu vaidharmyeṇāpi dṛśyate” iti ||
yathā
phaṇigiripatiguṇaparimalamabhijānanti hi tadekaniṣṇātāḥ| mṛgamadaparimalabharamiha vidanti na grāmasūkarā jātu || 589 ||
yathāvā
majjati bhavatkṣamāyāṃ nāthāmandaṃ madāgasāṃ bṛndam | nonmajjati khalu paṅkaṃ gaṅgāyāṃ vimalatarataraṅgāyām || 590 ||
yathāvā
udadhisute tvāmantarnidadhānaṃ mama vibhāti hṛdayābjam | niṣkāsitamuktāmaṇiśuktipuṭaṃ naiva jātvapi samindhe || 591 ||
nanvidaṃ vyāhataṃ yadvaidharmyeṇopameti | | sādharmyarūpatvāttasyā iti cetsatyaṃ, vaidharmyeṇopanyastena mṛgamadaparimalabharamityādivākyārthenākṣiptasya apitu jñātāra eva jānantīti vākyārthasyopamānatvena vivakṣaṇāt | āmukhe vaidharmyapratītāvapi sādharmya eva paryavasānānna doṣaḥ | vaidharmyeṇetyasya vaidharmyadvāretyarthaḥ ||
atra sādhāraṇadharmasya vastuprativastubhāvakathanena taditarapadārthānāṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvo ghaṭanāyā ānurūpyaṃ ca vivakṣitam | anyathā
khalāstukuśalāssvīyahitapratyūhakarmaṇi |
nipuṇāḥ phaṇinaḥ prāṇānapahartuṃ nirāgasām ||
ityatrāsaṃṣṭhulavākyārthe’pi prativastūpamā syāt | atra hi khalaphaṇinoḥ prāṇahitayośca satyapi bimbapratibimbabhāve haraṇapratyūhakaraṇayornāśaprāgabhāvaparyavasitayoranānurūpyānna bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | yadvā astvatra prativastūpamā, paraṃtu pūrvavākyārthaghaṭakānāṃ hitapratyūhakarmaṇīti nāmārthavibhaktyarthādīnāmuttaravākyārthaghaṭake prāṇānapahartumityatrābhāvenāsaṃṣṭhulatālakṣaṇasya vākyārthasāmānyadoṣasya jāgarūkatvādacamatkāriṇyeva, duṣṭopamādivat | vākyārtho hi nirbharataravyutpattiniśātitāntaḥkaraṇaiḥ vividhapadārthasaṃdarbhaparivartananipuṇaireva mahākavibhirviracitaḥ kimapi puṣṇāti kāmanīyakaṃ, netaraḥ| tasmādevaṃjātīyakeṣvalaṃkāreṣu pūrvavākyārthaghaṭakanāmārthānurūpairnāmārthaiḥ tadghaṭakavibhaktyanurūpābhirvibhaktibhiḥ tadanvayānurūpeṇa cānvayenottaravākyārthe bhāvyamiti sahṛdayahṛdayameva praṣṭavyamiti vadanti||
alaṃkārakaustubhakāro viśveśvarastu iyamalaṃkārāntaraviṣaye’pi dṛśyata ityāha | tatrāprastutapraśaṃsāviṣaye yathā
nīrasamiva sārasamapi sāndratamā iva sa candramā janani | salayamiva kisalayaṃ nanu vidruta iva vidrumastava purastāt || 592 ||
tava atimātraramaṇīyanayanavadanakarādharāyā iti bhāvaḥ | atra sārasādīnāmapakarṣarūpa eko dharmaḥ prativākyaṃ bhinnena nīrasatvādinā pratipāditaḥ| utprekṣayā ceyaṃ saṃkīrṇetyavadheyam ||
mālārūpeṇāpīyaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
sahakāraḥ phalamatulaṃ vahate sa haraḥ kalānidhiṃ dhatte | samrāṇbibharti makuṭaṃ sarīsṛpagirirdarīdharīti harim || 593 ||
yathāvā
indhe kaviḥ pratibhayā bhāti prabhayā sarojīnībandhuḥ | rājyaśriyā’vanīndro rājati nityaśriyā harirlasati ||594 || atrādye padye vahanadhānabharaṇadhāraṇānāṃ dvitīye indhanabhānarājanalasanānāṃ ca vastuta aikarūpyātprativastūpameyaṃ mālārūpā ||
ityalaṅkāramaṇihāre prativastūpamāsara ekonaviṃśaḥ
<21>
atha dṛṣṭāntasaraḥ
bimbatvapratibimbatvāpannadharmādikaṃ dvayoḥ|
vākyārthayoścedaupamyamārthaṃ dṛṣṭānta īryate ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannasādhāraṇadharmādikaṃ vākyārthayorārthamaupamyaṃ dṛṣṭānta ityarthaḥ | taduktam ‘dṛṣṭāntaḥ punareteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pratibimbanam’ iti | sarveṣāṃ upamādīnām | dṛṣṭaḥ antaḥ niścayo yatreti yathārthābhidhāno’yaṃ, dārṣṭāntike sandigdhasyārthasyātra niścayadarśanāt ||
yathā
gaṇahīnā’pi ca kavitā phaṇigiripatināmamasṛṇitā ślāghyā | yoṣā spṛhaṇīyā khalu bhūṣāhīnā’pi pūrṇalāvaṇyā || 595 ||
atra kavitāyoṣayorupameyopamānayoḥ ślāghyatvaspṛhaṇīyatvayordharmayorapi bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | asya cālaṃkārasya prativastūpamāyā bhedakametadevayatasyāṃ dharmo na pratibimbitaḥ kiṃtu śuddhasāmānyātmanaiva sthitaḥ, iha tu pratibimbita iti || yathāvā
śreyasvinī trilokī bhūyaḥ kṛpayaiva bhujagaśikharīndoḥ | ojasvinī dinaśrīstejasphūrtyaiva tāmarasabandhoḥ || 596 ||
yathāvā
śuddhimatīyaṃ jagatī śrīśaileśāṅghrijanmanā saritā | ṛddhimatī tridivaśrīrmandārabhuvā mahāvadānyatayā || 597 ||
yathāvā
gireiśādistvadapāṅgāttvaṃ tu svata eva hīśitā bhagavan | kācamaṇirācakāsti dyumaṇikarādeṣa tu svabhāsaiva || 598 ||
yathāvā
nissvopi tvayi bhaktaḥ pūjyo nānyo mahāsamṛddhirapi | abhirūpo’naṅgopi ślāghyo nānīdṛśo mahāṅgo’pi || 599 ||
anaṅgaḥ alpāṅgaḥ naño’trānudarā kanyetyādāvivālpatvamarthaḥ| kṛśatanurapītyabhiprāyaḥ | kāma ityapyupaskāryam | mahāṅgaḥ pīvarāvayavopi | pakṣe uṣṭra ityarthaḥ | ‘uṣṭre kramelakamayamahāṅgāḥ’ ityamaraḥ || vaidharmyeṇa yathā
marudātmaṃbharigiripativaradānādeva dehino vibhavaḥ | śaradāgamaṃ vinā kaḥ karadāyī syādvidhuprasādasya || 600 ||
vidhuprasādasya induprasannatāyāḥ | karadāyī hastālambavitarītā ||
yathā vā
tāvadbhavaḥ prabhavitā yāvadbhagavanna netrapātramasi | sauparṇamantravarṇe karṇodgīrṇe na pannagatrāsaḥ || 601 ||
atrādyodāharaṇe prastutavākyārthagatayorvaradānavibhavayoḥ, dvitīyodāharaṇe bhavaprabhavitṛtvabhagavatkaṭākṣapātratābhāvayośca vaidharmyeṇa śaradāgamavidhuprasādadānābhāvau pannagatrāsābhāvasauparṇamantrakarṇagocaratve ca yathākramamuttaravākye pratibimbatayā niradeśiṣātām ||
yathā vā
bhajatāṃ dadāti jagatāṃ bhavatī lokottaraṃ phalaṃ sakalam | ahiśailamaṇivadhūmaṇi na hi mallīsumamasaurabhaṃ sūte || 602 ||
ityatrāpi vaidharmyeṇa dṛṣṭānto draṣṭavyaḥ | ‘atra dṛṣṭāntālaṃkāramahimnaiva bimbapratibimbabhāvena pratīyamānamupamānopameyabhāvamanādṛtya dṛṣṭāntodāharaṇanidarśanādiśabdāḥ kavinā na prayoktavyāḥ paunaruktyāpatteḥ ‘ ityekāvalīkāraḥ| ata eva
tṛptiyogaḥ pareṇāpi na mahimnā mahīyasām |
pūrṇaścandrodayākāṅkṣī dṛṣṭānto’tra mahārṇavaḥ ||
iti māghapadyavyākhyānāvasare ‘nāyaṃ dṛṣṭāntālaṃkāra bimbapratibimbabhāvenaupamyasya gamyatve tasyotthānāt | kiṃtu dṛṣṭāntaśabdena tulyārthābhidhānāḍhupamālaṃkarāraḥ’ ityuktvā upadarśitaikāvalīgranthamudāhārṣīnmallināthaḥ | vastutastu dṛṣṭāntodāharaṇādiśabdaghaṭiteṣvīdṛśasthaleṣu pūrvaṃ vyutpādita udāraṇālaṃkāra evetyutpaśyāmaḥ | sa ca samyageva pūrvaṃ nyarūṣyateti neha pratāyate ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre dṛṣṭāntasaro viṃśaḥ
<22>
atha nidarśanālaṃkārasaraḥ
upāttayorarthayoścedārthābhedaḥ prakīrtyate |
aupamyaparyavasito bhavetseyaṃ nidarśanā ||
upāttayoḥ arthayoḥ ārtho’bheda aupamyaparyavasāyī nidarśanetyarthaḥ | atiśayoktyādīnāṃ dhvanyamānarūpakasya ca vāraṇāya upāttayoriti vācyarūpakavāraṇāyārtha iti | ārthatvaṃ ca prāthamikānvayabodhāviṣayatvam | yadi ca viśiṣṭopamāyāṃ viśeṣaṇayorabhedaḥ pratīyata ityucyate, tadā bimbapratibimbabhāvānāpannapradhānanirūpitaguṇībhāvavatoriti viśeṣaṇamarthayorupādeyam | viśiṣṭopamāyāṃ pradhānabhūtayorupamānopameyayorbimbapratibimbabhāvāpannatayā tannirūpitaguṇabhāvavatorviśeṣaṇayorārthābhedabhāne’pi nāti prasaṅgaḥ | nidarśanāyāṃ tu vakṣyamāṇodāharaṇeṣu abhijānan vettītyādikriyayorguṇabhūtayorbimbapratibimbabhāve satyapi tādṛśakriyānirūpitapradhānabhāvavatoḥ kartrorbimbapratibimbabhāvānāpannatayā tādṛśakartṛnirūpitaguṇabhāvavatyorabhijñānavedanādi kriyayorārthābhedapratīterbhaviṣyati lakṣaṇānugamaḥ| idaṃ ca śrautyā nidarśanāyā lakṣaṇam | ārthīsādhāraṇaṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ lalitālaṃkāraprakaraṇe pradarśayiṣyate ||
yathā
nityaṃ paramātmānaṃ pratyagabhinnaṃ bhavantamabhijānan | satyaṃ phaṇiśailapate bhṛtyābhedena vetti samrājam || 603 ||
atra paramātmanaḥ pratyagātmabhedena jñānaṃ samrājo bhṛtyābhedena jñānaṃ cābhinnamiti tatsādṛśyamūlā dhīḥ || 603 ||
yathāvā
tava mahimānamanantaṃ veditumicchan hṛdā paricchidya | kakṣe’ntarikṣamakhilaṃ kāṅkṣati pariveṣṭya hanta nikṣeptum || 604 ||
atra
yadā carmadavākāśaṃ veṣṭayiṣyanti mānavāḥ |
tadā śivamāvijñāya duḥkhasyāntaṃ nigacchati ||
iti śruticchāyā gṛhītā | atrānantasya bhagavanmahimno manasā paricchidya vedanecchā antarikṣasya kaṭādivatpariveṣṭya kakṣanikṣepaṇecchā cābhinne || 604 ||
yathāvā
