tvāmavigaṇayya yo’nyattāmarasavilocanāśrayeddaivam | sa śiromaṇiṃ vidhūnvanpiśunaśirasā bibhartyayaḥpiṇḍam || 605 ||
yathāvā
niravadhidivyavibhūterbhavato vibhavaṃ samarpayedyo’nyam | kanakācalasya sīsākalitābharaṇaṃ rameśa sa hi datte || 606 ||
yathāvā
apalilapiṣati murāre bhūmānaṃ yastavātisīmānam | cicchādayiṣati kāmaṃ pāṇitalenākhilaṃ sa gaganatalam || 607 ||
yathāvā
anyāṃ vivakṣati kathāṃ mānyāṃ yastāvakīṃ kathāṃ hitvā | śeṣagirīśa jighatsatyūṣarareṇuṃ sitāṃ vihāyaiṣaḥ || 608 ||
yathāvā
phaṇigiripatisamamanyaṃ bhaṇati suraṃ yastu paṇḍitaṃmanyaḥ | maṇisadṛśaṃ loṣṭaṃ kharaghṛṇisadṛśaṃ timiramapi bhaṇati soyam || 609 ||
atra saptamudāharaṇeṣu ādyayorabheda ekavākyagataḥ| antimeṣu tu bhinnavākyagataḥ | tatrāpi ‘tvāmavigaṇayya’ ityādiṣu caturṣu vastumātrayoraupamyamūlo’bhedaḥ| antime “phaṇigiri’ iti | padye tvaupyayoraupamyamūlassa iti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
kalaśena jaladhisalilaṃ sakalaṃ śaknoti yo hi parimātum | akhilaṃ bhavato vibhavaṃ manasā sa bhavetkṣamaḥ paricchettum || 610 ||
atropamānopameyavākyārthayoḥ prātilomyenābhedaḥ mitthyādhyavasitisaṃkīrṇatvaṃ ca pūrvodāharaṇebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
vṛṣagirimalaṃkariṣṇorviṣṇorvīryāṇi sa hi vaditumīṣṭe | prabhaviṣṇuḥ parimātuṃ rajāṃsi yaḥ pārthivāni nikhilāni || 611 ||
atra ‘viṣṇornu kaṃ vīryāṇi pravocaṃ yaḥ pārthivāni vimame rajāṃgsi’ iti śrutyarthānudhāvanaṃ viśeṣaḥ | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavadeva | śrutāvapīdṛśālaṅkārasadbhāvaṃ jñāpayitumidam | evaṃ ‘tava mahimānaṃ’ iti prāgudāhṛtapadye’pi bodhyam | eṣā vākyārthanidarśanetyucyate | viśiṣṭārthayoḥ prakṛtaikadharmigatayorārthābhede nidarśanāyā iṣṭeḥ | asyāṃ ca ghaṭakapadārthānāṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvosvaśyaṃbhāvī ||
mālārūpā’pīyaṃ dṛśyate | yathā
vikirati sa sudhāṃ paritaḥ kṣipati śaraccandracandrikādhārām | vitarati candanacarcāṃ sucāru yo harikathāṃ pracārayati || 612 || idaṃ cāparaṃ bodhyaṃ
vātātmaṃbharigirivaranetā ceto mamaiṣa yadayāsīt | velātītaṃ duritaṃ śūlākārṣīttadetaditi manye ||
śūlākārṣīt śūlenāpākṣīdityarthaḥ | ‘śūlātpāke’ iti śūlaśabdāḍḍāc | atra ‘bhāvapradhānamākhyātam’ iti yāskoktarītyā kriyāviśeṣyakavodhavādināṃ mate śābda evābhedāropaḥ kriyayoriti mukhaṃ candra ityādāviva rūpakamucitam | prathamāntaviśeṣyakabodhavādināṃ tvārthassa iti nidarśaneti bhedaḥ ||
padārthanidarśanā yathā
dinavigamasamayavikasannavakuvalayavalayavilasadasitarucim | jayatu cirāya dadhāno dayitaḥ kalaśāmburāśikanyāyāḥ || 614 ||
atra kuvalayadalabhagavadrucyorāśrayabhedādbhinnayorapi sādṛśyamūlastādrūpyābhimānaḥ āropo vā bhagavati kuvalayaruceḥ ||
yathāvā
bhavadavaparitapto’yaṃ bhavadīyakṛpāsudhāpluto’dya hare | tuṅgātapasaṅgārditagaṅgāhradamagnagajasukhaṃ dhatte ||
atra ‘eṣa brahma praviṣṭo’smi grīṣme śītamiva hṛdam’ iti pramāṇacchāyā’nusaṃhitā | atra tuṅgātapasaṅgārditatvagaṅgāhradanimagnatvābhyāṃ bhavadavaparitaptatvabhagavatkṛpāsudhāplutatvayoḥ pratibimbanamiti pūrvasmādudāharaṇādbhedaḥ rūpakasaṃkīrṇatvaṃ ca | āśrayabhedabhidurayossukhayossādṛśyahetukastādrūpyābhimānastu tulya eva|| yathāvā
caladalakānanacandiravilolamakhilāmba nayanayugalaṃ te | vistṛtajālajalāśayavicalanmīnadvayaśriyaṃ vahate || 616 ||
atrāpi caladalakatvavistṛtajālatvayoḥ ānanacandirajalāśayayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ vilolanayanayugalacalanmīnadvayayostu vastuprativastubhāvakarambhitassa iti viśeṣaḥ | anyattulyam ||
asyāṃ copamānopameyagatadharmayorārthābhedapratipattiḥ | ataḥ padārthanidarśanetyucyate | atra ca padārthanidarśanāyāṃ bimbapratibimbabhāvastūpamānopameyayossaviśeṣaṇatve bhavati, yathā avyavahitodāharaṇayoḥ | anyathā tu neti vivekaḥ | nanu vākyārthanidarśanāyāṃ viśiṣṭavācakaśabdābhyāṃ viśeṣaṇayorapyupādānādastunāmopāttayorārthābhedaḥ| padārthanidarśanāyāṃ tūpamānabhūtarucyāderanyatarasyaivopāttatva na dvayoriti cet, ruciśabdena rucitvena dvayorapyupāttatvāt | na hyupamānopameyatāvacchedakarūpeṇopāttatvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ, yenāvyāptissyāt | yadvā prāguktalakṣaṇaṃ vākyārthanidarśanāyā eva, na padārthanidarśanāyāḥ | asyāstvevamastu lakṣaṇaṃ
padārthapūrvā sā’nyā syādupamānopameyayoḥ |
yatrānyataradharmasyānyatrāropaṇamucyate ||
upamānopameyayoranyataradharmasyānyatrāropaḥ padārthanidarśanetyarthaḥ | nanvevamapi vākyārthanidarśanāyāṃ rūpakadhvaninā padārthanidarśanāyāṃ ca nigīryādhyavasānarūpayā’tiśayoktyā gatārthateti cenna | vākyārthanidarśanāyāṃ rūpakasya guṇībhūtatvena taddhvanitvāyogāt | anyathā guṇībhūtopamayā rūpakasyāpi cāritārthyāpatteḥ | kiṃca, asyāśśarīraṃ tādṛśapadārthayoḥ parasparābhedamātramubhayatra viśrāntam | rūpakasya tūpameyagata upamānābhedaḥ, ātiśayokteśca nigaraṇānigaraṇābhyāṃ tayorviśeṣa ityanyadetat | evaṃca sphuṭamevāsyā rūpakātiśayoktibhyāṃ vailakṣaṇyam | ata eva ‘kalaśena jaladhisalilaṃ sakalaṃ, vṛṣagirimalaṃkariṣṇoḥ’ iti prāgudāhṛtapadyayorarthayoḥ paurvāparye’pi na cārutāhāniḥ | evaṃ ‘tava mahimānamanantaṃ’ ityādipadyeṣvapi kāṃkṣatītyādau kāṃkṣannityādyanūdya icchannityādau icchedityādividhāne’pīti bodhyam | atra vyaṅgyarūpakādau tūcyamāne vyaṅgyakakṣyoddeśyavidheyabhāvasyāpi vācyakakṣyoddeśyavidheyabhāvānurodhitayā ‘kalaśena jaladhisalilaṃ sakalaṃ śaknoti yo hi parimātum’ ityādau kalaśakaraṇakasakalajaladhisalilakarmakaparimāṇaśaktimadādirūpe upamāne manaḥkaraṇakākhilabhagavadvibhavakarmakaparicchedakṣamarūpopameyābhedasyaiva pratyetavyatayā rūpakāsāmañjasyāpatterityālaṃkārikasārvabhaumairākalanīyam ||
evaṃ padārthanidarśanāyāṃ lakṣaṇavailakṣaṇye’pyudāharaṇāni prākpradarśitānyeva samanvayaprakārabhedena yojanīyāni | sopi vakṣyamāṇarītyā buddhimadbhirūhanīyaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvāmastutvā bhagavanyo bhavati stavanakārako’nyeṣām | sadbhiḥ kṛto nirastassatyaṃ vanakākatāṃ vahatyeṣaḥ || 617 ||
sadbhiḥ brahmavidbhiḥ | nirastaḥ pratyādiṣṭaḥ kutassan vanakākatāṃ araṇyavāyasatāṃ, atinikarṣadyotanāya vanetiviśeṣaṇam | vahati | pakṣe stavanakārakaśabdaḥ nirastaḥ nirgatau rephastakārau yasya sa tathoktassan vanakāka iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | anyatsugamam | atra vanakākatvasyopamānadharmasya upameye stavanakārake āropaḥ| sa caupamye paryavasyati | vanakākavāśitavadbhagavadanyastavanamanarthakamamanojñamaśravyaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ | atra śabdaparārthāntaravarṇanaṃ camatkārātiśayāya ||
yathāvā
satyapi tavābhidhāne mudāṃ nidhāne mudhaiva yo mukharaḥ | satyaṃ sa hi munnyūnassaṃprathayati sārasākṣa kharabhāvam || 618 ||
he sārasākṣa mudāṃ ānandānāṃ nidhāne nidhibhūte muktyaiśvaryaparyantasarvavidhānandapradānadhuraṃdhare iti bhāvaḥ | tava abhidhāne nāmani satyapi tvadabhidhānamanādṛtyetyarthaḥ | yaḥ mudhaiva nirarthakameva mukharaḥ vācāṭaḥ bhavati saḥ pumān mudā nyūnassan ānandanidhānabhūtanāmābhilāpavaidhuryāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe muvarṇena nyūnaḥ viśliṣṭassan | asminpakṣe nyū ityatra nakārasya ‘anaci ca’ iti vaikalpikaṃ dvitvaṃ pakṣāntarārthānurūpyāyeti bodhyam | kharasya rāsabhasya bhāvaṃ svabhāvaṃ ceṣṭāṃ vā ‘bhāvassattvāsvabhāvābhiprāyaceṣṭātmajanmasu’ ityamaraḥ | saṃprathayati | uktaprakārabhagavannāmābhilāpavidhuratayā’timātraparuṣāśrāvyavyarthālāpaissamayaṃ , kṣipanrāsabhatulya eveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe mukharaśabdo muvarṇacyāvane kharatvaṃ prakāśayatītyarthaḥ | atrāpi bhagavannāmābhilāpavidhuravṛthālāpavidhāyinyupameye upamānabhūtakharadharmāropaḥ| camatkārāntaraṃ ca pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
sumanoramohimānyāmalamūrtirasau śivābhyudayahetuḥ | bhujagādhirājaśailo bhajate himavanmahībhṛta2śailīm || 619 ||
sumanoramaḥ atimanojña | ramayatīti ramaḥ pacādyac | sumanasāṃ vibudhānāṃ rama iti vā śeṣādripakṣe | himavatpakṣe tu śobhanā manoramā tadabhidhā jāyā yasya sa tathoktaḥ,
purā manoramā nāma sumerorabhavatsutā |
gṛhamedhī tayaivāsīccakravartī dharābhṛtām ||
ityukteḥ | ahibhiḥ mānyā amalā mūrtiryasya sa tathoktaḥ tasya bhujagādhirājarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā hīti bhinnaṃ padaṃ prasiddhyarthakam | mānyā amalā mūrtiryasya sa tathoktaḥ | pakṣe himānyā himasaṃhatyā amalā viśadā mūrtiryasyeti | śivānāṃ kalyāṇānāṃ śivāyāḥ umāyāśca abhyudayasya upacayasya janmanaśca hetuḥ | atrāpyupamānadharmasyopameye āropaḥ pūrvavadeva śleṣottambhitatvaṃ ca ||
yathāvā
taruṇatamālaviyattalamarakatajalanidhivalāhitaśilāsu | suṣamāsitimā bhavato vaṣagirimāṇikya viharati svairam || 620 || he vṛṣagirimāṇikyeti saṃbodhanam | suṣamāyāḥ asitimā nailyam ||
yathāvā
idamacyutasudatīmaṇipadamiti vadati śrutirjalajamucitam | mṛdulasmeramanojñe padalakṣmīryadiha dṛśyate tasyāḥ || 621 ||
acyutasudatīmaṇeḥ śriyaḥ padaṃ caraṇaṃ sthānaṃ ca | padalakṣmīḥ caraṇaśobhā ||
yathāvā
siṃhalakhanijanitamaṇīsaṃhatiṣu daśāsyapuraniśācaryaḥ | saṃhananarucaṃ tava raghusiṃha samīkṣyādhunā’pi muhyanti || 622 ||
atrādyeṣu ‘dinavigamasamaya’ ityādāvudāharaṇānāṃ ṣaṭke upameye upamānadharmāropaḥ | tatrāpi pūrvatrike śuddhaḥ antime trike ‘tvāmastutvā’ ityādau śleṣasaṃkīrṇaḥ | anantaraṃ ‘taruṇatamāla’ ityādau udāharaṇatraye upamāne upameyadharmāropaḥ | tatrāpyantime’ sihalakhani’ itipadye nidarśanayā bhrāntimadalaṃkāro vyajyata iti vailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhyam | ubhayatrāpyanyadharmasyānyatrāsaṃbhavena tatsadṛśadharmākṣepādaupamye paryavasānaṃ tu tulyameva | imāṃ padārthavṛttinidarśanāṃ lalitopameti vyavāhārṣījjayadevaḥ ||
alaṃkārasarvasvakārastu
tvatpādanakharatnānāṃ yadalaktakamārjanam |
idaṃ śrīkhaṇḍalepena pāṇḍurīkaraṇaṃ vidhoḥ ||
iti padyaṃ vākyārthanidarśanāyāmudājahāra | āha ca yatra tu prakṛtavākyārthe vākyārthāntaramāropyate sāmānādhikaraṇyena tatra sambandhānupapāttamūlā nidarśanaiva yukteti | tanna, vākyārtharūpakasya dattajalāñjalitvāpatteḥ | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, vākyārthanidarśanaiva nirvāsyatāṃ svīkriyatāṃ ca vākyārtharūpakamiti paryanuyogasyāpi tulyatvāt | yuktaṃ caitat, padārtharūpake mukhaṃ candra ityādau | klṛptasya śrautasyābhedāropasya ‘rūpakajīvātutvakalpanāyā aucityāt ‘induśobhāṃ vahatyāsyam’ ityādipadārthanidarśanāyāmabhedāropasyābhāvāttajjīvātutvāyogācca | rūpake bimbanaṃ nāstīti tu śapathamātraṃ yuktyabhāvāt, asmaduktodāharaṇe vākyārthanidarśanāyāssāvakāśatvācca | yattu tenaiva lakṣaṇaṃ nirmitaṃ ‘sambhavatā’sambhavatā vā vastusambandhena gamyamānamaupamyaṃ nidarśanā, iti tadapi na, rūpakātiśayoktyādiṣvativyāpanāt | yadi tu
tvatpādanakharatnāni yo rañjayati yāvakaiḥ |
induṃ candanalepena pāṇḍurīkurute hi saḥ ||
iti nirmīyate padyaṃ tadā nidarśanā yuktā | na cāsmaduktā vācyā nidarśanā, iya tu pratīyamāneti vācyam | mukhaṃ candra iveti vācyopamā | mukhaṃ candra iti pratīyamānā, na tvalaṅkārāntaramityasyāpi suvacatvāt | evaṃcāropādhyavasānamārgabahirbhūta ārtha evābhedo nidarśanājīvitam | sa ca kartrādyabhedapratipādanadvārā pratipādyate vākyārthanidarśanāyām| pare tu ‘tvatpādanakharatnānām’ ityatra dṛṣṭāntālaṅkāramāhuḥ | tadapyasat, bimbapratibimbabhāvāpannapadārthaghaṭitasya nirapekṣavākyārthadvayasyaiva dṛṣṭāntatvāt | tasmāt ‘tvatpādanakha’ ityatra vākyārtharūpakameva na nidarśaneti sthitamiti vimarśinyanurodhini rasagaṅgādhare pratyapādi | evamasambhavadvastusaṃbandhanibandhanā vākyārthapadavṛttinidarśanā darśitā ||
sadarthanidarśanā
sadasadbodhanaṃ syāccetkriyayā’nyā nidarśanā ||
kasyacitkiṃcitkriyāviśiṣṭasya svakriyayā parānprati sato’sato vā’rthasya bodhanaṃ yannibadhyate sā’nyā sambhavadvastusambandhanibandhanā nidarśanetyarthaḥ ||
yathā
sarasijanayano mānyaśirasā satataṃ mahattvamabhilaṣatā | iti sumahānahiśailo vibodhayaṃstaṃ śikhāmaṇīkurute || 623 ||
mahattvaṃ pūjyatvam | sumahān atipūjyaḥ atipṛthulaśca | atra kārīṣo’gniradhyāpayati bhikṣā vāsayatītivadānukūlye ṇicaḥ prayogādahiśailasya ca bhagavacchikhāmaṇīkaraṇarūpasya mahatvābhilāṣijanagatabhagavatkarmakaśiromānanaviṣayakabodhānukūlācaraṇasya saṃbhavānmayevānyenāpi bhagavān śirasā mānya ityaupamyasadbhāvāśca saṃbhavadvastusaṃbandhamūlā’pei nidarśanā saṃbhavati | na ca vibodhayanniti gamyotprekṣaiva syāditi vācyaṃ, vastuto vibodhanasaṃbhavādeva tasyāḥ prasaktyabhāvāt | evameva hi
cūḍāmaṇipade dhatte yo devaṃ ravimāgatam |
satāṃ kāryā’’titheyīti bodhayangṛhamedhinaḥ ||
ityatrāhuḥ | idaṃ ca ‘dhātunoktakriye nityaṃ kārake kartṛteṣyate’ ityuktena pathā dhātūpāttavyāpārāśrayatvaṃ kartṛtvamiti mate saṃgacchate | yadi tu yatnārthakātkṛñastṛci saviṣayārthakadhātūttarakartṛpratyayasyāśrayatve nirūḍhalakṣaṇayā yatnāśrayaḥ kartṛpadārthaḥ | sa eva ca kartṛpratyayānāṃ mukhyārthaḥ acetanastu bhākta iti nyāyena paryālocyate tadā bodhayannityatra gamyotprekṣā saṃbhavatyevetyapyāhuḥ | tadvadatrāpyutprekṣā saṃbhavatīti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
saṃsiddhyaiva śrīmānbhavati mahībhṛcchirobhipūjya padaḥ | iti darśayituṃ phaṇabhṛtpatigiriśikhare padaṃ nyadhācchauriḥ || 624 ||
saṃsiddhyā prakṛtyaiva ‘saṃsiddhiprakṛtī ime’ ityamaraḥ | padaṃ caraṇaṃ sthānaṃ vā | atra bhagavān svakīyayā phaṇigiriśikharādhikaraṇakacaraṇādhānakriyayā parānprati nisargata eva śrīmānmahībhṛcchiro’bhipūjyapado bhavatīti sadarthaṃ bodhayannibaddha iti bhavatīyaṃ sadarthanidarśanā | evamuttaratrāpi ||
yathāvā
vanamālāmāśritamapi lakṣmīrna jahāti puruṣamatipuṇyam | iti vṛṣaśikhariṇi lakṣmīrvanamālina urasi satatamapi vasati || 625 ||
atiśayitaṃ puṇyaṃ yasya taṃ atimātrasukṛtaśālinamityarthaḥ| bhagavatpakṣe atikrāntaḥ puṇyaṃ atipuṇyaḥ taṃ tathoktaṃ ‘na sukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno’to nivartante, tasyoditi nāma sa eṣa sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhya uditaḥ’ ityādiśrutibhyaḥ | pāpmaśabdo’tra puṇyapāparūpobhayavidhakarmapara iti sthāpitamākareṣu | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam || ādye udāharaṇe vibodhayanniti śātrā dvitīye darśayitumiti tumunnantena śabdena bodhanam | iha tu gamyamānaṃ tat | ādyaṃ śuddhaṃ anye tu śleṣasaṃkīrṇe iti ca viśeṣaḥ ||
asadarthanidarśanā yathā
sadgotrapravaro’pi prabhuṇā bahuvañcako hyadhaḥkriyate | iti darśayate hariṇā phaṇigiriradharīkṛto bahukroṣṭā || 626 ||
gotraṃ pravaraśca gotrapravarau, santau gotrapravarau yasya sa tathoktopi satkulaprasūtopītyarthaḥ | pakṣe uttamaśailaśreṣṭho’pi | bahūnāṃ vañcakaḥ atisaṃghātā | pakṣe bahavo vañcakāḥ sṛgālāḥ yasmiṃstathoktaḥ adhaḥkriyate nyakkriyate adharīkriyate ca | atra phaṇigirirbhagavatkartṛkasvakarmakādhaḥkaraṇakriyayā svakīyayā dṛṣṭāntabhūtayā anyo’pyevaṃ sadgotrapravaro’pi bahuvañcakaścetprabhuṇā’dhaḥkriyata ityasadarthaṃ bodhayannibaddha iti bhavatīyamasadarthanidarśanā ||
yathāvā
bhagavanmukhaprasādāsahanānāṃ gatiriyaṃ bhavitrīti | saṃdarśayan janānamindurupaidrauravānubandhamalam ||
indorbhagavanmukhaprasādāsahanatvaṃ ca tadaupamye paryavasyatīti rahasyam | rauravasya tannāmno narakasya anubandhaṃ saṃbandhaṃ alaṃ upait | vastusthitistu ruroḥ mṛgasyāyaṃ rauravaḥ | aṇ | sa cāsāvanubandhaśca tena malaṃ kalaṅkamiti | atra rauravānubandhaṃ bhajannindurātmavadbhagavanmukhaprasādāsahiṣṇūnāṃ rauravaprāptirbhaviṣyatītyasadarthaṃ bodhayannibaddhaḥ || yathāvā
vaiśvānaro murāre tvattejasspardhināṃ gatiṃ prathayan | vaimukhyabhāk svavarṇe śvāmadhya upaidathāntyajanaratvam || 628 ||
vaiśvānaraḥ tvattejasā saha spardhamānānāṃ gatiḥ evaṃ bhavitrīti prathayan san svavarṇe svakīyavarṇe svavācakākṣarasamudāye ca | jātyabhiprāyakamekavacanam | vaimukhyaṃ vimukhatāṃ bhajatīti tathoktaḥ svavarṇādbhraṣṭa iti yāvat | pakṣe vai ityākārakaṃ mukhyaṃ mukhe bhavaṃ varṇaṃ akṣaraṃ bhajatīti tathokta ityarthaḥ | ata eva madhye etasminnantare śvā śunakassan | pakṣe śvā iti varṇo madhye yasya saḥ śvāmadhya ityarthaḥ | atha anantaraṃ antyajanaratvaṃ caṇḍālatāṃ upait labdhavān ‘śvānayoniśataṃ prāpya caṇḍāleṣvabhijāyate’ ityuktarītyeti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe antye antimabhāge jātau narau nakārarephau yasya sa tathoktaḥ tasya bhāvaṃ upait | atra vaimukhyādibhāgvaiśvānaraḥ bhagavattejasspardhināṃ gatirīdṛśītyasadarthaṃ bodhayannibaddhaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvadrucihīnohantāmamatāspadatāṃ masāra etyādau | bhūtvā’sāraḥ prathayatyanarthakāraṇamahaṃmamagrāham || 629 ||
masāraḥ indranīlaḥ ‘masāra indranīle syāt’ ityukteḥ ‘masāragalvarkamukhaḥ’ iti śrīrāmāyaṇe prayogaśca | tvadrucihīnaḥ tvayi rucyā prītyā hīnaḥ | pakṣe tvadrucyapekṣayā hīnaḥ nyūnaruciriti yāvat | ādau prathamaṃ ahantāmamatayaḥ ahaṃkāramamakārayoḥ | ahaṃmametyubhayamapi vibhaktipratirūpakamavyayaṃ, tābhyāṃ tal | āspadatāṃ āśrayatāṃ etya ata eva asāraḥ nissāraḥ bhūtvā ahaṃmamagrāhaṃ ahaṃmamābhimānaṃ anarthakāraṇaṃ prathayati |
anātmanyātmabuddhiryā asve svamiti yā matiḥ |
avidyātarusaṃbhūtibījametaddvidhā sthitam ||
ityādyuktarītyā saṃsāracakrabhramaṇapūrvakanissāratālakṣaṇānarthahetutāṃ bodhayatītyabhiprāyaḥ | pakṣe masāraśabdaḥ tvadrucihīnaḥ masāraśabde bhagavadrucihīnatoktiśśabdārthatādātmyābhimānakṛtā | hanta amaṃ atāspadatāmiti cchedaḥ | ādau prathamabhāge amaṃ makārarahitaṃ yathāsyāttathā atāspadatāṃ atāyāḥ avarṇatvasya āspadaṃ āśrayaḥ avarṇa ityarthaḥ | tasya bhāvaṃ atvaṃ etya makāralopena tatraivākāratāṃ prāpyetyarthaḥ | ‘na girā gireti brūyādairaṃ kṛtvodgāyet’ ityatra girāpadaṃ pratiṣidhya vidhīyamānabhirāpadaṃ yathā girāpadasthāna eva bhavati, evamihāpi mavarṇaṃ pratiṣidhya vidhīyamānaḥ avarṇopi tadīya eva sthāne bhavatīti dhyeyam | evaṃ sarvatredṛśasthaleṣu draṣṭavyam | ata eva asāraḥ bhūtvā uktarītyā makārātsāraṇena akāranyasanena ca asāra iti niṣpanna ityabhiprāyaḥ | sphuṭamanyat | atra ahaṃtāmamatāspadībhūtatayā bhagavatyaruciṃ bhajamāno’sārobhavanmasāraḥ ahaṃmamagrāhaṃ uktavidhānarthakāraṇamityasadarthaṃ prathayannibaddhaḥ | śabdārthatādātmyāvalambiśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyamūlātiśayanirvyūḍheyam | evaṃ pūrvodāharaṇe’pi draṣṭavyam || yathāvā
apyuccairjanmajuṣo jaḍajantvanugāḥ patantyadho’dha iti | marudātmambhariparivṛḍhagiriśikharānnirjharāḥ praṇipatanti || 630 ||
atrādho’dhaḥ patantīti bodhayanta iti vaktavye bodhayanta ityasya gamyamānatvādaprayogaḥ | tataśva uccaiḥ unnatapradeśe janmajuṣopi jaḍajantvanugāmitayā svayaṃpatanakriyāviśiṣṭā nirjharāḥ svakīyayā patanakriyayā dṛṣṭāntabhūtayā anye’pyevaṃvidhā adho’dhaḥ patantītyaniṣṭaparyavasāyinamarthaṃ bodhayanta eva patantītyevaṃvidhārthanibandhanādasadarthanidarśanā śleṣottambhiteti dhyeyam ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre nidarśanāsara ekaviṃśaḥ
<23>
atha vyatirekālaṃkārasaraḥ
uktaḥ kaścidviśeṣaścedupamānopameyayoḥ|
tamāhurvyatirekākhyamalaṃkāraṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ ||
adhunā bhedapradhānālaṃkāravarṇanam | tatra upamānopameyayoryathākathaṃcidbhedavarṇanaṃ vyatirekaḥ| tena upameyotkarṣe upamānotkarṣe ubhayotkarṣāpakarṣāpratītāvapi yatkiṃcidvaidharmyamātre’pyayaṃ bhavatīti dhyeyam || tatropamayotkarṣe yathā
uragācalapatimānasamudadhermadhyamiva bhāti gambhīram | mandarasaṃkṣubhitamabhūccaramaṃ prathamaṃ tu no kadācidapi || 631 ||
mandareṇa saṃkṣubhitaṃ, pado mando rasaḥ yasya tat kṣubhitaṃ vyākulitaṃ ca | spaṣṭamanyat ||
yathāvā
añjanagirināyakatanurasitimarucirā cakāsti yamuneva | tapanaṃ dūrīkurute prathamā caramā tu tapanatanujātā || 632 ||
tapanaṃ narakaviśeṣam | tapanasya bhānoḥ tanujātā tanayā ‘tapano duṣkare’pi syādbhāskare nirayāntare’ iti medinī ||
yathāvā
kiraṇasphuṭāravindaḥ prabhāvitānemitasphuranmūrtiḥ| raviriva harihetīśassa hi durdarśassudarśano’yaṃ tu || 633 ||
harerbhagavataḥ hetīśaḥ cakrarājaḥ raviriva kiraṇaiḥ sphuṭāni vikacāni aravindāni yasya sa tathoktaḥ | anyatra kiraṇaiḥ sphuṭāḥ pravyaktāḥ ye arāḥ parito nābhyarpitaśalākāḥ ‘araṃ śīghre ca cakrāṅke’ iti medinī | tānvindatīti tathoktaḥ | karmaṇyaṇ | prabhāvitāne tejomaṇḍale | yadvā prakṛṣṭā bhāḥ yeṣāṃ te prabhāsaḥ agnyādayastejasvinaḥ teṣāṃ vitāne samudaye amitaṃ aparicchinnaṃ yathāyathā sphurantī mūrtiryasya sa tathoktaḥ | anyatra prabhāyāḥ avitā rakṣakaḥ prāptā vā| avateḥ rakṣaṇārthakādavāptyarthakādvā tācchīlye tṛn | nemitaḥ nemyā paritasthitena valayena sphuranmūrtiḥ ‘cakraṃṃ rathāṅgaṃ tasyānte nemisstrī’ ityamaraḥ | saḥ raviḥ durdarśaḥ ayaṃ hetīśaḥ sudarśanaḥ śobhanaṃ darśanaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ| sudarśanaśabdaḥ puṃstve’pyanuśiṣṭaḥ | tathāca medinī ‘sudarśano hareścakre merujambūdrume pumān’ iti ‘sudarśanostriyāṃ cakre’ iti nāmanidhānaṃ ca | māghaśca ‘bandhureṣa jagatāṃ sudarśanaḥ’ iti prāyuṅkta ||
yathāvā
niśvasanavicaladalakaṃ lasadalikaṃ nalinamiva bhavadvadanam | janani paraṃ tvatrāntyaṃ kanati tarāmabjakaraśubhālokaiḥ || 634 ||
niśvasanena vicalantaḥ alakāḥ cūrṇakuntalāḥ yasya tattathoktam | pakṣe nitarāṃ śvasanena marutā vicalantīti vicalāni, pacādyac | dalāni patrāṇi yasya tattathoktaṃ ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | lasat alikaṃ lalāṭaṃ yasya tathoktaṃ ‘lalāṭamalikaṃ’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe lasantaḥ alayo bhramarāḥ yasmiṃstattathoktaṃ, pūrvavatkap | paraṃtu antyaṃ bhavadvadanaṃ abjasya candramasaḥ karāṇāṃ kiraṇānāṃ śubhaiḥ ālokaiḥ prakāśaiḥ, pakṣe abjaḥ śaṅkhaḥ kare yasya sa tathoktaḥ bhagavānityarthaḥ | tasya śubhaiḥ kalyāṇaiḥ ālokaiḥ kaṭākṣaiḥ kanatitarām || yathāvā
parakīyaḥ pakkaṇarāṭṇṭṭaṇarāṅbhavati tāvakīnastu hare | sidhyati nṝṇāmanayorutkaṭasaṃyogamātrato rūpabhidā || 635 ||
he hare parakīyaḥ tvaditaropāsakaḥ pakkaṇarāṭ śabaranivāsabhūtakugrāmamātraprakāśavān bhavati | pakkaṇe rājata iti vigrahe kvip ‘pakkaṇaśśābarālayaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tāvakīnaḥ tvāmupāsīnastu paṭṭaṇarāṭ rājadhānyāḥ rājā bhavati ‘rājñi rāṭ’ ityamaraḥ | ‘paṭṭaṇaṃ puṭabhedanam’ iti paṭhanto vācaspatiprabhṛtayaḥ tavargyamadhyāntyapattanaśabdavaṭṭavargyamadhyāntyapaṭṭaṇaśabdopyastīti manyante | ata eva ‘aṭṭaśūlāḥ katipaye paṭṭaṇe’sminpratiṣṭhitāḥ ‘ iti prayogaśca prāsabaddhassaṃgacchate | rūḍhaścāyaṃ śabdo nātra vipratipattavyam |anayoḥ pakkaṇapaṭṭaṇarājoḥ utkaṭo’timātraḥ yassaṃyogassaṃsargaḥ tanmātreṇa tāvataiva nṝṇāṃ saṃsargiṇāṃ narāṇāṃ rūpābhidā sidhyati ‘saṃsargajā doṣaguṇā bhavanti’ iti nyāyātpakkaṇasaṃsargeṇa mahatāmapi nihīnatvaṃ paṭṭaṇarāṭsaṃsargeṇa nihīnānāmapi mahattvaṃ cetyevaṃrūpabhedassidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | utkṛṣṭaḥ kaṭayorvarṇayoḥ yassaṃyogaḥ ‘halo’nantarāssaṃyogaḥ, ityuktalakṣaṇaḥ aucityāt kakārasya kakāreṇa ṭakārasya ṭakāreṇaiva saṃyogo’tra vivakṣitaḥ | utkaṭasaṃyoga eva utkaṭasaṃyogamātraṃ tasmāt tataḥ rūpabhidā sidhyati | saṃyuktakakāraṭakārābhyāmeva pakkaṇarāṭpaṭṭaṇarāṭchabdayorvailakṣaṇyaṃ nānyavarṇeṣviti bhāvaḥ | atrādye udāharaṇacatuṣṭaye vyatirekamūlabhūtā upamā śābdī | atra tu upāsanādiprayuktā gamyeti bhidā || yathāvā
karibhayakārī giriśaḥ karibhayavārī phaṇādharagirīśaḥ | varṇe dhavalāsitayoranayoḥ kāvā bhidā’sti haraharyoḥ || 636 ||
giriśaḥ dhūrjaṭiḥ kariṇaḥ gajāsurasya bhayakārī, phaṇādharāgirīśastu karibhayavārī gajendrabhayanivārayitā | varṇe varṇaviṣaye dhavalāsitayoḥ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ sitanīlayoḥ anayoḥ haraharyoḥ kā vā abhidā asti na kā’pyabhidā, kiṃtu bheda eva spaṣṭa ityarthaḥ | pakṣe varṇe akṣare viṣaye kāvābhidā kāvābhayamevabhedaḥ karibhayakārikaribhayavāriśabdayoḥ kāvāvarṇābhyāmeva bhedo na varṇāntareṣvityarthaḥ | kiṃca haraharyoḥ kāvā kā’pi abhidā akāreṇaiva bhidā haraśabde dvāvakārau hariśabdetveka eva sa iti bheda ityarthaḥ | atra bhayakāryapekṣayā bhayavāriṇa utkarṣassarvalokasākṣikaḥ, bhayābhayapradayorabhayapradasyaiva śreyastvāt | atra hariśabdasya ‘dvaṃdve ghi, abhyarhitaṃ pūrvam’ iti pūrvaprayogāvaśyaṃbhāve’pi ‘lakṣaṇahetvoḥ’ iti sūtranirdeśāttasya prakaraṇasyānityatvamanusṛtaṃ vivakṣitacamatkārānurodhāditi dhyeyam | ihāpi pūrvodāharaṇavadīśvaraśabdavācyatvādiprayuktamaupamyaṃ gamyam ||
yathāvā
sārasanābhikhyāḍhyā daśanālistava madamba raśa nāliśva | mukhyā’’dyā madhyasthā parā’nayorbhavati devi daramātrabhidā || 637 || he devi madamba sā prasiddhā tava daśanāliḥ rasanāyāḥ rasajñāyāḥ abhikhyayā śobhayā āḍhyā paripakvadāḍimabījarājivatpāṭalavarṇatayā bhāsamānetyarthaḥ | raśanāliḥ mekhalāpaṅktiśca | yadvā raśanā ālisseturiva raśanāliḥ ‘seturālau striyāṃ pumān’ ityamaraḥ | striyāṃ vidyamānāyāmālau setuḥ pumāniti kośārthaḥ| sārasanamityabhikhyayā nāmnā āḍhyā ‘saptakī raśanā tathā | klībe sārasanaṃ ca, abhikhyānāmaśobhayoḥ’ iti cāmaraḥ | anayormadhye ādyā daśanāliḥ mukhyā śreṣṭhā mukhe bhavā ca, aparā raśanāliḥ madhyasthā madhyamā avalagnasthā ca | anayoḥ daramātrī alpaparimāṇā bhidā bhedaḥ| daramātrī ca sā bhidā ceti vigrahaḥ | mukhyatvamadhyasthatve eva bhedo nānya ityarthaḥ | pakṣe darābhyāṃ dakārarephābhyāmeva bhedaḥ daśanāliraśanāliśabdayostāvataiva bhedaḥ tulyatvādanyavarṇānupūrvyā iti bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
tava kṛpayā muktendrāvāvirbhūtasvarūpasaṃpannau | ekassvarāṭparastu svārāḍāsphūrtimāṃstayorbhedaḥ ||
he bhagavanniti prakaraṇāllabhyate | tava kṛpayā muktaḥ prakṛtibandhavinirmuktaḥ pumān indraśśakraśca muktendrau āvirbhūtasvarūpasaṃpannau | muktapakṣe āvirbhūtaṃ yatsvarūpaṃ apahatapāpmatvādibrāhmaguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ svarūpaṃ tena saṃpannaḥ, pūrvaṃ karmārabdhaśarīrasaṃbandhavattayā tirohitāpahatapāpmatvādiḥ paścāt ‘paraṃ jyotirupasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate’ ityuktarītyā’’virbhūtasvasvarūpa ityarthaḥ | atra saṃpadyāvirbhāvādhikaraṇārtho’nusaṃdheyaḥ | indrapakṣe āvirbhūtaḥ prādurbhūtaḥ prakāśamāna’ iti yāvat | yaḥ svaruḥ śatakoṭiḥ ‘śatakoṭissvaruśśambaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tena upasaṃpannaḥ indrasya vajrāyudhalābho’pi bhagavatkṛpāyatta eveti bhāvaḥ | ekaḥ anayormadhye prathamanirdiṣṭo muktaḥ svarāṭ svena rājata iti svarāṭ akarmavaśya ityarthaḥ | ‘sa svarāṭ bhavati’ iti śruteḥ | sūtritaṃ ca ‘ata eva cānanyādhipatiḥ’ iti | ‘apahatapāpmā satyasaṅkalpaḥ’ iti śravaṇādevākarmavaśya iti sūtrārthaḥ | paraḥ anantaranirdiṣṭa indrastu svārāṭ svena na rājata iti svārāṭ karmaparatantra ityarthaḥ | pakṣe svaḥ svargasya rāṭ rājā | svariti rephāntamavyayam | ‘rori’ iti rephalope ‘ḍhralope pūrvasya’ iti dīrghaḥ ‘svārāṇṇamucisūdanaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tayoḥ muktamahendrayoḥ bhedaḥ āsphūrtimān īṣatsphuraṇavān | āṅīṣadarthe | atyalpa eva bheda ityarthaḥ | yadvā ā samantāt, āṅbhividhau | sphūrtimān akarmavaśyakarmaparatantrayormahān bheda iti bhāvaḥ | āṅīṣadarthe’bhivyāptau’ ityamaraḥ | yadvā uktarītyā anayostaulye’pi bhedopi kaścitsaṃbhavedvā na veti mīmāṃsamānassmarannāha āḥ sphūrtimāniti | tayoḥ muktamahendrayoḥ bhedaḥ ekassvarāṭ anyassvārāḍiti vailakṣaṇyaṃ sphūrtimān idānīṃ smṛta ityarthaḥ | ā iti smaraṇārthakaṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñakamavyayam | ‘ā pragṛhyassmṛtau’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe āsphūrtimān hrasvākāradīrghākārābhyāṃ sphuraṇavān | svarāṭsvārāṭchabdayostāvataiva bhedadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
kamalānano hare tvaṃ kamalāsana eva tava suto jajñe | nasabhedo gaṇanīyo lokeśāviti yuvāṃ yato viditau || 639 || he hare ! tvaṃ kamalānanaḥ kamalāyāḥ śriyaḥ ananaḥ prāṇayitā | anyate anenetyananaḥ, kamalavadanaśca | tava sutaḥ brahmā kamalāsanaḥ śrīnirāsakaḥ asyate anenetyasanaḥ, kamalāyāḥ asana iti vigrahaḥ | padmopaviṣṭa iti ca | saḥ uktaprakāraḥ bhedaḥ vailakṣaṇyaṃ na gaṇanīyaḥ upekṣaṇīyaḥ atyalpabhedasyākiṃcitkaratvāditi bhāvaḥ | yato hetoḥ yuvāṃ sa ca tvaṃ ca yuvāṃ ‘tyadādīnāṃ mithassahoktau yatparaṃ tacchiṣyate’ ityanuśāsanātparabhūtayuṣmacchabdaikaśeṣaḥ | lokeśāviti viditau | lokeśatvena viditayoryuvayoḥ kiyānayaṃ bheda iti bhāvaḥ | prasiddhyati hi caturānanasyāpi lokeśatvaṃ ‘hiraṇyagarbho lokaśaḥ ityukteḥ | pakṣe nasābhyāṃ nasavarṇābhyāṃ bhedaḥ gaṇanīyaḥ | kamalānanakamalāsanayostāvataiva bhedadarśanāditi bhāva | atra ‘sārasanābhikhyāḍhyā’ ityādyudāharaṇatraye sārasanetyādiśleṣapratibhonmīlitatulyayogatānuprāṇito vyatireka iti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
patriṇyāsīcchyenī phaṇigirinātha tvadīyakīrtiśca | prathamā śaśādanīti prathitā’ntyā’’tmaujasā śaśinamatti || 640 ||
he phaṇigirinātha ! patriṇī tadākhyā kācidvihagī śyenī śyenākhyā āsīt ‘atha śaśādanaḥ | patrī śyenaḥ’ ityamaraḥ| ‘śyene patriśaśādanau’ ityamaramālā | “ śyenākhyo vihagaḥ patrī patriṇau śarapakṣiṇau’ iti śāśvataśca | patriśabdāt nāntalakṣaṇo ṅīp | jātilakṣaṇo ṅīṣ vā | tvadīyakīrtiśca śyenī āsīt ukto’rthaḥ | pakṣe śvetavarṇā abhūt ‘viśadaśyetapāṇḍurāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | śyetaśabdāt strītvavivakṣāyāṃ ‘varṇādanudāttāttopadhātto naḥ’ iti ṅīpsaṃniyogaśiṣṭastakārasya nakāraḥ | śyenaḥ pakṣiṇi pāṇḍure’ iti medinīkośāttu puṃsyapi nopadhaśśyenaśabdostīti jñāyate | tasmāt pippalyāderākṛtigaṇatvāt ṅīṣ bodhyaḥ prathamā tayorādyā patriṇī śaśamattīti śaśādanī prathitā tannāmnā prasiddhā | antyā tvadīyakīrtistu ātmaujasā nijatejasā śaśinaṃ śaśavantaṃ, indumiti vāstavārthaḥ | atti kabalayati ācchādayatīti yāvat ||
yathāvā
kāko rarāda hṛdayaṃ pikastu tava vacanadāsyato hṛdyaḥ| dvijayorapi janani tayoḥ kā’pi bhidā vyañjane svare’pyasti || 641 ||
he janani ! kākaḥ vāyasaḥ aindririti bhāvaḥ | tava hṛdayaṃ vakṣaḥ rarāda vilalekha ‘rada vilekhane ‘ liṭ | yadvā kākaḥ sādhāraṇo vāyasaḥ hṛdayaṃ śṛṇvatāṃ cittaṃ rarāda svarakārkaśyena udvejayāmāseti yāvat | pikaḥ kokilastu tava vacane dāsyataḥ dāsabhāvāt ‘tvadāsyadāsye’pi gato’dhikāritāṃ na śāradaḥ’ ityādāvivāsya āpamye paryavasānam | hṛdyaḥ tava hṛdayapriyo’bhūt | pakṣe śṛṇvatāṃ svaramādhuryeṇa manoharo’bhūdityarthaḥ | tayoḥ kākāpikayoḥ dvijayoḥ viprayossatorapi pakṣiṇossato’rapīti vastusthitiḥ | kā’pi anirvacanīyā bhidā bhedaḥ vyañjane cañcupakṣādicihne svare dhvanau ca asti ‘vyañjanaṃ lāñchanaśmaśruniṣṭhānāvayaveṣvapi’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe kākapikaśabdayoḥ kā pi iti varṇayorvidyamānā bhidā vyañjane kakārapakārarūpe halavarṇe svare ākārekārarūpe avarge’pi asti halajvarṇayorubhayorapyasti na tvantimakakārayoḥ, tatrobhayorapyaikarūpyadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ | udāharaṇadvaye’smin śleṣapratibhottambhitadīpakānuprāṇito vyatireka iti viśeṣaḥ | sa ca ‘uragācalapatimānasam’ ityādyetadanteṣvekādaśasu padyeṣūpameyotkarṣaparyavasāyīti dhyeyam ||
upamānotkarṣe yathā
ākāśagaṅgaṅgyāḍhyau meruḥ phaṇibhūdharaśca tatrādyaḥ | viśvaṃbharāmadharayatyanyo viśvaṃbharādharībhūtaḥ ||
viśvaṃbharāṃ bhuvaṃ adharayati adhastātkaroti | viśvaṃbharāyāḥ bhuvaḥ | pakṣe viśvaṃbharasya bhagavataḥ adharībhūtaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvamivānantāśrayabhāgahamapyambhoda nau bhidā tviyatī| ūṣmāgamabhavatāpaṃ tvaṃ tirayasyahamimaṃ vahe mūḍhaḥ || 643 ||
anantasya antarikṣasya bhagavataśca | ūṣmāgamabhavaḥ grīṣmajanyaḥ tāpaḥ saṃjvaraḥ tam | pakṣe ūṣmā, tīkṣṇaḥ āgamaḥ prāptirasya sa tathābhūtaḥ bhavassaṃsāraḥ tasmāttāpaṃ ādhyātmi kādikam | anyatsugamam ||
yathāvā
tvadapāṅgācchrīlābho diśāṃpatīnāṃ viśāṃpatīnāṃ ca | tulyo divi sthitau punarasti bhidā phaṇigirīndramaṇidayite || 644 || divi svarge sthitau punaḥ avasthāne tu bhidā bhedaḥ asti | diśāṃpatīnāṃ svargasthitirasti, viśāṃpatīnāṃ tu sā nāstīti bhidā | pakṣe diśāṃpatīnāṃ viśāṃpatīnāmityatra di vi iti varṇayoḥ sthitāveva bhidā anyavarṇānāṃ taulyāt | anyadvyaktameva | asminnudāharaṇatraye’pi upamānotkarṣaḥ | anyatsarvaṃ pūrvavadeva ||
ubhayotkarṣāpakarṣāpratītau yathā
ghanasāraśitiśśrutiśataviditā mūrtistaveśa kīrtiśca | ete stotumaśakye mūkībhāvo viśiṣyate’tra param || 645 ||
he īśa ! śrīnivāsa ! tava mūrtiḥ kīrtiśca ghanasāraḥ jaladavaraḥ ‘sāro bale sthirāṃśe ca nyāyye klībaṃ vare triṣu’ ityamaraḥ | sa iva śitiḥ nīlā | pakṣe ghanasāraḥ karpūraḥ ‘atha karpūramastriyāṃ | ghanasāraḥ’ ityamaraḥ | sa iva śitiḥ dhavalā ‘śitī dhavalamecakau’ ityamaraḥ | śrutiśate viditā ‘nīlato yadamadhyasthā, śyāmācchabalaṃ prapadye śabalācchyāmam’ ityādiśrutiṣu nīlatoyadaśyāmalatayā prathitā | kīrtipakṣe śrutiśataiḥ śrotṛjanaśrotrasahasraiḥ viditā jñātā | yadvā dhavalatayā prathitetyarthaḥ | yadvā mūrtiraprākṛtatvādibhiḥ prathitā | kīrtistu ‘tasya nāma mahadyaśaḥ’ ityuktarītyā mahattvena prathitetyarthaḥ | ete mūrtikīrtī stotumaśakye śrutiśataprathite te varṇayituṃ kasya mitaṃpacavacasaśśaktiriti bhāvaḥ | atra anayormūrtikīrtyoḥ mūkībhāvaḥ mū kī ityākāravarṇasattaiva viśiṣyate tulyatvādvarṇāntarasyāyameva viśeṣo nānya iti bhāvaḥ | vācyavācakayostādātmyamiha śaraṇam | anyathā vivakṣitavyatirekasyārthagatatvāsaṃbhavāt | kiṃca atra etatstutiviṣaye mūkībhāvo viśiṣyate| asmāddaśāṃ maunenāvasthānameva śreya ityarthaḥ | atra śleṣānuprāṇita utkarṣāpakarṣayoravibhāvanādanubhayavyatirekaḥ| evamuttarodāharaṇeṣvapi draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
tvaṃ khalu jagatīmātā jagatītrātā’bdhiputri tava dayitaḥ | sarvairguṇaissadṛśayormātrābhedaḥ paraṃ bhavati yuvayoḥ || 646 ||
mātrayā leśataḥ bhedaḥ anirvacanīyaṃ yatkiṃcideva vailakṣaṇyaṃ na tu sarvātmaneti bhāvaḥ ‘striyāṃ mātrā truṭiḥ puṃsi lavaleśakaṇāṇavaḥ ‘ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe māvarṇatrāvarṇābhyāmeva bheda ityarthaḥ ||
yathāvā
kāmo’tra bādhate māṃ yamaḥ paratrānayossvatassamayoḥ | bhedastu kāyamātrādādhatsva kṛpāṃ tato mayi mukunda || 647 ||
svataḥ svabhāvata eva samayo bādhitṛtvena tulyayoḥ | pakṣe mavarṇasahitayoḥ anayoḥ kāmayamayoḥ bhedastu kāyamātrāt śarīrata eva bhedo na tu guṇata iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā kāmasya kāyo nāsti yamasya tu so’stīti tanmātrādveda ityarthaḥ | kākārayakārābhyāmeva bheda ityapyathā’nusaṃdheyaḥ || yathāvā
pūrvaṃ narakānuguṇāvāśugacakreṇa lūnamūrdhānau | bhavatā sadūṣaṇau yau mukhamātrabhidā tayorhi murakharayoḥ || 648 ||
narakasya narakāsurasya nirayasya ca anuguṇau anurūpau ekatra tanmitratvāt aparatra pāpakāritvāditi bhāvaḥ | dūṣaṇena doṣeṇa dūṣaṇanāmnā rakṣasā ca saha vartete iti sadūṣaṇau | yau pūrvaṃ bhavatā āśugena cakreṇa sudarśanena āśugānāṃ viśikhānāṃ cakreṇa jālena vilūnamūrdhāno abhūtāmiti śeṣaḥ | tayoḥ murakharayoḥ tannāmnordaityarakṣasoḥ mukhamātreṇa bhidā murasya triśīrṣatvena trimukhatā kharasya ekaśīrṣatayā ekamukhataivetīyāneva bhedaḥ uktaguṇānāṃ taulyāt | suvarṇakhavarṇābhyāmeva bheda ityarthāntaraṃ ca ||
yathā vā
ye nindantyakhilaguruṃ govinda tvāmamandaduritavaśāḥ | bhāṣaṇameṣāṃ bhaṣaṇaṃ kevalamākārato’nayorbhedaḥ || 649 ||
bhāṣaṇaṃ uktiḥ, bhāṣaterlyuṭ| bhaṣaṇaṃ śvaravaḥ ‘bhaṣa bhartsane’ ‘iha bhartsanaṃ śvaravaḥ’ ityukteḥ | asmāllyaṭ | anayoḥ bhāṣaṇabhaṣaṇayoḥ ākāramātrataḥ manujaśunakamukhodgīrṇatvarūpasvarūpamātre bhedaḥ anyatra akāradīrghākārābhyāmeva bhedaḥ | anyavarṇānupūrvī tu tulyaiva || yathā vā
parabalaharaharikalitau śveto dvīpo dvipaśca khalu tulyau | kiyatī bhidā’nayossyātsavarṇayoreva sarvathā’pi satoḥ || 650 ||
śveto dvīpaḥ śveto dvipaḥ airāvataśca | pareṣāṃ dviṣāṃ balaṃ sāmarthyaṃ sainyaṃ vā tasya haraḥ hartā harirbhagavān tena kalita iti śvetadvīpapakṣe | airāvatapakṣe tu paraḥ vairī balaḥ balāsuraḥ tasya haraḥ haririndraḥ tena kalitaḥ | tayorekaśeṣaḥ | ata eva tulyau | sarvathā’pi sarvaprakāreṇāpi savarṇayoḥ tulyavarṇayoreva satoḥ dvayorapi śvetatvāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe samānānupūrvīkākṣarayoreva satoḥ anayoḥ śvetadvīpaśvetadvipayoḥ bhidā kiyatī syāt sarvathā tulyayoranayoranirvacanīyayatkiṃcidvailakṣaṇyasadbhāvo’pyakiṃcitkara ityarthaḥ| pakṣe kiyatī ībhidā iti cchedaḥ | ībhidā dīrghekārahṛsvekārābhyāṃ bhedaḥ kiyatī atyalpetyarthaḥ | yatkiṃcidvarṇabhedassannapi na gaṇanīya iti bhāvaḥ ||
yathā vā
tadvacanaśikṣitadhvanimādhuryau dvāvimau samau manye | atra sadāpiśubhedaścitte kriyatāṃ madamva pikaśukayāḥ || 651 ||
he madamba ! tvadvacanena śikṣitaṃ dhvanimādhuryaṃ yayostau tathoktau dvau imau pikaśukau samau manye | atra anayoḥ pikaśukayoḥ piśubhedaḥ pi śu iti varṇābhyāmeva vailakṣaṇyaṃ nānyavarṇayoriti bhāvaḥ | citte kriyatāṃ avadhāryatām | śabdagato’pyayaṃ piśuvarṇabhedaḥ arthe śabdatādātmyābhimānakṛta ityavadheyam | arthāntaraṃ ca he śubhe ! iti saṃbuddhiḥ | ada iti chedaḥ | atra anayoḥ pikaśukayoḥ adaḥ parasparasādṛśyaṃ sadā citte kriyatāṃ anayostvadvacanaśikṣitadhvanimādhuryatvena taulyamastīti tvayā | paricintyatāmiti ||
yathā vā
amṛtepsuvibudhasevyau sa vāsudevaśca vāsukiścāpi | tulyau vailakṣaṇyaṃ tvanayornanu devakīti mātraiva || 652 ||
saḥ prasiddhaḥ vāsudevaśca vāsukiścāpi amṛtaṃ īpsavo ye vibudhāḥ brahmavidaḥ devāśca taiḥ sevyau ata eva tulyau | anayoḥ vailakṣaṇyaṃ tu devakīti mātraiva devakīti prasiddhayā jananyaiva nanu | vāsudevasya devakīti mātrā prasiddhā vāsukestu tādṛśī mātā netyetāvadeva vailakṣaṇyam | amṛtepsuvibudhasevyatvaṃ tu tulyameveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe vāsudevavāsukiśabdayoḥ devakī iti mātraiva alpameva vailakṣaṇyam | ādye deveti varṇau antye kīti varṇa ityetāvāneva bhedaḥ ādimavarṇadvayasyobhayatrāpyaikarūpyāditi bhāvaḥ ‘striyāṃ mātrā truṭiḥ puṃsi lavaleśakaṇāṇavaḥ’ ityalpaparyāyeṣvamaraḥ |
mātrā karṇavibhūṣāyāṃ vittamāne paricchade |
akṣarāvayave svalpe klībaṃ kārtsnye’vadhāraṇe ||
iti medinī ca || yathā vā
yasyāsa sahajadeyaṃ rājyaṃ yasyāpi sahajaheyaṃ tat | dehe bhidaiva nānyā bhidā’nayorbhavati rāmalakṣmaṇayoḥ || 653 ||
yasya rāmasya rājyaṃ sahajadeyaṃ sahaje bhrātari bharate deyaṃ āsa babhūva | āseti tiṅantapratirūpakamavyayam | ‘ idamāsa cihnam’ itivat | na tvasterliṭ ‘tiṅantatve ‘asterbhūḥ’ iti liṭi bhvādeśāpatteḥ | yadvā ‘asa dītau ‘ ityasmālliḍeva | dīyata iti deyaṃ karmaṇi ‘aco yat’ iti yat | rājyaṃ sahajadeyaṃ didīpe ityarthaḥ|
ahaṃ hi sītāṃ rājyaṃ ca prāṇāniṣṭāndhanāni ca |
hreṣṭā bhrātre pradāsyāmi bharatāyāpracoditaḥ |
iti tadukteḥ | yadvā sahajena bharatena deyaṃ dātuṃ yogyamāsīdityarthaḥ | ‘taddadāmi punastusyaṃ yathā tvamadadā mama’ iti bharatokteḥ | yasya lakṣmaṇasyāpi tat rājyaṃ sahaje bhrātari sahajena bhrātrā vā heyaṃ deyaṃ āsa | ‘ohāk tyāge’ ‘aco yat’ iti yat ‘tyāgo vihāpitaṃ dānam’ ityamaraḥ | vastutastu sahajena nisargeṇaiva heyaṃ tyājyamityarthaḥ| ‘sahajastu nisarge nā sahotthe punaranyavat’ iti medinī | ‘sarvātmanā paryanunīyamāno yadā na saumitrirupaiti yogam’ ityādikamihānusandheyam | kaiṅkaryaikaratitvāttasya rājyaṃ parihāryamāsīditi bhāvaḥ | anayoḥ sahajadeyasahajaheyarājyayoḥ rāmalakṣmaṇayoḥ dehe vigrahe eva bhidā bhedaḥ nānyā, svarūpe uktaguṇe vā bhedo nāstītyarthaḥ |
tataḥ padmapalāśākṣaḥ kṛtvā’’tmānaṃ caturvidham |
pitaraṃ rocayāmāsa tadā daśarathaṃ nṛpam ||
ityukteḥ | atrātmaśabdo dehavācī | pakṣe sahajadeyarājyasahajaheyarājyaśabdayoḥ de he iti varṇābhyāmeva bhidā na tu varṇāntarairityarthaḥ | eṣu ‘ghanasāraśitiḥ’ ityādiṣu navasūdāharanaṇeṣūtkarṣāpakarṣayoravibhāvanādanubhayavyatireko draṣṭavyaḥ ||
evaṃ kuvalayānandānurodhena vyatireko darśitaḥ | atha kāvyaprakāśarasagaṅgādharādyanurodhena pradarśayiṣyate
prācīnarītyā vyatirekaḥ
upameyasyopamānādyadā guṇaviśeṣataḥ |
utkarṣo varṇyate prāhurvyatirekaṃ tadā budhāḥ ||
upamānādupameyasya guṇaviśeṣavattvenotkarṣo vyatirekaḥ pratīpādivāraṇāya tṛtīyāntaṃ vaidharmyaparam | tatra copameyasyopamānatāmātrakṛta evotkarṣaḥ na vaidharmyakṛtaḥ, sādharmyasyaiva pratyayāt | adhikaguṇatvamātraṃ upamānagatāpakarṣamātraṃ vā na vyatirekasvarūpaṃ, tayorupameyotkarṣākṣepamantareṇāsundaratvāt | ata eva na sādṛśyābhāvamātraṃ tat, upamānādupameyasyāpakarṣe’pi tatsaṃbhavāt tasya ca hīnaguṇasyopameyatāyā vāstavatvenāsundaratvāt | upameyotkarṣaviśiṣṭatvena sādṛśyābhāvasya viśeṣitatve ca tasyopameyotkarṣasyaivālaṃkārataucityenādhikasya niṣprayojanatvāt || yathā
amlānaśrīkamidaṃ sadā’pi vadanaṃ sarojasadanāyāḥ | mlānaśriyopamāṃ kkacidaindavabimbena kimavalambeta || 654 ||
mlānā na bhavatītyamlānā nirmaleti vastusthitiḥ ‘amlānastvamale jhaṇṭībhede’ iti hemacandraḥ | tādṛśī śrīryasya tattathoktaṃ ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | kkacit mlānaśriyetyanvayaḥ’ ayaṃ copameyotkarṣakopamānāpakarṣakayorvaidharmyayordvayorapyupādānānupādānābhyāmekatarānupādāne ca tāvaccaturdhā | sopyupamāyāḥ śrautītvārthītvākṣiptātvairdvādaśavidho bhavan saśleṣaniśśleṣatvābhyāṃ caturviṃśatiprakāra iti prāñcaḥ ||
yathā
jalpantu yathākāmaṃ jalpākā deva tāvakaḥ pāṇiḥ | kalpadruriva kimeṣo’nalpāṃ datte śriyaṃ sa tu svalpām || 655 ||
tāvakaḥ pāṇiḥ kalpadruriva kiṃ? sa iva na syāditi bhāvaḥ | kuta ityata āha eṣa iti | eṣaḥ tāvakaḥ pāṇiḥ | anyatsugamam | atrobhayorupādānaṃ, upamā ca śrautī | atraiva ‘kalpadruma iva kathamayamalpāṃ datte śriyaṃ na jātvapi yaḥ’ ityuttarārdhe racite ‘kalpadruriva kimeṣa svalpāṃ śriyameva yassadā datte’ iti vā racite ekatarānupādānam | śrautī copamā | ‘kalpadruriveti vacaḥ kalpeta budhasya kasya toṣāya’ iti kṛte hatusāmānyānupādānaṃ sā śrautī ca | hetudvaye hi yasyaivānupādānaṃ tasyākṣepeṇāvagatiḥ | ubhayorapyanupādāne tathaiva, na tvanavagatiḥ vyatirekasyotkarṣāpakarṣarūpatvāt | tayośca prayojakajñānamantareṇānavabodhāt ||
evam
satatamanāvilabhāvāṃ kāle kvacidāvilatvamupayāntī | bahirmukhasravantī sā’rhati kiṃ tava kṛpāṃ samāṃ kartum || 656 ||
atrobhayopādānaṃ, upamā tvārthī | ‘bāle sudhāpayodheḥ kāle kvacidāvilatvamupayāntī’ iti paṭhite pūrvārdhe, asyaiva ‘kāle kkacidapyanāvilatvajuṣam | bahirmukhasravantī’ iti dvitīyatṛtīyacaraṇayośca kalpitayorekatarānupādānaṃ, ārthī copamā | ‘vyāleśagirīśahṛdayakṛtaśāle’ iti, ‘bāle sudhāpayodheḥ’ ityasya dvitīyacaraṇe nirmite ubhayānupādānaṃ sā ca ||
niravadhikānandadughā dvirasanagirināthasaṃnidhau vasatiḥ | adharayati mitānandāṃ vidhimukhalokeṣu nivasatiṃ nṝṇām || 657 ||
atra ivādessādṛśyamātraśaktasya sadṛśādeśca tadviśiṣṭaśaktasya śabdasyābhāvācchutyarthapathātyāsādinī adharīkaraṇenākṣiptaivopamā | atraiva ‘niravadhikasukṛtasulabhe’ ityādyacaraṇanirmitau, ‘adharayati vidhinidhīśvaravidhuśekharalokanivasatiṃ nṝṇām’ ityuttarārdhanirmāṇe vā ekatarānupādānam | ākṣiptā copamā | asyaiva ‘phaṇadharadharādharādhipamadhumathanapadābjasaṃnidhau vasatiḥ’ iti pūrvārdhe nirmite ubhayānupādānaṃ, sā ca || evaṃ niśśleṣavyatirekadik pradarśitā | atha saśleṣavyatirekadikpradarśyate | yathā
nanu niśśrīkamalinyāḥ karāvamarśena vidadhadāmodam | bhāsvāniva kiṃ bhagavānapahatapāpmā yato hyeṣaḥ || 658 ||
niśśrīkāyāḥ anāplutatayā mlānaprabhāyāḥ ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ itei kap | malinyāḥ puṣpavatyāḥ ‘malinī puṣpavatyapi’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe niśśriyaḥ avikasitatayā lakṣmīnivāsavidhurāyāḥ kamalinyāḥ padminyāḥ karāvamarśena hastasaṃsparśena kiraṇasaṃparkeṇa ca āmodaṃ pramodaṃ saurabhaṃ ca vidadhat bhāsvān bhānuriva kiṃ bhagavān syāt | kuto na syādityata āha yata iti | yataḥ yasmāt eṣaḥ apahatapāpmā “na sukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno’to nivartante apahatapāpmā hyeṣa brahmalokaḥ’ iti śruteḥ | atrobhayopādanaṃ, śrautī copamā | śleṣastu spaṣṭa eva ||
yathāvā
kanakācalamiva kathayatu kathamiva kākodarācalaṃ lokaḥ | sa surālaya iti viditaḥ kaluṣaharaṃ tīrthameṣa vāsayati || 659 ||
kaluṣaharaṃ tīrthaṃ pāpavināśanamiti prasiddhaṃ tīrtham | tacca śeṣādrau sthitam | atrāpyubhayopādānaṃ, śrautyevopamā śleṣaśca ||
tvaṃ khalu na taṭitvāniva yanna vihitasādhughanarasatyāgaḥ | saṃkalitatapasphūrtirveṃkaṭanāthāsi vimalahaṃsagatiḥ || 660 || he veṃkaṭanātha ! tvaṃ taṭitvāniva na khalu | kuta ityata āha yaditi | yasmāt sādhuṣu satsu ghanassāndraḥ yo rasaḥ anurāgaḥ tasya tyāgaḥ sa na vihito yena sa tathoktaḥ | yadvā sādhuḥ ślāghyaḥ ghanassaṃpūrṇaḥ yo rasaḥ ānandaḥ tasya tyāgo na vihito yena niratiśayānanda ityarthaḥ | pakṣe avihitanirmalasalilatyāga ityarthaḥ | saṃkalitā tapasaḥ tapasya grīṣmasya ca sphūrtiryena sa tathoktaḥ | vimalānāṃ akaluṣāṇāṃ viśadānāṃ ca | haṃsānāṃ jñānināṃ marālānāṃ ca | gatiḥ gamyata iti gatiḥ karmaṇi ktin | prāpya ityarthaḥ | atropamānāpakarṣakānupādānaṃ, śrautī copamā śleṣaśca ||
ādāveva kuvarṇaṃ prāptaṃ madhye’vamānitamathānte | layamupayātaṃ kuvalayamavayanti kathaṃ tavāmva nayanamiva || 661 ||
kuvalayaṃ ādāveva prathamameva kuvarṇaṃ kutsitaṃ varṇaṃ ku ityākārakamakṣaraṃ ca prāptaṃ, kartari ktaḥ | madhye madhyakāle avamānitaṃ avajñātaṃ tvannayananeneti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe madhye svavācakaśabdamadhye vamānitaṃ vakāreṇa yuktamiti yāvat | ata eva ante paryavasāne layaṃ kvāpi līnatāṃ upayātaṃ, kartari ktaḥ | pakṣe layeti varṇadvayamupagatamityarthaḥ | atropameyotkarṣakānupādānaṃ, upamā śrautī śleṣaśca ||
ārthe sāmye yathā
vahadapi suvarṇamantarmukhe kuvarṇaṃ sadā dadhatkusumam | śrīssumukhi hariṇyāstava paścātsamamastu na tu puro vapuṣaḥ || 662 || he sumukhi ! he śrīḥ! antaḥ madhye pracchannaṃ yathā syāttatheti vā | suvarṇaṃ kanakam | pakṣe antaḥ svavācakaśabdamadhye suvarṇaṃ su ityākārakamakṣaraṃ vahadapi dadhānamapi mukhe vakre ādau ca kuvarṇaṃ kutsitavarṇaṃ ku ityākārakamakṣaraṃ ca, sadā dadhat kusumaṃ prasūnaṃ kusumapadaṃ ca | hariṇyāḥ suvarṇapratimāyāḥ, anenāsyāḥ apracchannasarvāṅgasuvarṇatvaṃ darśitam | haritavarṇāyāśca ‘hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīm’ iti śruteḥ| ‘hariṇī syānmṛgī hemapratimā haritā ca yā’ ityamaraḥ | tava vapuṣaḥ paścāt parokṣamiti yāvat | samaṃ samaṃmanyamiti yāvat astu | puraḥ samakṣaṃ tu na, nasamaṃ syādityarthaḥ | parokṣaṃ samamiti vikatthatāṃ nāma pratyakṣaṃ tu kathaṃ samamiti brūyāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā vapuṣaḥ paścāt caramāṅge samamastu puraḥ agrabhāge tu na samamevetyarthaḥ | athavā paścāt bhāvini kāle yadākadā vā samaṃ astu saddaśaṃ bhaveccedbhavatvityanādare | puraḥ prathamameva tu na samaṃ syāt | kālatraye’pi samaṃ na bhavatyeveti hṛdayam | pakṣe ku su iti varṇadvayādanantaraṃ samaṃ makārasahitamityarthaḥ, kusumaśabdasya tathaiva darśanāt | purataḥ ādau tu na, makārasahitaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ | uktaśabdasyātathātvāditi bhāvaḥ | atrobhayopādānaṃ, upamā cārthī, sadṛśaśabdena tatkathanāt | śleṣaśca ||
yathāvā
śrīmankanakagirīndro vyoma sarūpaṃ vadantu tava mugdhāḥ | vyokāramāśritaṃ tatsujātarūpasya tava kathaṃ sadṛśam || 663 || śrīman! he kanakagirīndo! mugdhāḥ atattvavidaḥ vyoma gaganaṃ tava samānaṃ rūpaṃ yasya tat sarūpaṃ vadantu kathayantu tattvavidastu na vadantīti bhāvaḥ | kuta ityata āha vyokāramiti | tat vyoma kartṛ vyokāraṃ ayaskāraṃ āśritam | kartari ktaḥ | ‘vyokāro lohakārakaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | ‘vyokāro’yaskaro’yaskāro lohakārassyāt’ iti ratnakośaḥ| ‘vyokāraḥ karmāro lohakāraḥ’ iti hemacandraśca | vyo karotīti vigrahaḥ | karmaṇyaṇ ‘aṅgārīyadārusaṃgrahairyokāraiḥ’ iti harṣacarite prayogaśca | vāstavārthastu vyo iti varṇamāśritamiti | ‘varṇātkāre ‘ iti kārapratyayaḥ | sujātarūpasya śobhanasuvarṇasya | pakṣe ramaṇīyarūpasyetyarthaḥ || tava kathaṃ sadṛśaḥ, tulyo na bhavatyeva | ayaskārāśritasujātarūpayossāmyāyogāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva sarvam | upameyotkarṣakopamānāpakarṣakahetustvatraikaḥpūrvatra dvāviti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
mukhavarṇamitā’tirucā’gresarakā devadeva pāvanayā | tanvā tava kiṃ tulyā milindajābhā kalindajābhā vā || 664 ||
he devadeva ! atirucā sarvatomukhadīptimatyā tava tanvā mukhavarṇamitā mukhasya varṇo rūpaṃ dīptirvā mito’lpo yasyāssā tathoktā |
varṇo dvijādiśuklādiyaśoguṇakathāsu ca |
stutau nānā striyāṃ bhedarūpākṣaravilepane ||
iti medinī | milindājjātā milindajā bhramaragatetyarthaḥ | ābhā prabhā | karmadhārayaḥ | sadṛśī kiṃ na sadṛśītyarthaḥ, atidīptimitadīptimatossāmyāyogāt | pakṣe mukhavarṇe ādimākṣare mitāmivarṇatvaṃ yasyāssā tathoktā | milindajābhāśabdasya tathātvāt| tathā pāvanayā tava tanvaiva agre puraḥ sarakaḥ madyapānaṃ tatpātraṃ ikṣumadyaṃ vā yasyāssā tathoktā atimātramapavitreti bhāvaḥ | ‘caṣako’strī pānapātraṃ sarako’pi’ ityamaraḥ | ‘sarakā’strī śīdhupāne śīdhupātrekṣumadyayoḥ’ iti medinī ca | agre ityedantamavyayam | pakṣe agresaraḥ kaḥ kavarṇo yasyāsseti tu tattvam | kalindajābhāśabdasya tathātvāt | śabdārthatādātmyaṃ na vismartavyam | kalindajāyāḥ yamunāyāḥ ābhā vā sadṛśī kiṃ | na sadṛśītyarthaḥ | pāvanāpāvanayostaulyāyogāt | atrāpyupamānāpakarṣakopameyotkarṣakayorubhayorupādānaṃ ārthī copamā śleṣaśceti sarvaṃ tulyam | dve upamāne yathāsaṃkhyasaṃkīrṇe iti tu viśeṣaḥ||
yathāvā
ghananirghoṣatrastaḥ kiṃ sadṛśo mānasaikajanyassyāt | haṃsaśśaṅkhena hareḥ paṭunirghoṣeṇa pāñcajanyena || 665 ||
ghananirghośaḥ gaṃbhīradhvaniḥ yasyakasyacidati bhāvaḥ | tasmāt trastaḥ ekameva janyaṃ yuddhaṃ ‘yuddhamāyodhanaṃ janyaṃ’ ityamaraḥ | tadapi mānasaṃ manassaṃkalpitameva na tu kriyayā nirvartitaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | mānasasarojanmeti vāstavārthaḥ | paṭunirghoṣeṇa pāñcajanyena pañcajanyāni yuddhāni soḍhānyasya pāñcajanyaḥ | ‘tadasya soḍham’ ityarthe prāgdīvyatīyo’ṇ | pañcasaṃkhyākayuddhajeteti yāvat | pakṣe pāñcajanyanāmnā śaṅkhenetyarthaḥ | atrāpyubhayopādānaṃ ārthī copamā śleṣaśca | atropameyasyāpi śliṣṭatvaṃ prāthamikakodāharaṇādviśeṣaḥ || yathāvā
santāna udārasstānnantā sassyātparaṃ pratīpopagame | kiṃ tādṛśena kaiṭabhahantā tulyostu kasyacidanantā’sau || 666 ||
santānaḥ devataruviśeṣaḥ udāraḥ anupādhikanirdeśena sarvamahān stāt | sarvamahān bhavatu cedbhavatādityanādare | dāteti tu vastusthitiḥ | ‘udāro dātṛmahatoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | paraṃ paraṃtu pratīpānāṃ pratyarthināṃ upagame samīpagamane saḥ santānaḥ atrānusvārasya “vā padantasya’ iti vaikalpikaḥ parasavarṇo bodhyaḥ arthāntare śabdānurūpyāya | nantā namanakartā syāt | sādhvasena praṇamedityarthaḥ | tasmādanudāra eveti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe pratīpopagame vaiparītyena gatau nantāsaḥ santānaśabdo nantāsa iti syādityarthaḥ | prāthamikārthe sassyādityatra dvitīyasakārasya ‘anaci ca’ iti vā dvitvam | anyathā tacchabdaprathamaikavacanāntasya sa ityasya ‘etattadossulopaḥ’ ityādinā sulopena sakārasya tasya dvitvāyogāt | tādṛśena pratīpanamanakartā kasyacidapi anantā anamanakartā, namestṛc | ‘nantavyaḥ paramaśśeṣī’ ityuktarītyā sarvalokanantavyasya tasya nantavyāntarāsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | atra ‘vṛkṣa iva stabdhaḥ’ iti śrutyartho’nusandheyaḥ | asau kaiṭabhahantā tulyo’stu kiṃ tulyo na bhavedevetyarthaḥ | atra nantṛtvamupamānāpakarṣahetuḥ | anantṛtvamupameyotkarṣahetuśceti dvayamapyupāttam | upamā ca tulyaśabdopādānādārthī | śleṣastūpamānāpakarṣamātre iti viśeṣaḥ || tīvrakṣāra nadīna tvaṃ na samo mādhavasya saviśeṣam | adharitaśīrṣo’pi bhavanna dīnarakṣāvratī bhaveryasmāt || 657 ||
tīvrakṣāra! atimātrakaṭo! nirdayeti yāvat | atimātralavaṇeti vastusthitiḥ | ‘tīvraṃ kaṭūṣṇātyartheṣu’ iti, ‘kṣāraḥ kāce rase guḍe | bhasmani lavaṇe dhūrte’ iti ca hemacandraḥ | he atidhūrteti vā arthaḥ | vastusthitistu pūrvavadeva | he nadīna! saritpate ! tvaṃ mādhavasya śrīnivāsasya bhagavataḥ na samaḥ | yadyapi nadīnastvaṃ tathā’pi tīvrakṣāratvānna tulya iti bhāvaḥ | tatra hetvantaraṃ cāha yasmāt tvaṃ saviśeṣaṃ sātiśayaṃ yathā tathā adharitaśīrṣaḥ adhaḥkṛtaśirāḥ bhavannapi dīnānāṃ rakṣaiva vrataṃ tadasyāstīti dīnaralakṣāvratī na bhaveḥ | nirghṛṇasyātidhūrtasya vā dīnarakṣanaṇaikatā na saṃbhavediti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nadīnaśabdaḥ viśeṣyate bhedyate aneneti viśeṣa, karaṇe ghañ | viśeṣaṇamityarthaḥ | saviśeṣaṃ tīvrakṣāreti viśeṣaṇasahitaṃ yathā syāttathā adharitaśīrṣaḥ prātilomyena paṭhyamāna iti yāvat |nadīnarakṣāvratītyānupūrvīvānbhavatītyarthaḥ | atropamānāpakarṣahetustīvrakṣāratvaṃ nadīnarakṣābratitvaṃ copāttaṃ, upameyokarṣakatvaṃ tu niravadhidayānidhitvaṃ sakṛtprapannajanatāsaṃrakṣaṇaikavratitvaṃ ca nopāttam | upamā cārthī śleṣaśca ||
yathāvā
ādau mahito vijitastvadadharasudhayā tataḥ karau dhūnvan | mando babhūva bhārgavi makarandassa tu samaḥ kathaṃ tasyāḥ || 658 ||
he bhārgavi ! makarandaḥ ādau tvadadharasudhāsamabhivyāhārābhāvadaśāyāṃ mahitaḥ svādutayā pūjitaḥ | anantaraṃ tvadadharasudhayā vijitaḥ ata eva karau hastau dhūnvan kampayannityavamānānubhāvakathanam | mandaḥ svādimabhāgyavidhuraḥ ‘mūḍhālpāpaṭunirbhāgyā mandāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | pakṣe ādau mahitaḥ maḥ makāraḥ hitaḥ nihitaḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ | makāraviśiṣṭa ityarthaḥ | tataḥ karau kakārarephau dhūnvan visṛjanniti yāvat | makarandaḥ makarandaśabdaḥ mandaḥ babhūva uktaśabdo madhyasthakaravarṇavisarjane manda iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | sugamamanyat | atrāpi pūrvavadupamānāpakarṣakadharmamātropādānaṃ, ārthī copamā śleṣaśca ||
kalyāṇakoṭinayano vajrasahasrātidīptikaramūrtiḥ| kathamindreṇa samassyātprathamaḥ puruṣaḥ pṛdākugirināthaḥ || 669 ||
kalyāṇāni śubhāni koṭisaṃkhyākāni nayanāni locanāni yasya sa tathoktaḥ | pakṣe kalyāṇakoṭīnāṃ maṅgalakoṭīnāṃ nayanaḥ prāpakaḥ nīyate aneneti nayanaḥ lokānāmiti śeṣaḥ | vajrāṇām dambholīnāṃ sahasraiḥ atiśayitā dīptiryasya tathābhūtaḥ karo hasto yasyāṃ sā tathoktā mūrtiḥ yasya saḥ | anyatra vajrasahasrāṇāṃ harisahasrāṇāṃ dīptiṃ karotīti dīptikarī mūrtiryasya sa tathoktaḥ | mūrteḥ śyāmalatayā vajrasahasrāṇāmābharaṇanyuptānāṃ parabhāgalābhāddīptikaratvamiti bhāvaḥ | atropameyotkarṣakadharmamātropādānaṃ, ārthī copamā śleṣaśca || ākṣipte aupamye yathā
viṣaharaṇena pinākinamujjīvayatā tvayā’cyutānanta | pītvā viṣaṃ vimuñcan jīvanamambhodharaḥ parābhūtaḥ || 679 ||
acyutānanteti saṃbuddhidvayaṃ viṣaharaṇena śivojjīvayitṛtvasāmarthyābhiprāyagarbham ||
acyutānantagovindamantramānuṣṭubhaṃ param |
oṃ namassaṃpuṭīkṛtya japanviṣadharo haraḥ ||
iti nāmatrayavidhānokteḥ | viṣaṃ pītvā jīvanaṃ vimuñcanniti yojanā | atra ivādessadṛśādeśca padasyābhāvātparābhūtapadenākṣiptaivopamā | viṣaharaṇenojjīvanamupameyotkarṣakaṃ viṣapānena jīvanavimocanamupamānāpakarṣakaṃ cetyubhayamupāttaṃ śleṣaśca sphuṭaḥ ||
yathāvā
abhiyātivipadbhugnaḥ phaṇirāṭkvacidāttabhogasaṃkocaḥ | dhutavipadā nityocchritabhogena bhujena sa tava nirdhūtaḥ || 671 ||
he bhagavanniti prakaraṇāllabhyate | phaṇirāṭ vāsukiḥ ‘vāsukistu sarparājaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | abhiyātinaḥ vipakṣāt yā vipat vipattiḥ tayā bhugnaḥ rugṇaḥ ‘rugṇabhugne’ ityamaraḥ | ata eva kvacit āttaḥ svīkṛtaḥ bhogasya sukhasya saṃkoco yena sa tathoktaḥ | pakṣe abhiyātīti bhinnaṃ padaṃ tiṅantam | kvacit āttaḥ bhogasya phaṇasya saṃkoco yena sa tathoktaḥ | phaṇasya sarvadā’pyavistṛtatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ‘bhogassukhe sryādibhṛtāvaheśca phaṇakāyayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | vipat padbhirvihīnaḥ gūḍhapāttvāttathoktiḥ ‘padaṅghriḥ’ ityamaraḥ | bhugnaḥ kuṭilaḥ | ‘āviddhaṃ kuṭilaṃ bhugnaṃ vellitaṃ vakramityapi’ ityamaraḥ | abhiyāti abhito gacchati prasiddhaṃ hi tasya jiṃhagatvam | spaṣṭamanyat | atra vivadbhugnatvakvacidāttabhogatve upamānāpakarṣake | dhutavipattvanityocchritabhogatve upameyotkarṣake ca dvedve abhihite | śleṣassabhaṅgo’bhaṅgaśceti viśeṣaḥ pūrvasmāt | ākṣiptā upamā tu tulyaiva ||
yathāvā
niravadhivaibhavabhūmnā dhanādhipo’dhaḥkṛtastvayā tadapi | nādhigataṃ pāntamamuṃ nādhipamevākhileśvara prāhuḥ || 672 ||
he akhileśvara! niravadhirvaibhavabhūmā prābhavātiśayo yasya tena tathoktena tvayā | dhanādhipaḥ vaiśravaṇaḥ kevaladhanika iti bhāvaḥ |adhaḥkṛtaḥ tiraskṛtaḥ | dhakāravidhuraśca kṛtaḥ | śabdārthayo stādātmyam ! tadapi tathā’pi ādhiṃ manovyathāṃ gataḥ prāptā na bhavatītei nādhigataḥ taṃ tathoktaṃ naśabdena samāsaḥ | ata eva pāntaṃ dhanaṃ rakṣantamityarthaḥ | nādhipameva anadhipameva adhanādhipameva anīśvarameva vā āhuḥ | avadhīritatve’pi bhagavatā nirapatrapatayā avigaṇayya manovyathāmapi yathāpurameva svadhanādhipatāprakhyāpanāya rakṣantamapi dhanaṃ janāstanmarmābhijñā anadhipamevāhuriti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe nādhigataṃ nādhī nākāraṃ dhikāraṃ ca gataṃ ‘dvitīyā śrita’ ityādinā samāsaḥ | pāntaṃ pakāraḥ ante yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ amuṃ dhanādhipaṃ nādhipamevāhuḥ | dhanādhipaśabdasya dhakāralope nādhīti varṇadvayasāhitye pakārāntatve ca nādhipa ityeva niṣpatteḥ | atra akhileśvaratvaniravadhivaibhavabhūmatve upameyotkarṣahetū dhanādhipatvanādhipatve upamānāpakarṣahetū pūrvavadevopātte | adhaḥkṛta ityanenopamākṣepaḥ śleṣopi pūrvavadeva | upamākṣepake adhaḥkṛta iti śabde’pi śleṣa iti tu viśeṣaḥ||
yathāvā
tvadbhuktaśeṣabhokturviśuddhajananasya tāvakīnasya hare| sakalāda iti prathitassakhyāyārhaḥ kimagnirindurvā’pi || 673 ||
he hare! tvadbhuktaśeṣabhoktuḥ tvanniveditaśeṣameva bhoktuḥ viśuddhajananasya pavitragotrasya pavitrajanmana iti vā | tāvakasya tvadbhaktasya sakalamattīti sakalādaḥ | karmaṇyaṇ | sarvānnīna ityarthaḥ| vastutastu ‘yaiḥ kṛtassarvabhakṣo’gniḥ ‘ ‘vahnessarvabhujo yathā’ ityādyuktessarvabhakṣaka ityarthaḥ | agniḥ vahniḥ, saḥ kalādaḥ rukmakāraka ityarthaḥ| ‘kalādo rukmakārakaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | vāstavārthastu kalāḥ ādatte bhānossakāśāditi kalādaḥ | yadvā prathamāṃ pibate vahniḥ’ ityādyukteḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe vahnyādibhyaḥ kalāḥ datta iti kalādaḥ | induścandro vā’pi sakhyāyārhaḥ kim , nārha ityarthaḥ | atra bhagavanniveditaikabhojitvasya viśuddhajananatvasya copameyotkarṣakasya upamābhūtayoragnīndvoryathāsaṅkhyaṃ sarvānnīnatvasvarṇakāratvayorapakarṣakayośca śleṣeṇopādānamiti viśeṣaḥ | upamākṣepastu tulya eva ||
yathāvā
yo’dhaśśirāḥ patannapi rasālapākaḥ kapālasārassyāt | sa tu sakalādhikamadhurima paricarituṃ vā’mba tava vaco neṣṭe || 674 ||
he amba ! rasālapākaḥ rasāla ikṣuviśeṣaḥ tatpākaḥ guḍaśśarkarā vā | adhaśśirāḥ patannapi tvadvacanasāmyalābhāyeti bhāvaḥ | kapālasya śiro’sthnaḥ ghaṭaśakalasya vā sāra iva sāro yasya sa tathoktaḥ | tadrasatulyarasa eva syāt | atra sāraśabdo viparītalakṣaṇayā asāraparaḥ |
anarthakamanāyuṣyaṃ goviṣāṇasya bhakṣaṇam |
dantā api pramṛjyante rasaścāpi na labhyate ||
iti śrībhāratoktanyāyena nīrasa eva bhavediti bhāvaḥ | ‘kapālaḥ kuṣṭharugbhede ghaṭādiśakale gaṇe | śiro’sthāni’ iti, ‘sāro majjāsthirāṃśayoḥ | bale śreṣṭhe ca sāraṃ tu draviṇanyāyyavāriṣu’ iti ca hemaḥ | pakṣe rasālapākaśabdo vilomapāṭhe kapālasāra iti niṣpadyata ityarthaḥ | spaṣṭamanyat | atra kapālasāratvamupamānāpakarṣahetuḥ sakalādhikamadhurimatvamupameyotkarṣahetuścopāttaḥ | upamākṣepaśca tulya eva | śleṣastu vilomapaṭhitaśabdārthatādātmyasaṃpādita iti viśeṣaḥ ||
utpalabhujo nikāmaṃ satpathabhājo’pi kilamṛgāṅkasya | mitraṃ kathaṃ nu bhavitā vaktraṃ bhavataḥ patatrivaraketo || 675 ||
utpalabhujaḥ udbhūtāmiṣabhoktuḥ | pakṣe utpalāni kuvalayāni bhunakti pālayatīti tathoktasya | spaṣṭamanyat | atrotpalabhoktṛtvamupamānāpakarṣakāraṇamātramupāttaṃ, na tu sakalalokapāvanatvamupameyotkarṣakāraṇam | mitraśabdopādānādākṣiptaivopamā | śleṣastu vyakta eva ||
yathāvā
apyullasantamantaḥ pādyaṃ kathitaṃ janena vāntatayā | svajayārthinamabdhisute pallavamiha tava karastiraskurute || 676 ||
he abdhisute! antaḥ ullasantaṃ prakāśamānamapi na tu bahiriti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe antaḥ madhye utkṛṣṭaṃ yathā syāttathā llena lakārasaṃyuktalakāreṇa santaṃ praśastam | ucchabdatakārasya ‘torli’ iti lakārādeśe lakāratrayaśravaṇena yadyapi bhāvyam, tathā’pi ‘halo yamāṃ yami lopaḥ’ iti dvitīyalakārasya vaikalpikalopāśrayaṇena lakāradvayasyaiva śravaṇaṃ, arthāntare dvilakārakaśabdaikarūpyāyetyavadheyam | pādyaṃ pādārthaṃ pādasādṛśyārhameveti yāvat | na tu karasādṛśyārhamiti bhāvaḥ | ‘pādārghābhyāṃ ca’ iti tādarthye yat | pakṣe pakāraḥ ādyo yasya taṃ tathoktam | janena pāmareṇāpi ‘jano loke maharlokātparaloke’pi pāmare’ iti medinī | vāntatayā udgīrṇatayā kathitaṃ sakalalokabahiṣkṛtamiti yāvat | pakṣe vakārāntatayā kathitamityarthaḥ | pallavaṃ kisalayaṃ, pakṣe pallavaśabdam | anyatsugamam | atrāpyupamānāpakarṣakamātropādānam | sā ca śleṣaśca | tasya śabdārthatādātmyamūlakatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ ||
paṅkavilopanapaṭuno veṅkaṭagirirājanāthanayanasya| kiṅkaratāmapi kamalaṃ saṃkalayitumarhatīti ko brūyāt || 677 ||
atropameyotkarṣakaṃ paṅkavilopanapaṭutvamevopāttaṃ, upamānāpakarṣakaṃ paṅkadigdhatvaṃ tu nopāttam | kiṃkaratāmityādinā upamā ākṣiptaiva | śleṣaśca ||
yathāvā
atyujjvalatāṃ vindasi yā tvaṃ śrīrharikarāvamarśena | tasyāste’mba haridrā chātratvaṃ chāyayā’pi kiṃ bhajatām || 678 ||
he amba ! śrīḥ ! yā tvaṃ hareḥ bhānoḥ bhagavataśca karāṇāṃ kiraṇānāṃ pāṇīnāṃ ca avamarśena atyujjvalatāṃ atidīptatvaṃ atimātraśṛṅgārarasavattvaṃ ca | vindasi ‘ujjvalastu vikāsini | śṛṅgāre viśade dīpte’ iti hemaḥ | tasyāḥ te haridrā chāyayā’pi varṇenāpi na tu guṇāntareṇeti bhāvaḥ | leśato’pītyapi gamyate | chātratvaṃ antevāsitvaṃ bhajatāṃ kiṃ, na bhajedevetyarthaḥ | yadvā chāyayā chātratvamapi bhajatāṃ kimiti yojanā | tadapi bhaktumanarhaṃ kimuta sāmyamiti bhāvaḥ | atra harikarāvamarśahetukātyujjvalatābhāktvamupameyotkarṣakamātramupāttaṃ, na tu harikarāvamarśahetukavicchāyatvamupamānāpakarṣakam | sā ca śleṣaśca ||
yathāvā
lokālokātītaṃ nāvikhyātaṃ na cāmṛtaviśoṣi | brahma jyotistadidaṃ bradhno nidhyātumapi kimīśīta || 679 || jyotiḥ ‘atha yadataḥparo divo jyotiḥ’ iti śrutaniratiśayadīptiyuktaṃ brahma paraṃ brahma lokālokaṃ cakravālācalaṃ atītaṃ ‘dvitīyā śrita’ ityādinā samāsaḥ sarvavyāpīti bhāvaḥ ! yadvā lokānāṃ janānāṃ ālokaṃ darśanaṃ atītaṃ acakṣurgrāhyamityarthaḥ | ‘na cakṣuṣā gṛhyate | na tatra cakṣurgacchati | yattadadreśyaṃ | adṛśye’nātmye’ ityādiśruteḥ| vikhyātaṃ na bhavatītyavikhyātaṃ na, sakalajagatkāraṇatvādinā sarvavedāntaprathitamityarthaḥ | saṃbhāvyavikhyātyabhāvapratiṣedhāya nañdvayam | amṛtaṃ niśśreyasaṃ viśoṣayatītyamṛtaviśoṣi ca na na bhavati ‘amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ’ iti niśśreyasaprāpakatvaśravaṇāt ‘etadamṛtam’ ityādibhiramṛtatvaśravaṇācca | pakṣe aviriti khyātaṃ jalaviśoṣi ca netyarthaḥ ‘avayaśśailameṣārkāḥ, amṛtaṃ yajñaśeṣe syātpīyūṣe salile ghṛte’ ityamaramedinyau | tadidaṃ jyotīrūpaṃ brahma bradno bhānuḥ nidhyātuṃ draṣṭumapi īśīta kiṃ neśītaiveti bhāvaḥ | atra lokālokātītatvādikā upameyotkarṣahetava evopāttāḥ, na tu lokālokānatītatvāvikhyātatvāmṛtaviśoṣitvānyupamānāpakarṣahetavaḥ | śleṣopamākṣepau ca spaṣṭau ||
idamatrāvadheyam iha śleṣāvalambivyatirekaprabhede ubhayānupādānena upamāyāśśrautītvārthītvākṣiptatvaprayuktabhedatritayaṃ durupapādam| vaidharmyānuktau kimavalambaśśleṣa unmiṣet | na ca yatra dvijarājaratnākaraprabhañjanamātariśvādiśabdasamadhigamyeṣūpamānopameyeṣu svaśabdopātta eva śleṣo vyatirekonmeṣakastatraiva tadudāharaṇaṃ supapādamiti vācyaṃ, tatra svaśabdavedyasyaiva vaidharmyasya saṃbhavāt | evaṃca caturviṃśatirbhedā iti prācāmuktirbhūyiṣṭhodāharaṇadarśibhiryathākathaṃcidupapādanīyā | kiṃcātropamābhedāssarva eva sambhavantītyalaṃ caturviṃśatibhedaparigaṇanayā ||
nanvasyālaṃkārasya vaidharmyamūlakasyopamāpratikūlatvamevocitaṃ na tūpamāgarbhatvam, tasyāssādharmyamūlakatvāt | asya ca tanniṣedharūpeṇaiva pravṛtteḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, siddhāntabhaṅgaprasaṅgāditi cet satyam, yadguṇapuraskāreṇa yasya yatsādṛśyaniṣedha utkarṣaparyavasāyī tasya tadguṇapuraskāreṇa tatsādṛśyāpratiṣṭhāne’pi guṇāntareṇa sādṛśyapratyayasya durvāratvāt | yadi ca tatsādṛśyasāmānyaniṣedho vivakṣitassyāttarhi guṇaviśeṣapuraskāro nirarthakassyāt | kulenāyamasmādadhika ityukte rūpeṇa vayasā vidyayā dhanena ca sama iti sārvalaukikapratyayāt | evaṃca pratīyamānamapi sādṛśyaṃ guṇāntarakṛtaniṣedhonmiṣitenotkarṣeṇa nirastaprabhamiva nigalitamiva ca prabhavati na camatkṛtimādhātumiti prācāmāśayaḥ ||
atrālaṃkāre kvacicchābdasādṛśyaniṣedhākṣiptāvupameyotkarṣopamānāpakarṣau | kvacicca śābdenopameyotkarṣeṇākṣiptāvupamānāpakarṣasādṛśyābhāvau | kvacittādṛśenopamāpakarṣeṇākṣiptāvupameyotkarṣatadabhāvau camatkṛtijanakau tathā | tatrādyaḥ prakāraḥ prācīnarītyā sabheda udāhṛtaḥ | dvitīyatṛtīyāvapi prāyaśastāvadbhedāveva | tatra diṅmātramudāhriyate 
nityaṃ prasannahṛdayassa tvaṃ śreyānvidhormalinahṛdayāt | yogidhyeyāttvatto nyūnassa vidho viyogitāpakaraḥ || 680 || he vidho ! he viṣṇo! iti saṃbuddhiḥ | atra pūrvārdhe upameyotkarṣaśśābdaḥ | upamānāpakarṣasādṛśyābhāvāvākṣiptau | dvitīyārdhe upamānāpakarṣaśśābdaḥ | upameyotkarṣasādṛśyābhāvāvākṣiptau | evaṃ kvacit dvayostrayāṇāṃ vā śābdatvaṃ saṃbhavadapi nātīva ramaṇīyamiti nodāhriyata ||
kvacittrayamapyākṣiptameva
vipule’smin bhuvanatale vyadadhādratnākarānvidhissapta | avadhirdṛśorasaṃkhyāṃstavāmba ratnākarān sṛjati lokān || 681 ||
dṛśoravadhiḥ kaṭākṣa ityarthaḥ | vidheḥ bhuvanatale sataiva ratnākarān sindhūn vyadadhāt asṛjat | he amba ! tava kaṭākṣastu asaṃkhyākān lokāneva ratnākarān sindhūn sṛjati uttamavastūnāmādhārān sṛjatīti tu tattvam | sarvavidhasamṛddhimataḥ kalpayatīti bhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
gambhīro’nunnatibhāgambudhiragabhīra unnato meruḥ| gambhīronnata ekaḥ kumbhīnasakumbhinībhṛdavataṃsaḥ ||
bhāsvānbhānuraśiśiraḥ prasanna indussadā’pyanuṣṇa iha | śrīmānsadā prasannassvāmī śiśiroṣṇa eka eva tvam || 683 ||
śiśiraḥ śītalaḥ dayārdraśca | uṣṇaḥ tīkṣṇaḥ dakṣaśca ‘uṣṇo grīṣme pumāndakṣāśītayoranyaliṅgakaḥ’ iti medinī | yadvā ‘tīkṣṇaścaiva mṛduścaiva rājā bhavati sammataḥ’ ityuktarītyā nātimṛdurnātitīkṣṇaḥ | kiṃtu nigrahānugrahaśīla ityarthaḥ | anyathā ‘tīkṣṇādudvijate loko mṛdurhi paribhūyate’ iti kevalataikṣṇyamārdavayordoṣasmaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ | spaṣṭamanyat |
yathāvā
antarbahirapyamalastvaṃ candro’ntarmalīmasaśśaure | tvaṃ nanu paramodārassa tu lubdhaśiromaṇissarojākṣa || 684 ||
nirgarjanamamalīmasavadanaḥ pradadāsi sadamṛtaṃ svāmin | garjanmalīmasamukhaḥ parjanyo vitaratīha viṣameva || 685 ||
paripakvabandhasamanaḥparaṃparāṃ sparśanena pātayati | mādhava eko’nyastāṃ prāpayati svapadameva tenoccaiḥ || 686 ||
paripakvāḥ pariṇatāḥ vināśonmukhāśca bandhāḥ vṛntāni bha vabandhāśca yasyāstāṃ tathoktāṃ sumanasāṃ kusumānāṃ viduṣāṃ ca paraṃparāṃ sparśanena marutā abhayādivitaraṇena ca ‘sparśano mārute puṃsi dāne sparśe napuṃsakam’ iti medinī | eko mādhavaḥ vasantaḥ pātayati adho nayati | anyaḥ mādhavaḥ bhagavān tāṃ paripakvabandhasumanaḥparaṃparāmityarthaḥ | tena sparśanena uccaiḥ unnataṃ pūjyaṃ ca ‘mahatyuccaiḥ’ ityamaraḥ | svapadameva svāvasthānataruśākhāsthānameva | pakṣe paramaṃ padameva prāpayati || janani janastāvakajanasaṅgātkukurādhināthapadamayate | anyo’śnute’ntareṇakarasaṃyogātkukkurādhināthapadam || 687 ||
he janani ! tāvakajanasaṅgāt bhāgavatasaṃsargādityarthaḥ | janaḥ pāmaro’pīti bhāvaḥ | kukurādhināthasya yadukulasārvabhaumasya bhagavataḥ padaṃ caraṇaṃ ayate prapanno bhavatītyarthaḥ | yadvā bhagavatassthānaṃ prāpnotītyartha | anyaḥ tāvakajanātiriktaḥ janaḥ pāmaraḥ jano loke maharlokātparaloke’pi pāmare’ iti medinī | antare antaraṅge aṇakānāṃ garhyāṇāṃ saṃyogāt | ‘kheṭagarhyāṇakāssamāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | kukurāṇāṃ śunāṃ ‘kukkuro mṛgadaṃśakaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | adhināthaḥ śvakrīḍī kaścijjnaḥ yo hi ‘śvakrīḍī śyenajīvī ca’ ityādismṛtibhirhavyakabyānarhatayā nindyate | tasya padaṃ sthānaṃ caraṇaṃ vā aśnute prāpnoti garhitasthānasevī bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe antareṇeti chedaḥ madhye ityarthaḥ | ‘athāntare’ntarā | antareṇa ca madhye syuḥ ‘ ityamaraḥ | kasaṃyogāt kavarṇayossaṃyogāt ajbhiravyavahitatayā’vasthānāt | kukkurādhināthapadaṃ aśnute | kukurādhināthapadasyaiva uktarītyā madhyagatakakārasya kakārāntareṇa saṃyuktatve kukkurādhināthapadatāniṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ | atra antareṇetyetatkukkuraśabdamātrakaṭākṣeṇa, tathā satyeva kakārasaṃyogasya madhyasthatāsaṃbhavāt | yadvā kukkurādhināthapadaśabdābhiprāyeṇaivāstu, tasmiṃśca śabde ādyantavarṇayormadhyavartitāyāḥ rephādīnāmiva kakārasyāpi durapalapatvāditi dhyeyam || iha kaściddvijarājaḥ puruṣottamamantare dadhadvimalaḥ| malino’nyo dvijarājassaṃtatamapyantareṇakaṃ bibhrat || 688 ||
kaścit dvijarājaḥ agrajanmanāmagragaṇyaḥ | antare manasi puruṣottamaṃ dadhat dhyāyanniti yāvat | vimalaḥ nirmalaḥ | anyaḥ dvijarājaḥ candramāḥ antare aṇakaṃ garhyaṃ yaṃ kaṃcit bibhrat dhyāyan malinaḥ | anyatra antaḥ svabimbamadhye eṇakaṃ kuraṅgaṃ bibhradityarthaḥ | eṣūdāharaṇeṣūpamānatadviśeṣaṇopādānasāmarthyādākṣipta eva vyatirekaḥ, na tu vyaṅgya iti jātvapi bhramitavyam | satyanupapattileśe vyañjanāyā anunmeṣāt | iha ca bhagavadviśeṣaṇānāṃ yathākathaṃcitpraśaṃsārthatve’pyupamānatadviśeṣaṇopādānasya bhagavadutkarṣamantareṇānupapatterjāgarūkatvāt | yatra tūpamānatadviśeṣaṇopādānamantareṇaivopameyaviśeṣaṇaissundaro devadatta ityādāviva vastusthitiprakāśanena caritārthairapyabhiprāyaviśeṣeṇa svavilakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭadharmantarāpekṣayā varṇyasyotkarṣaḥ pratīyate sa vyañjanāviṣayaḥ ||
yathā
jagatāṃ jīvanadāne śaradāṃ śatake’pi naiṣi nīrasatām | na ca pāṇḍimānamayase kāmapi dhatse śriyaṃ sadopacitām ||689||
śaradāṃ śaradṛtūnāṃ vatsarāṇāṃ ca | ayaṃ vāridādbhagavato vyatirekadhvanirarthaśaktimūlaḥ || atra rasagaṅgādharakṛt ”yattūpamānādupameyasya nyūnatve’pi vyatirekaḥ, vailakṣaṇyamātrasyaiva vyatirekatvādityalaṃkārasarvasvakāroktamanusaratā kuvalayānandakṛtā nyūnatāyāmudāhṛtaṃ
raktastvaṃ navapallavairahamapi ślāghyaiḥ priyāyā guṇaiḥ
tvāmāyānti śilīmukhāssmaradhanurmuktāstathā māmapi |
kāntāpādatalāhatistava mude tadvanmamāpyāvayoḥ
sarvaṃ tulyamaśoka kevalamahaṃ dhātrā saśokaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
atra saśokatvenāśokāpekṣayā’pakarṣaḥ paryavasyatīti, tadapi cintyaṃ ratyādyanukūlatayā kutaścidaṅgābhūṣaṇāpasāraṇaṃ yathā śobhāviśeṣāya bhavati, evaṃ prakṛte upamālaṃkārāddūrīkaraṇamātrameva rasānuguṇatayā ramaṇīyam, na vyatirekaḥ | ata evāsamālaṃkāraṃ prāñco na bahumanvate | anyathā tavālaṃkārāntaratayā tatsvīkārāpatteḥ | yathā
bhuvanatritaye’pi bhānavaiḥ paripūrṇe vibudhaiśca dānavaiḥ |
na bhaviṣyati nāsti nābhavannṛpa yaste bhajate tulāpadam ||
iti | ata eva dhvanikṛtā sahṛdayadhurandhareṇa ‘sukavistu rasānusāreṇa kvacidalaṃkārasaṃyogaṃ kvacidalaṃkāraviyogaṃ ca kuryāt ityuktvā ‘raktastvaṃ’ iti padyaṃ sādṛśyadūrīkaraṇe udājahre’| ata eva ca mammaṭabhaṭṭaiḥ ‘ ādhikyamātraṃ vyatirekaḥ’ ityuktam , nirastaṃ ca nyūnatvaṃ vyatireke | tasmādupamānādupameyasyotkarṣa eva vyatirekālaṃkāraḥ nāpakarṣa iti sthitam | yadi tu nyūnatvamapi vyatireka ityāgrahaḥ tadedamudāhāryam
jagattrayatrāṇadhṛtavratasya kṣamātalaṃ kevalameva rakṣan |
kathaṃ samārohasi hanta rājansahasranetrasya tulāṃ dvinetraḥ ||
atra dharmadvayenaiva nyūno’si dharmāntareṇa tu sama iti pratītikṛtavicchittiviśeṣādalaṃkāratā | evaṃca lakṣaṇe apakarṣo’pyevaṃ
jātīyo deyaḥ ||
yadapi kuvalayānandakṛtā’nubhavaparyavasāyino vyatirekasyodāharaṇamuktam
dṛḍhataranibaddhamuṣṭeḥ kośaniṣaṇṇasya sahajamalinasya |
kṛpaṇasya kṛpāṇasya ca kevalamākārato bhedaḥ ||
iti, tannipuṇaṃ na nīrīkṣitamāyuṣmatā | tathāhi kimatropamānādutkarṣarūpo vyatireko’nubhavaparyavasāyī? āhosvitsarvasvakārādyuktadiśā apakarṣarūpaḥ? nādya utkarṣaprayojakadharmasyātrānupasthiteḥ na ca śleṣeṇa dīrghākṣarasyopasthitirastyeveti vācyam, tasyopamānavṛttitvenopameyānutkarṣakatvāt | arthāntareṇākṛtirūpeṇa saha śleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasāyena sādhāraṇīkaraṇācca | anyathā śleṣamūlakopamocchedāpatteḥ | sudhāṃśubimbamiva nagaraṃ sakalakalamityādāvapi kalakalasahitatvakalāsākalyayorvastuto vaidharmyarūpatvāt | na ca sakalakalamityatropamāyāmeva kavernirbharaḥ prakṛte tu bhedaśabdoktyā vailakṣaṇye sa iti bhramitavyam | yadyatropamāvighaṭanarūpo vyatireko nirbharasahassyāt ākāśabdaśleṣo’narthakassyāt | kṛpaṇasya kṛpāṇasya ca bhedo dīrghākṣarādevetyeva brūyāt | na hyatra vyatireke śleṣo’nukūlaḥ | pratyuta pratikūla eva | upamāyāṃ punaranukūlaḥ | pratikūlasya dīrghākṣararūpavaidharmyasya sādhāraṇīkaraṇāt ākṛtibhedasya copamānopameyayorapi sattvāt | evaṃ hi kaverāśayaḥ ‘kṛpaṇakṛpāṇayostulyataiva, dṛḍhataretyādiviśeṣaṇasāmyāt | akṣarabhedastvākārabhedatvādaviruddha eva’ iti sahṛdayairākalanīyam | na dvitīyaḥ tasyoktimātreṇāpyasaṃgateḥ, ahṛdyatvācca | tasmādatra gamyopamaiva supratiṣṭhitetyāstāṃ tāvatkūṭakārṣāpaṇodghāṭanam | prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ” ityabhāṇīt | evaṃcaitanmate prāgasmatpradarśitānyanubhayavyatirekodāharaṇāni yathāyogamanenaiva pathā neyāni ||
nyūnavyatirekodāharaṇaṃ tvevam
akhilajagaccheṣitvaṃ tasya tavaikonasarvaśeṣitvam | ārohasi tena tulāṃ jagatsavitrā jagatsavitri katham || 690 ||
idaṃ tu cintanīyam upameyādhikyaparyavasāyī tannyūnatvaparyavasāyī anubhayaparyavasāyī ceti vyatirekastredhā vibhaktaḥ kuvalayānande | tatra anubhavaparyavasāyīti pāṭhastu lekhakaskhalanakṛtaḥ | anubhayatādrūpyarūpakādivadanubhayaparyavasāyitvenaiva vyatirekatṛtīyaprabhedasya kartumucitatvāt | udāharaṇe’pi ‘dṛḍhataranibaddhamuṣṭeḥ’ ityatra upameyādhikyatannyūnatvobhayaparyavasānarāhityasyaiva spaṣṭaṃ darśanāt | rasagaṅgādharakārastu anubhavaparyavasāyītyeva kuvalayānandapāṭhaṃ manvānaḥ vyatirekasyotkarṣāpakarṣānyatararūpatāmadhyavasyan kuvalayānandapradarśitaṃ tṛtīyaprabhedodāharaṇamadūduṣat | upadarśite anubhayaparyavasāyīti pāṭhe tu sarvamidaṃ taduktadūṣaṇamalagnakameva | kiṃca vyatirekasyotkarṣāpakarṣānyatararūpatayā kuvalayānandakṛdvivakṣitatve tasya upameyotkarṣaparyavasāyitvena tannyūnatvaparyavasāyitvena ca vibhajanameva nopapadyeta | utkarṣarūpasya vyatirekasya utkarṣaparyavasāyitvavācoyukterayuktatvāt | tasmādvaidharmyamātrameva vyatirekaḥ | sa ca upameyotkarṣaparyavasāyī tannyūnatvaparyavasāyī upameyotkarṣatannyūnatvobhayaparyavasānarahitaśceti trividha ityeva hi kuvalayānandakṛdāśayaḥ | etatsarvamabhipretyaiva candrikāyāṃ vaidyanāthaḥ ‘kiṃciddharmaprayuktasāmyavattayā pratīyamānayoḥ kiṃciddharmaprayuktavailakṣaṇyaṃ vyatirekaśarīram | vailakṣaṇyaṃ tu kvacidupameyasyotkarṣe kvacicca tadapakarṣe paryavasannam | kvacittu tadanyataraparyavasānavirahe’pi svavaicitryamātraviśrāntamiti bodhyam | etena caramabhedadvayatadudāharaṇadūṣaṇaṃ kasyacidāpātaramaṇīyamiva bhāsamānaṃ pratyuktamiti veditavyam | atyantāsāratayā tu nānuvādamarhatītyuparamyate’ ityavādīt ||
alaṃkārāntarottambhito’pyayaṃ saṃbhavati | yathā
vṛkṣaṃ pariṣvajete sayujau suhṛdau samaṃ suparṇau dvau | ekassvādvattyanayoḥ pippalamanyo’bhicākaśītyanadan || 691 ||
idaṃ padyaṃ
dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte |
tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvattyanaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti ||
iti śrutyarthānuvādi | ‘yujyata iti yuk | yukchabdo guṇaparaḥ samānaguṇakassayugiti vyāsāryairvivṛtatvāt | sayujau samānaguṇakau apahatapāpmatvādiguṇaiḥ parasparasamānau | sakhāyau sahacarau dvau suparṇau pakṣisadṛśau | samānaṃ ekaṃ vṛkṣaṃ vṛkṣavacchedanārhaṃ śarīraṃ samāśritāvityarthaḥ | tayormadhye anyataro jīvaḥ svādu paripakvaṃ pippalaṃ karmaphalaṃ bhuṅkte | anyastu paramātmā abhuñjāna eva prakāśate’ iti raṅgarāmānujamunayaḥ | ayaṃ rūpakātiśayoktyuttambhitaḥ | evaṃ yathāyogamalaṃkārāntarasaṃkīrṇatā draṣṭavyā || asya cālaṃkārasya sādṛśyagarbhatvātsādṛśyasya ca trividhadharmonmīlitatvādatrāpi tatprakārānugamo’nusandheyaḥ ||
tatrānugāmini dharme yathā
āśaṃsanīyamakhilairāsvādyaṃ cāmṛtaṃ jaḍadhyuditam | ajaḍadhyuditaṃ lalitaṃ caritaṃ bhavato hare parākurute ||692||
jaḍadhībhiḥ mandamatibhiḥ uditaṃ uktam | jaladhāvutpannamiti vastusthitiḥ | ajaḍadhībhiḥ sūkṣmamatibhiḥ uditaṃ uktaṃ jaladhāvanudbhūtaṃ ca | atrāśaṃsanīyatvāsvādyatvalalitatvānyanugāmino dharmāḥ | śleṣopātte jaḍadhyajaḍadhyuditatve apakarṣotkarṣahetū ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvāpanne yathā
paricitamuktāhāraḥ kaṇṭhaḥ kaṇṭhīravādripatidayite | parigatapayaḥpayonidhitaraṅgamapahasati kambumamba tava || 693 ||
atra muktāhāradugdhodataraṅgayorbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ paricitaparigatayośśuddhasāmānyatā ||
evaṃ sādṛśyālīḍho vyatireko nirūpitaḥ | abhedālīḍho’pyeṣa saṃbhavati | yathā
nirdhūtapalāśakulo nātha viśālekṣaṇo vidhutakalikaḥ | bhajasi sadānandanatāṃ mandārastvamiti kathamiva vadema || 694 || nirdhūtaṃ palāśānāṃ patrāṇāṃ kravyādānāṃ ca kulaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ | śālānāṃ śākhānāṃ īkṣaṇaṃ darśanaṃ tadvigataṃ yasya sa viśālekṣaṇaḥ | śākhāvalokanamapyasya nāsti kimuta tatpariṣkṛtatvamiti bhāvaḥ | ‘śākhāśāle’ ityamaraḥ | anyatra vipulanayanaḥ | vidhutāḥ kalikāḥ korakāḥ yena | pakṣe vidhutaḥ kaliryena sa tathoktaḥ, parāstakalidoṣa iti yāvat | ‘kalimalasaṃhatikālano’khileśaḥ’ ityādyukteḥ | ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap ! sadā anandanatāṃ nandanavanasaṃbandhavaighuryaṃ, pakṣe ānandayatītyānandanaḥ satāmānandanaḥ sadānandanaḥ tasya bhāvaṃ bhajasi| evaṃ sati kathaṃ tvāṃ mandāra iti vadema ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vyatirekasaro dvāviṃśaḥ
<24>
atha sahoktyalaṃkārasaraḥ
guṇapradhānatābhājorarthayorubhayoryadā | varṇyassahārthasaṃbandhassahoktiṃ tāṃ tadā viduḥ ||
guṇapradhānabhāvāvacchinnayorarthayoḥ sahārthasaṃbandhassahoktiḥ | iyamapi bhedaprādhānya eva | guṇapradhānabhāvanibandhanamatra bhedaprādhānyam | sahārthaprayuktaśca guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ ‘sahayukte’pradhāne’ ityanuśāsanāt | upamānopameyatvaṃ cātra vaivakṣikam | dvayorapi prākaraṇikatvādaprākaraṇikatvādvā | sahārthabalāddhi tayostulyakakṣyatvaṃ, atulyakakṣyayorupamānopameyabhāvāyogāt | tatra ca tṛtīyāntasya niyamena guṇatvādupamānatvam | arthātpariśiṣṭasyopameyatvam | guṇapradhānabhāvo’pyatra śābda eva, na tvārthaḥ vastuto viparyayasyāpi saṃbhavāt | sāhityaṃ cātra kartrādinānā bhedamiti dhyeyam | hṛdyatvaṃ cālaṃkārasāmānyalakṣaṇāgataṃ sarvālaṃkārasādhāraṇameva | taccātra kāryakāraṇapaurvāparyaviparyayātmikayā śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasānātmikayā kevalābhedādhyavasānātmikayā cātiśayoktyā’nuprāṇane bhavatīti vadanti ||
yāṃstvaṃ paśyasi teṣāṃ vikasanti samaṃ mukhāni ca sukhāni | arayo nirayāśca samaṃ sarayaṃ dūrībhavanti muravairin || 695 ||
iti tulyayogitādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāyāvacchinnayorityantam| tatra hi dvayorapi pradhānayoreva sahārthasaṃbandhaḥ | iha tu sahaśabdayogino’ pradhānatvaniyamādguṇapradhānabhāvāvacchinnayorevārthayossahārthasaṃbandha iti nātiprasaṅgaḥ ||
yathā
daśavadanādimakadanairviśakalitaṃ sakalalokamaviviṣatā | bhūmerbhāreṇa samaṃ bhavatā bhagavan javādavātāri || 696 ||
aviviṣatā avitumicchatā | atra bhagavadavatārakāryasya bhūbhārāvataraṇasya paurvāparyaviparyayānuprāṇitassahabhāvaḥ ||
yathāvā
pāvanatāvakapadayugasevanasaṃsphītakautukā lokāḥ | siṃhācalena sākaṃ siṃhāsanamacyutādhirohanti || 697 || pūrvaṃ kartussahoktiḥ | iha tu karmaṇaḥ | siṃhācalaśśeṣādriḥ | atrāpi siṃhācalārohaṇakāryasya siṃhāsanārohaṇasya paurvāparyaviparyayeṇānuprāṇitassahabhāvaḥ | kāraṇaṃ hi niyatapūrvakālavṛtti kāryaṃ tvaparakālavṛtti tayostulyakālatvenoktirapi tadviparyaya evetyavadheyam ||
tvatpāñcajanyaninade nanu deva visṛtvare yathā’bhīṣṭam | amarīvadanābjaissamamasurīvadanāmbujāni vidalanti || 698 ||
amarīvadanābjani vidalanti vikasanti | asurīvadanāmbujāni tu vidalanti vigatāni dalāni yeṣāṃ tānīvācaranti nirdalakamalānīva śobhāvikalānīti bhāvaḥ | atra vidalantīti śleṣeṇābhedādhyavasitiḥ ||
yathāvā
kṛtināṃ tava caritāni śrutisṛtijaṅgālatāṃ prapannāni | duritaissaha hṛdayānyapi paritaśśrīnātha dhāvayanti tarām ||699 ||
atra drāvaṇaśodhanayordhāvayantīti śabdagataśleṣeṇābhedādhyavasitiḥ | pūrvā kartṛsahoktiḥ | iyaṃ tu karmasahoktiḥ ||
yathāvā
ārtebharavaśravaṇe nakreṇa samaṃ tvayoddhṛtaṃ cakram| tenaiva saha sa muktastvattaḥ prakṛtau hi na ca bhidā’styanayoḥ ||700 || uddhṛtaṃ utkṣiptaṃ unmūlitaśceti liṅgavipariṇāmena yojyam| tenaiva cakreṇaiva saha nakraḥ tvattaḥ tvatsakāśāt muktaḥ tyaktaḥ ‘apetāpoḍhamukta’ iti samāsavidhānāt jñāpakātpañcamī | pakṣe devalaśāpānmukta ityarthaḥ | ‘mukto devalaśāpena hūhūrgandharvasattamaḥ’ iti śrībhāgavatokteḥ | nakracakrayostulyatayoddharaṇamocane kathamityata āha prakṛtāvityādi | hi yasmāt anayoḥ nakracakrayoḥ prakṛtau svabhave na ca bhidā naiva bhedo’sti tasmāduddharaṇamocanataulyamanayoryuktamiti bhāvaḥ | pakṣe anayoḥ nakracakraśabdayoḥ prakṛtau nakracakretyākārake pratyayavidhāvuddeśyabhūte śabdarūpe nacabhidā nakāracakārābhyāmeva bhedaḥ kretyākārakavarṇasyobhayatra tulyatvāt | svādau pratyaye puṃnapuṃsakaliṅgayoranayośśabdayoḥ visargānusvārādikṛtacailakṣaṇyasadbhāve’pi prakṛtimātre nakāracakāramātrakṛtaṃ vailakṣaṇyamiti bhāvaḥ | iyamapi karmasahoktiḥ śleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyamūlā | atra kāryakāraṇapaurvāparyaviparyayalakṣaṇātiśayoktisadbhāve’pi śleṣamūlakābhedādhyavasitereva camatkārātiśayādhāyakatvam | atra pūrvārdhe nakracakrayorguṇapradhānabhāvaḥ | uttarārdhe cakranakrayossa iti vaiparītyaṃ ca viśeṣaḥ ||
śuddhaśleṣabhittikābhedādhyavasāyamūlā yathā
niravadhikaruṇānidhinā bhavatā bhavatāpato natānavatā | iṣṭena sahāniṣṭaṃ nirastamāraci samastamapi bhagavan || 701 ||
iṣṭena abhīpsitārthena saha aniṣṭamaśubhaṃ nirastamāraci | iṣṭapakṣe nirgataṃ astaṃ adarśanaṃ yasya tat nirastam | astamiti māntamavyayaṃ, tena saha bahuvrīhiḥ| ‘astamadarśane’ itya vyayeṣvamara | anastaṃgataṃ prakāśamānamevākārītyarthaḥ | aniṣṭapakṣe nirastaṃ prakṣiptaṃ nāśitamityarthaḥ | atra nirastamiti śleṣeṇaiva bhedādhyavasāyaḥ, na tvanyaprakāreṇa | atrāpi karmasāhityameva ||
nyapatan śirāṃsyarīṇāṃ murāribāṇaissahārinārīṇām | aśrujharaissātaṅkaistāṭaṅkaissurasumaiśca vijayāṅkaiḥ || 702 ||
vijayāṅkaiḥ vijayacihnabhūtairityarthaḥ | atra nipatanasya bāṇādyāśrayabhedena bhidurasyāpi cyutatvarūpaikopādhyavacchinnatayā abhidurīkṛtasyopādānamityasyaikakriyāsaṃbandhaḥ | ata eva na śleṣaḥ tasya pratipādyatāvacchedakabheda evābhyupagamāt | eṣūdāharaṇeṣu sahayogatṛtīyāprayukto guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ | prādhānyena kriyānvaye tu yathāyathaṃ tulyayogitā dīpakaṃ vā bhavati | sahādiśabdaprayogeṇa vinā’pīyaṃ saṃbhavati ‘vṛddho yūnā’ iti nirdeśena tṛtīyāyā niṣpratyūhaprasaratvāt | paraṃtvivādiśabdaprayogavidhurotprekṣāvadgamyā | apradhānabhāvastu śābda eva | yadyapi prakṛtatvāprakṛtatve prāyeṇopameyopamānatayornirṇāyake | tathā’pīha na tābhyāṃ tayornirṇayaḥ, prakṛtayorapi sāhityasaṃbhavāt | kiṃtu prādhānyāprādhānyābhyāmeva | hṛdyatvaṃ cāsyā atiśayoktikṛtamityavocāma | yatra tu sā nāsti tatra ‘putreṇa sahāgataḥ pitā’
nākaṃ pareṇa bhāntaṃ lokaṃ paramaṃ vihāya divyaṃ tam | lokaṃpṛṇassa bhagavān sākaṃ lakṣmyaiva viharati vṛṣādrau || 703 || ityādau na sahoktiralaṃkāraḥ | lokaṃ pṛṇati prīṇayatīti lokaṃpṛṇaḥ | pṛṇatiḥ prīṇanārthakaḥ | asmānmūlavibhujāditvātkaḥ | ‘lokasya pṛṇe’ iti mumāgamaḥ ||
atra vicāryate ‘daśavadanādimakadanaiḥ’ ityādau paurvāparyaviparyayānuprāṇitā sahoktiralaṃkāra iti na yuktam | atiśayokterevātra camatkārāvahatvena sahoktessaṃjñāmātratvāt | ‘tava kopo’rināśaśca jāyate yugapannṛpa’ ityasmādatiśayoktyalaṃkārāt ‘tava kopo’rināśena sahaiva nṛpa jayate’ ityatra guṇabhāvamātraprayukte satyapi vaijātye vicchittiviśeṣasyādarśanāttasyaiva cālaṃkāravibhājakatvāt | na ca sādṛśyānuprāṇitasya rūpakāderapyapārthakyāpattiḥ, ‘navanīradasadṛśo’sau, eṣa navīnāmbudassākṣāt’ ityādau vicchittivailakṣaṇyasya jāgarūkatvāt | anyathā sādṛśyaprayuktasya vyatirekasyānutthānāpatteḥ | apica sādṛśyaprayuktarūpakādiṣu sādṛśyasya guṇabhāvāccamatkāraviśrāntibhūmibhyo rūpakādibhyo yathā na pṛthagvyapadeśyatvaṃ tathā sahabhāvoktyunmīlitāyāḥ kāryakāraṇapaurvāparyaviparyayātmikāyā atiśayoktessakāśādasyāssahokterapārthakyamevocitam | nanvevaṃ sati nirviṣayataiva syātsahokteḥ, sahoktyantarasyāpyabhedādhyavasānarūpātiśayena kabalīkārāditi cennābhedādhyavasānamūlāyāṃ hi sahoktau abhedādhyavasānenopaskriyate sahoktiriti ‘na guṇena tiraskāraḥ pradhānasya apitu pradhānena guṇasya’ ityuktadiśā labdhāvakāśaiva sahoktiḥ | guṇapradhānabhāvaścānāgrahaissūkṣmayā dṛśā’vadhātavyaḥ | kiṃca parasparābhedādhyavasānamātramatiśaya eva | nātiśayoktiḥ, tasya śleṣādāvapi sattvāt | sā tūpamānena viṣayasya nigaraṇam | evaṃca ‘vidalanti, nyapatan’ ityādavekenāparasya nigaraṇābhāvānnātiśayoktigandho’pi| atiśayamātraṃ tu prāyeṇa sādhāraṇadharmāṃśe bhūyasāmupaskarotyalaṃkārāṇām | na hi ‘śobhate padmavanmukham’ ityādau padmamukhaśobhayorvastuto bhinnayorabhedādhyavasānamantareṇopamā prakāśate | tasmātkāryakāraṇapaurvāparyaviparyayamūlassahokterekaḥ prakāra iti sarvasvakārādīnāmuktirāgrahamūlaiva | abhedādhyavasānamūlastu prakāro bhavatu nāma sahokterviṣayaḥ | yadi tu dīpake tulyayogitāyāṃ copamānopameyayoḥ prādhānyena kriyādirūpadharmānvayaḥ, iha tu guṇapradhānabhāvenaiveti viśeṣassannapi vicchittiviśeṣānādhāyakatayā nālaṃkārāntaratvaprayojakaḥ apitu tadavāntarabhedatāyā iti vibhāvyate, apāsyate ca jaradālaṃkārikavadanāvalokanadākṣiṇyaṃ, tadā niviśatāmiyamapi klṛptālaṃkāreṣveva | kiṃcidvailakṣaṇyamātreṇaivālaṃkārabhede vacanabhaṅgīnāmānantyādalaṃkārānantyaprasaṅgāditi | satyaṃ guṇapradhānabhāvāliṅgitasya sahabhāvasyālaṃkārāntarādvicchittiviśeṣamanubhavantaḥ prācīnā eva sahokteḥ pṛthagalaṃkāratāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ, anyathā evaṃjātīyopaplavena bhūyasī syādvaiyākulī | naiva pramāṇīkurmahe vayaṃ mṛṣānimīlitalocanānprācīnān, niveśyatāṃ ceyamalaṃkārāntarāntaḥpurakārāgṛhaṃ varākīti tu prabhutaiva, na tu sahṛdayatetyalaṃ dūradhāvanena ||
evaṃ kriyāyāssādhāraṇadharmatāyāmudāharaṇāni darśitāni | guṇasya tathātve yathā
