bhuvanādhipa tava nābhyā saha mānasamapyupaiti gāmbhīryam | aṃsaissaha mahimā’pi ca kaṃsaripo māṃsalairmahaunnatyam || 704 || atra yadyapi kriyā’pi guṇena saha samānadharmatāmaśnute | tathā’pi tasyāḥ nāntarīyakatvenāsundaratvātparyavasāne guṇasyaiva samagrabhārasahiṣṇutvam | atra gāmbhīryaunnatyayorāśrayabhedabhinnayorabhedādhyatrasānena sahabhāvaḥ | evaṃ śleṣe’pi bodhyam | yatra caikamevopameyaṃ parasparavilakṣaṇasahoktyālambanaṃ sā mālātulyatayā mālāsahoktiḥ ‘nyapatan śirāṃsyarīṇām’ ityatra murāribāṇaissaha arinārīṇāmaśrujharaissaha tāṭaṅkaissaha surasumaissahetyupamānabhedena sāhityasyānekatve’pi nipatanaikyātsahokterabhedaḥ | sati vā yathākathaṃcidbhede nāsti vailakṣaṇyam, dharmaikyāt | dharmopamānobhayakṛtavailakṣaṇyasya cātra vivakṣitatvāt |
mathanoccalakalaśāmbudhikaṇaissahāmarasumaiśca visarantaḥ | padmākṣe nipatantaḥ pāntu kaṭākṣāḥ payodhikanyāyāḥ || 705 ||
ityatra dharmavailakṣaṇyasadbhāve’pi visaraṇadharmonmīlitasahoktighaṭakopamānabhūtānāṃ kalaśāmbudhikaṇāmarasumānāmeva nipatanadharmottambhitāyāmapi sahoktau ghaṭakatvānna mālārūpatā | atra payodhikanyāyā iti viśeṣyaṃ kaṭākṣāṇāṃ kṣīrodanvadavatārakālikatvaṃ dhvanayitum ||
idaṃ tūdāharaṇam
bhāgyaissamamunmiṣitaṃ jagatāṃ sārdhaṃ vijṛmbhitaṃ vibhavaiḥ | saha vismayairamāntaṃ dhanyāḥ phaṇigiripatermahaṃ yānti || 706 ||
atronmiṣitatvādidharmāṇāṃ bhāgyādyupamānānāṃ ca vailakṣaṇyāḍhu pameyasya śrīnivāsotsavasyaikatvācca sahoktermālārūpateti dhyeyam|| śoṇoruṣā vyaraci dṛkkoṇena samaṃ daśāsyakāyaśca | bāṇena sahāmuṣya prāṇo’pyudadhāri raghupate bhavatā || 707 ||
he raghupate ! bhavatā dṛkkoṇena samaṃ daśāsyakāyaśca ruṣā śoṇo vyaraci | pakṣe aruṣeti chedaḥ | dṛkkoṇaḥ ruṣā śoṇaḥ daśasyakāyaḥ aruṣā vraṇena śoṇaḥ lohitaḥ vyaracītyarthaḥ | ‘vraṇo’striyāmīrmamaruḥ’ ityamaraḥ | bāṇena saha amuṣya daśasyasya prāṇaḥ pañcavṛttirmukhyaprāṇopi udadhāri bāṇa utkṣiptaḥ prāṇa unmūlita ityarthaḥ | utpūrvakasya dharaterupapāditobhayārthaśaktatvāt | ‘syāduddharaṇamunnaye | bhuktojjhitonmūlanayoḥ’ iti hemacandraḥ | atra dvayoreva parasparavilakṣaṇasahoktyossadbhāve’pi mālātvamastyeva | evamapi rasagaṅgādharādau mālāsahokterudāharaṇāt | pūrvārdhe guṇasāhityaṃ uttarārdhe kriyāsāhityaṃ śleṣamūlātiśayoktyā porvāparyaviparyayātmakātiśayoktyā cānuprāṇitam | pūrvodāharaṇe tu anu gāmidharmottambhitaṃ sāhityamityādi vailakṣaṇyaṃ draṣṭavyam ||
yathāvā
ārtiṃ sārdhaṃ maurvyā niśicaratatiradhyaropi raghupatinā | khedena samaṃ sātirmodasyātāni tena savanabhujām || 708 ||
raghupatinā maurvyā śiñjinyā sārdhaṃ niśicarāṇāṃ tatiḥ ārtiṃ adhyaropi | maurvī dhanuṣkoṭiṃ niśicaratātiḥ pīḍāṃ ca adhyaropītyarthaḥ | ‘ārtiḥ pīḍādhanuṣkoṭyoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tena ārtyadhyāropaṇena | raghupatinā vā savanabhujāṃ devānāṃ khedena samaṃ modasya sātiḥ atāni | khedasyāvasānaṃ modasya dānaṃ ca kṛtamityarthaḥ | ‘sātirdānāvasānayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇavanmālārūpatādikaṃ yathāyogamanusandheyam ||
yathāvā
gurubhissaha śuśrūṣā carite tava lasati tāvakīnānām | kīrtirdānonmiṣitā gṛhāṅgaṇe’cyuta diśāṃgaṇena samam || 709 ||
he acyuta ! tāvakīnānāṃ tvadbhaktānāṃ gurubhiḥ ācāryaissaha tava carite śuśrūṣā lasati guruṣu viṣaye śuśrūṣā paricaryā tvaccarite viṣaye śravaṇecchā prakāśata ityarthaḥ | ‘atha śuśrūṣopāsanāśravaṇecchayoḥ’ iti hemacandraḥ | diśāṃ kakubhāṃ gaṇena samam | yadvā diśāśabdaṣṭābantaḥ diśānāmaṅgaṇena samamityarthaḥ | gṛhāṅgaṇe teṣāmeva bhavanājire dānonmiṣitā kīrtiḥ lasatītyanuṣajyate | diśāṅgaṇe dānena vitaraṇena unmiṣitā udañcitā kīrtiḥ yaśaḥ lasati gṛhāṅgaṇe dānena dviradamadasalilena unmiṣitā janitā kīrtiḥ kardamaḥ vistṛtirvā lasatītyarthaḥ | ‘dānaṃ gajamade tyāge, kīrtiḥ prasāde yaśasi, kardame vistṛtāvapi’ iti ca ratnamālā | atrāpi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ mālārūpatādikaṃ draṣṭavyam | adhikaraṇasāhityaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ | pūrvārdhe carite iti vaiṣayikamadhikaraṇaṃ, uttarārdhe gṛhāṅgaṇe iti tu aupaśleṣikamabhivyāpakaṃ vā taditibhidā| abhivyāptiścātrāpekṣikī bodhyā ||
bimbapratibimbabhāvādyāpannadharmatā’pyatra saṃbhavati | yathā
phaṇamaṇighṛṇiparivītaṃ jvālāmālāvṛtena cakreṇa | saha muñcannahimañcaṃ sahasā sa hariḥ punātu karivaradaḥ || 710 || atra phaṇamaṇighṛṇijvālāmālayorbimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ | parivītatvāvṛtatvayostu vastuprativastubhāvaḥ | cakramañcamocanayoḥ paurvāparyaviparyayaḥ tanmūlātiśayoktyā’nuprāṇitā sahoktiḥ ||
alaṃkārāntarasaṃkīrṇā’pīyaṃ saṃbhavati | yathā
manasā samaṃ vilīnaṃ ghanasārasyeva śakalamanalajvālāt | dvirasanagiriśekharataḥ duritaṃ mama vitatamekapada eva bata || 711 ||
analajvālāt ghanasārastha karpūrasya śakalamiva jātyabhiprāyakamekavacanam | dvirasanagiriśekharataḥ śeṣācalotttaṃsācchrīnivāsāt vitataṃ vistṛtaṃ mama duritaṃ, pūrvavadekavacanam | ekapada eva ekacaraṇanyāsamātra eva atikṣipramevetyarthaḥ| athavā ekapade ityedantamavyayamekasmādityarthe cādiṣu paṭhyate | atarkitamevetyarthaḥ | atisatvarameveti yāvat | manasā samaṃ vilīnam | karpūrapakṣe vilīnaṃ vidrutamityarthaḥ | ‘vilone vidrutadrutau’ ityamaraḥ | duritapakṣe darśanāviṣayamāsīdityarthaḥ | manaḥpakṣe tu dvirasanagiriśekharataḥ śrīnivāsāt pañcamyāssārvavibhaktikastasi | mama manaḥ ekapada eva ekasminvyavasāya eva tatprāptyekavyavasāya eva līnaṃ lagnamityarthaḥ| ‘dadāmi buddhiyogaṃ taṃ yena māmupayānti te’ ityuktarītyā tatprāptyekavyavasāyasya tadadhīnatvāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā saptamyāstasi | śrīnivāse ekasminpade vastunyeva līnamityarthaḥ | athavā ṣaṣṭhyāstasiḥ| śrīnivāsasya ekasminpade caraṇe lagnamityarthaḥ | ‘padaṃ vyavasitatrāṇasthānalakṣmāṅghrivastuṣu’ ityamaraḥ | atropamāśleṣamūlātiśayoktibhyāmujjīvitā sahoktiḥ || yathāvā
vibudhābhayadaḥ kathamasyasurakadambena saha surakadambam | bhavatā vṛṣaśailamaṇe susādhvasaṃ yadvyatānyatiprasabham || 712 ||
he vṛṣaśailamaṇe tvaṃ kathaṃ vibudhābhayadaḥ asi | kuto nāsmītyata āhāsuretyādi | bhavatā asurakadambena saha surakadambaṃ atiprasabhaṃ susādhvasaṃ vyatāni asurakadambaṃ atiprasahya atetrastamatānītyarthaḥ | suṣṭhu sādhvasaṃ yasya tatsusādhvasamīti samāsaḥ | surakadambapakṣe susādhu asaṃ iticchedaḥ| susādhu asaṃ atiprasabhamatānīti vaiparītyena yojanā | susādhu atisamyak asaṃ avidyamānasakāraṃ atiprasabhaṃ atiprabhamityarthaḥ | śabdārthayostādātmyaṃ na vismartavyam | atāni atitejasvi viracitamityabhiprāyaḥ | iyaṃ hitāhitavṛttitaulyalakṣaṇatulyayogitayā śleṣeṇa nindayā stutyabhivyaktilakṣaṇavyājastutyā cānuprāṇitā sahoktiriti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
audāryamadbhutaṃ tava sākaṃ vidveṣibhiḥ priyebhyopi| pradadāsi satvaraṃ tvaṃ nārakamatiduḥkhamahigirisuradro || 713 ||
he ahigirisuradro etacca mahodāratābhiprāyagarbham | tava audāryaṃ adbhutaṃ lokavilakṣaṇam | tadevopapādayati sākamiti | vidveṣibhissākaṃ priyebhyopi ‘sa ca mama priyaḥ’ ityuktadiśā prītipātrebhyo jñānibhyopi satvaraṃ hitāhitavivecanavyavadhānavidhurameva atiduḥkhaṃ nārakaṃ pradadāsītyupālabhmaḥ | vidveṣibhyaḥ atimātravyathā karaṃ nārakaṃ narakaṃ ‘syānnārakastu narakaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | pradadāsi ‘ye māṃ dviṣanti saṃmūḍhā nārake pātayāmi tān’ ityuktarītyā durgatiṃ vitarasītyartha | priyebhyastu satvaramityatra saḥ tu araṃ iti chedaḥ | saḥ tvamiti yojanā | atikrānto duḥkhamatiduḥkhaḥ taṃ tathoktam | atyādayaḥ krāntādyarthe dvitīyayā’ iti samāsaḥ | ‘yanna duḥkhena saṃbhinnaṃ’ ityuktadiśā duḥkhāsaṃbhinnamityarthaḥ | araṃ rephavidhuraṃ nārakaṃ nākamityarthaḥ ‘te ha nākaṃ mahimānaḥ’ ityādāviva nākaśabdotra paramavyomaparaḥ | pradadāsi ‘nayāmi paramāṃ gatim’ ityādinā svenaivoktatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇavadeva tulyayogitāśleṣavyājastutyanuprāṇitatvam | vailakṣaṇyaṃ tu ahigirisuradro iti rūpakātiśayoktisaṃkirṇasābhiprāyaviśeṣyalakṣaṇaparikarāṅkurānuprāṇitatvaṃ saṃpradānasasāhityaṃ cetyavadheyam ||
evaṃ ‘gurubhissaha śuśrūṣā’ iti pūrvadarśitamālāsahoktyudāharaṇe’pi śuśrūṣāderlasanarūpaikadharmānvayalakṣaṇatulyayogitānuprāṇitatvamanusandheyam |
sarvāṇyetānyudāharaṇāni sahārthakaśabdaghaṭitānyeva sahokteḥ pradarśitāni | tadaghaṭitatvena gamyā yathā
puravimathanottamāṅgaṃ dharaṇitalena trivikrama śrīman| asti stimitaṃ tvatpadakamalena kṛtaṃ purā balessavane || 714 ||
he trivikrama ! idaṃ vivakṣitārthasya tadavatārāsādhāraṇatāṃ dyotayitum | balaiḥ vairocaneḥ savane yāge | dharaṇitalena bhūtalena sahetyarthaḥ | vinā’pi sahaśabdayogaṃ tṛtīyā bhavatītyavocāma | puravimathanasya śivasya uttamāṅgaṃ mastakaṃ tvatpadasya tvaccaraṇasya kamalena salilena gaṅgārūpeṇetyarthaḥ | stimitaṃ klinnaṃ kṛtaṃ racitaṃ purā asti āsīdetyarthaḥ | ‘puri luñcāsme’ iti bhūtānadyatane laṭ | dharaṇitalapakṣe tu tvatpadakamalena tvaccaraṇāravindena ‘kamalaṃ salile tāmre jalaje’ iti medinī | avidyamānaḥ stiḥ stīti varṇasamudāyo yasmiṃstattathābhūtaṃ stimitaṃ mitaṃ mānakarmīkṛtamityarthaḥ||
evaṃ prāgupadarśitodāharaṇeṣu sarveṣvapi sahārthakaśabdāpanayane tānyapi gamyasahoktyudāharaṇāni bhavantīti dhyeyam ||
|| ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre sahoktisarastrayoviṃśaḥ ||
<25>
atha vinoktisaraḥ
kiṃcidvinā prastutasya ramyatā’ramyatā’pi vā |
nibadhyate yadi tadā sā vinoktiralaṃkṛtiḥ ||
varṇyasya kiṃcidabhāvaprayuktaṃ ramaṇīyatvāramaṇīyatvānyataravarṇanaṃ vinoktiralaṃkāraḥ | yathā
phaṇiramaṇatu śabdanaye kaṇacaraṇatu vā’pikopi tarkanaye | gaṇanāṃ nārhati manujaḥ phaṇināthagirīśvare vinā bhaktim || 715 ||
atra bhagavadbhaktivinākṛtasyāramyatvam ||
yathāvā
sakalāmacalāmaṭatu truṭatu ca śatadhā tapovidhānādyaiḥ | tava darśanādvinā’cyuta bhavitā janatā kṛtārthatāṃ na gatā || 716 ||
atra bhagavaddarśanena vinākṛtasyāramyatā ||
yathāvā
tālena vinā gānaṃ śīlena vinā na śobhate jñānam | bālena vinā gṛhiṇī pālena vinā hare tvayā dharaṇī || 717 ||
atrāpi tālādivinākṛtasyāramyatā
ramyatāyāṃ yathā
mohena vinā’cyutabhāgdāhena vinā davasya bhāti vanam | grāheṇa vinā jaladhiḥ snehena vinā khaleṣu sujanaśca || 718 ||
acyutaṃ bhajatītyacyutabhāk bhagavadbhakta ityarthaḥ | ‘bhajo ṇviḥ’ iti ṇviḥ| jaladhiḥ saraḥ jalāni dhīyante’sminniti vigrahaḥ ‘abdhī sarassarasvantau’ iti ratnamālā | atra mohādinā vinākṛtasya ramyatā | ādye kevale, itare tu dīpakānukūle ||
miśritā yathā
kārśyena vinā vibhavastava devi vibhāti jātu na tu madhyam | cāñcalyena vinā hṛdvibhāsate na tu vilocanadvandvam || 719 || atra kārśyacāñcalyavinākṛtayorvastunoḥ ramyatā’ramyate iti bhavatīyaṃ miśratā | prativastūpamanukūlā parisaṃkhyānukūlā ca ||
phaṇirājagirīśiromaṇiguṇarājī bhajati ramyatāṃ samyak | doṣaṃ vinā manāgapi vinā kalaṅkaṃ śaśāṅkarekheva || 720 ||
nityaṃ phaṇigirināthe pratyaṅṅātmā’’tmabharasamarpaṇataḥ | hṛllekho maṇiriva bhātyullekhanato vinā malīmasatām || 721 ||
pratyaṅ ātmā jīvaḥ ityarthaḥ | ‘ṅamo hrasvādaci ṅamuṇṇityam’ iti ṅakārātparasyāco nityaṃ ṅamuḍāgamassaṃhitāyām | maṇiḥ ullekhanāt śāṇoddharṣaṇādiva pratyagātmā bhagavati bharasamarpaṇāt malīmasatāṃ mṛdādisaṃsargakṛtāṃ malinatāṃ vinā hṛllekhaḥ hṛdyaḥ bhāti | hṛdayaṃ likhatīti hṛllekhaḥ hṛdayaśabdopapadakāt likheḥ karmaṇyaṇ | ‘hṛdayasya hṛllekhayadaṇlāseṣu’ iti hṛdayaśabdasya hṛdādeśaḥ | pakṣe avidyākṛtamajñānamālinyaṃ vinā ‘maṇivara iva śaurernityahṛdyaḥ’ ityādyuktarītyā bhagavato hṛdayaṃgamassan bhāti nityāsaṃkucitajñāno dīpyata ityarthaḥ | anayorādyā śuddhopamānukūlā itarā tu śleṣamūlopamānukūlā ||
mūlaṃ vinā’pi vīrudvilasetkūlaṃ vinā’pi śaivalinī | nālaṃ vyālendrācalapālaṃ devaṃ vinā kvacitkamalā || 722 || iyaṃ dīpakaparisaṃkhyānukūlā ||
rāgaṃ vinā yathā strī vegaṃ payaso vinā yathā taṭinī | tyāgaṃ vinā yathā śrīryogaṃ śaurervinā tathaiva matiḥ || 723 ||
pūrvaṃ kriyāguṇādisaṃbandha āvaśyakaḥ | iha tūpamāmahimnaivāvagamyate sa iti na tathā ||
iyaṃ ca na kevalaṃ vināśabdasadbhāva eva bhavati apitu vināśabdārthavācakasadbhāve’pi | tena nañ, nira, vi , antareṇa , ṛte , vikala, viyukta, rahita, dūrita, vidhura ityādiprayogasadbhāve’pi | yathā
phalamantareṇa śākhī jaladhirjalamantareṇa yadi bhāyāt | balamantareṇa rājā’pyalamahigirimantareṇa bhūrbhāyāt || 724 ||
balamantareṇa rājā’pi yadi bhāyādityanvayaḥ | atrāntareṇaśabdaḥ vinārthakaḥ prayuktaḥ | mithyādhyavasitidīpakānukūleyam ||
ambaramabhānubimbaṃ bimbaṃ bhānoranamburuhanayanam | ambujanayano’nambudhisutassutejastayā vibhāyātkim || 725 ||
atra vinārthako nañ prayuktaḥ | anamburuhanayanaṃ ‘tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī ‘ iti śrutiprathitapuṇḍarīkākṣarahitamityarthaḥ| anambudhisutaḥ śrīviśliṣṭa ityarthaḥ | sutejastayā suvarṇavarṇatayeti bhāvaḥ | vibhāyātkim | śrīviśliṣṭatve tādvidhyasya durlabhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ata eva hyadhikaraṇasārāvalyāṃ “nityaśrīrbradhnabimbe’ iti viśeṣitamācāryaiḥ ‘āpraṇakhātsarva eva suvarṇaḥ’ iti śrutiratrānusandheyā | iyaṃ vakṣyamāṇamālādapikānukūlā ||
śeṣācalābhimukhatā yeṣāṃ prodañcitā na kiṃcidapi | śeṣyacalābhimukhatvaṃ naiṣyantyete janussahasre’pi || 726 ||
śeṣiṇaḥ sarveśvarasya bhagavataḥ acalaṃ niścalaṃ yat abhimukhatvaṃ tatkaṭākṣagocaratvamiti yāvat | naiṣyanti na prāpnuvanti śeṣācalābhimukhyarūpopāyābhāve bhagavadābhimukhyarūpaphalaṃ durlabhamiti bhāva | atrāpi vināśabdārthavācako na prayuktaḥ | iyamekaguṇenānyadoṣodayarūpollāsālaṃkārānukūlā ||
trāsasparśavimuktā muktā iva doṣagandhasaṃtyaktāḥ |aujjvalyamukhāssuguṇā jājvalyante phaṇābhṛdacalamaṇeḥ || 727 ||
atra saṃtyaktaśabdaḥ | upamānukūlā ceyam ||
jñānānandatvādyāḥ śrīnāthaguṇāssudūritāvadyāḥ | sūrakarā iva hṛdyā vāritatamasaśśubhāssadāsvādyāḥ || 728 ||
atra sudūritaśabdaḥ | iyamapyupamānukūlaiva ||
alaṃkārabhāṣyakārastu ‘nityasaṃbandhānāmasaṃbandhavacanaṃ vinoktiḥ’ ityāha | tasya mate tu naitānyudāharaṇāni || idaṃ tūdāharaṇam
nivahena suraripūṇāṃ bhavapratāpoṣṇimānamanubhavatām | madhumathana citrabhānudvitayaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ vinoṣṇatayā || 729 ||
citrabhānvossūryavahnyoḥ dvitayaṃ ‘sūryavahnī citrabhānū’ ityamaraḥ | atra sūryavahnyorauṣṇyasyāvinābhāve’pi vinābhāvo darśitaḥ ||
yathāvā
niratiśayaviśadimāḍhye paritassphuratīha jagatei tava yaśasi | mādhava vinā dhavalatāṃ vidhuvidhivanitādayo vilokyante || 730||
vidhivanitā sarasvanī | atra dhāvalyāvinābhāve’pi tadvinābhāvo darśitaḥ | alaṃkārāntaraparirambhasaṃbhūtamevāsyā ramaṇīyatvam, na tu svataḥ | tenālaṃkārāntaratvamapi śithilameveti vadnti | pare tu kiṃcidvyatirekaprayuktaramyatvāramyatvajñānādeva camatkārodayasya niṣpratyūhatvenālaṃkārāntarānāliṅgitasyāsya ramaṇīyatāyāssahṛdayahṛdayaikasākṣikatvātsvato’syālaṃkārāntaratvamakṣatamevetyāhuḥ ||
athāsyā dhvaniḥ
kiṃ tena janmanā syātsvānte yasminna cintito’nantaḥ | kiṃ tena karmaṇā vā’nante yannārpitaṃ bhavatyante || 731 || atra bhagavaccintanaṃ vinā svāntasya bhagavadarpaṇaṃ vinā karmaṇaścāramaṇīyatvaṃ kimiti kṣepeṇa phalapraśnena vā vyajyate | tasya ca bhagavadviṣayakabhāvadhvanyanugrāhakatve’pyapratihataiva dhvanivyapadeśyatā | anyathā’nugrāhakatvalakṣaṇasaṃkarocchedāpattiriti ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre vinoktisaraścaturviṃśaḥ
<26>
atha samāsoktisaraḥ
sāmyādbhedakamātrasya gamyamaprastutaṃ yadi | samāsoktimimāṃ prāhuḥ prāñco’laṃkāravedinaḥ ||
bhedakaṃ viśeṣaṇam | viśeṣaṇamātrasāmyagamyāprastutavṛttāntā samāsoktiḥ samāsena saṃkṣepeṇa prakṣtutāprastuta vṛttāntayorvacanāt |
asāvudayamārūḍhaḥ kāntimānraktamaṇḍalaḥ |
rājā harati lokānāṃ hṛdayaṃ mṛdubhiḥ karaiḥ ||
iti prakṛtāprakṛtaśleṣe cāprakṛtanṛpavṛttāntasya viśeṣyavācinā śliṣṭena rājapadenāpyavagamānna tatrātivyāptiḥ ||
yathā
sāmodabāṣpagadgadaromodgamavepathupralayalalitam| āliṅgati phaṇadharagirimauliṃ bhaktyunmukhā madīyamatiḥ || 732 ||
atra bhaktyunmukhasvamativṛttānte varṇyamāne sāmodetyādikriyāviśeṣaṇasāmyādvallabhasvayaṃgrahāśleṣaprasaktanāyikāvṛttāntaḥ pratīyate ||
sphuṭakucamukhavarṇaiṣā vṛttātulanābhito’tigambhīrā| kavivāṇī lalitapadāśleṣeṇa harermudaṃ dadātei parām || 733 ||
kuḥ kuvarṇaḥ kavargo vā caḥ cavarṇaśca mukhaṃ yeṣāṃ tāni varṇāni akṣarāṇi sphuṭāni yasyāssā tathoktā | anyatra sphuṭāḥ kucamukhavarṇāḥ stanavadanavarṇāḥ yasyāssā tathoktā | avidyamānā tulanā sāmyaṃ yasyāssā tathoktā | vṛttaiḥ mātrāvarṇabhedabhiduraghchandoniṣpannaiḥ ślokaiḥ vṛttena bhagavaccaritreṇa vā atulanā asadṛgityarthaḥ | lokottaravṛttaghaṭitatayā’tiślāghyeti bhāvaḥ |’vṛtaṃ padye caritre triṣvatīte dṛḍhanistale’ ityamaraḥ | abhitaḥ samantataḥ atigambhīrā ‘dhvānimattā tu gāmbhīryam’ ityuktagāmbhīryaguṇaśālinītyarthaḥ | pakṣe vṛttā āvartavadvartulā atulā asadṛśī yā nābhiḥ tayā nābhitaḥ sārvabibhaktikastasiḥ | atigambhīrā atigambhīravartulāsadṛśanābhirityarthaḥ | lalitāni padāni suptiṅantātmakāni yasyāṃ sā, lalite pade caraṇe yasyāssā tathoktā ca kavivāṇī śleṣeṇa pakṣe āśleṣeṇeti chedaḥ | āliṅganenetyarthaḥ| atra kavibhāratīvṛttānte prastute sphuṭakucamukhetyādiviśeṣaṇasāmyāt vāṇīśabdagatastrīliṅgamahimnā cāprastutotkaṇṭhitanāyikāvṛttāntaḥ pratīyate | pūrvatra viśeṣaṇāni sādhāraṇāni | iha tu śliṣṭānīti viśeṣaḥ | sādhāraṇatvaṃ ca śleṣeṇa vinaiva prakṛtāprakṛtavṛttāntānugatatvam || yathāvā
ādau madamalino’cyuta jalā (ḍā)śayānupraveśitapado’tha | viṣamatamāsadgrāhagrasto’dya viraumyudastahasto’ham || 734 ||
he acyuta ! ādau madena garveṇa dānodakena ca malinaḥ, atha jalāśayeṣu jaḍadhīṣu jalādhāreṣu ca anupraveśitaṃ padaṃ vyavasāyaḥ caraṇaṃ ca yena sa tathoktaḥ | ata eva viṣamatamena asatā grāheṇa durāgraheṇa | pakṣe jalajantuviśeṣeṇa avahārāparaparyāyeṇa grastassan ‘grāho grahe’vahāre ca’ iti medinī | udastaḥ utkṣiptaḥ hastaḥ karaḥ śuṇḍā ca yena sa tathoktassan adya karmaparipākadaśayāṃ, viraumi rodimi | atra saṃsāranirviṇṇajanavṛttānte prastute śliṣṭaviśeṣaṇasāmyāt grāhagrastajaradgajavṛttāntaḥ pratīyate ||
sārūpyādapīyaṃ dṛśyate yathā
janma sarasvati phaṇipatigiripatilasadurasi bhavati tava vasatiḥ | kaustubha vibhāsi dhanyo nistulavibhavo bhavādṛśaḥ ko’nyaḥ || 735 ||
sarasvati sāgare | atra śrīnivāsakaustubhavarṇane prastute yatra kvā janma labdhvā’timātravideśe ślāghanīyatame’dhigatasāmrājyasaṃpado bhāgyaśālinaḥ puruṣasya vā mahāviduṣo vā vṛttāntaḥ pratīyata ita sārūpyanibandhaneyam ||
yathāvā
phaṇigiripatihṛdi kaustubhamaṇivarasaṃdarśanena nayanayugam | hṛṣyati hṛdayaṃ vikasati galati tamo milati mañjulacchāyā || 736 ||
atra prastutena kaustubhadarśanavṛttāntenāprastutodayamānabhānudarśanavṛttāntaḥ pratīyate | atra nayanayugaṃ hṛṣyati, hṛdayaṃ vikasatītyatra sarūpyāccakravākamithunaharṣakamalavikāsau pratīyete | galati tamaḥ, milati mañjulacchāyetyatra śleṣeṇāndhakāranirāsakāntilābhāviti pūrvodāharaṇādvailakṣaṇyam ||
atra kuvalayānandakṛtaḥ “prastutāprastutasādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇabalātsārūpyādvā yadaprastutavṛttāntasya pratyāyanaṃ tat prastute viśeṣye samāropārthaṃ, sarvathaiva prastutānanvayinaḥ kavisaṃrambhagocaratvāyogāt | tataśca samāsoktāvaprastutavyavahārasamāropaścārutohatuḥ | na tu rūpaka iva prastute aprastutarūpasamāropo’sti | mukhacandra ityatra mukhe candratvāropahetucandrapadasamabhivyāhāravat ‘raktaścumbati candramāḥ’ ityādisamāsoktyudāharaṇe candrādau jāratvādyāropahetostadvācakapadasamabhivyāhārasyābhāvāt ‘nirīkṣya vidyunnayanaiḥ’ ityādyekadeśavivartirūpakodāharaṇa iva prastute aprastutarūpasamāropagamakasyāpyabhāvāt | tatra hi vidyunnayanairityatra nirīkṣaṇānuguṇyāduttarapadārthapradhānarūpamayūravyaṃsakādisamāsavyavasthitādutttarapadārthabhūtanayanānvayānurodhātpayode’nuktamapi draṣṭṛpuruṣatvarūpaṇaṃ gamyamavagamyate | na ceha tathā nirīkṣaṇavat ‘tvayyāgate kimiti vepata eṣa sindhuḥ’ iti śloke setukṛttvādivaccāprastutāsādhāraṇavṛttānta upāttosti | nāpi śliṣṭasādhāraṇādiviśeṣaṇasamarpitayoḥ prastutāprastutavṛttāntayoraprastutavṛttāntasya vidyunnayanavatprādhānyamasti | yena tadanurodhāt ‘tvaṃ setumanthakṛt’ ityatreva prastute’nuktamapyaprastutarūpasamāropamabhyupagacchema | tasmādviśeṣaṇasamarpitāprastutavyavahārasamāropamātramiha cāratāhetuḥ | yadyapi prastutāprastutavṛttāntayoriha śliṣṭasādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇasamarpitayorbhinnapadopāttaviśeṣaṇayoriva viśeṣyeṇaiva sākṣādanvayādasti samaprādhānyam | tathā’pyaprastutavṛttāntānvayānurodhānna prastute aprastutarūpasamāropo’ṅgīkāryaḥ | tathāhi yathā prastutaviśeṣye nastyaprastutavṛttāntasyānvayayogyatā, tathaivāprastute’pi nāsti prastutavṛttāntasyānvayayegyatā | evaṃ ca samapradhānayoḥ prastutāprastutavṛttāntayoranyatarasyārope’vaśyamabhyupagantavye śruta eva prastute aprastutasyāropaścārutāheturiti yuktam” ityāhuḥ ||
rasagaṅgādharakārastu samasokterevaṃ lakṣaṇādikamabhāṇīt | tathāhi ‘yatra prastutadharmiko vyavahārassādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇamātropa sthāpitāprastutadharmikavyavahārābhedena bhāsate sā samāsoktiḥ | sādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇamātraśrutyupasthāpitāprastutadharmikavyavahārābhinnatvena bhāsamānaprakṛtadharmikavyavahāratvamiti caikoktiḥ | śabdaśaktimūladhvanivāraṇāya mātreti, viśeṣyasyāpi śliṣṭatayā prakṛtetaradharmyupasthāpanaddhārā tādṛśadharmikavyavahāropasthāpakatvāt |
evamapi
ābadhnāsyalakānnirasyasi tamāṃ colaṃ rasākāṃkṣayālaṅktāyā vaśatāṃ tanoṣi kuruṣe jaṅghālalāṭakṣatam |
pratyaṅgaṃ parimardanirdayamaho cetassamālambase vāmānāṃ viṣaye nṛpendra bhavataḥ prāgalbhyamatyadbhutam ||
ityatra prakṛtadharmikaprakṛtāprakṛtavyavahāraviṣayake śleṣe’tivyāptervāraṇāya prastutāprastutatve dharmiviśeṣeṇatayopātte | vyavahāraviśeṣaṇatvena tayorupādāne tu sādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇamātraśrutyupasthāpitāprakṛtaśrṛṅgāravṛttantābhinnatvena sthita evātra prakṛto vīravṛttanta iti syādevātiprasaṅgaḥ | na cātra rājño varṇanasya prastutatvāttadgatayordvayorapi vṛttāntayoḥ prastutatvamiti kathamatiprasaṅga iti vācyaṃ, na syādatiprasaṅgaḥ yadi varṇanamātraṃ prastutaṃ syāt , tatsaṃgrāmādau dhīratāmātravarṇanaprastāve tu syādevātiprasaṅgaḥ ||
maline’pi rāgapūrṇāṃ vikasitavadanāmanalpajalpe’pi |
tvayi capale’pica sarasāṃ bhramara kathaṃ vā sarojinīṃ tyajasi ||
ityādyaprastutapraśaṃsāyāmaprakṛtavyavahārassākṣādupāttatvādviśeṣyeṇāpyupasthāpita eveti na tatrātivyāptiḥ | yadi tu jalakrīḍādau bhramaravṛttānta eva prastutastadā bhavatyeveyaṃ samāsoktiḥ ||
tatra tāvat
vibodhayan karasparśaiḥ padminīṃ mudritānanām |
paripūrṇo’nurāgeṇa prātarjayati bhāskaraḥ ||
ityatra kiraṇasparśakaraṇakamukulitapadminīkarmakavikāsānukūlavyāpāravadabhinno bhāskaro jayatīti vākyārthaśśaktyaiva tāvatpratīyate | hastasparśakaraṇakanāyikāviśeṣakarmakānunayanānukūlavyāpāravadabhinna ityādiścāparo’rtha ubhayatrānuṣaktatayā tayaiva śaktyā śaktyantareṇa vyaktyā vā sarvathaiva pratīyata ityatra sahṛdaya eva pramāṇam | evaṃ ca dvāvimau vākyārthau savyetaragoviṣāṇavadatyantāsaṃspṛṣṭau yadi syātāṃ tadā bhagavato bhāskarasya kāmukatvaṃ kamalinyā nāyikātvaṃ ca sakalapratītisiddhaṃ viruddhaṃ syāt | dvipradhānatvena vākyabhedaścāpadyeta | yadi cāparo’rthaḥ prakṛtakartaryāropyate tadā kamalinīvikāsakartā nāyikānunayakartā ca sūrya ityekatra dvayamiti viṣayatāśālī bodhassyāt | na tu pūrvoktānupapattiparihāraḥ | yadi ca śleṣamūlābhedādhyavasānena kamalinyādīnāṃ nāyikātvādipratyaya upapādyate tadā’pyaśliṣṭapadopasthito bhagavānnāyakatvānāghrāta eva | padminīśabdasthāne nalinīśabdopādāne sāSpi nāyikātvena kathaṃ nāma pratītiviṣayatvamiyāt | tasmādviśeṣaṇasāmyamahimnā pratīti’prakṛtavākyārthassvānuguṇaṃ nāyikādimarthamākṣipya tena paripūrṇaviśiṣṭaśarīraḥ prakṛtavākyārthe svāvayavatādātmyāpannatadavayavo’bhedenāvatiṣṭhate | sa ca pariṇāma iva prakṛtātmanaiva kāryopayogī | svātmanā ca rasādyupayogī | atra cāprakṛtārthasya pṛthakchabdānupādānādrūpakādvākyārthasaṃbandhino vailakṣaṇyam | padārtharūpakāttu sphuṭameva | ākṣiptārthaghaṭitatvācca vākyārthaśleṣāt | evaṃ cātra śaktyākṣepābhyāṃ sarvārthanirvāha iti bhāmahodbhaṭaprabhṛtīnāṃ cirantanānāmāśayaḥ | ‘niśāmukhaṃ cumbati candra eṣaḥ’ ityādau niśācandraśabdayoraśliṣṭatvānmukhacumbanamātrasya ca putrādisādhāraṇyena kathaṃ tāvanniyatanāyakākṣepakatvam, kathaṃ vā ākṣiptasyāpi nāyakāderniśācandrayorevābhedenānvayaḥ, na bhedena cumbanādau | tathātveca tayornāyakatātāṭasthye rasānudbodhāpatteḥ | tasmāt ‘niśāmukhaṃ cumbati candrikaiṣā, aharmukhaṃ cumbati caṇḍabhānuḥ’ ityādāvapratīyamānaṃ nāyakatvaṃ prakṛte ṭāpprathamābhyāṃ pratipāditena prakṛtyarthagatena strītvena puṃstvena ca svādhikaraṇa evābhivyajyate | evaṃ ca niśāśaśinornāyakatvasiddhiśśliṣṭaviśeṣaṇaiḥ | vyañjanavyāpāreṇaivāprakṛtārthabodhanam, śakteḥ prakaraṇādinā niyantraṇāt | taditthaṃ vyañjanamāhātmyādevāprakṛtavākyārthatādātmyena prakṛtavākyārtho’vatiṣṭhate | guṇībhūtavyaṅgyabhedaścāyamiti tu ramaṇīyaḥ panthāḥ” iti | anyatsarvaṃ tatraiva draṣṭavyaṃ vistarābhilāṣibhiri tyalamatibhūmisvairavisarpaṇena ||
athāsyāḥ kevalabhedāḥ pratipādyante | viśeṣaṇasāmyaṃ śleṣeṇa bhavati śuddhasādhāraṇyena vā | tadapi dharmāntarapuraskāreṇa kāryapuraskāreṇa veti pratyekaṃ dvividham | tatra śliṣṭadharmāntarapuraskāreṇa viśeṣaṇasāmyaṃ yathā
vilikhitavicitrapatrā succhāyā bhāsvadatanutāpaharā | uragagiritaṭavanī paricaraṇācchrīvallabhasya sollāsā || 737 ||
vilikhitāni viśeṣeṇa racitāni, vibhiḥ pakṣibhiḥ likhitāni cañcupuṭairdalitāni ca | citrāṇi makarikādyākāravattathā nānārūpāṇi patrāṇi patrākārarekhānyasanāni | pakṣe nānāvarṇāni palāśāni yasyāssā tathoktā | succhāyā śobhanānātapāmanoharakāntiśca | bhāsvataḥ bhānoḥ atanuḥ akṛśaḥ yastāpaḥ tasya harā | anyatra bhāsvān udīrṇaḥ atanormadanasya tāpassaṃjvaraḥ tasya harā | śrīvallabhasya paricaraṇāt paritassaṃcārāt śuśrūṣaṇācca sollāsā saprakāśā saharṣā ca | atra dakṣiṇanāyakalabdhopacārottamamahilāvṛttāntābhedena sthitaḥ prakṛtavṛttāntaḥ succhāyatvasollāsatvādidharmapuraskāreṇa śleṣeṇa viśeṣaṇasāmyam| evaṃ ‘sphuṭakucamukha’ iti prāgudāhṛte padye’pi draṣṭavyam ||
śliṣṭakāryapuraskāreṇa viśeṣaṇasāmyaṃ yathā
pūrvaṃ nipātya vigrahakāle śaure samānamadhyamavīrān| paścādanipātyaikaṃ padamapi loke suśabdatāṃ prakaṭayase || 738 || he śaure ! vigrahakāle āyodhanasamaye ekaṃ padamapi paścādanipātya ekacaraṇāpasarpaṇamapyakṛtvetyarthaḥ | samānāḥ tulyāḥ uttamā iti yāvat | madhyamāḥ svāpekṣayā’pakṛṣṭāḥ ye vīrāśśūrāḥ tān | yadvā asamāniti chedaḥ | asamān śauryavīryādibhirasadṛśān | ata eva amadhyamān utkṛṣṭān vīrān pūrvaṃ ādāveva na tu vilambena nipātya pātayitvā nihatyetyarthaḥ | anenāsya paranirāsa īṣatkara iti dyotitam | śobhanaḥ śabdaḥ yaśaḥ yasya tasya bhāvaṃ suśabdatāṃ yaśasvitāmityarthaḥ | prakaṭayase | ‘śabdo’kṣarayaśogītavyomavākyadhvaniṣvapi’ iti ratnamālā | pakṣe vigrahakāle samāsārthaṃ vigrahapradarśanāvasare samānamadhyamavīrān uktavidhān śabdān pūrvaṃ nipātya teṣāṃ śabdānāṃ ‘pūrvāparaprathamacaramajaghanyasamānamadhyamadhyamavīrāśca’ iti sūtreṇa pūrvanipātasya niyamitatvāt pūrvameva prayujyetyarthaḥ | ekaṃ padamapi ekamityākārakaṃ śabdamapi | paścādanipātya pūrvameva prayujyetyarthaḥ | tasya ‘pūrvakālaika’ iti sūtreṇa pūrvanipātaniyamāt | suśabdatāṃ śabdaśāstraprāvīṇyaṃ prakaṭayase prathayase ityarthaḥ | atra vaiyākaraṇavṛttāntābhedena sthitaḥ prastutavṛttāntaḥ| nipātyetyādiśliṣṭakāryapuraskāreṇa viśeṣaṇasāmyam ||
evaṃ kāryātiriktadharmapratipādakaśliṣṭaviśeṣaṇasāmyena kāryarūpadharmapratipādakatatsāmyena ca samāsoktidvaidhī darśitā ||
śuddhasādhāraṇyena dharmāntarapuraskāreṇa yathā
skhalitamimaṃ saṃsāre lalitatamā dṛktavātra kaṃsāre | niravadhivātsalyā māṃ sarabhasamuddhartumutsukā’’patati || 739 || utsukā āpatatīti chedaḥ | āpatati āyātītyarthaḥ | atra bālakajananīvṛttāntābhedena sthitaḥ prakṛtavṛttāntaḥ | niravadhivātsalyetyādiśuddhasādhāraṇyena dharmāntarapuraskāraḥ ||
śuddhasādhāraṇyena kāryapuraskāreṇa yathā
stotuṃ trapāmahe tvāṃ yasya tavocchṛṅkhalo vṛṣādrimaṇe | viṣphāro nanu samitāvambaramādhūya daśa diśo bhuṅkte || 740 ||
samitau sabhāyām | ambaraṃ vasanam | tvadviṣphārasya nirllajatayā sabhāyāmevāmbaravyādhūnanapūrvakaṃ daśadigupabhogakāritvāttvāṃ stotuṃ vayameva lajjāmaha iti bhāvaḥ | vastusthitistu yudhi tāvakadhanurviṣphārassakalagaganadaśadigvyāpī īdṛśaṃ mahāvikrāntacūḍāmaṇiṃ tvāṃ stotuṃ mitaṃpacavacaso vayaṃ lajjāmaha iti | atra prastutabhagavaddhanurjyānirghoṣavṛttānto’prastutanirlajjakāmukavṛttāntābhedenāvatiṣṭhate | bhuṅkte iti kāryapuraskāreṇa viśeṣaṇasāmyam | bhogaśca prakṛte aupacārikaḥ | evaṃ prāgudāhṛte ‘sāmodabāṣpagadgada’ iti padye’pi draṣṭavyam ||
kāryadharmāntarayossaṃkareṇa sādhāraṇyaṃ yathā
śubhavibhavasaṃprasāraṇamākalayanpūrvarūpamapi bhagavan| racayasi maghoni guṇamapi subhe’tra dṛḍhapāṇinihitaśāstrastvam || 741 ||
he bhagavan ! dṛḍhaṃ pāṇau nihitaṃ śāstraṃ sudarśanādiśastrasamudayo yasya sa tathoktaḥ | āśritāniṣṭanivāraṇeṣṭaprāpaṇārthamāttapañcāyudha iti bhāvaḥ | tvaṃ maghoni indre śubhavibhavasaṃpra sāraṇaṃ śubhasya maṅgalakarasya vibhavasya trailokyasāmrājyasya saṃprasāraṇaṃ saṃpradānamiti yāvat | ākalayan san ata eva pūrvarūpaṃ durvāsaśśāpātpūrvamavasthitamūrjitatvādirūpaṃ ca | ākalayanniti saṃbadhyate | pravartayannityarthaḥ | ata eva subhe śobhanā bhā dīptiryasya tasmiṃstathokte ujjvalatejasītyarthaḥ | atra asminnindre guṇamapi nayavinayasamṛddhyādisuguṇamapi racayasi | paripūrṇajñānaśaktibalaiśvaryavīryatejasastavānugrahādevendrasyedṛśasamṛddhyādilābha iti bhāvaḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa bhagavanniti saṃbodhanam | pakṣe dṛḍhaṃ svabhyastaṃ pāṇinaye hitaṃ yacchāstraṃ vyākaraṇaṃ yasya sa tathoktastvam | asmin pakṣe śubhavibhavasaṃprasāraṇamityatra śubhavibheticchedaḥ | śubhā vibhā prabhā yasya tathoktaḥ | tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he śubhavibha ! he bhagavan! subhe suṣṭhubhe ‘yaci bham’ ityuktājādivibhaktiparakatvalakṣaṇāṃ bhasaṃjñāṃ prāpte maghoni maghavaśabde | vasaṃprasāraṇaṃ vasya vakārasya saṃprasāraṇaṃ ‘igyaṇassaṃprasāraṇam’ ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ yaṇsthānikamikaṃ ukāramityarthaḥ | ‘śvayuvamaghonāmataddhite’ iti bhasaṃjñakamaghavaśabdasthacakārasya saṃprasāraṇavidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | pūrvarūpamapi ‘saṃprasāraṇācca’ iti sūtreṇa saṃprasāraṇādaci pare pūrvarūpamekādeśaṃ ca ākalayan niṣpādayan atra asminmaghavaśabde guṇamapi ‘ādguṇaḥ’ ityanena guṇasaṃjñikamekādeśaṃ ca racayasi | tathāca maghavaśabde bhasaṃjñāyāṃ vakārasya saṃprasāraṇaṃ pūrvarūpaṃ guṇaṃ ca vidhāya maghonītyādirūpaṃ niṣpādayasīti nirgalito’rthaḥ | atra prastute mahendravaibhavādipradātṛbhagavadvṛttānte aprastutavaiyākaraṇavṛttānto’bhedena sthitaḥ | atra dṛḍhapāṇinihitaśāstratvena dharmeṇa śubhavibhavasaṃprasāraṇākalanādikaṃ kāryaṃ saṃkīrṇam || samāsoktau vyajyamānasyāprakṛtavyavahārasyopaskārakatvameva, na prādhānyam | tadupaskṛtavācyasyaiva prādhānyamityuktam | tatra yadi vyaṅgyasyaiva prādhānyaṃ syāt na vācyasya, tadā ‘stotuṃ trapāmahe tvāṃ’ iti prāgudāhṛte padye nindāvyājena stutau paryavasānaṃ na syāt | stutinindayoḥ prakṛtāprakṛtavyavahārāśrayatvāditi dhyeyam ||
alaṃkārasarvasvakāreṇa tu “aupamyagarbhatvenāpi viśeṣaṇasāmyaṃ saṃbhavati | yathā
dantaprabhāpuṣpacitā pāṇipallavaśobhinī |
keśapāśālibṛndena suveṣā hariṇekṣaṇā ||
atra hāriṇekṣaṇāmātravṛttessuveṣatvasya viśeṣaṇasya mahimnā dantaprabhāsadṛśāni puṣpāṇītyādiyojanāṃ vihāya dantaprabhāḥ puṣpāṇīvetyādyupamitasamāsāśrayeṇa kṛte yojane prakṛtārthasiddhau satyāṃ vṛttyantareṇa tyaktāyā eva yojanāyāḥ punarujjīvane puṣpapallavālibṛndairupameyaiḥ ākṣiptāyā latāyāḥ pratyayāttadvyavahārāropaḥ| evaṃ suveṣetyapahāya parīteti kṛte upamārūpakasādhakabādhakapramāṇābhāvāttadubhayasaṃśayarūpasaṃkarāśrayeṇa kṛte yojane paścātpūrvoktarītyā latāpratītessamāsoktireva | samāsabhedenārthabhede’pi śabdaikamādāya śliṣṭamūlāyāmiva viśeṣaṇasāmyaṃ bodhyam | ādāvante vā rūpakāśrayeṇa dantaprabhā eva puṣpāṇīti yojane kṛte tu hariṇekṣaṇāṃśe ākṣiptalatātādātmyakena ekadeśavivartirūpakeṇaivāprakṛtārthapratyayopapatternārthassamāsokteratra” ityabhihitam |
tanna vicārasahaṃ dantaprabhāḥ puṣpāṇīvetyupamāgarbhatvenāpyādau yojane kṛte hariṇekṣaṇāṃśe ākṣiptalatopamānikayā ekadeśavivartinyā upamayaiva gatārthatvātsamāsokterānarthakyādatrāprasakteḥ | na codbhaṭamate ekadeśavivartinorupamāsaṃkarayorasvīkārāttathoktamiti vācyam | anupadameva svayaṃ tatsvīkārāt ||
hālāhalasamo manyuranukampā sudhopamā |
kīrtiste candrasadṛśī bhaṭāstu makarodbhaṭāḥ ||
ityādau gatyantarābhāvāttenāpyekadeśavivartyupamāyā eva svīkṛtatvācca | avaśyaklṛptenopapattau bhedāntarakalpanānaucityāt | tasmādaupamyagarbhaviśeṣaṇotthāpitassamāsoktiprakāro na saṃgacchate | yatra śliṣṭaviśeṣaṇena śuddhasādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇena vā sahacaritamaupamyagarbhaviśeṣaṇaṃ tatra yadyapyasti samāsoktiḥ | tathā’pi nāsāvaupamyagarbhaviśeṣaṇotthāpitastṛtīyaḥ prabhedo bhavitumīṣṭe | svatantraviṣayatvābhāvāt | yathā
nirmalāmbararamyaśrīḥ kiṃciddarśitatārakā |
haṃsāvalīhārayutā śaradvijayate tarām ||
atra pūrvārdhagataśliṣṭaviśeṣaṇotthāpitaiva samāsoktiḥ uttarārdhagatenaupamyagarbhaviśeṣaṇena vidvadutthāpitā yuktistadanugāminā mūrkheṇevānumodyate | evaṃ ‘dattānandā samastānāṃ praphullotpalamālinī’ iti pūrvārdhe kṛte | śuddhasādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇotthāpitaiva ||
pariphullābjanayanā candrikācāruhāsinī |
haṃsāvalīhārayutā śaradvijayate tarām ||
ityatropamārūpakayossādhakasya bādhakasya cābhāvātsaṃkarālaṃkārasvīkartṛnaye tadubhayasaṃśayātmaka ekadeśavivartī saṃkarālaṃkāra eva | tadasvīkartṛnaye ca yadopamitasamāsasphūrtistadaikadeśavivartinyupamā | viśeṣaṇasamāsasphūrtau tathāvidhameva rūpakamiti prathamayojanayaivāprakṛtārthāvagateḥ dvitīyayojanāyāḥ pariphullābjānīva nayanānītyupamāgarbhāyā vaiyarthyādanutthānameva | yadā tu ‘śaradvarṣāsakhī babhau’ iti caturthacaraṇaṃ nirmīyate tadā tu śaranmātravṛttervarṣāsakhītvasyopādānādabjacandrikāhaṃsapradhānasyopamitasamāsasyāvaśyakatvātprathamaprādurbhūtayā nayanahasahārākṣiptakāminīrūpopamānikayā ata evaikadeśavivartinyā upamayaiva nirvāha iti veditamapi sahṛdayaprītaye punaniveditamiti hi rasagaṅgādhare sthitam | seyaṃ laukike vyavahāre laikikasya vyavahārasya śāstrīye śāstrīyasya laukike śāstrīyasya śāstrīye laukikasya vā samāropa iti caturdhā bhavati | tatrādyā prāgudāhṛtā’pi punarudāhriyate bālabuddhivaiśadyāya ||
yathā
pradarakṣobhādasraṃ pramuñcamānā tava dviṣāṃ senā | dūnā’bhūdbībhatsā mlānā ca raghūdvahātivicikitsā ||
he raghūdvaha ! tava dviṣāṃ senā pradareṇa vidāraṇena bāṇena bhaṅgena vā yaḥ kṣobhaḥ tasmāt | pakṣe pradareṇa śoṇitasrāviṇā strīrogaviśeṣeṇa yaḥ kṣobhaḥ tasmāt ‘pradarā bhaṅganārīrugbāṇāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | ‘pradaro rogabhede syādvidāre śarabhaṅgayoḥ’ iti medinī ca | asraṃ aśru keśaṃ vā jātyekavacanam | anyatra rudhiraṃ ‘asraḥ koṇe kace puṃsi klībamaśruṇi śoṇite’ iti medinī | pramuñcamānā visṛjantī | muñcatiḥ svaritet tataśśānac | ekatra vidāraṇabāṇabhaṅgānyatamajanitavaikalyena bāṣpāyamāṇā vikīrṇakeśāvetyarthaḥ| anyatra vyādhiviśeṣeṇa rudhirasrāviṇītyarthaḥ | dūnā paritaptā bībhatsā vikṛtā bībhatsarasālambanabhūtā ca ‘bībhatsaṃ vikṛtaṃ triṣvidaṃ dvayam’ ityamaraḥ | ativicikitsā atimātrasaṃśa yagrastā prāṇasaṃśayamāpannetyarthaḥ | pakṣe vigatā cikitsā rukpratikriyā yasyāssā tathoktā atimātraṃ vicikitsā ativicikitsā ativelarogapratīkāravidhuretyarthaḥ | mlānā kṣīṇaharṣā ca abhūt | atra prastute laukeke bhagavatparāhataripusenāvyavahāre laukikavyādhitavanitāvyavahārāropaḥ ||
śāstrīye vyavahāre śāstrīyavyavahārāropeṇa yathā
śrutavidhinā mantrā’’ptrā nānāmūlārthavādasaṃdhātrā | sa bhavānavedi niścitamacyuta viduṣā sanātano dharmaḥ || 743 ||
he acyuta! śrutavidhinā śrutaḥ niśamitaḥ vidhiḥ ‘draṣṭavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ’ ityāmnāto vidhiḥ yena tathoktena | ata eva mantrā mananaśīlena | mananaṃ nāma śrutasyārthasya pratiṣṭhāpanāya yuktibhirevameveti cintanam | nānamūlārthavādasaṃdhātrā nānā vakṣyamāṇarītyā bahuprakārāḥ mūlārthaḥ ‘kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ’ iti dhyeyatayoktajagatkāraṇarūpavastu mūlaṃ cāsāvarthaśceti vigrahaḥ tadviṣaye ye vādāḥ teṣāṃ saṃdhātrā śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā samāsaḥ ‘mūlamādye śiphābhayoḥ, artho’bhidheyaraivastuprayojanavivṛttiṣu’ iti cāmaraḥ | “sanmūlāssomyemāḥ, adomūlāḥ kriyāssarvāḥ, mūlamūleti khinne” ityādau mūlaśabdaḥ kāraṇavācitayā prayuktaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ ‘sadeva somyedamagra asīt, brahma vā idamekamevāgra āsīt, ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt, hiraṇyagarbhassamavartatāgre, na sanna cāsacchiva eva kevalaḥ’ ityādīnāṃ nānāvidhānāṃ karaṇavākyānāmekaviṣayatvāvaśyaṃbhāve ‘chāgo vā mantravarṇāt’ ityuktanyāyena sadbrahmātmahiraṇyagarbhaśivādisāmānyaśabdān ‘eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt’ iti śrute nārāyaṇarūpaviśeṣe paryavasāyayateti | āptrā prāptrā | anena ‘brahmavidāpnopti’ ityatroktaṃ paraprāptṛtvaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam | viduṣā ‘dve vidye veditavye parā caivāparā ca ‘ ||
tatprāptiheturjñānaṃ ca karma coktaṃ mahāmune |
āgamotthaṃ vivekācca dvidhā jñānaṃ tathocyate |
śabdabrahmāgamamayaṃ paraṃ brahma vivekajam ||
ityatroktāgamotthajñānavatetyarthaḥ, vivekādisādhanasaptakānugṛhītopā sanātmakajñānasyāvedīti pṛthagvakṣyamāṇatvāt | sanātanaḥ nityaḥ dharmaḥ siddhadharmarūpaḥ | alaukikaśreyassādhanaṃ hi dharmaḥ | sa ca siddhasādhyabhedena dvividhaḥ siddharūpe vastuni dharmaśabdaprayogo’pi mahābhārate dṛśyate | yathā
ye ca vedavido viprā ye cādhyātmavido janāḥ |
te vadanti mahātmānaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dharmaṃ sanātanam ||
iti siddhopāyabhūta ityarthaḥ | sa bhavān prāpyarūpastvam | niścitaṃ yuktyantarairacālyaṃ yathā syāttathā avedi upāsyata | āvṛttyadhikaraṇe vidyupāsyoraikārthyasya bhāṣitatvāt | upāsanaṃ nāma vijātīyapratyayānantaritaṃ aharaharabhyāsādheyātiśayaṃ prītirūpāpannaṃ darśanasamānākāraṃ dhyānam | anena ‘draṣṭavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ’ ityasāvartho’nusṛtaḥ ||
pakṣe śrutāḥ vidhayaḥ apūrvaniyamaparisaṃkhyābhedabhidurāḥ vidhayaḥ yena sa tathoktaḥ | tena īdṛśavidhisvarūpaparijñātrota yāvat | tatra yo vidhiratyantāprāptamarthaṃ prāpayati so’pūrvavidhiḥ| yathoktaṃ ‘vidhiratyantamaprāptau’ iti | yathā darśapaurṇamāsaprakaraṇe “vrīhīn prokṣati’ iti etadvidhyabhāve darśapaurṇamāsīyavrīhiṣu prokṣaṇaṃ kathamapi na prāpnoti | etadvidhisadbhāve tu tadīyavrīhiṣu prokṣaṇaṃ prāpnotyevetyatyantāprāptaprokṣaṇaprāpakatvādayamapūrvavidhiḥ | yo vidhiḥ pakṣe prāptamarthaṃ niyamayati sa niyamavidhiḥ| yathocyate ‘niyamaḥ pākṣike sati’ iti | yathā tatraiva ‘vrīhonavahanti’ iti | etadvidhyabhāve dārśapaurṇamāsikeṣu vrīhiṣūtpattivākyāvagatapuroḍāśopayogitaṇḍulaniṣpattyanukūlavaituṣyarūpakāryāyāvahananavatkadācinnakhavidalanamapi prāpnuyāditi | tasminpakṣe’vahananasya prāptyabhāvātkāryānyathānupapatteravahananasya pākṣikī prāptissyāt | sati cāsminvidhau avahananenaiva vaituṣyaṃ saṃpādanīyamiti niyame sati vidalanamarthānnivartata iti niyamavidhirayam | dvayossamuccitya prāptāvitaranivṛttiphalako vidhiḥ parisaṃkhyāvidhiḥ | yathocyate ‘tatra cānyatra ca prāptau parisaṃkhyeti gīyate’ iti | yathā cayanaprakaraṇe imāmagṛbhṇanraśanāmṛtasyetyaśvābhidhānīmādatte’ ityaśvaraśanāgrahaṇāṅgatvena mantravidhiḥ | etadvidhyabhāve hi raśanāgrahaṇaprakāśako mantro raśanādānaprakāśanasāmarthyarūpālliṅgādaśvaraśanādāna iva gardabharaśanādāne’pi niyamena prāpnuyāt | sati cāsminvidhau anena mantreṇāśvaraśanamevādadīta | na tu gardabharaśanāṃ, sā tu tūṣṇīmeva grāhyeti gardabharaśanāto mantranivṛttirbhavatīti dvayossamuccitya prāptāvitaranivṛttiphalakatvādayaṃ | parisaṃkhyāvidhiriti saṃkṣepaḥ | prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ mantrāptrā | mantrā ṇāmāptā prāptā tena adhigatamantreṇetyarthaḥ | pūrvavadeva samāsaḥ | prayogasamavetārthasmārakā mantrāḥ | teṣāṃ ca tādṛśārthasmārakatvenaivārthavattvam, na tu taduccāraṇamadṛṣṭārthaṃ, saṃbhavati dṛṣṭaphalakatve adṛṣṭaphalakalpanāyā anyāyyatvāt | naca dṛṣṭasyārthasmaraṇasya prakārāntareṇāpi saṃbhavānmantrāmnānaṃ vyarthamiti vācyam | mantraireva smartavyamiti niyamavidhyāśrayaṇāt | nānāmūlārthavādasaṃdhātrā nānāmūlānāṃ bahukāraṇakānāṃ arthavādānāṃ arthavādavākyānāṃ saṃdhātrā arthavādavākyāni vidhivākyaikavākyatayā saṃyojya pramāṇīkurvateti yāvat |arthavādo nāma prāśastyanindānyataraparaṃ vākyam | tasya ca lakṣaṇayā prayojanavadarthaparyavasānam | tathāhi arthavādavākyaṃ hi svārthapratipādane prayojanābhāvādvidheyaniṣedhyayoḥ prāśastyaninditatve lakṣaṇayā pratipādayati | svārthamātraparatve ānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | āmnāyasya ca kriyārthatvāt | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ ‘svādhyāyo’dhyetavyaḥ’ ityadhyayanavidhinā sakalavedādhyayanasya kartavyatāṃ pratipādayatā sarvasyāpi vedasya prayojanavadarthaparyavasānaṃ sūcayatopāttatvenānarthakyānupapatteḥ | sa ca dvividhaḥ vidhiśeṣo niṣedhaśeṣaśca | tatra ‘vāyavyaṃ śvetamālabheta bhūtikāmaḥ’ ityādividhiśeṣasya ‘vāyurvai kṣepiṣṭhā devatā’ isyādervidheyārthaprāśastyabodhakatayā’rthavattvam | ‘bārhiṣi rajataṃ na deyam’ ityādiniṣedhaśeṣasya ‘so’rodīdyadarodīttadrudrasya rudratvam’ ityāderniṣedhyasya ninditatvabodhakatayā’rthavattvam | naca prāśastyādibodhanasya niṣprayojanatvena nārthavādasyārthavattvamiti vācyam | ālasyādivaśādapravartamānasya puṃsaḥ pravṛttyādijanakatvena tadbodhasyopayogāt | sa punastredhā | taduktam
virodhe guṇavādassyādanuvādo’vadhārite |
bhūtārthavādastaddhānādarthavādastridhā mataḥ || iti ||
ayamarthaḥ pramāṇāntaravirodhe satyarthavādo guṇavādaḥ | yathā ‘ādityo yūpaḥ’ ityādi | yūpe ādityābhedasya pratyakṣabādhitatayā’’dityavadujjvalatvaguṇo’nena lakṣaṇayā pratipādyate | pramāṇāntarā vagatārthabodhako’nuvādaḥ | yathā ‘agnirhimasya bheṣajam’ iti | atra himavirodhitvasyāgnau pratyakṣāvagatatvāt | pramāṇāntaravirodhatatprāptirahitārthabodhako vādo bhūtārthavādaḥ | yathā ‘indro vṛtrāya vajramudayacchat’ ityādiḥ | ityalaṃ prasaktānuprasaktikācāpalena | viduṣā uktarītyā vidhimantrārthavādavettrā puruṣeṇa vipaścitā | sanātanaḥ ‘eṣa dharmassanātanaḥ’ ityuktarītyā śrutismṛtyādiparaṃparāgataḥ | dharmaḥ sādhyarūpo dharmaḥ niścitaṃ mīmāṃsānyāyairnissaṃdigdhaṃ yathā syāttathā sa bhavānavedi jñātaḥ | dharmo nāma vedapratipādyaḥ prayojanavadarthaḥ | sa ca yāgādireva | prayojane’tivyāptivāraṇāya prayojanavaditi | bhojanādāvativyāptivāraṇāya vedapratipādya iti | anarthaphalakatvādanarthabhūte śyenādāvativyāptivāraṇāyārtha iti | naca ‘codanālakṣaṇo’rtho dharmaḥ’ iti sautratallakṣaṇavirodhaḥ | codanāpadasya vidhirūpavedaikadeśaparatvāditi vācyam | tatrāpi codanāśabdasya vedamātraparatvāt | vedasya sarvasya dharmatātparyavattvena dharmapratipādakatvādityalaṃ vistareṇa | atra brahmamīmāṃsāśāstrīye prastute vyavahāre aprastutakarmamīmāṃsāśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
apprāpte pūrvaṃ tvayi bahavo’pi guṇāḥ paraṃ vidhīyante | anyatra tu na vidhātuṃ śakyo’neko guṇo bhagavan || 744 ||
he bhagavan ! idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaguṇapauṣkalyavattābhiprāyagarbham | pūrvaṃ sṛṣṭeḥ prāk | apaḥ prāptaḥ apprāptaḥ | tasmiṃstathokte ‘dvitīyāśrita’ ityādinā samāsaḥ | mahārṇavajale śayāna iti yāvat | ‘yamantassamudre kravayo’vayanti, mahārṇave śayāno’psu’ ityādipramāṇat | anena
āpo nārā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ|
tā yadasyāyanaṃ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇassmṛtaḥ ||
iti smṛtyuktanārāyaṇaśabdanirvacanaṃ darśitam | tena ‘eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīnna brahmā neśāno neme dyāvāpṛthivī’ iti śrutaṃ kāraṇatvamapyākṣiptaṃ bhavati | tvayi paraṃ tvayyeva | bahavo’saṃkhyātā api guṇāḥ kāraṇatvaupayikāḥ sarvajñatvasarvaśaktitvādayaḥ kalyāṇaguṇāḥ vidhīyante ‘yassarvajñassarvavit’ ityādiśrutibhiriti bhāvaḥ | anyatra tvadatirikte deve tu anekoguṇaḥ vidhātuṃ na śakyaḥ | eko’pi guṇo na śakyo vidhātuṃ kimutānekaḥ | tavevānyeṣāṃ daivatānāmekayā’pi śrutyā uktavidhakalyāṇakaguṇasyaikasyāpyanukteriti bhāvaḥ | etena kāraṇavākyaghaṭitānāṃ hiraṇyagarbhaśivādiśabdānāmaindrīnyāyena kayācidavayavaśaktyā rūḍhyā aparyavasānavṛttyā vā nārāyaṇaparatvameveti pūrvaślokokta evārthaḥ sthūṇānikhananyāyena sthirīkṛta ityavadheyam ||
anyatra
prāpte karmaṇi nāneko vidhātuṃ śakyate guṇaḥ |
aprāpte tu vidhīyante bahavo’pyekayatnataḥ ||
iti mīmāṃsakakārikāyāḥ pūrvottarārdhayorarthaḥ etatpadyottarapūrvārdhābhyāṃ vaiparītyena varṇitaḥ | tathāhi pūrvaṃ guṇavidhānātpūrvaṃ apprāpte śāstrāntareṇāvihite karmaṇi atra ‘anaci ca’ iti rephaghaṭitapakārasya vaikalpikaṃ dvitvam | bahavo’pi guṇāḥ apradhānabhūtā arthāḥ paraṃ ekayatnenaiveti yāvat| vidhīyante vihitā bhavanti | tathāca sūtraṃ ‘tadgṛṇāstu vidhīyeranna vibhāgādvidhānārthena cedanyena śiṣṭāḥ’ iti | anyatra prāpte karmaṇi tu aneko guṇo na vidhātuṃ śakyaḥ | ata eva ‘dadhi madhu ghṛtaṃ payo dhānā udakaṃ taṇḍulāstatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ prājāpatyam’ iti prakṛtya ‘citrayā yajeta paśukāmaḥ’ iti mānāntaraprāptakarmānuvādena citratvastrītvaguṇadvayavidhāne vākyabheda iti citrāpadasya nāmadheyatvameva ‘yadāgneyo’ṣṭākapālo bhavatyamāvāsyāyāṃ ca paurṇamāsyāṃ cācyuto bhavati’ ityatra mānāntarāprāpte karmaṇi ekadevatāṣṭākapālapuroḍāśāmāvāsyāpaurṇamāsyādyanekaguṇaviśiṣṭo dravyadevatāsaṃbandhenānumito yāgo vidhīyata iti viśiṣṭakarmavidhānānna vākyabheda iti vyavasthāpitamākare | evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyamiti saṃkṣepaḥ| atrāpi pūrvavadeva śāstrīye vyavahāre śāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
brahma jayatādapūrvaṃ karaṇādikamapi yadanvayādeva | prāpnoti śaktimattvaṃ prabhākarādyāśca yadanugā vibudhāḥ || 745 ||
apūrvaṃ ‘na cāsya kaścijjanitā’ ityuktarītyā kāraṇāntararahitaṃ kāryāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ pūrvam’ ityuktarītyā sakalajatkāraṇabhūtamityarthaḥ | brahma bhagavān jayatāt | yasya brahmaṇaḥ anvayāt preraṇādeveti yāvat | karaṇādikaṃ indriyādikamapi ādiśabdena prāṇādikaṃ gṛhyate | śaktimattvaṃ svasvaviṣayakāryakṣamatvaṃ prāpnoti
keneṣitaṃ patati preṣitaṃ manaḥ kena prāṇaḥ prathamaḥ praiti yuktaḥ|
keneṣitāṃ vācamimāṃ vadanti cakṣuśśrotraṃ ka u devo yunakti ||
iti parabrahmalasvarūpapaśne
śrotrasya śrotraṃ manaso mano yadvāco ha vācaṃ sa u prāṇasya prāṇaḥ |
cakṣuṣaścakṣuratimucya dhīrāḥ pretyāsmāllokādamṛtā bhavanti ||
ityuttararūpā śrutiratrānusaṃhitā || prabhākarādyāḥ sūryapramukhāḥ vibudhāḥ devāḥ yadanugāḥ yacchāsanānativartina iti yāvat | ‘etasya nā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi sūryācandramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ, bhīṣāsmādvātaḥ pavate bhīṣodeti sūryaḥ’ ityādiśrutibhyaḥ||
pakṣe apūrvaṃ mīmāṃsakābhyupagataṃ kiṃcidvastu | vihitaniṣiddhakarmaṇāṃ tattadvākyaistattatphalasādhanatve’vagate āśuvināśināṃ karmaṇāṃ kālāntarabhāviphalasādhanatvasyopapattyarthamantarā puṇyapāparūpamadṛṣṭāparaparyāyamapūrvaṃ nāma kiṃcittairhi kalpitam | taccotpattyapūrvaṃ phalāpūrvamityādyanekavidham | karaṇādikaṃ ‘sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam’ ityādyṛktaṃ kārakavāci padajātamapi | yena apūrveṇa anvayāt anvitatvādeva śaktimattvaṃ mukhyārthavattvaṃ prāpnoti | te hi padānāmanvitābhidhāyitvena kārakapadānāmapūrvakāryānvitābhidhāyināṃ tadanvita eva mukhyārthaḥ, tadanvayaparityāge lakṣaṇaiveti vadanti | prabhākarādyāḥ prabhākaro nāma tāntrikāgraṇīḥ sa evādyo yeṣāṃ te vibudhāḥ vipaścitaḥ yadanugāḥ yat apūrvaṃ anugacchantīti tathoktāḥ apūrvapratipādanaparā ityarthaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva vyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
vividhapadanāmarūpavyākaraṇakṛtā tvayaiva nanu bhagavan | mṛtyuṃjayasya sutarāmumāgamaśrīrvyadhīyatārdhāṅge || 746 || he bhagavan ! vividhānāṃ padānāṃ vastūnāṃ nāmarūpayoḥ devādināmadheyavicitrasṛṣṭyoḥ vyākaraṇaṃ vibhāgaḥ tatkarotīti tathoktaḥ tena | ‘anena jīvenātmanā’nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi’ iti śruteḥ | tvayaiva mṛtyuṃjayasya pinākinaḥ ardhāṅge ardhaśarīre sutarāṃ atisuṣṭha umāgamaśrīḥ umāyāḥ gauryāḥ āgamaśrīḥ saṃbandhasaṃpat | vyadhīyata akriyata | vividhaprapañcanāmarūpavyākartāraṃ tvāmantareṇa purārāterardhāṅganāṅgatvaṃ ko nu nāmānyo vidadhīteti bhāvaḥ ||
anyatra vividhānāṃ padānāṃ suptiṅṅantātmakānāṃ nāmnāṃ kṛdantādiprātipadikānāmapi yāni rūpāṇi teṣāṃ vyākaraṇaṃ prakṛtipratyayādyanvākhyānaṃ karotīti tathoktena | mṛtyuṃjayasya mṛtyuṃjayaśabdasya ardhāṅge tasya śabdasya varṇacatuṣṭayātkatayā varṇadvayasyārdhāṅgatvaṃ, tatra ādyavarṇadvayacaramabhāge iti yāvat | sutarā ativelaramaṇīyā uccāraṇe komalatāsaṃpādanāditi bhāvaḥ | mumāgamasya mumityākārakāgamasya śrīḥ ‘saṃjñāyāṃ bhṛtṛvṛji’ ityādinā khaci ‘arudviṣadajantasya mum’ ityanena mumāgama ityarthaḥ| vyadhīyata vihitā | atra prastute vedāntaśāstrīyavyavahāre aprastutaśabdaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
sādhukriyāpradhānakamākhyātaṃ yena viśvamanuśiṣṭam | nāmāni tasya sattvapradhānatāṃ dadhati tava vibhānti vibho || 747 ||
he vibho! yena tvayā ‘sattvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ’ iti sattvapravartakatayā śrutena bhavatā | viśvaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat sādhukriyā praśastakarma pradhānaṃ anuṣṭheyatayā mukhyaṃ yasya tat sādhukriyāpradhānakaṃ sat anuśiṣṭaṃ lakṣitaṃ ujjīvitamiti yāvat | yadvā sādhukriyāpradhānakaṃ yathā tathā anuśiṣṭamiti yojyam | ākhyātaṃ uktarītyā pratipāditaṃ ‘eṣa hyeva sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamebhyo lokebhya unninīṣati’ ityādiśrutibhiriti bhāvaḥ | ‘viśvamevedaṃ puruṣastadviśvamupajīvati | patiṃ viśvasya’ ityādiśrutayo’pyatrānusandheyāḥ | tasya uktarītyā viśvānuśāsituḥ tava nāmāni abhidhānāni sattvapradhānatāṃ sattvaguṇaprādhānyaṃ sattvapravṛttinimittakatāmiti yāvat | sattvavānsātvikaḥ’ iti nāmasahasrapāṭhāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā sato bhāvassatvaṃ tatprādhānyaṃ dadhātadadhānāni ‘sadasatkṣaramakṣaram’ iti tatraiva pāṭhāt | ‘oṃ tatsaditi nirdeśo brahmaṇāstrividhassmṛtaḥ’ ityukteśceti bhāvaḥ | dadhatītyatra ‘vā napuṃsakasya’ iti numo vaikalpikatvāttadabhāvaḥ | vibhānti | athavoktarītyā sakalabhuvanānuśāsitustava divyanāmāni kartṝṇi kīrtayatāṃ janānāṃ sāttvikatāṃ puṣṇanti prakāśanta ityarthaḥ |
pakṣe yena yāskeneti pratīyate | viśvaṃ sarvaṃ ākhyātaṃ tiṅ kriyāpradhānakaṃ sādhu yathā syāttathā anuśiṣṭaṃ devadattastaṇḍulaṃ pacatītyādau śābdabodhe devadattakartṛkastaṇḍulakarmakaḥ pāka ityākhyātasya kriyāprādhānyaṃ ‘bhāvapradhānamākhyātam’ iti tenānuśiṣṭamiti bhāvaḥ | bhāvaḥ kriyā | āhuśca
phalavyāpārayordhāturāśraye tu tiṅassmṛtāḥ |
phale pradhānaṃ vyāpārāstiṅarthastu viśeṣaṇam ||
iti | tasya yāskasyeti pratīyate | nāmāni kṛdantādiprātipadikāni satvapradhānāni dravyapradhānāni santi vibhānti | pācaka ityādau nāmni bodhe pākāśraya ityāderdravyaprādhānyasya ‘sattvapradhānāni nāmāni’ iti tenānuśiṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi prastute vedāntaśāstrīyavyavahāre aprastutaśabdaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
api pāpakāriṇamaho subhajantaṃ sādhukārivajjānan | asvapadavigrahamatha svanibhaṃ ca tanoṣi nātha sumanāstam || 748 ||
he nātha! tvaṃ pāpakāriṇamapi pāpaṃkarotīti pāpakārī tathoktamapi ‘supyajātau’ ite tacchīlye ṇiniḥ | subhajantaṃ ātmānaṃ samyagananyabhāktvena bhajamānaṃ janaṃ sādhu karotīti sadhukārī sādhukarmakṛt | pūrvavadeva ṇiniḥ | tena tulyaṃ tadvat jānan sādhukāripāpakāripadābhyāṃ “sādhukārī sādhurbhavati pāpakārī pāpo bhavati” iti śrutiḥ pratyabhijñāpyate | ādau duṣkṛtakāriṇamapyātmānaṃ samyagbhajamānaṃ pumāṃsaṃ sādhukārivajjānan |
api cetsudurācāro bhajate māmananyabhāk |
sādhureva sa mantavyassamyagvyavasito hi saḥ ||
iti svenaiva gītatvāditi bhāvaḥ | taṃ pumāṃsaṃ asvapadavigrahaṃ svapadābhyāṃ svacaraṇābhyāṃ vigrahaḥ kalahaḥ vivāda iti yāvat sa na vidyate yasya taṃ tathoktaṃ tanuṣe |
yamo vaivasvato rājā yastavaiṣa hṛdi sthitaḥ |
tena cedavivādaste mā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūngamaḥ ||
ityuktarītyā sarvāntaryāmiṇā sarvasvāminā svenāvivadamānaṃ vidhatsa ityarthaḥ | atra ‘tvaṃ me’haṃ me’ ityuktarītyā bhagavatā saha pratyagātmano vivādastatpadayoraupacārikastadgauravapradarśanāyoktaḥ, caraṇānugraha ityādivat | udāhṛte’sminvacane yamavaivasvatarājaśabdāḥ bhagavatparāḥ | avivādaśabdastu tatpadayorātmarakṣābharasamarpaṇapara iti dhyeyam | yathaitadvivṛtaṃ varadagurubhiḥ
dakṣiṇāśāpateratra na mukhyā hṛdayasthitiḥ |
antaḥpraviṣṭaśśāstā yo janānāṃ yamayatyapi ||
atmānamantarastasya mṛtyumṛtyorhṛdi sthitaḥ |
tena sarvādhirājena vivasvadbimbavartinā |
avivādastu tasyaiva padayorātmano’rpaṇam |
iti | svacaraṇāravindasamarpitātmarakṣābharaṃ vidadhāsīti bhāvaḥ | atha evamātmarakṣābharārpaṇānantaraṃ ārtyādhikye tatkṣaṇameva | dṛptatve taccharīrāvasāne iti bhāvaḥ | taṃ prapannaṃ svanibhaṃ svena tulyaṃ ca tanoṣi ‘nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti’ ityādyuktarītyā paramasāmyāpannaṃ vidadhāsītyarthaḥ | ukte’rthe hetumabhiprayannāha sumanā iti | dayamānamahodāramānasa ityarthaḥ ||
pakṣe sumanāḥ vidvānityarthaḥ | pāpakāriṇaṃ subhajantaṃ ca śabdaṃ sādhukārivat sādhukāriśabdatulyaṃ jānan asvapadavigrahaṃ svaghaṭakayāvatpadavigrahavirahiṇaṃ tanoṣi | pāpakārisādhukāriśabdayoḥ ‘upapadamatiṅ’ ityanena subhajacchabdasya ‘kugatiprādayaḥ’ ityanena ca sūtreṇa samāsavidhānāt | tayośca ‘nityaṃ krīḍā’ ityato nityamityanuvṛttyā tadvihitasamāsasya nityatvāt | ata eva hyasvapadavigrahaḥ ‘avigraho nityasamāsaḥ asvapadavigraho vā’ ityukteḥ | vigraho nāma samasyamānānāṃ padānāṃ vyastatayā pradarśanaṃ, ‘vibhajya gṛhyate yassa vigrahaḥ parikīrtitaḥ’ ityukteḥ, ‘vigrahassamare dehe vistare’pi’ iti ratnamālā | atha svanibhaṃ svanibhaśabdaṃ ca asvapadavigrahaṃ tanuṣe iti yojanā | tasyāpi nityasamāsatvāditi bhāvaḥ ‘syuruttarapade tvamī’| ‘nibhasaṃkāśanīkāśapratīkāśopamādayaḥ’ ityuttarapadabhūtasyaiva nibhaśabdasya tulyārthakatvānuśāsanāt | uttarapadaśabdaśca samāsacaramāvayave rūḍhaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva vyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
śāstraprasiddhisubhage nirantaraṃ bhātyato guṇe parataḥ | apadāntasthitibhājastasyāsya budhasya bhavati pararūpam || 749 ||
śāstre aupaniṣadadaharavidyādau prasiddhiḥ khyātiḥ tayā subhage ramaṇīye guṇe ‘apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuḥ, vijñānaghana eva’ ityādiśrute guṇe | jātyabhiprāyakamekavacanam | parataḥ parasmāt ataḥ akāravācyānnārāyaṇāt ‘a iti bhagavato nārāyaṇasya prathamābhidhānam’ ityādyukteḥ | parabrahmaṇassakāśāt tadanugrahāditi yāvat | nirantaraṃ niśśeṣāvidyānivṛttyā nirargalaṃ yathā syāttathā bhāti sati | yadvā ataḥparata iti ṣaṣṭhyarthe | sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | parataḥ ataḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ guṇe iti yojanā | apahatapāpmatvādau brāhme guṇe ityarthaḥ | nirantaraṃ bhāti nityaṃ prakāśamāne satītyarthaḥ | apadāntasthitibhājaḥ asya akāravācyasya nārāyaṇasya padaṃ ‘tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam’ ityāmnātaṃ sthānaṃ tasmin tasya vā antaḥ madhye rephāntamadhikaraṇaśaktipradhānamavyayam | sthitibhājaḥ tasyāsya parabrahmaprasādāvirbhūtabrāhmaguṇasyāsya budhasya brahmavidaḥ pararūpaṃ parasya brahmaṇo rūpaṃ ‘nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti’ iti śrutaṃ paramasāmyamiti yāvat | bhavati niṣpadyate | atra phalādhyāyasthabrāhmādhikaraṇārtho’nusandheyaḥ | tatra hi ‘svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate’ ityatra abhiniṣpādyatathoktaṃ kimapahatapāpmatvādikameva uta jñānamātrameva āhosvidubhayamapīti viśaye muktamadhikṛtyāpahatapāpmatvādyupanyāsāttena rūpeṇaivāvirbhavatīti ‘brāhmeṇa jaiminirupanyāsādibhyaḥ’ iti sūtreṇa jaiminimatamanūdya ‘vijñānaghana eva’ ityādiṣu jñānamātramevāsya svarūpamityavagamāt, apahatapāpmādiśabdānāṃ śokādyabhāvaparatvācca jñānamātrasvarūpeṇāvirbhavatīti ‘cititanmātreṇa tadātmakatvādityauḍulomiḥ’ ityauḍulomimatamupanyasya pakṣadvaye’pyanyataraśrutibādhāt “vijñānaghana eva’ ityavadhāraṇasya ekadeśajaḍatvaśaṅkāvyudāsaparatvena guṇāntarapratikṣepakatvābhāvādubhayarūpeṇāvirbhavati” iti ‘evamapyunyāsātpūrvabhāvādavirodhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ’ iti sūtreṇa matadvayamapi pratikṣiptam ||
pakṣe ataḥ hrasvākarāt parataḥ paratra vidyamāne śāstraprasiddhyā śabdavidyāprasiddhyā subhage guṇe guṇasaṃjñake akārādau varṇe nirantaraṃ bhāti saṃhitāyāṃ avyavadhānena bhāsamāne sati padaṃ suptiṅantātmakaṃ tasya antaḥ caramabhāgaḥ padāntaḥ sa na bhavatītyapadantaḥ tasmin padāntabhinne sthitibhājaḥ tasya guṇāvadhitvena pūrvamuktasya asya hrasvākārasya pararūpaṃ parasya guṇasaṃjñakavarṇasya rūpaṃ tadrūpa ekādeśa ityarthaḥ | bhavatīti ‘ato guṇe’ iti sautroktaprakriyayā’rtho’nusandheyaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadevāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
acidiha yo’naḍvāniva sanmārgasradapi tava dṛśo viṣayaḥ | taṃ svapadānte damitaṃ vidvāṃsamaghadhvasaṃ ca vidadhāsi || 750 ||
yaḥ pumān anaḍvān vṛṣabha iva acit jñānahīnaḥ ‘citsaṃvitpratipadjñapticetanāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | ‘jñānena hīnaḥ paśubhissamānaḥ’ ityukte | sanmārgātsraṃsata iti sanmārgasradapi tava dṛśaḥ kaṭākṣasya viṣayaḥ yādṛcchikādisukṛtavaśāditi bhāvaḥ | bhavediti śeṣaḥ | taṃ pumāṃsaṃ damitaṃ dāntaṃ ‘damu upaśame’ ṇijantādasmāt ktaḥ ‘vā dānta’ iti nipātanasya vaikalpikatayā pakṣe iṭ | ‘dāntastu damite’ ityamaraḥ | ‘śānto dāntaḥ’ ityuktarītyā sādhanasaṃpannamityarthaḥ | atha vidvāṃsaṃ brahmavidaṃ ata eva aghe uttarapūrve puṇyapāparūpe dhvaṃsayata ityaghadhvat tathoktaṃ aghadhvasaṃ ca ‘tadadhigama uttarapūrvāghayoraśleṣavināśau tadvyapadeśāt’ ityuktaprakriyayā aśliṣṭavinaṣṭottarapūrvāghaṃ santamiti bhāvaḥ | dhvaṃserṇijantāt kvipi ‘ṇeraniṭi’ iti ṇilopaḥ | vidvāṃsamaghadhvasamityābhyāṃ ‘tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya’ ityasyāśśruterartha uktaḥ | svapadānte paramapadamadhye svacaraṇasannidhau vā vidadhāsi sthāpayasītyarthaḥ ||
pakṣe yaḥ acit acidityākārakaśśabdaḥ ‘citī saṃjñāne’ saṃpadāditvātkvip | tānto’yaṃ cicchabdaḥ | tato nañsamāsaḥ | yaḥ anaḍvānava anaḍucchabda iva vidyamānaḥ sanmārgasracchabdaśca tava dṛśo jñānasya viṣayo gocaro bhavati taṃ acicchabdaṃ anaḍucchabdatulyaṃ sanmārgasracchabdaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vidvacchabdaṃ aghadhvasaṃ aghadhvacchabdaṃ ca svapadānte svasvarūpaṃ yatpadaṃ ‘svādiṣvasarvanāmasthāne’ iti vihitapadasaṃjñāśāli prātipadikaṃ tasya ante antimavarṇe takārahakārasakārarūpe daṃ dakāraṃ itaṃ prāptaṃ takārādisthānikadakāravantaṃ vidadhāsi acicchabdasya takārāntasyapadānte ‘jhalāṃ jaśo’nte’ iti jaśtvena dakāravidhānāt anaḍudādiśabdānāṃ vasusraṃsudhvaṃsvanaḍuhāṃ daḥ’ iti sūtreṇa padānte datvavidhānācceti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva śāstrīye vyavahāre śāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
nanu sarvanāmatāṃ harirayate svayamantaro bahiryogāt | pūrvaḥ paraśca vidito’saṃjñāyāṃ dhīmatāṃ vyavasthāyām || 751 ||
ayaṃ hariḥ svayaṃ antaraḥ ‘yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaraḥ’ ityādyuktarītyā antaryāmitayā’ntarvyāpanaśīlaḥ bahiryogāt bahirvyāpteḥ sarvanāmatāṃ sarvanāmadheyatāṃ ‘antarbahiśva tatsarvam’ ityuktarītyā sarvavyāpitayā sarvaśabdavācyatāmityarthaḥ | ayate prāpnoti ‘antarbahiśca nārāyaṇaḥ, nārāyaṇa evedam̐ sarvam, puruṣa evedam̐ sarvam, yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhavyam, sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma, sarvata eva sarvaḥ’ ityādīni pramāṇānyatra smartavyāni | ata eva sarvaśśarvaḥ’ iti tannāmasu paṭhyate | kiṃca asaṃjñāyāṃ saṃjñā nāma saṃketaḥ saṃketamātreṇānirvṛttāyāṃ kiṃtu śāstrīyeṇaiva pathā niṣpāditāyāmityarthaḥ | dhīmatā brahmavidāṃ vyavasthāyāṃ siddhāntasthityāmiti yāvat | pūrvaḥ ‘eṣa pūrvasya devasya lokakartussanātanaḥ’ ityādyuktarītyā sakalajagatkāraṇabhūtaḥ paraḥ sarvottara iti ca ‘paraḥ parāṇāṃ, tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaram’ ityādiśravaṇāt | viditaḥ jñāta ityarthaḥ | yadvā ‘bradaivedamamṛtaṃ purastādbrahma paścādbrahma dakṣiṇataścottareṇa | adhaścordhvaṃ ca prasṛtaṃ brahmaivedaṃ viśvamidaṃ variṣṭham’ ityādyuktarītyā pūrvaparādidigdṛśyamānassarvaḥ padārtho’pyayameveti jñāta ityarthaḥ | atra mūle pūrvaparaśabdau śrutigatadakṣiṇottarādiśabdānāmupalakṣakāviti dhyeyam ||
pakṣe ayaṃ antaraḥ antaraśabdaḥ bahiryogat bāhyarūpārthavattvāt sarvanāmatāṃ sarvanāmasaṃjñāṃ ayate | asaṃjñāyāṃ saṃjñābhinne vyavasthāyāṃ svābhidheyāpekṣāvadhiniyamarūpārthe dhīmatāṃ viduṣāṃ viditaḥ| matibuddhītyādinā vartamāne ktaḥ ‘ktasya ca vartamāne iti tadyoge ṣaṣṭhī | pūrvaḥ pūrvaśabdaḥ paraḥ paraśabdaśca sarvanāmatāmayata iti yojanā | atrārthe’pi pūrvaparaśabdau vakṣyamāṇasūtrasthāvaradakṣiṇādiśabdānāmupalakṣakāviti dhyeyam | antaraśabdasya ‘antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ’ iti bāhyarūpārthe pūrvaparaśabdayoḥ ‘pūrvaparāvara’ iti sūtreṇāsaṃjñāyāṃ vyavasthāyāṃ ca sarvanāmatānuśāsanāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva samāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
tvayyacyutāntaraṅge sakalaṃ bahiraṅgamapyasiddhaṃ naḥ | nājānantarye tava bahiṣṭvaklṛptiḥ punaḥ kadā’pi bhavet || 752 ||
he acyuta ! tvayi viṣaye naḥ antaraṅge manasi sati tvāṃ dhyāyati satīti yāvat | sakalaṃ bahiraṅgaṃ karacaraṇādyavayavajātamapi asiddhaṃ bāhyaviṣayakapratyayavaidhuryātsakalāvayavānāmavidyamānavadbhānāditi bhāvaḥ | anena dhyānasyaikāgryaṃ darśitam | tatra hetumāha neti | na, aja, ānantarye, iticchedaḥ | he aja ! bhagavan! ‘ajaśśambhau viṣṇudhātroḥ’ iti ratnamālā | tava ānantarye anantarasya bhāvaḥ ānantaryaṃ tasmin avinabhāve satītyarthaḥ | antaraṅgasyetyarthāllabhyate | punaḥ bahiṣṭvaklūptiḥ bahirbhāvakalpanaṃ punarviśleṣa iti yāvat | kadā’pi na bhavet | ato vahiraṅgamakhilaṃ na jñānaviṣayatāṃ prapadyata ityarthaḥ | yadi tvayi lagnaṃ mano bahirmukhaṃ bhavettarhi bahiraṅgamasiddhavanna syāditi bhāvaḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇācyuteti bhagavatsaṃbodhanam | ‘yasmātprāptā na cyavante so’cyutaḥ’ iti hi nirucyane | bhagavati lagnasya yasyakasyāpi vastunastasmāccyutirna bhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ ||
pakṣe pūrvārdhena ‘asiddhaṃ bahiraṅgamantaraṅge’ iti paribhāṣā, uttarārdhena tadapavādabhūtā “nājānantarye bahiṣṭvapraklūptiḥ’ iti paribhāṣā ca darśitā | antaraṅge kārye kartavye bahiraṅgamasiddhaṃ syādityantaraṅgaparibhāṣāyā arthaḥ | aco’nyānantaryanimittake’ntaraṅgaṃ kartavye jātasya bahiraṅgasya bahiṣṭvapraklūptiḥ bahiṣpadena bahiraṅgaṃ gṛhyate tasya bhāvo bahiṣṭvaṃ bahiraṅgatvaṃ tatprayuktāsiddhatvasya na praklūptiḥ na prāptiriti nājānantaryaparibhāṣāyā arthaḥ | vistarastvanyatra | atrāpi pūrvavadevāropaḥ |
yathā vā
vyāptyā prathitamabādhaṃ niṣpratipakṣaṃ ca sādhupakṣastham | saddhetumīśa jagatāṃ tvāṃ prāpyeṣṭāni sādhayanti budhāḥ || 753 ||
he īśa ! bhagavan ! budhāḥ sudhiyaḥ vyāptyā ‘antarbahiśca tatsarvam’ ityādiśrutyuktavyāpanena prathitaṃ avidyamānā bādhā duḥkhaṃ yasya taṃ ‘pīḍā bādhā vyathā duḥkham’ ityamaraḥ | ‘na jarāḥ na śoko na mṛtyuḥ’ ityādiśrutirihānusandheyā | niṣpratipakṣaṃ ‘tena ko’rhati spardhitum’ iti śruteḥ | sādhūnāṃ jñānināṃ pakṣe tiṣṭhatīti sādhupakṣasthaḥ taṃ ‘jñānī tvātmaiva me mataṃ, sa ca mama priyaḥ’ iti gānāt | saddhetuṃ ‘sadeva somya’ ityādyuktarītyā sakalajagatkāraṇamityartha | tvāṃ prāpya upāsya iṣṭāni muktaiśvaryādīni sādhayanti ||
anyatra sādhu pakṣasthamiti padadvayam | jagatāmīśeti saṃbodhanam | vyāptyā yatra dhūmastatrāgnirityādisāhacaryaniyamena prathitaṃ bādho nāma sādhyābhāvavatpakṣaḥ | sa nāsti yasya taṃ abādham | pratipakṣo nāma svasādhyaviruddhasādhyābhāvasādhakapratihetumatpakṣaḥ sa nirgato yasya taṃ niṣpratipakṣam | pakṣe saṃdigdhasādhyavati parvatādau tiṣṭhatīti pakṣasthaḥ tam | ata eva saddhetuṃ dhūmādiṃ prāpya jñātvā iṣṭāni vahnyādīni sādhayantītyarthaḥ | atra vedāntaśāstrīyavyavahāre prastute aprastutanyāyaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
yatra tvaṃ tatra śrīriti yuvayossāhacaryaniyamo’sti | vyāptiścaiṣā’cyuta tatsādhyā satpakṣavartinastvacchrīḥ || 754 ||
he acyuta ! yatra yasmin deśe tvaṃ bhavasi, tatra śrīrlakṣmīrapi bhavati | iti yuvayoḥ tava śriyaśca ‘tyadādīni sarvairnityam’ iti yuṣmacchabdaikaśeṣaḥ sāhacaryaniyamaḥ asti
devatve devadeheyaṃ manuṣyatve ca mānuṣī |
viṣṇordehānurūpāṃ vai karotyeṣā’’tmanastanum ||
ityuktaprakriyayā niyatasāhacaryamastīti bhāvaḥ | ‘nityaivaiṣā jaganmātā viṣṇośśrīranapāyinī’ ityādikamapi bhāvyam | eṣā vyāptiśca
yathā mayā jagadvyāptaṃ svarūpeṇa svabhāvataḥ |
tayā vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ niyantrī ca tatheśvarī |
mayā vyāptā tathā sā’pi tayā vyāpto’hamīśvaraḥ ||
iti lakṣmītantroktā vyāptirapi astīti saṃbadhyate | tat tasmāt satpakṣavartinaḥ brahmavitpakṣapātinaḥ
caturvidhā mama janā bhaktā eva hi te matāḥ |
teṣāmekāntinaśśreṣṭhāste caivānanyadaivatāḥ ||
iti svevaivoktatvāditi bhāvaḥ | sādhujanapakṣapātina iti vā |’mamaprāṇā hi pāṇḍavāḥ’ iti tadukteriti bhāvaḥ | tvat nityānapāyilakṣmīkādbhavataḥ | śrīḥ sādhyate 
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo mamāvaha |
tāṃ ma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm |
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ vindeyaṃ gāmaśvaṃ puruṣānaham ||
ityādyuktarītyā mahāvibhūtestvatta eva aihikāmuṣmikāpavargikasarvavidhasamṛddhissādhyata iti bhāvaḥ | jātavedaśśabdo bhagavatparatayā vyākhyātaśrīsūktabhāṣye | ‘sakalaphalaprado hi viṣṇuḥ | kimalabhyaṃ bhagavati prasanne śrīniketane’ ityādipramāṇānyatrānusaṃdheyāni ||
pakṣe yuvayoḥ yatra tvaṃ tatra śrīriti yatra dhūmastatrāgnirityādivatsāhacaryaniyamaḥ asti vyāptiśca vyāptirapi eṣā iyameva | sarvaṃ hi vākyaṃ sāvadhāraṇam | sāhacaryaniyama evetyarthaḥ | ‘sāhacaryaniyamo vyāptiḥ iti hi tallakṣaṇam | iyamiti vidheyavyāptiśabdagataliṅgakaṭākṣeṇa | tataḥ uktavidhavyāptisadbhāvādeva pakṣavartinaḥ, pakṣe ‘sandigdhasādhyavānpakṣaḥ’ ityuktalakṣaṇe parvatādau vartata iti tathoktaḥ | tasmāt tvattaḥ saddhetubhūtādbhavataḥ śrīḥ lakṣmīḥ sādhyate vahnyādiriveti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi pūrvodāharaṇa ivāropo’nusandheyaḥ ||
yathā vā
yasminniścitamacyuta sādhyaṃ niśśreyasaṃ vasatyantaḥ | tvadbhakto nyāyavidāṃ sa sapakṣassyātphaṇādharagirīndo || 755 ||
he phaṇādharagirīndo! acyuta ! yasmin puṃsi antaḥ manasi niḥśreyasaṃ muktiḥ niścitaṃ yathā syāttathā sādhyaṃ sādhanīyaṃ sat vasati | sādhyaṃ niścitamiti vā yojanā, sādhyatvena niścitaṃ sadvasatītyarthaḥ | ‘saviśeṣaṇe hi’ iti nyāyena sādhyatvena niśśreyasaviṣayakaniścayo manasvastīti yāvat |
yasminnidaṃ vicikitsanti mṛtyo yatsāṃparāye mahati brūhi nastat |
yo’yaṃ varo gūḍhamanupraviṣṭo nānyaṃ tasmānnaciketā vṛṇīte ||
ityuktarītyā naciketaso manasīveti bhāvaḥ | antarniśśreyasaṃ vasatītyayaṃ nirdeśaḥ ‘kartuṃ vā kaccidantarvasati vasumatīdakṣiṇassaptatantuḥ’ itivaditi dhyeyam | saḥ tvadbhaktaḥ nyāyavidāṃ brahmamīmāṃsānayavidāṃ gautamīyanyāyavettṝṇāṃ ca | sapakṣaḥ sadṛśaḥ pakṣo’sya sapakṣaḥ svavargīṇa ityarthaḥ | syāt tvādṛṅno bhūyānnaciketaḥ praṣṭā’ ityuktarītyā chātratvena svīkāryo bhavediti bhāvaḥ| atrāpi vedāntaśāstrīye prastute vyavahāre ‘niścitasādhyavānsapakṣaḥ’ iti nyāyaśāstrīyasyāprastutavyavahārasyāropaḥ | niścitaṃ yatsādhyaṃ vahnyādi tadvānmahānasādiḥ sapakṣaḥ sapakṣasaṃjñaka iti tadarthaḥ||
yathā vā
sa prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaṃ bhajate sākṣī bhavānvarada puruṣaḥ| avikṛtirādyaprakṛtirmahato’pyanyasya sa bhavitaiva khalu || 756 ||
he varada ! avikṛtiḥ nirvikārā ādyaprakṛtiḥ akhilabhuvanānāṃ prathamopādānakāraṇabhūtaḥ | prakṛtiśabdo nityastrīliṅgaḥ ‘vāsudevaḥ parā prakṛtiḥ, prakṛtiśca pratijñādṛṣṭāntānuparodhāt’ ityādikamihānusandheyam | avikṛtiriti viśeṣaṇena ‘acetanā parārthā ca nityā satatavikriyā’ ityuktarītyā mūlaprakṛterivāsyāḥ prakṛtessvarūpānyathābhāvarāhityamuktam | cidaciccharīrakatayā parasya brahmaṇa upādānakāraṇatve’pi vikārāṇāṃ śarīragatatvasya śrībhāṣyādau nirūpitatvāt ‘niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ, avikārāya śuddhāya’ ityādipramāṇapratipāditanirvikārabhāve tasminna tatprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ | ata eva puruṣaḥ ‘pūrvamevāhamihāsamiti | tatpuruṣasya puruṣatvam’ iti nirvacanaviṣayaḥ paramaḥ pumān sākṣī sarvadā sarvadraṣṭā ‘sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaśca’ ityādiśruteḥ bhavān prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaṃ prakṛteḥ svabhāvasya yā vikṛtiḥ vikāraḥ tasyāḥ bhāvaṃ sthitiṃ jñānatvānandatvādisvabhāvānyathābhāvamityarthaḥ | na bhajate | tasya karmakṛtatvena tava tatsaṃsparśābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | ‘prakṛtirguṇasāmye syādamātyādisvabhāvayoḥ | yonau liṅge ca’ iti medinī | kośe yoniśabdaḥ prakṛtivācī | anyasya tvaditarasya caturmukhādeḥ pratyagātmanastu mahato’pi tvaditarāpekṣayā pūjyasyāpi | saḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaḥ bhavitaiva | tasya sarvasyāpi
ābrahmastambaparyantā jagadantarvyavasthitāḥ |
prāṇinaḥ karmajanitasaṃsāravaśavartinaḥ ||
iti karmaparatantratāpratipādanena svabhāvānyathābhāvasyāvaśyaṃbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | khalviti śāstraprasiddhiṃ dyotayati ||
anyatra tu
mūlaprakṛtiravikṛtirmahadādyāḥ prakṛtivikṛtayassapta |
ṣoḍaśakaśca vikāro na prakṛtirna vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ ||
iti sāṃkhyatantraprakriyā nibaddhā | tathāhi tadarthaḥ mūlaprakṛtirnāma sattvarajastamasāṃ sāmyarūpā prakṛtiḥ | tathāca kāpilaṃ sūtraṃ sattvarajastamasāṃ sāmyāvasthā prakṛtiḥ’ iti | seyamekā svayamacetanā anekacetanabhogāpavargārthā nityā sarvagatā satatavikriyā na kasyacidvikṛtiḥ | tathāca sūtram ‘mūle mūlābhāvādamūlaṃ mūlam’ iti | api tu paramakāraṇameva | mahadādyāḥ sapta | mahānahaṃkāraśśabdatanmātrāditanmātrapañcakaṃ ceti sapta | prakṛtivikṛtayaḥ svasvakāryanirūpitaprakṛtitvaṃ svasvakāraṇanirūpitavikṛtitvaṃ ca teṣāmastīti bhāvaḥ | yathā mahataḥ prakṛtinirūpitavikṛtitvaṃ ahaṃkāranirūpitaprakṛtitvaṃ ca | evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam | yathāca pūrvodāhṛtasūtrakhaṇḍaḥ ‘prakṛtermahān mahato’haṃkāraḥ ahaṃkārātpañca tanmātrāṇi’ iti | ākāśādibhūtapañcakaṃ śrotrādijñānendriyapañcakaṃ vāgādikarmendriyapañcakaṃ manaścaikamiti ṣoḍhaśa kevalaṃ vikārāḥ | na tu kasya citprakṛtayaḥ | sūtrakhaṇḍaśca ‘ubhayamindriyaṃ tanmātrebhyassthūlabhūtāni’ iti | puruṣaḥ ‘śarīrādivyatiriktaḥ pumān’ iti sūtroktaḥ pumāṃstu niṣpariṇāmatvena na kasya citprakṛtiḥ na kasyacidvikṛtiriti | udāharaṇapadyārthastu puruṣaḥ sākṣī kevalaṃ sākṣibhūtaḥ ||
saṃghātaparārthatvāttriguṇādiviparyayādadhiṣṭhānāt |
puruṣo’sti bhoktṛbhāvātkaivalyārthaṃ pravṛtteśca ||
tasmācca viparyāsātsiddhaṃ sākṣitvamasya puruṣasya |
kaivalyaṃ mādhyasthyaṃ draṣṭṛtvamakartṛbhāvaśca ||
iti hi tadīyaprakriyā | prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaṃ uktarītyā kasyacitprakṛtitvaṃ vikṛtitvaṃ vā na bhajate | ādyaprakṛtiḥ mūlaprakṛtiḥ avikṛtiḥ nirvikārā mahataḥ mahattattvasya apiśabdo bhinnakramaḥ | anyasyāpi ahaṃkārāditanmātraparyantasya tattvaṣaṭkasyāpi saḥ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaḥ uktarītyā prakṛtitvaṃ vikṛtitvaṃ ca bhavitaiva khalu | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ, ṣoḍaśakaśca vikāra ityasyāpīti saṃkṣepaḥ | atra prastute vedāntaśāstrīyavyavahāre aprastutakāpilatantrasiddhavyavahārāropa iti pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
vidadhāsi vastuni dairghyaṃ viśvasya tathā pare’pi padarāji | na vikalpamatra tanuṣe nanu raghunandana samastapadavittvam || 757 ||
nanu raghunandana !
śakto’pi rakṣaṇe lobhādbhayādvā śaraṇāgatam |
yastyajetpuruṣo loke brahmahatyāṃ sa vindati ||
ityādinā rākṣasaṃ prati vyatirekamukhena śaraṇāgataparitrāṇasya dharmamūrdhanyatvamupadiśato raghorapyānandadetyarthaḥ | anena ca saṃbuddhyantapadena śaraṇāgataparitrāṇādidharmapravartanāya tadanuguṇe’bhijane’vatīrṇatvaṃ tādṛśeṣu śaraṇyeṣu ca śīrṣaṇyatvaṃ dyotitam | samastapadavit samastasya akhilalokasya padaṃ śaraṇavaraṇaviṣayakaṃ vyavasāyaṃ vettīti tathoktaḥ, sarvavastuvettā sarvajña iti vā | sarveṣāṃ padaṃ trāṇaṃ vettīti vā | sarvabhūtānāmabhayapradānena rakṣiteti yāvat |
ya evamāha
sakṛdeva prapannāya tavāsmīti ca yācate |
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo dadāmyetadvrataṃ mama ||
iti | ‘padaṃ vyavasitatrāṇasthānalakṣmāṅghrivastuṣu’ ityamaraḥ | tvaṃ 
vibhīṣaṇo vā sugrīva yadi vā rāvaṇassvayam |
ānayainaṃ hariśreṣṭha dattamasyābhayaṃ mayā ||
iti vādīti bhāvaḥ | pare’pi śatrupakṣīyatayā sugrovādibhiśśaṅkite’pi vibhīṣaṇa iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā pare rāvaṇādāvapi kimuta vibhīṣaṇa iti bhāvaḥ | apiśabdo’tra saṃbhāvane| saṃbhāvanaṃ nāmātra dānaśauṇḍatāviṣkaraṇamityuktiḥ | padarāji padayoścaraṇayoḥ rājata iti tathoktaḥ tasmin | ‘tyaktvā dārāṃśca putrāṃśca rāghavaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ’ ityuktarītyā sarvasvaparityāgena caraṇāravindaśaraṇavaraṇavidhātarīti bhāvaḥ | ayamartho rājītyanena dhvanyate | tathā viśvasya tathāśabdo’trānirvacanīyatādyotakaḥ | ‘tathāgatāṃ tām’ ityādivat | anirvacanīyaṃ yathā syāttathā viśvasya visrabhyetyarthaḥ | vipūrvakāt śvaseḥ ktvo lyap | vasuni dhanopalakṣite aiśvaryaṃ viṣaye dairghyaṃ ākalpāntakālāvasthāyitvaṃ aparicchinnatvaṃ vā vidadhāsi karoṣi | atra īdṛśaiśvaryaviśrāṇanaviṣaye vikalpaṃ vividhaṃ kalpaṃ deyaṃ vā na vetyādisaṃdehaṃ na tanuṣe nirvicikitsameva datse ityarthaḥ ||
pakṣe samastapadavit samagraśabdaśāstravettetyarthaḥ | yadvā samastāni samāsaṃ prāptāni yāni padāni tāni vettīti tathoktaḥ | sarvasamāsāśrayavidhijña iti yāvat | tvaṃ vasuni vasuśabde tathā evaṃ padarāji padasaṃjñābhāji rāṭchabde pare sati viśvasya viśvaśabdasya dairghyaṃ dīrghaṃ, svārthe ṣyañ | vidadhāsi ‘viśvasya vasurāṭoḥ iti sūtreṇa vidhatse ityarthaḥ| viśvaśabdasya dīrghassyādvasau rāṭchabde ca pare iti sūtrārthaḥ | ‘rāḍiti padāntopalakṣaṇārthaṃ cartvamavivakṣitam’ ityāhuḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa padarājīti rāṭchabdaḥ padaśabdena viśeṣita iti dhyeyam | atra asmin dīrghavidhau vikalpaṃ vibhāṣāṃ na tanuṣe | tasya nityatayā vidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | atraitihāsike bhagavadvṛttānte śabdaśāstrīyavyavahārāropa iti pūrvasmādudāharaṇagaṇādviśeṣaḥ ||
yathā vā
gahanāyamānamapyahigirinātha hare samastamapi ditijam | asvapadavigrahaṃ tvaṃ purā viracayan sudhāṃ ca sādhayase || 758 ||
he ahigirinātha! hare! gahanāyamānaṃ pāpaṃ cikīrṣantamapi devaviṣaye drohaṃ cikīrṣantamapītyarthaḥ | ‘na paraḥ pāpamādatte pareṣāṃ pāpakarmaṇām’ ityādau pāpaśabdo drohārthako dṛṣṭaḥ | ‘satrakakṣakaṣṭakṛchragahanebhyaḥ kaṇvacikīrṣāyāmiti vaktavyam’ ityanena vṛttiviṣaye pāpārthakāddvitīyāntādgahanaśabdāccikīrṣāyāṃ kyaṅ | pāpaṃ cikīrṣatītyasvapadavigraha ityāhuḥ | samastaṃ sarvamapi ditijaṃ daityaṃ asvapadavigrahaṃ asvapantaḥ asvapnā devāḥ taiḥ teṣu vā avigrahaṃ tyaktakalahaṃ ‘kalahavigrahau’ ityamaraḥ | racayan surāsurāṇāṃ sindhumanthanaviṣaye sandhiṃ vidadhāna iti bhāvaḥ | sudhāṃ amṛtaṃ ca purā sādhayase kūrmāvatāre sādhitavān ‘puri luṅcāsme’ ityanena purāśabdayoge bhūtānadyatane vaikalpiko laṭ | atra śrībhāgavatīyaṃ kūrmāvatāracaritamanusandheyam ||
anyatra gahanāyamānaṃ gahanāyamānaśabdaṃ samastaṃ samāsaṃ prātaṃ ditijaśabdaṃ ca asvapadavigrahaṃ svapadarahitavṛttyarthāvabodhakavākyamityarthaḥ | racayan gahanāyamānaśabdasya prāgupadarśitarītyā asvapadavigrahaḥ | ditijaśabdasya tu dityāṃ jātā iti | ‘saptamyāṃ janerḍaḥ’ iti ḍapratyayaḥ | asyopapadasamāsatvānnityasamāsatvenāsvapadavigraha iti vibhāvanīyam | sudhāṃ ca sudhāśabdaṃ ca asvapadavigrahaṃ racayanniti saṃbadhyate | sādhitavān niṣpāditavān | suṣṭhu dhīyata iti sudhā | supūrvakāt ‘dheṭ pāne’ ityasmāddhātoḥ ‘ātaścopasarge’ iti karmaṇyaṅ | ‘ātaścopasarge’ iti kapratyaya ucita ityamarasudhāyām | ayamapi prāguktarītyā nityasamāsavādasvapadavigraha eva | atra prastute paurāṇikavyavahāre aprastutaśabdaśāstrīyavyahārāropa iti prācīnodāharaṇebhyo vailakṣaṇyamiti dhyeyam ||
yathāvā
bhūstvatsātkṛtibhāgyata upendrabhāve guṇādagādbhavyatvam | tatkṛtyāntanipāto’yuktaḥ kṣmādānakartari balidhvaṃsin || 759 ||
he balidhvaṃsin! idaṃ ca vāmanāvatārāsādhāraṇacaritamatra vivakṣitamiti dyotayitum | upendrabhāve vāmanatve yataḥ yasmin yajñiyadānakṛtye |sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | tvatsātkṛtibhāk tvatsākṛtiḥ deyasya vastunaḥ tvadadhīnīkaraṇaṃ, tāṃ bhajatīti tathoktā ‘vibhāṣā sāti kārtsnye’ ityatassātītyasminnanuvartamāne ‘deye trā ca’ ityanena vaikalpikassātipratyayaḥ kṛño yoge | balinā tubhyaṃ datteti bhāvaḥ | bhūḥ bhūmiḥ guṇāt dātṛpratigrahītrādiguṇāt | yathoktaṃ devalena
dātā pratigrahītā ca śraddhā deyaṃ ca dharmayuk |
deśakālau ca dānānāmaṅgānyetāni ṣaḍviduḥ ||
apāparogī dharmātmā ditsuravyājataśśuciḥ |
anindyajīvakarmā ca ṣaḍbhirdātā praśasyate ||
triśuklaśuklavṛttiśca ghṛṇālussaṃyatendriyaḥ|
vimukto yonidoṣebhyo brāhmaṇaḥ pātramucyate ||
triśuklaḥ
tribhirmātāpitrācāryaiśśikṣitatvena śuddhaḥ |
śaucaṃ śuddhirmahāprītirarthināṃ darśane tathā ||
satkṛtiścānasūyā ca dāne śraddhetyudāhṛtā |
ācārābādhamakleśaṃ svayatnenārjitaṃ dhanam ||
svalpaṃ vā vipulaṃ vā’pi deyamityabhidhīyate |
yadyatra durlabhaṃ dravyaṃ yasmin kāle’pi vā punaḥ ||
dānarhau deśakālau tau syātāṃ śreṣṭhau na cānyathā |
avasthā deśakālānāṃ pātradātrośca saṃpadā ||
hīnaṃ cāpi bhavecchreṣṭhaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vā’pyanyathā bhavet ||
iti | bhavyatvaṃ śubhatvaṃ yogyatvaṃ vā ‘bhavyaṃ śubhe ca yogye ca satye ca’ iti medinī | śreyaskaratvamiti yāvat | agāt alabhata | kṣmādānakartari bhūvitaraṇavidhātari balidānavendra ityarthaḥ | tatkṛtyāntanipātaḥ tasya kṛtyasya pūrvoktadānakāryasya ante avasāna eva | na tu kālāntare vā | nipātaḥ adhobhuvanapātanam | nipāto’yukta ityatra ayukta iti chedaḥ | ayuktaḥ anucitaḥ| yena yasya yasmin dānakṛtye dattaṃ vastu pātrādiguṇabhūmnā ‘yuktāṅgaissakalaiṣṣaḍbhirdānaṃ syādvipulodayam’ ityuktarītyā mahāphalaṃ saṃpadyeta, tasyaiva dānakartustatkāryānta eva pratigrahītrā nipātanamanucitamiti nirgalito’rthaḥ | ayaṃ cārthaḥ
yatpadayoracaladhīssalilaṃ pradāya |
dūrvāṅkurairapi vidhāya satīṃ saparyām ||
apyuttamāṃ gatimasau labhate trilokīṃ |
dāśvānaviklabamanāḥ kathamārtimṛcchet ||
iti balinigrahāvasare bhagavantaṃ prati brahmaṇā lokadṛṣṭyoktamarthamabhisaṃdhāya varṇita iti dhyeyam ||
pakṣe kṣmādānakartarītyatra kṣmādāneti upendrabhāve ityatra upendreti ca bhinne pade saṃbuddhyante na tu samaste | ādatte svīkarotītyādānaḥ, nandyāditvātkartari lyuḥ | kṣmāyāḥ ādānaḥ kṣmādānaḥ, tasya saṃbuddhiḥ | yadvā kṣmāṃ dattavān kṣmādāna iti ‘kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam’ iti bahulagrahaṇāt bhūte kartari lyuḍeva ‘tatkṛtārthena guṇavacanena’ ityādau ‘guṇamuktavān guṇavacanaḥ’ iti tattvabodhinyādau vyākhyānāt | athavā kṣmā dīyate asminniti ‘karaṇādhikaraṇayośca’ ityanena adhikaraṇe lyuḍeva ‘tadasminvṛddhyāyalābhaśulkopadā dīyate, samamabrāhmaṇe dānam’ ityādāviva saṃpradānasyaivādhikaraṇatvavivakṣayā saptamī |bhūdānakriyāsaṃpradānabhūtetyarthaḥ| he upendra! indrāvaraja! he balidhvaṃsin ! tvatsātkṛtiḥ tvadadhīnatā tvadbāgvidheyateti yāvat | tāṃ bhajatīti tathoktaḥ | ‘tadadhīnavacane ca’ iti sātipratyayaḥ | bhūḥ bhūdhāturityarthaḥ | bhāve bhāvārthe ‘tayorava kṛtyaktakhalarthāḥ’ ityanuśāsanāt | tayoreva bhāvakarmaṇorevetyarthaḥ | yataḥ ‘aco yat’ iti vihitādyatpratyayāt yatpratyayaṃ prāpyetyarthaḥ | lyablope karmaṇi pañcamī | guṇāt ‘sārvadhātukārdhadhātukayoḥ ityanena vihitādārdhadhātukatvalakṣaṇādgṛṇāt | bhavyatvaṃ bhavyamiti rūpaṃ agāt prāpnot | bhūdhātorakarmakatayā bhāve yati guṇe ca bhavyamiti niṣpatteriti bhāvaḥ| kartari kartrarthe tatkṛtyāntanipātaḥ tasya bhūdhātoreva kṛtyāntanipātaḥ ‘bhavyageya’ ityādinā kṛtyapratyayāntatvena nipātanamityarthaḥ | nipāto nāma lakṣaṇābhāve sādhutvāya sūtrakṛtā svarūpeṇa śabdānāmuccāraṇam | yukta iti chedaḥ | ucita ityarthaḥ | ‘kṛtyāḥ’ ityadhikāre bhūdhātoḥ ‘aco yat ‘ iti kṛtyapratyayassiddhaḥ | ‘bhavyageya’ ityādinā kartari sa nipātita iti vivekaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadevāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
hanta tvadgiryudakaṃ jātusvaparaikabindusaṃsargāt | nāthodetyādeśātprathayati sādhuṃ samastamātmānam ||
he nātha! tvadgiriḥ veṃkaṭādriḥ tasya udakaṃ ā deśāt sarvadeśamabhivyāpya ‘āṅmaryādāvacane’ iti | karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāyāṃ ‘pañcamyapāṅ’ ityādinā pañcamī ‘āṅmaryādābhividhyoḥ’ ityasya mahāvibhāṣādhikārasthatvātsamāsābhāvaḥ | udetya ākāśagaṅgākalyādinadīrūpeṇodgatya jātu kadācit svaparaikabindusaṃsargāt svasya paraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ ekaḥ yo binduḥ śīkaraḥ tasya saṃsargāt saṃparkamātrāditi yāvat | samastaṃ svasaṃsargiṇaṃ sarvaṃ ātmānaṃ cetanaṃ sādhu nirdhūtapāpmānaṃ santaṃ prathayati prakhyāpayati | veṃkaṭārdrernissṛto nirjharassvakīyaikaśīkarasaṃsparśamātreṇāpyakhilacetanajātaṃ pāvayatīti saṃpiṇḍito’rthaḥ ||
yadvā tvadgiryudakaṃ udetya ādeśāt ātmānaṃ jātu svaparaikabindusaṃmargāt sādhuṃ prathayatīti yojanā | udetya udayaṃ prāpya svāmitīrthādināmnā utkarṣametyetyarthaḥ | ‘udayastu pumān pūrvaparvate ca samunnatau iti medinī | anyatsarvamuktārthakameva | anyatra tvadgiri tvadvāci sthitaṃ tvayoccāritamiti yāvat | udakaṃ udakapadaṃ jātu samāsavivakṣāyāmiti yāvat | svaparaikabindusaṃsargāt svasmāt udakapadāt svasmāt paraḥ paratra vidyamānaḥ ekaḥ kevalaḥ padāntarāvyavahita iti yāvat | yo binduḥ binduśabdaḥ tasya saṃsargāt saṃbandhāt | udetyādeśāt uda ityākārakamādeśaṃ prāpyetyarthaḥ | lyablope karmaṇi pañcamī ||
yadvā jātu ityetadatra saṃbadhyate | jātu udetyākārakamādeśaṃ prāpyetyarthaḥ | anenāyamudādeśaḥ anantarodāhariṣyamāṇasūtravihito vaikalpika ityuktaṃ bhavati | samastaṃ samāsaṃ prāptaṃ ātmānaṃ svaṃ sādhuṃ prathayati | udakaśabdaḥ padāntarāvyavahitabinduśabdena samastastasminnuttarapade ‘manthaudanasaktubinduvajrabhārahāravīvadhagāheṣu ca’ ityanena udādeśaṃ prāptassvasya sādhutāṃ prakāśayatīti pariniṣpanno’rthaḥ | atrāpi paurāṇike vyavahāre pūrvavadāropaḥ | prasiddhyati hi veṃkaṭādrijanmanāṃ saritāṃ tīrthānāṃ ca mahimā | yathāhi skānde veṃkaṭagirimāhātmye
tasminveṃkaṭaśailendre surāsuranamaskṛte |
viyadgaṅgeti nāmnā vai tīrthamasti mahattaram ||
iti | tatraivānyatra
vṛṣabhācalasaṃbhūtā tīrtharājivirājitā |
nadīnāmuttamā kalyā kaluṣaughavināśinī ||
iti | vāmanapurāṇe ca
tīrthānyetāni saṃbhūya vartante parvatottame |
nadyassamastāḥ pāpaghnyastāsāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate ||
iti ca |
svāmitīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
yasya tīre jagannātho ramate ramayā saha |
viśrutaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśeṣeṇa nṛpātmaja ||
iti ||
svāmitvātsarvatīrthānāṃ svāmitīrthamitīritam ||
ityadi cetyalaṃ dūradhāvanena ||
yathā vā 
taddhitakṛdantamacyuta tava nāma yadasti nanu samastamapi| prātipadikaṃ hi tadidaṃ prabhāti satsuprayojyamitaranna || 761 ||
nanu acyuta ! tat sakalapurāṇādiprasiddhaṃ hitakṛdantaṃ hitakṛt śreyaskāri antaḥ paryavasānaṃ yasya tat antataśśreyaḥ pradamityarthaḥ | yadvā hitakṛt antaḥ svarūpaṃ yasya tat svabhāvata eva śreyaskaramityarthaḥ| ‘anto’strī niścaye nāśe svarūpe’gre’ntike’ntare’ iti ratnamālā | atra |
yathākathaṃcidgovinde kīrtite vā śrute’pi vā |
pāpino’pi viśuddhāssyuśśuddhā mokṣamavāpnuyuḥ ||
sakṛtsmṛto’pi govindo nṛṇāṃ varṣaśataiścitam |
pāparāśiṃ dahatyāśu tūlarāśimivānalaḥ ||
baddhaḥ parikarastena mokṣāya gamanaṃ prati |
sakṛduccāritaṃ yena harirityakṣaradvayam |
avaśenāpi yannāmni kīrtite sarvapātakaiḥ |
pumānvimucyate sadyassiṃhatrastairmṛgairiva ||
harirharati pāpāni duṣṭacittairapi smṛtaḥ |
anicchayā’pi saṃspṛṣṭo dahatyeva hutāśanaḥ ||
ityādipramāṇasahasramanundheyam | samastaṃ nikhilamapi tava nāma abhidhānaṃ yadasti, tadidaṃ tadetattava nāma | padepade pratipadaṃ, yathārthe’vyayībhāvaḥ | pratipadaṃ bhavaṃ prātipadikaṃ, adhyātmāderākṛtigaṇatvādbhavārthe ṭhañ | paunaḥpunikaṃ yathā syāttathā | satsu prayojyaṃ sadbhiḥ brahmavidbhiḥ suṣṭhu prayoktavyaṃ saṃkīrtanīyamityarthaḥ | yadvā ‘tannāmakaraṇaṃ ceti dāsyametaccatuṣṭayam’ ityuktarītyā dāsyasūcakatvena putraśiṣyādau suṣṭhu nyasanīyamityarthaḥ | prabhāti prakāśate | athavā prātipadikamiti nāmna eva viśeṣaṇaṃ prātipadikaṃ tadidaṃ satsu prayojyamiti yojanā | pratipat jñānaṃ ‘citsaṃvitpratipadjñapticetanā’ ityamaraḥ | pratipat jñānaṃ prayojanamasya pratipadakaṃ ‘prayojanam’ ityanena prāgvahatīyaṣṭhak | jñānaphalakamityarthaḥ | jñānaṃ cātra
saṃjñāyate yena tadastadoṣaṃ śuddhaṃ paraṃ nirmalamekarūpam |
saṃdṛśyate vā’pyadhigamyate vā tajjñānam .......||
ityuktaṃ niśśreyasopāyabhūtamupāsanātmakamiti vedyam | pratipacchabdasya pratipūrvekapadadhātuniṣpannatayā dakārāntatvena nātra ‘isusuktāntātkaḥ’ iti tāntalakṣaṇakādeśa iti dhyeyam | itarat bhagavaditaranāmadheyaṃ tu na | satsu prayojyaṃ na bhavati | teṣāmananyaśeṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ | yathocyate bharadvājasaṃhitāyāṃ
tāmasān rājasāṃścaiva vyapadeśānvivarjayet |
dhruvaṃ vyapaiti bhakto’pi vyapadeśairasāttviktaiḥ ||
vyapadeśaiḥ nāmabhiḥ |
sāttvikaṃ nāma śuddhānāmaśuddhānāṃ tu rājasam |
bāhyāditantrasiddhānāṃ daivatānāṃ tu tāmasam ||
iti | śuddhānāṃ ananyayājinām | aśuddhānāmanyayājināmityarthaḥ | yadvā pūrvārdhe’pi samastamapi tava nāma yadasti tat hitakṛdantamiti yojanā | arthastūkta eva | hitakṛdantamityetat vidheyamasminpakṣe iti viśeṣaḥ |
