anyatra taddhitakṛdantaṃ taddhitāntaṃ kṛdantaṃ ca yat śabdarūpam | samastamapi samāsarūpamapi yat | karmaṇi bhāve vā ktaḥ | nāma nāmatvena saṃketitaṃ taddhi tadeva ‘hi hetāvavadhāraṇe’ ityamaraḥ | prātipadikaṃ prātipadikasaṃjñakam | nāmaprātipadikaśabdayorekārthatvāt | ‘kṛttaddhitasamāsāśca’ iti nāmnāṃ prātipadikasaṃjñāvidhānāt | idaṃ pūrvoktaṃ prātipadikaṃ supprayojyaṃ suppratyāhāraghaṭakavibhaktibhiḥ prayojyaṃ prayogārhaṃ subvaiśiṣṭyenaiva hi teṣāṃ prayogoloke dṛśyate | sat sādhu ‘satye sādhau vidyamāne praśaste’bhyarhite ca sat’ ityamaraḥ | prabhāti | itarat asupprayojyaṃ subaviśiṣṭamityarthaḥ | na sādhu na prabhāti ‘na kevalā prakṛtiḥ prayoktavyā’ iti bhāṣaṇāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi purāṇaprasiddhe prastutavyavahāre aprastutaśabdaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
paramaṃ padamadhyavasatsaramaṃ yadbrahma tattvamasi bhagavan | iha phaṇiśikhariṇi lagnastadbhāgatyāgalakṣaṇāśrayaṇāt || 762 ||
he bhagavan ! śrīnivāsa ! yadbrahma paramaṃ padaṃ ‘tadviṣṇoḥ’ ityādiśrutiprathitaṃ divyaṃ sthānaṃ saramaṃ saśrīkaṃ sat adhyavasat bhaktānugrahārthaparigṛhītadivyamaṅgalavigrahaviśiṣṭatayā adhyatiṣṭhat | iha asmin bhūloke phaṇiśikhariṇi lagnaḥ tvaṃ tadbrahma asīti yojanā | arthastu sphuṭa eva |

śrīnivāso’pi śeṣādrau tatassvāmisarastaṭe |
ityupakramya,
purāṇapuruṣo dhātā puruṣastamasaḥ paraḥ |
vaikuṇṭhaṃ pūrvavasatiṃ tyaktvedānīṃ hi he tiṣṭhati ||
ite pādmaveṃkaṭādrimāhātmyoktiḥ, ‘māyāvī paramānandam’ ityādyuktiścehānusaṃhitā | ata eva tadbhā tasya paramapadasya bhā | ādanto’yaṃ śabdaḥ |
brahmaṇassadanāttasya paraṃ sthānaṃ prakāśate |
devā hi yanna paśyanti divyaṃ tejomayaṃ padam ||
atyarkānaladīptaṃ tat sthānaṃ viṣṇormahātmanaḥ |
svayaiva prabhayā rājan duṣprekṣaṃ devadānavaiḥ ||
iti mahābhāratādyuktā divyā dīptiḥ | āgatya paramapadādbhuvametya | agalakṣaṇāśrayaṇāt agalakṣaṇaṃ uktaphaṇigirirūpaṃ yat āśrayaṇaṃ ādhāraḥ taṃ atati satataṃ gacchatītyagalakṣaṇāśrayaṇāt ‘ata sātatyagamane’ asmāt kvip | bhavati | parasmin brahmaṇi paramavyomnaḥ phaṇigirāvavatīrṇe paramavyomadivyadīptirapi phaṇigirimeva satatamāśritetyarthaḥ |
pakṣe tat tvamiti padadvayaṃ bhāgatyāgalakṣaṇāyāḥ jahadajahallakṣaṇāyāḥ āśrayaṇāt arthabodhakaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | parasya brahmaṇo divyamaṅgalavigrahaviśiṣṭasya yaddeśakālavaiśiṣṭyaṃ pūrvamāsīt taddeśakālavaiśiṣṭyamadhunā nāstīti tattvamityatra tattāṃśe svārthatyāgaḥ tvattāṃśe tu tadatyāga itīyaṃ bhavati jahadajahallakṣaṇeti dhyeyam ||
aṃśabhedamupādāya jahāti na jahāti ca |
śabdassvārthaṃ yadi jahadajahatsvārthalakṣaṇā ||
iti tallakṣaṇam | atra prastute paurāṇikavyavahāre aprastutasyālaṃkāraśāstrīyavyavahārasyāropaḥ |
yadvā atra tattvamasīti padaiḥ ‘tattvamasi śvetaketo’ iti śrutivākyapratyabhijñāpanāt tatra jīvabrahmābhedamupapādayatāmadvaitināṃ vyavahāro’prastutaḥ pratīyamānaḥ prastute paurāṇikavyavahāre samāropyata iti | te hi ‘tattvamasi’ ityatra tattvaṃpadārthayossarvajñatvakiṃcidjñatvarūpaviruddhāṃśaparityāgena aviruddhacinmātropādānena cobhayopalakṣitamekamakhaṇḍaṃ vastu bhāgatyāgalakṣaṇayā pratipādyata iti varṇayanti | anayoriyāneva bhedaḥ | tathāhy ālaṃkārikāstāvat ‘so’yaṃ devadattaḥ’ ityādau tattāṃśasyedānīmasaṃbhavāttasyaiva hānaṃ idaṃtāṃśasya tvidānīṃ saṃbhavādahānamiti jahadajahatsvārthaṃ lakṣaṇāmācakṣate | advaitabrahmavādinastu dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayoraikarūpyāya ‘so’yaṃ devadattaḥ’ ityatrāpi tattedaṃtāṃśayoḥ parasparaviruddhayorubhayoḥ parityāgena aviruddhasvarūpamātrāparityāgena dvayopalakṣitaṃ svarūpamātraṃ lakṣyata iti jahadajahatsvārthaṃ vyutpādya ‘tattvamasi ityādimahāvākye’pi tattvaṃpadārthagataviruddhāṃśatyāgena cinmātropādānena cobhayopalakṣitamakhaṇḍamekameva vastūktalakṣaṇayā pratipādyata iti | yadāhuḥ
svārthaikāṃśatyāgādaṃśāntarameva lakṣyate yatra |
sā jahadajahatsvārthā tattvamasītyādiviṣayaddaśyeyam ||
itītyalamatidūrotsarpaṇena ||
yathāvā
suviditaśaktiṃ svāmin kṛtalakṣaṇamañjanācalollāsam | sāhityametyabhāntaṃ śriyā śraye tvāmananta suślokam || 763 ||
anantaḥ deśakālavastubhiraparicchinnaḥ ‘satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma’ iti śruteḥ | tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he ananta! he svāmin! sarvaśeṣin! suviditā sakalaśrutisuprasiddhā śaktiḥ yasya tam | śaktirnāma svetarasarvasāmarthyanirvāhakaḥ ‘jagatprakṛtibhāvo me yassā śaktiritīryate’ ityādiṣūktassarvopādānatvātmako guṇaviśeṣaḥ |yadanyairaśakyatvādaghaṭitamiva bhavati taddhaṭanasamarthyarūpo vā | atra ‘parā’sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate’ iti śrutirapyanusandheyā | kṛtalakṣaṇaṃ ‘samastakalyāṇaguṇātmako’sau’ ityādyuktarītyā kalyāṇaguṇaiḥ pratītaṃ ‘guṇaiḥ pratīte tu kṛtalakṣaṇāhitalakṣaṇau’ ityamaraḥ | añjanācale ullāso harṣaḥ prakāśo vā yasya tam | añjanācalena ullāsyata iti vā tathoktam | śriyā lakṣmyā sāhityaṃ vaiśiṣṭayaṃ etya prāpya bhāntaṃ śrīvaiśiṭyādeva hi tava bhānamiti bhāvaḥ | atra ‘svāmipuṣkariṇītīre ramayā saha modate’ ityeta dapyanusandheyam | ata eva suślokaṃ śobhanakīrtiṃ ‘hrīśca te lakṣmīśca patnyau’ iti lakṣmīpatitvena śrutasyaiva hi tava ‘tasya nāma mahadyaśaḥ’ iti yaśasvitā śrūyate ‘sa āpaḥ pradughe ubhe ime’ iti ‘jagatkāraṇatvena prastutasya nikhalajagajjananādilīlatvasarvarakṣakatvakīrtistasyaiva nānyasyetyarthaḥ | nāmeti prasiddhau’ ityupaniṣadbhāṣyam | tvāṃ śraye | nārāyaṇīye prathamānuvāke ‘adbhyassaṃbhūto hiraṇyagarbha ityaṣṭau’ iti pāṭhāt tasmiṃścānuvāke hrīśca te lakṣmīśca patnyau’ iti śravaṇāt lakṣmīpatereva ‘tasya nāma’ iti tadanuvākoktaṃ yaśasvitvaṃ nānyasyetyavadheyam |
pakṣe śaktiḥ abhidhāparaparyāyaḥ sākṣātsaṃketaviṣayaviṣayakadhīheturmukhyaśśabdaniṣṭho vyāpāraḥ ‘vyāpāraśśakyadhīheturmukhyaśśabdagato’bhidhā’ iti lakṣaṇāt | sā suṣṭhuviditā yena tam | kṛtā vicāritā lakṣaṇā yena taṃ tathoktam | lakṣaṇā nāma
tātparyaviṣayībhūtārthānvayānupapattitaḥ |
śaktibhinnārthadhīheturvyāpāro lakṣaṇocyate ||
ityuktalakṣaṇaśśabdavyāpāraḥ | kṛño vyāpārasāmānyārthakatvena kṛtetyasya vicāraṇārthakatvamavaseyam | ata eva kṛtahastaḥ kṛtadhīrityādau kṛñaśśiādyarthakatvaṃ dṛśyate | añjanaṃ ‘vācyalakṣyavibhinnārthadhīkṛdvyāpṛtirañjanam’ ityuktalakṣaṇo vyañjanāparaparyāyaśśabdavyāpāraviśeṣaḥ | tasmin acalaḥ sthiraḥ ullāsaḥ harṣaḥ yasya tam | etairviśeṣaṇaiḥ abhidhālakṣaṇāvyañjanārūpaśabdavyāpāratritayakūlaṃkaṣapratibhonmeṣatayā vācakalakṣakavyañjakapadaprayoganiraṅkuśakauśalaṃ dyotyate | sāhityaṃ sahitāni sarvaśāstrāṇyeva sāhityam | svārthe ṣyañ | yathoktaṃ bhāvaprakāśe 
tarkavyākṛtimīmāṃsājyotiśśāstrādyapekṣayā |
sāhityātsāhitī proktā svārthe ṣyañ kīrtyate budhaiḥ || iti,
samaṣṭissarvaśāstrāṇāṃ sāhityamiti kathyate |
ityetadapyuktārthe’nukūlam | etya jñātvetyarthaḥ | śriyā upadarśitopakaraṇajñānānurūpakāvyagrathanaupayikavāgvibhavenetyarthaḥ | ‘śrīrlakṣmyāṃ saraladrume | veṣopakaraṇe veṣaracanāyāṃ matau giri| śobhātrivargasaṃpattyoḥ’ iti hemacandraḥ | ata eva anantāḥ aparicchinnāḥ suślokāḥ adoṣasaguṇasālaṃkāraśabdārtharūpakāvyalakṣaṇaparipūrṇāni padyāni yasya vaśyavāktvena bhūyiṣṭhasatkāvyanirmāṇapravīṇamiti bhāvaḥ | atra vedāntapurāṇapratipādite’rthe pūrvavadāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
dāridryadavaṃ śamayatyākhyaikā’bjākṣabhāgadheyasya | sāropāsādhyavasitiriha gauṇī sādhulakṣaṇā śuddhā ||
iha loke sārā upāsāyāḥ upāsanāyāḥ adhyavasitiḥ niścayaḥ yasyāssā tathoktā yadviṣayaka upāsanādhyavasāyaḥ avaśyaṃbhāviphalatayā śreyān sā sāropāsādhyavasitirityarthaḥ | ākhyāyā upāsanaṃ ca ‘oṃ namo nārāyaṇāyeti mantropāsakaḥ’ ityādāviva japa eveti dhyeyam | gauṇī ‘yāni nāmāni gauṇāni’ ityuktarītyā guṇata āgatā
śṛṇāti nikhilāndoṣān śrīṇāṇi ca guṇairjagat |
śrīyate cākhilairnityaṃ śrayate ca paraṃ padam ||
śrayantīṃ śrīyamāṇāṃ ca śṛṇvatīṃ śṛṇatīmapi ||
ityādinirukteriti bhāvaḥ | ata eva sādhu lakṣaṇaṃ pravṛttinimittaṃ yasyāssā tathoktā | yadvā lakṣyanta iti lakṣaṇāḥ ‘kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam’ iti bahulagrahaṇātkarmaṇi lyuṭ | sādhavo lakṣaṇāḥ yasyāssā yadākhyādhāraṇena sādhavassajjanā iti lakṣyante jñāpyante sā sādhulakṣaṇetyarthaḥ | ‘sādhurvārdhuṣike cārau sajjane cābhidheyavat’ iti medinī | śuddhā pāvanī ‘śuddhaṃ syāttriṣu kevale | nirdoṣe ca pavitre ca’ iti medinī | abjākṣasya śrīnivāsasya bhāgadheyaṃ tasya bhāgadheyatvenādhyavasitāyāśśriya ityarthaḥ | ekā ākhyā śrīrityādikamekamevābhidheyaṃ dāridryameva davaṃ vanahutāśanaṃ śamayati | ‘sarvadāridryanāśinī’ iti tannāmasu pāṭhāditi bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe iha dāridryadavamiti pade sāropā gauṇī lakṣaṇā abjākṣabhāgadheyasyetyatra tu sādhyavasitiḥ śuddhā lakṣaṇeti pāṭhakrameṇa yathāsaṃkhyamanvayaḥ | sādhu sādhvītyarthaḥ | sāmānye napuṃsakaṃ ‘śakyamaravindasurabhiḥ’ ityādivat | yadvā sādhutvena lakṣaṇaiva viśeṣyate | athavā bhavatītyadhyāhṛtakriyāviśeṣaṇamiti bodhyam | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ dāridryadavamityatra viṣayaviṣayivācakayordāridryadavapadayossāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśātsāropā lakṣaṇā | sā ca davaśabdasya tāpakāritvādiguṇasādṛśyena vṛttergauṇī ||
sāropā dvividhā seyaṃ gauṇī śuddheti bhedataḥ |
sādṛśyātmakasaṃbandharūpā gauṇītyudīryate ||
śuddhā tadanyasaṃbandharūpā sā tu nigadyate |
iti lakṣaṇāt | abjākṣabhāgadheyasyetyatra tu āropaviṣayaṃ śriyaṃ nigārya tasya viṣayiṇo bhāgadheyasyaiva nibandhanātsādhyavasānālakṣaṇā | bhāgadheyaśriyoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvasaṃbandhaṃ puraskṛtya viṣayipadamātropādānena śuddheti vivekaḥ | tathāca tallakṣaṇam
nigīrya viṣayaṃ yatra viṣayyeva nibadhyate |
tatra sādhyavasānā syādgauṇī śuddheti bhedataḥ ||
iti | adhyavasāyo nāma ekāṃśe utkaṭaṃ jñānaṃ, jñānasyotkaṭatvaṃ ca tattatkoṭisahacaritānekadharmavaddharmiviṣayatvādikam | sādṛśyasaṃbandhapuraskāreṇa pravṛttā gauṇī | tadanyasaṃbandhapuraskāreṇa pravṛttā śuddheti sāropalakṣaṇāyāmivāsyāmapi bhedo draṣṭavyaḥ | atrāpi paurāṇike śrīlakṣmīnāmavaibhave vyavahāre prastute alaṃkaraśāstrīyavyavahārasyāprastutasyāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
tāmeva yānti vṛttiṃ dhāmni pare gauṇalakṣaṇābhijñāḥ ! kavyocitā na cānyāṃ goṇīṃ| bhaktaḥ prabhākarādyeṣu || 765 ||
kāvyā buddhiḥ ucitā samañjasā yeṣāṃ te kāvyocitāḥ ‘dṛśyate tvagryayā buddhyā sūkṣmayā sūkṣmadarśibhiḥ’ ityuktaprakārasukṣmadhiya iti yāvat | ‘kāvyā syātpūtanādhiyoḥ’ iti viśvamedinīhemacandrāḥ| ‘ucitaṃ syānmite yogye jñāte’bhyaste samañjase’ iti ratnamālā | pare dhāmni aujjvalyarāmaṇīyakatamohāritvādiguṇasādṛśyavattayā tejastvenādhyavasite bhagavati śrīnivāse bhaktāḥ bhaktimantaḥ pumāṃsaḥ agauṇeti chedaḥ | agauṇaṃ mukhyaṃ yat lakṣaṇaṃ śaṅkhacakralakṣma tena abhijñāḥ bhāgavatatvena jñāyamānāssantaḥ abhipūrvakāt jñādhātoḥ ‘ātaścopasarge’ iti karmaṇyaṅ | upajñādivat | svabhāvato’yaṃ strīliṅgaḥ | paścāyudhāṅkanādapi śaṅkhacakrābhyāmevāṅkanasya mukhyatvādagauṇalakṣaṇābhijñā ityuktam |
tathāca śrutiḥ
pra te viṣṇo abjacakre pavitre
janmāmbhodhiṃ tartave carṣaṇīndrāḥ |
mūle bāhvordadhate ye purāṇāḥ
liṅgānyanye tāvakānyarpayanti ||
iti | bhagavacchāstre bharadvājaśca
agnīṣomau hi cakrābjau sarva eva tadātmakāḥ |
ūno vā mānayannekaṃ pūrṇo hi phalamaśnute ||
śaṅkhacakrapradhānaṃ hi sarvamanyadgadādikam |
aṅkitaśśaṅkhacakrābhyāṃ sarvairaṅkita eva vā ||
iti | yadvā
nārāyaṇaikaniṣṭhā ye sāttvikāṃstānnibodhata |
ityupakramya
munayassattvaniṣṭhānāṃ lakṣaṇāni nibodhata |
viṣṇordāsyaikaphalatā tadekopāyatāsthitiḥ ||
tadājñākaraṇe prītistatkarmaguṇakīrtanam |
taccihnāṅkitadehatvaṃ tadīyārādhane ratiḥ ||
tadanyasparśavairāgyamindriyārtheṣvalolatā |
ācāryādhīnavṛttitvamācāryārthātmavṛttitā ||
krodhalobhamadālasyamānamohavihīnatā |
satyaśaucadayādhairyakṣamāsaṃtoṣayuktatā ||
ityetairlakṣaṇairyuktā vaiṣṇavā vītakalmaṣāḥ|
sabhājyā darśanenaiva nāvajñeyāḥ kadācana ||
iti bharadvājapariśiṣṭoktā mukhyabhāgavatalakṣaṇajñā ityarthaḥ| abhipūrvakājjānāteḥ kartari ‘ātaścopasarge’ iti kapratyayaḥ | ata eva tāmeva tadanuguṇāṃ mukhyāmeva vṛttiṃ ananyaratitvādivakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāṃ vṛttiṃ yānti śrayanti | yathā hi bhagavacchāstrīyabharadvājasaṃhitāyām
yathā prapattirvihitā bhagavaccaraṇābjayoḥ |
tathaiva tatra vṛttiśca kāryā gurunideśataḥ ||
vṛttiśca vihitācāraḥ pratiṣiddhavivarjanam |
dṛṣṭirbhaktistathā lakṣma satāṃ seveti ṣaḍvidhāḥ ||
iti vṛtteṣṣāḍvidhyaṃ pradarśya
ananyagatiratyantaṃ vihitāni samācaran |
varjayan pratiṣiddhāni vedavedāntatattvavit ||
arcādiṣvarcayanviṣṇumaṅkito harilāñcanaiḥ|
seveta yadgurūnbhaktyā seyaṃ vṛttiḥ parā matā ||
iti pūrvoktaślokārtho vivṛtaḥ | atra pareti viśeṣaṇenāsyā eva vṛttermukhyatvamuktam | tāmevetyavadhāraṇena vyañjitamitaravyavacchedaṃ kaṇṭhato darśayati na cānyāmityādinā | prabhākarādyeṣu bhāskarādiṣu bhaktāssantaḥ anyāṃ gauṇīṃ vṛttiṃ ca na yānti nāśrayante | caśabdastvarthakaḥ|
atha vṛttimimāṃ śaśvatkurvāṇo vigatavyathaḥ |
visṛjya dehaṃ prāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam ||
ityavilambitaniśśreyasanidānatayā bharadvājasaṃhitoktabhāgavatavṛttimapekṣya
varṇāśramācāraparassapta janmāni yo dvijaḥ |
ekāntasūryabhaktassyātsa viprassaptajanmasu ||
tatastatpuṇyamāhātmyādrudrabhakto bhaviṣyati |
tathaiva saptajanmāni śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
mahatā tena puṇyena vaiṣṇavatvaṃ labheta saḥ ||
viṣṇubhaktiparastadvatsaptajanmāni mānavaḥ |
ekāntabhagavadyājī vipro bhāgavatānvaye ||
tatassa labhate muktiṃ devairatyantadurlabhām |
iti vārāhe,
tribhiḥ prajāpaterbhaktaḥ pañcabhiśśaṃkarasya tu |
viṃśatyā’gnīṃndrasūryāderjanmabhirvaiṣṇavo bhavet ||
ityādibharadvājasaṃhitāyāṃ ca bahujanmavyavadhānena muktisādhanatayā ukteṣu bhāskarādiṣvananyaratitvādikāṃ jaghanyāṃ vṛttiṃ nādriyanta iti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe kāvyocitāḥ abhyastakāvyāḥ ālaṃkārikā iti yāvat | gauṇī ca sā lakṣaṇā ca tasyāḥ abhijñāḥ ‘agnirmāṇavakaḥ, agniradhīte’ ityādau |
sādṛśyātmakasaṃbandharūpā gauṇītyudīryate |
ityuktalakṣaṇāṃ gauṇīṃ lakṣaṇāṃ jānānāḥ, ata eva pare dhāmni dhāmnītyākārake pade’pi tāmeva vṛtiṃ uktodāharaṇa iva gauṇalakṣaṇākhyāmeva śabdavyāpṛtiṃ yānti manyante | yadāhuḥ
abhidheyāvinābhūtā pratītirlakṣaṇocyate |
lakṣyamāṇaguṇairyogādvṛtteriṣṭā tu gauṇatā ||
iti | avinābhāvaścātra saṃbandhamātraṃ na tu nāntarīyakatvaṃ tathā sati mañcamañcasthayordaiśikasya kālikasya vā’vinābhāvasyābhāvāt ‘mañcāḥ krośanti’ ityatra lakṣaṇābhāvaprasaṅgāt | vṛtteḥ lakṣaṇākhyavyāpṛteḥ gauṇatā guṇaprayuktatā lakṣyamāṇaguṇairyogāt iṣṭā bhavatīti tadarthaḥ | tāmevetyavadhāraṇena vyavacchedyaṃ darśayati na cānyāmityādinā | prabhākarastāntrikāgraṇīḥ sa ādiryeṣāṃ te bhaṭṭādayaḥ | teṣu bhaktāstanmatānuyāyinassantaḥ anyāṃ lakṣaṇāpekṣayā vilakṣaṇāṃ gauṇīṃ guṇata āgatāṃ vṛttiṃ saṃbandhavyāpṛtiṃ na yānti na manyanta ityarthaḥ | te hi ‘agnirmāṇavakaḥ” ityādau samānādhikaraṇapadaprayogādeva paiṅgalyādiguṇaviśiṣṭe agnipadasya tātparyam | ‘gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ’ ityādau lakṣaṇāyāṃ tu tīrapadaprayogeṇa vinaiva tatsaṃbandhini tīre gaṅgāpadasya tātparyamiti padaprayogatadaprayogaprayuktaviśiṣṭāviśiṣṭatātparyābhyāṃ gauṇīlakṣaṇayorbhedaḥ | yatrāpi padaprayogo nasti ‘agniradhīte’ ityādau tatrāpi sādṛśyaviśiṣṭa eva padatātparyam | padaprayogasahite sthale tathaiva tatparyāvadhāraṇāt | yathā ‘jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeta’ ityādau kāmye kāminiyojyānvayabalādapūrve liṅgādestātparyamiti ‘aharahassandhyāmupāsīta’ ityādinityasthale’pi tatheti varṇayanti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ ālaṃkārikāstāvat
abhidheyena saṃbandhātsādṛśyātsamavāyataḥ |
vaiparītyātkriyāyogāllakṣaṇā pañcadhā matā ||
iti sādṛśyasyāpi saṃbandhatvenābhyupagamāt ‘agnirmāṇavakaḥ, agniradhīte’ ityādāvapi tathāvidhasaṃbandhasya sāmānādhikaraṇyādyanupapatteḥ, abhedapratītirūpaprayojanasya ca lakṣaṇābījasya jāgarūkatayā gauṇīṃ vṛttimapi lakṣaṇāyāmevāntarbhāvayanti na tu pṛthagvṛttitvenābhyupayantīti | atrāpi bhagavacchāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
piprīṣayaiva te’cyuta kanakamukharyāṃ makhairyajati hi tvām | budhatatireṣā kathamiha na syātsallakṣaṇā jahatsvārthā || 766 ||
he acyuta! budhatatiḥ vidvatsaṃhatiḥ te tava piprīṣayaiva prīṇanecchayaiva na tu prayojanāntarāpekṣayā | kanakamukharyāṃ suvarṇamukharyākhyāyāṃ taraṅgiṇyāṃ tattīre veṅkaṭādrāviti yāvat | makhaiḥ kratubhiḥ tvāṃ |
ahaṃ hi sarvayajñānāṃ bhoktā ca prabhureva ca |
iti sarvayajñabhoktṛtvena ca svenaiva gītamiti bhāvaḥ | yajati ārādhayati |
sarvabhūtāntarātmānamīśamārādhyamacyutam |
budhvā caranti sudhiyo dharmāṃstatprītaye param ||
iti bharadvājapariśiṣṭoktaprakāreṇa kevalaṃ tvatprīṇanāyaiva yāgādikarmāṇyanutiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ | sallakṣaṇā satāṃ lakṣaṇāni cihnāni tānyasyāssantīti tathoktā | arśa āderākṛtigaṇatvānmatvarthīyo’c | atra satāṃ sallakṣaṇānāṃ ca lakṣaṇaṃ
patyurjñānamayī vṛttirviṣṇoḥ priyamihocyate |
ye santi tatra niratāste santa iti kīrtitāḥ ||
iti,
prataptairbhagavaddivyāyudhairgātreṣu lāñchanam |
satataṃ ca harikṣetroddhṛtamṛtsnordhvapuṇḍrakam ||
ityupakramya,
viṣṇostatsaṃśrayāṇāṃ vā cetanācetanātmanām |
ākhyayā vyapadeśaśna lakṣaṇāni satāṃ viduḥ ||
ityantena bharadvājapariśiṣṭoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | eṣā tvatprīṇanārthameva
tvāṃ yajantī budhatatiḥ iha yajanakarmaṇi kathamiva jahatsvārthā jahāt svārtho yāṃ sā tathoktā na syāt jahatsvārthaiva syādityarthaḥ |
mayi sarvāṇi karmāṇi saṃnyasyādhyātmacetasā |
nirāśīrnirmamo bhūtvā .....................||
iti tvadgānānurodhena sarvāṇi karmāṇi kevalaṃ tvadārādhanarūpatayā tvatprīṇanānīti matvā’nutiṣṭhantyā asyā budhatateranyadarthāśaṃsā kathamapi nodiyādeveti bhāvaḥ | anena
aphalākāṅkṣibhiryajño vidhidṛṣṭo ya ijyate |
yaṣṭavyameveti manassamādhāya sa sāttvikaḥ ||
ityuktarītyā tadanuṣṭhitānāṃ makhānāṃ sāttvikatvaṃ dyotyate | anenaiva jahatsvārtheti viśeṣaṇena tasyāḥ pāramekāntyaṃ darśitam | paramaikāntino hi bhagavaddāsyaikaratitvānna tatprīṇanamantarā manāgapi svaprayojanamabhilaṣyanti yathocyate bharadvājasaṃhitāyām
sa ekāntyanyavimukho hariṃ sarveśvaraṃ param |
yassamāśrayate kiṃcitphalamabhyarthayannaraḥ ||
nyastāśeṣabharaśśrīśe yastu dāsyaikajīvitaḥ |
sa eṣa paramaikāntī dvāvetau vaiṣṇavau smṛtau ||
iti | pakṣe pūrvārdhamanatiriktārthakameva | uttarārdhe tv iha kanakamukharyāṃ yajatītyatra jahatsvārthā satī ca sā lakṣaṇā sallakṣaṇā lakṣaṇākhyā śabdavyāpṛtiḥ kathaṃ na syāt | ‘gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ’ ityatrevātrāpi svārthatyāgena lakṣaṇā syādeveti bhāvaḥ ||
syājjahallakṣaṇā yatra svārthaṃ tyaktvā’nyavṛttitā |
iti lakṣaṇāt | yatra svārthatyāgena parārthabṛttitā tatra jahallakṣaṇeti tadarthaḥ | atra ca kanakamukharyāmityatra vācyasya pravāhasya makhakaraṇakayajanānvayāyogyatvātkanakamukharīśabdassvārthaṃ parityajya svārthasaṃbandhini tīre vartata iti jahatsvārthā lakṣaṇaiṣeti dhyeyam | atra gītābhagavacchāstrādisiddhe prastutavyavahāre aprastutanyāyādiśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
paramapuruṣārthamāptuṃ phaṇādharagiriṃ prapattiradhyuṣitā | madhyamavṛttiravijahatsvārthā bhavatīha ko nu saṃdigdhām || 767 ||
madhyamavṛttiḥ madhyamā vṛttiḥ | vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇā yasyāssā tathoktā prapattiḥ, prapattiviśiṣṭāḥ puruṣā iti yāvat | ‘kuntāḥ praviśanti’ ityatra kuntaviśiṣṭāḥ puruṣā itivat | paramapuruṣārthaṃ niśśreyasarūpamuttamapuruṣārthaṃ āptuṃ labdhuṃ phaṇādharagiriṃ adhyuṣitā satī avijahat svārthaḥ paramapuruṣārtho yāṃ sā tathoktā bhavati |
upāyāpāyasaṃtyāgī madhyamāṃ vṛttimāśritaḥ|
rakṣiṣyatīti niścitya nikṣiptasvasvagocaraḥ |
budhyeta devadevaṃ taṃ goptāraṃ puruṣottamam ||
iti lakṣmītantroktamārgeṇa upāyāpāyamadhyasthāṃ vṛttimāsthito bhagavantaṃ prapanno janaḥ
yatra nārāyaṇo devaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ |
tatpuṇyaṃ paramaṃ brahma tattīrthaṃ tattapovanam ||
tatra devarṣayassiddhāssarve caiva tapo dhanāḥ|
iti bhāratādyuktarītyā prapannanivāsārhe veṅkaṭamahībhṛti kṛtavasatissvavivakṣitaṃ paramapuruṣārthaṃ sādhayatīti nirgalito’rthaḥ | iha asminviṣaye ko nu saṃdigdhāṃ kaḥ pumān saṃdegdhu, īdṛśasya paramapuruṣārthādhigame pāmaro’pi na saṃśayīteti bhāvaḥ | saṃpūrvāt ‘diha upacaye’ ityasmāddhātoḥ kartari loṭprathamapuruṣaikavacanam | upāyāpāyādisvarūpaṃ ca tatraiva lakṣmītantre
upāyāpāyayormadhye kīdṛśī sthitirambike |
upāyāpāyatāmeva kriyā sarvā’valambate ||
svīkāre vyatireke vā niṣedhavidhiśāstrayoḥ |
dṛśyate karmaṇo vyaktamupāyāpāyarūpatā ||
iti pṛcchate śakrāya
trividhāṃ paśya deveśa karmaṇo gahanāṃ gatim |
niṣedhavidhiśāstrebhyastāṃ vidhāṃ ca nibodha me ||
anarthasādhakaṃ kiṃcitkiṃciccāpyarthasādhakam |
anarthaparihārāya kiṃcitkarmopadiśyate ||
trairāśyaṃ karmaṇāmevaṃ vijñeyaṃ śāstracakṣuṣā |
apāyopāyasaṃjñau tu pūrvarāśī parityajet ||
tṛtīyo dvividho rāśiranarthaparihārakaḥ |
prāyaścittātmakaṃ kaścidutpannānarthanāśanaḥ ||
tamaṃśaṃ naiva kurvīta manīṣī pūrvarāśivat |
kriyamāṇaṃ na kasmaicidyadarthāya prakalpate ||
akriyāvadanarthāya tattu karma samācaret |
eṣā sā vaidikī niṣṭhā hyupāyāpāyamadhyamā ||
asyāṃ sthito jagannāthaṃ prapadyeta janārdanam ||
iti bhagavatyā śrīlakṣmyā svayamevoktam | vivṛtaṃ caitadvātsyavaradagurubhiḥ
etaduktaṃ bhavatyatra hiṃsāsteyādikaṃ ca yat |
anarthasādhanaṃ karma kāmyaṃ tvarthasya sādhanam ||
caityādikaṃ tathā karma sāṃkhyayogādikaṃ tathā |
upāyaṃ karma sāṃkhyādi prāyaścittādikaṃ tathā ||
cāndrāyaṇādikaṃ karma na kuryācca kadācana |
bhāvyanarthasya yatkarma parihārāya kīrtitam ||
sandhyā’rcāpañcayajñādi tanmumukṣubhirādṛtam |
naimittikaṃ cāgrayaṇaṃ sīmantonnayanādikam ||
agnyādhānādikaṃ kāryamadhikāre sati dvijaiḥ |
niṣiddhakāmyopāyākhyakarmāṇyanyāni nācaret ||
prāyaścittaṃ prapannasya viśeṣeṇa yaducyate |
lakṣmītantre kamalayā nimitte tattadācaret ||
ityādinā | pakṣe iha prapattiriti pade aviṃjahatsvārthā ajahatsvārthākhyā madhyamavṛttiḥ abhidhā lakṣaṇā vyañjaneti krameṇa parigaṇitāsu vṛttiṣu madhyamā lakṣaṇākhyā vṛttiḥ śabdavyāpṛtiḥ bhavati | prapattiradhyuṣitetyatra prapattipadaṃ svavācyāyāḥ prapatteḥ phaṇādharagiryadhikaraṇakanivāsaniṣpattaye prapattimatpuruṣe vartate | atra prapatterapi puruṣasāhityena kriyānvayādajahallakṣaṇā
svasiddhaye parākṣepe tvajahallakṣaṇocyate ||
iti lakṣaṇāt | yatra svavācyasyānvayasiddhyarthaṃ svavācyasaṃbandhī lakṣyate tatrājahallakṣaṇeti tadarthaḥ | atra bhagavacchāstrīyavyavahāre śāstrāntarīyavyavahārāropaḥ pūrvavadeva ||
atredamavadheyam ‘paramaṃ padamadhyavasat’ ityādīni padyāni ‘suviditaśaktiṃ svāmin’ iti padyavarjitāni aprastutavyavahā rātmana ālaṃkārikādyabhimatatattatpadagatatattadarthabodhaupayikalakṣaṇākhyavṛttiviśeṣasya sphūrtisadbhāvamātramavalambya samāsoktyudāharaṇatāṃ nītāni | yadā tu prakṛtāprakṛtadharmidvayasādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇopādānamūlikā aprakṛtadharmibyavahārasphūrtirhi samāsoktimūrtiḥ sā ca na prakṛtapadyeṣu suprasarā | tattvaṃpadādirūpasyāprakṛtadharmiṇastadādipadairevopādānāt | prakṛtasya phaṇiśikharilagnabhagavadādirūpasya aprakṛtasya tattvaṃpadādirūpasya ca dharmiṇossādhāraṇasya kasyacidapi viśeṣaṇasyānupādānācca | yadyapi tadbhāgatyāgalakṣaṇāśrayaṇāditi turīyacaraṇārthau paramapadāvadhikāgamanapūrvakaphaṇiśikharyāśrayaṇakartṛparamapadādhivāsibhagavatprabhā bhāgatyāgalakṣaṇāśrayaṇahetukārthabodhakatāvattattvaṃpadadvayaṃ cetyetau śleṣato’bhedenādhyavasīyetām, evamanyeṣvapi padeṣu tau tāvarthau | tathā’pi naitau kasyacidviśeṣaṇatayā pratīyete, na tarāṃ ca prakṛtāprakṛtayoriti tattvaṃ susūkṣmamokṣyate, tadā punaśleṣasya prakṛtāprakṛtagocarasyaivaitānyudāharaṇāni bhaviṣyantīti ||
yadvā tadbhāgatyāgalakṣaṇāśrayaṇādityataḥ paramapadāvadhikāgamanapūrvakaphaṇiśikharyāśrayaṇakartṛsvakīyaprabhākatvena prakṛtabhagavadviśeṣaṇatayā’rthātpratīyamāne prakṛtavyavahāre aprakṛtālaṃkārikādigatasya svābhyupagatabhāgatyāgalakṣaṇāśrayaṇahetukārthabodhakatāvattatvaṃ padadvayakatvasyāprakṛtavyavahārasya pratītirityabhiprāyeṇeha paramaṃ padamiti padyaṃ samāsoktyudāharaṇatāṃ prāpitam | samāsoktiśarīre praviṣṭasya viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvasya śābdatāniyamābhyupagame tūktarītyā śleṣasyaivedamudāharaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati | evaṃ padyāntareṣvapyūhyamiti ||
yathāvā
jagadānandakṛtastava nāmno grahaṇe phaṇādharagirīndo| puṃso’naghagatikārakapūrvasyāpi svayaṃ bhavedgrahaṇam || 768 ||
he anaghagatikāraka! divyagatidāyin ! phaṇādharagirīdo! jagadānandakṛtaḥ sakalalokāmodadāyinaḥ tava nāmnaḥ nārāyaṇavāsudevaviṣṇuhariprabhṛtināmadheyasya grahaṇe uccāraṇe satīti yāvat | pūrvasya ‘pūrveṣāmapi pūrvajaḥ’ ityuktarītyā akhilajagatkāraṇabhūtasya puṃsaḥ puruṣasya tavetyetadatrāpyanuṣajyate | tavāpi yogibhirapi durgrahasyāpīti bhāvaḥ | svayaṃ prayatnāntaranirapekṣameva grahaṇaṃ ‘upayāma gṛhītosi brahmaṇe tvā mahasa omityātmānaṃ yuñjīta’ ityuktarītyā ātmahavissamarpaṇoddeśyatvena vaśīkaraṇaṃ bhavet ||
omityekākṣaraṃ sākṣādvāsudevasya vācakam |
omityuccāraṇenaiva vācyamānīyate param ||
dvādaśākṣarayogena dūrasthaṃ tadihāntike |
smṛtamātre mahāmantre susūkṣme dvādaśākṣare ||
cittadarpaṇasaṃkrāntassusukhaṃ lakṣyate hariḥ|
ataśca dvādaśārṇena svādhyāyena janārdanaḥ ||
āsannatāṃ vrajatyāśu brahmaṇyarpitacetasām |
iti,
harirityavaśenāpi kīrtayennāma yo naraḥ|
kimanyaissādhanaiḥ kāryaṃ tasya vaśyo bhaveddhariḥ||
iti ca śāṇḍilyaparamadharmaśāstrabhagavacchāstrokteriti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe jagadānandakṛta ityatra jagadānandeti, anaghagatītyatra anagheti ca chedaḥ | he jagadānanda! lokānandakāraṇa! he anagha ! apahatapāpman! kutaḥ kṛtpratyayasya grahaṇe gatikārakapūrvasyāpi grahaṇaṃ bhavediti ‘kṛdgrahaṇe gatikārakapūrvasyāpi grahaṇam’ iti paribhāṣārtho darśitaḥ | tena ‘kartṛkaraṇe kṛtā bahulam’ iti samāsavidhāyake śāstre kṛdgrahaṇe nakhairbhinno nakhabhinna ityatreva nakhaiḥ nirbhinnaḥ hariṇā nakhanirbhinnaḥ ityādau gatikārakapūrveṇāpi kṛdantena samāso bhavatīti veditavyam | gatiratra prādigaṇapaṭhita upasargāparaparyāyaśśabdaḥ “upasargāḥ kriyāyoge, gatiśca’ ityanuśāsanāt | kārakāṇi ca karmakaraṇādīni suprasiddhāni | atra bhagavacchāstrīye vyavahāre śabdaśātrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
śirasaḥ parato nyaste padesamaste visarjanīyo’pyante | sattvaṃ bhajate bhagavannayogavāheṣu hanta gaṇito manujaḥ || 769 ||
he bhagavan ! yogaṃ vahantīti yogavāhāḥ, karmaṇyan | tena bhavantītyayogavāhāḥ tvaddhyānavidhurā ityarthaḥ | teṣu gaṇitopi ante visarjanīyaḥ sādhujanagoṣṭhyā bahiṣkāryo’pi manujaḥ pumān , samaste ityatra samaḥ te iti chedaḥ | te tava pade caraṇe śirasaḥ parataḥ śīrṣādūrdhvaṃ śīrṣe ityarthaḥ | vṛkṣāgre śyena ityasminnarthe vṛkṣāgrātparataśśyena ityapi prayogadarśanāt | nyaste nikṣipte sati samaḥ sādhussan ‘sarvasādhusamāneṣu samaṃ syādabhidheyavat’ iti medinī | ‘tulyo mitrāripakṣayoḥ’ ityuktadiśā sarvasamassanniti vā | yadvā asama iti chedaḥ | asadṛśassannityarthaḥ | satvaṃ bhajate |
mahānprabhurvai puruṣassattvasyaiṣa pravartakaḥ |
iti śrutyuktarītyā sattvapravartakabhagavaccaraṇāravindaśironidhānamahimnā balidānavendra iva sātvikatāṃ sato bhāvaṃ vā pratipadyata ityarthaḥ | pakṣe śirasaḥ śiraḥpadāt parataḥ paratra nyaste prayukte pade padaśabde samaste samāsaṃ prāpte sati | anyathā vakṣyamāṇaṃ satvaṃ na syāt ‘nityaṃ samāse’nuttarapadasthasya’ ityadhikārāt | ayogavāhāḥ anusvāravisargajihvāmūlīyopadhmānīyāḥ teṣāṃ bhagavatā patañjalinā tathā saṃjñāyā vidhānāt | teṣu gaṇitaḥ | ante śiraśśabdante vidyamānaḥ visarjanīyopi visargopi satvaṃ sakāratvaṃ bhajate ‘visarjanīyasya saḥ’ iti kharparakatvalakṣaṇasatvāpavādatayā ‘kupvoḥ kaḥpau ca’ iti prāptasya visarjanīyasya ‘adhaśśirasī pade’ ityanena satvavidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | atrāpi
śirasā pratigṛhṇāno devadevasya pādukām |
pāpopi sātviko bhūtvā yāti tadvaiṣṇavaṃ padam ||
iti bhagavacchāstrapratipādite vastuni śabdaśāstīyavyavahāra āropyate ||
yathāvā
kamalā vaiśampāyanakathanīyodārabhāgavatagītā | bhayamupaśamayati bhavināṃ priyaśāntyanuśāsanāhitodyogā || 770 ||
vai iti bhinnaṃ padam | śampānāṃ śatahradānāṃ ayane mārge kathanīyā ‘ādityavarṇe, hiraṇyavarṇām’ ityādiśrutiprathitavarṇatayā vidyuttulyeti varṇanīyeti yāvat | yadvā śampā dambholiḥ tasyāḥ ayanaḥ prāptā | śampayā īyata iti vā | sarvadhā’pi vajrī indra ityarthaḥ | ‘śampā vidyutpaviśśampā’ ityanekārthadhvanikośaḥ | tena kathanīyā,
namaste sindhusaṃbhūte namaste padmasambhave |
namassaroruhāvāse nārāyaṇakuṭumbini ||
iti,
namāmi sarvalokānāṃ jananīmabdhisaṃbhavām |
ityādinā ca lakṣmītantraviṣṇupurāṇādau devarājena stutetyarthaḥ | yadvā śampāyane indre kathanīyaṃ upadeṣṭavyaṃ lakṣmītantrādi yasyāssā tathoktā | lakṣmītantrasya lakṣmyā indrāyopadiṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe vaiśampāyanetyekaṃ padam | vaiśampāyanena paramarṣiṇā kathyata iti kathanīyā ‘tayoreva’ iti karmaṇyanīyar | janamejayāyopadiṣṭetyarthaḥ| udārabhāgavatagītā udārāḥ ‘udarāssarva evaite’ iti bhagavataiva praśastāḥ vadānyā ye bhāgavatāḥ bhagavadbhaktāḥ ‘caturvidhā bhajante māṃ janāssukṛtino’rjuna’ ityādinoktā ārtajijñāsvarthārthijñāninaḥ | te hi bhagavato yatkiṃcidapi gṛhṇanti tasmai tu sarvasvadāyina ityudāratā teṣām | tairgītā | pakṣe bhagavata iyaṃ bhāgavatī gītā bhagavadgītopaniṣadityarthaḥ | sā udārā yasyāṃ sā tathoktā | udāratvaṃ cāsyāḥ paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ ca paramapuruṣārthadātṛtvam | mahābhāratāntarvidyamānākhyāneṣu mahattvaṃ vā | ‘udāro dātṛmahatoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | priyasya svavallabhasya bhagavataḥ śāntiḥ āśritāparādhanirvarṇanodīrṇakrodhāvegapraśamanaṃ tasyāḥ yadanuśāsanaṃ vāllabhyātiśayenājñāpanaṃ tasmin āhitaḥ udyogaḥ yayā sā tathoktā |
piteva tvatpreyān janani paripūrṇāgasi jane
hitasrotovṛttyā bhavati ca kadācitkaluṣadhīḥ |
kimevaṃ nirdoṣaḥ ka iha jagatīti tvamucitaiḥ
upāyairvismārya svajanayasi mātā tadasi naḥ ||
ityādyuktarītyā bhagavanmānasaprasādanaikavyāpāreti bhāvaḥ |
atra
aparādhaikasaktānāmanarhāṇāṃ ciraṃ nṛṇām |
bharturāśrayaṇe pūrvaṃ svayaṃ puruṣakāratām ||
vāllabhyenānutiṣṭhantī vātsalyādyupabṛṃhaṇīm |
upāyasamaye bharturjñānaśaktyādivardhanīm ||
ityādikaṃ ca smartavyam | pakṣe priye śṛṇvatāṃ prītijanake śāntiścānuśāsanaṃ ceti parvaṇī yasyāṃ sā tathoktā | hitaḥ śreyaḥpradaḥ udyoga iti parva yasyāṃ sā tathoktā ca | kamalā śrīḥ bhavināṃ saṃsāriṇāṃ bhavabhayaṃ śamayati | atra pañcarātrādiśāstrīye vyavahāre prastute bhāratasaṃhitāvyavahārasyāropa iti viśeṣaḥ |
yathāvā
suprathatejasi jagatāṃ prathame divyasvabhā vaśī bhāvaḥ | tvayi bhātyacyuta sutarāmasarvasādhāraṇenityaḥ || 771 ||
divyā ramaṇīyā svabhāḥ ‘na tatra sūryo bhāti’ ityā dinā ‘tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhāti’ ityuktaprakārā dīptiḥ yasya tasya saṃbuddhiḥ he divyasvabhāḥ! he acyuta ! śobhanā prathā yasya tattathoktaṃ tejaḥ yasya tasmin suprathatejasi | tejo nāma asvādhīnasahakāryanapekṣatvam | taduktaṃ ‘tejastvananyāpekṣatā’ iti |
sahakāryanapekṣā me sarvakāryāvidhau hi yā |
tejaṣṣaṣṭhaṃ guṇaṃ prāhustamimaṃ tattvavedinaḥ ||
iti ca, yadvā
jyotīṃṣyādityavadrājankurūn pracchādayan śriyā |
iti nidarśitaṃ parābhibhavasāmarthyaṃ,
parābhibhavasāmarthyaṃ tejaḥ kecitpracakṣate |
ityukteḥ | jagatāṃ prathame kāraṇe ityarthaḥ | etairviśeṣaṇaiḥ ‘mahāśaktirmahādyutiḥ, mahātejā mahoragaḥ, ojastejodyutidharaḥ, siddhissarvādiracyutaḥ’ ityādīni smāritāni | ata eva asarvasādhāraṇe sakalalokavilakṣaṇe tvayi nityaḥ śāśvataḥ vaśī vaśaḥ prabhutvamasyāstīti vaśī bhāvassattā prabhutvavaiśiṣṭyena sthitirityarthaḥ | bhāti ‘sarvasya vaśī’ ityetadatrābhisaṃhitam | yadvā vaśī prābhavavān bhāvaḥ kriyā vibhūtirvā | athavā vaśo janma tadasyāstīti vaśī avaśinaḥ vaśinassaṃpadyamānasya bhāvaḥ vaśībhāvaḥ, abhūtatadbhāve cviḥ | anitya iti chedaḥ | anityaḥ akarmavaśyatvenecchāparigṛhītatayā kādācitka iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā bhāvaḥ vaśīti yojanā | bhāvaḥ janma | vaśo’syāstīti vaśī | vaśa icchā aicchika ityarthaḥ | abhiprāyastu pūrvavadeva | atra ‘icchāgṛhītābhimatorudehaḥ’ ityādikamanusaṃdheyam | ata eva anityaḥ ukto’rthaḥ | yadvā jagatāmityetadatraiva saṃbadhyate | jagatāṃ vaśībhāvaḥ āyattatvaṃ ‘vaśo janmaspṛhāyatteṣvayattatvaprabhutvayoḥ’ iti hemacandraḥ| sutarāṃ bhāti prakāśate | asminpakṣe prathametyasyaiva sarvajagadādikāraṇetyarthaḥ ||
jagadvaśe vartatedaṃ kṛṣṇasya sacarācaram |
ityetadatra smartavyam |
bhāvassattāsvabhāvābhiprāyaceṣṭātmajanmasu |
kriyālīlāpadārtheṣu vibhūtibudhajantuṣu ||
iti medinī | uktaprakāre tvayyadhīnamakhilaṃ jagadityabhiprāyaḥ |
pakṣe suprathate iti divyasvabhāveti ca chedaḥ | he divyasvabhāva! he acyuta! bhāti prakāśamāne prathame prathamaśabde asarvasādhāraṇe sarvaśabdatulyatvenāvidyamāne’pi ‘sādhāraṇassamānaśca’ iti tulyaparyāyeṣvamaraḥ | apiśabdaḥ ‘na dvandvaduḥkhamiha kiṃcit’ ityatreva sāmarthyāllabhyate |sarvādigaṇāpaṭhite’pīti yāvat | jasi prathamābahuvacane śībhāvaḥ śī ityākārakādeśaḥ anitya iti chedaḥ | anityaḥ vikalpitaḥ jagatāṃ sutarāṃ prathate suṣṭhu prasiddhyati “prathamacaramataya’ iti sūtreṇāsarvanāmnopi prathamaśabdasya jasyeva śībhāvasya vaikalpikatayā’nuśāsanāditi bhāvaḥ | atra sahasranāmādhyāyādiprasiddhavyavahāre śabdaśāstrasiddhavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
bahudhāmāsavrajabhūrmalimluco jātu yo niṣiddhopi | kārayatu sa māṃ śaurissvasminnāvaśyakaṃ śubhaṃ karma || 772 ||
bahudhāmā amitatejāḥ yaḥ śauriḥ jātu kadācit vrajabhūḥ | nandavrajajanmā san niṣiddhopi yaśodādibhirvāritopi malimlucaḥ dugdhadadhinavanītādistenaḥ āsa abhūt | tiṅantapratirūpakamavyayam | saḥ śauriḥ svasmin svaviṣaye āvaśyakaṃ avaśyakartavyaṃ śubhaṃ karma
na viṣṇvārādhanātpuṇyaṃ vidyate karma vaidikam |
ityādyuktamārādhanādikaṃ māṃ kārayatu `hṛkroranyatarasyām’ iti dvikarmakatvam | pakṣe bahudhā bahuvāramiti yāvat| yadvā bahuprakāreṇa caitrādyāśvināntamāseṣvaniyamenetyarthaḥ | teṣveva māseṣvadhikamāsasaṃbhavāt | māsānāṃ cāndramāsānāṃ vrajo nivahaḥ ‘goṣṭhādhvanivahā vrajāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | tasmin bhavatīti māsavrajabhūḥ |
dvātriṃśadbhirgatairmāsairdinaiṣṣoḍaśabhistathā |
ghaṭikānāṃ catuṣkeṇa bhavatyadhikamāsakaḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt | yaḥ malimlucaḥ
eṣa ṣaṣṭidino māsaḥ pūrvārdhastu malimlucaḥ |
ityuktalakṣaṇaḥ adhimāsa ityarthaḥ | malimlucatvaṃ cāsya
vatsarāntargataḥ pāpo yajñānāṃ phalanāśakṛt |
nairṛtairyātudhānaiśca samākrānto vināśakaiḥ ||
malimlucaissamākrāntassūryasaṃkrāntivarjitaḥ |
malimlucaṃ taṃ jānīyādgarhitaṃ sarvakarmasu |
iti śatātapoktarītyā taskarairyātudhānairākrāntatvāditi dhyeyam |
malimluco māsabhede corajvalanayoḥ pumān |
iti medinī | saḥ niṣiddhopi
malimlucastuṃ yo māsassa māsaḥ pāpasaṃjñitaḥ |
tyaktvā tamuttare kuryātpitṛdevādikāḥ kriyāḥ ||
varjitaḥ pitṛdevābhyāṃ sa māsaḥ pāpasaṃjñitaḥ |
iti smaraṇāt | jātu kadācit sati saṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ |
svasmin āvaśyakaṃ sīmantonnayanādikaṃ pratiniyatakālamavaśyakartavyaṃ śubhaṃ maṅgalaṃ karma māṃ kārayatu |
māsaprokteṣu kāryeṣu na moḍhyaṃ guruśukrayoḥ |
adhimāsādidoṣāśca na syuḥ kālavidherbalāt ||
iti smaraṇāt | atra purāṇavṛttānte prastute aprastutadharmaśāstrīyavyavahārāropa iti pūrvebhyo viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
tvayi sakalaśubhagaṇāsmayamātābhānasajarālagantīśa | tvacchandasthitibhājāṃ bhāti śubhaṃ śrīprabhādivṛttaṃ ca || 773 ||
atra śrutītihāsapurāṇādiśāstrīye vyavahāre chandaśśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ | tathāhi sakalaśubhagaṇā asmayeti chedaḥ | avidyamānaḥ smayaḥ garvaḥ sa tathoktaḥ asmayaḥ ‘smayo nā’dbhutagarvayoḥ, smayo garve’dbhute’ iti medinīviśvau | ‘na dṛpto na ca matsarī | anuddhatamanā vidvān’ iti śrīrāmāyaṇokteḥ | tasya saṃbuddhiḥ, avidyamānaḥ smayo vismayo yasyeti vā ‘vīryabānna ca vīryeṇa mahatā svena vismitaḥ’ iti tatraivokteḥ | yadvā avidyamānaḥ smayaḥ āścaryakaraḥ padārthaḥ yasmātsa iti anuttamādivatpañmībahuvīhiḥ |
yatrādbhutāni sarvāṇi bhūmau viyati vā jale |
taṃ tvā nu paśyato brahmankiṃ me iṣṭamihādbhutam ||
ityakrūroktaprakāreṇa sarvāścaryānidhe ityarthaḥ | smaya ityeva chitvā he smayeti saṃbodhanaṃ vā | arthastūkta eva ‘anekādbhatadarśanam’ ityādyapyatrānusaṃdheyam | he īśa! sarveśvara! ‘kṣarātmā nāvīśate deva ekaḥ, vyaktāvyaktaṃ bharate viśvamīśaḥ, eṣa sarveśvaraḥ’ ityādiśruteḥ | tvayi lagantī vyatiṣajantī śubhānāṃ kalyāṇānāṃ gaṇāḥ nivahāḥ sakalāssamagrāḥ śubhagaṇā yasyāssā tathoktā | mātā asya jagatāmityādiḥ | yadvā anaupādhikanirdeśena śabdaśakterasaṃkocānmatetyasyaivākhilajagajjananītyarthaḥ | ābhā iti chedaḥ| ā samantāt bhātītyābhāḥ | bhāteḥ kartari kvip | viśvavyāpinītyarthaḥ ||
yathā mayā jagadvyāptaṃ svarūpeṇa svabhāvataḥ|
tayā vyāptamidaṃ sarvaṃ niyantrī ca tatheśvarī ||
iti viṣvaksenasaṃhitoktaḥ | yadvā ā samantāt bhāḥ prabhā yasyāssā | viśvatomukhadyutirityarthaḥ | ‘candrāṃ prabhāsāṃ, jvalantīṃ tṛptām’ ityādiśruteḥ | jarayā saha vartata iti sajarā sā na bhavatīti nasajarā | naśabdena supsupeti samāsaḥ ‘jarāṃ mṛtyumatyeti, vijaro vimṛtyuḥ’ ityādiśrutyuktarītyā bhagavata ivāsyā api jarātītatvādyakhilaheyapratyanīkatāyā avyāhatatvāt | ata evoktaṃ lakṣmītantre 
kāntasya devadevasya viṣṇossadguṇaśālinaḥ |
dayitā’haṃ sadā devī jñānānandamayī parā ||
anavadyā’navadyāṅgī nityaṃ taddharmadharmiṇī ||
iti,
nityanirdoṣanissīmakalyāṇaguṇaśālinī |
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī nāma sā sattā vaiṣṇavī parā ||
ityādi ca | yadvā mātā abhā na sajarā iti chedaḥ | netyetanmadhyamaṇinyāyenobhayato’nvayi | abhā niṣprabhā na, kiṃtu saprabhaiva | pratiṣedhadvayena kādācitkaniṣprabhātvasaṃbhāvanā’pi vyudasyate | ‘saṃbhāvyaniṣadhanivartane dvau pratiṣedhau’ ita cāmanaḥ | sajarā ca na | ukta evārthaḥ | bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | asterbhavantiparasyāprayujyamānasyāpyastitvabhāṣaṇāt | avyāhatamahāvibhūtiviśiṣṭastvamiti tātparyārthaḥ | ata eva tvacchandasthitibhājāṃ tava chandaḥ abhiprāyaḥ ‘abhiprāyavaśau chandau’ ityamaraḥ | tasmin sthitibhājāṃ ‘tvadīyagambhīramano’nusāriṇaḥ’ itivattvadabhiprāyānatilaṅghināmityarthaḥ | yadvā tvadadhīnatāsthitiśālināmityarthaḥ | tvaccheṣabhūtānāṃ nṛṇāmiti bhāvaḥ | śubhaṃ kalyāṇaṃ | śrīḥ saṃpat, prabhā tejaḥ te ādī yasya tat | ādiśavdena yaśaādīnāṃ grahaṇam | vṛttaṃ tadanurūpaṃ caritraṃ ca bhāti prakāśate ‘kimalabhyaṃ bhagavati prasanne śrīniketane, sakalaphalaprado hi viṣṇuḥ’ ityakhilaphalapradātṛbhagavaccittānuvartināmīdṛśaviśeṣo na durlabha iti bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣe sakalaśubhagaṇāḥ sma yamātābhānasajarāḥ iti chedaḥ | he īśa! tvayi yamātābhānasajarāḥ atra mādivarṇeṣu dīrgho’pi yādivarṇeṣvakāra ivoccāraṇasaukaryārtha eva | yamatabhanasajarāḥ yādisaṃjñakāḥ sakalāśśubhāśca gaṇāḥ
sarvagurmo mukhāntarlau yarāvantagalau satau |
madhyādyau jbhau trilau no’ṣṭau bhavantyatra gaṇāstrikāḥ ||
iti chandaśśāstraprasiddhā gaṇāḥ varṇatrayātmakāḥ laganti sma lagnā bhavanti sma | tvadbuddhisthā abhavanniti yāvat | asyāmānupūrvyāṃ yakārādivarṇatritaye yathā ādilaghuryagaṇo niṣpadyate | evaṃ ekaikavarṇāpāyena mā ityādau varṇatritayatritaye sarvagururmagaṇa antyalaghustagaṇaḥ ādigururbhagaṇaḥ sarvalaghurnagaṇaḥ antyagurussagaṇaḥ madhyagururjagaṇaḥ madhyalaghū ragaṇaścetyaṣṭau gaṇā niṣpadyante | te cādimavarṇaistattatsaṃjñakā iti jñāpyante | evaṃ lagantītyatra laghurguruśca jñāpyete ityavahitahṛdayairdraṣṭavyam | tvacchandasthitibhājāṃ tava chandāṃsi uktāyuktādīni chandāsi teṣu sthitibhājāṃ tadviṣayakajñānavatāmityarthaḥ | śubhaṃ svanāmnaiva maṅgalaṃ śrīprabhādivṛttaṃ, śrīḥ uktāyāṃ ‘gśrīḥ’ ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ, prabhā jagatyāṃ ‘svaraśaraviratirnanau rau prabhā’ ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ ca vṛttaṃ te ādī yasya tat | vṛttaṃ padyaṃ jātyekavacanaṃ bhāti | cakāraḥ pūrvoktasamuccayārthakaḥ |
yathāvā
sarvagurucaraṇamāptvā’’dyagurupadādhassakhe laghunyāsāttvam | śeṣamuparīva darśaya gurumūne śaśvadakhilalaghu tadbhavati || 774 ||
he sakhe! sarveṣāṃ gurūṇāṃ svācāryaprabhṛtibhagavadavadhikānāmācāryāṇāṃ caraṇaṃ jātyekavacanam | āptvā prapadya ādyagurupadādhaḥ ādyasya guroḥ bhagavataḥ ‘kamapyādyaṃ guruṃ vanda’ ityukteḥ | padayoḥ adhaḥ ‘tvatpādamūlaṃ śaraṇaṃ’ ityādyuktarītyā caraṇamūle ityarthaḥ | laghu kṣipraṃ yathā syāttathā nyāsāt ātma rakṣābharasamarpaṇāt nāsminnarthe vilambitavyam ||
mahatā puṇyapaṇyena krīteyaṃ kāyanaustvayā |
prāptuṃ dukhāmbudheḥ pāraṃ tvara yāvanna bhidyate ||
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ | yadvā laghunyāsāt bhaktyapekṣayā laghūpāyarūpātprapadanāt prapadanaṃ kṛtvetyarthaḥ | lyablope karmaṇi pañcamī | evaṃ pūrvasminnarthe’pīti dhyeyam | atra ‘gurumukhācchrīśaṃ prapadya’ ityukto’rtho’nusaṃhitaḥ | uparīva ādau pradarśite gurucaraṇa iva prathamaguroścaraṇe’pīti bhāvaḥ | svamiti śeṣaḥ | ātmānamityarthaḥ | śeṣaṃ śeṣabhūtaṃ paragatātiśayādhānecchayā upādeyasvarūpaṃ darśaya | yadvā upari sakalalokopari vidyamāne akhilajagadīśvare bhagavatītyarthaḥ | tasminniva sarvagurucaraṇe’pīti bhāvaḥ | śeṣaṃ darśayeti yojyam | athavā uparītyasya sakalalokaśirodhārye bhagavaccaraṇa ivatyarthaḥ | anyattulyam |
yasya deve parā bhaktiryathā deve tathā gurau ||
ityādiśruteḥ | ūne upāyānuṣṭhānasamayabhāvyaṅgādivaiguṇye saṃbhāvite sati | śaśvat guruṃ svācāryaṃ darśayetyetanmadhyamaṇinyāyenātrāpi saṃbadhyate |
pitāmahaṃ nāthamuniṃ vilokya prasīda madvṛttamacintayitvā |
ityādyuktarītyā bhagavatprasādanāyācāryaṃ darśayeti bhāvaḥ | tat tasmāt uktaprakāreṇānuṣṭhānāt akhilaṃ ca tat laghu ca akhilalaghu niśśreyasaparyantasakalābhiṣṭaṃ bhavati siddhyati ‘triṣviṣṭe’lpe laghuḥ’ ityamaraḥ | iṣṭe’rthe 
darśanena laghunā yathā tayoḥ prītimāpurubhayostapasvinaḥ |
iti prayogaśca | laghunā iṣṭeneti vivṛtam | yadā |
kṛṣṇāguruṇi śīghre ca laghu klībegurau triṣu |
nissāre ca manojñe ca pṛkkāyāṃ ca laghu striyām ||
ityādirabhasādikośaikarasyāyeṣṭaśabdo’marakośagato manojñavācī tyāśrīyate, tadā akhilalaghu ityasya sarvamanojñamityarthaḥ | tadidaṃ guruparaṃparāśaraṇavaraṇapūrvakabhagavaccaraṇāravindaśaraṇāgatyādikakhilamapi nyāsavidyāniṣṇātānāṃ sarveṣāṃ manoharaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ||
pakṣe chandaśśāstrīyaprastārakramo darśitaḥ | tathāhi he sakhe! tvaṃ sarve guravaḥ guruvarṇajñāpakavakrarekhāviśeṣāḥ yasmiṃstatsarvaguru | caraṇaṃ padyacaturthāṃśaṃ āptvā uktavidharekhābhirlikhitveti yāvat | ādyagurupadādhaḥ ādyaṃ prathamaṃ gurupadaṃ guruvarṇacihnaṃ tajjñāpakavakrarekhāviśeṣa ityarthaḥ | ‘padaṃ vyavasitatrāṇasthānalakṣmāṅghrivastuṣu’ ityamaraḥ | tasya adhaḥ adhastā | laghoḥ laghuvarṇajñāpakaṛjurekhāviśeṣasya nyāsāt tallekhanaṃ kṛtvā | pūrvavatpañcamī | śeṣaṃ avaśiṣṭaṃ uparīva uparitanapaṅktāviva | darśaya likheti yāvat | ūne upariguruvarṇajñāpakarekhāhīne laghuvarṇasūcakarekhāsadbhāve iti bhāvaḥ | guruṃ guruvarṇasūcakavakrarekhāviśeṣaṃ darśayetyatrāpyanveti | evaṃ śaśvat punaḥpunaḥ darśaya | evaṃ sati tat caraṇaṃ akhilalaghu akhilāḥ laghavaḥ laghuvarṇajñāpakarekhāviśeṣāḥ yasya tattathoktaṃ bhavati iti,
pāde sarvagurāvādyālaghuṃ nyasya guroradha |
yathopari tathā śeṣaṃ bhūyaḥ kuryādamuṃ vidhim ||
ūne dadyādgurūneva yāvatsarvalaghurbhavet |
prastāro’yaṃ samākhyātaśchandovicitivedibhiḥ ||
iti ślokārthassarvopyanusandheyaḥ | tathāhi tadarthaḥ sarveṣāṃ chandasābhādyaṃ vṛttaṃ sarvaguru antyaṃ sarvalaghu ataḥ pādopi tādṛśa eva evaṃ sati sarvagurau pāde chandonurūpatayā ardhacandrākṛtivakrarekhābhirlikhite satīti yāvat | ādeḥ guroḥ adhaḥ ādyavakrarekhāyā adhastāt dvitīyapaṅktau laghuṃ ṛjurekhāṃ nyasya likhitvā yathopari laghurekhāyāḥ paratra sthāne dakṣiṇapārśve uparipaṅktau yadvat gurulaghujñāpakarekhāssanti tathā tena prakāreṇaiva śeṣaṃ avaśiṣṭagurulaghurekhāḥ pūrvapaṅktyavasānaparyantaṃ nyasya bhūyaḥ punarapi tadadhaḥ tṛtīyapaṅktyādau amuṃ vidhiṃ āderguroradha ityādikaṃ kuryāt likhet dvitīyapaṅktau ādau gurvabhāvāddvitīyasthānagata evādyo gururbhavati | evaṃ tṛtīyādau tṛtīyādisthānagata evādya iti dhyeyam | ūne kramaprāptalekhanahīne laghurekhāyāḥ pūrvatra sthāne vāmabhāge gurūneva vakrarekhā eva dadyāt likhet| evaṃ kiyantamavadhiṃ likhedityata āha yāvaditi | yāvatā sarvalaghuḥ pādo bhavet tāvadayaṃ vidhiriti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ evaṃcāsminnudāharaṇapadye atyuktācchandaḥprastārassarvopi pradarśita ityavadheyam | tatra hi prathamaṃ vṛttaṃ dviguru dvitīyaṃ laghugurvātmakaṃ tṛtīyaṃ gurulaghvātmakaṃ caturthaṃ sarvalaghu iti catvāri vṛttāni bhavantīti | atrāpi pūrvavadevāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
avisarjanīyasattvaṃ svāntastava darśanena yo vindeta | vācaspatissa bhagavaṃśchandasi sādhuḥ prayogato loke’pi || 775 ||
he bhagavan! tava darśanena avalokanena yaḥ pumān svasya antaḥ manasi avisarjanīyaṃ aparityājyaṃ naisargikamityarthaḥ | sattvaṃ sattvaguṇaṃ vindeta
jāyamānaṃ hi puruṣaṃ yaṃ paśyenmadhusūdanaḥ |
sāttvikassa hi vijñeyassa vai mokṣārthacintakaḥ ||
iti mokṣadharmokteriti bhāvaḥ | sa pumān vācaspatiḥ vāgmī san ‘vāgīśo vākpatissamau’ ityamaraḥ| bṛhaspatitulya iti vā ‘jīva āṅgiraso vācaspatiḥ’ ityamaraḥ | chandasi abhilāṣe sādhuḥ sādhvabhilāṣa ityarthaḥ | kṣodiṣṭaprayojanānabhilāṣuka iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā chandasi vede sādhuḥ adhītasāṅgaśiraskavedatayā pūjya iti bhāvaḥ | ‘sādhunipuṇābhyāmarcāyāṃ saptamyaprateḥ’ iti sādhuśabdayoge chandaśśabdātsaptamī ||
chandaḥ padye ca vede ca svairācārābhilāṣayoḥ |
iti medinī | prayogataḥ prakṛṣṭo vijātīyapratyayāsaṃvalitatayā śreṣṭhaḥ yogaḥ dhyānaṃ tasmāt loke’pi sādhussabhyaḥ mokṣārthacintakatayā upāsanīya iti yāvat | bhavati |
sa vedaitatparaṃ brahmadhāma yatra viśvaṃ nihitaṃ bhāta śubhram |
upāsate puruṣaṃ ye hyakāmāste śukrametadativartanti dhīrāḥ ||
iti brahmavidupāsakānāṃ janmavaidhuryaśravaṇāditi bhāvaḥ | ‘sādhustriṣūcite cārau sabhye vārdhuṣike’pi nā’ iti ratnamālā |
pakṣe tava darśanena śāstreṇa vyākaraṇenetyarthaḥ ‘darśanaṃ darpaṇasvapnabuddhiśāstrākṣidṛṣṭiṣu’ iti ratnamālā | yaḥ vācaspatiśabdaḥ strāntaḥ svamadhye avisarjanīyaḥ visargaśunyaḥ visargasthānika iti yāvat | saḥ sakāro yasya sa tathoktaḥ tasya bhāvaṃ avisarjanīyasattvaṃ vindeta | ‘ṣaṣṭhyāḥ patiputrapārapadapayaspoṣeṣu’ iti ṣaṣṭhīvisargasya sakārādeśavidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | sa vācaspatiḥ vācaspatiśabdaḥ chandasi vede sādhuḥ prayogārhaḥ | udāhṛtasūtreṇa sakārasya chandasyeva vidhānāt | loke’pi prayogataḥ ‘vaktā vācaspatiryathā’ iyādiprāmāṇikaprayogabhūmnā sādhurbhavati, yathā chandomātraviṣayopi paripanthyādiśabda iti bhāvaḥ | atra śrutītihāsādiśāstrīyavyavahāre śabdaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ || yathāvā
bahudhātvātmā vindennātheṣadduritasūpapadatāṃ cet | na khalu na khalāśritassyātpratyayitassopi darśanena tava || 776 ||
he nātha ! ātmā pratyagātmā | īṣat alpaṃ duritena vṛjinena sūpapadatāṃ sukhena upapadyata iti sūpapadaḥ| vakṣyamāṇaḥ khalpratyayaḥ | tasya bhāvaṃ iyāccet yatkiñcidduṣkṛtopapannaścedityarthaḥ | bahudhā bahuprakāreṇa prāyeṇeti yāvat | khalāśritaḥ durjanasaṃsṛṣṭaḥ na syāditi tu na khalu | syādeveti bhāvaḥ | tuśabdo’vadhāraṇe, khalviti jijñāsāyāṃ vākyālaṅkāre vā | ‘niṣedhavākyālaṅkārajijñāsānunaye khalu’ ityamaraḥ | duritamūlako hi khalasaṃsargaḥ | yathocyate 
aho nu khalasaṃsargaḥ pāpānāṃ mahatāṃ phalam |
iti | sopi evaṃ khalasaṃsargavidūṣitopi tava darśanena yādṛcchikādisukṛtaparipākasulabhena tavāvalokanena pratyayitaḥ saṃjātapratyayaḥ jñānavānityarthaḥ | itac | syādityetadatrāpi saṃbadhyate |
ajñānaṃ naśyati jñānamudeti haridarśanāt |
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe bahuḥ bahuprakāraḥ dhātūnāṃ bhvādīnāṃ ātmā svarūpaṃ īṣaddurbhāṃ śabdābhyāṃ itaḥ suḥ suityākārakaśśabdaḥ upapadaṃ upoccāritaṃ padaṃ ‘tatropapadaṃ saptamīstham’ iti saṃketitaṃ yasya saḥ tathoktaḥ | tasya bhāvaḥ īṣaddussūpapadatetyarthaḥ | tām | yadvā īṣaddurbhāṃ upapadabhūtābhyāmiti bhāvaḥ | itaḥ yuktaḥ suḥ upapadaṃ yasya saḥ sūpapadaḥ īṣadduritaścāsau sūpapadaśceti viśeṣaṇobhayapadakarmadhārayaḥ | tatastal | tattāṃ ityāccet | sopītyatra śrutaḥ apiśabdo bhinnakramaḥ | prayayita ityanena saṃbadhyate | saṃjātapratyayopi | cedityetadatrāpyanuṣajyate | pratyayānto’pi bhavedyadītyarthaḥ | khalā khalpratyayena śrito na syāditi na | yadi yaḥ kaścidapi vā dhāturīṣaddussūpapadatayā vihitapratyayassyāttarhi ‘īṣaddussuṣu kṛcchrākṛcchrārtheṣu khal’ itīṣadādyupapadakāddhātoḥ khalvidhānādīṣatkaraḥ duṣkaraḥ sukara itivat khalpratyayānto bhavedeveti bhāvaḥ | udāharaṇamapi sūpapadatāmityatreva diṅmātratayā pradarśitamiti veditavyam | atrāpi pūrvavadevāropo’nusandheyaḥ ||
yathā
saṃkucito’ñjalirayamiti na gṛhāṇa kadā’pi nanu phaṇinagendo | dṛṣṭvā’sāmarthyamiha grāhyo’ñja lireṣa hi sphuṭohome || 777 ||
nanu phaṇinagendo ! ayaṃ mayā kṛtaḥ añjaliḥ saṃkucitaḥ saṃkṣiptaḥ yathāśāstramakṛtatvādapūrṇa iti na gṛhāṇa | mā jñāsīḥ | mopekṣiṣṭhā iti bhāvaḥ | iha me mama asāmarthyaṃ yathāvadañjalikaraṇāśaktatāṃ dṛṣṭvā sphuṭaḥ ayathāvadanuṣṭhitatayā vyaktaḥ eṣaḥ yathātathātrā’pi kṛta iti bhāvaḥ | añjaliḥ ‘añjaliḥ paramā mudrā kṣipraṃ devaprasādinī’ ityuktastvatprasādanāśukārimudrāviśeṣaḥ anyonyābhimukhamilitaprasṛtidvitayalakṣaṇaḥ ‘pāṇirnikubjaḥ prasṛtistau yutāvañjaliḥ pumān’ ityamaraḥ | ekavacanena sakṛtkṛtopyayaṃ tvayyekāñjalikiṃkare ‘ ityuktarītyā’’śritavaśaṃvadena paramakāruṇikasārvabhaumeṇa etāvadvyājalābhamapi mahattaralābhaṃ manvānena tvayā’nudāsitavya iti dyotyate | grāhyāḥ, hītyavadhāraṇe, sādaraṃ tvayā svīkārya eva | aho svāśaktatā bhagavatparamakāruṇikatānucintanajanite harṣe āścarye vā | ayaṃ cāñjaliḥ prayojanāntaraparasya tattatprayojananiṣpādakaḥ | ananyaprayojanasya tu niśśreyasaparyantaparamapuruṣārthaprada iti draṣṭavyam | atra
tvadaṅghrimuddiśya kadā’pi kenacit
yathātathāvā’pi sakṛtkṛto’ñjaliḥ |
tadaiva muṣṇātyaśubhānyaśeṣataḥ
śubhāni puṣṇāti na jātu hīyate ||
hastīśaduḥkhaviṣadigdhaphalānubandhinyābrahmakīṭamaparāhatasaṃprayoge |
duṣkarmasaṃcayavaśādduratikrame naḥ pratyastramañjalirasau tava nigrahāstre ||
ityādyukto’ñjaliprabhāvo’nusandheyaḥ |
pakṣe sphuṭohome ityatra sphuṭaḥ home iti chedaḥ | home ‘añjalinā juhuyāt’ iti vakṣyamāṇāñjalikaraṇakahome ayaṃ añjaliḥ vihito’ñjaliḥ saṃkucitaḥ mukulita iti na gṛhāṇeti kaṃcidbubhutsuṃ pratyupadeśaḥ | kiṃtu iha home sāmarthyaṃ dṛṣṭvā paryālocya eṣaḥ vihitaḥ añjaliḥ sphuṭaḥ vyākoca iti grāhyaḥ ‘sphuṭo vyaktapraphullayoḥ’ iti medinī | atra pūrvamīmāṃsāprathamādhyāyāntimasāmarthyādhikaraṇārtha udapāditaḥ | tatra hi añjalinā saktūnpradāvye juhuyāt’ ityatra kiṃ devatāprasādaprārthanāyāmiva hastasaṃyogamātramañjalirhome grāhyaḥ, yathāsāmarthyaṃ vyākoca eva vā | iti saṃśayya, tatra viśeṣāśravaṇāt aniyama iti pūrvapakṣaṃ prāpayya, aśakyārthavidhyasaṃbhavādavaśyaṃvidhāyakasya śaktissahakāriṇyaṅgīkāryeti śaktisahāyo vidhireva yathāsāmarthyaṃ vidheyaṃ vyavasthāpayati | tasmādvyākocasya saktuṣvarthāpattivaśādvyavastheti siddhāntitam | sāmarthyaṃ nāma kāraṇatvam
sāmarthyaṃ syātkāraṇatvaṃ matto’yaṃ madhunā pikaḥ |
iti sodāharaṇalakṣaṇāt | yathā kokilamadakāraṇatvarūpasāmarthye na makarandādisādhāraṇasya madhuśabdasya vasantaikadeśe caitramāse’bhidhā niyamyate, tathā’trāpi homasādhanatārūpasāmarthyena saṃkucitavyākocasādhāraṇasyāñjaliśabdasya vyākocāñjalāvabhidhā niyamyata ityavadheyam | atra pañcarātrāgamādivyavahāre karmamīmāṃsāvyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
sevyātithivārārcāsubhagā’naghayogakaraṇanipuṇātmā | bhaktatatistvāmañcati pañcāṅgaprapadanena paramātman || 778 ||
he paramātman! sevyāḥ
sevyāśśreyo’rthibhissantaḥ puṇyatīrthaphalopamāḥ |
kṣaṇopāsanayogopi na teṣāṃ niṣphalo bhavet ||
iti cyavananahuṣasaṃvādoktaprakāraguṇavattyā sevanīyāḥ ye athithayaḥ teṣāṃ vārāḥ vyūhāḥ teṣāṃ arcayā pūjayā subhagā śobhanaśrīḥ śobhanakīrtirvā |
dūrādhvopagataṃ śrāntaṃ vaiśvadeva upasthitam |
atithiṃ taṃ vijānīyānnātithiḥ pūrvamāgataḥ ||
ekagrāmīṇamatithiṃ na gṛhṇīta kadācana |
anityamāgato yasmāttasmādatithirucyate ||
atithiṃ tatra saṃprāptaṃ pūjayetsvāgatādinā |
ityādiparāśarādyuktalakṣaṇātithisatkāraprāptasaubhāgyetyarthaḥ ||
pakṣe sevyeti bhinnaṃ padam | jātakādiphalajijñāsubhissevanīyā | tithayaḥ pratipadādyāḥ vārāḥ bhānucandrādivāsarāḥ | arcatītyarcā pacādyac | tithivārāṇāmarcā pūjayitrītyarthaḥ | idamapi bhinnaṃ padaṃ ‘vārassūryādidivase bṛndāvasarayorapi’ iti ratnamālā | suṣṭhu bhāni aśvinyādīnyuḍūni gacchati jānātīti subhagā | anaghaḥ bhagavadekaviṣayatayā niravadyaḥ yogaḥ dhyānaṃ tasya karaṇe nipuṇātmā kuśalamatiḥ | anyatra he anagheti bhagavato viśeṣaṇam | yogāḥ viṣkambhādayaḥ | karaṇāni bavabālavādīni teṣu nipuṇātmā tatsvarūpaguṇādiparikalanaśikṣitadhīrityarthaḥ |
yogo’pūrvārthasaṃprāptau saṃgatidhyānayuktiṣu |
vapussthairye prayoge ca viṣkambhādiṣu bheṣaje || iti,
karaṇamiha bavādau kāraṇe kṣetramukhye
vapuṣi ca rathabandhe karmagītendriyeṣu |
iti ca medinīratnamāle | anena viśeṣaṇatrayeṇa tithivāratārayogakaraṇanirūpaṇalakṣaṇapaścāṅgakaraṇanaipuṇī sūcitā | īdṛśī bhaktatatiḥ bhaktaśabdenātra sādhanabhaktiniṣṭhāḥ phalabhaktiniṣṭhā vā vivakṣitāḥ ‘phalasādhanabhaktibhyāṃ bhaktāvapi ca darśitāḥ’ ityubhayorapi bhaktaśabdavyapadeśyatvasyācāryeranugṛhītatvāt | pañca aṅgāni ‘anukūlyasya saṃkalpaḥ’ ityuktāni ānukūlyasaṃkalpaprātikūvyavarjanamahāviśvāsagoptṛtvavaraṇakārpaṇyākhyāni yasya tatprapadane aṅgarūpaṃ svatantrarūpaṃ vā bharanyasanaṃ tena upāyabhūtena tvāṃ añcatiṃ ārādhayati
nikṣepāparaparyāyo nyāsaḥ pañcāṅgasaṃyutaḥ|
prapattiṃ tāṃ prayuñjīta svāṅgaiḥ pañcabhirāvṛtām ||
ityādibhagavacchāstrokteḥ,
ānukūlyasya saṃkalpaḥ prātikūlyasya varjanam |
rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāso goptṛtvavaraṇaṃ tathā ||
ātmanikṣepakārpaṇye ṣaḍvidhā śaraṇāgatiḥ |
iti prapatteṣṣāḍvidhyoktistu aṅgāṅgisamuccayābhiprāyā | ‘ṣaḍaṅgaṃ tamupāyaṃ ca’ ityādau ṣaḍaṅgatvoktiraṣṭāṅgayogavyapadeśavat | anyathā ‘pañcāṅgasaṃyutaḥ ‘ ityanupadopadarśitapramāṇavirodhaprasaṅgāt | aṅginaścātra bharanyāsarūpatvaṃ ‘ātmātmīyabharanyāso hyātmanānikṣepa ucyate’ iti nirdhāritamiti vyavasthāpitamācāryaiḥ | pakṣe pañcāṅgasya tithyādisvarūpaprakāśakasya tithipatrāparaparyāyasya prapadanena prāptyā upalakṣitaḥ tvāmañcati prāpnoti pūjayati vā | atra bhagavacchāstrīyādivyavahāre prakṛte’prakṛtajyotiśśāstrīyavyavahārāropa iti viśeṣaḥ ||
yathāvā
pramadāṃ hareḥ purastātprapadya vindeta yadi ca sādhutulām | mandapyuccagatissyānmanujo na tu jātu tarhi nīcagatiḥ || 779 ||
mandopi alpopi vā | bhaktyādāvapaṭurvā ‘mūḍhālpāpaṭunirbhāgyāmandāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | anena bhaktyādau śaktyabhāvaḥ ityādyṛktādhikārasaṃpattiḥ puruṣakāraprapadanāvaśyaṃbhāvaśca sūcitaḥ | purastāt bhagavatprapadanātpūrvaṃ hareḥ pramadāṃ bhagavadvallabhāṃ śriyaṃ prapadya śaraṇaṃ prāpya sādhūnāṃ prapannānāṃ tulāṃ samatāṃ ca yadi vindeta tarhi ucce tato lakṣmyā apyucce bhagavati gatiḥ prapattiḥ yasya sa tathoktassyāt | ādau śriyaṃ prapadya ‘astu te tayaiva sarvaṃ saṃpatsyate’ iti śriyā’nugṛṃhīto bhagavantaṃ prapadyata ityarthaḥ | yadvā ucca ūrdhvalokanetrī gatiḥ arcirādisaraṇigatiḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ syāt | jātu kadācidapi nīcagatiḥ adhogatiḥ na tu syāt naiva syāt | arcirādinā pathā paramaṃ vyoma prapannasya śrīvallabhacaraṇāravindaśaraṇāgatasya kathaṃ punassaṃsṛtivyatikarassaṃpadyeta ‘eṣa devapatho brahmapatha etena pratipadyamānā imaṃ mānavamāvartaṃ nāvartante’ iti śravaṇāditi bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe hareḥ siṃharāśeḥ purastāt agrataḥ anantaramityarthaḥ | pramadāṃ kanyārāśiṃ prapadya prāpya sādhu avakraṃ yathā syāttathā tulāṃ tulārāśiṃ yadi vindeta | tarhi mandaḥ śanaiścaraḥ ‘śanimandau’ ityamaraḥ | uccagatirapi syāt | tulāyāstaduccasthānatvāt na kevalaṃ mitrarāśigataḥ apitūccagatirityāpiśabdābhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ tulārāśigaśśaniḥ jātvapi nīcagatiḥ na tu syāt naiva bhavet | meṣarāśestannīcasthānatvasmaraṇāttulāsthitasya tasya na tatprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ | atrāpi bhagavacchāstrīyādivyavahāre jyotiśśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ pūrvavadeva ||
yathāvā
saṃpaśyenmadhumathanassaumyo jīvaṃ yadā tadā saṃjātam | śreyaścintakamāhustaṃ sattvavatāṃ śiraspadairmānyaṃ ca || 780 || saumyaḥ śuddhasattvamayatayā nityaprasannaḥ | budha ityapi gamyate | ‘saumyo jñe nā triṣvanugre manojñe somadaivate’ iti medinī | madhumathanaḥ bhagavān | anena tamoguṇanirasanapaṭīyastvaṃ dyotyate | madhurnāma daityo hi mūrtatamoguṇarūpatvena bhāratādau prasiddhyati | yadā yasmin kāle jīvaṃ pratyagātmānaṃ vākpatiṃ ca | ‘jīvaḥ prāṇini vṛttau ca vṛkṣabhede vṛhaspatau’ iti medinī | saṃpaśyet samyagavaloketa | tadā tasmin kāle saṃjātaṃ taṃ jīvaṃ śreyaścintakaṃ mokṣārthacintakam | pakṣe tadā budhasya vākpativilokanāvasare jātaṃ pumāṃsaṃ śreyaścintakaṃ sarveṣāṃ kalyāṇānudhyātārāṃ dharmacintayitāraṃ vā ‘śreyo muktau śubhe dharme’tipraśaste ca vācyavat’ iti medinī | ata eva sattvavatāṃ sāttvikānām, anyatra balavatāṃ vyavasāyavatāṃ dravyavatāṃ vā rājñāmiti yāvat |
sattvaṃ guṇe piśācādau bale dravyasvabhāvayoḥ |
ātmatve vyavasāyāsucitteṣvastrī tu jantuṣu ||
iti medinī | śiraspadaiḥ śīrṣasthānaiḥ mānyaṃ ca āhuḥ | ‘adha’śśirasī pade’ iti śiraśabdavisargasya satvam | sattvavatāṃ śiraspadairmānyaṃ śreyaścintakaṃ cāhuriti vā yojanā | asyāṃ yojanāyāṃ anupadodāhariṣyamāṇavacanārthātimātrānurūpyaṃ bhavati |
atra
jāyamānaṃ hi puruṣaṃ yaṃ paśyenmadhusūdanaḥ |
sāttvikassa hi vijñeyassa vai mokṣārthacintakaḥ ||
iti mahābhāratīyavyavahāre prastute,
yujyate vīkṣyate vā’pi candrajena bṛhaspatiḥ |
śirasā śāsanaṃ tasya vahanti vasudhādhipāḥ ||
iti jyotiśśāstrīyavyavahārasyāprastutasyāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
prāsyāvaidyānguruṇā dattaṃ vaidyena lokanātharasam | puruṣo niṣevamāṇaḥ puṇyātmā bhavati vigatasarvarujaḥ || 781 ||
avaidyān prakṛtān janān prāsya parityajya
avaidyaḥ prākṛtaḥ prokto vaidyo vaiṣṇava ucyate |
avaidyena na kenāpi vaidyaḥ kiṃcitsamācaret ||
iti bhagavacchāstrokteriti bhāvaḥ | vaidyena ‘sā vidyā yā vimuktaye’ ityuktabrahmavidyāvidā| vidyāśabdāt tadvedetyarthe prāgdīvyatīyo’ṇ | guruṇā ācāryeṇa | ‘ābhyāṃ padābhyāṃ tadvijñānārtham’ iti śrutyuktaśrotriyabrahmaniṣṭhaguruśabdārthāssmāritāḥ | dattaṃ anugṛhītaṃ lokanātharasaṃ bhagavadanubhavānandaṃ niṣevamāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ vigatāḥ sarvarujāḥ yasya sa tathoktaḥ ‘strī rugrujā copatāparogavyādhigadāmayāḥ’ ityamaraḥ | galitasakalopatāpa ityarthaḥ | puṇyātmā dhanyasvabhāvaḥ bhavati ‘puṇyavāndhanyaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | atra
na paśyo mṛtyuṃ paśyati na rogaṃ nota duḥkhatām |
sarvaṃ ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvamāpnoti sarvaśaḥ ||
iti bhūmavidyāgataśrutyarthassmāritaḥ|
pakṣe avaidyān abhiṣajaḥ prāsya parihṛtya guruṇā śreṣṭhena vaidhena bhiṣajā dattaṃ lokanātharasaṃ tannāmānamauṣadhaviśeṣaṃ niṣevamāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ rogīti bhāvaḥ | vigatasarvarujaḥ śāntasakalarogassan vaidyacintāmaṇau lokanātharasasvarūpaṃ prapañcya
ityayaṃ lokanāthasya rasassarvarujo jayet |
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ | ata eva puṇyātmā nīrogatayā cāruśarīro
bhavati | ‘puṇyaṃ tu cārvapi’ iti, ‘ātmā yatno dhṛtirbuddhissvabhāvo brahma varṣma ca’ iti cāmaraḥ | atra pañcarātravedāntaśāstrīyavyavahare vaidyaśāstrīyavyavahārāropa iti pūrvebhyo vailakṣaṇyam ||
yathāvā
bahudhā kaṣāyitā’pi ca vaiṣṇavarasamelanāttaparabhāgā | mānasavṛttirviṣṇukrāntaiṣāmajjvarādikaṃ śamayet || 782 ||
eṣā madīyeti bhāvaḥ | mānasavṛttiḥ bahudhā kaṣāyitā’pi ca rāgādikaluṣitā’pi vaiṣṇavānāṃ manassthitiḥ bhāgavatānāṃ rasaḥ anurāgaḥ tasya melanena tadanugrahavyatikareṇetyarthaḥ | āttaḥ upāttaḥ parabhāgaḥ parasya paramātmanaḥ bhāgo bhajanaṃ bhaktiḥ | bhajerbhāve ghaṅ, upadhāvṛddhiḥ ‘cajoḥ kughiṇyatoḥ iti kutvam | yayā sā tathoktā | yādṛcchikādisukṛtapariṇatisulabhabhāgavatābhimānaviṣayatāsaṃpāditaparabrahmabhaktirityarthaḥ | ata eva viṣṇukrāntā bhagavadākrāntā satī tadākārākāritā satīti yāvat | majjvarādikaṃ madīyabhavatāpādikaṃ śamayet | manovṛtterbhāgavatapravaṇatve bhagavadadhiṣṭhitatve ca sakalasāṃsārikābhitāpanirvāpaṇaṃ saṃbhāvitameveti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe eṣā viṣṇukrāntā prasiddhā | upalakṣaṇamidaṃ bhūnimbādīnām | bahudhā kaṣāyitā kathitā | naitāvadeva, apica kiṃca vaiṣṇavarasasya auṣadhaviśeṣasya melanena yojanena āttaparabhāgā svīkṛtaguṇotkarṣā ‘parabhāgo guṇotkarṣe’ iti yādavaḥ | viṣṇukrāntā svayameva jvarahāriṇī jvaraharavaiṣṇavarasamelane tadguṇotkarṣaḥ kiṃpunarnyāyasiddhaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | āmajvarādikaṃ āmajvaro nāma
jvaropadravatīkṣṇatvamaglānibahumūtratā |
na pravṛttirna viḍjīrṇā na kṣutsā’’majvarākṛtiḥ ||
iti bāhaṭoktalakṣaṇaḥ sa adiryasya tam | jvaropadravāśca daśavidhāḥ | yathoktaṃ tenaiva
śvāsamūrchāruciccharditṛṣṇātīsāranidritāḥ |
hikkākāsāṅgabhedāśca jvarasyopadravā daśa ||
iti | ādiśabdena
jvaravego’dhikaṃ tṛṣṇā pralāpaśśvasanaṃ bhramaḥ |
malapravṛttirutkleśaḥ pacyamānasya lakṣaṇam ||
ityāditaduktapacyamānajvarādikaṃ gṛhyate | śamayet | tathācoktaṃ vaidyacintāmaṇau
bhūnimbāścitrakaṃ cavyaṃ śarapuṅkhī guḍūcikā |
viṣṇukrāntā śṛṅgiberaṃ kvāthassarvajvarāpahaḥ ||
iti, śṛṅgiberamityeṣāṃ kvātha ityadhyāhāreṇa yojyam
dvaṃdvajvaraṃ sannipātaṃ purāṇaṃ viṣamajvaram |
nāśayedakhilaṃ ghoraṃ nāmna’yaṃ vaiṣṇavo rasaḥ ||
iti ca | vaiṣṇavarasalakṣaṇādikaṃ tatraiva draṣṭavyam | atrāpi śrutismṛtipurāṇāgamādivyavahāre vaidyaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ||
yathā vā
śānto mama pāpmā śrīkāntaniṣevaṇata eṣa dhutatāntiḥ | dīptārcissatkaraṇaśrīsubhago’nnādatecchurasmi yataḥ || 783 || idaṃ kasyacitprapitsorvacanam | tathāhi śrīkāntasya śrīnivāsasya niṣevaṇataḥ prapadanāt mama pāpmā vidyādhigamāduttaraṃ pūrvaṃ cāghaṃ śāntaḥ aśliṣṭo vinaṣṭaśca ‘āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavacca’ ityāśaṃsāyāṃ bhūte ktaḥ | na śliṣyati vinaśyatītyāśaṃsāviṣayabhūta ityarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha eṣa ityādinā | yataḥ yasmātkāraṇāt | eṣaḥ ahaṃ dhutā vinaṣṭā tāntiḥ aśanāyāpipāsādiglāniḥ yasya saḥ| dīptā yā arcissatkaraṇaśrīḥ arcirādisatkārasaṃpat tayā subhagaḥ dīpyamānārcirādyātivāhikapuraskaraṇasaubhāgyavān san annādatecchuḥ annādatāṃ ‘ahamannādo’hamannādaḥ’ iti śrutiprathitaparipūrṇabrahmānubhavānandaṃ icchuḥ abhilāṣukaḥ asmi | yato’hamaśanāyādiglānyatīto’rcirādipathena paramaṃ padaṃ prapannaḥ paripūrṇaparabrahmānububhūṣurbhavāmi tadāśaṃse mamottarapūrvāghāśleṣavināśau saṃpadyate itīti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe śrīyuktaṃ ca tat kāntaṃ ca śrīkāntaṃ kāntabhasma ‘vinā’pi pratyayaṃ pūrvottarapadayorlopaḥ’ iti bhasmaśabdasya lopaḥ|
kāntaṃ tu śītaṃ madhuraṃ kaṣāyamāyuṣkaraṃ dhātuvivardhanaṃ ca |
iti yogaratnākare kāntabhasmanaḥ kāntamiti vyapadeśadarśanāt | tasya niṣevaṇataḥ upayogāt mama pāpmā jvaraḥ |
jvaro rogapatiḥ pāpmā mṛtyurojo’śano’ntakaḥ |
iti bāhaṭena pāpmaśabdasya tannāmasu pāṭhāt | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ pāṇḍurogādīnām | śāntaḥ gata evetyarthaḥ | yataḥ yasmāt eṣa janaḥ ahamityarthaḥ | vidhutatāntiḥ vigalitaglāniḥ dīptārciḥ prajvalajjāṭharāgniḥ satī praśastā yā karaṇaśrīḥ indriyasauṣṭhavaṃ tayā subhagaḥ annadatāyāṃ odanāśane icchuścāsmi | idamupalakṣaṇamavyathatvādīnām | atra śrutītihāsādivyavahāre prakṛte aprakṛtasya |
deho laghurvyapagataklamamohatāpaḥ
pāko mukhe karaṇasauṣṭhavamavyathatvam |
svedaḥ kṣavaḥ prakṛtiyogimado’nnalipsā
kaṇḍūśca mūrdhni vigatajvaralakṣaṇāni ||
iti bāhaṭopadarśitavigatajvarapuruṣavyavahārasyāropaḥ | kāntasya jvarādiśamayitṛtvaṃ yogaratnākare 
ye guṇā mṛtarūpyasya te guṇāḥ kāntabhasmanaḥ |
iti rūpyabhasmaguṇātideśāllabhyate | tathāca tatraiva rajatabhasmaguṇā nirūpyante 
tāraṃ śītakaṣāyamamlamadhuraṃ doṣatrayacchedanaṃ
snigdhaṃ dīpanamakṣikukṣigadajiddāhaṃ viṣārtiṃ haret |
medodbhedimadātyayātyayakaraṃ kāntyāyurārogyakṛt
yakṣmāpasmṛtiśūlapāṇḍupalitaplīhajvaraghnaṃ param || iti ||
evamāropyāropaviṣayayośśāstrīyavyavahārayorvijātīyatve darśitānyudāharaṇānei | tayossajātīyatve’pīyaṃ saṃbhavati | yathā
sahakārye’haṃkāre mahati vilīne tavekṣaṇādbhagavan | prakṛtistho’yamiyaṃ dhīrvilīyate tvayi tataḥ pare deve || 784 ||
he bhagavan! tava īkṣaṇāt kaṭākṣāt sahakārye kāryeṇa vakṣyamaṇena saha vartata iti tathokte mahati aparicchedye ahaṃkāre|
avidyāyāmantare vartamānāssvayaṃ dhīraḥ paṇḍitaṃmanyamānā |
jaṅghanyamānāḥ pariyanti mūḍhā andheneva nīyamānā yathā’ndhāḥ ||
ityādyukte svatantrātmabhramamūlake garvāparanāmanyabhimane | yena jarārogādyanekānarthavrātairbhṛśaṃ hanyamānā mūḍhāssaṃsāre bhramanti prāṇinaḥ | vilīne sati | ayaṃ ahamityarthaḥ | svasyaiva vinayātiśayena anyatayā nirdeśaḥ | prakṛtisthaḥ prakṛtiḥ svābhāvikaśeṣatā tasyāṃ tiṣṭhatīti tathoktaḥ
dāsabhūtāssvatassarve hyātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ |
ityādyuktasvābhāvikaśeṣatvānusaṃdhātetyarthaḥ | āsamiti śeṣaḥ | tataḥ iyaṃ dhīḥ madīyā eṣā matiḥ pare deve tvayi vilīyate tvadākārākāritā bhavatītyarthaḥ | ahaṃkārādimalarāhityena śuddhāntaḥkaraṇatayā svābhāvikaśeṣatānusaṃdhāturmama sarvagranthivipramokṣahetubhūtā dhruvā’nusmṛtirbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | atra
līyate brahmaṇi matirnirmamasyānahaṃkṛteḥ| iti,
māmanusmarataścittaṃ mayyeva pravilīyate ||
ityādikaṃ ca smāryate |
pakṣe tava īkṣaṇāt saṃkalpāt sahakārye ekādaśendriyaśabdāditanmātrākāśādibhūtapañcakātmakakāryaissahite ahaṃkāre sāttvikādyahaṃkārākhye tattve mahati mahattatve svakāraṇabhūte vilīne sati ayaṃ mahān mahattattvamityarthaḥ | prakṛtisthaḥ mūlaprakṛtau vilīna ityarthaḥ | iyaṃ prakṛtiḥ avyaktādyaparaparyāyā tataḥ anantaraṃ pare deve parasmin brahmaṇi tvayi līyate | kramādakṣaratvatamastvāvasthāpannā satī ekībhavatītyarthaḥ | atra vedāntaśāstrīyabhagavadbhaktivyavahāre prastute aprastutasya tacchāstrīyasyaiva ‘ahaṃkāro mahati vilīyate mahānavyakte līyate avyaktamakṣare līyate akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate tamaḥ pare deva ekībhavati’ iti śrutyuktapralayavyavahārasyāropaḥ ||
laukike vyavahāre śāstrīyavyavahārāropo yathā
hārāstava pratīkālambanabhājo yathocitaguṇāḍhyāḥ | muñcantyarcissaraṇiṃ dūrānnārāyaṇācalasudhāṃśo || 785 ||
haṃ nārāyaṇācalasudhāṃśo! nārāyaṇācala iti śeṣādrerevanāmāntaramityavocāma | tava pratīkaḥ vakṣorūpo’vayavaḥ tasya ālambanamāśrayaṇaṃ pratīkarūpa āśrayo vā tadbhajantīti tathoktāḥ | yathocitāḥ yathārhāḥ ye guṇāḥ suvarṇāditantavastaiḥ āḍhyāḥ abhirūpā iti yāvat | hārāḥ muktādiratnamayāḥ sarā arcissaraṇiṃ rociḥparaṃparāṃ ‘saraṇiḥ paṅktivartmanoḥ’ iti ranamālā | dūrāt muñcanti prasārayantīti yāvat |
pakṣe pratīkālambanabhājaḥ nāmādipratīkopāsakāḥ ata eva yathocitaguṇāḍhyāḥ yathākratunayena upāsanānuguṇayatkiṃcidguṇasaṃpannāḥ na tu sākṣādbrahmopāsakavadāvirbhūtāpahatapāpmatvādibrāhmaguṇā iti bhāvaḥ | arcissaraṇiṃ arcirādimārgaṃ dūrāt muñcanti ‘apratīkālambanānnayatīti bādarāyaṇa ubhayathā ca doṣāttatkratuśca’ iti sūtreṇa bhagavatā bādarāyaṇenāpratīkālambanānāmevārcirādigaternirṇītatvena pratīkopāsakānāṃ tasyā asaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | pratīkopāsanaṃ nāma abrahmāṇi nāmādau brahmadṛṣṭiḥ yadvidhīyate ‘nāma brahmetyupāsīta’ ityādibhūmavidyādau | atra laukike bhagavanmuktāhāravyavahāre prastute aprastutavedāntaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ || yathāvā
devacchandānuguṇāstamupāsīnāśśritojjvalanasṛtayaḥ | muktākhyāṃ labhamānā maṇayo’śnuvate hareḥ paraṃ dhāma || 786 ||
devacchadasya śatayaṣṭikamuktāhārasya ‘devacchando’sau śatayaṣṭikaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | anuguṇāḥ anurūpāḥ tadghaṭanārhā ityarthaḥ | ata eva taṃ devacchandaṃ upāsīnā āśritā ityarthaḥ | śritojjvalanasṛtayaḥ| śritā prāptā ujjvalanasya utkṛṣṭaprakāśasya sṛtiḥ saraṇaṃ prasaraḥ yaiste tathoktāḥ muktā ityākhyāṃ labhamānāḥ maṇayaḥ moktikānītyarthaḥ | hareḥ śrīnivāsasya paraṃ dhāma divyavigrahaṃ ‘gṛhadehatviṭprabhāvā dhāmāni’ ityamaraḥ| aśnuvate prāpnuvanti tatra prakāśanta iti bhāvaḥ |
anyatra devasya paramātmanaḥ ‘adhyātmayogādhigamena devam’ ityādau devaśabdasya paramātmani prayogāt | chandaḥ abhiprāyaḥ vaśo vā ‘abhiprāyavaśau chandau’ ityamaraḥ | tasyānuguṇāḥ bhagavadadhīnasvarūpasthitipravṛttaya iti yāvat | taṃ devameva ‘sarvaṃ vākyaṃ sāvadhāraṇam’ iti nyāyāt | na tu tatpratīkaṃ nāmādikamiti bhāvaḥ | upāsīnāḥ | ata eva śritojjvalanasṛtayaḥ prāptottamārcirādimārgāssantaḥ ‘padavī sṛtiḥ’ ityamaraḥ | muktāḥ prakṛtibandhavisṛṣṭā iti bhāvaḥ | hareḥ paraṃ dhāma aśnuvate ‘tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam’ ityuktamaprākṛtaṃ deśaviśeṣaṃ vindantītyarthaḥ | atrāpi pūrvavadeva laukike vyavahāre śastrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
muktāḥ katicana maṇayotanavaśśrīnātha mūrtibhājaśca | svairaṃ divyālokān sṛjanti hṛdyāstava svarājaśca || 787 ||
he śrīnātha! tatra hṛdyāḥ hṛdayasthāḥ ‘śarīrāvayavācca’ iti bhavārthe yat ‘hṛdayasya hṛllekha’ ityādinā hṛdayaśabdasya hṛdādeśaḥ | svena rājanta iti svarājaḥ ananyādhīnaprakāśā ityatiśayoktiḥ | atanavaḥ sthūlāḥ | mūrtibhājaḥ kāṭhinyabhājaśca | mūrtiḥ kāṭhinyakāyayoḥ’ ityamaraḥ | katicana muktā iti maṇayaḥ svairaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ divyāśca te ālokāśca divyālokāḥ tān ramaṇīyān prakāśānityarthaḥ | ‘divyaṃ valgulavaṅgayoḥ’ iti hemacandraḥ ‘āloko darśane dyotau’ ityamaraḥ | sṛjanti prasārayantīti yāvat ||
pakṣe tava hṛdyāḥ hṛdayapriyāḥ ‘sa ca mama priyaḥ’ iti gānāt | hṛdayasya priyaḥ’ iti yat | ata eva muktāḥ prakṛtibandhaviyuktāḥ katicana cetanā iti gamyate | svairamityetadatrāpyapakṛṣyate | svairaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ atanavaḥ aśarīrāḥ mūrtibhājaḥ śarīrabhājaśca | divyāḥ divi paramapade bhavāḥ divyāssantaḥ, digāditvādyat | svairaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ lokān ‘sa yadi pitṛlokakāmo bhavati saṃkalpādevāsya pitarassamuttiṣṭhante’ ityādyuktaprakārān lokān sṛjanti | ata eva svarājaśca ananyādhipatayaśca bhavantīti śeṣaḥ | atra pūrvārdhena abhāvādhikaraṇasiddhāntārthaḥ, uttarārdhena saṃkalpādhikaraṇārthaśca saṃgṛhītaḥ|
tatra hy abhāvādhikaraṇe śarīrasya duḥkhahetutvānmuktasya śarīraṃ neti ‘abhāvaṃ bādarirāha hyevam’ iti sūtreṇa bādarimatamanūdya sa ekadhā bhavati’ ityādinā muktasyānekadhā bhāvaśravaṇādacchedyasya niravayavasya śarīreṇa vinā’nekībhāvānupapatteḥ karmakṛtaśarīrasyaiva duḥkhahetutvācca saśarīra evāyamiti ‘bhāvaṃ jaiminirvikalpāmananāt’ iti jaiminimatena pratikṣipya satyasaṃkalpatvaśrutyā icchānurodhena kadācitsaśarīraḥ aśarīraśca kadāciditi svamatamupanyastam | ‘dvādaśāhavadubhayavidhaṃ bādarāyaṇo’taḥ’ iti sūtreṇa bhagavatā bādarāyaṇena | saṃkalpādhikaraṇe tu satyasaṃkalpatvena vyavahriyamāṇānāṃ rājñāṃ kāryaniṣpādane prayatnasāpekṣatvadarśanānmuktasyāpi prayatnasāpekṣaiva pitrādisṛṣṭirityāśaṅkya ‘saṃkalpādevāsya pitarassamuttiṣṭhante’ ityavadhāraṇāt prayatnāpekṣatve pramāṇābhāvācca saṃkalpamātrādeva sṛṣṭiriti ‘saṃkalpādeva tacchrūteḥ’ iti sūtreṇa nirṇīya ‘ata eva cānanyādhipatiḥ’ iti satyasaṃkalpatvameva ‘sa svarāḍbhavati’ iti śrutyudāharaṇena sthāpitaṃ śārīrakabhāṣye | tadetatsarvaṃ mūle yathāsthānaṃ pratyabhijñāpitaṃ taistaiḥ padairiti sāvadhānaṃ draṣṭavyam | atrāpi laukike śāstrīyāropaḥ||
yathāvā
na kṣiptaṃ na ca mūḍhaṃ na ca vikṣiptaṃ śrayettvadīyajanaḥ | tvayyekāgraniruddhasthitirbhaveddeva sārvabhaumo’yam || 788 ||
atra laukike vyavahāre prastute aprastutayogaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ | tathāhi he deva! tvadīyajanaḥ kṣiptaṃ anyairadhikṣiptaṃ pumāṃsaṃ na śrayet | mūḍhaṃ ajñaṃ ca na śrayet | vikṣiptaṃ anavasthitātmānaṃ ca na śrayet | saṃbhāvanāyāṃ liṅ | kiṃtu ayaṃ tvadīyajanaḥ tvayi ekāgraṃ avyagraṃ yathā syāttathā niruddhasthitiḥ niruddhacittavṛttiriti yāvat | sārvabhaumaḥ sarvabhūmerīśvaraḥ sarva bhūmividitaśca bhavet | na ca puṃvākyeṣvanekārthatvaṃ doṣamāvahati| ata eva śiśupālavadhe kriyāsamabhihāreṇa’ ityatra ‘bhṛśaṃ paunaḥpunyena cetyarthaḥ’ iti mallināthaḥ | ‘tasyeśvaraḥ, tatra vidita iti ca’ ityadhikāre ‘sarvabhūmipṛthivībhyāmaṇañau’ ityaṇ | anuśatikāditvādubhayapadavṛddhiḥ ||
pakṣe yogaśāstrīyaprakriyayā’rtho’nusandheyaḥ | tathāhi ‘yogaścittavṛttinirodhaḥ’ iti pātañjalaṃ sūtram | cittasya rajastamovṛttīnāṃ nirodho yoga iti tadarthaḥ | ‘sa ca sārvabhaumaḥ’ iti tadbhāṣyam | cittasya pañca bhūmayo bhavanti kṣiptaṃ mūḍhaṃ vikṣiptaṃ ekāgraṃ niruddhamiti | tāsu sarvāsu bhūmiṣu vidita iti tadarthaḥ yadāha bhojarajaḥ ‘sa ca nirodhassarvāsāṃ cittavṛttīnāṃ sarvaprāṇināṃ ca dharmaḥ kadācitkasyāṃcidvṛddhibhūmāvāvirbhavati’ iti | ayamāśayaḥ yadā cittasya yā buddhivṛttirudeti tadā tasyāmanyasyā vṛtternirodho dṛśyate | yathā kṣiptāvasthāyāṃ mūḍhāvasthānirodha iti tasmādayaṃ nirodhassarvāsvapi cittabhūmiṣūktaprakāreṇa vidita iti | vācaspatimiśrastu ‘ekāgrasya cittasya tisro bhūmayaḥ madhumatī madhupratīkā viśoketi | niruddhasya tu saṃskāraśaṣataikaiveti | sarvāsu ca tāsu catasṛṣu bhūmiṣu anyāsāṃ pramāṇādivṛttīnāṃ nirodho viditaḥ’ iti tadarthamāha | yuktaṃ caitat kṣiptādiṣu pañcasu bhūmiṣu kṣitaṃ sadaiva rajasā teṣu teṣu viṣayeṣu kṣipyamāṇamatyantamasthiram | mūḍhaṃ tu tamassamudrekānnidrādimat | kṣiptādviśiṣṭaṃ vikṣiptam | viśeṣaścāsthemabahulasya kādācitkasthemā | tatra kṣiptamūḍhayossatyapi parasparāpekṣayā vṛttinirodhe pāraṃparyeṇāpi niśśreyasahetutāvirahāttadupaghātukatvācca samādhiśaṅkaiva nāsti | vikṣipte tu cetasi kādācitkassamādhirna yogapakṣe vartate | kiṃtu tīvrapavanavikṣiptapradīpavatsvayameva naśya ti | ekāgraṃ tvekaviṣayakadhārāvāhikavṛttisāmarthaṃ sattvodrekeṇa tamoguṇakṛtatandrādivilayādekākārā vṛttiḥ | sā ca rajoguṇakṛtacāñcalyarūpavikṣepābhāvādekaviṣayaiveti śuddhe sattve bhavati cittamekāgram | asyāṃ ca bhūmau saṃprajñātaḥ atra dhyeyākārāvṛttirapi bhāsate | tasyā api nirodhe niruddhaṃ cittamasaṃprajñātasamādhibhūmirbhavatīti kramaḥ | tasmāt kṣiptamūḍhavikṣiteṣu samadherasaṃbhavādavaśiṣṭayorekāgraniruddhayoreva samādhibhūmitayā tadavāntarabhūtamadhumatīprabhṛtibhūmicatuṣṭayamādāya sārvabhaumatvaṃ yogasya nirvyūḍhaṃ vācaspatimiśreṇeti | ayameva hi pakṣo’sminnudāharaṇapadye vivakṣitaḥ | kṣiptamūḍhavikṣiptabhūmitrayāśrayaṇaṃ pratiṣidhya ekāgraniruddhākhyabhūmidvayasthityaiva yogasya sārvabhaumatāyā darśitatvādityalam ||
yathā vā
avisannidhisātaṅkastavasenāreṇurullalan śaure| manye’bhrasaritsalilopasparśātsakalamapi khamasprākṣīt || 789 ||
he śaure! tava senāreṇuḥ aveḥ bhānoḥ strīdharmiṇyāśca |
avirnā parvate sūrye meṣe mūṣakakambale |
prākāre bhavane bhāsi strī tu puruṣavatībhuvoḥ ||
iti ratnamālā | sannidheḥ sannikarṣāt sātaṅkaḥ tāpānvitaḥ upahatiśaṅkānvitaśca ‘ruktāpaśaṅkāsvātaṅkaḥ’ ityamaraḥ | ullalan ūrdhvaṃ gacchan | pakṣe utplavamāna ityācāraśīlānubhāvoktiḥ | abhrasaritaḥ salilasya upasparśāt avamarśāddhetoḥ | pakṣe snānādācamanācca tatkṛtvetyarthaḥ |puṃvākyeṣvanekārthatvamadoṣāyetyanantarapūrvaślokavyākhyāyāmavocāma | lyablope pañcamī ‘upasparśassparśamātre snānācamanayorapi’ iti medinī | sakalaṃ khaṃ antarikṣaṃ asprākṣīt | aspṛśadeveti manye anuminomi anyathā mandākinīsparśāsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | pakṣe sakalaṃ khaṃ cakṣurādīndriyaṃ asprākṣīt upāspṛśadityarthaḥ |
caṇḍālasūtikodakyāpatitān dūratastyajet |
atassannidhimātreṇa sacelaṃ snānamācaret ||
iti, snātvā pītvā ca bhuktvā ca suptvā kṣutvā dvijottamaḥ|
anena vidhinā samyagācāntaśśucitāmiyāt ||
iti ca saṃvartasmṛteḥ,
adbhistu prakṛtisthābhirhīnābhiḥ phenabudbudeḥ |
triḥ prāśyāpo dvirunmṛjya khānyadbhissamupaspṛśet ||
iti yājñavalkyena ācamanānantaraṃ cakṣurādīndriyasparśasmaraṇāśca | ‘indriye’pi kham’ ityamaraḥ | atra laukike bhagavatsenāreṇuvyavahāre gharmaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathā vā
hṛdgatamuktāvalikaśśitikaṇṭhīyātivelarucisubhagaḥ | prauḍhaśiromaṇidīdhitiparicayaparabhāgavān harirjayati || 790 ||
hṛdgatā vakṣassthā manassthā ca muktāvaliḥ muktāhāraḥ nyāyagranthaviśeṣaśca yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kap | śitikaṇṭhīyā mayūrasaṃbandhinī ativelā ruciḥ tayā su bhagaḥ | pakṣe śitikaṇṭhīyamiti nyāyagranthaviśeṣaḥ | tasminnativelā ruciḥ āsthā tayā subhagaḥ | prauḍhaḥ śiromaṇiḥ cūḍāratnaṃ tasya dīdhiteḥ prabhāyāḥ, pakṣe śiromaṇidīdhitiḥ nyāyagranthaviśeṣaḥ tasya paricayataḥ saṃparkāt vyāsaṅgācca parabhāgavān guṇotkarṣavān ‘parabhāgo guṇotkarṣe’ iti kośaḥ | atra laukike śrīnivāsadivyavigrahavyavahāra nyāyaśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ | atrāprakṛtavyavahārasyāpi laukikatvābhisandhau prāthamikaprabhedasyaivedaṃ syādudāharaṇam ||
yathāvā
haste’nantocce tava lagnaṃ dhanuruditamucchritaṃ nanu rājye | saumyaminaṃ ca dadhānaṃ suśrīkaṃ svakalitodayaṃ tvātanute || 791 ||
nanu ananta! he bhagavan! ucce utkṛṣṭe ucchrite vā tava haste pāṇau lagnaṃ ata eva uditaṃ utkarṣaṃ prāptaṃ dhanuḥ cāpaṃ kartṛ ucchritaṃ utkarṣeṇāśritaṃ janaṃ vibhīṣaṇādikamiveti bhāvaḥ | rājye ādhirājyasthāne saumyaṃ anugraśāsanaṃ inaṃ rājānaṃ ca ‘saumyo jñe nā triṣvanugre’ iti, ‘inaḥ patyau nṛpārkayoḥ’ iti ca medinī | dadhānaṃ puṣṇat rājye nirābādhaṃ niveśayadityarthaḥ | suśrīkaṃ śobhanatejasaṃ svakalitodayaṃ svena dhanena ‘svo’striyāṃ dhane’ ityamaraḥ | kalitaḥ udayaḥ prābhavaṃ yasya taṃ tathoktam | tuśabdo’vadhāraṇe | uktavidhamevetyarthaḥ | nātra saṃdegdhavyamiti bhāvaḥ | ātanute iti |
itaratra nanu iti kasyacitsaṃbodhanam | haste hastarkṣe anantocce anante antarikṣe ucce sati draṣṭurbhrūmadhyatulyabhāgasthe sati uditaṃ udayaṃ prāptaṃ dhanuḥ dhanurākhyaṃ tava lagnaṃ tvajjanmalagnaṃ, tathācoktaṃ jyautiṣopakaraṇe 
avācyabhimukho bhūtvā niśi kālaṃ khamadhyama |
svabhrūmadhyagatāṃ yogatārāṃ dṛṣṭvā vadetsudhīḥ ||
akṣarāṇāṃ kaṭapayādīnāṃ saṃkhyākramāśrayāt |
tattannakṣatra uccasthe lagnānāṃ gatirucyate ||
ityupakramya ‘hasto dhanurgṛhe bālā’ iti | hastanakṣatramuccasthaṃ cet dhanurlagne tisro nāḍikāḥ trayo nāḍikāṣṭāṃśāśca gatā iti tadarthaḥ |
prativākyaṃ paṭhyamānavarṇayugmādimākṣaram |
lagne’vabodhayennāḍīṃ tadaṣṭāṃśaṃ parākṣaram ||
iti tatraivokteḥ | rājye daśame kanyārāśau ucchritaṃ uccasthamityarthaḥ | idaṃ saumyamityanenaivānveti, tasyaiva kanyāyā uccasthānatvāt | na tvinamityanena | tasya hi meṣo rāśiruccasthānam | saumyaṃ budhaṃ inaṃ bhānuṃ ca dadhānaṃ sat svakalitodayaṃ svasmin dhanurlagne kalitodayaṃ prāptajanmānaṃ tvā tvāṃ suśrīkaṃ śobhanaiśvaryaṃ tanute | bhāgyādhipasya bhānoruccagatena rājyādhipena budhena saha rājyasthāne’vasthānāditi bhāvaḥ | dhanurlagnasya siṃhakanye bhāgyarājyasthāne | ‘bhāgyaṃ dharmaśca navamaṃ, karma rājyaṃ ca daśamam’ iti navamadaśamarāśyorbhāgyarājyasthānatvābhidhānāt | atra laukike bhagavaddhanurvyavahāre
bhāgyarājyādhipau syātāṃ bhāgye rājye’pi vā yadi |
tayoreko’pyuccagaścenmahadrājyaṃ prayacchati ||
iti jyautiṣavyavahārāropa || yathāvā
phaṇiśikhariṇīśvara tvaṃ nivasankoṇe kvavā’pi surapūjyaḥ| bhavitā yadudayadṛṣṭirdhīmān śrīmān bhavetsa ca vapuṣmān || 792 ||
phaṇiśikhariṇi śeṣādrau | īśvara ! svāmin ! ‘svāmīśvarādhipati’ ityādinā saptamī | surapūjyaḥ devārādhanīyaḥ | pakṣe bṛhaspatirityarthaḥ | tvaṃ kvavā’pi koṇe phaṇiśikhariṇītyetadatrānuṣajyate | śeṣādrau kasmiṃścitpradeśe ityarthaḥ | pakṣe lagnātpañcame navame vā trikoṇākhyasthāne nivasan san yasya puṃsaḥ udaye abhyudaye | pakṣe udayaśabdaḥ svadraṣṭavyasthānopalakṣakaḥ udaye janmalagne tadupalakṣite trikoṇāntare ca | dṛṣṭiḥ abhiprāyaḥ dṛkca yasya sa tathokto bhavitā | sa pumān dhīmān śrīmān vapuṣmāṃśca bhavati | tvatkaṭākṣitasya kiṃ durlabhamiti bhāvaḥ | dvitīyapakṣe yajjanmalagnātpañcame navame vā koṇe vidyamāno gururlagnaṃ trikoṇadṛṣṭyā paśyati sa dhīmān śrīmān vapuṣmāṃśca bhavati | pañcamasya buddhisthānatvāttatra gurusthitau dhīmān tasmātpañcamakoṇadṛṣṭyā navamasthānadarśane śrīmān navamakoṇadṛṣṭyā vapussthānasya lagnasya vīkṣaṇe vapuṣmāṃśca bhavati | evaṃ navamasya bhāgyasthānatvāttatra tadavasthāne śrīmān tasmātpañcamakoṇadṛṣṭyā lagnāvalokane pūrvavadvapuṣmān navamakoṇadṛṣṭyā pañcamasthānāvalokane dhīmāṃśca bhavediti vivekaḥ | atra laukike bhagavatkaṭākṣitajanavyavahāre,
paśyanti saptamān sarve śanijīvakujāḥ punaḥ |
viśeṣatastu tridaśatrikoṇacaturaśrakān ||
yoyo bhāvassvāmidṛṣṭo yuto vā |
saumyairvā syāttasyatasyābhivṛddhiḥ ||
ityādijyotiśśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
na namaya śiro yatastvayi nanu vṛtte kā’pi mālinī bhavati kṛtin | sādhvanvayaguṇarucirā hyatiśakvaryantarujjvalā’naghalakṣmīḥ || 793 ||
idaṃ padyaṃ kaṃcitkṛtinaṃ prati kasyacidvacanam | he kṛtin ! śiraḥ mūrdhānaṃ na namaya na prahvaya | yataḥ yasmātkāraṇāt tvayi vṛtte caritre viṣaye kā’pi mālinī mālinyaṃ ‘guṇavacana’ iti bhāve ṣyañi ṣitvalakṣaṇo ṅīṣ | na bhavati nu | nu ityetadavyayamanunaye | ‘nu vitarkāpamānayoḥ ! vikalpānunayātīte praśne hetvapadeśayoḥ’ iti medinī | yadi cāritre yatkiṃcidapi saṃbhavenmālinyaṃ tarhi śiro namayitavyaṃ, taddhi śironamanakāraṇaṃ, na hi tvayi taddṛśyata iti bhāvaḥ | tatra hetumāha sādhviti | he anagha ! hi yasmāt sādhvanvayaguṇarucirā sādhuḥ anvayassaṃbandhaḥ yeṣāṃ te nityasaṃbaddhā ityarthaḥ | tathāvidhāḥ ye guṇāḥ satyatvajñānatvānandatvādayaḥ apahatapāpmatvādayaśca kalyāṇaguṇāḥ taiḥ rucirā ramaṇīyā | atiśakvarī duritanigrahāśritānugrahayoratimātraśaktā | śaknoteḥ ‘anyebhyopi dṛśyate’ iti kvanip | strītvavivakṣāyāṃ ‘vano ra ca’ iti ṅīp, raścāntādeśaḥ | īdṛśī lakṣmīḥ śrīḥ antaḥ tava hṛdaye ujjvalā dīptā tvayā dhyāteti yāvat | uktaprakāralakṣmīnivāsabhūtahṛdayatayā tava mālinyasparśo na bhavatyeveti bhāvaḥ || anyatra tvayi ityatra tu ayi iti chedaḥ | ayi kṛtin ! nanamayaśiroyataḥ nanamayāḥ krameṇa nagaṇadvayamagaṇayagaṇāḥ śirasi ādau yasya sa tathoktaḥ | yaḥ yagaṇaḥ tasmāt nanamayaśiroyataḥ | sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ | vṛtte, jātyekavacasam | vṛtteṣu madhye kā’pi iyamapyeketyarthaḥ | mālinī bhavati mālinīnāma vṛttaṃ bhavati | ‘nanamayayayuteyaṃ mālinī bhogiloktaiḥ’ iti tallakṣaṇāt | tāmeva viśinaṣṭi sādhviti | anvayaḥ avidyamānaḥ uvarṇo yasya saḥ anuḥ | nañpūrvapadabahuvrīhau naño nalope ‘tasmānnuḍaci ‘ iti nuṭ | sa cāsau ayaśca yaṃ yātītyayaḥ | yādhātoḥ ‘āto’nupasarge kaḥ’ iti kaḥ | ukārasthānikākāravānityarthaḥ | guṇaḥ gaṇa ityarthaḥ | guṇaśabde uktarītyā ukārotsāraṇe tatraiva akāranyāse gaṇa iti niṣpatteḥ | sādhuḥ vakṣyamāṇarītyā śubhapradaḥ yaḥ anvayaguṇaḥ, jātyekavacanam | yadvā sādhavaḥ anvayaguṇā iti bahuvacanāntaśabdābhyāmeva vigrahaḥ | anvayaguṇāḥ pūrvoktāḥ nanamayayagaṇā ityarthaḥ | taiḥ rucirā | atiśakvarī tannāmapañcadaśaṃ chandaḥ | ‘uktā’tyuktā’ ityārabhya ‘śakvarī sā’tipūrvā syāt’ iti tasyāḥ pañcadaśatvena gaṇanāt tasyāṃ antaḥ madhye ujjvalā dīpyamānā anaghāḥ nirdoṣāḥ lakṣmyaḥ saṃpado yasyāsseti pañcamībahuvrīhiḥ | hītyavadhāraṇe | asyāḥ prabandhādau prayoge kṛtinetustatpraṇetuśca saṃpadassiddhyantyeveti bhāvaḥ | yathoktamalaṃkāraśirobhūṣaṇe
nagaṇastrilaghurdadātyajasraṃ sakalārthānapi devatā’sya yajvā |
haritālanibho graheṇa hīnastadamuṣyārigaṇo na kaścidasti ||
magaṇatriguruśśriyaṃ vidhatte budhabhūmigrahadevatastaṭicchrīḥ |
tanute sagaṇānvito’tha lakṣmīṃ bhavati krūrayutastvaśobhano’sau ||
yagaṇe jaladevato’tiśubhro dhanadāyyādilaghuḥ kavigrahaśca |
tagaṇena yuto’śubhaṃ pradatte vipamiśraudanavatkaveśca netuḥ ||
yasya yasya tu śubhā’dhidevatā tasya tasya tu gaṇasya saumyatā |
yasya yasya bhavatīha sā’nyathā krūratā bhavati tasya tasya hi ||
iti | evaṃca nagaṇopakrame’sminvṛtte nagaṇasya grahavidhuratayā sarvagaṇayoge’pi śubhapradatvāt budhādidevatākasya magaṇāderiva ripupāpagrahadevatākatvaprayuktāśubhagaṇasaṃbandhanibandhanāśubhatvānavakāśānnisargata eva śubhayormagaṇayagaṇayoryogācca lakṣmīpradatvamiti dhyeyam | atredaṃ bodhyam ‘nadyutaśca’ iti kāpa siddhe uraḥprabhṛtiṣu lakṣmīśabdapāṭhasya ekavacanāntalakṣmīśabdāvayavakabahuvrīhereva nityaṃ kap, anyatra ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti vikalpa eveti niyamārthatāyā uktatvādiha ca bahuvacanāntalakṣmīśabdena vigrahasya pradarśitatvānna nityakapprasaktiḥ | ‘śeṣādvibhāṣā’ iti kapaśca vaikalpikatayā’tra tasyāvivakṣaṇam | nāpyupasarjanahṛsvaḥ ‘gostriyoḥ’ ityanena strīpratyayāntasyaiva hrasvavidhānāt | lakṣmīśabdasya ca strīpratyayāntatvābhāvāditi | atra laukike prastutavyavahāre aprastutacchandaśśāstrīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
ramaṇīyasandhibandhaṃ hṛdyāṅkaṃ sarvato’bhinandyarasam | rūpaṃ tavābhirūpaṃ vilokya sabhyā mukundayānti mudam || 794 ||
ramaṇīyassandhibandho yasya tat | anena
aṅgapratyaṅgakānāṃ yassanniveśo yathocitam ||
susnigdhasaṃdhibandhaṃ yattatsaundaryamitīryate |
ityuktalakṣaṇasaundaryavattvaṃ dyotitam | pakṣe ramaṇīyāḥ sandhīnāṃ | ‘mukhaṃ pratimukhaṃ garbhassāvamarśopasaṃhṛtiḥ’ ityuktāḥ ye pañca sandhayasteṣāṃ bandhaḥ yasmiṃstat | hṛdyaḥ hṛdaye bhavaḥ aṅkaḥ śrīvatsacihnaṃ yasya tat | pakṣe hṛdayapriyāḥ aṅkāḥ ||
pratyakṣanetṛcarito binduvyaktipuraskṛtaḥ |
aṅko nānāprakārārthasaṃvidhānarasāśrayaḥ ||
ityuktalakṣaṇāḥ vicchedaviśeṣāḥ yasmiṃstat | sarvataḥ abhinandyā rasā bhūḥ yena tat abhinandanīyaśṛṅgārādirasamiti ca | tava abhirūpaṃ manojñaṃ rūpaṃ divyavigrahaṃ vilokya sabhyāḥ sādhavaḥ sāmājikāśca mudaṃ yānti | atra laukikavyavahāre nāṭakīyavyavahārāropaḥ ||
śāstrīye laukikavyavahāropo yathā
