vijaro vimṛtyurapahatapāpmā tvaṃ satyakāma iti bhagavan | tvayi tatparā prasūte śrutiranaghā | kāṅkṣitārthamakhilamapi || 795 ||
atra vedāntaśāstrīye vyavahāre laukikavallabhānuraktasuprajaḥ kuṭumbinīvyavahārāropaḥ | ‘apahatapāpmā vijaraḥ’ iti śrutyartho’trānusaṃhitaḥ | pakṣe satyakāmaḥ svanāyikānurakta iti yāvat | kāṅkṣitārthaṃ apekṣitaṃ abhidheyaṃ prayojanaṃ ca ||
yathāvā
pravihāya bhuktabhogāmacitaṃ prakṛtiṃ svalābhasotkaṇṭham | tāvakamacyuta dhanyaṃ kuśalatarā saṃpratīkṣate muktiḥ || 796 || bhuktabhogāṃ anubhūtasukhāṃ virakteḥ prāgeva bhogā bhuktāḥ na tūpariṣṭādbhokṣyanta iti bhāvaḥ | acitaṃ acetanarūpāṃ ajñāṃ ca prakṛtiṃ pravihāya ‘jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo’nyaḥ ‘ iti śrutiratrānusaṃhitā | svalābhe apahatapāpmatvādiviśiṣṭasvasvarūpaprāptau sotkaṇṭham | pakṣe svalābhe ātmaprāptau sotkaṇṭhaṃ
sarvendriyasukhasvādo yatrāstītyabhimanyate |
tatprāptīcchāṃ sasaṃkalpāmutkaṇṭhāṃ kavayo viduḥ ||
ityuktecchāviśeṣasahitam | he acyuta! tāvakaṃ tvadbhaktaṃ dhanyaṃ kṛtakṛtyaṃ dhanaṃ labdhāraṃ ca pumāṃsaṃ kuśalatarā atimātrakṣemaṃkarā caturatarā ca ‘paryāptau sukṛte kṣeme kuśalaṃ nipuṇe triṣu’ iti ratnamālā | muktiḥ pratīkṣate pratipālayati | atra vedāntaśāstrīye prakṛte vyavahāre bhuktabhogamūḍhavanitāparityajanapūrvakasvābhilāṣināyakapratipālayitṛvāsakasajjikāvyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
sā devatā vijayatetarāṃ paravyūhavibhavabhedena | sādhvarcā bhuvane’ntaryāmitayā cāpi vikhyātyā ||
sā devatā seyaṃ devataikṣata’ ityuktāparabrahmarūpā devatā | paravyūhavibhavabhedena paraḥ paravāsudevaḥ vyūhāḥ vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇādyāḥ vibhavāḥ rāmakṛṣṇādyavatārarūpāḥ tadrūpeṇa bhedena vijayatetarām | kiṃca bhuvane asmin jagati sādhvarcā śrīraṅgaveṃkaṭācalādidivyakṣetranivāsaramaṇīyārcārūpā | sādhvī arcā yasyāsseti bahuvrīhiḥ | sādhvī ca sā arceti tatpuruṣo vā | ‘pratikṛtirarcā’ ityamaraḥ | antaryāmitayā antaryāmirūpeṇa, hetau tṛtīyā | vikhyātyā cāpi antaryāmitvaprayuktakīrtyā cāpītyarthaḥ| yadvā viśiṣṭā khyātiryasyāssā tathoktā, antaryāmitayā cāpīti yojyam | vijayatetarāmityanuṣajyate | parabrahmarūpā devatā paravyūhavibhavārcāntaryāmirūpairjagati vijayatetarāmiti nirgalito’rthaḥ | pakṣe uttarārdhe bhuvane antaḥ yā amitayā iticchedaḥ | bhuvane’ntaḥ lokasya madhye ityarthaḥ | ‘saptamī śauṇḍaiḥ’ iti saptamīsamāsavidhāyakasūtrasthaśauṇḍādigaṇe antaśśabdapāṭhāt jñāpakāt tadyoga avayavina ādhāratvavivakṣayā saptamī vṛkṣe śākhetivaditi dhyeyam | yā devatā pareṣāṃ vairiṇāṃ vyūhāḥ balavinyāsāḥ teṣāṃ vibhavasya bhedena vidāraṇena amitayā aparicchinnayā vikhyātyā kīrtyā cāpi sādhvī arcā yasyāssā tathoktā pūjyā bhavatītyarthaḥ | sā devatā vijayatetarāmityanvayaḥ | atra prastute pañcarātrāgamasiddhe vyavahāre 
mahatī devatā hyeṣā nararūpeṇa tiṣṭhati |
ityuktaprabhāvapratāpayaśassubhagamahārājavyavahāro laukika āropyate ||
yathāvā
nanu nityadasyuvargatrasto’haṃ kvāpi rakṣaṇamavindan | viśvasya tvayi nātha nyastasvo dhanya eva na kathaṃ syām || 798 ||
nanu nātha ! nityāḥ anapāyinaḥ ye dasyavaḥ kāmādayaśśatravaḥ teṣāṃ vargāt vargasya vā trastaḥ bhītaḥ | pakṣe nityaṃ dasyuvargāt coranivahāt trastaḥ udvignaḥ ‘dasyuścore puṃsi ripau’ ityubhayatrāpi medinī | kvāpi rakṣaṇamavindan śaraṇamalabhamānassan viśvasya rakṣiṣyatīti pratītya tvayi nyastaṃ arpitaṃ svaṃ ātmā dhanaṃ ca yena sa tathoktaḥ ‘svo jñātāvātmani svaṃ triṣvātmīye svo’striyāṃ dhane’ ityubhayatrāpyamaraḥ | dhanyaḥ kṛtārtha eva kathaṃ na syām | bhagavati nyastātmarakṣābharasya kṛtārthatā syādeveti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣe dhanaṃ labdhā dhanyaḥ pratyayite sujanasārvabhaume nyastadhanasya kathaṃ punastaddhanalābho na syāditi bhāvaḥ | atra bhagavacchāstrīye vyavahāre laukikavyavahārāropaḥ ||
yathāvā
prollasadamalābjakaraḥ prakāśavibhavaiḥ prasādhitāśāntaḥ | prathate phaṇādharagiriprabhorapaghano ghanāgamasyānte || 799 ||
prollasan amalaḥ abjaśaṅkhaḥ kare yasya saḥ, prollasantaḥ amalāḥ abjakarāḥ candrakiraṇāḥ yasmin sa iti ca | prakāśavibhavaiḥ prabhātiśayaiḥ prakṛṣṭaiḥ kāśakusumavibhavaiśca prasādhitāśāntaḥ | phaṇādharagiriprabhoḥ apaghanaḥ divyavigrahaḥ ghanasya gambhīrārthasya āgamasya vedasya ante upaniṣadītyarthaḥ | pakṣe ghanāgamasya prāvṛṭsamayasya ante avasāne ityarthaḥ | prathate | atrāpi śāstrīye vyavahāre laukikaśaratsamayavyavahārāropaḥ ||
saṃkhyāvadbhissarvavidbhiścamatkāraikajīvitaiḥ |
udāharaṇabhūyastvaṃ soḍhavyaṃ cāpalātkṛtam ||
iyaṃ cālaṃkārāntareṣu bhūyassvānuguṇyena vartate | yathā
kaṭakaścaraṇe vyalasacchiro hareradhiruroha maṇimakuṭī | datte’ntare vadhūṭī svalpe’pi viśṛṅkhalapravṛttissyāt || 800 ||
atra sāmarthyatvena vartamānā samāsoktirarthāntaranyāsānuguṇyamādhatte || yathāvā
gṛhṇāti ko nu hṛdayānyabalānāṃ bhuvi nisargacapalānām | daramanabhimukhe bhagavati ciraparicitamapi naraṃ tyajati saṃpat || 801 ||
atra samarthakatvena vartate ||
prasṛmaraghṛṇayo maṇayo hāragatāśśaurihṛtpadagatāya | prābhṛtamarpayituṃ maṇivarāya manye prasārayanti karān || 802 ||
atra sāmrājyasiṃhāsanādhirūḍhasārvabhaumopahārārpaṇaparaparicchinnajanapadādhipanarapatisamudayavyavahāramūlā karaprasāraṇotprekṣā | anenaiva pathā acetanavyavahāre prastute cetanavyavahārasaṃbandhisvarūpahetuphalotprekṣāyāṃ, cetanavyavahāre prastute cācetanavyavahārasaṃbandhisvarūpahetuphalotprekṣāyāṃ ca samāsoktireva mūlam | sārūpyādapīyaṃ samāsoktirbhavatīti dīkṣitairabhyadhāyi| na kṣamate tāṃ samāsoktijīvātorviśeṣaṇasāmyasya ‘purā yatra srotaḥ’ ityatrābhāvena samāsoktitāyā evānupapatteriti vadanrasagaṅgādharakṛt | tanmate ‘purā yatra strotaḥ ityādāvaprastutapraśaṃsaiva, tathaiva tenavyavasthāpanāt | tatsarvamapyupariṣṭādaprastutapraśaṃsālaṃkāranirūpaṇāvasare darśayiṣyāmaḥ ||
ityalaṃkāramaṇihāre samāsoktisaraḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ
pūrvabhāgassamāptaḥ
